 adhyāya 1
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ  || 1.1 ||
oṃ namaḥ śrīpuruṣottamāya  |
oṃ namaḥ śrīnarmadāyai  |
oṃ namo hariharahiraṇyagarbhebhyo namo vyāsavālmīkiśukaparāśarebhyo namo gurugobrāhmaṇebhyaḥ  |
oṃ majjanmātaṅgagaṇḍacyutamadamadirāmodamattālimālaṃ snānaiḥ siddhāṅganānāṃ kucayugavigalatkuṅkumāsaṅgapiṅgam  |
sāyaṃ prātarmunīnāṃ kusumacayasamācchannatīrasthavṛkṣaṃ pāyādvo narmadāmbhaḥ karimakarakarākrāntarahaṃstaraṃgam  || 1.2 ||
ubhayataṭapuṇyatīrthā prakṣālitasakalalalokaduritaughā  |
devamunimanujavandyā haratu sadā narmadā duritam  || 1.3 ||
nāśayatu duritamakhilaṃ bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhavacca bhuvi bhavinām  |
sakalapavitri tava sudhā puṇyajalā narmadā bhavati  || 1.4 ||
taṭapulinaṃ śivadevā yasyā yatayo'pi kāmayante vā  |
muninivahavihitasevā śivāya mama jāyatāṃ revā  || 1.5 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ namaskṛtvā naraṃ caiva narottamam  |
devīṃ sarasvatīṃ vyāsaṃ tato jayamudīrayet || 1.6 ||
naimiṣe puṇyanilaye nānāṛṣiniṣevite  |
śaunakaḥ satramāsīnaḥ sūta papraccha vistarāt || 1.7 ||
manye'haṃ dharmanaipuṇyaṃ tvayi sūta sadārcitam  |
puṇyāmṛtakathāvaktā vyāsasaśiṣyastvameva hi  || 1.8 ||
atastvāṃ paripṛcchāmi dharmatīrthāśrayaṃ kave  |
bahūni santi tīrthāni bahuśo me śrutāni ca  || 1.9 ||
śrutā divyanadī brāhmī tathā viṣṇunadī mayā  |
tṛtīyā na mayā kvāpi śrutā raudrī saridvarā  || 1.10 ||
tāṃ vedagarbhāṃ vikhyātāṃ vibudhaughābhivanditām  |
vada me tvaṃ mahāprājña tīrthapūgapariṣkṛtām  || 1.11 ||
kaṃ deśamāśritā revā kathaṃ śrīrudrasaṃbhavā  |
tatsaṃśritāni tīrthāni yāni tāni vadasva me  || 1.12 ||

sūta uvāca -
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ kulapate caritraṃ narmadāśritam  |
citraṃ pavitraṃ doṣaghnaṃ śrutamuktaṃ ca sattama  || 1.13 ||
vedopavedavedāṅgādīnyabhivyasya pūritaḥ  |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ vaktā satyavatīsutaḥ  || 1.14 ||
taṃ namaskṛtya vakṣyāmi purāṇāni yathākramam  |
yeṣāmabhivyāharaṇādabhivṛddhirvṛṣāyuṣoḥ  || 1.15 ||
śrutiḥ smṛtiśca viprāṇāṃ cakṣuṣī parikīrtite  |
kāṇastatraikayā hīno dvābhyāmandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 1.16 ||
śrutismṛtipurāṇāni viduṣāṃ locanatrayam  |
yastribhirnayanaiḥ paśyetso'ṃśo māheśvaro mataḥ  || 1.17 ||
ātmano vedavidyā ca īśvareṇa vinirmitā  |
śaunakīyā ca paurāṇī dharmaśāstrātmikā ca yā  || 1.18 ||
tisro vidyā imā mukhyāḥ sarvaśāstravinirṇaye  |
purāṇaṃ pañcamo veda iti brahmānuśāsanam  || 1.19 ||
yo na veda purāṇaṃ hi na sa vedātra kiṃcana  |
katamaḥ sa hi dharmo'sti kiṃ vā jñānaṃ tathāvidham  || 1.20 ||
anyadvā tatkimatrāha purāṇe yanna dṛśyate  |
vedāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ pūrvaṃ purāṇe nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 1.21 ||
bibhetyalpaśrutādvedo māmayaṃ pratariṣyati  |
itihāsapurāṇaiśca kṛto'yaṃ niścayaḥ purā  || 1.22 ||
ātmā purāṇaṃ vedānāṃ pṛthagaṃgāni tāni ṣaṭ |
yacca dṛṣṭaṃ hi vedeṣu taddṛṣṭaṃ smṛtibhiḥ kila  || 1.23 ||
ubhābhyāṃ yattu dṛṣṭaṃ hi tatpurāṇeṣu gīyate  |
purāṇaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ prathamaṃ brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam  || 1.24 ||
anantaraṃ ca vaktrebhyo vedāstasya vinirgatāḥ  |
purāṇamekamevāsīdasmin kalpāntare mune  || 1.25 ||
trivargasādhanaṃ puṇyaṃ śatakoṭipravistaram  |
smṛtvā jagāda ca munīnprati devaścaturmukhaḥ  || 1.26 ||
pravṛttiḥ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ purāṇasyābhavattataḥ  |
kālenāgrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā purāṇasya tato muniḥ  || 1.27 ||
vyāsarūpaṃ vibhuḥ kṛtvā saṃharetsa yuge yuge  |
aṣṭalakṣapramāṇe tu dvāpare dvāpare sadā  || 1.28 ||
tadaṣṭādaśadhā kṛtvā bhūloke'smin prabhāṣyate  |
adyāpi devaloke tacchatakoṭipravistaram  || 1.29 ||
tathātra caturlakṣaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa niveśitam  |
purāṇāni daśāṣṭau ca sāmprataṃ tadihocyate  |
nāmatastāni vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvamṛṣisattama  || 1.30 ||
sargaśca pratisargaśca vaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca  |
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam  || 1.31 ||
brāhmaṃ purāṇaṃ tatrādyaṃ saṃhitāyāṃ vibhūṣitam  |
ślokānāṃ daśasāhasraṃ nānāpuṇyakathāyutam  || 1.32 ||
pādmaṃ ca pañcapañcāśatsahasrāṇi nigadyate  |
tṛtīyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃnāma trayoviṃśatisaṃkhyayā  || 1.33 ||
caturthaṃ vāyunā proktaṃ vāyavīyamiti smṛtam  |
śivabhaktisamāyogācchaivaṃ taccāparākhyayā  || 1.34 ||
caturviṃśatisaṃkhyātaṃ sahasrāṇi tu śaunaka  |
caturbhiḥ parvabhiḥ proktaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ pañcamaṃ tathā  || 1.35 ||
caturdaśasahasrāṇi tathā pañca śatāni tat |
mārkaṇḍaṃ navasāhasraṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tatparikīrtitam  || 1.36 ||
āgneyaṃ saptamaṃ proktaṃ sahasrāṇi tu ṣoḍaśa  |
aṣṭamaṃ nāradīyaṃ tu proktaṃ vai pañcaviṃśatiḥ  || 1.37 ||
navamaṃ bhagavannāma bhāgadvayavibhūṣitam  |
tadaṣṭādaśasāhasraṃ procyate granthasaṃkhyayā  || 1.38 ||
daśamaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ tāvatsaṃkhyamihocyate  |
laiṅgamekādaśaṃ jñeyaṃ tathaikādaśasaṃkhyayā  || 1.39 ||
bhāgadvayaṃ viracitaṃ talliṅgamṛṣipuṃgava  |
caturviṃśatisāhasraṃ vārāhaṃ dvādaśaṃ viduḥ  || 1.40 ||
vibhaktaṃ saptabhiḥ khaṇḍaiḥ skāndaṃ bhāgyavatāṃ vara  |
tadekāśītisāhasraṃ saṃkhyayā vai nirūpitam  || 1.41 ||
tatastu vāmanaṃ nāma caturdaśatamaṃ smṛtam  |
saṃkhyayā daśasāhasraṃ proktaṃ kulapate purā  || 1.42 ||
kaurmaṃ pañcadaśaṃ prāhurbhāgadvayavibhūṣitam  |
daśasaptasahasrāṇi purā sāṃkhyapate kalau  || 1.43 ||
mātsyaṃ matsyena yatproktaṃ manave ṣoḍaśaṃ kramāt |
taccaturdaśasāhasraṃ saṃkhyayā vadatāṃ vara  || 1.44 ||
gāruḍaṃ saptadaśamaṃ smṛtaṃ caikonaviṃśatiḥ  |
aṣṭādaśaṃ tu brahmāṇḍaṃ bhāgadvayavibhūṣitam  || 1.45 ||
tacca dvādaśasāhasraṃ śatamaṣṭasamanvitam  |
tathaivopapurāṇāni yāni coktāni vedhasā  || 1.46 ||
idaṃ brahmapurāṇasya sulabhaṃ sauramuttamam  |
saṃhitādvayasaṃyuktaṃ puṇyaṃ śivakathāśrayam  || 1.47 ||
ādyā sanatkumāroktā dvitīyā sūryabhāṣitā  |
sanatkumāranāmnā hi tadvikhyātaṃ mahāmune  || 1.48 ||
dvitīyaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ ca purāṇe pādmasaṃjñite  |
śaukeyaṃ hi tṛtīyaṃ tu purāṇe vaiṣṇave matam  || 1.49 ||
bārhaspatyaṃ caturthaṃ ca vāyavyaṃ saṃmataṃ sadā  |
daurvāsasaṃ pañcamaṃ ca smṛtaṃ bhāgavate sadā  || 1.50 ||
bhaviṣye nāradoktaṃ ca sūribhiḥ kathitaṃ purā  |
kāpilaṃ mānavaṃ caiva tathaivośanaseritam  || 1.51 ||
brahmāṇḍaṃ vāruṇaṃ cātha kālikādvayameva ca  |
māheśvaraṃ tathā sāmbaṃ sauraṃ sarvārthasaṃcayam  || 1.52 ||
pārāśaraṃ bhāgavataṃ kaurmaṃ cāṣṭādaśaṃ kramāt |
etānyupapurāṇāni mayoktāni yathākramam  || 1.53 ||
purāṇasaṃhitāmetāṃ yaḥ paṭhedvā śṛṇoti ca  |
so'nantapuṇyabhāgī syānmṛto brahmapuraṃ vrajet || 1.54 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe purāṇasaṃhitāvarṇanāṃ prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 2
sūta uvāca  || 2.1 ||
narmadāyāstu māhātmyaṃ kṛṣṇadvaipāyano'bravīt |
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi yattvayā paripṛcchitam  || 2.2 ||
vistaraṃ narmadāyāstu tīrthānāṃ munisattama  |
ko'nyaḥ śakto'sti vai vaktumṛte brahmāṇamīśvaram  || 2.3 ||
etameva purā praśnaṃ pṛṣṭavāñjanamejayaḥ  |
vaiśaṃpāyanasaṃjñaṃ tu śiṣyaṃ dvaipāyanasya ha  || 2.4 ||
revātīrthāśritaṃ puṇyaṃ tatte vakṣyāmi śaunaka  |
purā pārīkṣito rājā yajñādīkṣāsu dīkṣitaḥ  || 2.5 ||
saṃbhṛte tu havirdravye vartamāneṣu karmasu  |
āsīneṣu dvijāgryeṣu hūyamāne hutāśane  || 2.6 ||
vartamānāsu sarvatra tathā dharmakathāsu ca  |
śrūyamāṇe tathā śabde janairukte tvaharniśam  || 2.7 ||
yajñabhūmau kulapate dīyatāṃ bhujyatāmiti  |
vividhāṃśca vinodānvai kurvāṇeṣu vinodiṣu  || 2.8 ||
evaṃvidhe vartamāne yajñe svargasadaḥsame  |
vaiśaṃpāyanamāsīnaṃ papraccha janamejayaḥ  || 2.9 ||

janamejaya uvāca -
dvaipāyanaprasādena jñānavānasi me mataḥ  |
vaiśaṃpāyana tasmāttvāṃ pṛcchāmi ṛṣisannidhau  || 2.10 ||
brūhi me tvaṃ purāvṛttaṃ pitḥṇāṃ tīrthasevanam  |
ciraṃ nānāvidhānkleśān prāptāsta iti me śrutam  || 2.11 ||
kathaṃ dyūtajitāḥ pārthā mama pūrvapitāmahāḥ  |
āsamudrāṃ mahīṃ vipra bhramantastīrthalobhataḥ  || 2.12 ||
kena te sahitāstāta bhūmibhāgānanekaśaḥ  |
ceruḥ kathaya tatsarvaṃ sarvajño'si mato mama  || 2.13 ||
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca  || 2.14 ||
kathayiṣyāmi bhūnātha yatpṛṣṭaṃ tu tvayā'nagha  |
namaskṛtya virūpākṣaṃ vedavyāsaṃ mahākavim  || 2.15 ||
pitāmahāstu te pañca pāṇḍavāḥ saha kṛṣṇayā  |
uṣitvā brāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ kāmyake vana uttame  || 2.16 ||
pradhānoddālake tatra kaśyapo'tha mahāmatiḥ  |
vibhāṇḍakaśca rājendra muruścaiva mahāmuniḥ  || 2.17 ||
pulastyo lomaśaścaiva tathānye putrapautriṇaḥ  |
snātvā niḥśeṣatīrtheṣu gatāste vindhyaparvatam  || 2.18 ||
te ca tatrāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvairvṛkṣaiḥ samākulam  |
campakaiḥ karṇakāraiśca punnāgairnāgakesaraiḥ  || 2.19 ||
bakulaiḥ kovidāraiśca dāḍimairupaśobhitam  |
puṣpitairarjunaiścaiva bilvapāṭalaketakaiḥ  || 2.20 ||
kadambāmramadhūkaiśca nimbajambīratindukaiḥ  |
nālikeraiḥ kapitthaiśca kharjūrapanasaistathā  || 2.21 ||
nānādrumalatākīrṇaṃ nānāvallībhirāvṛtam  |
sapuṣpaṃ phalitaṃ kāntaṃ vanaṃ caitrarathaṃ yathā  || 2.22 ||
jalāśrayaistu vipulaiḥ padminīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitam  |
sitotpalaiśca saṃchannaṃ nīlapītaiḥ sitāruṇaiḥ  || 2.23 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ cakravākopaśobhitam  |
āḍīkākabalākābhiḥ sevitaṃ kokilādibhiḥ  || 2.24 ||
siṃhairvyāghrairvarāhaiśca gajaiścaiva mahotkaṭaiḥ  |
mahiṣaiśca mahākāyaiḥ kuraṅgaiścitrakaiḥ śaśaiḥ  || 2.25 ||
gaṇḍakaiścaiva khaḍgaiśca gomāyusurabhī yutam  |
sāraṅgairmallakaiścaiva dvipadaiśca catuṣpadaiḥ  || 2.26 ||
tathāca kokilākīrṇaṃ manaḥkāntaṃ suśobhitam  |
jīvaṃjīvakasaṃghaiśca nānāpakṣisamāyutam  || 2.27 ||
duḥkhaśokavinirmuktaṃ sattvotkaṭamanoramam  |
kṣuttṛṣārahitaṃ kāntaṃ sarvavyādhivivarjitam  || 2.28 ||
siṃhīstanaṃ pibantyatra kuraṃgāḥ snehasaṃyutam  |
mārjāramūṣakau cobhāvavalehata unmukhau  || 2.29 ||
pañcāsyāḥ potakebhāśca bhoginastu kalāpinaḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā tadvipinaṃ ramyaṃ praviṣṭāḥ pāṇḍunandanāḥ  || 2.30 ||
mārkaṇḍaṃ dṛṣṭavāṃstatra taruṇādityasannibham  |
ṛṣibhiḥ sevyamānaṃ tu nānāśāstraviśāradaiḥ  || 2.31 ||
kulīnaiḥ sattvasampannaiḥ śaucācārasamanvitaiḥ  |
dhīsaṃgataiḥ kṣamāyuktaistrisaṃdhyaṃ japatatparaiḥ  || 2.32 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmavihitairmantrairhomaparāyaṇaiḥ  |
kecitpañcāgnimadhyasthāḥ kecidekāntasaṃsthitāḥ  || 2.33 ||
ūrdhvabāhunirālambā ādityabhramaṇāḥ pare  |
sāyaṃprātarbhujaścānye ekāhārāstathā pare  || 2.34 ||
dvādaśāhāttathā cānye anye māsārdhabhojanāḥ  |
darśe darśe tathā cānye anye śaivālabhojanāḥ  || 2.35 ||
piṇyākamapare'bhujan kecitpālāśabhojanāḥ  |
apare niyatāhārā vāyubhakṣyāmbubhojanāḥ  || 2.36 ||
evaṃbhūtaistathā vṛddhaiḥ sevyate munipuṃgavaiḥ  |
tato dharmasutaḥ śrīmānāśramaṃ taṃ praviśya saḥ  || 2.37 ||
dṛṣṭvā munivaraṃ śāntaṃ dhyāyamānaṃ paraṃ padam  |
prādakṣiṇyena sahasā daṇḍavatpatito'grataḥ  || 2.38 ||
bhaktyānupatitaṃ dṛṣṭvā cirādādāya locanam  |
ko bhavānityuvācedaṃ dharmaṃ dhīmānapṛcchata  || 2.39 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dārakastatsamīpagaḥ  |
āhāyaṃ dharmarājaste darśanārthaṃ samāgataḥ  || 2.40 ||
tacchrutvādārakeṇoktaṃ vacanaṃ prāha sādaraḥ  |
ehyehi vatsavatseti kiṃcitsthānāccalanmuniḥ  |
taṃ tu snehādupāghrāya āsane upaveśayat || 2.41 ||
upaviṣṭe sabhāyāṃ tu pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
vanyairdhānyaiḥ phalairmūlai rasaiścaiva pṛthagvidhaiḥ  || 2.42 ||
pāṇḍavā brāhmaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ yathāyogyaṃ prapūjitāḥ  |
muhūrtādatha viśramya dharmaputro yudhiṣṭhiraḥ  || 2.43 ||
pṛcchati sma muniśreṣṭhaṃ kautūhalasamanvitaḥ  |
bhagavansarvalokānāṃ dīrghāyustvaṃ mato mama  || 2.44 ||
saptakalpānaśeṣeṇa kathayasva mamānagha  |
kalpakṣaye'pi lokasya sthāvarasyetarasya ca  || 2.45 ||
na vinaṣṭo'si viprendra kathaṃ vā kena hetunā  |
gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāntāśca yā mune  || 2.46 ||
tāsāṃ madhye sthitāḥ kāḥ svitkāścaiva pralayaṃ gatāḥ  |
kā nu puṇyajalā nityaṃ kānu na kṣayamāgatā  || 2.47 ||
etatkathaya me tāta prasannenāntarātmanā  |
śrotumicchāmyaśeṣeṇa ṛṣibhiḥ saha bāndhavaiḥ  || 2.48 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhusādhu mahāprājña dharmaputra yudhiṣṭhira  |
kathayāmi yathā nyāyaṃ yatpṛcchasi mamānagha  || 2.49 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ purāṇaṃ rudrabhāṣitam  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 2.50 ||
aśvamedha sahasreṇa vājapeyaśatena ca  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti rājannāstyatra saṃśayaḥ  || 2.51 ||
brahmaghnaśca surāpī ca steyī goghnaśca yo naraḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo rudrasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 2.52 ||
gaṅgā tu saritāṃ śreṣṭhā tathā caiva sarasvatī  |
kāverī devikā caiva sindhuḥ sālakuṭī tathā  || 2.53 ||
sarayūḥ śatarudrā ca mahī carmilayā saha  |
godāvarī tathā puṇyā tathaiva yamunā nadī  || 2.54 ||
payoṣṇī ca śatadruśca tathā dharmanadī śubhā  |
etāścānyāśca saritaḥ sarvapāpaharāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 2.55 ||
kiṃ tu te kāraṇaṃ tāta vakṣyāmi nṛpasattama  |
samudrāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ kalpe kalpe kṣayaṃ gatāḥ  || 2.56 ||
saptakalpakṣaye kṣīṇe na mṛtā tena narmadā  |
narmadaikaiva rājendra paraṃ tiṣṭhetsaridvarā  || 2.57 ||
toyapūrṇā mahābhāga munisaṃghairabhiṣṭutā  |
gaṃgādyāḥ saritaścānyāḥ kalpe kalpe kṣayaṃ gatāḥ  || 2.58 ||
eṣā devī purā dṛṣṭā tena vakṣyāmi te'nagha  || 2.59 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe revāmāhātmyavarṇanaṃnāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 3

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
saptakalpakṣayā ghorāstvayā dṛṣṭā mahāmune  |
na cāpīhāsti bhagavandīrghāyuriha kaścana  || 3.1 ||
tvayā hyekārṇave suptaḥ padmanābhaḥ surārihā  |
dṛṣṭaḥ sahasracaraṇaḥ sahasranayanodaraḥ  || 3.2 ||
tvaṃ kilānugrahāttasya dahyamāne carācare  |
na kṣayaṃ samanuprāpto varadānānmahātmanaḥ  || 3.3 ||
kiṃ tvayāścaryabhūtaṃ hi dṛṣṭaṃ ca bhramatānagha  |
etadācakṣva bhagavanparaṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 3.4 ||
samprāpte ca mahāghore yugasyānte mahākṣaye  |
anāvṛṣṭihate loke purā varṣaśatādhike  || 3.5 ||
auṣadhīnāṃ kṣaye ghore devadānavavarjite  |
nirvīrye nirvaṣaṭkāre kalinā dūṣite bhṛśam  || 3.6 ||
saritsarastaḍāgeṣu palvalopavaneṣu ca  |
saṃśuṣkeṣu tadā brahmannirākāre yugakṣaye  || 3.7 ||
janaṃ prāpte maharloke brahmakṣatraviśādayaḥ  |
ṛṣayaśca mahātmāno divyatejaḥsamanvitāḥ  || 3.8 ||
sthitāni kāni bhūtāni gatānyeva mahāmune  |
etatsarvaṃ mahābhāga kathayasva pṛthakpṛthak || 3.9 ||
bhūtāni kāni viprendra kathaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
brahmaviṣṇvindrarudrāṇāṃ kāle prāpte sudāruṇe  || 3.10 ||
evamuktastataḥ so'tha dharmarājena dhīmatā  |
mārkaṇḍaḥ pratyuvācedamṛṣisaṃghaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  || 3.11 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve tvayā saha nareśvara  |
mahatpurāṇaṃ pūrvoktaṃ śaṃbhunā vāyudaivate  || 3.12 ||
vāyoḥ sakāśātskandena śrutametatpurātanam  |
vasiṣṭhaḥ śrutavāṃstasmātparāśarastataḥ param  || 3.13 ||
tasmācca jātūkarṇyena tasmāccaiva maharṣibhiḥ  |
evaṃ paramparāproktaṃ śatasaṃkhyairdvijottamaiḥ  || 3.14 ||
saṃhitā śatasāhasrī puroktā śaṃbhunā kila  |
āloḍya sarvaśāstrāṇi vadārthaṃ tattvataḥ purā  || 3.15 ||
yugarūpeṇa sā paścāccaturdhā viniyojitā  |
madaprajñānusāreṇa narāṇāṃ tu maharṣibhiḥ  || 3.16 ||
ārādhya paśubhartāraṃ mayā pūrvaṃ maheśvaram  |
purāṇaṃ śrutametaddhi tatte vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 3.17 ||
yacchrutvā mucyate jantuḥ sarvapāpairnareśvara  |
mānasaiḥ karmajaiścaiva saptajanmasu saṃcitaiḥ  || 3.18 ||
saptakalpakṣayā ghorā mayā dṛṣṭāḥ punaḥpunaḥ  |
prasādāddevadevasya viṣṇośca parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 3.19 ||
dvādaśādityanirdagdhe jagatyekārṇavīkṛte  |
śrānto'haṃ vibhramaṃstatra taranbāhubhirarṇavam  || 3.20 ||
athāhaṃ salile rājannādityasamarūpiṇam  |
purā puruṣamadrākṣamanādinidhanaṃ prabhum  || 3.21 ||
śṛṅgaṃ caivādrirājasya bhāsayantaṃ diśo daśa  |
dvitīyo'nyo manurdṛṣṭaḥ putrapautrasamanvitaḥ  || 3.22 ||
agādhe bhramate so'pi tamobhūte mahārṇave  |
aviśramanmuhūrtaṃ tu cakrārūḍha iva bhraman  || 3.23 ||
athāhaṃ bhayādudvignastaranbāhubhirarṇavam  |
tatrastho'haṃ mahāmatsyamapaśyaṃ madasaṃyutam  || 3.24 ||
tato'bravītsa māṃ dṛṣṭvā ehyehīti ca bhārata  |
paraṃ pradhānaḥ sarveṣāṃ matsyarūpo maheśvaraḥ  || 3.25 ||
tato'haṃ tvarayā gatvā tanmukhe manujeśvara  |
suśrānto vigatajñānaḥ paraṃ nirvedamāgataḥ  || 3.26 ||
tato'drākṣaṃ samudrānte mahadāvartasaṃkulām  |
udyattaraṃgasalilāṃ phenapuñjāṭṭahāsinīm  || 3.27 ||
nadīṃ kāmagamāṃ puṇyāṃ jhaṣamīnasamākulām  |
nadyāstasyāstu madhyasthā pramadā kāmarūpiṇī  || 3.28 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmā mahatprakṣobhavāhinī  |
divyahāṭakacitrāṅgī kanakojjvalaśobhitā  || 3.29 ||
dvābhyāṃ saṃgṛhya jānubhyāṃ mahatpotaṃ vyavasthitā  |
tāṃ manuḥ pratyuvācedaṃ kā tvaṃ divyavarāṅgane  || 3.30 ||
tiṣṭhase kena kāryeṇa tvamatra surasundari  |
surāsuragaṇe naṣṭe bhramase līlayārṇave  || 3.31 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāḥ kṣayaṃ prāptā hyanekaśaḥ  |
tvamekā tu kathaṃ sādhvi tiṣṭhase kāraṇaṃ mahat |
śrotumicchāmyahaṃ devi kathayasva hyaśeṣataḥ  || 3.32 ||

abalovāca -
īśvarāṅgasamudbhūtā hyamṛtānāma viśrutā  |
saritpāpaharā puṇyā māmāśritya bhayaṃ kutaḥ  || 3.33 ||
sāhaṃ potamimaṃ tubhyaṃ gṛhītvā hyāgatā dvija  |
na hyasya potasya kṣayo yatra tiṣṭhati śaṃkaraḥ  || 3.34 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā vismayotphullalocanaḥ  |
manunā saha rājendra potārūḍho hyahaṃ tadā  || 3.35 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā praṇamya śirasā vibhum  |
vyāpinaṃ parameśānamastauṣamabhayapradam  || 3.36 ||
sadyojātāya devāya vāmadevāya vai namaḥ  |
bhave bhave namastubhyaṃ bhaktigamyāya te namaḥ  || 3.37 ||
bhūrbhuvāya namastubhyaṃ rāmajyeṣṭhāya vai namaḥ  |
namaste bhadrakālāya kalirūpāya vai namaḥ  || 3.38 ||
acintyāvyaktarūpāya mahādevāya dhāmane  |
vidmahe devadevāya tanno rudra namonamaḥ  || 3.39 ||
jagatsṛṣṭivināśānāṃ kāraṇāya namonamaḥ  |
evaṃ stuto mahādevaḥ pūrvaṃ sṛṣṭayā mayānagha  || 3.40 ||
prasanno māvadatpaścādvaraṃ varaya suvrata  || 3.41 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye mārkaṇḍeyadharmarājasaṃvāde kalpakṣaye mārkaṇḍeyakṛtapotārdhārohaṇavṛttāntavarṇanaṃnāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 4

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato'rṇavātsamuttīrya trikūṭaśikhare sthitam  |
mahākanakavarṇābhe nānāvarṇaśilācite  || 4.1 ||
mahāśṛṅge samāsīnaṃ rudrakoṭisamanvitam  |
mahādevaṃ mahātmānamīśānamajamavyayam  || 4.2 ||
sarvabhūtamayaṃ tāta manunā saha suvrata  |
bhūyo vavande caraṇau sarvadevanamaskṛtau  || 4.3 ||
tatkāle yugasāhasraṃ saha rudreṇa mānada  |
tasminnekārṇave ghore sthito'haṃ kurunaṃdana  || 4.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
etacchrutvā tu me tāta paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hṛdi  |
jātaṃ tatkathayasveti śṛṇvataḥ saha bāndhavaiḥ  || 4.5 ||
kā sā padmapalāśākṣī tamobhūte mahārṇave  |
yogivadbhramate nityaṃ rudrajāṃ svāṃ ca yābravīt || 4.6 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etameva mayā praśnaṃ purā pṛṣṭo manuḥ svayam  |
tadeva te'dya vakṣyāmi abalāyāḥ samudbhavam  || 4.7 ||
vyatītāyāṃ niśāyāṃ tu brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
tataḥ prabhāte vimale sṛjyamāneṣu jantuṣu  || 4.8 ||
manuṃ praṇamya śirasā pṛcchāmyetadyudhiṣṭhira  |
keyaṃ padmapalāśākṣī śyāmā caṃdranibhānanā  || 4.9 ||
ekārṇave bhramatyekā rudrajāsmīti vādinī  |
sāvitrī vedamātā ca hyathavā sā sarasvatī  || 4.10 ||
mandākinī saricchreṣṭhā lakṣmīrvā kimatho umā  |
kālarātrirbhavetsākṣātprakṛtirvā sukhocitā  || 4.11 ||
etadācakṣva bhagavankā sā hyamṛtasaṃbhavā  |
caratyekārṇave ghore pranaṣṭoragarākṣase  || 4.12 ||

manuruvāca -
śṛṇu vatsa yathānyāyamasyā vakṣyāmi saṃbhavam  |
yayā rudrasamudbhūtā yā ceyaṃ varavarṇinī  || 4.13 ||
purā śivaḥ śāntatanuścacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
hitārthaṃ sarvalokānāmumayā saha śaṃkaraḥ  || 4.14 ||
ṛkṣaśailaṃ samāruhya tapastepe sudāruṇam  |
adṛśyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvabhūtātmako vaśī  || 4.15 ||
tapatastasya devasya svedaḥ samabhavatkila  |
taṃ giriṃ plāvayāmāsa sa svedo rudrasaṃbhavaḥ  || 4.16 ||
tasmādāsītsamudbhūtā mahāpuṇyā saridvarā  |
yā sā tvayārṇave dṛṣṭā padmapatrāyatekṣaṇā  || 4.17 ||
strīrūpaṃ samavasthāya rudramārādhayatpurā  |
ādye kṛtayuge tasminsamānāmayutaṃ nṛpa  || 4.18 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādeva umayā saha śaṃkaraḥ  |
brūhi tvaṃ tu mahābhāge yatte manasi vartate  || 4.19 ||

sariduvāca -
pralaye samanuprāpte naṣṭe sthāvarajaṃgame  |
prasādāttava deveśa akṣayāhaṃ bhave prabho  || 4.20 ||
saritsu sāgareṣveva parvateṣu kṣayiṣvapi  |
tava prasādāddeveśa puṇyā kṣayyā bhave prabho  || 4.21 ||
pāpopapātakairyuktā mahāpātakino'pi ye  |
mucyante sarvapāpebhyo bhaktyā snātvā tu śaṃkara  || 4.22 ||
uttare jāhnavīdeśe mahāpātakanāśinī  |
bhavāmi dakṣiṇe mārge yadyevaṃ surapūjitā  || 4.23 ||
svargādāgamya gaṃgeti yathā khyātā kṣitau vibho  |
tathā dakṣiṇagaṅgeti bhaveyaṃ tridaśeśvara  || 4.24 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrtheṣu snātvā yallabhate phalam  |
tatphalaṃ labhate martyo bhaktyā snātvā maheśvara  || 4.25 ||
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ yadāste saṃcitaṃ kvacit |
māsamātreṇa taddeva kṣayaṃ yātvavagāhanāt || 4.26 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvavedeṣu sarvayajñeṣu śaṃkara  |
avagāhena tatsarvaṃ bhavatviti matirmama  || 4.27 ||
sarvadānopavāseṣu sarvatīrthāvagāhane  |
tatphalaṃ mama toyena jāyatāmiti śaṃkara  || 4.28 ||
mama tīre narā ye tu arcayanti maheśvaram  |
te gatāstava lokaṃ syuretadeva bhavecchiva  || 4.29 ||
mama kūle maheśāna umayā saha daivataiḥ  |
vasa nityaṃ jagannātha eṣa eva varo mama  || 4.30 ||
sukarmā vā vikarmā vā śānto dānto jitendriyaḥ  |
mṛto janturmama jale gacchatādamarāvatīm  || 4.31 ||
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātā mahāpātakanāśinī  |
bhavāmi devadeveśa prasanno yadi manyase  || 4.32 ||
etāṃścānyānvarāndivyānprārthito nṛpasattama  |
narmadayā tataḥ prāha prasanno vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 4.33 ||

śrīmaheśa uvāca -
evaṃ bhavatu kalyāṇi yattvayoktamanindite  |
nānyā varārhā lokeṣu muktvā tvāṃ kamalekṣaṇe  || 4.34 ||
yadaiva mama dehāttvaṃ samudbhūtā varānane  |
tadaiva sarvapāpānāṃ mocinī tvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 4.35 ||
kalpakṣayakare kāle kāle ghore viśeṣataḥ  |
uttaraṃ kūlamāśritya nivasanti ca ye narāḥ  || 4.36 ||
api kīṭapataṅgāśca vṛkṣagulmalatādayaḥ  |
ā dehapatanāddevi te'pi yāsyanti sadgatim  || 4.37 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ kūlamāśritya ye dvijā dharmavatsalāḥ  |
ā mṛtyornivasiṣyanti te gatāḥ pitṛmandire  || 4.38 ||
ahaṃ hi tava vākyena kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  |
tvattīre nivasiṣyāmi sadaiva hyumayā samam  || 4.39 ||
evaṃ devi mahādevi evameva na saṃśayaḥ  |
brahmendracandravaruṇaiḥ sādhyaiśca saha viṣṇunā  || 4.40 ||
uttare devi te kūle vasiṣyanti mamājñayā  |
dakṣiṇe pitṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ tathānye surasundari  || 4.41 ||
vasiṣyanti mayā sārddhameṣa te vara uttamaḥ  |
gaccha gaccha mahābhāge martyānpāpādvimocaya  || 4.42 ||
sahitā ṛṣisaṃghaiśca tathā siddhasurāsuraiḥ  |
evamuktā mahādeva umayā sahito vibhuḥ  || 4.43 ||
vandyamāno'tha manunā mayā cādarśanaṃ gataḥ  |
tena caiṣā mahāpuṇyā mahāpātakanāśinī  || 4.44 ||
kathitā pṛcchyate yā te mā te bhavatu vismayaḥ  |
eṣā gaṃgā mahāpuṇyā triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  || 4.45 ||
daśābhiḥ pañcabhiḥ srotaiḥ plāvayantī diśo daśa  |
śoṇo mahānadaścaiva narmadā surasā kṛtā  || 4.46 ||
mandākinī daśārṇā ca citrakūṭā tathaiva ca  |
tamasā vidiśā caiva karabhā yamunā tathā  || 4.47 ||
citrotpalā vipāśā ca rañjanā vāluvāhinī  |
ṛkṣapādaprasūtāstāḥ sarvā vai rudrasaṃbhavāḥ  || 4.48 ||
sarvapāpaharāḥ puṇyāḥ sarvamaṃgaladāḥ śivāḥ  |
ityetairnāmabhirdivyaiḥ stūyate vedapāragaiḥ  || 4.49 ||
purāṇajñairmahābhāgairājyapaiḥ somapaistathā  |
ityetatsarvamākhyātaṃ mahābhāgyaṃ narottama  || 4.50 ||
manunoktaṃ purā mahyamamṛtāyāḥ samudbhavam  |
puṇyaṃ pavitramatulaṃ rudrodgītamidaṃ śubham  || 4.51 ||
ye narāḥ kīrtayiṣyanti bhaktyā śṛṇvanti ye'pi ca  |
prātarutthāya nāmāni daśa pañca ca bhārata  || 4.52 ||
te narāḥ sakalaṃ puṇyaṃ labhiṣyantyavagāhajam  |
vimānenārkavarṇena ghaṇṭāśataninādinā  || 4.53 ||
tyaktvā mānuṣyakaṃ bhāvaṃ yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim  || 4.54 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāpañcadaśanāmavarṇanaṃ nāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 5

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryametadakhilaṃ kathitaṃ bho dvijottama  |
vismayaṃ paramāpannā ṛṣisaṃghā mayā saha  || 5.1 ||
aho bhagavatī puṇyā narmadeyamayonijā  |
rudradehādviniṣkrāntā mahāpāpakṣayaṃkarī  || 5.2 ||
saptakalpakṣaye prāpte tvayeyaṃ saha suvrata  |
na mṛtā ca mahābhāgā kimataḥ puṇyamuttamam  || 5.3 ||
ke te kalpāḥ samuddiṣṭāḥ sapta kalpakṣayaṃkarāḥ  |
na mṛtā cediyaṃ devī tvaṃ caiva ṛṣipuṃgava  || 5.4 ||
apakṣigaṇasaṃghāte jagatyekārṇavīkṛte  |
kīdṛgrūpaḥ samabhavanmahādevo yugakṣaye  || 5.5 ||
kathaṃ saṃharate viśvaṃ kathaṃ cāste mahārṇave  |
kathaṃ ca sṛjate viśvaṃ kathaṃ dhārayate prajāḥ  || 5.6 ||
kīdṛgrūpā bhaveddevī saridekārṇavīkṛte  |
kimarthaṃ narmadā proktā revatī ca kathaṃ smṛtā  || 5.7 ||
añjaneti kimarthaṃ vā kimarthaṃ suraseti ca  |
mandākinī kimarthaṃ ca śoṇaśceti kathaṃ bhavet || 5.8 ||
trikūṭeti kimarthaṃ vā kimarthaṃ vāluvāhinī  |
koṭikoṭyo hi tīrthānāṃ praviṣṭā yā mahārṇavam  || 5.9 ||
kiyatyaḥ saritāṃ koṭyo narmadāṃ samupāsate  |
yajñopavītairṛṣibhirdevatābhistathaiva ca  || 5.10 ||
vibhakteyaṃ kimarthaṃ ca śrūyate munisattama  |
vaiṣṇavīti purāṇajñaiḥ kimarthamiha cocyate  || 5.11 ||
keṣu sthāneṣu tīrtheṣu pūjanīyā saridvarā  |
tīrthāni ca pṛthagbrūhi yatra saṃnihito haraḥ  || 5.12 ||
yatpramāṇā ca sā devī yā rudreṇa vinirmitā  |
kīdṛśāni ca karmāṇi rudreṇa kathitāni te  || 5.13 ||
kathaṃ mlecchasamākīrṇo deśo'yaṃ dvijasattama  |
etadācakṣva māṃ brahmanmārkaṇḍeya mahāmate  || 5.14 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve tvaṃ ca tāta yudhiṣṭhira  |
purāṇaṃ narmadāyāṃ tu kathitaṃ ca triśūlinā  || 5.15 ||
vāyoḥ sakāśācca mayā tenāpi ca maheśvarāt |
aśakyatvānmanuṣyāṇāṃ saṃkṣiptamṛṣibhiḥ purā  || 5.16 ||
māyūraṃ prathamaṃ tāta kaurmyaṃ ca tadanantaram  |
puraṃ tathā kauśikaṃ ca mātsyaṃ dviradameva ca  || 5.17 ||
vārāhaṃ yanmayā dṛṣṭaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ param  |
nyagrodhākhyamataḥ cāsīdākāṅkṣaṃ punaruttamam  || 5.18 ||
padmaṃ ca tāmasaṃ caiva saṃvartodvartameva ca  |
mahāpralayamityāhuḥ purāṇe vedacintakāḥ  || 5.19 ||
etatsaṃkṣepataḥ sarvaṃ saṃkṣiptaṃ tairmahātmabhiḥ  |
vibhaktaṃ ca caturbhāgairbrahmādyaiśca maharṣibhiḥ  || 5.20 ||
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi purāṇārthaviśārada  |
sapta kalpā mahāghorā yairiyaṃ na mṛtā sarit || 5.21 ||
ā jaṅgamaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam  |
naṣṭacandrārkakiraṇamāsīdbhūtavivarjitam  || 5.22 ||
tamaso'to mahānāmnā puruṣaḥ sa jagadguruḥ  |
cacāra tasminnekākī vyaktāvyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 5.23 ||
sa cauṃkāramayo'tīto gāyatrīmasṛjaddvijaḥ  |
sa tayā sārddhamīśānaścikrīḍa puruṣo virāṭ || 5.24 ||
svadehādasṛjadviśvaṃ pañcabhūtātmasaṃjñitam  |
krīḍansamasṛjadviśvaṃ pañcabhūtātmasaṃjñitam  || 5.25 ||
krīḍan sṛjadvirāṭsaṃjñaḥ sabījaṃ ca hiraṇmayam  |
taccāṇḍamabhavaddivyaṃ dvādaśādityasannibham  || 5.26 ||
tadbhittvā puruṣo jajñe caturvaktraḥ pitāmahaḥ  |
so'sṛjadviśvamevaṃ tu sadevāsuramānuṣam  || 5.27 ||
satiryakpaśupakṣīkaṃ svedāṇḍajajarāyujam  |
etadaṇḍaṃ purāṇeṣu prathamaṃ parikīrtitam  || 5.28 ||
pūrvakalpe nṛpaśreṣṭha krīḍantyā parameṣṭhinā  |
umayā saha rudrasya krīḍataścārṇavīkṛtaḥ  || 5.29 ||
harṣājjajñe śubhā kanyā umāyāḥ svedasaṃbhavā  |
śarvasyoraḥsthalājjajñe umā kucavimardanāt || 5.30 ||
svedādvijajñe mahatī kanyā rājīvalocanā  |
dvitīyaḥ saṃbhavo yasyā rudradehādyudhiṣṭhira  || 5.31 ||
sā paribhramate lokān sadevāsuramānavān  |
trailokyonmādajananī rūpeṇa'pratimā tadā  || 5.32 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā devadaityendrā mohitā labhate katham  |
mṛgayanti sma tāṃ kanyāmitaścetaśca bhārata  || 5.33 ||
hāvabhāvavilāsaiśca mohayatyakhilaṃ jagat |
bhramate divyarūpā sā vidyutsaudāminī yathā  || 5.34 ||
meghamadhye sthitā bhābhiḥ sarvayoṣidanuttamā  |
tato rudraṃ surāḥ sarve daityāśca saha dānavaiḥ  || 5.35 ||
varayanti sma tāṃ kanyāṃ kāmenākulitā bhṛśam  |
tato'bravīnmahādevo devadānavayordvayoḥ  || 5.36 ||
balena tejasā caiva hyadhiko yo bhaviṣyati  |
sa imāṃ prāpsyate kanyāṃ nānyathā vai surottamāḥ  || 5.37 ||
tato devāsurāḥ sarve kanyāṃ vai samupāgaman  |
ahamenāṃ grahīṣyāmi ahamenāmiti bruvan  || 5.38 ||
paśyatāmeva sarveṣāṃ sā kanyāntaradhīyata  |
punastāṃ dadṛśuḥ sarve yojanāntaradhiṣṭhitām  || 5.39 ||
jagmuste tvaritāḥ sarve yatra sā samadṛśyata  |
tribhiścaturbhiśca tathā yojanairdaśabhiḥ punaḥ  || 5.40 ||
dhiṣṭhitāṃ samapaśyaṃste sarve mātaṃgagāminīm  |
yojanānāṃ śatairbhūyaḥ sahasraiścāpyadhiṣṭhitām  || 5.41 ||
tathā śatasahasreṇa laghutvātsamadṛśyata  |
agrataḥ pṛṣṭhataścaiva diśāsu vidiśāsu ca  || 5.42 ||
tāṃ paśyanti varārohāmekadhā bahudhā punaḥ  |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu bhrāmitāste tayā purā  || 5.43 ||
na cāvāptā tu sā kanyā mahādevāṅgasaṃbhavā  |
sahomayā tato devo jahāsoccaiḥ punaḥpunaḥ  || 5.44 ||
gaṇāstālakasaṃpātairnṛtyanti ca mudānvitāḥ  |
akasmāddṛśyate kanyā śaṃkarasya samīpagā  || 5.45 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayāpannā devā yānti parāṅmukhāḥ  |
tasyāścakre tato nāma svayameva pinākadhṛk || 5.46 ||
narma caibhyo dade yasmāttatkṛtaiśceṣṭitaiḥ pṛthak |
bhaviṣyasi varārohe saricchreṣṭhā tu narmadā  || 5.47 ||
svarūpamāsthito devaḥ prāpa hāsyaṃ yato bhuvi  |
narmadā tena cokteyaṃ suśītalajalā śivā  || 5.48 ||
saptakalpakṣaye jāte yaduktaṃ śaṃbhunā purā  |
na mṛtā tena rājendra narmadā khyātimāgatā  || 5.49 ||
tatastāmadadātkanyāṃ śīlavatīṃ suśobhanām  |
mahārṇavāya deveśaḥ sarvabhūtapatiḥ prabhuḥ  || 5.50 ||
tataḥ sā ṛkṣaśailendrātphenapuñjāṭṭahāsinī  |
viveśa narmadā devī samudraṃ saritāṃ patim  || 5.51 ||
evaṃ brāhme purā kalpe samudbhūteyamīśvarāt |
mātsye kalpe mayā dṛṣṭā samākhyātā mayā śṛṇu  || 5.52 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 6

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
punaryugānte samprāpte tṛtīye nṛpasattama  |
dādaśārkavapurbhūtvā bhagavānnīlalohitaḥ  || 6.1 ||
saptadvīpasamudrāntāṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
nirdagdhāṃ tu mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ kālo bhūtvā maheśvaraḥ  || 6.2 ||
tato mahāghano bhūtvā plāvayāmāsa vāriṇā  |
kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇavapustvenāṃ vidyuccandrāyudhāṅkitām  || 6.3 ||
plāvayitvā jagatsarvaṃ tasminnekārṇavīkṛte  |
suṣvāpa vimale toye jagatsaṃkṣipya māyayā  || 6.4 ||
tato'haṃ bhramamāstu tamobhūte mahārṇave  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu vāyubhūte maheśvare  || 6.5 ||
oṃkṛtvā devadeveśaṃ yenedaṃ gahanīkṛtam  |
dhyāyamānastato devaṃ rājendra vimale jale  || 6.6 ||
tasminmahārṇave ghore naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame  |
mayūraṃ svarṇapatrāḍhyamapaśyaṃsahasā jale  |
vicitracandrakopetaṃ nīlakaṃṭhaṃ sulocanam  || 6.7 ||
tato mayūraḥ sa mahārṇavānte vikṣobhayitvā hi mahāsveṇa  |
cacāra devastriśikhī śikhaṇḍī trailokyagoptā sa mahānubhāvaḥ  || 6.8 ||
śivaśca raudreṇa mayūrarūpiṇā vikṣobhyamāṇe salile'pi tasmin  |
saha bhramantīṃ ca mahārṇavānte sarinmahaughāṃ sumahāndadarśa  || 6.9 ||
sa tāṃ mahādevamayūrarūpo dṛṣṭvā bhramantīṃ sahasormijālaiḥ  |
kā tvaṃ śubhe śāśvatadehabhūtā kṣayaṃ na yātāsi mahākṣayānte  || 6.10 ||
devāsuragaṇe naṣṭe saritsaramahārṇave  |
kā tvaṃ bhramasi padmākṣi kva gatāsi ca na kṣayam  || 6.11 ||

narmadovāca -
tava prasādāddeveśa mṛtyurmama na vidyate  |
sṛja deva punarviśvaṃ śarvarī kṣayamāgatā  || 6.12 ||
evamukto mahādevo vyadhunotpakṣapañjaram  |
tāvatpañjaramadhyānte tasya pakṣādviniḥsṛtāḥ  || 6.13 ||
tāvanto devadaityendrāḥ pakṣābhyāṃ tasya jajñire  |
teṣāṃ madhye punaḥ sā tu narmadā bhramate sarit || 6.14 ||
tataścānyo mahāśailo dṛśyate bharatarṣabha  |
tribhiḥ kūṭaiḥ suvistīrṇaiḥ śṛṅgavāniva govṛṣaḥ  || 6.15 ||
trikūṭastu iti khyātaḥ sarvaratnairvibhūṣitaḥ  |
tatastasmāttrikūṭācca plāvayantī mahīṃ yayau  || 6.16 ||
trikūṭī tena vikhyātā pitḥṇāṃ trāyaṇī parā  |
dvitīyācca tato gaṅgā vistīrṇā dharaṇītale  || 6.17 ||
tṛtīyaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṅgaṃ saptadhā khaṇḍaśo gatam  |
jambūdvīpe tu saṃjātāḥ sapta te kulaparvatāḥ  || 6.18 ||
candranakṣatrasahitā grahagrāmanadīnadāḥ  |
aṇḍajaṃ svedajaṃ jātamudbhijjaṃ ca jarāyujam  || 6.19 ||
evaṃ jagadidaṃ sarvaṃ mayūrādabhavatpurā  |
samastaṃ naraśārdūla mahādevasamudbhavam  || 6.20 ||
tato nadīḥ samudrāṃśca saṃvibhajya pṛthakpṛthak |
narmadāmāha deveśo gaccha tvaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśam  || 6.21 ||
evaṃ sā dakṣiṇā gaṃgā mahāpātakanāśinī  |
uttare jāhnavī deśe puṇyā tvaṃ dakṣiṇe śubhā  || 6.22 ||
yathā gaṃgā mahāpuṇyā mama mastakasaṃbhavā  |
tadviśiṣṭā mahābhāge tvaṃ caiveti na saṃśayaḥ  || 6.23 ||
tvayā saha bhaviṣyāmi ekenāṃśena suvrate  |
mahāpātakayuktānāmauṣadhaṃ tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 6.24 ||
evamuktā tu devena mahāpātakanāśinī  |
dakṣiṇaṃ digvibhāgaṃ tu sā jagāmāśu vikramā  || 6.25 ||
ṛkṣaśailendramāsādya candramauleranugrahāt |
vāryaughaiḥ prasthitā yasmānmahādevapraṇoditā  || 6.26 ||
mahatā cāpi vegena yasmādeṣā samucchritā  |
mahatī tena sā proktā mahādevānmahīpate  || 6.27 ||
tapatastasya devasya śūlāgrādbindavo'patan  |
tenaiṣā śoṇasaṃjñā tu daśa sapta ca tāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 6.28 ||
sarveṣāṃ narmadā puṇyā rudradehādviniḥsṛtā  |
sarvābhyaśca saridbhyaśca varadānānmahātmanaḥ  || 6.29 ||
śaṃkarānuprahāddevī mahāpātakanāśinī  |
yasmānmahārṇave ghore dṛśyate mahatī ca sā  || 6.30 ||
suvyaktāṅgī mahākāyā mahatī tena sā smṛtā  |
tasmādvikṣobhyamāṇā hi diggajairambudopamaiḥ  || 6.31 ||
kaluṣatvaṃ nayatyeva rasena surasā tathā  |
kṛpāṃ karoti sā yasmāllokānāmabhayapradā  || 6.32 ||
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ tena caiṣā kṛpā smṛtā  |
purā kṛtayuge puṇye divyamandārabhūṣitā  || 6.33 ||
kalpavṛkṣasamākīrṇā rohītakasamākulā  |
vahatyeṣā ca mandena tena mandākinī smṛtā  || 6.34 ||
bhittvā mahārṇavaṃ kṣipraṃ yasmāllokamihāgatā  |
pūjyā suraiśca siddhaiśca tasmādeṣā mahārṇavā  || 6.35 ||
vicitrotpalasaṃghātairṛkṣadvipasamākulā  || 6.36 ||
bhittvā śailaṃ ca vipulaṃ prayātyevaṃ mahārṇavam  |
bhrāmayantī diśaḥ sarvā raveṇa mahatā purā  || 6.37 ||
plāvayantī virājantī tena revā iti smṛtā  |
bhāryāputrasuduḥkhāḍhyānnarāñchāpaiḥ samāvṛtān  || 6.38 ||
vipāpānkurute yasmādvipāpā tena sā smṛtā  |
viṇmūtranicayāṃ ghorāṃ pāṃśuśoṇitakardamām  || 6.39 ||
pāśairnityaṃ tu sambādhāṃ yasmānmocayate bhṛśam  |
vipāśeti ca sā proktā saṃsārārṇavatāriṇī  || 6.40 ||
narmadā vimalāmbhā ca vimalenduśubhānanā  |
tamobhūte mahāghore yasmādeṣā mahāprabhā  || 6.41 ||
vimalā tena sā proktā vidvadbhirnṛpasattama  |
karairindukaraprakhyaiḥ sūryaraśmisamaprabhā  || 6.42 ||
kṣarantī modate viśvaṃ karabhā tena cocyate  |
yasmādrañjayate lokāndarśanādeva bhārata  || 6.43 ||
rañjanādrañjanā proktā dhātvarthe rājasattama  |
tṛṇavīrudhagulmādyāstiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇastathā  |
tānudbhūtānnayetsvargaṃ tenoktā vāyuvāhinī  || 6.44 ||
evaṃ yo vetti nāmāni nirgamaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
sa yāti pāpavirmukto rudralokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 6.45 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye sahaitukarevānāmamāhātmyavarṇane mayūrakalpasamudbhavo nāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 7

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
punarekārṇave ghore naṣṭe sthāvarajaṃgame  |
salilenāplute loke nirāloke tamodbhave  || 7.1 ||
brahmaiko vicaraṃstatra tamībhūte mahārṇave  |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu khadyota iva rūpavān  || 7.2 ||
śete yojanasāhasramaprameyamanuttamam  |
dvādaśādityasaṃkāśaṃ sahasracaraṇekṣaṇam  || 7.3 ||
prasuptaṃ cārṇave ghore hyapaśyatkūrmarūpiṇam  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayāpanno brahmā bodhayate śanaiḥ  || 7.4 ||
stutibhirmaṃgalaiścaiva vedavedāṃgasaṃbhavaiḥ  |
vācaspate vibudhyasva mahābhūta namo'stu te  || 7.5 ||
tavodare jagatsarvaṃ tiṣṭhate parameśvara  |
tadvimuñca mahāsattva yatpūrvaṃ saṃhṛtaṃ tvayā  || 7.6 ||
vyatītā rajanī brāhmī dinaṃ samanuvartate  |
nirīkṣya sarvalokeśa yena saṃbhavate jagat || 7.7 ||
sa niśamya vacastasya utthitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
samudgiran sa lokāṃstrīn grastān kalpakṣaye tadā  || 7.8 ||
devadānavagandharvāḥ sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ  |
sacandrārkagrahāḥ sarve śarīrāttasya nirgatāḥ  || 7.9 ||
tato hyekārṇavaṃ sarvaṃ vibhajya parameśvaraḥ  |
vistīrṇopalatoyaughāṃ saritsaravivardhitām  || 7.10 ||
paśyate medinīṃ devaḥ savṛkṣauṣadhipalvalām  |
himavantaṃ giriśreṣṭhaṃ śvetaṃ parvatamuttamam  || 7.11 ||
śṛṅgavantaṃ mahāśailaṃ ye cānye kulaparvatāḥ  |
jaṃbudvīpaṃ kuśaṃ krauñcaṃ sagomedaṃ saśālmalam  || 7.12 ||
puṣkarāntāśca ye dvīpā ye ca saptamahārṇavāḥ  |
lokālokaṃ mahāśailaṃ sarvaṃ ca purataḥ sthitam  || 7.13 ||
catuḥprakṛtisaṃyuktaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  |
yugānte tu viniṣkrāntamapaśyatsa maheśvaraḥ  || 7.14 ||
viprakīrṇaśilājālāmapaśyatsa vasuṃdharām  |
kūrmapṛṣṭhopagāṃ devīṃ mahārṇavagatāṃ prabhuḥ  || 7.15 ||
tasmin viśīrṇaśailāgre saritsarovivarjite  |
nānātaraṃgabhinnoda āvartodvartasaṃkule  || 7.16 ||
nānauṣadhiprajvalite nānotpalaśilātale  |
nānāvihaṃgasaṃghuṣṭāṃ matsyakūrmasamākulām  || 7.17 ||
divyamāyāmayīṃ devīmutkṛṣṭāmbudasannibhām  |
nadīmapaśyaddeveśo hyanaupamyajalāśayām  || 7.18 ||
madhye tasyāmbudaśyāmāṃ pīnorujaghanastanīm  |
vastrairanupamairdivyairnānābharaṇabhūṣitām  || 7.19 ||
sanūpuraravoddāmāṃ hārakeyūramaṇḍitām  |
tādṛśīṃ narmadāṃ devīṃ svayaṃ strīrūpadhāriṇīm  || 7.20 ||
yogamāyāmayaiścitrairbhūṣaṇaiḥ svairvibhūṣitām  |
avyaktāṅgīṃ mahābhāgāmapaśyatsa tu narmadām  || 7.21 ||
ardhodyatabhujāṃ bālāṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇām  |
stuvantīṃ devadeveśamutthitāṃ tu jalāttadā  || 7.22 ||
vismayāviṣṭahṛdayo hyahamudvīkṣya tāṃ śubhām  |
snātvā jale śubhe tasyāḥ stotumabhyudyatastataḥ  || 7.23 ||
arcayāmāsa saṃhṛṣṭo mantrairvedāṃgasaṃbhavaiḥ  |
sṛṣṭaṃ ca tatpurā rājanpaśyeyaṃ sacarācaram  || 7.24 ||
sadevāsuragandharvaṃ sapannagamahoragam  |
paśyāmyeṣā mahābhāgā naiva yātā kṣayaṃ purā  || 7.25 ||
mahādevaprasādācca taccharīrasamudbhavā  |
bhūyo bhūyo mayā dṛṣṭā kathitā te nṛpottama  || 7.26 ||
prādurbhāvamimaṃ kaurmyaṃ ye'dhīyante dvijottamāḥ  |
ye'pi śṛṇvanti vidvāṃso mucyante te'pi kilbiṣaiḥ  || 7.27 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye kūrmakalpasamudbhavo nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 8

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
naṣṭe loke punaścānye salilena samāvṛte  |
mahārṇavasya madhyastho bāhubhyāmataraṃ jalam  || 8.1 ||
divye varṣaśate pūrṇe śrānto'haṃ nṛpasattama  |
dhyātuṃ samārabhaṃ devaṃ mahadarṇavatāraṇam  || 8.2 ||
dhyāyamānastataḥ kāle apaśyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ param  |
hārakundendusaṃkāśaṃ bakaṃ gokṣīrapāṇḍuram  || 8.3 ||
tato'haṃ vismayāviṣṭastaṃ bakaṃ samudīkṣya vai  |
asminmahārṇave ghore kuto'yaṃ pakṣisaṃbhavaḥ  || 8.4 ||
taranbāhubhiraśrāntastaṃ bakaṃ pratyabhāṣiṣi  |
pākṣarūpaṃ samāsthāya kastvamekārṇavīkṛte  || 8.5 ||
bhramase divyayogātmanmohayanniva māṃ prabho  |
etatkathaya me sarvaṃ yo'si so'si namo'stu te  || 8.6 ||
so'bravīnmāṃ mahādevo brahmāhaṃ viṣṇureva ca  |
jagatsarvaṃ mayā vatsa saṃhṛtaṃ kiṃ na budhyase  || 8.7 ||
tava mātā pitāhaṃ vai viśvasya ca mahāmune  |
kāruṇyaṃ mama saṃjātaṃ dṛṣṭvā magnaṃ mahārṇave  || 8.8 ||
pakṣirūpaṃ samāsthāya ato'trāhaṃ samāgataḥ  |
kimarthamāturo bhūtvā bhramasītthaṃ mahārṇave  || 8.9 ||
śīghraṃ praviśa matpakṣau yena viśramase dvija  |
evamuktastatastena devenāhaṃ nareśvara  || 8.10 ||
tato'haṃ tasya pakṣānte pralīnastu bhramañjale  |
kāle yugasāhasrānte aśrānto'rṇavamadhyagaḥ  || 8.11 ||
tataḥ śṛṇomi sahasā dikṣu sarvāsu suvrata  |
kiṃcinnūpurasaṃmiśramadbhutaṃ śabdamuttamam  || 8.12 ||
tadārṇavajalaṃ sarvaṃ saṃkṣiptaṃ sahasābhavat |
kimetaditi saṃcintya diśaḥ samavalokayam  || 8.13 ||
daśa kanyāstato dikṣu āgatāśca mahārṇave  |
vastrālaṃkārasahitā digbhyo nūpurabhūṣitāḥ  || 8.14 ||
kāciccandrasamābhāsā kācidādityasaprabhā  |
kācidaṃjanapuñjābhā kācidraktotpalaprabhā  || 8.15 ||
nānārūpadharā saumyā nānābharaṇabhūṣitā  |
arghyapādyādibhirmālyairbakamabhyarcya suvratāḥ  || 8.16 ||
tatastaṃ parvatākāraṃ guhyaṃ pakṣiṇamavyayam  |
praviveśa mahāghoraṃ parvato hyarṇavaṃ svarāṭ || 8.17 ||
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi tāvantyeva śatāni ca  |
triṃśadyojanasāhasraṃ yāvadbhūmaṇḍalaṃ tviti  || 8.18 ||
tato bhūmaṇḍalaṃ divyaṃ pañcaratnasamākulam  |
divyasphaṭikasopānaṃ rukmastaṃbhamanoramam  || 8.19 ||
yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ tu vistarāddviguṇāyatam  |
vāpīkūpasamākīrṇaṃ prāsādāṭṭālakāvṛtam  || 8.20 ||
kalpavṛkṣasamākīrṇaṃ dhvajaṣaṣṭivibhūṣitam  |
tasminpuravare ramye nānāratnopaśobhitam  || 8.21 ||
tathānyacca puraṃ ramyaṃ patākojjvalavedikam  |
śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ tāvaddviguṇamāyatam  || 8.22 ||
puramadhye tatastasminnadī paramaśobhanā  |
mahatī puṇyasalilā nānāratnaśilā tathā  || 8.23 ||
tasyāstīre mayā dṛṣṭaṃ taḍitsūryasamaprabham  |
indranīlamahānīlaiścitaṃ ratnaiḥ samantataḥ  || 8.24 ||
kvacidvahnisamākāraṃ kvacidindrāyudhaprabham  |
kvaciddhūmraṃ kvacitpītaṃ kvacidraktaṃ kvacitsitam  || 8.25 ||
nānāvarṇaiḥ samāyuktaṃ liṅgamadbhutadarśanam  |
brahmaviṣṇvindrasādhyaiśca samantātparivāritam  || 8.26 ||
nandīśvaragaṇādhyakṣaiścendrādityaiśca tadvṛtam  |
paśyāmi liṅgamīśānaṃ mahāliṅgaṃ tameva ca  || 8.27 ||
parivārya tatastaṃ tu prasuptāndevadānavān  |
nimīlitākṣānpaśyāmi divyābharaṇabhūṣitān  || 8.28 ||
tatastāḥ padmapatrākṣyo nāryaḥ paramasaṃmatāḥ  |
nadyāstasyā jale snātvā divyapuṣpairmanoramaiḥ  || 8.29 ||
dattvārghapādyaṃ vidhivalliṃgasya saha pakṣiṇā  |
arcayantīrvarārohā daśa tāḥ pramadottamāḥ  || 8.30 ||
tatastvabhyarcya talliṅgaṃ tasminneva purottame  |
sarvā adarśanaṃ jagmurvidyuto'bhragaṇeṣviva  || 8.31 ||
na cāsau pakṣirāṭtasminna striyo na ca devatāḥ  |
tadevaikaṃ sthitaṃ liṅgamarcayanvismayānvitaḥ  || 8.32 ||
tato'haṃ duḥkhamūḍhātmā rudramāyeti cintayan  |
tataḥ kanyāḥ samuttīrya divyāṃbaravibhūṣaṇāḥ  || 8.33 ||
bhāsayantyo jagatsarvaṃ vidyuto'bhragaṇāniva  |
padmairhiraṇmayairdivyairarcayitvā śubhānanāḥ  || 8.34 ||
viviśustajjalaṃ kṣipraṃ samaṃtādvarabhūṣaṇāḥ  |
tasminpuravare cānye tāmevāhaṃ punaḥpunaḥ  || 8.35 ||
paśyāmi hyamarāṃ kanyāmarcayantīṃ maheśvaram  |
tato'haṃ tāṃ varārohāmapṛcchaṃ kamalekṣaṇām  || 8.36 ||
kā tvamasminpure devi vasase śivamarcatī  |
tāścāgatāḥ striyaḥ sarvāḥ kva gatāste gaṇeśvarāḥ  || 8.37 ||
namo'stu te mahābhāge brūhi puṇye maheśvari  |
tava prasādādvijñātumetadicchāmi suvrate  |
dayāṃ kṛtvā mahādevi kathayasva mamānaghe  || 8.38 ||

śryuvāca -
vismṛtāhaṃ kathaṃ vipra dṛṣṭvā kalpe purātane  |
mā te'bhūtsmṛtivibhraṃśaḥ sā cāhaṃ kalpavāhinī  || 8.39 ||
narmadā nāma vikhyātā rudradehādviniḥsṛtā  |
yāstāḥ kanyāstvayā dṛṣṭā hyarcayantyo maheśvaram  || 8.40 ||
yābhistviha samānītaḥ pakṣirājasamanvitāḥ  |
diśastā viddhi sarveśāḥ sarvāstvaṃ munisattama  || 8.41 ||
tiryakpakṣisvarūpeṇa mahāyogī maheśvaraḥ  |
ebhiḥ śivapurādvipra ānītaḥ sa maheśvaraḥ  || 8.42 ||
saiṣa devo mahādevo liṅgamūrtirvyavasthitaḥ  |
arcyate brahmaviṣṇvindraiḥ surāsurajagadguruḥ  || 8.43 ||
layamāyāti yasmāddhi jagatsarvaṃ carācaram  |
tena liṅgamiti proktaṃ purāṇajñairmaharṣibhiḥ  || 8.44 ||
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve saṃkṣiptā māyayā purā  |
pralīnāścaiva lokeśa na dṛśyante hi sāṃpratam  || 8.45 ||
punardṛśyā bhaviṣyanti sṛjamānāḥ svayaṃbhuvā  |
sāhaṃ liṅgārcanaparā narmadā nāma nāmataḥ  || 8.46 ||
kālaṃ yugasahasrasya rudrasya paricārikā  |
asya prasādādamarastathā tvaṃ dvijapuṃgava  || 8.47 ||
satyārjavadayāyuktaḥ siddho'si tvaṃ śivārcanāt |
evamuktvā tu sā devī tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 8.48 ||
tāḥ striyaḥ sa ca deveśo bakarūpo maheśvaraḥ  |
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā avatīrya mahānadīm  || 8.49 ||
snātvā samarcaya tvaṃ hi vidhinā mantrapūrvakam  |
tato'haṃ sahasā tasmātsamuttīrya jalāśayāt || 8.50 ||
na ca paśyāmi talliṅgaṃ na ca tāṃ nimnagāṃ nṛpa  |
tadaiva lokāḥ saṃjātāḥ kṣitiścaiva sakānanā  || 8.51 ||
ṛkṣacandrārkavitataṃ tadeva ca nabhastalam  |
yathāpūrvamadṛṣṭaṃ tu tathaiva ca punaḥ kṛtam  |
nato'haṃ manasā devamapūjayaṃ maheśvaram  || 8.52 ||
evaṃ bake purā kalpe mayā dṛṣṭeyamavyayā  |
narmadā martyalokasya mahāpātakanāśinī  || 8.53 ||
tasmāddharmaparairvipraiḥ kṣatraśūdraviśādibhiḥ  |
sadā sevyā mahābhāgā dharmavṛddhyarthakāribhiḥ  || 8.54 ||
ye'pi bhaktayā sakṛttoye narmadāyā maheśvaram  |
snātvā te sarvaṃ pāpaṃ nāśayantyasaṃśayam  || 8.55 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye bakakalpasamudbhavo nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 9

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
punaryugāntaṃ te cānyaṃ sampravakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu  |
sūryairādīpite loke jaṅgame sthāvare purā  || 9.1 ||
saritsaraḥsamudreṣu kṣayaṃ yāteṣu sarvaśaḥ  |
nirmānuṣavaṣaṭkāre hyamaryādagatiṃ gate  || 9.2 ||
nānārūpaistato medhaiḥ śakrāyudhavirājitaiḥ  |
sarvamāpūritaṃ vyoma vāryaughaiḥ pūrite tadā  || 9.3 ||
tatastvekārṇavībhūte sarvataḥ salilāvṛte  |
jagatkṛtvodare sarvaṃ suṣvāpa bhagavānharaḥ  || 9.4 ||
prakṛtiṃ svāmavaṣṭabhya yogātmā sa prajāpatiḥ  |
śete yugasahasrāntaṃ kālamāviśya sārṇavam  || 9.5 ||
tatra suptaṃ mahātmānaṃ brahmalokanivāsinaḥ  |
bhṛgvādiṛṣayaḥ sarve ye cānye sanakādayaḥ  || 9.6 ||
paryaṅke vimale śubhre nānāstaraṇasaṃstṛte  |
śayānaṃ dadṛśurdevaṃ sapatnīkaṃ vṛṣadhvajam  || 9.7 ||
viśvarūpā tu sā nārī viśvarūpo maheśvaraḥ  |
gāḍhamāliṅgya suptastāṃ dadṛśe cāhamavyayam  || 9.8 ||
pādamūle tatastasya śyāmāṃ tāṃ padmalakṣaṇām  |
kanyāṃ paśyāmi suśroṇīṃ caraṇau tasya mṛdgatīm  || 9.9 ||
vimalāmbarasaṃvītāṃ vyālayajñopavītinīm  |
śyāmāṃ kamalapatrākṣīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  || 9.10 ||
sakalaṃ yugasāhasraṃ narmadeyaṃ vijānatī  |
prasuptaṃ devadeveśamupāste varavarṇinī  || 9.11 ||
hṛtairvedaiścaturbhiśca brahmāpyevaṃ maheśvaraḥ  |
bhṛgvādyairmānasaiḥ putraiḥ stauti śaṅkaramavyayam  || 9.12 ||
bhaktyā paramayā rājaṃstatra śambhumanāmayam  |
stuvantastatra deveśaṃ mantrairīśvarasambhavaiḥ  || 9.13 ||
prasuptaṃ devamīśānaṃ bodhayansamupasthitaḥ  |
uttiṣṭha hara piṅgākṣa mahādeva maheśvara  || 9.14 ||
mama vedā hṛtāḥ sarve ato'haṃ stotumudyataḥ  |
vedairvyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ divyādivyaṃ carācaram  || 9.15 ||
atītaṃ vartamānaṃ ca smarāmi ca sṛjāmyaham  |
tairvinā cāhamekastu mūko'dho jaḍavatsadā  || 9.16 ||
gatirvīryaṃ balotsāhau tairvinā na prajāyate  |
tairvinā devadeveśa nāhaṃ kiṃcitsmarāmi vai  || 9.17 ||
tānvedāndevadeveśa śīghraṃ me dātumarhasi  |
jaḍāndhabadhiraṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam  || 9.18 ||
sthānādi daśa catvāri na śobhante sureśvara  |
praṇamāmyalpavīryatvādvedahīnaḥ sureśvara  || 9.19 ||
vedebhyaḥ sakalaṃ jātaṃ yatkiṃcitsacarācaram  |
tāvacchobhanti śāstrāṇi samastāni jagadguro  || 9.20 ||
yāvadvedanidhirayaṃ nopatiṣṭhetsanātanaḥ  |
yathoditena sūryeṇa tamo yāti vināśatām  || 9.21 ||
evaṃ samastapāpāni yānti vedasya dhāraṇāt |
vede rahasi yatsūkṣmaṃ yattadbrahma sanātanam  || 9.22 ||
hṛdisthaṃ deva jānāmi gataṃ tadvedagarjanāt |
vedānuccarato me'dya tava śaṅkara cāgrataḥ  || 9.23 ||
akasmātte gatā vedā na sṛjeyaṃ vibho bhuvam  |
te'pi sarve mahādeva praviṣṭāḥ sammukhārṇavam  || 9.24 ||
te yācyamānā deveśa tiṣṭhantu smaraṇe mama  |
duhiteyaṃ viśālākṣī sarvaḥ sarvaṃ vijānate  || 9.25 ||
jāyatī yugasāhasraṃ nānyā kācidbhavedṛśī  |
ṛṣiścāyaṃ mahābhāgo mārkaṇḍo dhīmatāṃ varaḥ  || 9.26 ||
kalpe kalpe mahādeva tvāmayaṃ paryupāsate  |
jagattrayahitārthāya carate vratamuttamam  || 9.27 ||
evamuktastu deveśo brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā  |
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā narmadāṃ saritāṃ varām  || 9.28 ||
kathayasva mahābhāge brahmaṇastvaṃ tu pṛcchataḥ  |
kena vedā hṛtāḥ sarve vedhaso jagatīguroḥ  || 9.29 ||
evamuktā tu rudreṇa uvāca mṛgalocanā  |
brahmaṇo japato vedāṃstvayi supte maheśvara  || 9.30 ||
bhavataśchidramāsādya ghore'sminsalilāvṛte  |
pūrvakalpasamudbhūtāvasurau suradurjayau  || 9.31 ||
śriyāvṛttau mahādeva tvayā cotpāditau purā  |
surāsurasudurjeyau dānavau madhukaiṭabhau  || 9.32 ||
tau vāyubhūtau sūkṣmau ca paṭhato'smātpitāmahāt |
tāvāśu hṛtvā vedāṃśca praviṣṭau ca mahārṇavam  || 9.33 ||
etacchrutvā mahātejā hyamṛtāyāstato vacaḥ  |
sasmāra sa ca deveśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 9.34 ||
sa viveśa mahārāja bhūtalaṃ sasurottamaḥ  |
dānavāntakaro devaḥ sarvadaivatapūjitaḥ  || 9.35 ||
mīnarūpadharo devo loḍayāmāsa cārṇam  |
vedāṃśca dadṛśe tatra pātāle nihitānprabhuḥ  || 9.36 ||
tau ca daityau mahāvīryau dṛṣṭavānmadhusūdanaḥ  |
mahāvegau mahābāhū sūdayāmāsa tejasā  || 9.37 ||
vedāṃstatrāpi toyasthānānināya jagadguruḥ  |
caturvaktrāya devāyādadāccakravibhūṣitaḥ  || 9.38 ||
tataḥ prahṛṣṭo bhagavān vedāṃllabdhvā pitāmahaḥ  |
janayāmāsa nikhilaṃ jagadbhūyaścarācaram  || 9.39 ||
sā ca devī nadī puṇyā rudrasya paricārikā  |
pāvanī sarvabhūtānāṃ provāha salilaṃ tadā  || 9.40 ||
tasyāstīre tato devā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
yajanti tryambakaṃ devaṃ prahṛṣṭenāntarātmanā  || 9.41 ||
ekā mūrtirmaheśasya kāraṇāntaragatā  |
traiguṇyā kurute karma brahmacakrīśarūpataḥ  || 9.42 ||
eteṣāṃ tu pṛthagbhāvaṃ ye kurvanti sumohitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ dharmaḥ kutaḥ siddhirjāyate pāpakarmiṇām  || 9.43 ||
evametā mahānadyastisro rudrasamudbhavāḥ  |
ekā eva tridhā bhūtā gaṅgā revā sarasvatī  || 9.44 ||
gaṅgā tu vaiṣṇavī mūrtiḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśinī  |
rudradehasamudbhūtā narmadā caivameva tu  || 9.45 ||
brāhmī sarasvatī mūrtistriṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  |
divyā kāmagamā devī vāgvibhūtyai tu saṃsthitā  || 9.46 ||
narmadā paramā kācinmartyamūrtikalā śivā  |
divyā kāmagamā devī sarvatra surapūjitā  || 9.47 ||
vyāpinī sarvabhūtānāṃ sūkṣmātsūkṣmatarā smṛtā  |
akṣayā hyamṛtā hyeṣā svargasopānamuttamā  || 9.48 ||
sṛṣṭā rudreṇa lokānāṃ saṃsārārṇavatāriṇī  || 9.49 ||
sīrajalaṃ ye'pi pibanti loke mucyanti te pāpaviśeṣasaṅghaiḥ  |
vrajanti saṃsāramanādibhāvaṃ tyaktvā ciraṃ mokṣapadaṃ viśuddham  || 9.50 ||
yathā gaṅgā tathā revā tathā caiva sarasvatī  |
samaṃ puṇyaphalaṃ proktaṃ snānadarśanacintanaiḥ  || 9.51 ||
varadānānmahābhāgā hyadhikā cocyate budhaiḥ  |
kāruṇyāntarabhāvena na mṛtā samupāgatā  || 9.52 ||
mucyante darśanāttena pātakaiḥ snānamaṅgalaiḥ  |
narmadāyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha ye namanti trilocanam  || 9.53 ||
umārudrāṅgasambhūtā yena caiṣā mahānadī  |
lokānprāpayate svargaṃ tena puṇyatvamāgatā  || 9.54 ||
ya evamīśānavarasya dehaṃ vibhajya devīmiha saṃśṛṇoti  |
sa yāti rudraṃ mahatāraveṇa gandharvayakṣairiva gīyamānaḥ  || 9.55 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye narmadotpattitatsnānaphalādikathanaṃ nāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 10

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kasminkalpe mahābhāgā narmadeyaṃ dvijottama  |
vibhaktā ṛṣibhiḥ sarvaistapoyuktairmahātmabhiḥ  || 10.1 ||
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ brūhi me vadatāṃ vara  |
kalpānte yadbhavetkaṣṭaṃ lokānāṃ tattvameva ca  || 10.2 ||
atīte tu purā kalpe yatheyaṃ vartate'nagha  |
asyāntyasya ca kalpasya vyavasthāṃ kathaya prabho  |
evamuktaḥ sabhāmadhye mārkaṇḍo vākyamabravīt || 10.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
vakṣye'haṃ śrūyatāṃ sarvaiḥ katheyaṃ pūrvataḥ śrutā  || 10.4 ||
mahatkatheyaṃ vaiśiṣṭī kalpādasmātparaṃ tu yā  |
lokakṣayakaro ghora āsītkālaḥ sudāruṇaḥ  || 10.5 ||
tasminnapi mahāghore yatheyaṃ vā mṛtā satī  |
parituṣṭairvibhaktā ca śṛṇudhvaṃ tāṃ kathāmimām  || 10.6 ||
yugānte samanuprāpte pitāmahadinatraye  |
mānasā brahmaṇaḥ putrāḥ sākṣādbrahmeva sattamāḥ  || 10.7 ||
sanakādyā mahātmāno ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ  |
yamendravaruṇādyāśca lokapālā dinatraye  || 10.8 ||
kālāpekṣāstu tiṣṭhanti lokavṛttāntatatparāḥ  |
tataḥ kalpakṣaye prāpte teṣāṃ jñānamanuttamam  || 10.9 ||
sarveṣāṃ naśyate cāyuryugarūpānusārataḥ  |
bhūrlokaṃ te parityajya agamaṃśca bhuvaṃ tadā  || 10.10 ||
svarlokaṃ ca mahaścaiva janaścaiva tapastadā  |
āśrayaṃ satyalokaṃ ca sarvalokamanuttamam  || 10.11 ||
kālaṃ yugasahasrāntaṃ putrapautrasamanvitāḥ  |
satyaloke ca tiṣṭhanti yāvatsaṃjāyate jagat || 10.12 ||
brahmaputrāśca ye kecitkalpādau na bhavanti ha  |
trailokyaṃ te parityajya anādhāraṃ bhavanti ca  || 10.13 ||
taiḥ sārdhaṃ ye tu te viprā anye cāpi tapodhanāḥ  |
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāśca anye vaimānikā gaṇāḥ  || 10.14 ||
ṛṣayaśca mahābhāgā varṇāścānye pṛthagvidhāḥ  |
sīdanti bhūmyāṃ sahitā ye cānye talavāsinaḥ  || 10.15 ||
anāvṛṣṭirabhūttatra mahatī śatavārṣikī  |
lokakṣayakarī raudrā vṛkṣavīrudvināśinī  || 10.16 ||
trailokyasaṃkṣobhakarī saptārṇavaviśoṣaṇī  |
tato lokāḥ kṣudhāviṣṭā bhramantīva diśo daśa  || 10.17 ||
kaṃdairmūlaiḥ phalairvāpi vartayante suduḥkhitāḥ  |
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ kūpāḥ sevante pāvanāni ca  || 10.18 ||
tatrāpi sarve śuṣyanti saridbhiḥ saha sāgarāḥ  |
tato yānyalpasārāṇi sattvāni pṛthivītale  || 10.19 ||
tānyevāgre pralīyante bhinnānyurujalena vai  |
atha saṃkṣīyamāṇāsu saritsu saha sāgaraiḥ  || 10.20 ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ ṣaṣṭisāhasraṃ kurukṣetranivāsinām  |
ye ca vaikhānasā viprā dantolūkhalinastathā  || 10.21 ||
himācalaguhāguhye ye vasanti tapodhanāḥ  |
sarve te māmupāgamya kṣuttṛṣārtāstapodhanāḥ  || 10.22 ||
ūcuḥ prāñjalayaḥ sarve sīdayāmo mahāmune  |
saritsāgaraśailāntaṃ jagatsaṃśuṣyate dvija  || 10.23 ||
kutra yāsyāma sahitā yāvatkālasya paryayaḥ  |
dīrghāyurasi viprendra na mṛtastvaṃ yugakṣaye  || 10.24 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyacca sarvaṃ tava hṛdi sthitam  |
tasmāttvaṃ vetsi sarvaṃ ca kathayasva mahāvrata  || 10.25 ||
kīdṛkkālaṃ mahābhāga kṣapiṣyāmo'tha suvrata  |
anāvṛṣṭihataṃ sarvaṃ sīdate sacarācaram  || 10.26 ||
paritrāhi mahābhāga na yathā yāma saṃkṣayam  |
tataḥ saṃcintya manasā tvaranviprānathābravam  || 10.27 ||
kurukṣetraṃ tyajadhvaṃ ca putradārasamanvitāḥ  |
tyaktvodīcīṃ diśaṃ sarve yāmo yāmyāmanuttamām  || 10.28 ||
nagaragrāmaghoṣāḍhyāṃ purapattanaśobhitām  |
gacchāmo narmadātīraṃ bahusiddhaniṣevitam  || 10.29 ||
rudrāṅgīṃ tāṃ mahāpuṇyāṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  |
paśyāmastāṃ mahābhāgāṃ nyagrodhāvārasaṃkulām  || 10.30 ||
māheśvarairbhāgavataiḥ sāṃkhyaiḥ siddhaiḥ susevitām  |
anāvṛṣṭibhayādbhītāḥ kūlayorubhayorapi  || 10.31 ||
āśrame hyāśramāndivyānkārayāmo jitavratāḥ  |
evamuktāstu te sarve sametānucaraiḥ saha  || 10.32 ||
narmadātīramāsādya sthitāḥ sarve'kutobhayāḥ  |
kiṃcitpūrvamanusmṛtya purā kalpādibhirbhayam  || 10.33 ||
prāptāstu narmadātīramādāveva kalau yuge  |
tato varṣaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ divyaṃ revātaṭe'vasan  || 10.34 ||
ṣaḍviṃśacca sahasrāṇi varṣāṇāṃ mānuṣāṇi ca  |
tatrāścaryaṃ mayā dṛṣṭamṛṣīṇāṃ vasatāṃ nṛpa  || 10.35 ||
anāvṛṣṭihate loke saṃśuṣke sthāvare care  |
bhinne yugādikalane hāhābhūte vicetane  || 10.36 ||
cāturvarṇe pralīne tu naṣṭe homabalikrame  |
niḥsvāhe nirvaṣaṭkāre śaucācāravivarjite  || 10.37 ||
iyamekā saricchreṣṭhā ṛṣikoṭiniṣevitā  |
nānyā kācittriloke'pi ramaṇīyā nareśvara  || 10.38 ||
yatheyaṃ puṇyasalilā indrasyevāmarāvatī  |
devatāyatanaiḥ śubhrairāśramaiśca sukalpitaiḥ  || 10.39 ||
śobhate narmadā devī svarge mandākinī yathā  |
yāvadvṛkṣā mahāśailā yāvatsāgarasaṃbhavā  || 10.40 ||
ubhayoḥ kūlayostāvanmaṇḍitāyatanaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
hūyadbhiragnihotraiśca havirdhūmasamākulā  || 10.41 ||
babhūva narmadā devī prāvṛṭkāla iva śarvarī  |
devatāyatanairnaikaiḥ pūjāsaṃskāraśobhitā  || 10.42 ||
saridbhirbhrājate śreṣṭhā purī śākrī ca bhāskarī  |
kecitpañcāgnitapasaḥ kecidapyagnihotriṇaḥ  || 10.43 ||
keciddhūmakamaśnanti tapasyugre vyavasthitāḥ  |
ātmayajñaratāḥ kecidapare bhaktibhāginaḥ  || 10.44 ||
vaiṣṇavajñānamāsādya kecicchaivaṃ vrataṃ tathā  |
ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ ca kecitṣaṣṭhāhabhojanāḥ  || 10.45 ||
cāndrāyaṇavidhānaiśca kṛcchriṇaścātikṛcchriṇaḥ  |
evaṃvidhaistapobhiśca narmadātīraśobhitaiḥ  || 10.46 ||
yajadbhiḥ śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ keśavaṃ bhāti nityadā  |
ekatve ca pṛthaktve ca yajatāṃ ca maheśvaram  || 10.47 ||
kalau yuge mahāghore prāptāḥ siddhimanuttamām  |
yasya yasya hi yā bhaktirvijñānaṃ yasya yādṛśam  || 10.48 ||
yasminyasmiṃśca deve tu tāṃtāmīśo'dadātprabhuḥ  |
svabhāvaikatayā bhaktyā tāmetyāntaḥ pralīyate  || 10.49 ||
saṃsāre parivartante ye pṛthagbhājino narāḥ  |
ye mahāvṛkṣamīśānaṃ tyaktvā śākhāvalambinaḥ  || 10.50 ||
punarāvartamānāste jāyante hi caturyuge  |
devānte sthāvarānte ca saṃsāre cābhramankramāt || 10.51 ||
punarjanma punaḥ svarge punarghore ca raurave  |
ye punardevamīśānaṃ bhavaṃ bhaktisusaṃsthitāḥ  || 10.52 ||
yajanti narmadātīre na punaste bhavanti ca  |
ā dehapatanātkecidupāsantaḥ paraṃ gatāḥ  || 10.53 ||
keciddvādaśabhirvarṣaiḥ ṣaḍbharanye tapodhanāḥ  |
tribhiḥ saṃvatsaraiḥ kecitkecitsaṃvatsareṇa tu  || 10.54 ||
ṣaḍbhirmāsaistu saṃsiddhāstribhirmāsaistathāpare  |
munayo devamāśritya narmadāṃ ca yaśasvinīm  || 10.55 ||
chittvā saṃsāradoṣāṃśca agamanbrahma śāśvatam  |
evaṃ kaliyuge ghore śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 10.56 ||
narmadātīramāśritya munayo rudramāviśan  || 10.57 ||
ye narmadātīramupetya viprāḥ śaive vrate yatnamupaprapannāḥ  |
trikālamambhaḥ pravigāhya bhaktyā devaṃ samabhyarcya śivaṃ vrajanti  || 10.58 ||
dhyānārcanairjāpyamahāvrataiśca nārāyaṇaṃ vā satataṃ smaranti  |
te dhautapāṇḍurapaṭā iva rājahaṃsāḥ saṃsārasāgarajalasya taranti pāram  || 10.59 ||
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyamutkṣipya bhujamucyate  |
idamekaṃ suniṣpannaṃ dhyeyo nārāyaṇaḥ sadā  || 10.60 ||
yo vā haraṃ pūjayate jitātmā māsaṃ ca pakṣaṃ ca vasennarendra  |
revāṃ samāśritya mahānubhāvaḥ sa devadevo'tha bhavetpinākī  || 10.61 ||
kīṭāḥ pataṃgāśca pipīlikāśca ye vai mriyante'mbhasi narmadāyāḥ  |
te divyarūpāstu kulaprasūtāḥ śataṃ samā dharmaparā bhavanti  || 10.62 ||
kālena vṛkṣāḥ prapatanti ye'pi mahātaraṃgaughanikṛttamūlāḥ  |
te narmadāṃbhobhirapāstapāpā dedīpyamānāstridivaṃ prayānti  || 10.63 ||
akāmakāmāśca tathā sakāmā revāntamāśritya mriyanti tīre  |
jaḍāndhamūkāstridivaṃ prayānti kimatra viprā bhavabhāvayuktāḥ  || 10.64 ||
māsopavāsairapi śoṣitāṅgā na tāṃ gatiṃ yānti vimuktadehāḥ  |
mriyanti revājalapūtakāyāḥ śivārcane keśavabhāvayuktāḥ  || 10.65 ||
nīvāraśyāmākayaveṅgudādyairanyairmunīndrā iha vartayanti  |
āpritya kūlaṃ tridaśānugītaṃ te narmadāyā na viśanti mṛtyum  || 10.66 ||
bhramanti ye tīramupetya devyāstrikāladevārcanasatyapūtāḥ  |
viṇmūtracarmāsthitiropadhānāḥ kukṣau yuvatyā na vasanti bhūyaḥ  || 10.67 ||
kiṃ yajñadānairbahubhiśca teṣāṃ niṣevitaistīrthavaraiḥ samastaiḥ  |
revātaṭaṃ dakṣiṇamuttaraṃ vā sevanti te rudracarānupūrvam  || 10.68 ||
te vañcitāḥ paṅgujaḍāndhabhūtā lokeṣu martyāḥ paśubhiśca tulyāḥ  |
ye nāśritā rudraśarīrabhūtāṃ sopānapaṅktiṃ tridivasya revām  || 10.69 ||
yugaṃ kaliṃ ghoramimaṃ ya iccheddraṣṭuṃ kadācinna punardvijendraḥ  |
sa narmadātīramupetya sarvaṃ sampūjayetsarvavimuktasaṃgaḥ  || 10.70 ||
vighnairanekairatiyojyamānā ye tīramujhanti na narmadāyāḥ  |
te caiva sarvasya hitārthabhūtā vandyāśca te sarvajanasya mānyāḥ  || 10.71 ||
bhṛgvatrigārgeyavaśiṣṭhakaṅkāḥ śataiḥ sametairniyatāstvasaṃkhyaiḥ  |
siddhiṃ parāṃ te hi jalaplutāṅgāḥ prāptāstu lokānmarutāṃ na cānye  || 10.72 ||
jñānaṃ mahatpuṇyatamaṃ pavitraṃ paṭhantyado nityaviśuddhasattvāḥ  |
gatiṃ parāṃ yānti mahānubhāvā rudrasya vākyaṃ hi yathā pramāṇam  || 10.73 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye narmadāsnānaphalaśrutikathanaṃ nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 11

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
aho mahatpuṇyatamā viśiṣṭā kṣayaṃ na yātā iha yā yugānte  |
tasmātsadā sevyatamā munīndrairdhyānārcanasnānaparāyaṇaiśca  || 11.1 ||
yāmāśritya gatā mokṣamṛṣayo dharmavatsalāḥ  |
ye tvayoktāstu niyamā ṛṣīṇāṃ vedanirmitāḥ  || 11.2 ||
mokṣāvāptirbhavedyeṣāṃ niyamaiśca pṛthagvidhaiḥ  |
daśadvādaśabhirvāpi ṣaḍbhiraṣṭābhireva vā  || 11.3 ||
tribhistathā caturbhirvā varṣairmāsaistathaiva ca  |
mucyante kalidoṣaiste deveśānasamarcanāt || 11.4 ||
brahmāṇaṃ vā suraśreṣṭha keśavaṃ vā jagadgurum  |
arcayanpāpamakhilaṃ jahātyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 11.5 ||
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  |
yasminsaṃsāragahane nimagnāḥ sarvajantavaḥ  |
te kathaṃ tridivaṃ prāptā iti me saṃśayo vada  || 11.6 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
janmāntarairanekaistu mānuṣyamupalabhyate  |
bhaktirutpadyate cātra kathaṃcidapi śaṅkare  || 11.7 ||
tīrthadānopavāsānāṃ yajñairdevadvijārcanaiḥ  |
avāptirjāyate puṃsāṃ śraddhayā parayā nṛpa  || 11.8 ||
tasmācchraddhā prakartavyā mānavairdharmavatsalaiḥ  |
īśo'pi śraddhayā sādhyastena śraddhā viśiṣyate  || 11.9 ||
anyathā niṣphalaṃ sarvaṃ śraddhāhīnaṃ tu bhārata  |
tasmātsamāśrayedbhaktiṃ rudrasya parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 11.10 ||
teṣāṃhi saphalaṃ janma yeṣāṃ bhaktiracañcalā  |
sā caiva trividhā bhaktiḥ sāttvikī rājasī tathā  || 11.11 ||
tāmasī sarvalokasya trividhaṃ ca phalaṃ labhet |
te karmaphalasaṃyogādāvartante punaḥpunaḥ  || 11.12 ||
janmāntaraśataisteṣāṃ jñānināṃ devayājinām  |
devatraye bhavedbhaktiḥ kṣayātpāpasya karmaṇaḥ  || 11.13 ||
īśānāttu punarmokṣo jāyate chinnasaṃśayaḥ  |
ye punarnarmadātīramāśritya dvijapuṃgavāḥ  || 11.14 ||
trayīmārgamasandigdhāste yānti paramāṃ gatim  |
ekāgramanaso ye tu śaṅkaraṃ śivamavyayam  || 11.15 ||
arcayantīha niratāḥ kṣipraṃ sidhyanti te janāḥ  |
kālena mahatā siddhirjāyate'nyatra dehinām  || 11.16 ||
narmadāyāḥ punastīre kṣipraṃ siddhiravāpyate  |
ṣaḍbhirvarṣaistu sidhyanti ye tu sāṃkhyavido janāḥ  || 11.17 ||
vaiṣṇavā jñānasampannāste'pi sidhyanti cāgrataḥ  |
sarvayogavido ye ca samudramiva sindhavaḥ  || 11.18 ||
ekībhavanti kalpānte yoge māheśvare gatāḥ  |
sarveṣāmeva yogānāṃ yogo māheśvaro varaḥ  || 11.19 ||
tamāsādya vimucyante ye'pi syuḥ pāpayonayaḥ  |
śivamarcya nadīkūle jāyante te na yoniṣu  || 11.20 ||
gatireṣā durārohā sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  |
mucyante maṅkṣu saṃsārādrevāmāśritya jantavaḥ  || 11.21 ||
tasmātsnāyī bhavennityaṃ tathā bhasmavilepanaḥ  |
narmadātīramāsādya kṣipraṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt || 11.22 ||
trikālaṃ pūjayecchānto yo naro liṅgamādarāt |
sarvarogavinirmuktaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 11.23 ||
ṣaḍbhiḥ sidhyati masaistu yadyapi syātsa pāpakṛt |
ye punaḥ śuddhamanaso māsaiḥ śudhyanti te tribhiḥ  || 11.24 ||
yathā dinakaraspṛṣṭaṃ himaṃ śailādviśīryante  |
tadvadvilīyate pāpaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ bhasmakaṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 11.25 ||
vainateyabhayatrastā yathā naśyanti pannagāḥ  |
tadvatpāpāni naśyanti bhasmanābhyukṣitāni ha  || 11.26 ||
narmadātoyapūtena bhasmanoddhūlayanti ye  |
sadyaste pāpasaṅghācca mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 11.27 ||
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ bhaktayā yathoktaṃ pālayanti ye  |
śūdrānnena vihīnāstu te yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 11.28 ||
amṛtaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ kṣatriyānnaṃ payaḥ smṛtam  |
vaiśyānnamannameva syācchūdrānnaṃ rudhiraṃ smṛtam  || 11.29 ||
śūdrānnarasasaṃpuṣṭā ye mriyante dvijottamāḥ  |
te tapojñānahīnāstu kākā gṛdhrā bhavanti te  || 11.30 ||
duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyāṇāmannamāśritya tiṣṭhati  |
yo yasyānnaṃ samaśnāti sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam  || 11.31 ||
viśeṣādyatidharmeṇa tapolaulyaṃ samāśritāḥ  |
narakaṃ yāntyasandigdhamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 11.32 ||
īdṛgrūpāśca ye viprāḥ pāśupatye vyavasthitāḥ  |
te mahatpāpasaṃghātaṃ dahantyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 11.33 ||
viḍambena ca saṃyuktā laulupyena ca pīḍitāḥ  |
asaṃgrāhyā ityevaṃ śrutinodanā  || 11.34 ||
mātāpitṛkṛtairdoṣairanye kecitsvakarmajaiḥ  |
naṣṭā jñānāvalepena ahaṅkāreṇa'pare  || 11.35 ||
śāṅkare prasthitā dharme ye smṛtyarthabahiṣkṛtāḥ  |
kliśyamānāstu kalena te yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 11.36 ||
aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣā mūrkhā dambhavivardhitāḥ  |
na sidhyanti durātmānaḥ kudṛṣṭāntārthakīrtanāḥ  || 11.37 ||
mahābhāgye'pi tīrthasya śāṅkaraṃ vratamāsthitāḥ  |
viyoniṃ yāntyasandigdhaṃ laulupyena samanvitāḥ  || 11.38 ||
na tīrthairna ca dānaiśca duṣkṛtaṃ hi vilupyate  |
ajñānācca pramādācca kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vinaśyati  || 11.39 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu vidhinā vartitavyaṃ dvijātibhiḥ  |
paraṃ brahma japadbhiśca vārtitavyaṃ muhurmuhuḥ  || 11.40 ||
ūrdhvarūpaṃ virūpākṣaṃ yo'dhīte rudrameva ca  |
īśānaṃ paśyate sākṣātṣaṇmāsātsaṅgavarjitaḥ  || 11.41 ||
saṃhitāyā daśāvṛttīryaḥ karoti susaṃyataḥ  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya sa mucyetsarvapātakaiḥ  || 11.42 ||
purāṇasaṃhitāṃ vāpi śaivīṃ vā vaiṣṇavīmapi  |
yaḥ paṭhennarmadātīre śivāgre sa śivātmakaḥ  || 11.43 ||
ā bhūtasaṃkṣayaṃ yāvatsvargaloke mahīyate  |
saṃsākhyasanaṃ hātuṃ purā proktaṃ tu nandinā  || 11.44 ||
devarṣisiddhagandharvasamavāye śivālaye  |
nandigītāmimāṃ rājañchṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ śubhām  || 11.45 ||
svargamokṣapradāṃ puṇyāṃ saṃsārabhayanāśinīm  || 11.46 ||
saṃsāragahvaraguhāṃ pravihātumetāṃ cedicchatha pratipadaṃ bhavatāpakhinnāḥ  |
nānāvidhairnijakṛtairbahukarmapāśairbaddhāḥ sukhāya śṛṇutaikahitaṃ mayoktam  || 11.47 ||
śakra vakragatiṃ mā gā mā kṛthā yama yātanām  |
cetaḥ pracetaḥ śamaya laulupyaṃ tyaja vittapa  || 11.48 ||
dīnānāthaviśiṣṭebhyo dhanaṃ sarvaṃ parityaja  |
yadi saṃsārajaladhervīcīpreṅkhollanāturaḥ  || 11.49 ||
janmodvignaṃ mṛtestrastaṃ grastaṃ kāmādibhirnaram  |
srastaṃ yo na yamādibhyaḥ pinākī pāti pāvanaḥ  || 11.50 ||
mā dhehi garvaṃ kīnāśa hāsyaṃ yāsyasi pīḍayan  |
prāṇinaṃ sarvaśaraṇaṃ tadbhāvi śaraṇaṃ tava  || 11.51 ||
kālaḥ karālako bālaḥ ko mṛtyuḥ ko yamādhamaḥ  |
śivaviṣṇuparāṇāṃ hi narāṇāṃ kiṃ bhayaṃ bhavet || 11.52 ||
bhavabhārārtajantūnāṃ revātīranivāsinām  |
bhargaśca bhagavāṃścaiva bhavabhītivibhedanau  || 11.53 ||
śivaṃ bhaja śivaṃ dhyāya śivaṃ stuhi śivaṃ yaja  |
śivaṃ nama varāka tvaṃ jñānaṃ mokṣaṃ yadīcchasi  || 11.54 ||
paṭha pañcānanaṃ śāstraṃ mantraṃ pañcākṣaraṃ japa  |
dhehi pañcātmakaṃ tattvaṃ yaja pañcānanaṃ param  || 11.55 ||
kiṃ taiḥ karmagaṇaiḥ śocyairnānābhāvaviśeṣitaiḥ  |
yadi pañcānanaḥ śrīmān sevyate sarvathā śivaḥ  || 11.56 ||
kiṃ saṃsāragajonmattabṛṃhitairnibhṛtairapi  |
yadi pañcānano devo bhāvagandhopasevitaḥ  || 11.57 ||
re mūḍha kiṃ viṣādena prāpya karmakadarthanām  |
bhavānīvallabhaṃ bhīmaṃ japa tvaṃ bhayanāśanam  || 11.58 ||
narmadātīranilayaṃ duḥkhaughavilayaṃkaram  |
svargamokṣapradaṃ bhargaṃ bhaja mūḍha sureśvaram  || 11.59 ||
vihāya revāṃ surasindhusevyāṃ tattīrasaṃsthaṃ ca haraṃ hariṃ ca  |
unmattavadbhāvavivarjitastvaṃ kva yāsi re mūḍha digantarāṇi  || 11.60 ||
bhaja revājalaṃ puṇyaṃ yaja rudraṃ sanātanam  |
japa pañcākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ vraja sthānaṃ ca vāñchitam  || 11.61 ||
kleśayitvā nijaṃ kāyamupāyairbahubhistu kim  |
bhaja revāṃ śivaṃ prāpya sukhasādhyaṃ paraṃ padam  || 11.62 ||
evaṃ kailāsamāsādya nadīṃ sa śivasannidhau  |
jagau yallokapālānāṃ tanmayoktaṃ tavādhunā  || 11.63 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
snānadānaparo yastu nityaṃ dharmamanuvrataḥ  |
narmadātīramāśritya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 11.64 ||
vidhihīno japennityaṃ vedānsarvāñchataṃ samāḥ  |
mṛtyulāṅgalajāpyena samo yo'pyadhiko guṇaiḥ  || 11.65 ||
bījayonyaviśuddhastu yathā rudraṃ na vindati  |
tathā lāṅgalamantro'pi na tiṣṭhati gatāyuṣi  || 11.66 ||
gāyatrījapasaṃyuktaḥ saṃyamī hyadhiko guṇaiḥ  |
agnimīḍe iṣetvo vā agna āyāhi nityadā  || 11.67 ||
śanno devīti kūlastho japenmucyeta kilbiṣaiḥ  || 11.68 ||
sāṅgopāṅgāṃstathā vedāñjapannityaṃ samāhitaḥ  |
na tatphalamavāpnoti gāyatryā saṃyamī yathā  || 11.69 ||
rudrādhyāyaṃ sakṛjjaptvā vipro vedasamanvitaḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  || 11.70 ||
anyadvai japyasaṃsthānaṃ sūktamāraṇyakaṃ tathā  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  || 11.71 ||
yatkiṃcitkriyate jāpyaṃ yacca dānaṃ pradīyate  |
narmadājalamāśritya tatsarvaṃ cākṣayaṃ bhavet || 11.72 ||
evaṃvidhairvratairnityaṃ narmadāṃ ye samāśritāḥ  |
te mṛtā vaiṣṇavaṃ yānti padaṃ vā śaivamavyayam  || 11.73 ||
satyalokaṃ narāḥ kecitsūryalokaṃ tathāpare  |
apsarogaṇasaṃvītā yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 11.74 ||
evaṃ vai vartamāne'smiṃlloke tu nṛpapuṃgava  |
ṛṣīṇāṃ daśakoṭyastu kurukṣetranivāsinām  || 11.75 ||
mayā saha mahābhāga narmadātaṭamāśritāḥ  |
phalamūlakṛtāhārā arcayantaḥ sthitāḥ śivam  || 11.76 ||
tacca varṣaśataṃ divyaṃ kālasaṃkhyānumānataḥ  |
ṣaḍviṃśatisahasrāṇi tāni mānuṣasaṃkhyayā  || 11.77 ||
tatastasyāmatītāyāṃ sandhyāyāṃ nṛpasattama  |
śeṣaṃ mānuṣyamekaṃ tu kāle varṣaśataṃ sthitam  || 11.78 ||
tato'bhavadanāvṛṣṭirlokakṣayakarī tadā  |
yayā yātaṃ jagatsarvaṃ kṣayaṃ bhūyo hi dāruṇam  || 11.79 ||
ye pūrvamiha saṃsiddhā ṛṣayo vedapāragāḥ  |
teṣāṃ prabhāvādbhagavān vavarṣa balavṛtrahā  || 11.80 ||
mahatī bhūrisalilā samantādvṛṣṭirāhitā  |
tato vṛṣṭyā tu teṣāṃ vai vartanaṃ samajāyata  || 11.81 ||
punaryugānte samprāpte kiṃciccheṣe kalau yuge  |
niḥśeṣamabhavatsarvaṃ śuṣkaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam  || 11.82 ||
nirvṛkṣauṣadhagulmaṃ ca tṛṇavīrudvivarjitam  |
anāvṛṣṭihataṃ sarvaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamabhūdbhṛśam  || 11.83 ||
tataste ṛṣayaḥ sarve kṣuttṛṣārtāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
yugasvabhāvamāviṣṭā hīnasattvā abhavannṛpa  || 11.84 ||
naṣṭahomasvadhākāre yugānte samupasthite  |
kiṃ kāryaṃ kva nu yāsyāmaḥ ko'smākaṃ śaraṇaṃ bhavet || 11.85 ||
tānahaṃ pratyuvācedaṃ mā bhaiṣṭeti punaḥpunaḥ  |
īdṛgvidhā mayā dṛṣṭā bahavaḥ kālaparyayāḥ  || 11.86 ||
narmadātīramāśritya te sarve gamitā mayā  |
eṣā hi śaraṇaṃ devī samprāpte hi yugakṣaye  || 11.87 ||
nānyā gatirihāsmākaṃ vidyate dvijasattamāḥ  |
janitrī sarvabhūtānāṃ viśeṣeṇa dvijottamāḥ  || 11.88 ||
pitāmahā ye pitaro ye cānye prapitāmahāḥ  |
te samastā gatāḥ svargaṃ samāśritya mahānadīm  || 11.89 ||
bhṛgvādyāḥ sapta ye tvāsanmama pūrvapitāmahāḥ  |
dhaumṛṇī ca mahābhāgā mama bhāryā śucismitā  |
manasvatī ca yā matā bhārgavo'ṅgirasastathā  || 11.90 ||
pulastyaḥ pulahaścaiva vasiṣṭhātreyakāśyapāḥ  |
tathānye ca mahābhāgā niyamavratacāriṇaḥ  |
anye ca śatasāhasrā atra siddhiṃ samāgatāḥ  || 11.91 ||
tasmādiyaṃ mahābhāgā na moktavyā kadācana  |
nānyā kācinnadī śaktā lokatrayaphalapradā  || 11.92 ||
dvandvairanekairbahubhiḥ kṣuttṛṣādyairmahābhayaiḥ  |
mucyante te narāḥ sadyo narmadātīravāsinaḥ  || 11.93 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena sevitavyā saridvarā  |
vāñchadbhiḥ paramaṃ śreya iha loke paratra ca  || 11.94 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīnarmadāmāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 12

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etacchrutvā vaco rājansaṃhṛṣṭā ṛṣayo'bhavan  |
narmadāṃ stotumārabdhāḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭā dvijāḥ  || 12.1 ||
namo'stu te puṇyajale namo makaragāmini  |
namaste pāpamocinyai namo devi varānane  || 12.2 ||
namo'stu te puṇyajalāśraye śubhe viśuddhasattvaṃ surasiddhasevite  |
namo'stu te tīrthagaṇairniṣevite namo'stu rudrāṅgasamudbhave vare  || 12.3 ||
namo'stu te devi samudragāmini namo'stu te devi varaprade śive  |
namo'stu lokadvayasaukhyadāyini hyanekabhūtaughasamāśrite'naghe  || 12.4 ||
saridvare pāpahare vicitrite gandharvayakṣoragasevitāṅge  |
sanātani prāṇigaṇānukampini mokṣaprade devi vidhehi śaṃ naḥ  || 12.5 ||
mahāgajairghamahiṣairvarāhaiḥ saṃsevite devi mahormimāle  |
natāḥ sma sarve varade sukhaprade vimocayāsmānpaśupāśabandhāt || 12.6 ||
pāpairanekairaśubhairvibaddhā bhramanti tāvannarakeṣu martyāḥ  |
mahānilodbhūtataraṅgabhūtaṃ yāvattavāmbho hi na saṃspṛśanti  || 12.7 ||
anekaduḥkhaughabhayārditānāṃ pāpairanekairabhiveṣṭitānām  |
gatistvamambhojasamānavakre dvandvairanekairapi saṃvṛtānām  || 12.8 ||
nadyaśca pūtā vimalā bhavanti tvāṃ devi samprāpya na saṃśayo'tra  |
duḥkhāturāṇāmabhayaṃ dadāsi śiṣṭairanekairabhipūjitāsi  || 12.9 ||
spṛṣṭaṃ karaiścandramaso raveśca tadaiva dadyātparamaṃ padaṃ tu  |
yatropalāḥ puṇyajalāplutāste śivatvamāyānti kimatra citram  || 12.10 ||
bhramanti tāvannarakeṣu martyā duḥkhāturāḥ pāpaparītadehāḥ  |
mahānilodbhūtataraṅgabhaṅgaṃ yāvattavāmbho na hi saṃśrayanti  || 12.11 ||
mlecchāḥ pulindāstvatha yātudhānāḥ pibanti ye'mbhastava devi puṇyam  |
muktā bhavantīha bhayāttu ghorānniḥsaṃśayaṃ te'pi kimatra citram  || 12.12 ||
sarāṃsi nadyaḥ kṣayamabhyupetā ghore yuge'smin hi kalau pradūṣite  |
tvaṃ bhrājase devi jalaughapūrṇā divīva nakṣatrapathe ca gaṅgā  || 12.13 ||
tava prasādādvarade variṣṭhe kālaṃ yathemaṃ paripālayitvā  |
yāmo'tha rudraṃ tava suprasādādvayaṃ tathā tvaṃ kuru vai prasādam  || 12.14 ||
gatistvamambeva piteva putrāṃstvaṃ pāhi no yāvadimaṃ yugāntam  |
kālaṃ tvanāvṛṣṭihataṃ sughoraṃ yāvattarāmastava suprasādāt || 12.15 ||
paṭhanti ye stotramidaṃ dvijendrāḥ śṛṇvanti ye cāpi narāḥ praśāntāḥ  |
te yānti rudraṃ vṛṣasaṃyutena yānena divyāmbarabhūṣitāśca  || 12.16 ||
ye stotrametatsatataṃ paṭhanti snātvā tu toye khalu narmadāyāḥ  |
ante hi teṣāṃ sariduttameyaṃ gatiṃ viśuddhāmacirāddadāti  || 12.17 ||
prātaḥ samutthāya tathā śayāno yaḥ kīrtayetānudinaṃ stavaṃ ca  |
sa muktapāpaḥ suviśuddhadehaḥ samāśrayaṃ yāti maheśvarasya  || 12.18 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīnarmadāmāhātmye narmadāstotrakathanaṃ nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 13

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ bhagavatī puṇyā stutā sā munipuṃgavaiḥ  |
cintayāmāsa sarveṣāṃ dāsyāmi varamuttamam  || 13.1 ||
tataḥ prasuptāṃstāñjñātvā rātrau devī jagāma ha  |
ekaikasya ṛṣeḥ svapne darśanaṃ cāruhāsinī  || 13.2 ||
tato'rdharātre samprāpta utthitā jalamadhyataḥ  |
vimalāmbarasaṃvītā divyamālāvibhūṣitā  || 13.3 ||
ghṛtātapatrā suśroṇī padmarāgavibhūṣitā  |
jagāda mā bhairiti tānekaikaṃ tu pṛthakpṛthak || 13.4 ||
vasadhvaṃ mama pārśve tu bhayaṃ tyaktvā kṣudhādijam  || 13.5 ||
evamuktvā tadā devī svapnānte tānmahāmunīn  |
jagāmādarśanaṃ paścātpraviśya jalamātmikam  || 13.6 ||
tataḥ prabhāte munayo mitha ūcurmudanvitāḥ  |
tathā dṛṣṭā mayā dṛṣṭā svapne devī sudarśanā  || 13.7 ||
abhayaṃ dattamasmākaṃ siddhiścāpyacireṇa tu  |
praśastaṃ darśanaṃ tasyā narmadāyā na saṃśayaḥ  || 13.8 ||
athānyadivase rājanmatsyānāṃ rūpamuttamam  |
paśyanti saparīvārāḥ svakīyāśramasannidhau  || 13.9 ||
tāndṛṣṭvā vismayāviṣṭā matsyāṃstatra maharṣayaḥ  |
pūjayāmāsuravyagrā havyakavyena devatāḥ  || 13.10 ||
tānmatsyasaṅghānsamprāpya mahādevyāḥ prasādataḥ  |
saputradārabhṛtyāste vartayanti pṛthakpṛthak || 13.11 ||
dine dine tathāpyevamāśrameṣu dvijātayaḥ  |
matsyānāṃ sañcayaṃ dṛṣṭvā vismitāścābhavaṃstadā  || 13.12 ||
mṛtāṃstāṃstu supuṣṭāṅgān pāṭhīnāṃśca viśeṣataḥ  |
dvāre dvāre cāśramāṇāṃ tāpasānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 13.13 ||
hṛṣṭapuṣṭāstadā sarve narmadātīravāsinaḥ  |
ṛṣayaste bhayaṃ sarve tatyajuḥ kṣuttṛṣodbhavam  || 13.14 ||
te japantastapantaśca tiṣṭhanti bharatarṣabha  |
arcayanti pitḥndevānnarmadātaṭamāśritāḥ  || 13.15 ||
tairjapadbhistapadbhiśca satataṃ dvijasattamaiḥ  |
bhrājate sā saricchreṣṭhā tārābhirdyaurgrahairiva  || 13.16 ||
tatra tairbahulaiḥ śubhrairbrāhmaṇairvedaparāgaiḥ  |
narmadā dharmadā pūrvaṃ saṃvibhaktā yathākramam  || 13.17 ||
ṛṣibhirdaśakoṭibhirnarmadātīravāsibhiḥ  |
vibhakteyaṃ vibhaktāṅgī narmadā śarmadā nṛṇām  || 13.18 ||
yajñopavītaiśca śubhairakṣasūtraiśca bhārata  |
kūladvaye mahāpuṇyā narmadodadhigāminī  || 13.19 ||
pṛthagāyatanaiḥ śubhrairliṅgairvālukamṛnmayaiḥ  |
bhrājate yā saricchreṣṭhā nakṣatrairiva śarvarī  || 13.20 ||
evaṃ ta ṛṣayaḥ sarve tarpayantaḥ surānpitḥn  |
nyavasannarmadātīre yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 13.21 ||
kiṃcidgate tatastasminghore varṣaśatādhike  |
ardharātre tadā kanyā jalāduttīrya bhārata  || 13.22 ||
vidyutpuṃjasamābhāsā vyālayajñopavītinī  |
triśūlāgrakarā saumyā tānuvāca ṛṣīṃstadā  || 13.23 ||
āgacchadhvaṃ munigaṇā viśadhvaṃ māmayonijām  |
sametāḥ putradāraiśca tataḥ siddhimavāpsyatha  || 13.24 ||
yasya yasya hi yā vāñchā tasya tāṃ tāṃ dadāmyaham  |
viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇamīśānamanyaṃ vā suramuttamam  || 13.25 ||
tatra sarvānnayiṣyāmi prasannā varadā hyaham  |
prāṇāyāmaparā bhūtvā māṃ viśadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ  || 13.26 ||
saha putraiśca dāraiśca tyaktvāśramapadāni ca  |
kālakṣepo na kartavyaḥ pralayo'yamupasthitaḥ  || 13.27 ||
saṃhāraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ kalpadāhaḥ sudāruṇaḥ  |
ekāhamabhavaṃ pūrvaṃ mahāghore janakṣaye  || 13.28 ||
śeṣā nadyaḥ samudrāśca sarva eva kṣayaṃgatāḥ  |
varadānānmaheśasya tenāhaṃ na kṣayaṃ gatā  || 13.29 ||
amṛtaḥ śāśvato devaḥ sthāṇurīśaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
sa pūjitaḥ prārthito vā kiṃ na dadyāddvijottamāḥ  || 13.30 ||
evamuktvā ṛṣīvrevā praviveśa jalaṃ tataḥ  |
karāttaśūlā sā devī vyālayajñopavītinī  || 13.31 ||
tataste tadvacaḥ śrutvā vismayāpannamānasāḥ  |
abhivandya ca māṃ sarve kṣāmayantaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 13.32 ||
kṣamyatāṃ no yaduktaṃ hi vasatāṃ tava saṃśraye  |
gṛhāṃstyaktvā mahābhāgāḥ saśiṣyāḥ sahabāndhavāḥ  || 13.33 ||
japtvā caikākṣaraṃ brahma hṛdi dhyātvā maheśvaram  |
snātvā ca mantrapūtābhiratha cādbhirjitavratāḥ  || 13.34 ||
viviśurnarmadātoyaṃ sapakṣā iva parvatāḥ  |
dyotayanto diśaḥ sarvāḥ kuśahastāḥ sahāgrayaḥ  || 13.35 ||
gateṣu teṣu rājendra ahamekaḥ sthitastadā  |
amareśaṃ samāsādya pūjayannarmadāṃ nadīm  || 13.36 ||
anubhūtāḥ saptakalpā māyūrādyā mayā nṛpa  |
prasādādvedhasaḥ sarve revayā saha bhārata  || 13.37 ||
janmato'dya dinaṃ yāvanna jāne'syāḥ purāsthitim  || 13.38 ||
iyaṃ hi śāṃkarī śaktiḥ kalā śambhorilāhvayā  |
narmadā duritadhvaṃsakāriṇī bhavatāriṇī  || 13.39 ||
yadāhamapi nābhūvaṃ purākalpeṣu pāṇḍava  |
caturdaśasu kalpeṣu teṣviyaṃ sukhasaṃsthitā  || 13.40 ||
caturdaśa purā kalpā na mṛtā yeṣu narmadā  |
tānahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi devī prāha yathā mama  || 13.41 ||
kāpilaṃ prathamaṃ viddhi prājāpatyaṃ dvitīyakam  |
brāhmaṃ saumyaṃ ca sāvitraṃ bārhaspatyaṃ prabhāsakam  || 13.42 ||
māhendramagnikalpaṃ ca jayantaṃ mārutaṃ tathā  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ bahurūpaṃ ca jyautiṣaṃ ca caturdaśam  || 13.43 ||
ete kalpā mayā khyātā na mṛtā yeṣu narmadā  |
māyūraṃ pañcadaśamaṃ kaurmaṃ caivātra ṣoḍaśam  || 13.44 ||
bakaṃ mātsyaṃ ca pādmaṃ ca vaṭakalpaṃ ca bhārata  |
ekaviṃśatimaṃ caitaṃ vārāhaṃ sāṃpratīnakam  || 13.45 ||
ime sapta mayā sākaṃ revayā pariśīlitāḥ  |
ekaviṃśatikalpāstu narmadāyāḥ śivāṅgataḥ  || 13.46 ||
saṃjātāyā nṛpaśreṣṭha mayā dṛṣṭā hyanekaśaḥ  |
kathitā nṛpatiśreṣṭha bhūyaḥ kiṃ kathayāmi te  || 13.47 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmya ekaviṃśatikalpakathānakavarṇanaṃ nāma trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 14

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
tatasta ṛṣayaḥ sarve mahābhāgāstapodhanāḥ  |
gatāstu paramaṃ lokaṃ tataḥ kiṃ jātamadbhutam  || 14.1 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatasteṣu prayāteṣu narmadātīravāsiṣu  |
babhūva raudrasaṃhāraḥ sarvabhūtakṣayaṃkaraḥ  || 14.2 ||
kailāsaśikharasthaṃ tu mahādevaṃ sanātanam  |
brahmādyāḥ prāstuvan devamṛgyajuḥsāmabhiḥ śivam  || 14.3 ||
saṃhara tvaṃ jagaddeva sadevāsuramānuṣam  |
prāpto yugasahasrāntaḥ kālaḥ saṃharaṇakṣamaḥ  || 14.4 ||
madrūpaṃ tu samāsthāya tvayā caitadvinirmitam  |
vaiṣṇavīṃ mūrtimāsthāya tvayaitatparipālitam  || 14.5 ||
ekā mūrtistridhā jātā brāhmī śaivī ca vaiṣṇavī  |
sṛṣṭisaṃhārarakṣārthaṃ bhavedevaṃ maheśvara  || 14.6 ||
etacchrutvā vacastathyaṃ viṣṇośca parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
sagaṇaḥ saparīvāraḥ saha tābhyāṃ sahomayā  || 14.7 ||
samalokānvibhidyemānbhagavānnīlalohitaḥ  |
bhūrādyabrahmalokāntaṃ bhittvāṇḍaṃ parataḥ param  || 14.8 ||
śaivaṃ padamajaṃ divyamāviśatsaha tairvibhuḥ  |
na tatra vāyurnākāśaṃ nāgnistatra na bhūtalam  || 14.9 ||
yatra saṃtiṣṭhe deva umayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
na sūryo na grahāstatra na ṛkṣāṇi diśastathā  || 14.10 ||
na lokapālā na sukhaṃ na ca duḥkhaṃ nṛpottama  || 14.11 ||
brāhmaṃ padaṃ yatkavayo vadanti śaivaṃ padaṃ yatkavayo vadanti  |
kṣetrajñamīśaṃ pravadanti cānye sāṃkhyāśca gāyanti kilādimokṣam  || 14.12 ||
yadbrahma ādyaṃ pravadanti kecidyaṃ sarvamīśānamajaṃ purāṇam  |
tamekarūpaṃ tamanekarūpamarūpamādyaṃ paramavyayākhyam  || 14.13 ||
avarṇamapyarthamanāmagotraṃ turyaṃ padaṃ yatkavayo vadanti  |
dhyānārthavijñānamayaṃ susūkṣmamātmasthamīśānavaraṃ vareṇyam  || 14.14 ||
tatastrayaste bhagavantamīśaṃ samprāpya saṃkṣipya bhavantyarthakam  |
pṛthaksvarūpaistu punasta eva jagatsamastaṃ paripālayanti  || 14.15 ||
saṃhāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ rudratve kurute prabhuḥ  |
viṣṇutve pālayellokānbrahmatve sṛṣṭikārakaḥ  || 14.16 ||
prakṛtyā saha saṃyuktaḥ kālo bhūtvā maheśvaraḥ  |
viśvarūpā mahābhāgā tasya pārśve vyavasthitā  || 14.17 ||
yāmāhuḥ prakṛtiṃ tajjñāḥ padārthānāṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ  |
puruṣatve prakṛtitve ca kāraṇaṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 14.18 ||
tasmādetajjagatsarvaṃ carācaram  |
tasminneva layaṃ yāti yugānte samupasthite  || 14.19 ||
bhagaliṅgāṅkitaṃ sarvaṃ vyāptaṃ vai parameṣṭhinā  |
bhagarūpo bhavedviṣṇurliṅgarūpo maheśvaraḥ  || 14.20 ||
bhāti sarveṣu lokeṣu gīyate bhūrbhuvādiṣu  |
praviṣṭaḥ sarvabhūteṣu tena viṣṇurbhagaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 14.21 ||
viśanādviṣṇurityuktaḥ sarvadevamayo mahān  |
bhāsanādgamanāccaiva bhagasaṃjñā prakīrtitā  || 14.22 ||
brahmādistambaparyantaṃ yasminneti layaṃ jagat |
ekabhāvaṃ samāpannaṃ liṅgaṃ tasmādvidurbudhāḥ  || 14.23 ||
mahādevastato devīmāha pārśve sthitāṃ tadā  |
saṃharasva jagatsarvaṃ mā vilambasva śobhane  || 14.24 ||
tyaja saumyamidaṃ rūpaṃ sitacandrāṃśunirmalam  |
rudraṃ rūpaṃ samāsthāya saṃharasva carācaram  || 14.25 ||
raudrairbhūtagaṇairghorairdevi tvaṃ parivāritā  |
jīvalokamimaṃ sarvaṃ bhakṣayasvāmbujekṣaṇe  || 14.26 ||
tato'haṃ mardayiṣyāmi plāvayiṣye tathā jagat |
kṛtvā caikārṇavaṃ bhūyaḥ sukhaṃ svapsye tvayā saha  || 14.27 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
nāhaṃ deva jagaccaitatsaṃharāmi mahādyute  |
ambā bhūtvā viceṣṭaṃ na bhakṣayāmi bhṛśāturam  || 14.28 ||
strīsvabhāvena kāruṇyaṃ karoti hṛdayaṃ mama  |
kathaṃ vai nirdahiṣyāmi jagadetajjagatpate  || 14.29 ||
tasmāttvaṃ svayamevedaṃ jagatsaṃhara śaṅkara  |
athaivamuktastāṃ devīṃ dhūrjaṭirnīlalohitaḥ  || 14.30 ||
kruddho nirbhartsayāmāsa huṅkāreṇa maheśvarīm  |
oṃ huṃphaṭtvaṃ sa ityāha kopāviṣṭairathekṣaṇaiḥ  || 14.31 ||
huṃkāritā viśālākṣī pīnorujaghanasthalā  |
tatkṣaṇāccābhavadraudrā kālarātrīva bhārata  || 14.32 ||
huṃkurvatī mahānādairnādayantī diśo daśa  |
vyavardhata mahāraudrā vidyutsaudāminī yathā  || 14.33 ||
vidyutsampātaduṣprekṣyā vidyutsaṃghātacañcalā  |
vidyujjvālākulā raudrā vidyudagninibhekṣaṇā  || 14.34 ||
muktakeśī viśālākṣī kṛśagrīvā kṛśodarī  |
vyāghracarmāmbaradharā vyālayajñopavītinī  || 14.35 ||
vṛścikairagnipuñjābhairgonasaiśca vibhūṣitā  |
trailokyaṃ pūrayāmāsa vistāreṇocchrayeṇa ca  || 14.36 ||
bhāsurāṅgā tu saṃvṛttā kṛṣṇasarpaikakuṇḍalā  |
citradaṇḍodyatakarā vyāghracarmopasevitā  || 14.37 ||
vyavardhata mahāraudrā jagatsaṃhārakāriṇī  |
sṛkkiṇī lelihānā ca krūraphūtkārakāriṇī  || 14.38 ||
vyāttāsyā ghurghurārāvā jagatsaṃkṣobhakāriṇī  |
kheladbhūtānugā krūrā niḥśvāsocchvāsakāriṇī  || 14.39 ||
jātāṭṭaahāsā durnāsā vahnikuṇḍasamekṣaṇā  |
prodyatkilakilārāvā dadāha sakalaṃ jagat || 14.40 ||
dahyamānāḥ surāstatra patanti dharaṇītale  |
patanti yakṣagandharvāḥ sakinnaramahoragāḥ  || 14.41 ||
patanti bhūtasaṅghāśca hāhāhaihaivirāviṇaḥ  |
bumbāpātaiḥ sanirghātairuditārtasvarairapi  || 14.42 ||
vyāptamāsīttadā viśvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
saṃpatadbhiḥ patadbhiśca jvaladbhūtagaṇairmahī  || 14.43 ||
jātaiścaṭacaṭāśabdaiḥ patadbhirgirisānubhiḥ  |
tatra raudrotsave jātā rudrānandavivardhinī  || 14.44 ||
vihiṃsamānā bhūtāni carvamāṇācarānapi  |
tattadgandhamupādāya śivārāvavirāviṇī  || 14.45 ||
galacchoṇitadhārābhimukhā digdhakalevarā  |
caṇḍaśīlābhavaccaṇḍī jagatsaṃhārakarmaṇi  || 14.46 ||
ye'pi prāptā maharlokaṃ bhṛgvādyāśca maharṣayaḥ  |
te'pi naśyanti śataśo brahmakṣattraviśādayaḥ  || 14.47 ||
devāsurā bhayatrastāḥ sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ  |
viśanti ke'pi pātālaṃ līyante ca guhādiṣu  || 14.48 ||
sā ca devī diśaḥ sarvā vyāpya mṛtyuriva sthitā  |
yugakṣayakare kāle devena viniyojitā  || 14.49 ||
ekāpi navadhā jātā daśadhā daśadhā tathā  |
catuḥṣaṣṭisvarūpā ca śatarūpāṭṭahāsinī  || 14.50 ||
jajñe sahasrarūpā ca lakṣakoṭitanuḥ śivā  |
nānārūpāyudhākārā nānāvādanacāriṇī  || 14.51 ||
evaṃrūpā'bhavaddevī śivasyānujñayā nṛpa  |
dikṣu sarvāsu gagane vikaṭāyudhaśīlinaḥ  || 14.52 ||
rundhanto naśyamānāṃstāngaṇā māheśvarāḥ sthitāḥ  |
vicaranti tayā sārddhaṃ śūlapaṭṭiśapāṇayaḥ  || 14.53 ||
tato mātṛgaṇāḥ kecidvināyakagaṇaiḥ saha  |
vyavardhanta mahāraudrā jagatsaṃhārakāriṇaḥ  || 14.54 ||
tatastasyā vyavardhanta daṃṣṭrāḥ kundendusannibhāḥ  |
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi ayutānyarbudāni ca  || 14.55 ||
daṃṣṭrāvaliḥ kararuhāḥ krūrāstīkṣṇāśca karkaśāḥ  |
viyaddiśo likhantyeva saptadvīpāṃ vasuṃdharām  || 14.56 ||
tasyā daṃṣṭrābhisampātaiścūrṇitā vanaparvatāḥ  |
śilāsaṃcayasaṃghātā viśīryate sahasraśaḥ  || 14.57 ||
himavānhemakūṭaśca niṣadho gandhamādanaḥ  |
mālyavāṃścaiva nīlaśca śvetaścaiva mahāgiriḥ  || 14.58 ||
merumadhyamilāpīṭhaṃ saptadvīpaṃ ca sārṇavam  |
