lokālokena sahitaṃ prākampata nṛpottama  || 14.59 ||
daṃṣṭrāśanivispṛṣṭāśca viśīryante mahādrumāḥ  |
utpātaiśca diśo vyāptā ghorarūpaiḥ samantataḥ  || 14.60 ||
tārā grahagaṇāḥ sarve ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ  |
śivāsahasrairākīrṇā mahāmātṛgaṇaistathā  || 14.61 ||
sā cacāra jagatkṛtsnaṃ yugānte samupasthite  |
bhramadbhiśca bruvadbhiśca krośadbhiśca samantataḥ  || 14.62 ||
pramathadbhirjvaladbhiśca raudrairvyāptā diśo daśa  |
vistīrṇaṃ śailasaṅghātaṃ vighūrṇitagiridrumam  || 14.63 ||
prabhinnagopuradvāraṃ keśaśuṣkāsthisaṃkulam  |
pradagdhagrāmanagaraṃ bhasmapuṃjābhisaṃvṛtam  || 14.64 ||
citādhūmākulaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
hāhākārākulaṃ sarvamahahasvananisvanam  || 14.65 ||
jagadetadabhūtsarvamaśaraṇyaṃ nirāśrayam  || 14.66 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye kalpānukathane kālarātrikṛtajagatsaṃharaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 15

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato mātṛsahasraiśca raudraiśca parivāritā  |
kālarātrirjagatsarvaṃ harate dīptalocanā  || 15.1 ||
tatastā mātaro ghorā brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikāḥ  |
vāyvindrānalakauberā yamatoyeśaśaktayaḥ  || 15.2 ||
skandakroḍanṛsiṃhānāṃ vicarantyo bhayānakāḥ  |
cakraśūlagadākhaḍgavajraśaktyṛṣṭipaṭṭiśaiḥ  || 15.3 ||
khaṭvāṅgairulmukairdīptairvyacaranmātaraḥ kṣaye  |
umāsaṃnoditā sarvāḥ pradhāvantyo diśo daśa  || 15.4 ||
tāsāṃ caraṇavikṣepairhuṅkārodgāranisvanaiḥ  |
trailokyametatsakalaṃ vipradagdhaṃ samantataḥ  || 15.5 ||
hāhāravākranditanisvanaiśca prabhinnarathyāgṛhagopuraiśca  |
babhūva ghorā dharaṇī samantātkapālakośākulakarburāṅgī  || 15.6 ||
yadetacchatasāhasraṃ jambūdvīpaṃ nigadyate  |
sarvameva taducchannaṃ samādhṛṣya nṛpottama  || 15.7 ||
jambuṃ śākaṃ kuśaṃ krauñcaṃ gomedaṃ śālmalistathā  |
puṣkaradvīpasahitā ye ca parvatavāsinaḥ  || 15.8 ||
te grastā mṛtyunā sarve bhūtairmātṛgaṇaistathā  |
mahāsurakapālaiśca māṃsamedovasotkaṭaiḥ  || 15.9 ||
rudhirodgāraśoṇāṅgī mahāmāyā subhīṣaṇā  |
pibantī rudhiraṃ tatra mahāmāṃsavasāpriyā  || 15.10 ||
kapālahastā vikaṭā bhakṣayantī surāsurān  |
nṛtyantī ca hasantī ca viparītā mahāravā  || 15.11 ||
trailokyasaṃtrāsakarī vidyutsaṃsphoṭahāsinī  |
saptadvīpasamudrāntāṃ bhakṣayitvā ca medinīm  || 15.12 ||
tataḥ svasthānamagamadyatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
narmadātīramāśrityāvasanmātṛgaṇaiḥ saha  || 15.13 ||
amarāṇāṃ kaṭe tuṅge nṛtyantī hasitānanā  |
amarā devatāḥ proktāḥ śarīraṃ kaṭamucyate  || 15.14 ||
taiḥ kaṭairāvṛto yasmātparvato'yaṃ nṛpottama  |
chinnabhinnāsthinikarairvasāmedo'sraviplutaiḥ  || 15.15 ||
amaraṃkaṭa ityevaṃ tena prokto manīṣibhiḥ  |
mahāpavitro lokeṣu śambhunā sa vinirmitaḥ  || 15.16 ||
nityaṃ saṃnihitastatra śaṅkaro hyumayā saha  |
tato'haṃ niyatastatra tasya pādāgrasaṃsthitaḥ  || 15.17 ||
prahvaḥ praṇatabhāvena staumi taṃ nīlalohitam  |
tatastālakasampātairgaṇairmātṛgaṇaiḥ saha  || 15.18 ||
saṃpranṛtyati saṃhṛṣṭo mṛtyunā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
khaṭvāṅgairulmukaiścaiva paṭṭiśaiḥ parighaistathā  || 15.19 ||
māṃsamedovasāhastā hṛṣṭā nṛtyanti saṃghaśaḥ  |
vāmanā jaṭilā muṇḍā lambagrīvoṣṭhamūrddhajāḥ  || 15.20 ||
mahāśiśnodarabhujā nṛtyanti ca hasanti ca  |
vikṛtairānanairghorairarbhujolbaṇamukhādibhiḥ  || 15.21 ||
amaraṃ kaṇṭakaṃ cakruḥ prāpte kālaviparyaye  |
teṣāṃ madhye mahāghoraṃ jagatsantrāsakāraṇam  || 15.22 ||
mṛtyuṃ paśyāmi nṛtyantaṃ taḍitpiṅgalamūrddhajam  |
tasya pārśve sthitāṃ devīṃ vimalāmbarabhūṣitām  || 15.23 ||
kuṇḍalodghuṣṭagaṇḍāṃ tāṃ nāgayajñopavītinīm  |
vicitrairupahāraiśca pūjayantīṃ maheśvaram  || 15.24 ||
apaśyaṃ narmadāṃ tatra mātaraṃ viśvavanditām  |
nānātaraṅgāṃ sāvartāṃ suvelārṇavasaṃnibhām  || 15.25 ||
mahāsaraḥsaritpātairadṛśyāṃ dṛśyarūpiṇīm  |
vandyamānāṃ suraiḥ siddhairmunisaṅghaiśca bhārata  || 15.26 ||
etasminnantare ghorāṃ saptasaptakasaṃjñitām  |
mahāvīcyaughaphenāḍhyāṃ kurvantīṃ sajalaṃ jagat || 15.27 ||
dṛṣṭavānnarmadāṃ devīṃ mṛgakṛṣṇāmbarāṃ punaḥ  |
sadhūmāśaninirhrādairvahantīṃ saptadhā tadā  || 15.28 ||
iti saṃhāramatulaṃ dṛṣṭavānrājasattama  |
naṣṭacandrārkakiraṇamabhūdetaccarācaram  || 15.29 ||
mahotpātasamudbhūtaṃ naṣṭanakṣatramaṇḍalam  |
alātacakravattūrṇamaśeṣaṃ bhrāmayaṃstataḥ  || 15.30 ||
vimānakoṭisaṃkīrṇaḥ sa kiṃnaramahoragaḥ  |
mahāvātaḥ sanirghāto yenākampaccarācaram  || 15.31 ||
rudravaktrātsamudbhūtaḥ saṃvarto nāma viśrutaḥ  |
vāyuḥ saṃśoṣayāmāsa vitatan saptasāgarān  || 15.32 ||
uddhūlitāṅgaḥ kapilākṣamūrddhajo jaṭākalāpairavabaddhamūrddhajaḥ  |
mahāravo dīptaviśālaśūladhṛksa pātu yuṣmāṃśca dine dine haraḥ  || 15.33 ||
śūlī dhanuṣmānkavacī kirīṭī śmaśānabhasmokṣitasarvagātraḥ  |
kapālamālākulakaṇṭhanālo mahāhisūtrairavabaddhamauliḥ  || 15.34 ||
sa gonasaughaiḥ pariveṣṭitāṅgo viṣāgnicandrāmarasindhumauliḥ  |
pinākakhaṇṭūvāṅgakarālapāṇiḥ sa kṛttivāsā ḍamarupraṇādaḥ  || 15.35 ||
sa saptalokāntaraniḥsṛtātmā mahabhujāveṣṭitasarvagātraḥ  |
netreṇa sūryodayasannibhena pravālakāṅkūranibhodareṇa  || 15.36 ||
sandhyābhraraktotpalapadmarāgasindūravidyutprakarāruṇena  |
tatena liṅgena ca locanena cikrīḍamānaḥ sa yugāntakāle  || 15.37 ||
hiraṇmayenaiva samutsṛjan sa daṇḍena yadvadbhagavān sameruḥ  |
pādāgravikṣepaviśīrṇaśailaḥ kurvañjagatso'pi jagāma tatra  || 15.38 ||
saṃhartukāmastridivaṃ tvaśeṣaṃ pramuñcamāno vikṛtāṭṭahāsam  |
jahāra sarvaṃ tridivaṃ mahātmā saṃkṣobhayanvai jagadīśa ekaḥ  || 15.39 ||
taṃ devamīśānamajaṃ vareṇyaṃ dṛṣṭvā jagatsaṃharaṇaṃ maheśam  |
sā kālarātriḥ saha mātṛbhiśca gaṇāśca sarve śivamarcayanti  || 15.40 ||
nandī ca bhṛṅgī ca gaṇādayaśca taṃ sarvabhūtaṃ praṇamanti devam  |
jāgadvaraṃ sarvajanasya kāraṇaṃ haraṃ smarārātimaharniśaṃ te  || 15.41 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye sṛṣṭisaṃharaṇasaṃrambhavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 16

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
samātṛbhirbhūtagaṇaśca ghorairvṛtaḥ samantātsa nanarta śūlī  |
gajendracarmāvaraṇe vasānaḥ saṃhartukāmaśca jagatsamastam  || 16.1 ||
maheśvaraḥ sarvasureśvarāṇāṃ mantrairanekekhabaddhamālī  |
medovasāraktavicarcitāṅgastrailokyadāhe praṇanarta śambhuḥ  || 16.2 ||
sa kālarātryā sahito mahātmā kāle trilokīṃ sakalāṃ jahāra  |
saṃvartakākhyaḥ sahabhānubhāvaḥ śambhurmahātmā jagato variṣṭhaḥ  || 16.3 ||
sa visphuliṅgotkaradhūmamiśraṃ maholkavajrāśanivātatulyam  |
tato'ṭṭahāsaṃ pramumoca ghoraṃ vivṛtya vaktraṃ vaḍavāmukhābham  || 16.4 ||
sahasravajrāśanisaṃnibhena tenāṭṭahāsena harodgatena  |
āpūritāstatra diśo daśaiva saṃkṣobhitāḥ sarvamahārṇavāśca  || 16.5 ||
sa brahmalokaṃ prajagāma śabdo brahmāṇḍabhāṇḍaṃ pracacāla sarvam  |
kimetadityākulacetanāste vitrastarūpā ṛṣayo babhūvuḥ  || 16.6 ||
praṇamya sarve sahasaiva bhītā brahmāṇamūcuḥ parameśvareśam  |
bhītāśca sarve ṛṣayastataste surāsuraiścaiva mahoragaiśca  || 16.7 ||
vidyutprabhābhāsurabhīṣaṇāṅgaḥ ka eṣa cikrīḍati bhūtalasthaḥ  |
kālānalaṃ gātramidaṃ dadhāno yasyāṭṭahāsena jagadvimūḍham  || 16.8 ||
vitrastarūpaṃ prababhau kṣaṇena saṃhartumicchetkimayaṃ trilokīm  |
sārdhaṃ tvayā saptabhirarṇakaiśca janastapaḥ satyamabhiprayāti  || 16.9 ||
saṃhartukāmo hi ka eṣa deva etatsamastaṃ kathayāprameya  |
na dṛṣṭametadviṣamaṃ kadāpi jānāsi tattvaṃ paramo mato naḥ  || 16.10 ||
niśamya tadvākyamathābabhāṣe brahmā samāśvāsya surādisaṅghān  || 16.11 ||

śrībrahmovāca -
sa eṣa kālastridivaṃ tvaśeṣaṃ saṃhartukāmo jagadakṣayātmā  |
pūrṇe ca śete parivatsarāṇāṃ bhaviṣyatīśānavibhurna citram  || 16.12 ||
saṃvatsaro'yaṃ parivatsaraśca udvatsaro vatsara eṣa devaḥ  |
dṛṣṭo'pyadṛṣṭaḥ prahutaḥ prakāśī sthūlaśca sūkṣmaḥ paramāṇureṣaḥ  || 16.13 ||
nātaḥ paraṃ kiṃcidihāsti loke parāparo'yaṃ prabhurātmavādī  |
tuṣyeta me kālasamānarūpa ityevamuktvā bhagavānsureśaḥ  || 16.14 ||
sanatkumārapramukhaiḥ sametaḥ saṃtoṣayāmāsa tato yatātmā  || 16.15 ||

brahmovāca -
namo'stu sarvāya suśāntamūrtāye hyaghorarūpāya namonamaste  |
sarvātmane sarva namonamaste mahātmane bhūtapate namaste  || 16.16 ||
oṅkāra huṅkārapariṣkṛtāya svadhāvaṣaṭkāra namonamaste  |
guṇatrayeśāya maheśvarāya te trayīmayāya triguṇātmane namaḥ  || 16.17 ||
tvaṃ śaṅkaratvaṃ hi maheśvaro'si pradhānamagryaṃ tvamasi praviṣṭaḥ  |
tvaṃ viṣṇurīśaḥ prapitāmahaśca tvaṃ saptajihvastvamanantajihvaḥ  || 16.18 ||
sraṣṭāsi sṛṣṭiśca vibho tvameva viśvasya vedyaṃ ca paraṃ nidhānam  |
āhurdvijā vedavido vareṇyaṃ parātparastvaṃ parataḥ paro'si  || 16.19 ||
sūkṣmātisūkṣmaṃ pravadanti yacca vāco nivartanti mano yataśca  || 16.20 ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca -
tvayā stuto'haṃ vividhaiśca mantraiḥ puṣṇāmi śāntiṃ tava padmayone  |
īkṣasva māṃ lokamimaṃ jvalantaṃ vaktrairanekaiḥ prasabhaṃ harantam  || 16.21 ||
evamuktvā sa deveśo devyā saha jagatpatiḥ  |
pitāmahaṃ samāśvāsya tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 16.22 ||
idaṃ mahatpuṇyatamaṃ variṣṭhaṃ stotraṃ niśamyeha gatiṃ labhante  |
pāpairanekaiḥ pariveṣṭitā ye prayānti rudraṃ vimalairvimānaiḥ  || 16.23 ||
bhayaṃ ca teṣāṃ na bhavetkadācitpaṭhanti ye tāta idaṃ dvijāgryāḥ  |
saṅgrāmacaurāgnivane tathābdhau teṣāṃ śivastrāti na saṃśayo'tra  || 16.24 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye brahmakṛtaśivastutivarṇanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 17

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ saṃstūyamānastu brahmādyairmunipuṃgavaiḥ  |
brahmalokagataistatra saṃjahāra jagatprabhuḥ  || 17.1 ||
sa tadbhīmaṃ mahāraudraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ vaktramavyayam  |
mahādaṃṣṭrotkaṭārāvaṃ pātālatalasaṃnibham  || 17.2 ||
vidyujjvalanapiṅgākṣaṃ bhairavaṃ lomaharṣaṇam  |
mahājihvaṃ mahādaṃṣṭraṃ mahāsarpaśirodharam  || 17.3 ||
mahāsuraśiromālaṃ mahāpralayakāraṇam  |
grasatsamudranihitavātavārimayaṃ haviḥ  || 17.4 ||
vaḍavāmukhasaṅkāśaṃ mahādevasya tanmukham  |
jihvāgreṇa jagatsarvaṃ lelihānamapaśyata  || 17.5 ||
yojanānāṃ sahasrāṇi sahasrāṇāṃ śatāni ca  |
diśo daśa mahāghorā māṃsamedovasotkaṭāḥ  || 17.6 ||
tasya daṃṣṭrā vyavardhata śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  |
sāsurānsuragandharvān sayakṣoragarākṣasān  || 17.7 ||
tasya daṃṣṭrāgrasaṃlagnānsa dadarśa pitāmahaḥ  |
dantayantrāntasaṃviṣṭaṃ vicūrṇitaśirodharam  || 17.8 ||
jagatpaśyāmi rājendra viśantaṃ vyādite mukhe  |
nānātaraṅgabhaṅgāṅgā mahāphenaughasaṃkulāḥ  |
yathā nadyo layaṃ yānti samudraṃ prāpya sasvanāḥ  || 17.9 ||
tathā tataṃ viśvamidaṃ samastamanekajīvārṇavadurvigāhyam  |
viveśa rudrasya mukhaṃ viśālaṃ jvalattadugraṃ ghananādaghoram  || 17.10 ||
jvālāstatastasya mukhātsughorāḥ savisphuliṅgā bahulāḥ sadhūmāḥ  |
anekarūpā jvalanaprakāśāḥ pradīpayantīva diśo'khilāśca  || 17.11 ||
tato ravijvālasahasramāli babhūva vaktraṃ calajihvadaṃṣṭram  |
maheśvarasyādbhutarūpiṇastadā sa dvādaśātmā prababhūva ekaḥ  || 17.12 ||
tataste dvādaśādityā rudravaktrādvinirgatāḥ  |
āśritya dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ nirdahanto vasuṃdharām  || 17.13 ||
bhaumaṃ yajjīvanaṃ kiṃcinnānāvṛkṣatṛṇālayam  |
śuṣkaṃ pūrvamanāvṛṣṭyā sakalākulabhūtalam  || 17.14 ||
taddīpyamānaṃ sahasā sūryaistai rudrasambhavaiḥ  |
dhūmākulamabhūtsarvaṃ praṇaṣṭagrahatārakam  || 17.15 ||
jajvāla sahasā dīptaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamaśeṣataḥ  |
jvālāmālākulaṃ sarvamabhūdetaccarācaram  || 17.16 ||
saptadvīpasamudreṣu saritsu ca sarassu ca agniratti jagatsarvamājyāhutimivādhvare  || 17.17 ||
viśālatejasā dīptā mahājvālāsamākulāḥ  |
dadahurvai jagatsarvamādityā rudrasambhavāḥ  || 17.18 ||
ādityānāṃ raśmayaśca saṃspṛṣṭā vai parasparam  |
evaṃ dadāha bhagavāṃstrailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 17.19 ||
saptadvīpapramāṇastu so'gnirbhūtvā maheśvaraḥ  |
saptadvīpasamudrāntāṃ nirdadāha vasuṃdharām  || 17.20 ||
sumerumandarāntāṃ ca nirdahurvasudhāṃ tadā  |
bhittvā tu saptapātālaṃ nāgalokaṃ tato'dahat || 17.21 ||
bhūmyadhaḥ saptapātālānnirdahaṃstārakaiḥ saha  |
cacārāgniḥ samantāttu nirdahanvai yudhiṣṭhira  || 17.22 ||
dhamyamāna ivāṅgārairloharātririva jvalan  |
tathā tatprājvalatsarvaṃ saṃvartāgnipradīpitam  || 17.23 ||
nirvṛkṣā nistṛṇā bhūmirnirnirjharasaraḥ sarit |
viśīrṇaśailaśṛṅgaughā kūrmapṛṣṭhopamābhavat || 17.24 ||
jvālāmālākulaṃ kṛtvā jagatsarvaṃ cidāmakam  |
mahārūpadharo rudro vyatiṣṭhata maheśvaraḥ  || 17.25 ||
samātṛgaṇabhūyiṣṭhā sayakṣoragarākṣasā  |
tato devī mahādevaṃ viveśa harilocanā  || 17.26 ||
nirvāṇaṃ paramāpannā śānteva śikhinaḥ śikhā  |
jagatsarvaṃ hi nirdagdhaṃ tribhirlokaiḥ sahānagha  || 17.27 ||
rudraprasādānmuktvā māṃ narmadāṃ cāpyayonijām  |
yugānāmayutaṃ devo mayā cādya bubhakṣaṇāt || 17.28 ||
purā hyārādhitaḥ śūlī tenāhamajarāmaraḥ  |
aghamarṣaṇaghoraṃ ca vāmadevaṃ ca tryambakam  || 17.29 ||
ṛṣabhaṃ trisuparṇaṃ ca durgāṃ sāvitrameva ca  |
bṛhadāraṇyakaṃ caiva bṛhatsāma tathottaram  || 17.30 ||
raudrīṃ paramagāyatrīṃ śivopaniṣadaṃ tathā  |
yathā pratirathaṃ sūktaṃ japtvā mṛtyuṃjayaṃ tathā  || 17.31 ||
saritsāgaraparyantā vasudhā bhasmasātkṛtā  |
varjayitvā mahābhāgāṃ narmadāmamṛtopamām  || 17.32 ||
mahendro malayaḥ sahyo hemakūṭo'tha mālyavān  |
vindhyaśca pāriyātraśca saptaite kulaparvatāḥ  || 17.33 ||
dvādaśādityanirdagdhāḥ śailāḥ śīrṇaśilāḥ pṛthak |
bhasmībhūtāstu dṛśyante na naṣṭā narmadā tadā  || 17.34 ||
himavānhemakūṭaśca niṣadho gandhamādanaḥ  |
mālyavāṃśca giriśreṣṭho nīlaḥ śveto'tha śṛṅgavān  || 17.35 ||
ete parvatarā jāno devagandharvasevitāḥ  |
yugāntāgnivinirdagdhāḥ sarve śīrṇamahāśilāḥ  || 17.36 ||
evaṃ mayā purā dṛṣṭo yugānte sarvasaṃkṣayaḥ  |
varjayitvā mahāpuṇyāṃ narmadāṃ nṛpasattama  || 17.37 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīnarmadāmāhātmye dvādaśādityarūpeṇa jagatsaṃharaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 18

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nirdagdhe'smiṃstato loke sūryairīśvarasambhavaiḥ  |
saptabhiścārṇavaiḥ śuṣkairdvīpaiḥ saptabhireva ca  || 18.1 ||
tato mukhāttasya ghanā maholbaṇā niścerurindrāyudhatulyarūpāḥ  |
ghorāḥ payodā jagadandhakāraṃ kurvanta īśānavaraprayuktāḥ  || 18.2 ||
nīlotpalābhāḥ kvacidaṃjanābhā gokṣīrakundendunibhāśca kecit |
mayūracandrākṛtayastathā'nye kecidvidhūmānalasaprabhāśca  || 18.3 ||
kecinmahāparvatakalparūpāḥ kecinmahāmīnakulopamāśca  |
kecidgajendrākṛtayaḥ surūpāḥ kecinmahākūṭanibhāḥ payodāḥ  || 18.4 ||
calattaraṅgormisamānarūpā mahāpurodhānanibhāśca kecit |
sagopurāṭṭālakasaṃnikāśāḥ savidyudulkāśanimaṇḍitāntāḥ  || 18.5 ||
samāvṛtāṅgaḥ sa babhūva devaḥ saṃvartakonāma gaṇaḥ sa raudraḥ  |
pravarṣamāṇo jagadapramāṇamekārṇavaṃ sarvamidaṃ cakāra  || 18.6 ||
tato mahāmeghavivardhamānamīśānamindrāśanibhirvṛtāṅgam  |
dadarśa nāhaṃ bhayavihvalāṅgo gaṅgājalaughaiśca samāvṛtāṅgaḥ  || 18.7 ||
gajāḥ punaścaiva punaḥ pibanto jagatsamantātparidahyamānam  |
āpūritaṃ caiva jagatsamantātsarvaiśca tairjagmuradarśanaṃ ca te  || 18.8 ||
mahārṇavāḥ sapta sarāṃsi dvīpā nadyo'tha sarvā atha bhūrbhuvaśca  |
āpūryamāṇāḥ salilaughajālairekārṇavaṃ sarvamidaṃ babhūva  || 18.9 ||
na dṛśyate kiṃcidaho carācaraṃ niragnicandrārkamaye'pi loke  |
praṇaṣṭanakṣatratamo'ndhakāre praśāntavātāstamitaikanīḍeḥ  || 18.10 ||
mahājalaughe'sya viśuddhasattvā stutirmayā bhūpa kṛtā tadānīm  |
tato'hamityeva vicintayānaḥ śaraṇyamekaṃ kva nu yāmi śāntam  || 18.11 ||
smarāmi devaṃ hṛdi cintayitvā prabhuṃ śaraṇyaṃ jalasaṃniviṣṭaḥ  |
namāmi devaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapadye dhyānaṃ ca tasyeti kṛtaṃ mayā ca  || 18.12 ||
dhyātvā tato'haṃ salilaṃ tatāra tasya prasādādavimūḍhacetāḥ  |
glāniḥ śramaścaiva mama praṇaṣṭau devyāḥ prasādena narendraputra  || 18.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 19

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatastvekārṇave tasminmumūrṣurahamāturaḥ  |
kākūcchvāsastaraṃstoyaṃ bāhubhyāṃ nṛpasattama  || 19.1 ||
śṛṇomyarṇavamadhyastho niḥśabdastimite tadā  |
ambhoravamanaupamyaṃ diśo daśa vinādinam  || 19.2 ||
haṃsakudendusaṃkāśāṃ hāragokṣīrapāṇḍurām  |
nānāratnavicitrāṅgīṃ svarṇaśṛṅgāṃ manoramām  || 19.3 ||
suraiḥ pravālakamayairlāṅguladhvajaśobhitām  |
pralambaghoṇāṃ nardantīṃ khurairarṇavagāhinīm  || 19.4 ||
gāṃ dadarśāhamudvigno māmevābhimukhīṃ sthitām  |
kiṃkiṇījālamuktābhiḥ svarṇaghaṇṭāsamāvṛtām  || 19.5 ||
tasyāścaraṇavikṣepaiḥ sarvamekārṇavaṃ jalam  |
vikṣiptaphenapuñjaughairnṛtyantīva samaṃ tataḥ  || 19.6 ||
rarāsa salilotkṣepaiḥ kṣobhayantī mahārṇavam  |
sā māmāha mahābhāga ślakṣṇagambhīrayā girā  || 19.7 ||
mā bhaiṣīrvatsa vatseti mṛtyustava na vidyate  |
mahādevaprasādena na mṛtyuste mamāpi ca  || 19.8 ||
mamāśrayasva lāṅgūlaṃ tvāmatastārayāmyaham  |
ghorādasmādbhayādvipra yāvatsaṃplavate jagat || 19.9 ||
kṣuttṛṣāpratighātārthaṃ stanau me tvaṃ pibasva ha  |
payo'mṛtāśrayaṃ divyaṃ tatpītvā nirvṛto bhava  || 19.10 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā harṣātpīto mayā stanaḥ  |
na kṣuttṛṣā pītamātre stane mahyaṃ tadābhavat || 19.11 ||
divyaṃ prāṇabalaṃ jajñe samudraplavanakṣamam  |
tatastāṃ pratyuvācedaṃ kā tvamekārṇavīkṛte  || 19.12 ||
bhramase brūhi tattvena vismayo me mahānhṛdi  |
bhramato'tra mamārtasya mumūrṣoḥ prahatasya  || 19.13 ||
tvaṃ hi me śaraṇaṃ jātā bhāgyaśeṣeṇa suvrate  || 19.14 ||

gauruvāca -
kimahaṃ vismṛtā tubhyaṃ viśvarūpā maheśvarī  |
narmadā dharmadā nḥṇāṃ svargaśarmabalapradā  || 19.15 ||
dṛṣṭvā tvāṃ sīdamānaṃ tu rudreṇāhaṃ visarjitā  |
taṃ dvijaṃ tārayasvārye mā prāṇāṃstyajatāṃ jale  || 19.16 ||
gorūpeṇa vibhorvākyāttvatsakāśamihāgatā  |
mā mṛṣāvacanaḥ śambhurbhavediti ca satvarā  || 19.17 ||
evamuktastayāhaṃ tu indrāyudhanibhaṃ śubham  |
lāṅgūlamavyayaṃ jñātvā bhujābhyāmavalambitaḥ  || 19.18 ||
tato'taraṃ taṃ jaladhiṃ lāṅgūladhvajamāśritaḥ  |
asau devo mahādeva iti māṃ pratyabhāṣata  || 19.19 ||
tato yugasahasrāntamahaṃ kālaṃ tayā saha  |
vyacaraṃ vai tamobhūte sarvataḥ salilāvṛte  || 19.20 ||
mahārṇave tatastasmin bhramangoḥ pucchamāśritaḥ  |
nirvāte cāndhakāre ca nirāloke nirāmaye  || 19.21 ||
akasmātsalile tasminnatasīpuṣpasannibham  |
vibhinnāṃjanasaṅkāśamākāśamiva nirmalam  || 19.22 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ pītavāsasamavyayam  |
kirīṭenārkavarṇena vidyudvidyotakāriṇā  || 19.23 ||
bhrājamānena śirasā khamivātyantarūpiṇam  |
kuṇḍaloddhaṣṭagallaṃ tu hāroddyotitavakṣasam  || 19.24 ||
jāmbūnadamayairdivyairbhūṣaṇairupaśobhitam  |
nāgopadhānaśayanaṃ sahasrādityavarcasam  || 19.25 ||
anekabāhūrudharaṃ naikavaktraṃ manoramam  |
suptamekārṇave vīraṃ sahasrākṣaśirodharam  || 19.26 ||
jaṭājūṭena mahatā sphuradvidyutsamārciṣā  |
ekārṇavaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vyāpya devaṃ vyavasthitam  || 19.27 ||
grasitvā śaṅkaraḥ sarvaṃ sadevāsuramānavam  |
prapaśyāmyahamīśānaṃ suptamekārṇave prabhum  || 19.28 ||
sarvavyāpinamavyaktamanantaṃ viśvatomukham  |
tasya pādatalābhyāśe svarṇakeyūramaṇḍitām  || 19.29 ||
viśvarūpāṃ mahābhāgāṃ viśvamāyāvadhāriṇīm  |
śrīmayīṃ hrīmayīṃ devīṃ dhīmayīṃ vāṅmayīṃ śivām  || 19.30 ||
siddhiṃ kīrtiṃ ratiṃ brāhmīṃ kālarātrimayonijām  |
tāmevāhaṃ tadātyantamīśvarāntikamāsthitām  || 19.31 ||
adrākṣaṃ candravadanāṃ dhṛtiṃ sarveśvarīmumām  || 19.32 ||
śāntaṃ prasuptaṃ navahemavarṇamumāsahāyaṃ bhagavantamīśam  |
tamovṛtaṃ puṇyatamaṃ vīraṣṭhaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskaromi  || 19.33 ||
tataḥ prasuptaḥ sahasā vibuddho rātrikṣaye devavaraḥ svabhāvāt |
vikṣobhayan bāhubhirarṇavāmbho jagatpraṇaṣṭaṃ salile vimṛśya  || 19.34 ||
kiṃ kāryamityeva vicintayitvā vārāharūpo'bhavadadbhutāṅgaḥ  |
mahāghanāmbhodharatulyavarcāḥ pralambamālāmbaraniṣkamālī  || 19.35 ||
saśaṅkhacakrāsidharaḥ kirīṭī savedavedāṅgamayo mahātmā  |
trailokyanirmāṇakaraḥ purāṇo devatrayīrūpadharaśca kārye  || 19.36 ||
sa eva rudraḥ sa jagajjahāra sṛṣṭyarthamīśaḥ prapitāmaho'bhūt |
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jagataḥ sa eva hariḥ sucakrāsigadābjapāṇiḥ  || 19.37 ||
teṣāṃ vibhāgo na hi kartumarho mahātmanāmekaśarīrabhājām  |
mīmāṃsāhetvarthaviśeṣatarkairyasteṣu kuryātpravibhedamajñaḥ  || 19.38 ||
sa yāti ghoraṃ narakaṃ krameṇa vibhāgakṛddveṣamatirdurātmā  |
yā yasya bhaktiḥ sa tayaiva nūnaṃ dehaṃ tyajan svaṃ hyamṛtatvameti  || 19.39 ||
saṃmohayanmūrtibhiratra lokaṃ sraṣṭā ca goptā kṣayakṛtsa devaḥ  |
tasmānna mohātmakamāviśeta dveṣaṃ na kuryātpravibhinnamūrtiḥ  || 19.40 ||
vārāhamīśānavaro'pyato'sau rūpaṃ samāsthāya jagadvidhātā  |
naṣṭe triloke'rṇavatoyamagne vimārgitoyaughamaye'ntarātmā  || 19.41 ||
bhittvārṇavaṃ toyamathāntarasthaṃ viveśa pātālatalaṃ kṣaṇena  |
jale nimagnāṃ dharaṇīṃ samastāṃ samaspṛśatpaṅkajapatranetrām  || 19.42 ||
viśīrṇaśailopalaśṛṅgakūṭāṃ vasuṃdharāṃ tāṃ pralaye pralīnām  |
daṃṣṭraikayā viṣṇuratulyasāhasaḥ samuddadhāra svayameva devaḥ  || 19.43 ||
sā tasya daṃṣṭrāgravilambitāṅgī kailāsaśṛṅgāgragateva jyotsnā  |
vibhrājate sāpyasamānamūrtiḥ śaśāṅkaśṛṅge ca taḍidvilagnā  || 19.44 ||
tāmujjahārārṇavatoyamagnāṃ karī nimagnāmiva hastinīṃ haṭhāt |
nāvaṃ viśīrṇāmiva toyamadhyādudīrṇasattvo'nupamaprabhāvaḥ  || 19.45 ||
sa tāṃ samuttārya mahājalaughātsamudramāryo vyabhajatsamastam  |
mahārṇaveṣveva mahārṇavāmbho nikṣepayāmāsa punarnadīṣu  || 19.46 ||
śīrṇāṃśca śailānsa cakāra bhūyo dvīpānsamastāṃśca tathārṇavāṃśca  |
śailopalairye vicitāḥ samantācchiloccayāṃstānsa cakāra kalpe  || 19.47 ||
anekarūpaṃ pravibhajya dehaṃ cakāra devendragaṇānsamastān  |
mukhācca vahnirmanasaśca candraścakṣośca sūryaḥ sahasā babhūva  || 19.48 ||
jajñe'tha tasyeśvarayogamūrteḥ pradhyāyamānasya surendrasaṅghaḥ  |
vedāśca yajñāśca tathaiva varṇāstathā hi sarvauṣadhayo rasāśca  || 19.49 ||
jagatsamastaṃ manasā babhūva yatsthāvaraṃ kiṃcidihāṇḍajaṃ vā  |
jarāyujaṃ svedajamudbhijjaṃ vā yatkiṃcidā kīṭapipīlakādyam  || 19.50 ||
tato vijajñe manasā kṣaṇena anekarūpāḥ sahasā maheśā  |
cakāra yanmūrtibhiravyayātmā aṣṭābhirāviśya punaḥ sa tatra  || 19.51 ||
līlāṃ cakārātha samṛddhatejā ato'tra me paśyata eva viprāḥ  |
teṣāṃ mayā darśanameva sarvaṃ yāvanmuhūrtātsamakāri bhūpa  || 19.52 ||
kṛtvā tvaśeṣaṃ kila līlayaiva sa devadevo jagatāṃ vidhātā  |
sarvatradṛksarvaga eva devo jagāma cādarśanamādikartā  || 19.53 ||
yattanmuhūrtādiha nāmarūpaṃ tāvatprapaśyāmi jagattathaiva  |
dvīpaiḥ samudrairabhisaṃvṛtaṃ hi nakṣatratārādivimānakīrṇam  || 19.54 ||
viyatpayodagrahacakracitraṃ nānāvidhaiḥ prāṇigaṇairvṛtaṃ ca  |
tāṃ vai na paśyāmi mahānubhāvāṃ gorūpiṇīṃ sarvasureśvarīṃ ca  || 19.55 ||
kva sāṃprataṃ seti vicintya rājanvibhrāntacittastvabhavaṃ tadaiva  |
diśo vibhāgānavalokayānṛte punastāṃ kathamīśvarāṅgīm  || 19.56 ||
paśyāmi tāmatra punaśca śubhrāṃ mahābhranīlāṃ śuciśubhratoyām  |
vṛkṣairanekairupaśobhitāṅgīṃ gajaisturaṅgairvihagairvṛtāṃ ca  || 19.57 ||
yathā purātīramupetya devyāḥ samāsthitaścāpyamarakaṇṭake tu  |
tathaiva paśyāmi sukhopaviṣṭa ātmānamavyagramavāptasaukhyam  || 19.58 ||
tathaiva puṇyā malatoyavāhāṃ dṛṣṭvā punaḥ kalpaparikṣaye'pi  |
ambāmivāryāmanukampamānāmakṣīṇatoyāṃ virujāṃ viśokaḥ  || 19.59 ||
evaṃ mahatpuṇyatamaṃ ca kalpaṃ paṭhanti śṛṇvanti ca ye dvijendrāḥ  |
mahāvarāhasya maheśvarasya dine dine te vimalā bhavanti  || 19.60 ||
aśubhaśatasahasraṃ te vidhūya prapannāstridivamamarajuṣṭaṃ siddhagandharvayuktam  |
vimalaśaśinibhābhiḥ sarva evāpsarobhiḥ saha vividhavilāsaiḥ svargasaukhyaṃ labhante  || 19.61 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīnarmadāmāhātmye vārāhakalpavṛttāntavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 20

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
śrutā me vividhā dharmāḥ saṃhārāstvatprasādataḥ  |
kṛtā devena sarveṇa ye ca dṛṣṭāstvayānagha  || 20.1 ||
sāmprataṃ śrotumicchāmi prabhāvaṃ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ  |
tvayānubhūtaṃ viprendra tanme tvaṃ vaktumarhasi  || 20.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi prajāsaṃhāralakṣaṇam  |
yaccihnaṃ dṛśyate tatra yathā kalpo vidhīyate  || 20.3 ||
ulkāpātāḥ sanirghātā bhūmikampastathaiva ca  |
patate pāṃśuvarṣaṃ ca nirghoṣaścaiva dāruṇaḥ  || 20.4 ||
yakṣakinnaragandharvāḥ piśācoragarākṣasāḥ  |
sarve te pralayaṃ yānti yugānte samupasthite  || 20.5 ||
parvatāḥ sāgarā nadyaḥ sarāṃsi vividhāni ca  |
vṛkṣāḥ śeṣaṃ samāyānti vallījātaṃ tṛṇāni ca  || 20.6 ||
evaṃ hi vyākulībhūte sarvauṣadhijalojjhite  |
kāṣṭhabhūte tu saṃjāte trailokye sacarācare  || 20.7 ||
yāvatpaśyāmi madhyāhne snānakāla upasthite  |
trailokyaṃ jvalanākāraṃ durnirīkṣaṃ durāsadam  || 20.8 ||
dvau sūryau pūrvatastāta paścimottarayostathā  |
tathaiva dakṣiṇe dvau ca sūryau dṛṣṭau pratāpinau  || 20.9 ||
dvau sūryau nāgalokasthau madhye dvau gaganasya ca  |
ityete dvādaśādityāstapante sarvato diśam  || 20.10 ||
pṛthivīmadahansarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
nādagdhaṃ dṛśyate kiṃcidṛte revāṃ ca māṃ tathā  || 20.11 ||
pṛthivyāṃ dahyamānāyāṃ havirgandhaśca jāyate  |
tato me śuṣyate gātraṃ tṛṣāpyevaṃ durāsadā  || 20.12 ||
na hi vindāmi pānīyaṃ śoṣitaṃ ca divākaraiḥ  |
yāvatkamaṇḍaluṃ vīkṣe śuṣkaṃ tatrāpi tajjalam  || 20.13 ||
tato'haṃ śokasaṃtapto viśeṣātkṣuttṛṣārditaḥ  |
utpapāta kṣiterūrdhvaṃ paśyamāno divaṃ prati  || 20.14 ||
tāvatpaśyāmi gagane gṛhaṃ śṛṅgārabhūṣitam  |
tatastajjñātukāmo'haṃ prasthito rājasattama  || 20.15 ||
prākāreṇa vicitreṇa kapāṭārgalabhūṣitam  |
vicitraśikharopetaṃ dvāradeśamupāgataḥ  || 20.16 ||
ṣaḍaśītisahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ samucchraye  |
tadardhaṃ tu pṛthaktvena kāñcanaṃ ratnabhūṣitam  || 20.17 ||
tatra madhye parāṃ śayyāṃ paśyāmi nṛpasattama  |
śayyopari śayānaṃ tu puruṣaṃ divyamūrdhajam  || 20.18 ||
vikuñcitāgrakeśāntaṃ samastaṃ yojanāyatam  |
mukuṭena vicitreṇa dīptikāntena śobhitam  || 20.19 ||
śyāmaṃ kamalapatrābhaṃ suprabhaṃ ca sunāsikam  |
siṃhāsyamāyatabhujaṃ gallaśmaśruvarāṅkitam  || 20.20 ||
trivalībhaṅgasubhagaṃ karṇakuṇḍalabhūṣitam  |
viśālābhaṃ supīnāṅgaṃ pārśvasvāvartabhūṣitam  || 20.21 ||
śobhitaṃ kaṭibhāgena vibhaktaṃ jānujaṅghayoḥ  |
padmāṅkitatalaṃ devamātāmrasunakhāṅgulim  || 20.22 ||
meghanādasugambhīraṃ sarvāvayavasundaram  |
śayyāmadhyagataṃ devamapaśyaṃ puruṣottamam  || 20.23 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiṃ śayānaṃ dakṣiṇena tu  |
akṣasūtrodyatakaraṃ sūryāyutasamaprabham  || 20.24 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaktimāndevaṃ stotukāmo vyavasthitaḥ  |
jayeśa jaya vāgīśa jaya divyāṅgabhūṣaṇa  || 20.25 ||
jaya devapate śrīmansākṣādbrahma sanātana  |
tava lokāḥ śarīrasthāstvaṃ gatiḥ parameśvara  || 20.26 ||
tvadādhārā hi deveśa sarve lokā vyavasthitāḥ  |
tvaṃ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ tvaṃ kartā dharaṇīdharaḥ  || 20.27 ||
tvaṃ hautramagnihotrāṇāṃ sūtramantrastvameva ca  |
gokarṇaṃ bhadrakarṇaṃ ca tvaṃ ca māheśvaraṃ padam  || 20.28 ||
tvaṃ kīrtiḥ sarvakīrtīnāṃ dainyapāpapraṇāśinī  |
tvaṃ naimiṣaṃ kurukṣetraṃ tvaṃ ca viṣṇupadaṃ param  || 20.29 ||
tvayā tu līlayā deva padākrāntā ca medinī  |
tvayā baddho balirdeva tvayendrasya padaṃ kṛtam  || 20.30 ||
tvaṃ kalirdvāparaṃ deva tretā kṛtayugaṃ tathā  |
pralambadamanaśca tvaṃ sraṣṭā tvaṃ ca vināśakṛt || 20.31 ||
tvayā vai dhāryate lokāstvaṃ kālaḥ sarvasaṃkṣayaḥ  |
tvayā hi deva sṛṣṭāstāḥ sarvā vai devayonayaḥ  || 20.32 ||
tvaṃ panthāḥ sarvalokānāṃ tvaṃ ca mokṣaḥ parā gatiḥ  |
brahmā tvadudbhavo devo rajorūpaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
rudraḥ krodhodbhavo'pyevaṃ tvaṃ ca sattve vyavasthitaḥ  || 20.33 ||
etaccarācaraṃ deva krīḍanārthaṃ tvayā kṛtam  |
evaṃ saṃtaptadehena stuto devo mayā prabhuḥ  || 20.34 ||
bhaktyā paramayā rājansarvabhūtapatiḥ prabhuḥ  |
stuvanvai tatra paśyāmi vāripūrṇāṃstato ghaṭān  || 20.35 ||
tato mayā vismṛtā yā tṛṣā sā vardhitā punaḥ  |
upāsarpaṃ tatastasya pārśvaṃ vai puruṣasya hi  || 20.36 ||
pānīyaṃ pātukāmena cintitaṃ ca mayā punaḥ  |
nāpaśyata hi māṃ caiṣa supto'pi na ca budhyate  || 20.37 ||
yastu pāpena saṃmūḍhaḥ sukhaṃ suptaṃ prabodhayet |
jāyate tasya pāpasya brahmahatyāphalaṃ mahat || 20.38 ||
evaṃ saṃcintyamāne tu dvitīyo hyāgataḥ pumān  |
nekṣate jalpate kiṃcidvāmaskandhe mṛgājinī  || 20.39 ||
jaṭī kamaṇḍaludharo daṇḍī mekhalayā vṛtaḥ  |
bhasmonmṛditasarvāṅgo mahātejāstrilocanaḥ  || 20.40 ||
yāvattaṃ stotukāmo'hamapaśyaṃ svacchacakṣuṣā  |
tāvatsarvāṅgasambhūtyāmahatyā rūpasampadā  || 20.41 ||
apaśyaṃ saṃvṛtāṃ nārīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ patito bhūmau jayasveti bruvaṃstataḥ  || 20.42 ||
jaya rudrāṅgasambhūte jayavāhini sanātani  |
jaya kaumāri māhendri vaiṣṇavī vāruṇī tathā  || 20.43 ||
jaya kauberi sāvitri jaya dhātri varānane  |
tṛṣṇayā taptade hasya rakṣāṃ kuru carācare  || 20.44 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
prasannā vipraśārdūla tava vākyaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  |
vartate mānase yatte mayā jñātaṃ dvijottama  || 20.45 ||
śṛṇu vipra mamāpyasti vratametatsudāruṇam  |
strīlaghutvānmayārabdhaṃ duṣkaraṃ mandamedhayā  || 20.46 ||
yadi bhāvī ca me putro dharmiṣṭho lokaviśrutaḥ  |
viprasya tu stanaṃ dattvā paścāddāsyāmi bālake  || 20.47 ||
sa me putraḥ samutpanno yathokto me mahāmune  |
stanaṃ piba tvaṃ viprendra yadi jīvitumicchasi  || 20.48 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
akāryametadviprāṇāṃ yastvimaṃ pibate stanam  |
punaścaivopanayanaṃ vratasiddhiṃ na gacchati  || 20.49 ||
brāhmaṇatvaṃ tribhirlokairdurlabhaṃ padmalocane  |
saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛto vipro yaiśca jāyeta tacchṛṇu  || 20.50 ||
prathamaṃ caiva nārīṣu saṃskārairbījavāpatam  |
bījaprakṣepaṇādeva bījakṣepaḥ sa ucyate  || 20.51 ||
tadante ca mahābhāge garbhādhānaṃ dvitīyakam  |
puṃsavanaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu sīmantaṃ ca caturthakam  || 20.52 ||
pañcamaṃ jātakarma syānnāma vai ṣaṣṭhamucyate  |
niṣkrāmaḥ saptamaścaiva hyannaprāśanamaṣṭamam  || 20.53 ||
navamaṃ vai cūḍakarma daśamaṃ mauñjibandhanam  |
aiṣikaṃ dārvikaṃ caiva saumikaṃ bhaumikaṃ tathā  || 20.54 ||
patnīsaṃyojanaṃ cānyaddaivakarma tataḥ param  |
mānuṣyaṃ pitṛkarma syāddaśamāṣṭāsu śobhane  || 20.55 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ tatheṣṭaṃ ca pārvaṇaṃ ca tataḥ param  || 20.56 ||
śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇyāmāgrayaṇaṃ ca caitrāśvayujyāṃ daśapaurṇamāsyām  |
nirūḍhapaśusavanasautrāmaṇyagniṣṭomātyagniṣṭomāḥ  || 20.57 ||
ṣoḍaṣīvājapeyātirātrāptoryāmodaśavājapeyāḥ  |
sarvabhūteṣu kṣāntiranasūyā śaucamaṅgalamakārpaṇyamaspṛheti  || 20.58 ||
ebhiraṣṭacatvāriṃśadbhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ saṃkṛto brāhmaṇo bhavati  || 20.59 ||
evaṃ jñātvā mahābhāge na tu māṃ pātumarhasi  |
śiśupeyaṃ stanaṃ bhadre kathaṃ vai madvidhaḥ pibet || 20.60 ||
mamaitadvacanaṃ śrutvā nārī vacanamabravīt || 20.61 ||
yadi tvaṃ na pibeḥ stanyaṃ payo bālo mariṣyati  |
śrūyate triṣu lokeṣu vedeṣu ca smṛtiṣvapi  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo bhrūṇahatyā na muñcati  || 20.62 ||
bhavitrī tava hatyā ca mahābhāgavataḥ punaḥ  |
janmāni ca śatānyaṣṭau kliśyate bhrūṇahatyayā  || 20.63 ||
mṛtaḥ śunatvaṃ cāpnoti varṣāṇāṃ tu śatatrayam  |
tatastasya kṣaye jāte kākayoniṃ vrajetpunaḥ  || 20.64 ||
tatrāpi ca śatānyaṣṭau kliśyate pāpakarmaṇi  |
varāho daśa janmāni tadante jāyate kṛmiḥ  || 20.65 ||
tataścārohiṇīṃ prāpya gogajāśvanṛjanmabhāk |
śrūyate śrutiśāstreṣu vedeṣu ca paraṃtapa  || 20.66 ||
sarvapāpādhikaṃ pāpaṃ bālahatyā dvijottama  |
bālahatyāyuto vipraḥ pacyate narake dhruvam  || 20.67 ||
varṣāṇi ca śatānyaṣṭau prāpnoti yamayātanām  |
tasmādalpataro doṣaḥ pibato me stanaṃ tava  || 20.68 ||
tathaivāpibataḥ pāpaṃ jāyate bahuvarṣikam  |
kṣudhātṛṣāvirāmaste puṇyaṃ ca pibataḥ stanam  || 20.69 ||
ato na cetaḥ saṃdigdhaṃ kartavyamiha karhicit |
ehi vipra yathākāmaṃ bālārthe piba me stanam  || 20.70 ||
tato'haṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā stanaṃ pātuṃ samudyataḥ  |
na ca tṛptiṃ vijānāmi pibataḥ stanamuttamam  || 20.71 ||
triṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi bhārataivaṃ śatāni ca  |
tataḥ prabuddhotsaṅge'haṃ māyānidrāvimohitaḥ  || 20.72 ||
nidrāvigatamoho'haṃ yāvatpaśyāmi pāṇḍava  |
tāvatsuptaṃ na paśyāmi na ca taṃ bālakaṃ vibho  || 20.73 ||
caturastāṃśca vai kumbhān paśyāmi tatra bhārata  |
na ca paśyāmi tāṃ devīṃ gatā vai kutracicca te  || 20.74 ||
evaṃ vimṛśyamānasya cintayānasya tiṣṭhataḥ  |
īṣaddhasitayā vācā devī vacanamabravīt || 20.75 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
kṛṣṇaḥ sa puruṣaḥ supto dvitīyo'pyāgato haraḥ  |
ye catvāraśca te kumbhāḥ samudrāste dvijottama  || 20.76 ||
yaśca bālastvayā dṛṣṭo brāhmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
ahaṃ ca pṛthivī jñeyā saptadvīpā sarvatā  || 20.77 ||
yā gatā tvāṃ parityajya bhūtale supratiṣṭhitā  |
imāṃ ca prekṣase vipra narmadāṃ saritāṃ varām  || 20.78 ||
sarvasattvopakārāya bṛhate puṇyalakṣaṇā  |
revānadī tu vikhyātā na mṛtā tena narmadā  || 20.79 ||
evaṃ jñātvā śamaṃ gaccha svastho bhava mahāmune  |
ityuktvā māṃ tadā devī tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 20.80 ||
evaṃ hi śete bhagavānsattvasthaḥ pralaye sadā  |
sattvarūpo mahādevo yadādhāre jagatsthitam  || 20.81 ||
evaṃ mayānubhūtaṃ tu dṛṣṭamāścaryamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ kathitaṃ te narottama  || 20.82 ||
viṣṇoścaritamityuktaṃ yattvayā paripṛcchitam  |
bhūya eva mahābāho kimanyacchrotumicchasi  || 20.83 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vārāhakalpavṛttāntavarṇanaṃ nāma viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 21

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
śrutaṃ me vividhāścaryaṃ tvatprasādāddvijottama  |
bhūyaśca śrotumicchāmi tanme kathaya suvrata  || 21.1 ||
kathameṣā nadī puṇyā sarvanadīṣu cottamā  |
narmadā nāma vikhyātā bhūyo me kathayānagha  || 21.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī  |
tārayetsarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  || 21.3 ||
narmadāyāstu māhātmyaṃ yatpūrveṇa mayā śrutam  |
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  || 21.4 ||
gaṅgā kanakhale puṇyā kurukṣetre sarasvatī  |
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  || 21.5 ||
tribhiḥ sārasvataṃ toyaṃ saptāhena tu yāmunam  |
sadyaḥ punāti gāṅgeyaṃ darśanādeva nārmadam  || 21.6 ||
kaliṅgadeśātpaścārdhe parvate'marakaṇṭake  |
puṇyā ca triṣu lokeṣu ramaṇīyā pade pade  || 21.7 ||
tatra devāśca gandharvā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
tapastaptvā mahārāja siddhiṃ paramikāṃ gatāḥ  || 21.8 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājanniyamastho jitendriyaḥ  |
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ kulānāṃ tārayecchatam  || 21.9 ||
siddhikṣetraṃ paraṃ tāta parvato hyamaraṃkaṭaḥ  |
sarvadevāśrito yasmādṛṣibhiḥ parisevitaḥ  || 21.10 ||
siddhavidyādharā bhūtagandharvāḥ sthānamuttamam  |
dṛśyādṛśyāśca rājendra sevante siddhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ  || 21.11 ||
ahaṃ ca paramaṃ sthānaṃ tataḥ prabhṛti saṃśritaḥ  |
atra praṇavarūpo vai sthāne tiṣṭhatyumāpatiḥ  || 21.12 ||
śrīkaṇṭhaḥ sagaṇaḥ sarvabhūtasaṅghairniṣevitaḥ  |
asmādgirivarādbhūpa vakṣye tīrthasya vistaram  || 21.13 ||
yāni santīha tīrthāni puṇyāni nṛpasattama  |
yāni yānīha tīrthāni narmadāyāstaṭadvaye  || 21.14 ||
na teṣāṃ vistaraṃ vaktuṃ śakto brahmāpi bhūpate  |
yojanānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ śrūyate sariduttamā  || 21.15 ||
vistareṇa tu rājendra ardhayojanamāyatā  |
ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyastathaiva ca  || 21.16 ||
parvatādudadhiṃ yāvadubhe kūle na saṃśayaḥ  || 21.17 ||
saptaṣaṣṭisahasrāṇi saptaṣaṣṭiśatāni ca  |
saptaṣaṣṭistathā koṭyo vāyustīrthāni cābravīt || 21.18 ||
paraṃ kṛtayuge tāni yānti pratyakṣatāṃ nṛpa  |
paśyanti mānavāḥ sarve satataṃ dharmabuddhayaḥ  || 21.19 ||
yathāyathā kalirghoro vartate dāruṇo nṛpa  |
tathātathālpatāṃ yānti hīnasattvā yato narāḥ  || 21.20 ||
jāleśvarāditīrthāni parvate'sminnarādhipa  |
pitṛtṛptipradānyāhuḥ svargamokṣapradāni ca  || 21.21 ||
śreṣṭhaṃ dāruvanaṃ tatra carukāsaṃgamaḥ śubhaḥ  |
uttare narmadāyāstu carukeśvaramuttamam  || 21.22 ||
dārukeśvaratīrthaṃ ca vyatīpāteśvaraṃ tathā  |
pātāleśvaratīrthaṃ ca koṭiyajñaṃ tathaiva ca  || 21.23 ||
iti caivottare kūle revāyā nṛpasattama  |
amareśvarapārśve ca liṅgānyaṣṭottaraṃ śatam  || 21.24 ||
varuṇeśvaramukhyāni sarvapāpaharāṇi ca  || 21.25 ||
māndhātṛpurapārśve ca siddheśvarayameśvarau  |
oṅkārātpūrvabhāge ca kedāraṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 21.26 ||
tatsamīpe mahārāja svargadvāramaghāpaham  |
nāmnā brahmeśvaraṃ puṇyaṃ saptasārasvataṃ puraḥ  || 21.27 ||
rudrāṣṭakaṃ ca sāvitraṃ somatīrthaṃ tathaiva ca  |
etāni dakṣiṇe tīre revāyā bharatarṣabha  || 21.28 ||
asmiṃstu parvate tāta rudrāṇāṃ koṭayaḥ sthitāḥ  |
snānaistuṣṭirbhavetteṣāṃ gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ  || 21.29 ||
prītāste'pi bhavantyatra rudrā rājanna saṃśayaḥ  |
japena pāpasaṃśuddhirdhyānenānantyamaśnute  || 21.30 ||
dānena bhogānāpnoti ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt |
parvatātpaścime deśe svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ  |
sthitaḥ praṇavarūpo'sau jagadādiḥ sanātanaḥ  || 21.31 ||
tatra snātvā śucirbhūtvā brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ  |
pitṛkāryaṃ prakurvīta vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  || 21.32 ||
tilodakena tatraiva tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
ā saptamaṃ kulaṃ tasya svarge modati pāṇḍava  || 21.33 ||
ātmanā saha bhogāṃśca vividhān labhate sukhī  |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi krīḍate surapūjitaḥ  || 21.34 ||
modate suciraṃ kālaṃ pitṛpūjāphaladhitaḥ  |
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo jāyate vimale kule  || 21.35 ||
dhanavāndānaśīlaśca nīrogo lokapūjitaḥ  |
punaḥ smarati tattīrthaṃ gamanaṃ kurute punaḥ  || 21.36 ||
dvitīye janmani bhaveddhradasyānucarotkaṭaḥ  |
tathaiva brahmacaryeṇa sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  || 21.37 ||
sarvahiṃsānivṛttastu labhate phalamuttamam  |
evaṃ dharmasamācāro yastu prāṇānparityajet || 21.38 ||
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ yadvai tannibodha narādhipa  |
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇi svarge modati pāṇḍava  || 21.39 ||
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇe divyaśabdānunādite  |
divyagandhānuliptāṅgo divyālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ  || 21.40 ||
krīḍate daivataiḥ sārddhaṃ siddhagandharvasaṃstutaḥ  |
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati vīryavān  || 21.41 ||
hastyaśvarathayānaiśca dharmajñaḥ śāstratatparaḥ  |
gṛhe stambhaśatākīrṇe sauvarṇe rajatānvite  || 21.42 ||
saptāṣṭabhūmisudvāre dāsīdāsasamākule  |
mattamātaṅganiḥśvāsairvājiheṣitanāditaiḥ  || 21.43 ||
kṣubhyate tasya taddvāramindrasya bhuvanaṃ yathā  |
rājarājeśvaraḥ śrīmānsarvastrījanavallabhaḥ  || 21.44 ||
tasmingṛhe vasitvā tu krīḍābhogasamanvitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 21.45 ||
evaṃ teṣāṃ bhavetsarvaṃ ye mṛtā hyamareśvare  |
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryādbhaktyā hyamarakaṇṭake  || 21.46 ||
sa mṛtaḥ svargamāpnoti yāsyate paramāṃ gatim  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  || 21.47 ||
purāṇe śrūyate rājansarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet |
tasyāstīre tu ye vṛkṣāḥ patitāḥ kālaparyaye  || 21.48 ||
narmadātoyasaṃspṛṣṭāste yānti paramāṃ gatim  |
anivṛttikā gatistasya pavanasyāmbare yathā  || 21.49 ||
patanaṃ kurute yastu tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
kanyāstrīṇi sahasrāṇi pātāle bhogabhāginaḥ  || 21.50 ||
tiṣṭhanti bhavane tasya preṣaṇe prārthayanti ca  |
divyabhogaiḥ susampannaḥ krīḍate kālam  || 21.51 ||
pṛthivyāṃ hyāsamudrāyāṃ tādṛśo naiva jāyate  |
yādṛśo'yaṃ naraśreṣṭha parvato'marakaṇṭakaḥ  || 21.52 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ parvatasyānu paścime  |
hrado jāleśvaro nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 21.53 ||
tatra piṇḍapradānena sandhyopāsanakena tu  |
pitaro dvādaśābdāni tarpitāstu bhavanti vai  || 21.54 ||
dakṣiṇe narmadātīre kapilā tu mahānadī  |
saralārjunasaṃchannā khadirairupaśobhitā  || 21.55 ||
mādhavīsallakībhiśca vallībhiścāpyalaṃkṛtā  |
śvāpadairgarjamānaiśca gomāyuvānarādibhiḥ  || 21.56 ||
pakṣijātiviśeṣaiśca nityaṃ pramuditā nṛpa  |
sāgraṃ koṭiśataṃ tatra ṛṣīṇāmiti śuśruma  || 21.57 ||
tapastaptvā gataṃ mokṣaṃ yeṣāṃ janma na cāgamaḥ  |
yena tatra tapastaptaṃ kapilena mahātmanā  || 21.58 ||
tatra taccābhavattīrthaṃ puṇyaṃ siddhaniṣevitam  |
yena sā kāpilaistāta sevitā ṛṣibhiḥ purā  || 21.59 ||
tena sā kapilā nāma gītā pāpakṣayaṃkarī  |
tatra koṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ tīrthānāmamareśvare  || 21.60 ||
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  |
dānaṃ ca vidhivaddattvā yathāśaktyā dvijottame  || 21.61 ||
īśvarānugrahātsarvaṃ tatra koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet |
yasmādanakṣaraṃ rūpaṃ praṇavasyeha bhārata  || 21.62 ||
śivasvarūpasya tataḥ kṛtamātrākṣaraṃ bhavet |
tiryañcaḥ paśavaścaiva vṛkṣā gulmalatādayaḥ  || 21.63 ||
te'pi tatra kṣayaṃ yātāḥ svargaṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ  |
viśalyā tatra yā proktā tatraiva tu mahānadī  || 21.64 ||
snātvā dattvā yathānyāyaṃ tatrāpi sukṛtī bhavet |
tatra devagaṇāḥ sarve sakinnaramahoragāḥ  || 21.65 ||
yakṣarākṣasagandharvā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
sarve samāgatāstāṃ vai paśyanti hyamareśvare  || 21.66 ||
taiśca sarvaiḥ samāgamya vanditau tau śubhau kaṭau  |
purā yuge mahāghore sarvalokabhayaṃkare  || 21.67 ||
narmadāyāḥ sutastatra saśalyo viśalīkṛtaḥ  |
sarvadevaiśca ṛṣibhirviśalyā tena sā smṛtā  || 21.68 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
utpannā tu kathaṃ tāta viśalyā kapilā katham  |
kathaṃ vā narmadāputraḥ śalyayukto'bhavanmune  || 21.69 ||
āścaryabhūtaṃ lokasya śrotumicchāmi suvrata  || 21.70 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā dākṣāyaṇī nāma sahitā śūlapāṇinā  |
krīḍitvā narmadātoye parayā ca mudā nṛpa  || 21.71 ||
jalāduttīrya sahasā vastramanyatsamāharat |
devyāstu snānavastraṃ tatpīḍitaṃ līlayā nṛpa  || 21.72 ||
sahitānucarībhistu indrāyudhanibhaṃ bhṛśam  |
tasminniṣpīḍyamāne tu vāri yanniḥsṛtaṃ tadā  || 21.73 ||
tasmādiyaṃ sarijjajñe kapilākhyā mahānadī  |
saṃyogādaṅgarāgasya vastrodyatkapilaṃ jalam  || 21.74 ||
galitaṃ tena kapilā varṇato nāmato'bhavat |
tathā gandharasairyuktaṃ nānāpuṣpaistu vāsitam  || 21.75 ||
nānāvarṇāruṇaṃ śubhraṃ vastrādyadvāri niḥsṛtam  |
pīḍyamānaṃ karaiḥ śubhraistaistu pallavakomalaiḥ  || 21.76 ||
kapilaṃ jalamiśraistu tasmādeṣā saridvarā  |
kapilā cocyate tajjñaiḥ purāṇārthaviśāradaiḥ  || 21.77 ||
eṣā vai vastrasambhūtā narmadātoyasambhavā  |
mahāpuṇyatamā jñeyā kapilā sariduttamā  || 21.78 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye kapilāsaritsambhavavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 22

śrī mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sā viśalyā hyabhūdyathā  |
āścaryabhūtā lokasya sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  || 22.1 ||
brahmaṇo mānasaḥ putro mukhyo hyagnirajāyata  |
mukhyo vahniritiprokta ṛṣiḥ paramadhārmikaḥ  || 22.2 ||
tasya svāhābhavatpatnī smṛtā dākṣāyaṇī tu sā  |
tasyāṃ mukhyā mahārāja trayaḥ putrāstadā'bhavan  || 22.3 ||
agnirāhavanīyastu dakṣiṇāgnistathaiva ca  |
gārhapatyastṛtīyastu trailokyaṃ yaiśca dhāryate  || 22.4 ||
tathā vai gārhapatyo'gnirjajñe putradvayaṃ śubham  |
padmakaḥ śaṅkunāmā ca tāvubhāvagnisattamau  || 22.5 ||
vasannagnirnadītīre samāśritya mahattapaḥ  |
rudramārādhayāmāsa jitātmā susamāhitaḥ  || 22.6 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
tamuvāca mahādevaḥ prasanno vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ  || 22.7 ||
bhobho brūhi mahābhāga yatte manasi vartate  |
dātā hyahamasaṃdeho yadyapi syātsudurlabham  || 22.8 ||

agniruvāca -
narmadeyaṃ mahābhāgā sarito yāśca ṣoḍaśa  |
bhavantu mama patnyastāstvatprasādānmaheśvara  || 22.9 ||
tāsu vai cintitān putrānagryānutpādayāmyaham  |
eṣa eva varo deva dīyatāṃ me maheśvara  || 22.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
etāstu dhiṣṇināmnyo vai bhaviṣyanti saridvarāḥ  |
patnyastava viśālākṣyo vede khyātā na saṃśayaḥ  || 22.11 ||
tāsāṃ putrā bhaviṣyanti hyagnayo ye'dhvare smṛtāḥ  |
dhiṣṇyānāma suvikhyātā yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 22.12 ||
evamuktvā mahādevastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
narmadā ca saricchreṣṭhā tasya bhāryā babhūva ha  || 22.13 ||
kāverī kṛṣṇaveṇī ca revā ca yamunā tathā  |
godāvarī vitastā ca candrabhāgā irāvatī  || 22.14 ||
vipāśā kauśikī caiva sarayūḥ śatarudrikā  |
śiprā sarasvatī caiva hrādinī pāvanī tathā  || 22.15 ||
etāḥ ṣoḍaśā nadyo vai bhāryārthaṃ saṃvyavasthitāḥ  |
tadātmānaṃ vibhajyāśu dhiṣṇīṣu sa mahādyutiḥ  || 22.16 ||
vyabhicārāttu bharturvai narmadādyāsu dhiṣṇiṣu  |
utpannāḥ śucayaḥ putrāḥ sarve te dhiṣṇyapāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 22.17 ||
tasyāśca narmadāyāstu dhiṣṇīndro nāma viśrutaḥ  |
babhūva putro balavānrūpeṇāpratimo nṛpa  || 22.18 ||
tato devāsuraṃ yuddhamabhavallomaharṣaṇam  |
mayatārakamityevaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 22.19 ||
tatra daityairmahāghorairmayatārapurogamaiḥ  |
tāḍitāste surāstrastā viṣṇuṃ vai śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ  || 22.20 ||
trāyasva no hṛṣīkeśā ghorādasmānmahābhayāt |
daityānsarvānsaṃharasva mayatārapurogamān  || 22.21 ||
evamuktaḥ sa bhagavāndiśo daśa vyalokayat |
tato bhagavatā dṛṣṭau raṇe pāvakamārutau  || 22.22 ||
āhūtau viṣṇunā tau tu sakāśaṃ jagmatuḥ kṣaṇāt |
sthitau tau praṇatau cāgre devadevasya dhīmataḥ  || 22.23 ||
tato dhiṣṇiḥ pāvakendro devenokto mahātmanā  |
nirdahemānmahāghorānnārmadeya mahāsurān  || 22.24 ||
athaivamukau tau devau raṇe pāvakamārutau  |
daityān dadahatuḥ sarvānmayatārapurogamān  || 22.25 ||
dahyamānāstu te sarve śastrairagniṃ tvaveṣṭayan  |
divyairagnyarkasaṅkāśaiḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 22.26 ||
tāṃścāgniḥ śastranikarairnirdadāha mahāsurān  |
jvālāmālākulaṃ sarvaṃ vāyunā nirmitaṃ tadā  || 22.27 ||
dahyamānāstato daityā agnijvālāsamāvṛtāḥ  |
praviśya pātālatalaṃ jale līnāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 22.28 ||
tataḥ kumāramagniṃ tu narmadāputramavyayam  |
pūjayitvā surāḥ sarve jagmuste tridaśālayam  || 22.29 ||
saśalyastu mahātejā revāputro vṛto'gnibhiḥ  |
narmadāmāgataḥ kṣipraṃ mātaraṃ draṣṭumutsukaḥ  || 22.30 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā putramāyāntaṃ śastraugheṇa parikṣatam  |
narmadā puṇyasalilā abhyutthāya suvismitā  || 22.31 ||
paryaṣvajata bāhubhyāṃ prasnavāpīḍitastanī  |
saśalyaṃ putramādāya kāpilaṃ hradamāviśat || 22.32 ||
praviṣṭamātre tu hrade kāpile pāpanāśini  |
saśalyaṃ taṃ viśalyaṃ ca kṣaṇātkṛtavatī tadā  || 22.33 ||
sa viśalyo'bhavadyasmātprāpya tasyāḥ śivaṃ jalam  |
kapilā nāmatastena viśalyā cocyate budhaiḥ  || 22.34 ||
anye'pi tatra ye snātāḥ śucayastu samāhitāḥ  |
pāpaśalyaiḥ pramucyante mṛtā yānti surālayam  || 22.35 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ purā tvayā  |
utpattikāraṇaṃ tāta viśalyāyā nareśvara  || 22.36 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye viśalyāsambhavo nāma dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 23

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatraiva saṅgame rājanbhaktyā paramayā nṛpa  |
prāṇāṃstyajanti ye martyāste yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 23.1 ||
saṃnyastasarvasaṃkalpo yastu prāṇānparityajet |
amareśvaramāsādya sa svarge niyataṃ vaset || 23.2 ||
śailendraṃ yaḥ samāsādya ātmānaṃ muñcate naraḥ  |
vimānenārkavarṇena sa gacchedamarāvatīm  || 23.3 ||
naraṃ patantamālokya nagādamarakaṇṭakāt |
bruvantyapsarasaḥ sarvā mama bhartā bhavediti  || 23.4 ||
samaṃ jalaṃ dharmavido vadanti sārasvataṃ gāṅgamiti prabuddhāḥ  |
tasyopariṣṭātpravadanti tajjñā revājalaṃ nātra vicāraṇāsti  || 23.5 ||
anekavidyādharakinnarādyairadhyāsitaṃ puṇyatamādhivāsaiḥ  |
revājalaṃ dhārayato hi mūrdhnā sthānaṃ surendrādhipateḥ samīpe  || 23.6 ||
narmadā sarvadā sevyā bahunoktena kiṃ nṛpa  |
yadīcchenna punardraṣṭuṃ ghoraṃ saṃsārasāgaram  || 23.7 ||
trayāṇāmapi lokānāṃ mahatī pāvanī smṛtā  |
yatra tatra mṛtasyāpi dhruvaṃ gāṇeśvarī gatiḥ  || 23.8 ||
anekayajñāyatanairvṛtāṅgī na hyatra kiṃcidyadatīrthamasti  |
tasyāstu tīre bhavatā yaduktaṃ tapasvino vāpyatapasvino vā  || 23.9 ||
mriyanti ye pāpakṛto manuṣyāste svargamāyānti yathā'marendrāḥ  || 23.10 ||
evaṃ tu kapilā caiva viśalyā rājasattama  |
īśvareṇa purā sṛṣṭā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 23.11 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājansopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
aśvamedhasya mahato'saṃśayaṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 23.12 ||
anāśakaṃ ca yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto yāti vai śivamandiram  || 23.13 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ snānadānena yatphalam  |
viśalyāsaṅgame snātvā sakṛttatphalamaśnute  || 23.14 ||
evaṃ puṇyā pavitrā ca kathitā tava bhūpate  |
bhūyo māṃ pṛcchasi ca yattaccaiva kathayāmyaham  || 23.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe viśalyāsaṅgamamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 24

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
saṅgamaḥ karanarmadayoḥ pure māndhātṛsaṃjñite  |
gatvā snātvā tapayitvā pitḥnviṣṇupuraṃ nayet || 24.1 ||
mardayitvā karau pūrvaṃ viṣṇurdaityajighāṃsayā  |
cakraṃ jagrāha tatraiva svedājjātā saridvarā  || 24.2 ||
saṃgatā revayā tatra snātvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 24.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye karanarmadāsaṅgamamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 25

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
oṃkārātpūrvabhāge vai saṅgamo lokaviśrutaḥ  |
revayā saṃgatā yatra nīlagaṅgā nṛpottama  || 25.1 ||
tatra snātvā japitvā ca ko'rtho'labhyo bhavedbhuvi  |
ṣaṣṭirvarṣasahasrāṇi nīlakaṇṭhapure vaset || 25.2 ||
tarpayitvā pitḥñśrāddhe tilamiśrairjalairapi  |
uddharedātmanā sārdhaṃ puruṣānekaviṃśatim  || 25.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye nīlagaṅgāyāḥ saṅgamamāhātmyavarṇanaṃnāma pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 26

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
jāleśvare'pi yatproktaṃ tvayā pūrvaṃ dvijottama  |
tatkathaṃ tu bhavetpuṇyamṛṣisiddhaniṣevitam  || 26.1 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
jāleśvarātparaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
tasyotpattiṃ kathayataḥ śṛṇu tvaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 26.2 ||
purā ṛṣigaṇāḥ sarve sendrāścaiva marudgaṇāḥ  |
tāpitā asuraiḥ sarvaiḥ kṣayaṃ nītā hyanekaśaḥ  || 26.3 ||
bāṇāsuraprabhṛtibhirjambhaśumbhapurogamaiḥ  |
vadhyamānā hyanekaiśca brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 26.4 ||
vimānaiḥ parvatākārairhayaiścaiva gajopamaiḥ  |
syandanairnagarākāraiḥ siṃhaśārdūlayojitaiḥ  || 26.5 ||
kacchapairmakaraiścānye jagmuranye padātayaḥ  |
prāpya te paramaṃ sthānamaśakyaṃ yadadhārmikaiḥ  || 26.6 ||
dṛṣṭvā padmodbhavaṃ devaṃ sarvalokasya śaṅkaram  |
te sarve tatra gatvā tu stutiṃ cakruḥ samāhitāḥ  || 26.7 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
jayāmeya jayābheda jaya sambhūtikāraka  |
padmayone suraśreṣṭha tvāṃ vayaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 26.8 ||
tacchrutvā tu vaco devo devānāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
meghagambhīrayā vācā pratyuvāca pitāmahaḥ  || 26.9 ||
kiṃ vo hyāgamanaṃ devāḥ sarveṣāṃ ca vivarṇatā  |
kenāvamānitāḥ sarve śīghraṃ kathayatāmarāḥ  || 26.10 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
bāṇo nāma mahāvīryo dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
tenāsmākaṃ hṛtaṃ sarvaṃ dhanaratnairviyojitāḥ  || 26.11 ||
devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
cintayāmāsa deveśastasya nāśāya yā kriyā  || 26.12 ||
avadhyo dānavaḥ pāpaḥ sarveṣāṃ vai divaukasām  |
muktvā tu śaṅkaraṃ devaṃ na mayā na ca viṣṇunā  || 26.13 ||
tatraiva sarve gacchāmo yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
sa gatiścaiva sarveṣāṃ vidyate'nyo na kaścana  || 26.14 ||
evamuktvā suraiḥ sarvairbrahmā vedavidāṃvaraḥ  |
brāhmaṇaiḥ saha vidvadbhirato yatra maheśvaraḥ  || 26.15 ||
stutibhiśca supuṣṭābhistuṣṭāva parameśvaram  || 26.16 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
jaya tvaṃ devadeveśa jayomārdhaśarīradhṛk |
vṛṣāsana mahābāho śaśāṅkakṛtabhūṣaṇa  || 26.17 ||
namaḥ śūlāgrahastāya namaḥ khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇe  |
jaya bhūtapate deva dakṣayajñavināśana  || 26.18 ||
pañcākṣara namo deva pañcabhūtātmavigraha  |
pañcavaktramayeśāna vedaistvaṃ tu pragīyase  || 26.19 ||
sṛṣṭipālanasaṃhārāṃstvaṃ sadā kuruṣe namaḥ  |
aṣṭamūrte smarahara smara satyaṃ yathā stutaḥ  || 26.20 ||
pañcātmikā tanurdeva brāhmaṇaiste pragīyate  |
sadyo vāme tathāghore īśo tatpuruṣe tathā  || 26.21 ||
hemajāle suvistīrṇe haṃsavatkūjase hara  |
evaṃ stuto munigaṇairbrahmādyaiśca surāsuraiḥ  || 26.22 ||
prahṛṣṭaḥ sumanā bhūtvā surasaṅghānuvāca ha  || 26.23 ||

īśvara uvāca -
svāgataṃ devaviprāṇāṃ suprabhātādya śarvarī  |
kiṃ kurmo vadata kṣipraṃ ko'nyaḥ sevyaḥ surāsuraiḥ  || 26.24 ||
kiṃ duḥkhaṃ ko nu santāpaḥ kuto vo bhayamāgatam  |
kathayadhvaṃ mahābhāgāḥ kāraṇaṃ yanmanogatam  || 26.25 ||
evamuktāstu rudreṇa pratyavocansurarṣabhāḥ  |
svānsvāndehāndarśayanto lajjamānā adhomukhāḥ  || 26.26 ||
asti ghoro mahāvīryo dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
bāṇo nāmeti vikhyāto yasya tattripuraṃ mahat || 26.27 ||
tena vai sutapastaptaṃ daśavarṣaśatāni hi  |
tasya tuṣṭo'bhavadbrahmā niyamena damena ca  || 26.28 ||
purāṇi tānyabhedyāni dadau kāmagamāni vai  |
āyasaṃ rājataṃ caiva sauvarṇaṃ ca tathāparam  || 26.29 ||
tripuraṃ brahmaṇā sṛṣṭaṃ bhramattatkāmagāmi ca  |
tasyaiva tu balotkṛṣṭāstripure dānavāḥ sthitāḥ  || 26.30 ||
trailokyaṃ sakalaṃ deva pīḍayanti mahāsurāḥ  |
daṇḍapāśāsiśastrāṇi avikāre vikurvate  |
tripuraṃ dānavairjuṣṭaṃ bhramattaccakrasaṃnibham  || 26.31 ||
kvaciddṛśyamadṛśyaṃ vā mṛgatṛṣṇaiva lakṣyate  || 26.32 ||
yasminpatati taddivyaṃ dṛptasya tripuraṃ mahat |
na tatra brāhmaṇā devā gāvo naiva tu jantavaḥ  || 26.33 ||
na tatra dṛśyate kiṃcitpatedyatra puratrayam  |
nadyo grāmāśca deśāśca bahavo bhasmasātkṛtāḥ  || 26.34 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ caiva maṇimauktikameva ca  |
strīratnaṃ śobhanaṃ yacca tatsarvaṃ karṣate balāt || 26.35 ||
na śastreṇa na cāstreṇa na divā niśi vā hara  |
śakyate devasaṅghaiśca nihantuṃ sa kathaṃcana  || 26.36 ||
taddahasva mahādeva tvaṃ hi naḥ paramā gatiḥ  |
evaṃ prasādaṃ deveśa sarveṣāṃ kartumarhasi  || 26.37 ||
yena devāśca gandharvā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  |
parāṃ dhṛtiṃ samāyānti tatprabho kartumarhasi  || 26.38 ||

īśvara uvāca -
etatsarvaṃ kariṣyāmi mā viṣādaṃ gamiṣyatha  |
acireṇaiva kālena kuryāṃ yuṣmatsukhāvaham  || 26.39 ||
āśvāsayitvā tāndevānsarvānindrapurogamān  |
cintayāmāsa deveśastripurasya vadhaṃ prati  || 26.40 ||
kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa hantavyaṃ tripuraṃ mayā  |
tamekaṃ nāradaṃ muktvā nānyopāyo vidhīyate  || 26.41 ||
evaṃ saṃstabhya cātmānaṃ tato dhyātaḥ sa nāradaḥ  |
tatkṣaṇādeva samprāpto vāyubhūto mahātapāḥ  || 26.42 ||
kamaṇḍaludharo devastridaṇḍī jñānakovidaḥ  |
yogapaṭṭākṣasūtreṇa chatreṇaiva virājitaḥ  || 26.43 ||
jaṭājūṭābaddhaśirā jvalanārkasamaprabhaḥ  |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya daṇḍavatpatito bhuvi  || 26.44 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā nārado bhagavānmuniḥ  |
stotreṇa mahatā śarvaḥ stuto bhaktyā mahāmanāḥ  || 26.45 ||

nārada uvāca -
jaya śambho virūpākṣa jaya deva trilocana  |
jaya śaṅkara īśāna rudreśvara namo'stu te  || 26.46 ||
tvaṃ patistvaṃ jagatkartā tvameva layakṛdvibho  |
tvameva jagatāṃ nātho duṣṭātakaniṣūdanaḥ  || 26.47 ||
tvaṃ naḥ pāhi sureśāna trayīmūrte sanātana  |
bhavamūrte bhavāre tvaṃ bhajatāmabhayo bhava  || 26.48 ||
bhavabhāvavināśārthaṃ bhava tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ bhaje  |
kimarthaṃ cintito deva ājñā me dīyatāṃ prabho  || 26.49 ||
kasya saṃkṣobhaye cittaṃ ko vādya patatu kṣitau  |
kamadya kalahenāhaṃ yojaye jayatāṃvara  || 26.50 ||
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
utphullanayano bhūtvā idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 26.51 ||
svāgataṃ te muniśreṣṭha sadaiva kalahapriya  |
vīṇāvādanatattvajña brahmaputra sanātana  || 26.52 ||
gaccha nārada śīghraṃ tvaṃ yatra tattripuraṃ mahat |
bāṇasya dānavendrasya sarvalokabhayāvaham  || 26.53 ||
bhartāro devatātulyāḥ striyastatrāpsaraḥsamāḥ  |
tāsāṃ vai tejasā caiva bhramate tripuraṃ mahat || 26.54 ||
na śakyate kathaṃ bhettuṃ sarvopāyairdvijottama  |
gatvā tvaṃ mohaya kṣipraṃ pṛthagdharmairanekadhā  || 26.55 ||

nārada uvāca -
tava vākyena deveśa bhedayāmi purottamam  |
abhedyaṃ bahudhopāyairyattu devaiḥ savāsavaiḥ  || 26.56 ||
evamuktvā gato bhūpa śatayojanamāyatam  |
bāṇasya tatpuraśreṣṭhamṛddhivṛddhisamāyutam  || 26.57 ||
kṛtakautukasambādhaṃ nānādhātuvicitritam  |
anekaharmyasaṃchannamanekāyatanojjvalam  || 26.58 ||
dvāratoraṇasaṃyuktaṃ kapāṭārgalabhūṣitam  |
bahuyantrasamopetaṃ prākāraparikhojjvalam  || 26.59 ||
vāpīkṛpataḍāgaiśca devatāyatanairyutam  |
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ padminīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitam  || 26.60 ||
anekavanaśobhāḍhyaṃ nānāvihagamaṇḍitam  |
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇaṃ bāṇasya puramuttamam  || 26.61 ||
tasya madhye mahākāyaṃ saptakakṣaṃ suśobhitam  |
bāṇasya bhavanaṃ divyaṃ sarvaṃ kāñcanabhūṣitam  || 26.62 ||
mauktikādāmaśobhāḍhyaṃ vajravaiḍūryabhūṣitam  |
rukmapaṭṭatalākīrṇaṃ ratnabhūmyā suśobhitam  || 26.63 ||
mattamātaṅganiḥśvāsaiḥ syandanaiḥ saṃkulīkṛtam  |
hayaheṣitaśabdaiśca nārīṇāṃ nūpurasvanaiḥ  || 26.64 ||
khaḍgatomarahastaiśca vajrāṅkuśaśarāyudhaiḥ  |
rakṣitaṃ ghorarūpaiśca dānavairbaladarpitaiḥ  || 26.65 ||
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇaṃ bāṇasya bhavanottamam  |
kailāsaśikharaprakhyaṃ mahendrabhavanopamam  || 26.66 ||
nārado gagane śīghramagamatpurasaṃmukhaḥ  |
dvāradeśaṃ samāsādya kṣattāraṃ vākyamabravīt || 26.67 ||
bhobhoḥ kṣattarmahābuddhe rājakāryaviśārada  |
śīghraṃ bāṇāya cācakṣva nārado dvāri tiṣṭhati  || 26.68 ||
sa vandayitvā caraṇau nāradasya tvarānvitaḥ  |
sabhāmadhyagataṃ bāṇaṃ vijñaptumupacakrame  || 26.69 ||
vepamānāṅgayaṣṭistu kareṇāpihitānanaḥ  |
śṛṇvatāṃ sarvayodhānāmidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 26.70 ||
vandito devagandharvairyakṣakinnaradānavaiḥ  |
kalipriyo durārādhyo nārado dvāri tiṣṭhati  || 26.71 ||
dvārapālasya tadvākyaṃ śrutvā bāṇastvarānvitaḥ  |
dvāḥsthamāha mahādaityaḥ savismayamidaṃ tadā  || 26.72 ||

bāṇa uvāca -
brahmaputraṃ satejaskaṃ duḥsahaṃ duratikramam  |
praveśaya mahābhāgaṃ kimarthaṃ vārito bahiḥ  || 26.73 ||
śrutvā prabhorvacastasya prāveśayadudīritam  |
gatvā vegena mahatā nāradaṃ gṛhamāgatam  || 26.74 ||
dṛṣṭvā devarṣimāyāntaṃ nāradaṃ surapūjitam  |
sāhasotthāya saṃhṛṣṭo vavande caraṇau muneḥ  || 26.75 ||
dadau cāsanamarghyaṃ ca pādyaṃ pūjāṃ yathāvidhi  |
nyavedayacca tadrājyamātmānaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha  || 26.76 ||
papraccha kuśalaṃ cāpi muniṃ bāṇāsuraḥ svayam  || 26.77 ||

nārada uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābāho danorvaṃśavivarddhana  |
ko'nyastribhuvane ślāghyastvāṃ muktvā danupuṃgava  || 26.78 ||
pūjito'haṃ danuśreṣṭha dhanaratnaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  |
rājyena cātmanā vāpi hyevaṃ kaḥ pūjayetparaḥ  || 26.79 ||
na me kāryaṃ hi bhogena bhuṅkṣva rājyamanāmayam  |
tvaddarśanotsukaḥ prāpto dṛṣṭvā devaṃ maheśvaram  || 26.80 ||
bhramate tripuraṃ loke strīsatītvānmayā śrutam  |
tāndraṣṭukāmaḥ samprāptastvaddārāndānaveśvara  || 26.81 ||
manyase yadi me śīghraṃ darśayasva ca māciram  |
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā kañcukiṃ samudīkṣya vai  || 26.82 ||
antaḥpuracaraṃ vṛddhaṃ daṇḍapāṇiṃ guṇānvitam  |
uvāca rājā hṛṣṭātmā śabdenāpūrayandiśaḥ  || 26.83 ||
nāradāya mahādevīṃ darśayasveha kañcukin  |
antaḥpuracaraiḥ sarvaiḥ sametāmaviśaṅkitaḥ  || 26.84 ||
nāthasyājñāṃ puraskṛtya gṛhītvā nāradaṃ kare  |
praviśyākathayaddevyai nārado'yaṃ samāgataḥ  || 26.85 ||
dṛṣṭvā devī muniśreṣṭhaṃ kṛtvā pādābhivandanam  |
āsanaṃ kāñcanaṃ śubhramarghyapādyādikaṃ dadau  || 26.86 ||
tasyai sa bhagavāṃstuṣṭo hyāśīrvādamadātparam  |
nānyā devi triloke'pi tvatsamā dṛśyate'ṅganā  || 26.87 ||
pativratā śubhācārā satyaśaucasamanvitā  |
yasyāḥ prabhāvāttripuraṃ bhramate cakravatsadā  || 26.88 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ devī nāradasya sudānvitam  |
paryapṛcchadṛṣiṃ bhaktyā dharmaṃ dharmabhṛtāṃvarā  || 26.89 ||

rājñyuvāca -
bhagavanmānuṣe loke devāstuṣyanti kairvrataiḥ  |
kāni dānāni dīyante yeṣāṃ ca syānmahatphalam  || 26.90 ||
upavāsāśca ye kecitstrīdharme kathitā budhaiḥ  |
yaiḥ kṛtaiḥ svargamāyānti sukṛtinyaḥ striyo yathā  || 26.91 ||
yattatsarvaṃ mahābhāga kathayasva yathātatham  |
śrotumicchāmyahaṃ sarvaṃ kathayasvāviśaṅkitaḥ  || 26.92 ||

nārada uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābhāge praśno'yaṃ veditastvayā  |
yaṃ śrutvā sarvanārīṇāṃ dharmavṛddhistu jāyate  || 26.93 ||
upavāsaiśca dānaiśca patiputrau vaśānugau  |
bāndhavaiḥ pūjyate nityaṃ yaiḥ kṛtaiḥ kathayāmi te  || 26.94 ||
durbhagā subhagā yaistu subhagā durbhagā bhavet |
putriṇī putrarahitā hyaputrā putriṇī tathā  || 26.95 ||
bhartāraṃ labhate kanyā tathānyā bhartṛvarjitā  |
kṛtākṛtaiśca jāyante tannibodhasva sundari  || 26.96 ||
tiladhenuṃ suvarṇaṃ ca rūpyaṃ gā vāsasī tathā  |
pānīyaṃ bhūmidānaṃ ca gandhadhūpānulepanam  || 26.97 ||
pādukopānahau chatraṃ puṇyāni vyañjanāni ca  |
pādābhyaṅgaṃ śiro'bhyaṅgaṃ snānaṃ śayyāsanāni ca  || 26.98 ||
etāni ye prayacchanti nopasarpanti te yamam  |
madhu māṣaṃ payaḥ sarpirlavaṇaṃ guḍamauṣadham  || 26.99 ||
pānīyaṃ bhūmidānaṃ ca śālīnikṣurasāṃstathā  |
āraktavāsasī ślakṣṇe dampatyorlalitādine  || 26.100 ||
saubhāgyaṃ jāyate caiva iha loke paratra ca  |
brāhmaṇe vṛttasampanne surūpe ca guṇānvite  || 26.101 ||
tithau yasyāmidaṃ deyaṃ tatte rājñi vadāmyaham  |
pratipatsu ca yā nārī pūrvāhṇe ca śucivratā  || 26.102 ||
indhanaṃ brāhmaṇe dadyātprīyatāṃ me hutāśanaḥ  |
tasyā janmāni ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgapratyaṅgasandhiṣu  || 26.103 ||
na rajo naiva santāpo jāyate rājavallabhe  |
dvitīyāyāṃ tu yā nārī navanītamudānvitā  || 26.104 ||
dadāti dvijamukhyāya sukumāratanurbhavet |
lavaṇaṃ vipravaryāya tṛtīyāyāṃ prayacchati  || 26.105 ||
gaurī me prīyatāṃ devī tasyāḥ puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
kaumārikā patiṃ prāpya tena sārddhamumā yathā  || 26.106 ||
krīḍatyavidhavā cāpi labhate sā mahadyaśaḥ  |
naktaṃ kṛtvā caturthyāṃ vai dadyādviprāya modakān  || 26.107 ||
prīyatāṃ mama deveśo gaṇanātho vināyakaḥ  |
tasyāstena phalenāśu sarvakarmasu bhāmini  || 26.108 ||
vighnaṃ na jāyate kvāpi evamāha pitāmahaḥ  |
pañcamīṃ tu tataḥ prāpya brāhmaṇe tiladā tu yā  || 26.109 ||
sā bhavedrūpasampannā yathā caiva tilottamā  |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu yā madhūkasya phaladā tu bhavetsadā  || 26.110 ||
uddiśya cāgnijaṃ devaṃ brāhmaṇe vedapārage  |
tasyāḥ putro yathā skando devasaṅgheṣu cottamaḥ  || 26.111 ||
utpadyate mahārājaḥ sarvalokeṣu pūjitaḥ  |
saptamyāṃ yā dvijaśreṣṭhaṃ suvarṇena prapūjayet || 26.112 ||
uddiśya jagato nāthaṃ devadevaṃ divākaram  |
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ yadvai kathitaṃ dvijasattamaiḥ  || 26.113 ||
tatte rājñi pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ sati  |
dadrūcitrakakuṣṭhāni maṇḍalāni vicarcikā  || 26.114 ||
na bhavantīha cāṅgeṣu pūrvakarmārjitānyapi  |
kṛṣṇāṃ dhenuṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ yā prayacchati bhāminī  || 26.115 ||
brāhmaṇe vṛttasampanne prīyatāṃ me maheśvaraḥ  |
tasyā janmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ naśyate vibhavānvitā  || 26.116 ||
jāyate nātra sandeho yasmāddānamanuttamam  |
gandhadhūpaṃ tu yā nārī bhaktyā viprāya dāpayet || 26.117 ||
kātyāyanīṃ samuddiśya navamyāṃ śṛṇu yatphalam  |
tasyā bhrātā pitā putraḥ patirvā raṇamuttamam  || 26.118 ||
prāpyate naiva sīdanti tena dānena rakṣitāḥ  |
ikṣudaṇḍarasaṃ devi daśamyāṃ yā prayacchati  || 26.119 ||
lokapālānsamuddiśya brāhmaṇe vyaṅgavarjite  |
tena dānena sā nityaṃ sarvalokasya vallabhā  || 26.120 ||
jāyate nātra sandeha ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt |
ekādaśyāmupoṣyātha dvādaśyāmudakapradā  || 26.121 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ samuddiśya brāhmaṇe viṣṇutatpare  |
sā sadā sparśasambhāṣairdrāvayedbhāvayejjanam  || 26.122 ||
yasmāddānaṃ maharloke hyanantamudake bhavet |
pādābhyaṅgaṃ śiro'bhyaṅgaṃ kāmamuddiśya vai dvije  || 26.123 ||
dadāti ca trayodaśyāṃ bhaktyā paramayāṅganā  |
yasyāṃ yasyāṃ mṛtā jāyedbhūyo yonyāṃ tu janmani  || 26.124 ||
tasyāṃ tasyāṃ tu sā bharturna viyujyeta karhicit |
tathāpyevaṃ caturdaśyāṃ dadyātpātramupānahau  || 26.125 ||
brahmaṇe dharmamuddiśya tasyā lokā hyanāmayāḥ  |
evaṃ ca pakṣapakṣānte śrāddhe tarpeddvijottamān  || 26.126 ||
avyucchinnā sadā rājñi santatirjāyate bhuvi  |
evaṃ te tithimāhātmyaṃ dānayogena bhāṣitam  || 26.127 ||
tathā vanaspatīnāṃ tu ārādhanavidhiṃ śṛṇu  |
jambūṃ nimbataruṃ caiva tindukaṃ madhukaṃ tathā  || 26.128 ||
āmraṃ cāmalakaṃ caiva śālmaliṃ vaṭapippalau  |
śamībilvāmalīvṛkṣaṃ kadalīṃ pāṭalīṃ tathā  || 26.129 ||
anyānpuṇyatamānvṛkṣānupetya svargamāpnuyāt || 26.130 ||

nārada uvāca -
caitre māse tu yā nārī kuryādvratamanuttamam  |
tasya vratasya cānyāni kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 26.131 ||
śrutena yena subhage durbhagatvaṃ na paśyati  |
yathā himaṃ raviṃ prāpya vilayaṃ yāti bhūtale  || 26.132 ||
tathā duḥkhaṃ ca daurbhāgyaṃ vratādasmādvilīyate  |
madhukākhyāṃ tu lalitāmārādhayati yena vai  || 26.133 ||
vidhiṃ taṃ śṛṇu subhage kathyamānaṃ sukhāvaham  |
caitre śuklatṛtīyāyāṃ susnātā śuddhamānasā  || 26.134 ||
pratimāṃ madhuvṛkṣasya śāṅkarīmumayā saha  |
kārayitvā dvijavaraiḥ pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi  || 26.135 ||
sugandhikusumairdhūpaistathā karpūrakuṅkumaiḥ  |
pūjayedvidhinā devaṃ mantrayuktena bhāminī  || 26.136 ||
pādau namaḥ śivāyeti meḍhre vai manmathāya ca  |
kālodarāyetyudaraṃ nīlakaṃṭhāya kaṇṭhakam  || 26.137 ||
śiraḥ sarvātmane pūjya umāṃ paścātprapūjayet |
kṣāmodarāyaihyudaraṃ sukaṇṭhāyai ca kaṇṭhakam  || 26.138 ||
śiraḥ saubhāgyadāyinyai paścādarghyaṃ pradāpayet || 26.139 ||
namaste devadeveśa umāvara jagatpate  |
arghyeṇānena me sarvaṃ daurbhāgyaṃ nāśaya prabho  |
iti arghyamantraḥ  || 26.140 ||
arghyaṃ dattvā tataḥ paścātkarakaṃ vāripūritam  |
madhūkapātropabhṛtaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ tu śaktitaḥ  || 26.141 ||
karakaṃ vārisampūrṇaṃ saubhāgyena tu saṃyutam  |
dattaṃ tu lalite tubhyaṃ saubhāgyādivivardhanam  |
iti karakadānamantraḥ  || 26.142 ||
mantreṇānena viprāya dadyātkarakamuttamam  |
lavaṇaṃ varjayecchuklāṃ yāvadanyāṃ tṛtīyikām  || 26.143 ||
kṣamāpya devīṃ deveśāṃ naktamadyātsvayaṃ haviḥ  |
anena vidhinā sārdhaṃ māsi māsi hyapakramet || 26.144 ||
phālgunasya tṛtīyāyāṃ śuklāyāṃ tu samāpyate  |
vaiśākhe lavaṇaṃ deyaṃ jyeṣṭhe cājyaṃ pradīyate  || 26.145 ||
āṣāḍhe māsi niṣpāvāḥ payo deyaṃ tu śrāvaṇe  |
mudgā deyā nabhasye tu śālimāśvayuje tathā  || 26.146 ||
kārttike śarkarāpātraṃ karakaṃ rasasaṃbhṛtam  |
mārgaśīrṣe tu kārpāsaṃ karakaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam  || 26.147 ||
pauṣe tu kuṅkumaṃ deyaṃ māghe pātraṃ tilairbhṛtam  |
phālgune māsi samprāpte pātraṃ modakasaṃbhṛtam  || 26.148 ||
paścāttṛtīyādeyaṃ yattatpūrvasyāṃ vivarjayet |
vidhānamāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ sāmānyaṃ manasaḥ priye  || 26.149 ||
pratimāṃ madhuvṛkṣasya tāmeva pratipūjayet |
tasmai sarvaṃ tu viprāya ācāryāya pradīyate  || 26.150 ||
tataḥ saṃvatsarasyānte udyāpanavidhiṃ śṛṇu  |
madhuvṛkṣaṃ tato gatvā bahusambhārasaṃvṛtaḥ  || 26.151 ||
nikhanetpratimāṃ madhye mādhūkīṃ madhukasya ca  |
tatrasthaṃ pūjayetsarvamumādehārddhadhāriṇam  || 26.152 ||
pūjopahārairvipulaiḥ kuṅkumena punaḥpunaḥ  |
ślakṣṇābhiḥ puṣpamālābhiḥ kausumbhaiḥ kesareṇa ca  || 26.153 ||
kausumbhe vāsasī śubhre atasīpuṣpasannibhe  |
paridhāpya tāṃ pratimāṃ dampatī ravisaṃkhyayā  || 26.154 ||
upānadyugalaiśchatraiḥ kaṇṭhasūtraiḥ sakaṇṭhikaiḥ  |
kaṭakairaṅgulīyaiśca śayanīyaiḥ śubhāstṛtaiḥ  || 26.155 ||
kuṅkumena viliptāṅgau bahupuṣpaiśca pūjitau  |
bhojayedvividhai ratnairmadhūkāvāsake sthitau  || 26.156 ||
bhuktotthitau tu viśrāmya śayyāsu ca kṣamāpayet |
gurumūlaṃ yataḥ sarvaṃ gururjñeyo maheśvaraḥ  || 26.157 ||
prīte gurau tataḥ sarvaṃ jagatprītaṃ surāsuram  |
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yatkiṃciddayitaṃ gṛhe  || 26.158 ||
tatsarvaṃ gurave deyamātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  |
idaṃ tu dhanibhirdeyamanyairdeyaṃ yathocyate  || 26.159 ||
dāmpatyamekaṃ vidhivatpratipūjya śubhavrataiḥ  |
dvitīyaṃ gurudāmpatyaṃ vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet || 26.160 ||
tataḥ kṣamāpayeddevīṃ devaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ gurum  |
yathā tvaṃ devi lalite na viyuktāsi śambhunā  || 26.161 ||
tathā me patiputrāṇāmaviyogaḥ pradīyatām  |
anena vidhinā kṛtvā tṛtīyāṃ madhusaṃjñikām  || 26.162 ||
indrāṇī cendrapatnītvamavāpa sutamuttamam  |
saubhāgyaṃ sarvalokeṣu sarvarddhisukhamuttamam  || 26.163 ||
anena vidhinā yā tu kumārī vratamācaret |
śobhanaṃ patimāpnoti yathendrāṇyā śatakratuḥ  || 26.164 ||
durbhagā subhagatvaṃ ca subhagā putriṇī bhavet |
putriṇyakṣayamāpnoti na śokaṃ paśyati kvacit || 26.165 ||
anekajanmajanitaṃ daurbhāgyaṃ naśyati dhruvam  |
mṛtā tu tridivaṃ prāpya umayā saha modate  || 26.166 ||
kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ bhuktvā bhogān yathepsitān  |
punastu sambhave loke pārthivaṃ patimāpnuyāt || 26.167 ||
subhagā rūpasampannā pārthivaṃ janayetsutam  || 26.168 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ vratānāmuttamaṃ vratam  |
anyatpṛcchasva subhage vāñchitaṃ yaddhṛdi sthitam  || 26.169 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye madhūkatṛtīyāvratavidhānamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 27

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā rājñī vacanamabravīt |
prasādaṃ kuru viprendra gṛhṇa dānaṃ yathepsitam  || 27.1 ||
suvarṇamaṇiratnāni vastrāṇi vividhāni ca  |
tatte dārayāmi viprendra yaccānyadapi durlabham  || 27.2 ||
rājñyāstu vacanaṃ śrutvā nārado vākyamabravīt |
anyeṣāṃ dīyatāṃ bhadre ye dvijāḥ kṣīṇavṛttayaḥ  || 27.3 ||
vayaṃ tu sarvasampannā bhaktigrāhyāḥ sadaiva hi  |
ityuktā sā tadā rājñī vedavedāṅgapāragān  || 27.4 ||
āhūya brāhmaṇānniḥsvāndātuṃ samupacakrame  |
yatkiṃcinnāradenoktaṃ dānasaubhāgyavardhanam  || 27.5 ||
tena dānena me nityaṃ prīyetāṃ hariśaṅkarau  |
tato rājñī ca sā prāha nāradaṃ munipuṃgavam  || 27.6 ||

rājñyuvāca -
dānaṃ dattaṃ tvayoktaṃ yadbhartṛkarmaparaṃ hi tat |
ājanmajanma me bhartā bhavedbāṇo dvijottama  || 27.7 ||
nānyo hi daivataṃ tāta muktvā bāṇaṃ dvijottama  |
tena satyena me bhartā jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 27.8 ||
nānyo dharmo bhavetstrīṇāṃ daivataṃ hi patiryathā  |
tathāpi tava vākyena dānaṃ dattaṃ yathāvidhi  || 27.9 ||
svakaṃ karma kariṣyāmo bhartāraṃ prati mānada  |
brahmarṣe gaccha cedānīṃ tvamāśīrvādaḥ pradīyatām  || 27.10 ||
tatheti tāmanujñāpya nārado nṛpasattama  |
sarvāsāṃ mānasaṃ hṛtvā anyataḥ kṛtamānasaḥ  || 27.11 ||
jagāmādarśanaṃ vipraḥ pūjyamānastu khecaraiḥ  |
tato gatamanaskāstā bhartāraṃ prati bhārata  || 27.12 ||
vivarṇā niṣprabhā jātā nāradena vimohitāḥ  || 27.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye tripurakṣobhaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 28

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etasminnantare rudro narmadātaṭamāsthitaḥ  |
krīḍate hyumayā sārddhaṃ nāradastatra cāgataḥ  || 28.1 ||
praṇamya devadeveśamumayā saha śaṅkaram  |
vyajñāpayattadā devaṃ yadvṛttaṃ tripure tadā  || 28.2 ||
gato'haṃ svāminirdeśādyatra tadbāṇamandiram  |
dṛṣṭā bāṇaṃ yathānyāyaṃ gato hyantaḥpuraṃ mahat || 28.3 ||
tatra bhāryāsahasrāṇi dṛṣṭvā bāṇasya dhīmataḥ  |
yathāyogyaṃ yathākāmamāgataḥ kṣobhya tatpuram  || 28.4 ||
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā sādhu sādhviti pūjayan  |
cintayāmāsa deveśo bhramaṇaṃ tripurasya hi  || 28.5 ||
karamuktaṃ yathā cakraṃ viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā  |
mahāvegaṃ mahāyāmaṃ rakṣitaṃ tejasā mama  || 28.6 ||
sa ca me bhaktinirato bāṇo loke ca viśrutaḥ  |
bhāratī ca mayā dattā brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 28.7 ||
evaṃ sa suciraṃ kālaṃ devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
cintayitvā sunirvāṇaṃ kāryaṃ prati janeśvaraḥ  || 28.8 ||
tato'sau mandaraṃ dhyātvā cāpe kṛtvā guṇe mahīm  |
viṣṇuṃ sanātanaṃ devaṃ bāṇe dhyātvā trilocanaḥ  || 28.9 ||
phale hutāśanaṃ devaṃ jvalantaṃ sarvatomukham  |
suparṇaṃ puṅkhayormadhye jave vāyuṃ prakalpya ca  || 28.10 ||
rathaṃ mahīmayaṃ kṛtvā dhuri tāvaśvināvubhau  |
akṣe sureśvaraṃ devamagrakīlyāṃ dhanādhipam  || 28.11 ||
yamaṃ tu dakṣiṇe pārśve vāme kālaṃ sudāruṇam  |
ādityacandrau cakre tu gandharvānārakādiṣu  || 28.12 ||
yantāraṃ ca surajyeṣṭhaṃ vedānkṛtvā hayottamān  |
khalīnādiṣu cāṅgāni raśmīṃśchandāṃsi cākarot || 28.13 ||
kṛtvā pratodamoṃkāraṃ mukhagrāhyaṃ maheśvaraḥ  |
dhātāraṃ cāgrataḥ kṛtvā vidhātāraṃ ca pṛṣṭhataḥ  || 28.14 ||
mārutātsarvato digbhya ūrdhvayantre tathaiva ca  |
mahoragapiśācāṃśca siddhavidyādharāṃstathā  || 28.15 ||
gaṇāṃśca bhūtasaṅghāṃśca sarve sarvāṅgasaṃdhiṣu  |
yugamadhye sthito meruryugasyādho mahāgiriḥ  || 28.16 ||
sarpā yantrasthitā ghorāḥ śamye varuṇanairṛtau  |
gāyatrī caiva sāvitrī sthite te raśmibandhane  || 28.17 ||
satyaṃ rathadhvaje śaucaṃ damaṃ rakṣāṃ samantataḥ  |
rathaṃ devamayaṃ kṛtvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 28.18 ||
saṃnaddhaḥ kavacī khaḍgī baddhagodhāṅgulitravān  |
baddhā parikaraṃ gāḍhaṃ jaṭājūṭaṃ niyamya ca  || 28.19 ||
sajjaṃ kṛtvā dhanurdivyaṃ yojayitvā rathottamam  |
rathamadhye sthito devaḥ śuśubhe ca yudhiṣṭhira  || 28.20 ||
dhanuṣaḥ śabdanādenākampayacca jagattrayam  |
sthānaṃ kṛtvā tu vaiśākhaṃ nibhṛtaṃ saṃsthito haraḥ  || 28.21 ||
nirīkṣya suciraṃ kālaṃ kopasaṃraktalocanaḥ  |
dhyātvā taṃ paramaṃ mantramātmānaṃ ca nirudhya saḥ  || 28.22 ||
mumoca sahasā bāṇaṃ purasya vadhakāṅkṣayā  |
yadā trīṇi sametāni antarikṣasthitāni tu  || 28.23 ||
tataḥ kālanimeṣārdhaṃ dṛṣṭvaikyaṃ tripurasya ca  |
triparvaṇā triśalyena tatastānyavasādayat || 28.24 ||
tato lokā bhayatrastāstripure bharatottama  |
sarvāsuravināśāya kālarūpā bhayāvahāḥ  || 28.25 ||
aṭṭahāsān pramuñcanti kaṣṭarūpā narāstadā  |
nimeṣonmeṣaṇaṃ caiva kurvanti lipikarmasu  || 28.26 ||
niṣpandanayanā martyāścitreṣvālikhitā iva  |
devāyatanagā devā raṭanti prahasanti ca  |
svapne paśyanti cātmānaṃ raktāmbaravibhūṣitam  || 28.27 ||
raktamālyottamāṅgāśca patantaḥ kārdame hrade  |
paśyanti nāma cātmānaṃ satailābhyaṅgamastakam  || 28.28 ||
paśyanti yānamārūḍhaṃ rāsabhaiśca nṛpottama  |
saṃvartako mahāvāyuryugāntapratimo mahān  || 28.29 ||
gṛhānunmūlayāmāsa vṛkṣajātīnanekaśaḥ  |
bhūmikampāḥ sanirghātā ulkāpātāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 28.30 ||
rudhiraṃ varṣate devo miśritaṃ karkarairbahu  |
agnikuṇḍeṣu viprāṇāṃ hutaḥ samyagghutāśanaḥ  || 28.31 ||
jvalate dhūmasaṃyukto visphuliṅgakaṇaiḥ saha  |
kuṃjarā vimadā jātāsturagāḥ sattvavarjitāḥ  || 28.32 ||
avāditāni vādyante vāditrāṇi sahasraśaḥ  |
dhvajā hyakampitāḥ petuśchatrāṇi vividhāni ca  || 28.33 ||
jvalati pādapāstatra parṇāni ca sabhaṃ tataḥ  |
sarvaṃ tadvyākulībhūtaṃ hāhākārasamanvitam  || 28.34 ||
udyānāni vicitrāṇi prababhañja prabhañjanaḥ  |
tena saṃpreritāḥ sarve jvalanti viśikhāḥ śikhāḥ  || 28.35 ||
vṛkṣagulmalatāvallyo gṛhāṇi ca samantataḥ  |
digvibhāgaiśca sarvaiśca pravṛtto havyavāhanaḥ  || 28.36 ||
sarvaṃ kiṃśukaparṇābhaṃ prajvalaccaiva dṛśyate  |
gṛhādgṛhaṃ tadā gantuṃ naiva dhūmena śakyate  || 28.37 ||
harakopāgninirdagdhāḥ krandante tripure janāḥ  |
pradīptaṃ sarvato dikṣu dahyate tripuraṃ param  || 28.38 ||
patanti śikharāgrāṇi viśīrṇāni sahasraśaḥ  |
pāvako dhūmasaṃpṛkto dahyamānaḥ samantataḥ  || 28.39 ||
nṛtyanvai vyāptadigdeśaḥ kāntāreṣvabhidhāvati  |
devāgāreṣu sarveṣu gṛheṣvaṭṭālakeṣu ca  || 28.40 ||
pravṛtto hutabhuktatra pure kālapracoditaḥ  |
dadāha lokānsarvatra harakopaprakopitaḥ  || 28.41 ||
dahate traipuraṃ lokaṃ bālavṛddhasamanvitam  |
sapuraṃ sagṛhadvāraṃ savāhanavanaṃ nṛpa  || 28.42 ||
kecidbhojanasaktāśca pānāsaktāstathāpare  |
aparā nṛtyagīteṣu saṃsaktā vārayoṣitaḥ  || 28.43 ||
anyonyaṃ ca pariṣvajya hutāśanaśikhārditāḥ  |
dahyamānā nṛpaśreṣṭha sarve gacchantyacetanāḥ  || 28.44 ||
athānye dānavāstatra dahyante'gnivimohitāḥ  |
na śaktāścānyato gantuṃ dhūmenākulitānanāḥ  |
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇā nalinyo hemapaṅkajāḥ  || 28.45 ||
dahyante vividhāstatra vāpyaḥ kūpāśca bhārata  |
dṛśyante'naladagdhāni purodyānāni dīrghikāḥ  |
amlānaiḥ paṅkajaiśchannā vistīrṇāvasuyojanāḥ  || 28.46 ||
girikūṭanibhāstatra prāsādā ratnaśobhitāḥ  |
dṛśyante'nalasaṃdagdhā viśīrṇā dharaṇītale  || 28.47 ||
narastrībālavṛddheṣu dahyamāneṣu sarvataḥ  |
nirdayaṃ jvalate vahnirhāhākāro mahānabhūt |
kācicca sukhasaṃsuptāpramattānyā nṛpottama  || 28.48 ||
krīḍitvā ca suvistīrṇaśayanasthā varāṅganā  |
kācitsuptā viśālākṣī hārāvalivibhūṣitā  |
dhūmenākulitā dīnā nyapataddhavyavāhane  || 28.49 ||
kācittasminpure dīpte putrasnehānulālasā  |
putramāliṅgate gāḍhaṃ dahyate tripure'gninā  || 28.50 ||
kācitkanakavarṇābhā indranīlavibhūṣitā  |
bhartāraṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā patitā tasya copari  || 28.51 ||
kācidādityavarṇābhā prasuptā tu priyopari  |
agnijvālāhatā gāḍhaṃ kaṃṭhamāliṅgate nṛpa  || 28.52 ||
medhavarṇā parā nārī calatkanakamekhalā  |
śvetavastrottarīyā tu papāta dharaṇītale  || 28.53 ||
kācitkundenduvarṇābhā nīlaratnavibhūṣitā  |
śirasā prāñjalirbhūtvā vijñāpayati pāvakam  || 28.54 ||
kasyāścijjvalate vastraṃ keśāḥ kasyāśca bhārata  |
jvalajjvalanasaṅkāśairhemabhāṇḍaistrasaṃhita ca  || 28.55 ||
kācitprabhūtaduḥkhārtā vilalāpa varāṅganā  |
bhasmībhūtaṃ patiṃ dṛṣṭvā krandantī kurarī yathā  || 28.56 ||
āliṅgya gāḍhaṃ sahasā patitā tasya mūrdhani  |
kācicca bahuduḥkhārtā vyalapatstrī svaveśmani  || 28.57 ||
bhasmasācca kṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā krandate kurarī yathā  |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ kāciddṛṣṭvā vigatacetanam  || 28.58 ||
vepate patitā bhūmau kheditā vaḍavā yathā  |
itaścetaśca kācicca dahyamānā varāṅganā  || 28.59 ||
nāpaśyadbālamutsaṅge viparītamukhī sthitā  |
kumbhilasya gṛhaṃ dagdhaṃ patitaṃ dharaṇītale  || 28.60 ||
kūṣmāṇḍasya ca dhūmrasya kuhakasya bakasya ca  |
virūpanayanasyāpi virūpākṣasya caiva hi  || 28.61 ||
śumbho ḍimbhaśca raudraśca prahlādaścāsurottamaḥ  |
daṇḍapāṇirvipāṇiśca siṃhavaktrastathānagha  || 28.62 ||
dundubhaścaiva saṃhrādo ḍiṇḍirmuṇḍistathaiva ca  |
bāṇabhrātā ca bāṇaśca kravyādavyāghravaktrakau  || 28.63 ||
evamanye'pi ye keciddānavā baladarpitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ gṛhe tathā vahnirjvalate nirdayo nṛpa  |
dahyamānāḥ striyastāta vilapanti gṛhe gṛhe  || 28.64 ||
karuṇākṣaravādinyo nirādhārā gatāḥ śivam  |
yadi vairaṃ surāreśca puruṣoparipāvaka  || 28.65 ||
striyaḥ kimaparādhyanti gṛhapañjarakokilāḥ  |
anirdayo nṛśaṃsastvaṃ kaste kopaḥ striyaṃ prati  || 28.66 ||
kiṃ tvayā na śrutaṃ loke avadhyāḥ sarvathā striyaḥ  |
kiṃ tu tubhyaṃ guṇo hyasti dahane pavaneritaḥ  || 28.67 ||
na kāruṇyaṃ tvayā kiṃciddākṣiṇyaṃ ca striyaṃ prati  |
dayāṃ mlecchā hi kurvanti vacanaṃ vīkṣya yoṣitām  || 28.68 ||
mlecchānāmapi ca mleccho durnivāryo hyacetanaḥ  |
evaṃ vilapamānānāṃ strīṇāṃ tatraiva bhārata  || 28.69 ||
jvālākalāpabahulaḥ prajvalatyeva pāvakaḥ  |
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā tato bāṇo dahyamāna uvāca ha  || 28.70 ||
avajñāya vinaṣṭo'haṃ pāpātmā haramañjasā  |
mayā pāpena mūrkheṇa ye lokā nāśitā dhruvam  || 28.71 ||
gobrāhmaṇā hatā nityamiha loke paratra ca  |
nāśitānyannapānāni maṭhārāmāśramāstathā  || 28.72 ||
ṛṣīṇāmāśramāścaiva devārāmā gaṇālayāḥ  |
tena pāpena me dhvaṃsastapasaśca balasya ca  || 28.73 ||
kiṃ dhanena kariṣyāmi rājyeṇāntaḥpureṇa ca  || 28.74 ||
varaṃ śaṅkarapādau ca śaraṇaṃ yāmi mūḍhadhīḥ  |
na mātā na pitā caiva na bandhurnāparo janaḥ  || 28.75 ||
muktvā caiva maheśānaṃ paramārtiharaṃ param  |
ātmanā ca kṛtaṃ pāpamātmanaiva tu bhujyate  || 28.76 ||
ahaṃ punaḥ samastaiśca dahyāmi saha sādhubhiḥ  |
evamuktvā śivaṃ liṅgaṃ kṛtvā tanmastakopari  || 28.77 ||
nirjagāma gṛhācchīghraṃ pāvakenāvaguṇṭhitaḥ  |
sa khinnaḥ svinnagātrastu praskhalaṃstu muhurmuhuḥ  || 28.78 ||
haraṃ gadgadayā vācā stuvanvai śaraṇaṃ yayau  |
tvatkopānalanirdagdho yadi vadhyo'smi śaṅkara  || 28.79 ||
tvatprasādānmahādeva mā me liṅgaṃ praṇaśyatu  |
arcitaṃ me suraśreṣṭha dhyātaṃ bhaktyā mayā vibho  || 28.80 ||
prāṇādiṣṭatamaṃ deva tasmādrakṣitumarhasi  |
yadi te'hamanugrāhyo vadhyo vā surasattama  || 28.81 ||
pratijanma mahādeva tvadbhaktiracalāstu me  |
paśukīṭapataṅgeṣu tiryagyonigateṣu ca  |
svakarmaṇā mahādeva tvadbhaktiracalāstu me  || 28.82 ||
evamuktvā mahābhāgo bāṇo bhaktimatāṃ varaḥ  |
stotreṇa devadeveśaṃ chandayāmāsa bhārata  || 28.83 ||

bāṇa uvāca -
śiva śaṅkara sarvaharāya namo bhavabhītabhayārtiharāya namaḥ  |
kusumāyudhadehavināśaṃkara pramadāpriyakāmaka deva namaḥ  || 28.84 ||
jaya pārvatīśa paramārthasāra jaya viracitabhīmabhujaṅgahāra  |
jaya nirmalabhasmaviliptagātra jaya mantramūla jagadekapātra  || 28.85 ||
jaya viṣadharakapilajaṭākalāpa jaya bhairavavighṛtapinākacāpa  |
jaya viṣamanayanaparimuktasaṅga jaya śaṅkara dhṛtagāṅgataraṅga  || 28.86 ||
jaya bhīmarūpa khaṭvāṅgahasta śaśiśekhara jaya jagatāṃ praśasta  |
jaya sukhareśa suralokasāra jaya sarvasakalanirdagdhasāra  || 28.87 ||
jaya kīrtanīya jagatāṃ pavitra jaya vṛṣāṅka bahuvidhacaritra  |
jaya viracitanarakaṅkālamāla aghāsuradehakaṅkālakāla  || 28.88 ||
jaya nīlakaṃṭha varavṛṣabhagamana jaya sakalalokaduritānuśamana  |
jaya siddhasurāsuravinatacaraṇa jaya rudra raudrabhavajaladhitaraṇa  || 28.89 ||
jaya giriśa sureśvaramānanīya jaya sūkṣmarūpa saṃcitanīya  |
jaya dagdhatripura viśvasattva jaya sakalaśāstraparamārthatattva  || 28.90 ||
jaya duravabodha saṃsāratāra kalikaluṣamahārṇavaghoratāra  |
jaya surāsuradevagaṇeśa namo hayavānarasiṃhagajendramukha  || 28.91 ||
atihrasvasthūlasudīrghatama upalabdhirna śakyate te hyamaraiḥ  |
praṇato'smi nirañjana te caraṇau jaya sāmba sulocanakāntihara  || 28.92 ||
aprāpya tvāṃ kimatyantamucchrayī na vināśayet |
atipramāthi ca tadā tapo mahatsudāruṇam  || 28.93 ||
na putrabāndhavā dārā na samastaḥ suhṛjjanaḥ  |
saṅkaṭe'bhyupagacchanti vrajantamekagāminam  || 28.94 ||
yadeva karma kaivalyaṃ kṛtaṃ tena śubhāśubham  |
tadeva sārthavattasya bhavatyagre tu gacchataḥ  || 28.95 ||
nirdhanasyaiva carato na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit |
dhanībhayairna mucyeta dhanaṃ tasmāttyajāmyaham  || 28.96 ||
lubdhāḥ pāpāni kurvanti śuddhāṃśā naiva mānavāḥ  |
śrutvā dharmasya sarvasvaṃ śrutvā caivāvadhārya tat || 28.97 ||
tvaṃ viṣṇustvaṃ jagannātho brahmarūpaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
indrastvaṃ devadeveśa suranātha namo'stu te  || 28.98 ||
tvaṃ kṣitirvaruṇaścaiva pavanastvaṃ hutāśanaḥ  |
tvaṃ dīkṣā yajamānaśca ākāśaṃ soma eva ca  || 28.99 ||
tvaṃ sūryastvaṃ tu vitteśo yamastvaṃ gurureva ca  |
tvayā vyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ bhāsvatā yathā  || 28.100 ||
etadbāṇakṛtaṃ stotraṃ śrutvā devo maheśvaraḥ  |
krodhaṃ muktvā prasannātmā tadā vacanamabravīt || 28.101 ||

īśvara uvāca -
na bhetavyaṃ na bhetavyamadyaprabhṛti dānava  |
sauvarṇe bhavane tiṣṭha mama pārśve'thavā punaḥ  || 28.102 ||
putrapautraprapautraiśca bāndhavaiḥ saha bhāryayā  |
adyaprabhṛti vatsa tvamavadhyaḥ sarvaśatruṣu  || 28.103 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhūyastasya varo datto devadevena bhārata  |
svarge martye ca pātāle pūjitaḥ sasurāsuraiḥ  || 28.104 ||
akṣayaścāvyayaścaiva vasa tvaṃ vai yathāsukham  |
tato nivārayāmāsa rudraḥ saptaśikhaṃ tadā  || 28.105 ||
tṛtīyaṃ rakṣitaṃ tasya puraṃ devena śambhunā  |
jvālāmālākulaṃ cānyatpatitaṃ dharaṇītale  || 28.106 ||
ardhena prasthitādūrdhvaṃ tasya jvālā divaṃ gatāḥ  |
hāhākāro mahāṃstatra ṛṣisaṅghairudīritaḥ  || 28.107 ||
daivataiśca mahābhāgaiḥ siddhavidyādharādibhiḥ  |
ekaṃ tu patitaṃ tatra śrīśaile khaṇḍamuttaram  || 28.108 ||
dvitīyaṃ patitaṃ rājañchaile hyamarakaṇṭake  |
prajvalatpatitaṃ tatra tena jvāleśvaraṃ smṛtam  || 28.109 ||
dagdhe tu tripure rājanpatite khaṇḍa uttame  |
rudro devaḥ sthitastatra jvālāmālānivārakaḥ  || 28.110 ||
hāhākāraparāṇāṃ tu ṛṣīṇāṃ rakṣaṇāya ca  |
svayaṃ mūrtirmaheśānumāvṛṣabhasaṃyutaḥ  || 28.111 ||
manasāpi smaredyastu bhaktyā hyamarakaṇṭakam  |
cāndrāyaṇādhikaṃ puṇyaṃ sa labhennātra saṃśayaḥ  || 28.112 ||
atipuṇyo giriśreṣṭho yasmādbharatasattama  |
asmānnityaṃ bhavedrājansarvapāpakṣayaṃkaraḥ  || 28.113 ||
nānādrumalatākīrṇo nānāpuṣpopaśobhitaḥ  |
nānāgulmalatākīrṇo nānāvallībhirāvṛtaḥ  || 28.114 ||
siṃhavyāghrasamākīrṇo mṛgayūthairalaṃkṛtaḥ  |
śvāpadānāṃ ca ghoṣeṇa nityaṃ pramudito'bhavat || 28.115 ||
brahmendraviṣṇupramukhairhyamaraiśca sahasraśaḥ  |
sevyate devadeveśaḥ śaṅkarastatra parvate  || 28.116 ||
patanaṃ kurute yo'sminparvate'marakaṇṭake  |
krīḍate kramaśo rājanbhuvanāni caturdaśa  || 28.117 ||
aindraṃ vāhnaṃ ca kauberaṃ vāyavyaṃ yāmyameva ca  |
nairṛtyaṃ vāruṇaṃ caiva saumyaṃ sauraṃ tathaiva ca  || 28.118 ||
brāhmaṃ ca padamakliṣṭaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tadanantaram  |
umārudraṃ mahābhāga aiśvaraṃ tadanantaram  || 28.119 ||
paraṃ sadāśivaṃ śāntaṃ sūkṣmaṃ jyotiratīndriyam  |
tasminyāti layaṃ dhīro vidhinā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 28.120 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ko'pyatra vidhiruddiṣṭaḥ patane ṛṣisattama  |
etanme sarvamācakṣva saṃśayo'sti mahāmune  || 28.121 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi taṃ vidhiṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
yatkṛtvā prathamaṃ karma nipatettadanantaram  || 28.122 ||
kṛtvā kṛcchratrayaṃ pūrvaṃ japtvā lakṣaṃ daśaiva tu  |
śākayāvakabhukcaiva śucistriṣavaṇo nṛpa  || 28.123 ||
trikālamarcayedīśaṃ devadevaṃ trilocanam  |
daśāṃśena tu rājendra homaṃ tatraiva kārayet || 28.124 ||
lakṣavāraṃ japeddevaṃ gandhamālyaiśca pūjayet |
rātrau svapne tadā paśyedvimānasthaṃ tataḥ kṣipet || 28.125 ||
anenaiva vidhānena ātmānaṃ yastu nikṣipet |
svargalokamanuprāpya krīḍate tridaśaiḥ saha  || 28.126 ||
triṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi triṃśatkoṭyastathaiva ca  |
muktvā manoramānbhogāṃstadā gacchenmahītalam  || 28.127 ||
