pṛthivīmekacchatreṇa bhunakti lokapūjitaḥ  |
vyādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 28.128 ||
jvāleśvaraṃ tu tattīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
tatra jvālā nadī pārtha prasrutā śivanirmitā  || 28.129 ||
nirvāpya tadbāṇapuraṃ revayā saha saṃgatā  |
tatra snātvā mahārāja vidhinā mantrasaṃyutaḥ  || 28.130 ||
tilasaṃmiśratoyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
piṇḍadānena ca pitḥn paiṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 28.131 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 28.132 ||
amarāṇāṃ śataiścaiva sevito hyamareśvaraḥ  |
tathaiva ṛṣisaṅghaiśca tena puṇyatamo mahān  || 28.133 ||
samantādyojanaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyaṃ hyamarakaṇṭakam  |
rudrakoṭisamopetaṃ tena tatpuṇyamuttamam  || 28.134 ||
tasya parvatarājasya yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇam  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 28.135 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ caiva kāyikaṃ trividhaṃ ca yat |
naśyate pātakaṃ sarvamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 28.136 ||
amareśvarapārśve ca tīrthaṃ śakreśvaraṃ nṛpa  |
tapastaptvā purā tatra śakreṇa sthāpitaṃ kila  || 28.137 ||
kuśāvartaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ brahmaṇā ca kṛtaṃ śubham  |
brahmakuṇḍamiti khyātaṃ haṃsatīrthaṃ tathā param  || 28.138 ||
ambarīṣasya tīrthaṃ ca mahākāleśvaraṃ tathā  |
kāveryāḥ pūrvabhāge ca tīrthaṃ vai mātṛkeśvaram  || 28.139 ||
etāni dakṣiṇe tīre revāyā bharatarṣabha  |
saṃsevanasnānadānaiḥ pāpasaṅghaharāṇi ca  || 28.140 ||
bhṛgutuṅge mahārāja prasiddho bhairavaḥ śivaḥ  |
tasya yāmyavibhāge ca tīrthaṃ vai capaleśvaram  || 28.141 ||
etau sthitau duḥkhaharau revāyā uttare taṭe  |
tāvabhyarcya tathā natvā samyagyātrāphalaṃ bhavet |
adṛṣṭapūjitau tau hi narāṇāṃ vighnakārakau  || 28.142 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tripuravidhvaṃsane jvāleśvaratīrthāmareśvatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 29

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kāverīti ca vikhyātā triṣu lokeṣu sattama  |
māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāmi tasyā mārkaṇḍa tattvataḥ  || 29.1 ||
kīdṛśaṃ darśanaṃ tasyāḥ phalaṃ sparśe'thavā vibho  |
snāne jāpye'thavā dāna upavāse tathā mune  || 29.2 ||
kathayasva mahābhāga kāverīsaṅgame phalam  |
dharmaḥ śruto'tha dṛṣṭo vā kathito vā kṛto'pi vā  || 29.3 ||
anumodito vā viprendra punātīti śrutaṃ mayā  |
yathā dharmaprasaṅge tu mune dharmo'pi jāyate  || 29.4 ||
svargaśca narakaścaiva ityevaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ  || 29.5 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ tvayādhunā  |
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā kāverīphalamuttamam  || 29.6 ||
asti yakṣo mahāsattvaḥ kuberonāma viśrutaḥ  |
so'pi tīrthaprabhāvena rājanyakṣādhipo'bhavat || 29.7 ||
tacchṛṇuṣva vidhānena bhaktyā paramayā nṛpa  |
siddhiṃ prāpto mahābhāga kāverīsaṅgamena tu  || 29.8 ||
kāveryā narmadāyāstu saṅgame lokaviśrute  |
tatra snātvā śucirbhūtvā kuberaḥ satyavikramaḥ  || 29.9 ||
vidhivanniyamaṃ kṛtvā śāstrayuktyā narottama  |
ārādhayanmahādevamekacittaḥ sanātanam  || 29.10 ||
ekāhāro vasanmāsaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhāhnakālikaḥ  |
pakṣopavāsī nyavasatkaṃcitkālaṃ nṛpottama  || 29.11 ||
mūlaśākaphalaiścānyaṃ kālaṃ nayati buddhimān  |
kiṃcitkālaṃ vasaṃstatra tīrthe śaivālabhojanaḥ  || 29.12 ||
parākeṇānayatkālaṃ kṛcchreṇāpi ca mānada  |
cāndrāyaṇena cāpyanyamanyaṃ vāyvambubhojanaḥ  || 29.13 ||
evaṃ tatra naraśreṣṭha kāmarāgavivarjitaḥ  |
sthito varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ karṣayansvaṃ tathā vapuḥ  || 29.14 ||
tato varṣaśatasyānte devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
tuṣṭastu parayā bhaktyā tamuvāca hasanniva  || 29.15 ||
bhobho yakṣa mahāsattva varaṃ varaya suvrata  |
parituṣṭo'smi te bhaktyā tava dāsye yathepsitam  || 29.16 ||

yakṣa uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa umayā saha śaṅkara  |
adyaprabhṛti sarveṣāṃ yakṣāṇāmadhipo bhave  || 29.17 ||
akṣayaścāvyayaścaiva tava bhaktipuraḥsaraḥ  |
dharme matiṃ ca me nityaṃ dadasva parameśvara  || 29.18 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yattvayā prārthitaṃ sarvaṃ phalaṃ dharmasya tattathā  |
ityevamuktvā taṃ tatra jagāmādarśanaṃ haraḥ  || 29.19 ||
so'pi snātvā vidhānena saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
āmantrayitvā tattīrthaṃ kṛtārthaśca gṛhaṃ yayau  || 29.20 ||
pūjitastatra yakṣaistu so'bhiṣikto vidhānataḥ  |
cakāra vipulaṃ tatra rājyamīpsitamuttamam  || 29.21 ||
tatra cānye surāḥ siddhā yakṣagandharvakiṃnarāḥ  |
gaṇāścāpsarasāṃ tatra ṛṣayaśca tathānagha  || 29.22 ||
kāverīsaṅgamaṃ tena sarvapāpaharaṃ viduḥ  |
svargāṇāmapi sarveṣāṃ dvārametadyudhiṣṭhira  || 29.23 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
kāverīsaṅgame snātvā yairdattaṃ hi tilodakam  || 29.24 ||
daśa pūrve pare tāta mātṛtaḥ pitṛtastathā  |
pitaraḥ pitāmahāstena uddhṛtā narakārṇavāt || 29.25 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tatra snāyīta mānavaḥ  |
arcayedīśvaraṃ devaṃ yadīcchecchāśvatīṃ gatim  || 29.26 ||
kāverīsaṅgame rājansnānadānārcanaṃ naraiḥ  |
kṛtaṃ bhaktyā naraśreṣṭha aśvamedhādhikaṃ phalam  || 29.27 ||
homena cākṣayaḥ svargo japādāyurvivardhate  |
dhyānato nityamāyāti padaṃ śivakalātmakam  || 29.28 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe nareśvara  |
agniloke vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 29.29 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ yadvai tacchṛṇuṣva narottama  || 29.30 ||
gandharvāpsaraḥsaṃkīrṇe vimāne sūryasannibhe  |
vījyamāno varastrībhirdaivataiḥ saha modate  || 29.31 ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca  |
krīḍate rudralokasthastadante bhuvi cāgataḥ  || 29.32 ||
bhogavāndānaśīlaśca jāyate pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
ādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 29.33 ||
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇā kāverī sā sarinnṛpa  |
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātā narmadāsaṅgame sadā  || 29.34 ||
jitavākkāyacittāśca dhyeyadhyānaratāstathā  |
kāverīsaṅgame tāta te'pi mokṣamavāpnuyuḥ  || 29.35 ||
śṛṇu te'nyatpravakṣyāmi āścaryaṃ nṛpasattama  |
triṣu lokeṣu kā tvanyā dṛśyate saritā samā  || 29.36 ||
labdhaṃ yairnarmadātoyaṃ ye ca kuryuḥ pradakṣiṇam  |
ye pibanti jalaṃ tatra te puṇyā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 29.37 ||
na teṣāṃ santaticchedo daśa janmāni pañca ca  |
teṣāṃ pāpaṃ vilīyeta himaṃ sūryodaye yathā  || 29.38 ||
gaṅgāyamunasaṅge vai yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ  |
tatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ kāverīsnānamācaran  || 29.39 ||
bhaume tu bhūtajāyoge vyatīpāte ca saṃkrame  |
rāhusomasamāyoge tadevāṣṭaguṇaṃ smṛtam  || 29.40 ||
aśītiśca yavāḥ proktā gaṅgāyāmunasaṅgame  |
kāverīnarmadāyoge tadevāṣṭaguṇaṃ smṛtam  || 29.41 ||
gaṅgā ṣaṣṭisahasraistu kṣetrapālaiḥ prapūjyate  |
tadardhairanyatīrthāni rakṣante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 29.42 ||
amareśvare tu saritāṃ ye yogāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  |
te tvaśītisahasraistu kṣetrapālaistu rakṣitāḥ  || 29.43 ||
tathāmareśvare yāmye liṅgaṃ vai capaleśvaram  |
dvitīyaṃ caṇḍahastākhyaṃ dve liṅge tīrtharakṣake  || 29.44 ||
śivena sthāpite pūrvaṃ kāveryādyabhirakṣake  |
lakṣeṇa rakṣitā devī narmadā bahukalpagā  || 29.45 ||
dhanuṣāṃ ṣaṣṭyabhiyutaiḥ puruṣairīśayojitaiḥ  |
oṃ kāraśatasāhasraiḥ parvataścābhirakṣitaḥ  || 29.46 ||
anyadeśakṛtaṃ pāpamasmin kṣetre vinaśyati  |
asmiṃstīrthe kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vajralepo bhaviṣyati  || 29.47 ||
eṣā te kathitā tāta kāverī saritāṃ varā  |
rudradehasamutpannā tena puṇyā saridvarā  || 29.48 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye kāverīsaṅgamamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 30

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadottarakūle tu dārutīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahābhāga tapastaptvā dvijottamaḥ  || 30.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ko'sau dvijavaraśreṣṭhaḥ siddhastatra mahāmune  |
dāruketi sutaḥ kasya etanme vaktumarhasi  || 30.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhārgave vipule vaṃśe dhīmato devaśārmaṇaḥ  |
dārurnāma mahābhāgo vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  || 30.3 ||
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprastho vidhikramāt |
yatidharmavidhānena cacāra vipulatapaḥ  || 30.4 ||
dhyāyanvai sa mahādevaṃ nirāhāro yudhiṣṭhira  |
uvāsa tīrthe tasmin vai yāvatprāṇaparikṣayam  || 30.5 ||
tasya nāmnā tu tattīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena arcayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 30.6 ||
satyavādī jitakrodhaḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ  |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti rājannatraiva savarthā  || 30.7 ||
yaḥ kuryādupavāsaṃ ca satyaśaucaparāyaṇaḥ sautrāmaṇiphalaṃ cāsya sambhavatyavicāritam  || 30.8 ||
ṛgvedajāpī ṛgvedī sāma vā sāmapāragaḥ  |
yajurvedī yajurjaptvā labhate phalamuttamam  || 30.9 ||
prāṇāṃstyajati yo martyastasmiṃstīrthe vidhānataḥ  |
anivartikā gatistasya ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 30.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dārutīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma triṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 31

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchecca rājendra tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam  |
brahmāvartamiti khyātaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 31.1 ||
tatra saṃnihito brahmā nityasevī yudhiṣṭhira  |
ūrdhvabāhurnirālambacakāra bhramaṇaṃ sadā  || 31.2 ||
ekāhāravaśe'tiṣṭhaddvādaśābdaṃ mahāvratī  |
atra tīrthe vidhānena cintayan vai maheśvaram  || 31.3 ||
tena tatpuṇyamākhyātaṃ brahmāvartamiti prabho  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 31.4 ||
arcayeddevamīśānaṃ viṣṇuṃ vā parameśvaram  |
yatphalaṃ sarvayajñānāṃ vidhivaddakṣiṇāvatām  || 31.5 ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti tattīrthasya prabhāvataḥ  |
yasmiṃstīrthe tu yo devo dānavo vā dvijo'tha vā  || 31.6 ||
siddhastenaiva tannāmnā khyātaṃ loke mahacca tat |
na jalaṃ na sthalaṃ nāma kṣetraṃ vā hyūṣarāṇi ca  || 31.7 ||
pavitratvaṃ labhantyete pauruṣeṇa vinā nṛṇām  |
sāmarthyānniścayāddhairyātsidhyanti puruṣā nṛpa  || 31.8 ||
pramādāttasya lobhena patanti narake dhruvam  || 31.9 ||
saṃnirudhyendriyagrāmaṃ yatra yatra vasenmuniḥ  |
tatra tatra kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca  || 31.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe brahmāvartatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 32

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
pattreśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
yatra siddho mahābhāgaścitrasenasuto balī  || 32.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ko'sau siddhastadā brahmaṃstasmiṃstīrthe mahātapāḥ  |
putraḥ kasya tu ko heturetadicchāmi veditum  || 32.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
citronāma mahātejā indrasya dayitaḥ purā  |
tasya putro nṛpaśreṣṭha pattreśvara iti śrutaḥ  || 32.3 ||
rūpavān subhagaścaiva sarvaśatrubhayaṃkaraḥ  |
indrasya dayito'tyarthaṃ jaya ityeva cāparaḥ  || 32.4 ||
sa kadācitsabhāmadhye sarvadevasamāgame  |
menakānṛtyagītena mohitaḥ suciraṃ kila  || 32.5 ||
tiṣṭhate gatamaryādo gataprāṇa iva kṣaṇāt |
tāvatsurapatirdevaḥ śaśāpāthājitendriyam  || 32.6 ||
yasmāttvaṃ svargasaṃstho'pi martyadharmamupeyivān  |
tasmānmartye ciraṃ kālaṃ kṣapayiṣyasyasaṃśayam  || 32.7 ||
evamuktaḥ surendreṇa citrasenasuto yuvā  |
vepamānaḥ suraśreṣṭhaḥ kṛtāñjaliruvāca ha  || 32.8 ||

pattreśvara uvāca -
mayā pāpena mūḍhena ajitendriyacetasā  |
prāptaṃ vai yatphalaṃ tasya prasādaṃ kartumarhasi  || 32.9 ||

śakra uvāca -
narmadātaṭamāśritya dvādaśābdaṃ jitendriyaḥ  |
ārādhaya śivaṃ śāntaṃ punaḥ prāpsyasi sadgatim  || 32.10 ||
satyaśaucaratānāṃ ca dharmiṣṭhānāṃ jitātmanām  |
loko'yaṃ pāpināṃ naiva iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  || 32.11 ||
evamukte mahārāja sahasrākṣeṇa dhīmatā  |
gandharvatanayo dhīmānpraṇamyāgāttu bhūtalam  || 32.12 ||
revāyā vimale toye brahmāvartasamīpataḥ  |
snātvā japtvā vidhānena arcayitvā ca śaṅkaram  || 32.13 ||
vāyvambupiṇyākaphalaiśca puṣpaiḥ parṇaiśca mūlāśanayāvakena  |
tatāpa pañcāgnitapobhirugraistataśca toṣaṃ samagātsa devaḥ  || 32.14 ||
pinākapāṇiṃ varadaṃ triśūlinamumāpatiṃ hyandhakanāśanaṃ ca  |
candrārdhamauliṃ gajakṛttivāsasaṃ dṛṣṭvā papātāgragataṃ samīkṣya  || 32.15 ||

īśvara uvāca -
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te varado'haṃ tavānagha  |
yamicchasi dadāmyadya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 32.16 ||

pattreśvara uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
atra tvaṃ satataṃ tīrthe mama nāmnā bhava prabho  || 32.17 ||
etacchrutvā mahādevo harṣagadgadayā girā  |
tathetyuktvā yayau hṛṣṭa umayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  || 32.18 ||
so'pi tattīrthamāplutya gate deve divaṃ prati  |
snātvā jāpyavidhānena tarpayitvā pitḥn punaḥ  || 32.19 ||
sthāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe vidhānataḥ  |
pattreśvaraṃ tu vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu bhārata  || 32.20 ||
indralokaṃ gataḥ śāpānmuktaḥ so'pi nareśvara  |
hṛṣṭaḥ pramudito ramyaṃ jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  || 32.21 ||
eṣa te kathitaḥ praśnaḥ pṛṣṭo yo vai yudhiṣṭhira  |
tatra snānena caikena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 32.22 ||
yastvarcayenmahādevaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
snātvābhyarcya pitḥn devān so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 32.23 ||
mṛto varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ krīḍitvā ca śive pure  |
rājā vā rājatulyo vā paścānmartyeṣu jāyate  || 32.24 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajño jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam  |
vyādhiśokavinirmuktaḥ punaḥ smarati tajjalam  || 32.25 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe patreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 33

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra agnitīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra saṃnihito hyagnirgataḥ kāmena mohitaḥ  || 33.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ devo jagaddhātā kāmena kaluṣīkṛtaḥ  |
kathaṃ ca nityadā vāsa ekasthāneṣu jāyate  || 33.2 ||
etattvāścaryamatulaṃ sarvalokeṣvanuttamam  |
kathayasva mahābhāga paraṃ kautūhalaṃ mama  || 33.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāprājña pṛṣṭaḥ praśnastvayānava  |
kathayāmi yathāpūrvaṃ śrutametanmaheśvarāt || 33.4 ||
āsītkṛtayuge rājā nāmnā duryodhano mahān  |
hastyaśvarathasampūrṇo medinīparipālakaḥ  || 33.5 ||
rūpayauvanasampannaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ pṛthivīpatim  |
divyopabhogasampannaṃ prārthayāmāsa narmadā  || 33.6 ||
sa tu tāṃ cakame kanyāṃ tyaktvā'nyaṃ pramadājanam  |
mudā paramayā yukto māhiṣmatyāḥ patirnṛpa  || 33.7 ||
ramate sa tayā sārddhaṃ kāle vai nṛpasattama  |
narmadā janayāmāsa kanyāṃ padmadalekṣaṇām  || 33.8 ||
aṅgapratyaṅgasampannā yasmāllokeṣu viśrutā  |
tasyāṃ pitā ca mātā ca cakratuḥ premabandhanam  || 33.9 ||
kālenātisudīrgheṇa yauvanasthā varāṅganā  |
prārthyamānāpi rājanvai nātmānaṃ dātumicchati  || 33.10 ||
tato'nyadivase vahnirdvijarūpo mahātapāḥ  |
rājānaṃ prārthayāmāsa raho gatvā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 33.11 ||
bhobho raghukulaśreṣṭha dvijo'haṃ mandasantatiḥ  |
daridro hyasahāyaśca bhāryārthe varayāmi tām  || 33.12 ||
kanyā sudarśanā nāma rūpeṇāpratimā bhuvi  |
tāṃ dadasva mahābhāga vardhate tava mandire  || 33.13 ||
brahmacaryeṇa nirviṇṇa ekākī kāmapīḍitaḥ  |
yācamānasya me tāta prasādaṃ kartumarhasi  || 33.14 ||

rājovāca -
nāhaṃ dravyavihīnasya asavarṇasya karhicit |
dāsyāmi svāṃ sutāṃ śubhrāṃ gamyatāṃ dvijapuṃgava  || 33.15 ||
evamuktastadā vahniḥ parāṃ pīḍāmupāgataḥ  |
na kiṃciduktvā rājānaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 33.16 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ vipre rājā mantripurohitaiḥ  |
mantrayitvātha kāle tu tuṣṭo makhamukhe sthitaḥ  || 33.17 ||
yajataśca makhe bhaktyā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhārata  |
tataścādarśanaṃ vahniḥ sarveṣāṃ paśyatāmagāt || 33.18 ||
viprā durmanaso bhūtvā gatā rājño hi mandiram  |
vahnināśaṃ vimanaso rājānamidamabruvan  || 33.19 ||
brāhmaṇā ūcuḥ  |
duryodhana mahārāja śrūyatāṃ mahadadbhutam  |
na śrutaṃ na ca dṛṣṭaṃ vā kautukaṃ nṛpapuṃgava  || 33.20 ||
agnikāryapravṛttānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vidhivannṛpa  |
kenāpi hetunā vahnirdṛśyate na jvalatyuta  || 33.21 ||
tacchrutvā vipriyaṃ ghoraṃ rājā vipramukhāccyutam  |
āsanātpatito bhūmau chinnamūla iva drumaḥ  || 33.22 ||
āśvasya ca muhūrtena unmatta iva saṃstadā  |
nirīkṣya ca diśaḥ sarvā idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 33.23 ||
kimetadāścaryaparamiti bhobho dvijottamāḥ  |
kathyatāṃ kāraṇaṃ sarvaṃ śāstradṛṣṭyā vibhāvya ca  || 33.24 ||
mama vā duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcidutāho bhavatāmiha  |
yena naṣṭo'gniśālāyāṃ hutabhukkena hetunā  || 33.25 ||
mantracchidramathānyadvā naiva kiṃcidadakṣiṇam  |
kriyāhīnaṃ kṛtaṃ vātha kena vahnirna dṛśyate  || 33.26 ||
annahīno dahedrāṣṭraṃ mantrahīnastu ṛtvijaḥ  |
dātāraṃ dakṣiṇāhīno nāsti yajñasamo ripuḥ  || 33.27 ||
brāhmaṇā ūcuḥ  |
na mantrahīnā hi vayaṃ na ca rājanvrataistathā  |
dravyeṇa ca na hīnastvamanyatpāpaṃ vicintyatām  || 33.28 ||

rājovāca -
tathāpi yūyaṃ sahitā upāyaṃ cintayantviti  |
yena śreyo bhavennityamiha loke paratra ca  || 33.29 ||
evamuktāstataḥ sarve brāhmaṇāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ  |
nirāhārāḥ sthitāḥ śarve yatra naṣṭo hutāśanaḥ  || 33.30 ||
tataḥ svapne mahātejā hutabhugbrāhmaṇāṃstadā  |
uvāca śrūyatāṃ sarvairmama nāśasya kāraṇam  || 33.31 ||
prārthito'yaṃ mayā rājā sutāṃ dātuṃ na cecchati  |
tena naṣṭo'gniśaraṇādahaṃ bho dvijasattamāḥ  || 33.32 ||
yadi me svasutāṃ rājā dadāti paramārcitām  |
tadāsya jvalamāno'haṃ gṛhe tiṣṭhāmi nānyathā  || 33.33 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ viprā vaiśvānaramukhodgatam  |
vismayotphullanayanā rājānamidamabruvan  || 33.34 ||
bhavato matamājñāya sarve gatvāgnimandiram  |
nirāhārāḥ sthitā rātrau paśyāmo jātavedasam  || 33.35 ||
tenoktāḥ svasutāṃ cettu rājā me dātumicchati  |
tato'sya bhūyo'pi gṛhe jvale'haṃ nānyathā dvijāḥ  || 33.36 ||
evaṃ jñātvā mahārāja svasutāṃ dātumarhasi  || 33.37 ||

rājovāca -
bhavatāṃ tasya vā kāryaṃ devasya vacanaṃ hṛdi  |
samayaṃ kartumicchāmi kanyādāne hyanuttamam  || 33.38 ||
mama saṃnihito nityaṃ gṛhe tiṣṭhatu pāvakaḥ  |
dadāmi rucirāpāṅgīṃ nānyathā karavāṇi vai  || 33.39 ||
evaṃ te brāhmaṇāḥ śrutvā tathāgniṃ prāpya satvaram  |
kathayitvā vivāhena yojayāmāsurāśu vai  || 33.40 ||
sudarśanāyā lābhena parituṣṭo hutāśanaḥ  |
jvalate sannidhau nityaṃ māhiṣmatyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 33.41 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti tattīrthamagnitīrthaṃ pracakṣate  |
ye tatra pakṣasandhau tu snānadānaistu bhāvitāḥ  || 33.42 ||
tarpayanti pitḥn devāṃste'śvamedhaphalairyutāḥ  |
suvarṇaṃ ye prayacchanti tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 33.43 ||
pṛthvīdānaphalaṃ tatra jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 33.44 ||
sa mṛto hyagniloke tu krīḍate surapūjitaḥ  |
eṣa te hyagnitīrthasya sambhavaḥ kathito mayā  || 33.45 ||
sarvapāpaharaḥ puṇyaḥ śrutamātro narottama  |
dhanyaḥ pāpaharo nityamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 33.46 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe'gnitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayastriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 34

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatraiva tu bhavedanyadādityasya mahātmanaḥ  |
kīrtayāmi naraśreṣṭha yadi te śravaṇe matiḥ  || 34.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
etadāścaryamatulaṃ śrutvā tava mukhodgatam  |
vismayāddhṛṣṭaromāhaṃ jāto'smi munisattama  || 34.2 ||
sahasrakiraṇo devo hartā kartā nirañjanaḥ  |
avatāreṇa lokānāmuddhartā narmadātaṭe  || 34.3 ||
puruṣākāro bhagavānutāho tapasaḥ phalāt |
kasya gotre samutpannaḥ kasya devo'bhavadvaśī  || 34.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kulikānvayasambhūto brāhmaṇo bhaktimāñchuciḥ  |
īkṣyāmīti raviṃ tatra tīrthe yātrākṛtodyamaḥ  || 34.5 ||
yojanānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ nirāhāro gatodakaḥ  |
prasthito devadevena svapnānte vāritaḥ kila  || 34.6 ||
bhobho mune mahāsattva alaṃ te vratamīdṛśam  |
sarvaṃ vyāpya sthitaṃ paśya sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ca mām  || 34.7 ||
tapāmyahaṃ tato varṣaṃ nigṛhṇāmyutsṛjāmi ca  |
na bhṛtaṃ caiva mṛtyuṃ ca yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati  || 34.8 ||
varaṃ varaya bhadraṃ tvamātmano yastavepsitam  || 34.9 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva deyo yadi varo mama  |
uttare narmadākūle sadā saṃnihito bhava  || 34.10 ||
ye bhaktyā parayā deva yojanānāṃ śate sthitāḥ  |
smariṣyanti jitātmānasteṣāṃ tvaṃ varado bhava  || 34.11 ||
kubjāndhabadhirā mūkā ye kecidvikalendriyāḥ  |
tava pādau namasyanti teṣāṃ tvaṃ varado bhava  || 34.12 ||
śīrṇaghrāṇā gatadhiyo hyasthicarmāvaśeṣitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ tvaṃ karuṇāṃ deva acireṇa kuruṣva ha  || 34.13 ||
ye'pi tvāṃ narmadātoye snātvā tatra dine dine  |
arcayanti jagannātha teṣāṃ tvaṃ varado bhava  || 34.14 ||
prabhāte ye staviṣyanti stavairvaidikalaukikaiḥ  |
abhipretaṃ varaṃ deva teṣāṃ tvaṃ dada bhocyuta  || 34.15 ||
tavāgre vapanaṃ deva kārayanti narā bhuvi  |
svāmiṃsteṣāṃ varo deya eṣa me paramo varaḥ  || 34.16 ||
evamastviti taṃ coktvā muniṃ karuṇayā punaḥ  |
śatabhāgena rājendra sthitvā cādarśanaṃ gataḥ  || 34.17 ||
tatra tīrthe naro bhaktyā gatvā snānaṃ samācaret |
tarpayetpitṛdevāṃśca so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 34.18 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
dyotayanvai diśaḥ sarvā agnilokaṃ sa gacchati  || 34.19 ||
yastattīrthaṃ samāsādya tyajatīha kalevaram  |
sa gato vāruṇaṃ lokamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 34.20 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitsaṃnyāsena tanuṃ tyajet |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  || 34.21 ||
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇe divyaśabdānunādite  |
uṣitvāyāti martye vai vedavedāṅgavidbhavet || 34.22 ||
vyādhiśokavinirmukto dhanakoṭipatirbhavet |
putradārasamopeto jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam  || 34.23 ||
prātarutthāya yastatra smarate bhāskaraṃ tadā  |
ājanmajanitātpāpānmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 34.24 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ravitīrthavarṇanaṃ nāma catustriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 35

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
jalamadhye mahādevaḥ kena tiṣṭhati hetunā  |
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kūlaṃ varjayitvā dvijottama  || 35.1 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etadākhyānamatulaṃ puṇyaṃ śrutimukhāvaham  |
purāṇe yacchrutaṃ tāta tatte vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 35.2 ||
tretāyuge mahābhāga rāvaṇo devakaṇṭakaḥ  |
trailokyavijayī raudraḥ surāsurabhayaṃkaraḥ  || 35.3 ||
devadānavagandharvairṛṣibhiśca tapodhanaiḥ  |
avadhyo'tha vimānena yāvatparyaṭate mahīm  || 35.4 ||
tāvaddhindhyagirermadhye dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
mayo nāmeti vikhyāto guhāvāsī tapaścaran  || 35.5 ||
tasya pārśvagato rakṣo vinayādavaniṃ gataḥ  |
pūjito dānasanmānairidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 35.6 ||
kasyeyaṃ padmapatrākṣī pūrṇacandranibhānanā  |
kiṃnāmadheyā tapati tapa ugraṃ kathaṃ vibho  || 35.7 ||

maya uvāca -
dānavānāṃ patiḥ śreṣṭho mayo'haṃ nāma nāmataḥ  |
bhāryā tejovatī nāma tasyāstu tanayā śubhā  || 35.8 ||
mandodarīti vikhyātā tapate bhartṛkāraṇāt |
ārādhayantī bhartāramumāyā dayitaṃ śubham  || 35.9 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ tasya rāvaṇo madamohitaḥ  |
prasṛtaḥ praṇato bhūtvā mayaṃ vacanamabravīt || 35.10 ||
paulastyānvayasaṃjāto devadānavadarpahā  |
prārthayāmi mahābhāga sutāṃ tvaṃ dātumarhasi  || 35.11 ||
jñātvā paitāmahaṃ vṛttaṃ mayenāpi mahātmanā  |
rāvaṇāya sutā dattā pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 35.12 ||
gṛhītvā tāṃ tadā rakṣo'bhyarcyamāno niśācaraiḥ  |
devodyāne vimānaiśca krīḍate sa tayā saha  || 35.13 ||
kenacittvatha kālena rāvaṇo lokarāvaṇaḥ  |
putraṃ putravatāṃ śreṣṭho janayāmāsa bhārata  || 35.14 ||
tenaiva jātamātreṇa rāvo mukto mahātmanā  |
saṃvartakasya meghasya tena lokā jaḍīkṛtāḥ  || 35.15 ||
śrutvā tannarditaṃ ghoraṃ brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
nāma cakre tadā tasya meghanādo bhaviṣyati  || 35.16 ||
evaṃnāmā kṛtaḥ so'pi paramaṃ vratamāsthitaḥ  |
toṣayāmāsa deveśamumayā saha śaṅkaram  || 35.17 ||
vratairniyamadānaiśca homajāpyavidhānataḥ  |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇairnityaṃ kṛśaṃ kurvankalevaram  || 35.18 ||
evamanyaddine tāta kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  |
gatvā liṅgadvayaṃ gṛhya prasthito dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 35.19 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya snātukāmo mahābalaḥ  |
nikṣipya pūjayan devaṃ kṛtajāpyo nareśvara  || 35.20 ||
tatrāyatanāvāsena snāto hutahutāśanaḥ  |
kṛtakṛtyamivātmānaṃ mānayitvā niśācaraḥ  || 35.21 ||
gantukāmaḥ paraṃ mārgaṃ laṅkāyāṃ nṛpasattama  |
ekamuddharato liṅgaṃ praṇataḥ savyapāṇinā  || 35.22 ||
dvitīyaṃ tu dvitīyena bhaktyā paulastyanandanaḥ  |
tāvadeva mahāliṅgaṃ patitaṃ narmadāṃbhasi  || 35.23 ||
yāhi yāhīti cetyuktvā jalamadhye pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
namitvā rāvaṇistasya devasya parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 35.24 ||
jagāmākāśamāviśya pūjyamāno niśācaraiḥ  |
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ meghanādeti viśrutam  || 35.25 ||
pūrvaṃ tu garjanaṃ nāma sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra yastu snānaṃ samācaret || 35.26 ||
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
piṇḍadānaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 35.27 ||
yatphalaṃ sattrayajñena tadbhavennātra saṃśayaḥ  |
tena dvādaśavarṣāṇi pitaraḥ saṃpratarpitāḥ  || 35.28 ||
yastu bhojayate vipraṃ ṣaḍrasātrena bhārata  |
akṣayapuṇyamāpnoti tatra tīrthe narottama  || 35.29 ||
prāṇatyāgaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādbhāvito bhāvitātmanā  |
sa vasecchāṅkare loke yāvadā bhūtasamplavam  || 35.30 ||
eṣā te naraśārdūla garjanotpattiruttamā  |
kathitā snehabandhena sarvapāpakṣayakarī  || 35.31 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe meghanādatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 36

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchecca rājendra dārutīrthamanuttamam  |
dāruko yatra saṃsiddha indrasya dayitaḥ purā  || 36.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
dārukeṇa kathaṃ tāta tapaścīrṇaṃ purānagha  |
vidhānaṃ śrotumicchāmi tvatsakāśāddvijottama  || 36.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi vicitraṃ yatpurātanam  |
vṛttaṃ svargasabhāmadhye ṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 36.3 ||
sūto vajradharasyeṣṭo mātalirnāma nāmataḥ  |
sa putraṃ śaptavānpūrvaṃ kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  || 36.4 ||
śāpāhato vepamāna indrasya caraṇau śubhau  |
prapīḍya mūrdhnā deveśaṃ vijñāpayati bhārata  || 36.5 ||
tamuvācābhiśaptaṃ cāpyanāthaṃ ca sureśvaraḥ  |
karmaṇā kena śāpasya ghorasyānto bhaviṣyati  || 36.6 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya toṣayanvai maheśvaram  |
tiṣṭha yāvadyugasyāntaṃ punarjanma hyavāpsyasi  || 36.7 ||
punarbhūtvā tu pūtastvaṃ dāruko nāma viśrutaḥ  |
saṃsevya paramaṃ devaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 36.8 ||
mānuṣaṃ bhāvamāpannastataḥ siddhimavāpsyasi  |
evamuktastu devena sahasrākṣeṇa dhīmatā  || 36.9 ||
praṇamya śirasā bhūmimāgato'sau hyacetanaḥ  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya karṣayannijavigraham  || 36.10 ||
vratopavāsasaṃkhinno japahomarataḥ sadā  |
mahādevaṃ mahātmānaṃ varadaṃ śūlapāṇinam  || 36.11 ||
bhaktyā tu parayā rājanyāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
aṃśāvataraṇādviṣṇoḥ sūto bhūtvā mahāmatiḥ  || 36.12 ||
toṣayan vai jagannāthaṃ tato yāto hi sadgatim  || 36.13 ||
eṣa tatsambhavastāta dārutīrthasya suvrata  |
kathito'yaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt || 36.14 ||
tato yudhiṣṭhiraḥ śrutvā vismayaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  |
bhrātḥn vilokayāmāsa hṛṣṭaromā muhurmuhuḥ  || 36.15 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā vidhipūrvaṃ nareśvara  |
upāsya saṃdhyāṃ deveśamarcayedyaśca śaṅkaram  || 36.16 ||
vedābhyāsaṃ tu tatraiva yaḥ karoti samāhitaḥ  |
so'śvamedhaphalaṃ rājaṃllabhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 36.17 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo bhaktyā bhojayedbrāhmaṇāñchuciḥ  |
sa tu viprasahasrasya labhate phalamuttamam  || 36.18 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  |
yatkṛtaṃ śuddhabhāvena tatsarvaṃ saphalaṃ bhavet || 36.19 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dārukatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 37

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheta rājendra devatīrthamanuttamam  |
yena devāstrayastriṃśatsnātvā siddhiṃ parāṃ gatāḥ  || 37.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ tāta surāḥ sarve dānavairbalavattaraiḥ  |
nirjitāstatra tīrthe ca snātvā siddhiṃ parāṃ gatāḥ  || 37.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā daityagaṇairugrairyuddhe'tibalavattaraiḥ  |
indro devagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ svarājyāccyāvito nṛpa  || 37.3 ||
hastyaśvarathayānaughairmardayitvā varūthinīm  |
vidhvastā bhejire mārgaṃ prahārairjarjarīkṛtāḥ  || 37.4 ||
jambhaśumbhaiśca kūṣmāṇḍakuhakādibhiḥ  |
vepamānārditāḥ sarve brahmāṇamupatasthire  || 37.5 ||
praṇamya śirasā devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam  |
tadā vijñāpayāmāsurdevā vahnipurogamāḥ  || 37.6 ||
paśya paśya mahābhāga dānavaiḥ śakalīkṛtāḥ  |
viyojitāḥ putradāraistvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 37.7 ||
paritrāyasva deveśa sarvalokapitāmaha  |
nānyā gatiḥ sureśāna tvāṃ muktvā parameśvara  || 37.8 ||

brahmovāca -
dānavānāṃ vighātārthaṃ narmadātaṭamāsthitāḥ  |
tapaḥ kurudhvaṃ svasthāḥ stha tapo hi paramaṃ balam  || 37.9 ||
nānyopāyo na vai mantro vidyate na ca me kriyā  |
vinā revājalaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 37.10 ||
dāridryavyādhimaraṇabandhanavyasanāni ca  |
etāni caiva pāpasya phalānīti matirmama  || 37.11 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tataścaiva tapaḥ kuruta duṣkaram  |
tathā caiva surāḥ sarve devā hyagnipurogamāḥ  || 37.12 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ tathyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
narmadāmāgatāḥ sarve devā hyagnipurogamāḥ  || 37.13 ||
cerurvai tatra vipulaṃ tapaḥ siddhimavāpnuvan  |
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ devatīrthamanuttamam  || 37.14 ||
gīyate triṣu lokeṣu sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
tatra gatvā ca yo martyo vidhinā saṃyatendriyaḥ  || 37.15 ||
snānaṃ samācaredbhaktyā sa labhenmauktikaṃ phalam  |
yastu bhojayate viprāṃstasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 37.16 ||
sa labhenmukhyaviprāṇāṃ phalaṃ sāhasrikaṃ nṛpa  |
tatra devaśilā ramyā mahāpuṇyavivardhinī  || 37.17 ||
saṃnyāsena mṛtā ye tu teṣāṃ syādakṣayā gatiḥ  |
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  || 37.18 ||
rudraloke vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasaṃplavam  |
evaṃ snānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  || 37.19 ||
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vā'pi tatra tīrthe'kṣayaṃ bhavet |
eṣa te vidhiruddiṣṭa utpattiścaiva bhārata  || 37.20 ||
devatīrthasya nikhilā yathā vai śaṅkarācchrutā  |
paṭhanti ye pāpaharaṃ sarvaduḥkhavimocanam  || 37.21 ||
devatīrthasya caritaṃ devalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 37.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe devatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 38

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheta rājendra guhāvāsīti cottamam  |
yatra siddho mahādevo guhāvāsī samārbudam  || 38.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kena kāryeṇa bho tāta mahādevo jagadguruḥ  |
guhāyāmanayatkālaṃ sudīrghaṃ dvijasattama  || 38.2 ||
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  |
śrotumicchāmyahaṃ sarvaparaṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 38.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu praśno mahārāja pṛṣṭo yo vai tvayottamaḥ  |
purāṇe vistaro hyasya na śakyo hi mayādhunā  || 38.4 ||
kathituṃ vṛddhabhāvatvādatīto bahukālikaḥ  |
saṃkṣepāttena te tāta kathayāmi nibodha me  || 38.5 ||
purā kṛtayuge rājannāsīddāruvanaṃ mahat |
nānādrumalatākīrṇaṃ nānāvallyupaśobhitam  || 38.6 ||
siṃhavyāghravarāhaiśca gajaiḥ khaḍgairniṣevitam  |
bahupakṣiyutaṃ divyaṃ yathā caitrarathaṃ vanam  || 38.7 ||
tatra kecinmahāprājñā vasanti saṃśitavratāḥ  |
vasanti parayā bhaktyā caturāśramabhāvitāḥ  || 38.8 ||
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprastho yatistathā  |
svadharmaniratāḥ sarve vāñchantaḥ paramaṃ padam  || 38.9 ||
tāvadvasantasamaye kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  |
vimānastho mahādevo gacchanvai hyumayā saha  || 38.10 ||
dadarśa toya āvāsamṛksāmayajurnāditam  |
alakṣyāgatanirgamyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 38.11 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā muditā devī harṣagaṅgadayā girā  |
papraccha devadeveśaṃ śaśāṅkakṛtabhūṣaṇam  || 38.12 ||

devyuvāca -
kasyāyamāśramo deva vedadhvaninināditaḥ  |
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣutpipāsādyaiḥ śramaiśca parihīyate  || 38.13 ||

maheśvara uvāca -
kiṃ tvayā na śrutaṃ devi mahādāruvanaṃ mahat |
bahuviprajano yatra gṛhadharmeṇa vartate  || 38.14 ||
atra yaḥ strījanaḥ kaścidbhartṛśuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ  |
nānyo devo na vai dharmo jñāyate śailanandini  || 38.15 ||
etacchrutvā paraṃ vākyaṃ devadevena bhāṣitam  |
kautūhalasamāviṣṭā śaṅkaraṃ punarabravīt || 38.16 ||
yattvayoktaṃ mahādeva patidharmaratāḥ striyaḥ  |
tāsāṃ tvaṃ madano bhūtvā cāritraṃ kṣobhaya prabho  || 38.17 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yattvayoktaṃ ca vacanaṃ na hi me rocate priye  |
brāhmaṇā hi mahadbhūtaṃ na caiṣāṃ vipriyaṃ caret || 38.18 ||
manyupraharaṇā viprāścakrapraharaṇo hariḥ  |
cakrātkrūrataro manyustasmādvipraṃ na kopayet || 38.19 ||
na te devā na te lokā na te nagā na cāsurāḥ  |
dṛśyante triṣu lokeṣu ye tairdṛṣṭairna nāśitāḥ  || 38.20 ||
teṣāṃ mokṣastathā svargo bhūmirmartye phalāni ca  |
yeṣāṃ tuṣṭā mahābhāgā brāhmaṇāḥ kṣitidevatāḥ  || 38.21 ||
evaṃ jñātvā mahābhāge asadgrāhaṃ parityaja  |
tatra loke viruddhaṃ vai kupyante yena vai dvijāḥ  || 38.22 ||

devyuvāca -
nāhaṃ te dayitā deva nāhaṃ te vaśavartinī  |
akṛtvādhaśva vai tāsāṃ mānaṃ surasupūjitam  || 38.23 ||
lokaloke mahādeva aśakyaṃ nāsti te prabho  |
kriyatāṃ mama caivaikametatkāryaṃ surottama  || 38.24 ||
evamukto mahādevo devyā vākyahite rataḥ  |
kṛtvā kāpālikaṃ rūpaṃ yayau dāruvanaṃ prati  || 38.25 ||
mahāhitajaṭājūṭaṃ niyamya śaśibhūṣaṇam  |
kaṇṭhatrāṇaṃ paraṃ kṛtvā dhārayan karṇakuṇḍale  || 38.26 ||
vyāghracarmaparīdhāno mekhalāhārabhūṣitaḥ  |
nūpuradhvaninighoṣaiḥ kampayan vai vasuṃdharām  || 38.27 ||
mahānūrddhvajaṭāmālī kṛttibhasmānulepanaḥ  |
kṛtvā haste kapālaṃ tu brahmaṇaśca mahātmanaḥ  || 38.28 ||
mahāḍamarughoṣeṇa kampayan vai vasuṃdharām  |
prabhātasamaye prāpto mahādāruvanaṃ prati  || 38.29 ||
tāvatpuṇyajanaḥ sarvapuṣpapatraphalārthikaḥ  |
nirgato bahubhiḥ sārddhaṃ pavamānaḥ samantataḥ  || 38.30 ||
taddṛṣṭvā mahadāścaryaṃ rūpaṃ devasya bhārata  |
yuvatīnāṃ manastāsāṃ kāmena kaluṣīkṛtam  || 38.31 ||
śobhanaṃ puruṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvā api varāṅganāḥ  |
kledabhāvaṃ tato jagmurmudā dāruvanastriyaḥ  || 38.32 ||
vikārā bahavastāsāṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahādbhutam  |
saṃjātā viprapatnīnāṃ tadā tāsu narottama  || 38.33 ||
paridhānaṃ na jānanti kāściddṛṣṭvā varāṅganāḥ  |
uttarīyaṃ tathā cānyā mahāmohasamanvitāḥ  || 38.34 ||
keśabhāraparibhraṣṭā kācidevāsanotthitā  |
dātukāmā tadā bhaikṣyaṃ ceṣṭituṃ naiva cāśakat || 38.35 ||
kāciddṛṣṭvā mahādevaṃ rūpayauvanagarvitā  |
utsaṅge saṃsthitaṃ bālaṃ vismṛtā pāyituṃ stanam  || 38.36 ||
kāmabāṇahatā cānyā bāhubhyāṃ pīḍya sustanau  |
niḥśvasantī tadā coṣṇaṃ na kiṃcitpratijalpati  || 38.37 ||
evaṃ saṃkṣobhya taṃ sarvaṃ strījanaṃ parameśvaraḥ  |
jagāma tatra vai tāsāṃ kṣobhaṃ kṛtvā mahādbhutam  || 38.38 ||
tāvatte brāhmaṇāḥ sarve bhramitvā kānanaṃ mahat |
āgatāḥ svagṛhe dārān dadṛśuśca hataujasaḥ  || 38.39 ||
yāsāṃ pūrvatarā bhaktiḥ pātivratye patīnprati  |
calitāstā viditvāśu nirjagmurdvijasattamāḥ  || 38.40 ||
saṃvidaṃ paramāṃ kṛtvā jñātvā devaṃ maheśvaram  |
kṣobhayitvā manastāsāṃ tataścādarśanaṃ gatam  || 38.41 ||
krodhāviṣṭo dvijaḥ kaściddaṇḍamudyamya dhāvati  |
kalmāṣayaṣṭimanye ca tathānye darbhamuṣṭikām  || 38.42 ||
itaścetaśca te sarve bhramitvā kānanaṃ nṛpa  |
ekībhūtvā mahātmāno vyājahruśca ruṣā giram  || 38.43 ||
yadidaṃ ca hutaṃ kiṃcidguravastoṣitā yadi  |
tena satyena devasya liṅgaṃ patatu cottamam  || 38.44 ||
āśramādāśramaṃ sarve na tyajāmo vidhikramāt |
tena satyena devasya liṅgaṃ patatu bhūtale  || 38.45 ||
evaṃ satyaprabhāvena triruktena dvijanmanām  |
śivasya paśyato liṅgaṃ patitaṃ dharaṇītale  || 38.46 ||
hāhākāro mahānāsīllokāloke'pi bhārata  |
devasya patite liṅge jagataśca mahākṣaye  || 38.47 ||
patamānasya liṅgasya śabdo'bhūcca sudāruṇaḥ  |
ulkāpātā diśāṃ hāhā bhūmikampāśca dāruṇāḥ  || 38.48 ||
patanti parvatāgrāṇi śoṣaṃ yānti ca sāgarāḥ  |
devasya patite liṅge devā vimanaso'bhavan  || 38.49 ||
sametya sahitāḥ sarve brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭāḥ sarve stuvanti vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ  || 38.50 ||
tatastuṣṭo jagannāthaścaturvadanapaṅkajaḥ  |
ārtānprāha surānsarvānmā viṣādaṃ gamiṣyatha  || 38.51 ||
brahmaśāpābhibhūto'sau devadevastrilocanaḥ  |
tuṣṭaistaistapasā yuktaiḥ punarmokṣaṃ gamiṣyati  || 38.52 ||
etacchrutvā yayurdevā yathāgatamarindama  |
bhāvayitvā tataḥ sarve munayaścaiva bhārata  || 38.53 ||
viśvāmitravasiṣṭhādyā jābāliratha kaśyapaḥ  |
sametya sahitāḥ sarve tamūcustripurāntakam  || 38.54 ||
brahmatejo hi balavaddvijānāṃ hi sureśvara  |
kṣāntiyuktastapastaptvā bhaviṣyasi gataklamaḥ  || 38.55 ||
yataḥ kṣobhādṛṣīṇāṃ ca tadevaṃ liṅgamuttamam  |
patitaṃ te mahādeva na tatpūjyaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 38.56 ||
na tacchreyo'gnihotreṇa nāgniṣṭomena labhyate  |
prāpnuvanti ca yacchreyo mānavā liṅgapūjane  || 38.57 ||
devadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām  |
vacanena tu viprāṇāmetatpūjyaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 38.58 ||
brahmaviṣṇvindracandrāṇāmetatpūjyaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
yatphalaṃ tava liṅgasya iha loke paratra ca  || 38.59 ||
evamukto jagannāthaḥ praṇipatya dvijottamān  |
mudā paramayā yuktaḥ kṛtāñjalirabhāṣata  || 38.60 ||
brāhmaṇā jaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ nirjalaṃ sārvakāmikam  |
yeṣāṃ vākyodakenaiva śudhyanti malino janāḥ  || 38.61 ||
na tatkṣetraṃ na tattīrthamūṣaraṃ puṣkarāṇi ca  |
brāhmaṇe manyumutpādya yatra gatvā sa śudhyati  || 38.62 ||
na tacchāstraṃ yanna viprapraṇītaṃ na taddānaṃ yanna viprapradeyam  |
na tatsaukhyaṃ yannavipraprasādānna tadduḥkhaṃ yanna vipraprakopāt || 38.63 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni gaṅgādyāḥ saritastathā  |
ekasya vipravākyasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 38.64 ||
abhinandya dvijānsarvānanujñāto maharṣibhiḥ  |
tato'gamattadā devo narmadātaṭamuttamam  || 38.65 ||
paramaṃ vratamāsthāya guhāvāsī samārbudam  |
tapaścacāra bhagavāñjapasnānarataḥ sadā  || 38.66 ||
samāpte niyame tāta sthāpayitvā maheśvaram  |
vandyamānaḥ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ kailāsamagamatprabhuḥ  || 38.67 ||
narmadāyāstaṭe tena sthāpitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
tenaiva kāraṇenāsau narmadeśvara ucyate  || 38.68 ||
yo'rcayennarmadeśānaṃ yatirvai saṃjitendriyaḥ  |
snātvā caiva mahādevamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 38.69 ||
dadāti yaḥ pitṛbhyastu tilapuṣpakuśodakam  |
triḥsaptapūrvajāstasya svarge modanti pāṇḍava  || 38.70 ||
yastu bhojayate viprāṃstasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
pāyasaṃ ghṛtamiśraṃ tu sa labhetkoṭijaṃ phalam  || 38.71 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi brāhmaṇebhyo yudhiṣṭhira  |
dadāti toyamadhyasthaḥ so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 38.72 ||
aṣṭamyāṃvā caturdaśyāṃ nirāhāro vasettu yaḥ  |
narmadeśvaramāsādya prāpnuyājjanmanaḥ phalam  || 38.73 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
tasya vyādhibhayaṃ na syātsaptajanmasu bhārata  || 38.74 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudraloke bhaviṣyati  || 38.75 ||
eṣa te vidhiruddiṣṭastasyotpattirnarottama  |
purāṇe vihitā tāta saṃjñā tasya tu vistarāt || 38.76 ||
etaṃ kīrtayate yastu narmadeśvarasambhavam  |
bhaktyā śṛṇoti ca naraḥ so'pi snānaphalaṃ labhet || 38.77 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye narmadeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 39

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchecca rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam  |
snānamātrānnaro bhaktyā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 39.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryabhūtaṃ lokeṣu kathitaṃ dvijasattama  |
narmadeśvaramāhātmyaṃ kāpilaṃ kathayasva me  || 39.2 ||
yasmin kāle'tha sambandhe utpannaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ jātaṃ kathaṃ prabho  || 39.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇu vakṣye'dya te rājankapilātīrthamuttamam  |
yena te vismayaḥ sarvaḥ śrutvā gacchati bhārata  || 39.4 ||
purā kṛtayugasyādau brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
utpādayitvā sakalaṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  || 39.5 ||
japahomaparo bhaktyā kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā ca tiṣṭhati  |
jvalamānāttu kapilā tāvatkuṇḍātsamutthitā  || 39.6 ||
agnijvālojjvalaiḥ śṛṅgaistrinetrā supayasvinī  |
agnipūrṇā hyagnimukhā agnighrāṇāgnilocanā  || 39.7 ||
agnikhurā hyagnipṛṣṭhā agnisarvāṅgasaṃsthitiḥ  |
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇā ghaṇṭālalitaniḥsvanā  || 39.8 ||
dṛṣṭvā tu tāṃ mahābhāgāṃ kapilāṃ kuṇḍamadhyagām  |
brahmā lokagurustāta praṇamyedamuvāca ha  || 39.9 ||
namaste kapile puṇye sarvalokanamaskṛte  |
maṅgalye maṅgale devi triṣu lokeṣvanupame  || 39.10 ||
tvaṃ lakṣmīstvaṃ smṛtirmedhā tvaṃ dhṛtistvaṃ varānane  |
umādevīti vikhyātā tvaṃ satī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 39.11 ||
vaiṣṇavī tvaṃ mahādevī brahmāṇī tvaṃ varānane  |
kumārī tvaṃ mahābhāge bhaktiḥ śraddhā tathaiva ca  || 39.12 ||
kālarātristu bhūtānāṃ kumārī parameśvarī  |
tvaṃ lavastvaṃ truṭiścaiva muhūrtaṃ lakṣameva ca  || 39.13 ||
saṃvatsarastvaṃ māsastvaṃ kālastvaṃ ca kṣaṇastathā  |
nāsti kiṃcittvayā hīnaṃ trailokye sacarācare  || 39.14 ||
evaṃ stutā tu mānena kapilā parameṣṭhinā  |
tamuvāca mahābhāgaṃ prahṛṣya padmasambhavam  || 39.15 ||
prasannā tava vākyena devadeva jagadguro  |
kiṃ karomi priyaṃ te'dya brūhi sarvaṃ pitāmaha  || 39.16 ||

brahmovāca -
jagaddhitāya janitā mayā tvaṃ parameśvari  |
svargānmartyaṃ tato yāhi lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 39.17 ||
sarvadevamayī tvaṃ tu sarvalokamayī tathā  |
vidhinā ye pradāsyanti teṣāṃ vāsastriviṣṭape  || 39.18 ||
evamuktvā tato devī brahmāṇaṃ parameśvarī  |
vandyamānā suraiḥ siddhairājagāma dharātalam  || 39.19 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
yadāyāteha sā tāta brāhmaṇo vacanācchubhā  |
tadā devāśca lokāśca kathamaṅgeṣu saṃsthitāḥ  || 39.20 ||
kathaṃ vā saṃsthitāgatya kapilā sā dvijottama  |
tīrthe vā hyūṣare kṣetra etanme kathaya dvija  || 39.21 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sā tadā brahmaṇā coktā dhātrā lokasya bhārata  |
brahmalokādgatā puṇyāṃ narmadāṃ lokapāvanīm  || 39.22 ||
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritā  |
cacāra pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām  || 39.23 ||
tadāprabhṛti rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ khyātamṛṣisaṅghairniṣevitam  || 39.24 ||
tattīrthe vidhivatsnātvā kapilāyāḥ prayacchati  |
pṛthvī tena bhaveddattā saśailavanakānanā  || 39.25 ||
tāṃ tu paśyati yo bhaktyā dīyamānāṃ dvijottame  |
tasya varṣaśataṃ pāpaṃ naśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 39.26 ||
bhūrbhuvaḥ svarmahaścaiva janaḥ satyaṃ tapastathā  |
te tatpṛṣṭhaṃ samāśritya sthitā lokā nṛpottama  || 39.27 ||
mukhe hyagniḥ sthito devo danteṣu ca bhujaṅgamāḥ  |
dhātā vidhātā hyoṣṭhau ca jihvāyāṃ tu sarasvatī  || 39.28 ||
sahasrakiraṇau devau candrādityau sulocanau  |
nāsikāmadhyagaścaiva māruto nṛpasattama  || 39.29 ||
lalāṭe tu mahādevo hyaśvinau karṇasaṃsthitau  |
naranārāyaṇau śṛṅge śṛṅgamadhye pitāmahaḥ  || 39.30 ||
kambalo'dhigatastāta pāśadhṛgvaruṇastathā  |
yamaśca bhagavāndeva āśritya codaraṃ śritaḥ  || 39.31 ||
khureṣu pannagāścaivaṃ pucchāgre sūryaraśmayaḥ  |
evambhūtāṃ hi kapilāṃ sarvadevamayīṃ nṛpa  || 39.32 ||
ye dhārayanti ca gṛhe dhanyāste nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
prātarutthāya yastasyāḥ kurute tu pradakṣiṇām  || 39.33 ||
pradakṣiṇā kṛtā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
kapilāpañcagavyena yaḥ snāpayati śaṅkaram  || 39.34 ||
upavāsaparo yastu tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
snātvā hyuktavidhānena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 39.35 ||
tasya te vaṃśajāḥ sarve daśa pūrve daśāpare  |
tṛptā rohanti vai svarge dhyāyanto'sya manorathān  || 39.36 ||
eṣa te vidhiruddiṣṭaḥ sambhavo nṛpasattama  |
tīrthasya ca phalaṃ puṇyaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi  || 39.37 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 39.38 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīnarmadāmāhātmye kapilātīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 40

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra karañjeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahābhāgo daityo lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 40.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
yo'sau siddho mahābhāga tatra tīrthe mahātapāḥ  |
kasya putraḥ kathaṃ siddhaḥ kasminkāle vada dvija  || 40.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā kṛtayuge rājanmānaso brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajño marīcirnāma nāmataḥ  || 40.3 ||
tasyāpi tapaso rāśeḥ kālena mahatānagha  |
putro'tha mānaso jātaḥ sākṣādbrahmeva cāparaḥ  || 40.4 ||
kṣamā damo dayā dānaṃ satyaṃ śaucamathārjavam  |
marīceśca guṇā hyete santi tasya ca bhārata  || 40.5 ||
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇaṃ kaśyapaṃ dvijasattamam  |
jñātvā prajāpatirdakṣo bhāryārthe svasutāṃ dadau  || 40.6 ||
aditirditirdanuścaiva tathāpyevaṃ daśāparāḥ  |
yāsāṃ putrāśca saṃjātāḥ pautrāśca bharatarṣabha  || 40.7 ||
aditirjanayāmāsa putrānindrapurogamān  |
jātāstasya mahābāho kaśyapasya prajāpateḥ  || 40.8 ||
yaistu lokatrayaṃ vyāptaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ mahat |
tathānyasya mahābhāgo danoḥ putro vyajāyata  || 40.9 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampannaḥ karañjo nāma nāmataḥ  |
bāla eva mahābhāga cacāra sa mahattapaḥ  || 40.10 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya cātighoramanuttamam  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ ca kṛcchracāndrāyaṇaṃ nṛpa  || 40.11 ||
śākamūlaphalāhāraḥ snānahomaparāyaṇaḥ  |
tatastuṣṭo mahādeva umayā sahitaḥ kila  || 40.12 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa tripurāntakaraḥ prabhuḥ  |
bhoḥ karañja mahāsattva parituṣṭo'smi te'nagha  || 40.13 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva te dadmi hyamaratvamṛte mama  || 40.14 ||

karañja uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva yadi deyo varo mama  |
tarhi putrāśca pautrāśca santu me dharmavatsalāḥ  || 40.15 ||
tathetyuktvā mahādeva umayā sahitastadā  |
vṛṣārūḍho gaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 40.16 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve so'pi daityo mudānvitaḥ  |
svanāmnātra mahādevaṃ sthāpayitvā yayau gṛham  || 40.17 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  |
snānamātrānarastatra mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 40.18 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
so'gniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 40.19 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
anivartyā gatistasya rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 40.20 ||
athavāgnijale prāṇānyastyajeddharmanandana  |
ayutadvitayaṃ vaste varṣāṇāṃ śivamandire  || 40.21 ||
tataścaiva kṣaye jāte jāyate vimale kule  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 40.22 ||
rājā vā rājatulyo vā jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam  |
putrapautrasamopetaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 40.23 ||
evaṃ te sarvamākhyātaṃ pṛṣṭaṃ yadyattvayānagha  |
tīrthasya tu phalaṃ tasya snānadāneṣu bhārata  || 40.24 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  || 40.25 ||
yastu śrāvayate śrāddhe paṭhetpitṛparāyaṇaḥ  |
akṣayaṃ jāyate puṇyamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 40.26 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye karañjeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 41

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchecca rājendra kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahāyakṣaḥ kuṇḍadhāro nṛpottama  || 41.1 ||
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ surāsurabhayaṃkaram  |
paulastyamandire caiva cikrīḍa nṛpasattama  || 41.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kasminyuge samutpannaḥ kasya putro mahāmatiḥ  |
tapastaptvā suvipulaṃ toṣito yena śaṅkaraḥ  || 41.3 ||
etadvistaratastāta kathayasva mamānagha  |
śṛṇvataśca na tṛptirme kathāmṛtamanuttamam  || 41.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tretāyuge brahmasamaḥ paulastyonāma viśravāḥ  |
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ bharadvājasutodbhavaḥ  || 41.5 ||
putraṃ pautragaṇairyuktaṃ patnyā bhaktyā sutoṣitaḥ  |
dhanadaṃ janayāmāsa sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam  || 41.6 ||
jātamātraṃ tu taṃ jñātvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
cakāra nāma suprīta ṛṣidevasamanvitaḥ  || 41.7 ||
yasmādviśravaso jāto mama pautratvamāgataḥ  |
tasmādvaiśravaṇo nāma tava dattaṃ mayānagha  || 41.8 ||
tathā tvaṃ sarvadevānāṃ dhanagoptā bhaviṣyasi  |
caturtho lokapālānāmakṣayaścāvyayo bhuvi  || 41.9 ||
tasya bhāryā mahārāja īśvarīti ca viśrutā  |
yakṣo yakṣādhipaḥ śreṣṭhastasya kuṇḍo'bhavatsutaḥ  || 41.10 ||
sa ca rūpaṃ paraṃ prāpya mātāpitroranujñayā  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 41.11 ||
grīṣme pañcāgnisaṃtapto varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ  |
hemante jalamadhyastho vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ samāḥ  || 41.12 ||
evaṃ varṣaśate pūrṇe ekāṅguṣṭhe'bhavannṛpa  |
asthibhūtaḥ paraṃ tāta ūrdhvabāhustataḥ param  || 41.13 ||
atapacca ghṛtaśvāsaḥ kuṇḍalo bharatarṣabha  |
caturthe varṣaśatake tutoṣa vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 41.14 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bho vatsa yatte manasi rocate  |
dadāmi te na sandehastapasā toṣito hyaham  || 41.15 ||

kuṇḍala uvāca -
yakṣādhipaprasādena tasyaivānucaraḥ pure  |
vicarāmi yathākāmamavadhyaḥ sarvaśatruṣu  || 41.16 ||
tathetyuktvā mahādevaḥ sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ  |
jagāmākāśamāviśya kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  || 41.17 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve so'pi yakṣo mudānvitaḥ  |
sthāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam  || 41.18 ||
alaṃkṛtvā jagannāthaṃ puṣpadhūpānulepanaiḥ  |
vimānaiścāmaraiśchatraistathā vai liṅgapūraṇaiḥ  || 41.19 ||
tarpayitvā dvijānsamyagannapānādibhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
prīṇayitvā mahādevaṃ tataḥ svabhavanaṃ yayau  || 41.20 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
uttamaṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ kuṇḍaleśvaranāmataḥ  || 41.21 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścidupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
arcayeddevamīśānaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 41.22 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi maṇiṃ mauktikameva ca  |
dadyādbhojyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ sa sukhī modate divi  || 41.23 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā ṛgyajuḥsāmago'pi vā  |
ṛcamekāṃ japitvā tu sakalaṃ phalamaśnute  || 41.24 ||
gāṃ prayacchati viprebhyastatphalaṃ śṛṇu pāṇḍava  |
yāvanti tasyā romāṇi tatprasūtikuleṣu ca  || 41.25 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate  |
svarge vāso bhavettasya putrapautraiḥ samanvitaḥ  || 41.26 ||
tāvanti varṣāṇi mahānubhāvaḥ svarge vasetputrapautraiśca sārddham  |
tatrānnado yāti maheśalokamasaṃkhyavarṣāṇi na saṃśayo'tra  || 41.27 ||
sa vai sukhī modate svargaloke gandharvasiddhāpsaraḥsampragīte  |
evaṃ tu te dharmasuta prabhāvastīrthasya sarvaḥ kathitaśca pārtha  || 41.28 ||
śrutvā stuvanmucyate sarvapāpaiḥ punastrilokīmiha tatprabhāvāt || 41.29 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye kuṇḍaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 42

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra pippaleśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahāyogī pippalādo mahātapāḥ  || 42.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
pippalādasya caritaṃ śrotumicchāmyahaṃ vibho  |
māhātmyaṃ tasya tīrthasya yatra siddho mahātapāḥ  || 42.2 ||
kasya putro mahābhāga kimarthaṃ kṛtavāṃstapaḥ  |
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  || 42.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
mithilāstho mahābhāgo vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
yājñavalkyaḥ purā tāta cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 42.4 ||
tāpasī tasya bhaginī yājñavalkyasya dhīmataḥ  |
sā saptame'pi varṣe ca vaidhavyaṃ prāpa daivataḥ  || 42.5 ||
pūrvakarmavipākena hīnābhūtpitṛmātṛtaḥ  |
nābhūttatpatipakṣe'pi ko'pītyekākinī sthitā  || 42.6 ||
bhūmau bhramantī bhrātuḥ sā samīpamagamacchanaiḥ  |
cacāra ca tapaḥ so'pi paralokasukhepsayā  || 42.7 ||
cacāra sāpi tatrasthā śuśrūṣantī mahattapaḥ  |
kasmiṃścitsamaye sātha snātāhani rajasvalā  || 42.8 ||
antarvāso dhṛtavatī dṛṣṭvā karpaṭakaṃ rahaḥ  |
yājñavalkyo'pi tadrātrau supto yatra susaṃvṛtaḥ  || 42.9 ||
svapnaṃ dṛṣṭvātyajacchukraṃ kaupīne raktabinduvat |
virājitena tapasā siddhaṃ tadanalaprabham  || 42.10 ||
yāvatprabuddho vipro'sau vīkṣyocchiṣṭaṃ tadaṃśukam  |
cikṣepa dūrato'spṛśyaṃ śaucaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  || 42.11 ||
niṣiddhaṃ tu niśi snānamiti suṣvāpa sa dvijaḥ  |
niśīthe sāpi tadvastraṃ bhagasyāvaraṇaṃ vyadhāt || 42.12 ||
prātaranveṣayāmāsa munirvastramitastataḥ  |
tataḥ sā brāhmaṇī prāha kiṃ anveṣayase prabho  |
kena kāryaṃ tava tathā vadasva mama tattvataḥ  || 42.13 ||

yājñavalkya uvāca -
apavitro mayā bhadre svapno dṛṣṭo'dya vai niśi  |
sakledaṃ tatra me vastraṃ nikṣiptaṃ tanna dṛśyate  || 42.14 ||
tacchrutvā brāhmaṇī vākyaṃ bhītabhītāvadannṛpa  |
tadvastraṃ tu mayā vipra snātvā hyantaḥ kṛtaṃ mahat || 42.15 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā hāhetyuktvā mahāmuniḥ  |
nipapāta tadā bhūmau chinnamūla iva drumaḥ  || 42.16 ||
kimetaditi setyuktvā hyākāśamiva nirmalā  |
āśvāsayantī taṃ vipraṃ provāca vacanaṃ tadā  || 42.17 ||
vadasva kāraṇaṃ tāta guhyādguhyataraṃ yadi  |
pratīkāro'sya yenaiva vimṛśya kriyate tvarā  || 42.18 ||
tataḥ sa suciraṃ dhyātvā labdhavāgvai tataḥ kṣaṇam  |
provāca sādhvasamanā yattacchṛṇu nareśvara  || 42.19 ||
nātra doṣo'sti te kaścinmama caiva śubhavrate  |
tavodare tu garbho yastatra daivaṃ parāyaṇam  || 42.20 ||
tasya tattvena rakṣā ca tvayā kāryā sadaiva hi  |
vināśī naiva kartavyo yāvatkālasya paryayaḥ  || 42.21 ||
tatheti vrīḍitā sādhvī dūyamānena cetasā  |
apālayacca taṃ garbhaṃ yāvatputro hyajāyata  || 42.22 ||
jātamātraṃ ca taṃ garbhaṃ gṛhītvā brāhmaṇī ca sā  |
aśvatthacchāyāmāśritya tamutsṛjya vaco'bravīt || 42.23 ||
yāni sattvāni lokeṣu sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  |
tāni sarvāṇi rakṣantu tyaktaṃ vai bālakaṃ mayā  || 42.24 ||
evamuktvā gatā sā tu brāhmaṇī nṛpasattama  |
tathāgataḥ sa tu śiśustatra sthitvā muhūrtakam  || 42.25 ||
pāṇipādau vinikṣipya nikuñcya nayane śubhe  |
āsyaṃ tu vikṛtaṃ kṛtvā ruroda vikṛtaiḥ svaraiḥ  || 42.26 ||
tena śabdena vitrastāḥ sthāvarā jaṅgamāśca ye  |
ākampitā mahotpātaiḥ saśailavanakānanā  || 42.27 ||
tato jñātvā mahadbhūtaṃ kṣudhāviṣṭaṃ dvijarṣabham  |
na jahāti nagaśchāyāṃ pānārthāya tataḥ param  |
apibacca sutaṃ tasmādabhṛtaṃ caiva bhārata  || 42.28 ||
evaṃ sa vardhitastatra kumāro nijacetasi  |
cintayāmāsa viśrabdhaḥ kiṃ mama grahagocaram  || 42.29 ||
tataḥ krūrasabhācāraḥ krūraṃ dṛṣṭvā nirīkṣitaḥ  |
papāta sahasā bhūmau śanaiścārī śanaiścaraḥ  || 42.30 ||
uvāca ca bhayatrastaḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭastadā  |
kiṃ mayāpakṛtaṃ vipra pippalāda mahāmune  || 42.31 ||
caranvai gaganādyena pātito dharaṇītale  |
sauriṇā hyevamuktastu pippalādo mahāmuniḥ  || 42.32 ||
krodharūpo'bravīdvākyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |
pitṛmātṛvihīnasya mama bālasya durmate  |
pīḍāṃ karoṣi kasmāttvaṃ saure brūhi hyaśeṣataḥ  || 42.33 ||

śanaiścara uvāca -
krūrasvabhāvaḥ sahajo mama dṛṣṭistathedṛśī  |
muñcasva māṃ tathā kartā yadbravīṣi na saṃśayaḥ  || 42.34 ||

pippalāda uvāca -
adyaprabhṛti bālānāṃ varṣādā ṣoḍaśādgraha  |
pīḍā tvayā na kartavyā eṣa te samayaḥ kṛtaḥ  || 42.35 ||
evamastviti coktvā sa jagāma punarāgataḥ  |
devamārgaṃ śanaiścārī praṇamya ṛṣisattamam  || 42.36 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ tatra so'pi bālo mahāgrahaḥ  |
vicintayanvai pitaraṃ krodhena kaluṣīkṛtaḥ  || 42.37 ||
āgneyīṃ dhāraṇāṃ dhyātvā janayāmāsa pāvakam  |
kṛtyāmantrairjuhāvāgnau kṛtyā vai saṃbhavatviti  || 42.38 ||
tāvajjhaṭiti sā kanyā jvālāmālāvibhūṣitā  |
hutabhuksadṛśākārā kiṃ karomīti cābravīt || 42.39 ||
śoṣayāmi samudrān kiṃ cūrṇayāmi ca parvatān  |
avaniṃ veṣṭayāmīti pātaye kiṃ nabhastalam  || 42.40 ||
kasya mūrdhni patiṣyāmi ghātayāmi ca kaṃ dvija  |
śīghramādiśyatāṃ kāryaṃ mā me kālātyayo bhavet || 42.41 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā pippalādo mahātapāḥ  |
krodhasaṃraktanayana idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 42.42 ||
mahatā krodhavegena mayā tvaṃ cintitā śubhe  |
pitā me yājñavalkyaśca tasya tvaṃ pata māciram  || 42.43 ||
evamuktvāgamacchīghraṃ sphoṭayantī nabhastalam  |
mithilāstho mahāprājñastapastepe mahāmanāḥ  || 42.44 ||
yāvatpaśyati digbhāgaṃ jvalanārkasamaprabham  |
yājñavalkyo mahātejā mahadbhūtamupasthitam  || 42.45 ||
taddṛṣṭvā sahasāyāntaṃ bhītabhīto mahāmuniḥ  |
anuyukto'tha bhūtena janakaṃ nṛpatiṃ yayau  || 42.46 ||
śaraṇyaṃ māmanuprāptaṃ viddhi tvaṃ nṛpasattama  |
mahadbhūtabhayādrakṣa yadi śaknoṣi pārthiva  || 42.47 ||
brahmatejobhavaṃ bhūtamanivāryaṃ durāsadam  |
na ca śaknomyahaṃ trātuṃ rājā vacanamabravīt || 42.48 ||
tataścānyaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭhaṃ śaraṇārthī mahātapāḥ  |
jagāma tena mukto'sau cendrasya sadanaṃ bhayāt || 42.49 ||
devarāja namaste'stu mahābhūtabhayānnṛpa  |
kampamāno'bravīdvipro rakṣasveti punaḥpunaḥ  || 42.50 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devarājo'bravīdidam  |
na śaknomi paritrātuṃ brahmakopādahaṃ mune  || 42.51 ||
tataḥ sa brahmabhavanaṃ brāhmaṇo brahmavittamaḥ  |
jagāma viṣṇulokaṃ ca tenāpītyukta eva saḥ  || 42.52 ||
tataḥ sa munirudvigno nirāśo jīvite nṛpa  |
anugamyamāno bhūtena agacchacchaṅkarālayam  || 42.53 ||
tasya yogabalopeto mahādevasya pāṇḍava  |
nakhamāṃsāntare gupto yathā devo na paśyati  || 42.54 ||
tadante cāgamadbhūtaṃ jvalanārkasamaprabham  |
muñca muñceti puruṣaṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 42.55 ||
evamukto mahādevastena bhūtena bhārata  |
yogīndraṃ darśayāmāsa nakhamāṃsāntare tadā  || 42.56 ||
saṃsthāpya bhūtaṃ bhūteśaḥ paramāpadgataṃ munim  |
uvāca mā bhaistvaṃ vipra nirgacchasva mahāmune  || 42.57 ||
tataḥ susūkṣmadehasthaṃ bhūtaṃ dṛṣṭvābravīdidam  |
kimasya tvaṃ mahābhūta kariṣyasi vadasva me  || 42.58 ||

kṛtyovāca -
krodhāviṣṭena deveśa pippalādena cintitā  |
asya dehaṃ haniṣyāmi hiṃsārthaṃ viddhi māṃ prabho  || 42.59 ||
etacchrutvā mahādevo bhūtasya vadanāccyutam  |
kaṭisthaṃ yājñavalkyaṃ ca mantrayāmāsa mantravit || 42.60 ||
yogīśvareti viprasya kṛtvā nāma yudhiṣṭhira  |
visarjayitvā deveśastatraivāntaradhīyata  || 42.61 ||
preṣayitvā tu taṃ bhūtaṃ pippalādo'pi durmanāḥ  |
pitṛmātṛsamudvigno narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 42.62 ||
ekāṅguṣṭho nirāhāro varṣādā ṣoḍaśānnṛpa  |
toṣayāmāsa deveśamumayā saha śaṅkaram  || 42.63 ||
tatastattapasā tuṣṭaḥ śaṅkaro vākyamabravīt || 42.64 ||

īśvara uvāca -
parituṣṭo'smi te vipra tapasānena suvrata  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva te dadmi manasā cepsitaṃ śubham  || 42.65 ||

pippalāda uvāca -
yadi me bhagavāṃstuṣṭo yadi deyo varo mama  |
atra saṃnihito deva tīrthe bhava maheśvara  || 42.66 ||
evamuktastathetyuktvā pippalādaṃ mahāmunim  |
jagāmādarśanaṃ devo bhūtasaṅghasamanvitaḥ  || 42.67 ||
pippalādo gate deve snātvā tatra mahāmbhasi  |
sthāpayitvā mahādevaṃ jagāmottaraparvatam  || 42.68 ||
tatra tīrthe naro bhaktyā snātvā mantrayutaṃ nṛpa  |
tarpayitvā pitḥn devān pūjayecca maheśvaram  || 42.69 ||
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam  |
mṛto rudrapuraṃ yāti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 42.70 ||
atha yo bhojayedviprān pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni modante divi tarpitāḥ  || 42.71 ||
saṃnyāsena tu yaḥ kaścittatra tīrthe tanuṃ tyajet |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokātkadācana  || 42.72 ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭhe hi tvayānagha  |
māhātmyaṃ pippalādasya tīrthasyotpattireva ca  || 42.73 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 42.74 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye pippalādatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvicatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 43

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra vimaleśvaramuttamam  |
tatra devaśilā ramyā svayaṃ devairvinirmitā  || 43.1 ||
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā brāhmaṇānpūjayennṛpa  |
svalpenāpi hi dānena tasya cānto na vidyate  || 43.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kāni dānāni viprendra śastāni dharaṇītale  |
yāni dattvā naro bhaktyā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 43.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ maṇimauktikameva ca  |
bhūmidānaṃ ca godānaṃ mocayatpaśubhānnaram  || 43.4 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitkurute prāṇasaṃkṣayam  |
rudraloke vasettāvadyāvadā bhūtasamplavam  || 43.5 ||
tataḥ puṣkariṇīṃ gacchetsarvapāpakṣayaṃkarīm  |
tatra snātvārcayeddevaṃ tejorāśiṃ divākaram  || 43.6 ||
ṛcamekāṃ japetsāmnaḥ sāmavedaphalaṃ labhet |
yajurvedasya japanādṛgvedasya tathaiva ca  || 43.7 ||
akṣaraṃ vā japenmantraṃ dhyāyamāno divākaram  |
ādityahṛdayaṃ japtvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 43.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vidhinā japeddvijān  |
tasya koṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 43.9 ||
anāśakenāgnigatyā jale vā dehapātanāt |
tasmiṃstīrthe mṛto yastu sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 43.10 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā nṛpasattama  |
vihitaṃ karma kurvāṇaḥ sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  || 43.11 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
vyādhiṃ sattvakṣayaṃ mohaṃ jñātvā varṇā dvijottama  |
pāpebhyo vipramucyante kena tatsādhanaṃ vada  || 43.12 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tilodakī tilasnāyī kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
brāhmaṇo'naśanaiḥ prāṇāṃstyajallabhati sadgatim  || 43.13 ||
saṅgrāme sadgatiṃ tāta kṣatriyo nidhane labhet |
tadabhāvānmahāprājña sevamāno labhediti  || 43.14 ||
vyādhigrahagṛhīto vā vṛddho vā vikalendriyaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ dāhayitvāgnau vidhinā sadgatiṃ labhet || 43.15 ||
vaiśyo'pi hi tyajanprāṇānevaṃ vai śubhabhāgbhavet |
jale vā śuddhabhāvena tyaktvā prāṇāñchivo bhavet || 43.16 ||
śūdro'pi dvijaśuśrūṣustoṣayitvā maheśvaram  |
vimucya nānyathā pāpaḥ patate narake dhruvam  || 43.17 ||
athavā praṇavāśakto dvijebhyo gurave tathā  |
pañcāgnau śoṣayeddehamāpṛcchya dvijasattamān  || 43.18 ||
śāntadāntajitakrodhāñchāstrayuktān vicakṣaṇān  |
teṣāṃ caivopadeśena karīṣāgniṃ prasādhayet || 43.19 ||
evaṃ varṇā yathātvena mūḍhāhaṅkāramohitāḥ  |
patanti narake ghore yathāndho girigahvare  || 43.20 ||
ye śāstravidhimutsṛjya vartante kāmacārataḥ  |
kṛmiyoniṃ prapadyante teṣāṃ piṇḍo na ca kriyā  || 43.21 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmaṃ tyaktvā yathecchācārasevinaḥ  |
aṣṭāviṃśatirvai koṭyo narakāṇāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 43.22 ||
pratyekaṃ vā patantyete magnā narakasāgare  |
durlabhaṃ mānuṣaṃ janma bahudharmārjitaṃ nṛpa  || 43.23 ||
tallabdhvā madamātsaryaṃ yo vai tyajati mānavaḥ  |
saṃniyamya sadātmānaṃ jñānacakṣurnaro hi saḥ  || 43.24 ||
ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāṃjanaśalākayā  || 43.25 ||
yasya nonmīlitaṃ cakṣurjñeyo jātyandha eva saḥ  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ nṛpasattama  || 43.26 ||
tathāniṣṭatarāṇāṃ hi rudrasya vacanaṃ yathā  |
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā rudradehādviniḥsṛtā  || 43.27 ||
tārayetsarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  |
sarvadevādhidevena īśvareṇa mahātmanā  || 43.28 ||
lokānāṃ ca hitārthāya mahāpuṇyāvatāritā  |
mānasaṃ vācikaṃ pāpaṃ snānānnaśyati karmajam  || 43.29 ||
rudradehādviniṣkrāntā tena puṇyatamā hi sā  |
prātarutthāya yo nityaṃ bhūmimākramya bhaktitaḥ  || 43.30 ||
etanmantraṃ japettāta snānasya labhate phalam  |
namaḥ puṇyajale devi namaḥ sāgaragāmini  || 43.31 ||
namo'stu pāpanirmoce namo devi varānane  || 43.32 ||
namo'stu te ṛṣivarasaṅghasevite namo'stu te trinayanadehaniḥsṛte  |
namo'stu te sukṛtavatāṃ sadā vare namo'stu te satatapavitrapāvani  || 43.33 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vimaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 44

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |
revāyā dakṣiṇe kūle nirmitaṃ śūlapāṇinā  || 44.1 ||
mokṣārthaṃ mānavendrāṇāṃ nirmitaṃ nṛpasattama  |

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
śrutā me vividhā dharmāstīrthāni vividhāni ca  |
dānadharmāḥ samastāśca tvatprasādāddvijottama  || 44.2 ||
anyacca śrotumicchāmi saṃsāraśchidyate yathā  |
punarāgamanaṃ nāsti mokṣaprāptirbhavedyathā  || 44.3 ||
etadākhyāhi me sarvaṃ prasādāddvijasattama  || 44.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā tīrthāttīrthāntaraṃ mahat |
śrute yasya prabhāve tu mucyate cābdikādaghāt || 44.5 ||
vācikairmānasairvāpi śārīraiśca viśeṣataḥ  |
kīrtanāttasya tīrthasya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 44.6 ||
pañcakrośapramāṇaṃ tu tacca tīrthaṃ mahīpate  |
bhuktimuktipradaṃ divyaṃ prāṇināṃ pāpakarmiṇām  || 44.7 ||
revāyā dakṣiṇe kūle parvato bhṛgusaṃjñitaḥ  |
tasya mūrdhni ca tattīrthaṃ sthāpitaṃ caiva śambhunā  || 44.8 ||
śūlabhedeti vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu bhūpate  |
tatra sthitāśca ye vṛkṣāstīrthāccaiva caturdiśam  || 44.9 ||
patitā nilayaṃ yānti rudrasya nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
mṛtāstatraiva ye kecijjantavo bhuvi pakṣiṇaḥ  || 44.10 ||
te yānti paramaṃ lokaṃ tatra tīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  |
pātālānniḥsṛtā gaṅgā bhogavatītisaṃjñitā  || 44.11 ||
niṣkrāntā śūlabhedācca sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  |
yā sā gīrvāṇanāmnyanyā vahetpuṇyā mahānadī  || 44.12 ||
patitā kuṇḍamadhye tu yatra bhinnaṃ triśūlinā  |
śambhunā ca purā tāta utpādya ca sarasvatī  || 44.13 ||
sā tatra patitā rājan prācīnāghavimocinī  |
bhāsvatyā tritayaṃ yatra śilā gīrvāṇasaṃjñitā  || 44.14 ||
tatra tīrthe ca tattīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
kedāraṃ ca prayāgaṃ ca kurukṣetraṃ gayā tathā  || 44.15 ||
anyāni ca sutīrthāni kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
pañca sthānāni tīrthāni pṛthagbhūtāni yāni ca  || 44.16 ||
vakṣyāmi ca samāsena ekaikaṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak |
gayā nābhyāṃ yathā puṇyā cakratīrthaṃ ca tatsamam  || 44.17 ||
dharmāraṇye yathā kūpaṃ śūlabhedaṃ ca tatsamam  |
brahmayūpaṃ yathā puṇyaṃ devanadyāstathaiva ca  || 44.18 ||
yathā gayāśiraḥ puṇyaṃ surāṇāṃ ca yathā śilā  |
yathā ca puṣkaraṃ sthānaṃ mārkaṇḍahrada eva ca  || 44.19 ||
dattvā piṇḍodakaṃ tatra piṇḍāṇāṃ ca tathākṣayam  |
yastatra kurute śrāddhaṃ toyaṃ pibati nityaśaḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpaistu uragaḥ kañcukairiva  |
anindyānpūjayedviprān dambhakrodhavivarjitān  || 44.20 ||
trayodaśadinaṃ dānaṃ trayodaśaguṇaṃ bhavet |
abhyarcitaṃ suraṃ dṛṣṭvā gaṇanāthaṃ gajānanam  || 44.21 ||
sarve vighnā vinaśyanti dṛṣṭvā kambalakṣetrapam  || 44.22 ||
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā śūlapāṇiṃ maheśvaram  || 44.23 ||
devasya pūrvabhāge tu umā pūjyā prayatnataḥ  |
mārkaṇḍeśaṃ tato bhaktyā pūjayedguhavāsinam  || 44.24 ||
mucyante pātakaiḥ sarvairajñānajñānasaṃcitaiḥ  |
guhāmadhye praviṣṭastu japetsūktaṃ tu tryakṣaram  || 44.25 ||
nīlaparvatajaṃ puṇyaṃ ṣaṣṭhāṃśena labheta saḥ  |
trinarāstatra tiṣṭhanti sādityamarutaiḥ saha  || 44.26 ||
sarvadevamayaṃ sthānaṃ koṭiliṅgamanuttamam  |
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃkṣayam  || 44.27 ||
tathā pāpāni naśyanti śūlabhedasya darśanāt |
pratyakṣo dṛśyate'dyāpi pratyayo hyavanīpate  || 44.28 ||
visphuliṅgā liṅgamadhye spandante snānayogataḥ  |
dvitīyaḥ pratyayastatra tailabindurna sarpati  || 44.29 ||
evaṃ hi pratyayastatra śūlabhedaprabhāvajaḥ  |
yaḥ smarecchūlabhedaṃ tu trikālaṃ nityameva ca  || 44.30 ||
sa pūtaśca bhavetsākṣātsabāhyābhyantaro nṛpa  |
na kasyacinmayā khyātaṃ pṛṣṭo'haṃ tridaśairapi  || 44.31 ||
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ sadā gopyaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvadoṣaghnamuttamam  || 44.32 ||
sarvatīrthamayaṃ tīrthaṃ śūlabhedaṃ janeśvara  |
śrute yasya prabhāve tu mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 44.33 ||
śūlabhedaṃ mayā tāta saṃkṣepātkathitaṃ tava  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 44.34 ||


|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedapraśaṃsāvarṇanaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||


ṛKV adhyāya 45

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
eṣa eva purā praśnaḥ paripṛṣṭo maheśvaram  |
rājñā cottānapādena ṛṣidevasamāgame  || 45.1 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
idaṃ tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ param  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ sthānaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ hara  || 45.2 ||
śūlabhedaṃ kathaṃ jātaṃ kenaivotpāditaṃ purā  |
māhātmyaṃ tasya tīrthasya vistarācchaṃsa me prabho  || 45.3 ||

īśvara uvāca -
āsītpurā mahāvīryo dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
martye na tādṛśaḥ kaścidvikrameṇa balena vā  || 45.4 ||
sūnurbrahmasutasyāyamandhako nāma durmadaḥ  |
nijasthāne vasan pāpaḥ kurvan rājyamakaṇṭakam  || 45.5 ||
hṛṣṭapuṣṭo vasanmartye sa surairnābhibhūyate  |
bhavanaṃ tasya pāpasya vahnerupavanaṃ yathā  || 45.6 ||
etasminnandhakaḥ kāle cintayāmāsa bhārata  |
toṣayāmi mahādevaṃ yena sānugraho bhavet || 45.7 ||
prārthayāmi varaṃ divyaṃ yo me manasi vartate  |
paraṃ sa niścayaṃ kṛtvā so'ndhako nirgato gṛhāt || 45.8 ||
revātaṭaṃ samāsādya dānavastapasi sthitaḥ  |
ugraṃ tapaścacārāsau dāruṇaṃ lomaharṣaṇam  || 45.9 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ sa nirāhāro'bhavattataḥ  |
dvitīyaṃ tu sahasraṃ sa nyavasadvāribhojanaḥ  || 45.10 ||
tṛtīyaṃ tu sahasraṃ sa dhūmapānarato'bhavat |
caturthaṃ varṣasāhasraṃ yogābhyāsena saṃsthitaḥ  || 45.11 ||
kopīha nedṛśa cakre tapaḥ paramadāruṇam  |
asthicarmāvaśeṣo'sau yāvattiṣṭhati bhārata  || 45.12 ||
tasya mūrdhni tato rājan dhūmavārttirviniḥsṛtā  |
devalokamatītyāsau kailāsaṃ vyāpya saṃsthitā  || 45.13 ||
tāvaddevasamīpasthā umā vacanamabravīt |
ko'styayaṃ mānuṣe loke tapasogreṇa saṃsthitaḥ  || 45.14 ||
caturvarṣasahasrāṇi vyatīyuḥ parameśvara  |
na kenāpīdṛśaṃ taptaṃ tapo dṛṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ tathā  || 45.15 ||
avajñāṃ kuruṣe deva kimatra niyamānvite  |
sarvasya datse śīghraṃ tvamalpena tapasā vibho  || 45.16 ||
nākṣakrīḍāṃ kariṣye'dya tvayā saha maheśvara  |
yāvannotthāpyate hyeṣa dānavo bhaktavatsala  || 45.17 ||

īśvara uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahādevi sarvalakṣaṇalakṣite  |
ahaṃ taṃ na vijānāmi kliśyantaṃ dānaveśvaram  || 45.18 ||
yogābhyāse sthito bhadre dhyāyaṃstatparamaṃ padam  |
tatrāgaccha mayā sārddhaṃ yatra tapyatyasau tapaḥ  || 45.19 ||
umayā sahito devo gatastatra maheśvaraḥ  |
asthicarmāvaśeṣastu dṛṣṭo devena śambhunā  || 45.20 ||
pratyuvāca prasanno'sau devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
bhobhoḥ kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ bhīmaṃ dāruṇaṃ lomaharṣaṇam  || 45.21 ||
īdṛśaṃ ca tapo ghoraṃ kasmādvatsa tvayā kṛtam  |
varaṃ dāsyāmyahaṃ vatsa yaste manasi vartate  || 45.22 ||

andhaka uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varado yadi śaṅkara  |
surān sarvān vijeṣyāmi tvatprasādānmaheśvara  || 45.23 ||

īśvara uvāca -
svapne'pi tridaśāḥ sarve na yoddhavyāḥ kadācana  |
asaṃbhāvyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ manaso yanna rocate  || 45.24 ||
anyaṃ kimapi yācasva yaste manasi vartate  |
svarge vā yadi vā martye pātāleṣu ca saṃsthitān  || 45.25 ||
martyeṣu vividhān bhogān bhokṣyasi tvaṃ yathepsitān  |
kuru niṣkaṇṭakaṃ rājyaṃ svarge devapatiryathā  || 45.26 ||
devasya vacanaṃ śrutvā so'ndhako vimanāḥ sthitaḥ  |
vṛthā kleśaśca me jāto na kiṃcitsādhitaṃ mayā  || 45.27 ||
niśvāsaṃ paramaṃ muktvā nipapāta dharātale  |
mūlacchinno yathā vṛkṣo nirucchvāsastadābhavat || 45.28 ||
mūrcchāpannaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā devī vacanamabravīt |
yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayatyeṣa tamasmai dehi śaṅkara  || 45.29 ||
bhaktānupekṣamāṇasya tavākīrtirbhaviṣyati  || 45.30 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yadi dāsye varaṃ devi icchābhūtaṃ kadācana  |
tato na maṃsyate viṣṇuṃ na brahmāṇaṃ na māmapi  || 45.31 ||
uccatvamāpto deveśi anyānapi surāsurān  || 45.32 ||

devyuvāca -
kamapyupāyamāśritya utthāpaya maheśvara  |
viṣṇuvarjaṃ surānsarvāñjayasveti varaṃ vada  || 45.33 ||

īśvara uvāca -
upāyaḥ śobhano devi yo me manasi vartate  |
tamevāsmai pradāsyāmi yastvayā kathito varaḥ  || 45.34 ||
tato'mṛtena saṃsiktaḥ svastho'bhūttatkṣaṇādayam  |
tathā punarnavo jātaḥ sarvāvayavaśobhitaḥ  || 45.35 ||
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā gṛhāṇa varamuttamam  |
viṣṇuvarjaṃ pradāsyāmi yattavābhimataṃ priyam  || 45.36 ||
sarvaṃ ca saphalaṃ tubhyaṃ mā dharmaste'nyathā bhavet |
dadāmīti varaṃ tubhyaṃ manyase yadi cāsura  || 45.37 ||
viṣṇuvarjaṃ surān sarvāñjeṣyasi tvaṃ ca māṃ vinā  || 45.38 ||

andhaka uvāca -
bhavatvevamiti prāha balamāsthāya kevalam  |
viṣṇuvarjaṃ vijeṣye'haṃ svabalena maheśvara  || 45.39 ||
kṛtārtho'haṃ hi saṃjāta ityuktvā praṇatiṃ gataḥ  |
gaccha devomayāsārddhaṃ kailāsaśikharaṃ varam  || 45.40 ||
vṛṣapuṃgavamāruhya devo'sāvumayā saha  |
varaṃ dattvā sa tasyaivaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata  || 45.41 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe andhakavarapradānavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 46

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sa dānavo varaṃ labdhvā jagāma svapuraṃ prati  |
dadarśa svapuraṃ rājañchobhitaṃ citracatvaraiḥ  || 46.1 ||
udyānaiścaiva vividhaiḥ kadalīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitaiḥ  |
panasairbakulaiścaivāmrātairāmraiśca campakaiḥ  || 46.2 ||
aśokairnālikeraiśca mātuliṅgaiḥ sadāḍimaiḥ  |
nānāvṛkṣaiśca śobhāḍhyaṃ taḍāgairupaśobhitam  || 46.3 ||
devatāyatanairdivyairdhvajamālāsuśobhitaiḥ  |
vedādhyayananirghoṣairmaṅgalādyairvināditam  || 46.4 ||
prāviśadbhavane divye kāñcane rukmamālini  |
apaśyatsa sutān bhāryāmamātyān dāsabhṛtyakān  || 46.5 ||
tato jayapradān sarvānitaścetaśca dhāvataḥ  |
hṛcchobhāṃ ca prakurvāṇān vai jayantībhiruccakaiḥ  || 46.6 ||
kecittoraṇamābadhya kecitpuṣpāṇyavākiran  |
mātuliṅgakarāścānye dhāvanti hyandhakaṃ prati  || 46.7 ||
pure janāśca dṛśyante bhājanairannapūritaiḥ  |
pūrṇahastāḥ pradṛśyante tatraiva bahavo janāḥ  || 46.8 ||
sākṣatairbhājanaistatra śatasāhasrayoṣitaḥ  |
mantrān paṭhanti viprāśca maṅgalānyapi yoṣitaḥ  || 46.9 ||
amātyāścaiva bhṛtyāśca gajāṃścāḍhaukayanti ca  |
vardhāpayanti te sarve ye kecitpuravāsinaḥ  || 46.10 ||
hṛṣṭastuṣṭo'vasattatra sacivaiḥ saha so'ndhakaḥ  |
dadarśa sa jagatsarvaṃ turaṅgāṃśca padātikān  || 46.11 ||
tathaiva vividhān kośāṃstatra kāñcanapūritān  |
mahiṣīrgā vṛṣāṃścaivāpaśyacchatrāṇyanekadhā  || 46.12 ||
sa evamandhakastatra kiyantaṃ kālamāvasat |
hṛṣṭastuṣṭo vasanmartye sa surairnābhyabhūyata  || 46.13 ||
varaṃ labdhaṃ tu taṃ jñātvā śaṅkitāḥ svargavāsinaḥ  |
ekībhūtāśca te sarve vāsavaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 46.14 ||

śakra uvāca -
kathamāgamanaṃ vo'tra sarveṣāmapi nākinām  |
kasmādvo bhayamutpannamāgatāḥ śaraṇaṃ katham  || 46.15 ||
tataste hyamarāḥ sarve śakrametadvaco'bruvan  || 46.16 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
suranāthāndhako nāma daityaḥ śambhuvarorjitaḥ  |
ajeyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ kiṃ nu kāryamataḥ param  || 46.17 ||
tattvaṃ cintaya deveśa ka upāyo vidhīyatām  |
itthaṃ vadanti te devāḥ śakrāgre mantraṇodyatāḥ  || 46.18 ||
mantrayanti ca yāvadvai tāvaccāramukheritam  |
jñātvā tatra sa devaughaṃ dānavo nirgato gṛhāt || 46.19 ||
ekākī syandanārūḍha āyurdhaibahubhirvṛtaḥ  |
durgamaṃ merupṛṣṭhaṃ sa līlayaiva gato nṛpa  || 46.20 ||
svarṇaprākārasaṃyuktaṃ śobhitaṃ vividhāśramaiḥ  |
durgamaṃ śatruvargasya tadā pārthivasattama  || 46.21 ||
praviveśāsurastatra līlayā svagṛhe yathā  |
vṛtrahā bhayamāpannaḥ svakīyaṃ cāsanaṃ dadau  || 46.22 ||
upaviṣṭo'ndhakastatra śakrasyaivāsane śubhe  |
āsthānaṃ kalayāmāsa sarvatastridaśāvṛtam  || 46.23 ||

śakra uvāca -
kiṃ tavāgamanaṃ cātra kiṃ kāryaṃ kathayasva me  |
yadasmadīyaṃ vittaṃ hi tatte dāsyāmi dānava  || 46.24 ||

andhaka uvāca -
nāhaṃ vai kāmaye kośaṃ na gajāṃśca sureśvara  |
svakīyaṃ darśayasvādya svargaśṛṅgārabhūṣitam  || 46.25 ||
airāvataṃ mahānāgaṃ taṃ caivoccaiḥśravohayam  |
urvaśyādīni ratnāni mama darśaya gopate  || 46.26 ||
pārijātakapuṣpāṇi vṛkṣajātīnanekaśaḥ  |
vāditrāṇi ca sarvāṇi darśayasva śacīpate  || 46.27 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śakraścintitavānidam  |
yo'muṃ nihanti pāpmānaṃ na taṃ paśyāmi karhicit || 46.28 ||
nāsti rakṣāpradaḥ kaścitsvargalokasya duḥkhinaḥ  |
bhayatrasto dadāvanyadvāditrādyapsarogaṇaiḥ  || 46.29 ||
raṅgabhūmāvupāviśya kārayāmāsa tāṇḍavam  |
upaviṣṭāḥ surāḥ sarve yamamārutakinnarāḥ  || 46.30 ||
urvaśyādyā apsaraso gītavāditrayogataḥ  |
nanṛtuḥ puratastasya sarvā ekaikaśo nṛpa  || 46.31 ||
na vyaśrāmyata taccittaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāpsarasastadā  |
śacīṃ prati manastasya sakāmamabhavannṛpa  || 46.32 ||
gṛhītvā śakrabhāryāṃ sa prasthitaḥ svapuraṃ prati  |
tataḥ pravavṛte yuddhamandhakasya suraiḥ saha  || 46.33 ||
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve dhvastāḥ pārthivasattama  |
saṃgrāme vividhaiḥ śastraiścakravajrādibhirghanaiḥ  || 46.34 ||
saṃtāpitāḥ surāḥ sarve kṣayaṃ nītā hyanekaśaḥ  |
sarve'pi marutastena bhagnāḥ saṃgrāmamūrdhani  || 46.35 ||
yathā siṃhogajān sarvān vicitya vicaredvanam  |
tadvadekena te devā jitāḥ sarve parāṅmukhāḥ  || 46.36 ||
bālo'dhipo yathā grāme svecchayā pīḍayejjanān  |
svairamākramya gṛhṇāti kośavāsāṃsi cāsakṛt || 46.37 ||
gataṃ na paśyatyātmānaṃ prajāsaṃtāpanena ca  |
gṛhītvā śakrabhāryāṃ sa gato vai dānavottamaḥ  || 46.38 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamāhātmye śacīharaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 47

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gīrvāṇāśca tataḥ sarve brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  |
gajairgirivarākārairhayaiścaiva gajopamaiḥ  || 47.1 ||
syandanairnagarākāraiḥ siṃhaśārdūlayojitaiḥ  |
kacchapairmahiṣaiścānyairmakaraiśca tathāpare  || 47.2 ||
brahmalokamanuprāptā devāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā padmodbhavaṃ devaṃ sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇatāḥ surāḥ  || 47.3 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
jaya deva jagadvandya jaya saṃsṛtikāraka  |
padmayone suraśreṣṭha tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 47.4 ||
sodvegaṃ bhāṣitaṃ śrutvā devānāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
meghagambhīrayā vācā devarājamuvāca ha  || 47.5 ||
kimatrāgamanaṃ devāḥ sarveṣāṃ vai vivarṇatā  |
kenāpamānitāḥ sarve śīghraṃ me kathyatāṃ svayam  || 47.6 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
andhakākhyo mahādaityo balavān padmasambhava  |
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve dhanaratnairviyojitāḥ  || 47.7 ||
hatvā devagaṇāṃstāvadasicakraparaddviśvadhaiḥ  |
gṛhītvā śakrabhāryāṃ sa dānavo'pi gato balāt || 47.8 ||
devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
cintayāmāsa rājendra vadhārthaṃ dānavasya ha  || 47.9 ||
avadhyo dānavaḥ pāpaḥ sarveṣāṃ vo divaukasām  |
sa trātā sarvajagatāṃ nānyo vidyeta kutracit || 47.10 ||
evamuktāḥ surāḥ sarve brahmaṇā tadanantaram  |
brahmāṇaṃ te puraskṛtya gatā yatra sa keśavaḥ  |
tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stotrairbrahmādyāścakrapāṇinam  || 47.11 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
jaya tvaṃ devadeveśa lakṣmyā vakṣaḥsthalāśritaḥ  |
asurakṣaya deveśa vayaṃ te śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 47.12 ||
stūyamānaḥ suraiḥ sarvairbrahmādyaiśca janārdanaḥ  |
samprahṛṣṭamanā bhūtvā surasaṅghamuvāca ha  || 47.13 ||

śrīvāsudeva uvāca -
svāgataṃ devaviprāṇāṃ suprabhātādya śarvarī  |
kiṃ kāryaṃ procyatāṃ kṣipraṃ kasya ruṣṭā divaukasaḥ  || 47.14 ||
kiṃ duḥkhaṃ kaśca saṃtāpaḥ kuto vā bhayamāgatam  |
kathayantu mahābhāgāḥ kāraṇaṃ yanmanogatam  || 47.15 ||
parābhavaḥ kṛto yena so'dya yātu yamālayam  |
evamuktāstu kṛṣṇena kathayāmāsurasya tat || 47.16 ||
darśayantaḥ svakāndehān lajjamānā hyadhomukhāḥ  |
hṛtarājyā hyandhakena kṛtā nistejasaḥ prabho  || 47.17 ||
piteva putraṃ parirakṣa deva jahīndraśatruṃ saha putrapautraiḥ  |
tatheti coktaḥ kamalāsanena surāsurairvanditapādapadmaḥ  || 47.18 ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ cāpaṃ saṃgṛhya parameśvaraḥ  |
utthito bhogaparyaṅkāddevānāṃ puratastadā  || 47.19 ||

śrīvāsudeva uvāca -
pātāle yadi vā martye nāke vā yadi tiṣṭhati  |
taṃ haniṣyāmyahaṃ pāpaṃ yena saṃtāpitāḥ surāḥ  || 47.20 ||
svaṃ sthānaṃ yāntu gīrvāṇāḥ saṃtuṣṭā bhāvitaujasaḥ  |
viṣṇostadvacanaṃ śrutvā brahmādyāste savāsavāḥ  || 47.21 ||
svayānaistu hariṃ natvā hṛdi tuṣṭā divaṃ yayuḥ  || 47.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gīrvāṇasvargamanavarṇanaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 48

uttānapāda uvāca -
kasminsthāne'vasaddeva so'ndhako daityapuṃgavaḥ  |
sarvāndevāṃśca nirjitya kasminsthāne samāsthitaḥ  || 48.1 ||

śrīmaheśa uvāca -
praviṣṭo dānavo yatra kathayāmi narādhipa  |
pātālalokamāśritya kanyā vidhvaṃsate tu saḥ  || 48.2 ||
tatra sthitaṃ taṃ vijñāya cāpamādāya keśavaḥ  |
vyasṛjadbāṇamāgneyaṃ dahyatāmiti cintayan  || 48.3 ||
dahyamāno'gninā so'pi vāruṇāstraṃ sa saṃdadhe  |
vāruṇāstreṇa mahatā āgneyaṃ śamitaṃ tadā  || 48.4 ||
tato'sau cintayāmāsa kena bāṇo visarjitaḥ  |
kasyaiṣā pauruṣī śaktiḥ ko yāsyati yamālayam  || 48.5 ||
tato'ndhako mṛdhe kruddho bāṇamārgeṇa nirgataḥ  |
sa dṛṣṭvā bāṇamārgeṇa cāpahastaṃ janārdanam  || 48.6 ||

andhaka uvāca -
na śarma lapsyase hyadya mayā dṛṣṭyābhivīkṣitaḥ  |
na śaknoṣi tathā gantuṃ nāgaḥ śārdūladarśanāt || 48.7 ||
āgacchati yathā bhakṣyaṃ mārjārasya ca mūṣikaḥ  |
na śaknoṣi tathā yātuṃ saṃsthitastvaṃ mamāgrataḥ  || 48.8 ||
ahaṃ tvāṃ preṣayiṣyāmi yamamārge sudāruṇe  |
ahamanveṣayiṣyāmi kila yāsyāmi te gṛham  || 48.9 ||
upanīto'si kālena saṅgrāme mama keśava  |
ye tvayā nirjitāḥ pūrvaṃ dānavā apyanekaśaḥ  || 48.10 ||
na bhavanti pumāṃsaste striyastāścaiva keśava  |
paraṃ na śastrasaṅgrāmaṃ kariṣyāmi tvayā saha  || 48.11 ||
vadato dānavendrasya na cukopa sa keśavaḥ  |
ayudhyamānaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā cintayāmāsa dānavaḥ  || 48.12 ||
dvandvayuddhaṃ kariṣyāmi niścitya yuyudhe nṛpa  |
sa kṛṣṇena padākṣiptaḥ patitaḥ pṛthivītale  || 48.13 ||
muhūrtātsa samāśvasya utthāyedaṃ vyacintayat |
aśakto dvandvayuddhāya tataḥ sāma prayuktavān  |
pāṇibhyāṃ sampuṭaṃ kṛtvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇataḥ śuciḥ  || 48.14 ||

andhaka uvāca -
jaya kṛṣṇāya haraye viṣṇave jiṣṇave namaḥ  |
hṛṣīkeśa jagaddhātre acyutāya mahātmane  || 48.15 ||
namaḥ paṅkajanābhāya namaḥ paṅkajamāline  |
janārdanāya śrīśāya śrīpate pītavāsase  || 48.16 ||
govindāya namo nityaṃ namo jaladhiśāyine  |
namaḥ karālavaktrāya narasiṃhāya nādine  || 48.17 ||
śārṅgiṇe sitavarṇāya śaṅkhacakragadābhṛte  |
namo vāmanarūpāya yajñarūpāya te namaḥ  || 48.18 ||
namo varāharūpāya krāntalokatrayāya ca  |
vyāptāśeṣadigantāya keśavāya namonamaḥ  || 48.19 ||
vāsudeva namastubhyaṃ namaḥ kaiṭabhanāśine  |
lakṣmyālaya suraśreṣṭha namaste suranāyaka  || 48.20 ||
viṣṇordevādhidevasya pramāṇaṃ ye'pi kurvate  |
prajāpaterjagaddhātusteṣāmapi namāmyaham  || 48.21 ||
samastabhūtadevasya vāsudevasya dhīmataḥ  |
praṇāmaṃ ye prakurvanti teṣāmapi namāmyaham  || 48.22 ||
tasya yajñavarāhasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ  |
praṇāmaṃ ye prakurvanti teṣāmapi namāmyaham  || 48.23 ||
guṇānāṃ hi nidhānāya namaste'stu punaḥpunaḥ  |
kāruṇyāmbunidhe deva sarvabhaktipriyāya ca  || 48.24 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca -
tuṣṭaste dānavendrāhaṃ varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  |
dadāmi te varaṃ nūnamapi trailokyadurlabham  || 48.25 ||

andhaka uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dāsyasi cepsitam  |
tadā dadasva me deva yuddhaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
avaddhastapūto yenāhaṃ lokāngantāsmi śobhanān  || 48.26 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca -
kathaṃ dadāmi te yuddhaṃ toṣito'haṃ tvayā punaḥ  |
na tvāṃ tu prabhavetkopaḥ kathaṃ yudhyāmi te'ndhaka  || 48.27 ||
yadi te vartate buddhiryuddhaṃ prati na saṃśayaḥ  |
tato gacchasva yuddhāya devaṃ prati maheśvaram  || 48.28 ||

andhaka uvāca -
na tatra sidhyate kāryaṃ devaṃ prati maheśvaram  || 48.29 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca -
putra tvaṃ śikharaṃ gatvā dhūnayasva balena ca  || 48.30 ||
vidhūte tatra deveśaḥ kopaṃ kartā sudāruṇam  |
kopitaḥ śaṅkaro raudraṃ yuddhaṃ dāsyati dānava  || 48.31 ||
viṣṇuvākyādasau pāpo gato yatra maheśvaraḥ  |
kailāsaśikharaṃ prāpya dhunoti sma muhurmuhuḥ  || 48.32 ||
dhūnite tatra śikhare kampitaṃ bhuvanatrayam  |
nipetuḥ śikharāgrāṇi kampamānānyanekaśaḥ  || 48.33 ||
catvāraḥ sāgarāḥ kṣipramekībhūtā mahīpate  |
nipeturulkāpātāśca pādapā apyanekaśaḥ  || 48.34 ||
umayā sahito devo vismayaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  |
gāḍhamāliṅgya girijā devaṃ vacanamabravīt || 48.35 ||
kimarthaṃ kampate śailaḥ kimarthaṃ kampate dharā  |
kimarthaṃ kampate nāgo martyaḥ pātālameva ca  |
kiṃ vā yugakṣayo deva tanmamākhyātumarhasi  || 48.36 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kasyaiṣā durmatirjātā kṣiptaḥ sarpamukhe karaḥ  |
lalāṭe ca kṛtaṃ varma sa yāsyati yamālayam  || 48.37 ||
kailāsamāśrito yena supto'haṃ yena bodhitaḥ  |
taṃ vadhiṣye na sandehaḥ sammukho vā bhavedyadi  || 48.38 ||
cintayāmāsa deveśo hyandhako'yaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
upāyaṃ cintayāmāsa yenāsau vadhyate kṣaṇāt || 48.39 ||
āgatāśca surāḥ sarve brahmādyā vasubhiḥ saha  |
rathaṃ devamayaṃ kṛtvā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam  || 48.40 ||
keciddevāḥ sthitāścakre kecittuṇḍāgrapārśvayoḥ  |
kecinnābhyāṃ sthitā devāḥ keciddhuryeṣu saṃsthitāḥ  || 48.41 ||
dhurīṣu niścalāḥ kecitkecidyūpeṣu saṃsthitāḥ  |
kecitsyandanasaṃstambhāḥ kecitsyandanaveṣṭakāḥ  || 48.42 ||
āmalasārake'nye'pi anye'pi kalaśe sthitāḥ  |
riporbhayaṃkaraṃ divyaṃ dhvajamālādiśobhitam  || 48.43 ||
rathaṃ devamayaṃ kṛtvā tamārūḍho jagadguruḥ  |
niryayau dānavo yatra kopāviṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 48.44 ||
tiṣṭha tiṣṭhetyuvācātha kva prayāsyasi durmate  |
śarāsanaṃ kare gṛhya śarāṃścikṣepa dānave  || 48.45 ||
dānave'dhiṣṭhite yuddhe śaraiścicheda sāyakān  |
śarāsaneṇa tatraiva andhakaśchāditastadā  || 48.46 ||
na tatra dṛśyate sūryo nākāśaṃ na ca candramāḥ  |
āgneyamastraṃ vyasṛjaddānavo'pi śivaṃ prati  || 48.47 ||
dahyamānāḥ śarāṅgāraistatrasuḥ sarvadevatāḥ  |
rakṣa rakṣa mahādeva dahyamānāṃstu dānavāt || 48.48 ||
tato devādhidevo'sau vāruṇāstramayo'jayat |
vāruṇāstreṇa nimiṣādāgneyaṃ nāśitaṃ tadā  || 48.49 ||
dānavena tadā muktaṃ vāyavyāstraṃ raṇājire  |
vāruṇaṃ ca gataṃ tāta vāyavyāstravināśitam  || 48.50 ||
devo vyasarjayatsārpaṃ krodhāviṣṭena cetasā  |
mārutaṃ nāśitaṃ bāṇaiḥ sarpaistatra na saṃśayaḥ  || 48.51 ||
dānavena tato muktaṃ garuḍāstraṃ ca līlayā  |
gāruḍāstraṃ ca taddṛṣṭvā sārpaṃ naiva vyadṛśyata  || 48.52 ||
tato devādhidevena nārasiṃhaṃ visarjitam  |
