nārasiṃhāstrabāṇena gāruḍāstraṃ praśāmitam  || 48.53 ||
astramastreṇa śamyeta na bādhyeta parasparam  |
mahadyuddhamabhūttātasurāsurabhayaṃkaram  || 48.54 ||
cakranālīkanārācaistomaraiḥ khaḍgamudgaraiḥ  |
vatsadantaistathā bhallaiḥ karṇikāraiśca śobhanaiḥ  || 48.55 ||
evaṃ na śakyate hantuṃ dānavo vividhāyudhaiḥ  |
tadā jvālākarālāśca khaḍganārācatomarāḥ  || 48.56 ||
vṛṣāṅkena vimuktāstu samare dānavaṃ prati  |
na saṃspṛśanti śastrāṇi gātraṃ gauḍavadhūriva  || 48.57 ||
āyudhāni tatastyaktvā bāhuyuddhamupasthitau  |
karaṃ kareṇa saṃgṛhya praharantau svamuṣṭibhiḥ  |
raṇaprayogairyudhyantau yuyudhāte śivāndhakau  || 48.58 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
andhakaṃ prati deveśaścintayāmāsa nigraham  |
haniṣyāmi na sandeho duṣṭātmānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 48.59 ||
sa śivena yadā kṣiptaḥ patitaḥ pṛthivītale  |
ūrdhvabāhuradhovaktro dānavo nṛpasattama  || 48.60 ||
krodhāviṣṭena deveśaḥ saṅgrāme devaśatruṇā  |
kakṣayoḥ kuhare kṣiptvā bandhenākramya pīḍitaḥ  || 48.61 ||
nispandaścābhavaddevo mūrcchāyukto maheśvaraḥ  |
mūrcchāpannaṃ tu taṃ jñātvā cintayāmāsa dānavaḥ  || 48.62 ||
hāhā kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ me'dya duṣkṛtaṃ pāpakarmaṇā  |
kiṃ karomi kathaṃ karma kasminsthāne tu mocaye  || 48.63 ||
gṛhītvā devamutsaṅge gataḥ kailāsaparvatam  |
śayyāyāṃ śaṅkaraṃ nyasya niryayau daityarāṭtataḥ  || 48.64 ||
śayyāyāṃ patito devaḥ prapede vedanāṃ tataḥ  |
tāvaddadarśa cātmānaṃ svakīyabhavanasthitam  || 48.65 ||
parābhavaḥ kṛto madyaṃ kathaṃ tena durātmanā  |
krodhavegasamāviṣṭo niryayau dānavaṃ prati  || 48.66 ||
āyasīṃ laguḍīṃ gṛhya prabhurbhārasahasrajām  |
dānavaṃ ca tato dṛṣṭvā prākṣipattasya mūrdhani  || 48.67 ||
khaḍgena tāḍayāmāsa dānavaḥ prahasanraṇe  |
devenāthasmṛtaṃ cāstraṃ kauccherākhyaṃ mahāhave  || 48.68 ||
dīpyamānaṃ samutsṛjya hṛdaye tāḍitaḥ kṣaṇāt |
tataḥ sa tāḍitastena rudhirodgāramudvaman  || 48.69 ||
patito'dhomukho bhūtvā tataḥ śūlena bheditaḥ  |
punaśca devadevena śūlena dvidalīkṛtaḥ  || 48.70 ||
śūlāgre'sau sthitaḥ pāpo bhrāntavāṃścakravattadā  |
ye ye bhūmyāṃ patanti sma tatkāyādraktabindavaḥ  || 48.71 ||
te te sarve samuttasthurdānavāḥ śāstrapāṇayaḥ  |
vyākulastu tato devo dānavena tarasvinā  || 48.72 ||
devenātha smṛtā durgā cāmuṇḍā bhīṣaṇānanā  |
āyātā bhīṣaṇākārā nānāyudhavirājitā  || 48.73 ||
mahādaṃṣṭrā mahākāyā piṅgākṣī lambakarṇikā  |
ādeśo dīyatāṃ deva ko yāsyati yamālayam  || 48.74 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pibāsya rudhiraṃ bhadre yatheṣṭaṃ dānavasya ca  |
nipatadrudhiraṃ bhūmau durge gṛhṇīṣva māciram  || 48.75 ||
nihanmi dānavaṃ yāvatsāhāyyaṃ kuru sundari  |
evamuktā tu sā durgā papau ca rudhiraṃ tataḥ  || 48.76 ||
nihatā dānavāḥ sarve deveśena sahasraśaḥ  |
andhako'pi ca tān dṛṣṭvā dānavānavaniṃ gatān  |
tato vāgbhiḥ pratuṣṭāva devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 48.77 ||

andhaka uvāca -
jayasva devadeveśa umārdhārdhāśarīradhṛk |
namaste devadeveśa sarvāya triguṇātmane  || 48.78 ||
vṛṣabhāsanamārūḍha śaśāṅkakṛtaśekhara  |
jaya khaṭvāṅgahastāya gaṅgādhara namo'stu te  || 48.79 ||
namo ḍamaruhastāya namaḥ kapālamāline  |
smaradehavināśāya maheśāya namo'stu te  || 48.80 ||
pūṣṇo dantanipātāya gaṇanāthāya te namaḥ  |
jaya svarūpadehāya arūpabahurūpiṇe  || 48.81 ||
uttamāṅgavināśāya viriñcerapi śaṅkara  |
śmaśānavāsine nityaṃ nityaṃ bhairavarūpiṇe  || 48.82 ||
tvaṃ sarvago'si tvaṃ kartā tvaṃ hartā nānya eva ca  |
tvaṃ bhūmistvaṃ diśaścaiva tvaṃ gururbhārgavastathā  || 48.83 ||
sauristvaṃ devadeveśa bhūmiputrastathaiva ca  |
ṛkṣagrahādikaṃ sarvaṃ yaddṛśyaṃ tattvameva ca  || 48.84 ||
evaṃ stutiṃ tadā kṛtvā devaṃ prati sa dānavaḥ  |
saṃhatābhyāṃ tu pāṇibhyāṃ praṇanāma maheśvaram  || 48.85 ||

īśvara uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāsattva varaṃ yācasva dānava  |
dātāhaṃ yācakastvaṃ hi dadāmīha yathepsitam  || 48.86 ||

andhaka uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
tadātmasadṛśo'haṃ te kartavyo nāparo varaḥ  || 48.87 ||
bhasmī jaṭī trinetrī ca triśūlī ca caturbhujaḥ  |
vyāghracarmottarīyaśca nāgayajñopavītakaḥ  || 48.88 ||
etadicchāmyahaṃ sarvaṃ yadi tuṣṭo maheśvara  || 48.89 ||

īśvara uvāca -
dadāmi te varaṃ hyadya yastvayā yācito'nagha  |
gaṇeṣu me sthitaḥ putra bhṛṅgīśastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 48.90 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe'ndhakavadhatadvarapradānavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 49

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
andhakaṃ tu nihatyātha devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
umayā sahito rudraḥ kailāsamagamannagam  || 49.1 ||
āgatāśca tato devā brahmādyāśca savāsavāḥ  |
hṛṣṭāstuṣṭāśca te sarve praṇemuḥ pārvatīpatim  || 49.2 ||

īśvara uvāca -
upāviśantu te sarve ye kecana samāgatāḥ  |
nihato dānavo hyeṣa gīrvāṇārthe pitāmaha  || 49.3 ||
raktena tasya me śūlaṃ nirmalaṃ naiva jāyate  |
śubhavratatapojapyarato brahmanmayā hataḥ  || 49.4 ||
kartumicchāmyahaṃ samyaktīrthayānaṃ caturmukha  |
āgacchantu mayā sārddhaṃ ye yūyamiha saṃgatāḥ  || 49.5 ||
ityuktvā devadeveśaḥ prabhāsaṃ pratiniryayau  |
prabhāsādyāni tīrthāni gaṅgāsāgaramadhyataḥ  || 49.6 ||
avagāhyāpi sarvāṇi nairmalyaṃ nābhavannṛpa  |
narmadāyāṃ tato gatvā devo devaiḥ samanvitaḥ  || 49.7 ||
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kūlamavāgāhatpriyavrataḥ  |
gatastu dakṣiṇe kūle parvate bhṛgusaṃjñitam  || 49.8 ||
tatra sthitvā mahādevo devaiḥ saha mahīpate  |
bhrāntvā bhrāntvā ciraṃ śrānto nirviṇṇo niṣasāda ha  || 49.9 ||
manohāri yataḥ sthānaṃ sarveṣāṃ vai divaukasām  |
tīrthaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ tanmatvā sthito devo maheśvaraḥ  || 49.10 ||
giriṃ vivyādha śūlena bhinnaṃ tena rasātalam  |
nirmalaṃ cābhavacchūlaṃ na lepo dṛśyate kvacit || 49.11 ||
devairāhvānitā tatra mahāpuṇyā ca bhāratī  |
parvatānniḥsṛtā tatra mahāpuṇyā sarasvatī  || 49.12 ||
dvitīyaḥ saṅgamastatra yathā veṇyāṃ sitāsitaḥ  |
tatra brahmā svayaṃ devo brahmeśaṃ liṅgamuttamam  || 49.13 ||
saṃsthāpayāmāsa puṇyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
tasya yāmye diśo bhāge svayaṃ devo janārdanaḥ  || 49.14 ||
tiṣṭhate ca sadā tatra viṣṇupādāgrasaṃsthitā  |
ambhaso na bhavenmārgaḥ kuṇḍamadhyasthitasya ca  || 49.15 ||
śūlāgreṇa kṛtā rekhā tatastoyaṃ vahennṛpa  |
tattoyaṃ ca gataṃ tatra yatra revā mahānadī  || 49.16 ||
jalaliṅgaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ cakatīrthaṃ nṛpottama  |
śūlabhede ca deveśaḥ snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 49.17 ||
ātmānaṃ manyate śuddhaṃ na kiṃcitkalmaṣaṃ kṛtam  |
tasyaivottarakāṣṭhāyāṃ devadevo jagadguruḥ  || 49.18 ||
ātmanā devadeveśaḥ śūlapāṇiḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
sarvatīrtheṣu tattīrthaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ param  || 49.19 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe pratiṣṭhāpya devadevaṃ jagadguruḥ  || 49.20 ||
rakṣāpālāṃstato muktvā śataṃ sāṣṭavināyakān  |
kṣetrapālāḥ śataṃ sāṣṭaṃ tadrakṣanti prayatnataḥ  || 49.21 ||
vighnāstasyopajāyante yastatra sthātumicchati  |
kecitkuṭumbāttatāsu vyāgrāḥ kecitkṛṣīṣu ca  || 49.22 ||
kecitsabhāṃ prakurvanti keciddravyārjane ratāḥ  |
parokṣavādaṃ kurvanti ke'pi hiṃsāratāḥ sadā  || 49.23 ||
paradāraratāḥ kecitkecidvṛttivihiṃsakāḥ  |
anye kecidvadantyevaṃ kathaṃ tīrtheṣu gamyate  || 49.24 ||
kṣudhayā pīḍyate bhāryā putrabhṛtyādayastadā  |
mohajāleṣu yojyante evaṃ devagaṇairnarāḥ  || 49.25 ||
pāpācārāśca ye martyāḥ snānaṃ teṣāṃ na jāyate  |
saṃrakṣanti ca tattīrthaṃ devabhṛtyagaṇāḥ sadā  || 49.26 ||
dhanyāḥ puṇyāśca ye martyāsteṣāṃ snānaṃ prajāyate  |
sarasvatyā bhogavatyā devanadyā viśeṣataḥ  || 49.27 ||
ayaṃ tu saṅgamaḥ puṇyo yathā veṇyāṃ sitāsitaḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā tīrthaṃ tu te sarve gīrvāṇā hṛṣṭacetasaḥ  || 49.28 ||
devasya sannidhau bhūtvā varṇayāmāsuruttamam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tu deveśa gayātīrthena te samam  || 49.29 ||
guhyādguhyatamaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
śūlapāṇiḥ samabhyarcya indrādyairapsarogaṇaiḥ  || 49.30 ||
yakṣakinnaragandharvairdikpālairlokapairapi  |
nṛtyagītaistathā stotraiḥ sarvaiścāpi surāsuraiḥ  || 49.31 ||
pūjyamāno gaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ siddhairnāgairmaheśvaraḥ  |
devena bheditaṃ tatra śūlāgreṇa narādhipa  || 49.32 ||
tridhā yatrekṣyate'dyāpi hyāvartaḥ surapūritaḥ  |
kuṇḍatrayaṃ naravyāghra mahatkalakalānvitam  || 49.33 ||
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snāti upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  || 49.34 ||
dīkṣāmantravihīno'pi mucyate cābdikādaghāt |
ye punarvidhivatsnānti mantraiḥ pañcabhireva ca  || 49.35 ||
vedoktaiḥ pañcabhirmantraiḥ sahiraṇyaghaṭaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
akṣarairdaśabhiścaiva ṣaḍbhirvā tribhireva vā  || 49.36 ||
pṛthagbhūtairdvijātīnāṃ tīrthe kāryaṃ narādhipa  |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ vāpi strīśūdrāṇāṃ tathaiva ca  || 49.37 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ trayīṃ dhyātvā snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  |
daśākṣareṇa mantreṇa ye pibanti jalaṃ narāḥ  || 49.38 ||
te gacchanti paraṃ lokaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
kedāre ca yathā pītaṃ rudrakuṇḍe tathaiva ca  || 49.39 ||
pañcarephasamāyuktaṃ kṣakāraṃ surapūjitam  |
oṅkāreṇa samāyuktametadvedyaṃ prakīrtitam  || 49.40 ||
yastatra kurute snānaṃ vidhiyukto jitendriyaḥ  |
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 49.41 ||
kulānāṃ tārayedviṃśaṃ daśapūrvāndaśāparān  |
gayādipañcasthāneṣu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ  || 49.42 ||
sa tatra phalamāpnoti śūlabhede na saṃśayaḥ  |
yastatra vidhinā yukto dadyāddānāni bhaktitaḥ  || 49.43 ||
tudakṣayaṃ phalaṃ tatra sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tathā  |
gayāśiro yathā puṇyaṃ pitṛkāryeṣu sarvadā  || 49.44 ||
śūlabhedaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ snānadānāditarpaṇaiḥ  |
bhaktyā dadāti yastatra kāñcanaṃ gāṃ mahīṃ tilān  || 49.45 ||
āsanopānahau śayyāṃ varāśvān kṣatriyastathā  |
vastrayugmaṃ ca dhānyaṃ ca gṛhaṃ pūrṇaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 49.46 ||
sayoktraṃ lāṅgalaṃ dadyātkṛṣṭāṃ caiva vasuṃdharām  |
dānānyetāni yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage  || 49.47 ||
śrotriye kulasampanne śuciṣmati jitendriye  |
śrutādhyayanasampanne dambhahīne kriyānvite  |
trayodaśāhaḥsvekaikaṃ trayodaśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 49.48 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedotpattimāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 50

uttānapāda uvāca -
dvijāśca kīdṛśāḥ pūjyā apūjyāḥ kīdṛśāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
śrāddhe vaivāhike kārye dāne caiva viśeṣataḥ  || 50.1 ||
yadi śraddhā bhaveddaivayogācchrāddhādike vidhau  |
etadākhyāhi me deva kasya dānaṃ na dīyate  || 50.2 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ  |
brāhmaṇaścānadhīyānastrayaste nāmadhārakāḥ  || 50.3 ||
yathā ṣaṇḍho'phalaḥ strīṣu yathā gaurgavi cāphalā  |
yathā cājñe'phalaṃ dānaṃ tathā vipro'nṛco'phalaḥ  || 50.4 ||
yathā'nṛṇe bījamuptvā vaptā na labhate phalam  |
tathānṛce havirdattvā na dātā labhate phalam  || 50.5 ||
rogī hīnātiriktāṅgaḥ kāṇaḥ paunarbhavastathā  |
avakīrṇī śyāvadantaḥ sarvāśī vṛṣalīpatiḥ  || 50.6 ||
mitradhrukpiśunaḥ somavikrayī paranindakaḥ  |
pitṛmātṛgurutyāgī nityaṃ brāhmaṇanindakaḥ  || 50.7 ||
śūdrānnaṃ mantrasaṃyuktaṃ yo vipro bhakṣayennṛpa  |
so'spṛśyaḥ karmacāṇḍālaḥ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret || 50.8 ||
kunakhī vṛṣalī steyī vārddhuṣyaḥ kuṇḍagolakau  |
mahādānarato yaśca yaścātmahanane rataḥ  || 50.9 ||
bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ klībaḥ kanyādūṣyabhiśastakaḥ  |
ete viprāḥ sadā tyājyāḥ paribhāvya prayatnataḥ  || 50.10 ||
pratigrahaṃ gṛhītvā tu vāṇijyaṃ yastu kārayet |
tasya dānaṃ na dātavyaṃ vṛthā bhavati tasya tat || 50.11 ||
śrutādhyayanasampannā ye dvijā vṛttatatparāḥ  |
teṣāṃ yaddīyate dānaṃ sarvamakṣayatāṃ vrajet || 50.12 ||
daridrānbhara bhūpāla mā samṛddhān kadācana  |
vyādhitasyauṣadhaṃ pathyaṃ nīrujasya kimauṣadhaiḥ  || 50.13 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
kīdṛśo'tha vidhistatra tīrthaśrāddhasya kā kriyā  |
dānaṃ ca dīyate yadvattanmamākhyāhi śaṅkara  || 50.14 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā gṛhe bhaktyā śuciścāpi jitendriyaḥ  |
guruṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhojya sīmāntake tataḥ  || 50.15 ||
vāgyataḥ pravrajettāvadyāvatsīmāṃ na laṅghayet |
śūlabhedaṃ tato gatvā snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 50.16 ||
pañcasthāneṣu ca śrāddhaṃ havyakavyādibhiḥ kramāt |
piṇḍadānaṃ ca yaḥ kuryātpāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  || 50.17 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti dvādaśābdāni pañca ca  |
akṣatairbadarairbilvairgudamadhusarpiṣā  || 50.18 ||
sāpi tatphalamāpnoti tīrthe'sminnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
upānahau ca yo dadyādbrāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayatnataḥ  || 50.19 ||
so'pi svargamavāpnoti hayārūḍho na saṃśayaḥ  |
śayyāmaśvaṃ ca yo dadyācchattrikāṃ vā viśeṣataḥ  || 50.20 ||
gacchedvimānamārūḍhaḥ so'psarovṛndaveṣṭitaḥ  |
uttamaṃ yo gṛhaṃ dadyātsaptadhānyasamanvitam  || 50.21 ||
svecchayā me vaselloke kāñcane bhavane hi saḥ  |
tiladhenuṃ ca yo dadyātsavatsāṃ vastrasaṃplutām  || 50.22 ||
nākapṛṣṭhe vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
gṛhe vā yadi vāraṇye tīrthavartmani vā nṛpa  || 50.23 ||
toyamannaṃ ca yo dadyādyamalokaṃ sa nekṣate  |
sarvadānāni dīyante teṣāṃ phalamavāpyate  || 50.24 ||
udakaṃ cātra dānaṃ ca dadyādabhayameva ca  |
annadānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  || 50.25 ||
kanyādānaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādvṛṣaṃ vā yaḥ samutsṛjet |
tasya vāso bhavettatra yatrāhamiti nānyathā  || 50.26 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
kanyādānaṃ kathaṃ svāmin kartavyaṃ dhārmikaiḥ sadā  |
parigraho yathā poṣyaḥ kanyodvāhastathaiva ca  || 50.27 ||
anyatpṛcchāmi deveśa kasya kanyā na dīyate  |
dātavyaṃ kutra taddeva kasmai dattamathākṣayam  || 50.28 ||
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ vāpi kanīyaḥ syātkathaṃ vibho  |
rājasaṃ tāmasaṃ vāpi niḥśreyasamathāpi vā  || 50.29 ||

īśvara uvāca -
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ kanyādānaṃ viśiṣyate  |
yo dadyātparayā bhaktyābhigamya tanayāṃ nijām  || 50.30 ||
kulīnāya surūpāya guṇajñāya manīṣiṇe  |
sulagne sumuhūrte ca dadyātkanyāmalaṃkṛtām  || 50.31 ||
aśvānnā gāṃśca vāsāṃsi yo'tra dadyātsvaśaktitaḥ  |
tasya vāso bhavettatra padaṃ yatra nirāmayam  || 50.32 ||
yenātra duhitā dattā prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasī  |
tena sarvamidaṃ dattaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 50.33 ||
yaḥ kanyārthaṃ tato labdhvā bhikṣate caiva taddhanam  |
sa bhavetkarmacaṇḍālaḥ kāṣṭhakīlo bhavenmṛtaḥ  || 50.34 ||
gṛhe'pi tasya yo'śnīyājjihvālaulyātkathaṃcana  |
cāndrāyaṇena śudhyeta taptakṛcchreṇa vā punaḥ  || 50.35 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
vittaṃ na vidyate yasya kanyaivāsti ca yadgṛhe  |
kathaṃ codvāhanaṃ tasya na yāñcāṃ kurute yadi  || 50.36 ||

īśvara uvāca -
avitenaiva kartavyaṃ kanyodvahanakaṃ nṛpa  |
kanyānāma samuccārya na doṣāya kadācana  || 50.37 ||
abhigamyottamaṃ dānaṃ yacca dānamayācitam  |
bhaviṣyati yugasyāntastasyānto naiva vidyate  || 50.38 ||
abhigamyottamaṃ dānaṃ smṛtamāhūya madhyamam  |
yācyamānaṃ kanīyaḥ syāddehi dehīti cādhamam  || 50.39 ||
yathaivāśmāśmanābaddho nikṣipto vārimadhyataḥ  |
dvāvetau nidhanaṃ yātastadvadannamapātrake  || 50.40 ||
asamarthe tato dānaṃ na pradeyaṃ kadācana  |
dātāraṃ nayate'dhastādātmānaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 50.41 ||
samarthastārayeddvau tu kāṣṭhaṃ śuṣkaṃ yathā jale  |
yathā nauśca tathā vidvānprāpayedaparaṃ taṭam  || 50.42 ||
āhitāgniśca gṛhṇāti yaḥ śūdrāṇāṃ pratigraham  |
iha janmani śūdro'sau mṛtaḥ śvā copajāyate  || 50.43 ||
vṛthā kleśaśca jāyeta brāhmaṇe hyagnihotriṇi  |
asatpratigrahaṃ kurvanguptaṃ nīcasya garhitam  || 50.44 ||
abhojyaḥ sa bhavenmartyo dahyate kāriṣāgninā  |
kaṭakāro bhavetpaścātsapta janma na saṃśayaḥ  || 50.45 ||
lajjādākṣiṇyalobhācca yaddānaṃ coparodhajam  |
bhṛtyebhyaśca tu yaddānaṃ tadvṛthā niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 50.46 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamāhātmye pātrāpātraparīkṣādānādiniyamavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 51

uttānapāda uvāca -
kāle tatkriyate kasmiñchrāddhaṃ dānaṃ tatheśvara  |
yātrā tatra prakartavyā tithau yasyāṃ vadāśu tat || 51.1 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pitṛtīrthaṃ yathā puṇyaṃ sarvakāmikamuttamam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ snānadānāditarpaṇaiḥ  || 51.2 ||
viśeṣeṇa tu kurvīta śrāddhaṃ sarvayugādiṣu  |
manvantarādayo vatsa śrūyantāṃ ca caturdaśa  || 51.3 ||
aśvayukchuklanavamī dvādaśī kārttikasya ca  |
tṛtīyā caitramāsasya tathā bhādrapadasya ca  || 51.4 ||
āṣāḍhasyaiva daśamī māghasyaiva tu saptamī  |
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhasya pūrṇimā  || 51.5 ||
phālgunasya tvamāvāsyā pauṣasyaikādaśī sitā  |
kārttikī phālgunī caitrī jyaiṣṭhī pañcadaśī tathā  || 51.6 ||
manvantarādayaścaite anantaphaladāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
ayane cottare rājandakṣiṇe śrāddhamācaret || 51.7 ||
kārttikī ca tathā māghī vaiśākhasya tṛtīyikā  |
paurṇamāsī ca caitrasya jyeṣṭhasya ca viśeṣataḥ  || 51.8 ||
aṣṭakāsu ca saṃkrāntau vyatīpāte tathaiva ca  |
śrāddhakālā ime sarve dattameṣvakṣayaṃ smṛtam  || 51.9 ||
madhumāse site pakṣa ekādaśyāmupoṣitaḥ  |
niśi jāgaraṇaṃ kuryādviṣṇupādasamīpataḥ  || 51.10 ||
dhūpadīpādinaivedyaiḥ sraṅmālāgurucandanaiḥ  |
arcāṃ kurvanti ye viṣṇoḥ paṭheyuḥ prāktanīṃ kathām  || 51.11 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmamantroktaṃ sūktaṃ japati yo dvijaḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati  || 51.12 ||
prātaḥ śrāddhaṃ prakurvīta dvijān sampūjya yatnataḥ  |
dānaṃ dadyādyathāśakti gohiraṇyāmbarādikam  || 51.13 ||
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
śrāddhadastu vrajettatra yatra devo janārdanaḥ  || 51.14 ||
trayodaśyāṃ tato gacchedguhāvāsini liṅgake  |
dṛṣṭvā mārkaṇḍamīśānaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 51.15 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
guhāmadhye mahādeva liṅgaṃ paramaśobhitam  |
yena pratiṣṭhitaṃ deva tanmamākhyātumarhasi  || 51.16 ||

īśvara uvāca -
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyāto mārkaṇḍeyo munīśvaraḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ sa tapastepe sudāruṇam  || 51.17 ||
guhāmadhyaṃ praviṣṭo'sau yogābhyāsamupāśritaḥ  |
liṅgaṃ tu sthāpitaṃ tena mārkaṇḍeśvarasaṃjñitam  || 51.18 ||
tatra snātvā ca yo bhaktyā sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
tatra jāgaraṇaṃ kurvan dadyāddīpaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 51.19 ||
devasya snapanaṃ kuryānmṛtaiḥ pañcabhistathā  |
yathā śaktyā samālabhya pūjāṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 51.20 ||
svaśākhotpannamantraiśca japaṃ kuryurdvijātayaḥ  |
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu śatāṣṭakamathāpi vā  || 51.21 ||
etatkṛtvā nṛpaśreṣṭha janmanaḥ phalamāpnuyāt |
caturdaśyāṃ tu vai snātvā pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 51.22 ||
pātraṃ parīkṣya dātavyamātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti dvādaśābdānyasaṃśayam  || 51.23 ||
dātā sa gacchate tatra yatra bhogāḥ sanātanāḥ  |
guhāmadhye praviṣṭastu loṭayeccaiva śaktitaḥ  || 51.24 ||
nīle girau hi yatpuṇyaṃ tatsamastaṃ labhanti te  |
śūlabhede tu yaḥ kuryācchrāddhaṃ parvaṇi parvaṇi  || 51.25 ||
viśeṣāccaitramāsānte tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
kedāre caiva yatpuṇyaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  || 51.26 ||
sitāsite tu yatpuṇyamanyatīrthe viśeṣataḥ  |
arbude vidyate puṇyaṃ puṇyaṃ cāmaraparvate  || 51.27 ||
gayādisarvatīrthānāṃ phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
vidhimantrasamāyuktastarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 51.28 ||
kulānāṃ tārayedviṃśaṃ daśa pūrvān daśāparān  |
dakṣiṇasyāṃ tato mūrtau śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 51.29 ||
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvoktaṃ pradadyādaṣṭapuṣpikām  |
śāstroktairaṣṭabhiḥ puṣpairmānasaiḥ śṛṇu tadyathā  || 51.30 ||
vārijaṃ saumyamāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ pārthivaṃ punaḥ  |
vānaspatyaṃ bhavetṣaṣṭhaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu saptamam  || 51.31 ||
aṣṭamaṃ śivapuṣpaṃ syādeṣāṃ śṛṇu vinirṇayam  |
vārijaṃ salilaṃ jñeyaṃ saumyaṃ madhughṛtaṃ payaḥ  || 51.32 ||
āgneyaṃ dhūpadīpādyaṃ vāyavyaṃ candanādikam  |
pārthivaṃ kandamūlādyaṃ vānaspatyaṃ phalātmakam  || 51.33 ||
prājāpatyaṃ tu pāṭhādyaṃ śivapuṣpaṃ tu vāsanā  |
ahiṃsā prathamaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigrahaḥ  || 51.34 ||
tṛtīyaṃ tu dayā puṣpaṃ kṣamā puṣpaṃ caturthakam  |
dhyānapuṣpaṃ tapaḥ puṣpaṃ jñānapuṣpaṃ tu saptamam  || 51.35 ||
satyaṃ caivāṣṭamaṃ puṣpamebhistuṣyanti devatāḥ  |
bhaktyā tapasvinaḥ pūjyā jñāninaśca narādhipa  || 51.36 ||
chatramāvaraṇaṃ dadyādupānadyugalaṃ tathā  |
tena pūjitamātreṇa pūjitāḥ puruṣāstrayaḥ  || 51.37 ||
svargaloke vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
śūlapāṇestu bhaktyā vai jāpyaṃ kurvanti ye narāḥ  || 51.38 ||
pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyairyakṣakardamakuṅkumaiḥ  |
samālabheta deveśaṃ śrīkhaṇḍāgurucandanaiḥ  || 51.39 ||
nānāvidhaiśca ye puṣpairarcāṃ kurvanti śūlinaḥ  |
niśi jāgaraṇaṃ kuryurdīpadānaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 51.40 ||
dhūpanaivedyakaṃ dadyātpaṭhetpaurāṇikīṃ kathām  |
tatra sthāne sthitā bhaktyā japaṃ kurvanti ye narāḥ  || 51.41 ||
śrīsūktaṃ pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ vṛṣākapim  |
vedoktaiścaiva mantraiśca raudrīṃ vā bahurūpiṇīm  || 51.42 ||
brāhmaṇān pūjayedbhaktyā pūjayitvā praṇamya ca  |
nānāvidhairmahābhogaiḥ śivaloke mahīyate  || 51.43 ||
agnimityādi jāpyāni ṛgvedī japate tu yaḥ  |
rudrān puruṣasūktaṃ ca ślokādhyāyaṃ ca śukriyam  || 51.44 ||
iṣetvā dikamantraughaṃ jyotirbrāhmaṇameva ca  |
gāyatryaṃ vai madhu caiva maṇḍalabrāhmaṇāni ca  || 51.45 ||
etāñjapyāṃstu yo bhaktyā yajurvedī japedyadi  |
devavrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ puruṣarṣabhameva ca  || 51.46 ||
bṛhadrathāntaraṃ caiva yo japedbhaktitatparaḥ  |
sa prayāti naraḥ sthānaṃ yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 51.47 ||
pādaśaucaṃ tathābhyaṅgaṃ kurute yo'tra bhaktitaḥ  |
godāne caiva yatpuṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 51.48 ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra madhunā pāyasena ca  |
ekasmin bhojite vipre koṭirbhavati bhojitā  || 51.49 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ dadyādbhaktyā dvijottame  |
tarpitāstena devāḥ syurmanuṣyāḥ pitarastathā  || 51.50 ||
candrasūryagrahe bhaktyā snānaṃ kurvanti ye narāḥ  |
devārcanaṃ ye ca kuryurjapaṃ homaṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
dadyāddānaṃ yathāśakti brāhmaṇe vedapārage  || 51.51 ||
aśvaṃ rathaṃ gajaṃ yānaṃ tulāpuruṣameva ca  |
śakaṭaṃ yaḥ pradadyādvā saptadhānyaprapūritam  || 51.52 ||
sayoktraṃ lāṅgalaṃ dadyādyuvānau tu dhuraṃdharau  |
gobhūtilahiraṇyādi pātre dātavyamarcitam  || 51.53 ||
apātre viduṣā kiṃcinna deyaṃ bhūtimicchatā  |
yato'sau sarvabhūtāni dadhāti dharaṇī kila  || 51.54 ||
tato viprāya sā deyā sarvasasyaughamālinī  |
athānyacchṛṇu rājendra godānasya tu yatphalam  || 51.55 ||
yāvadvatsasya pādau dvau mukhaṃ yonyāṃ pradṛśyate  |
tāvadgauḥ pṛthivī jñeyā yāvadgarbhaṃ na muñcati  || 51.56 ||
yena kenāpyupāyena brāhmaṇe tāṃ samarpayet |
pṛthvī dattā bhavettena saśailavanakānanā  || 51.57 ||
tārayenniyataṃ dattā kulānāmekaviṃśatim  |
raupyakhurīṃ kāṃsyadohāṃ savastrāṃ ca payasvinīm  || 51.58 ||
ye prayacchanti kṛtino graste sūrye niśākare  |
teṣāṃ saṃkhyāṃ na jānāmi puṇyasyābdaśātairapi  || 51.59 ||
sarvasyāpi hi dānasya saṃkhyāstīha narādhipa  |
candrasūryoparāge ca dānasaṃkhyā na vidyate  || 51.60 ||
yatra gaurdṛśyate rājan sarvatīrthāni tatra hi  |
tatra parva vijānīyānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 51.61 ||
punaḥ smṛtvā tu tattīrthaṃ yaḥ kuryādgamanaṃ naraḥ  |
athavā mriyate yo'tra rudrasyānucaro bhavet || 51.62 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhede dānadharmapraśaṃsāvarṇanaṃ nāmaikapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 52

īśvara uvāca -
anyadākhyānakaṃ vakṣye purā vṛttaṃ narādhipa  |
sakuṭumbo gataḥ svargaṃ muniryatra mahātapāḥ  || 52.1 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
kathaṃ nākaṃ gato vipraḥ sakuṭumbo mahānṛṣiḥ  |
kautukaṃ paramaṃ deva kathayasva mama prabho  || 52.2 ||

īśvara uvāca -
citrasena iti khyātaḥ kāśīrājaḥ purābhavat |
śūro dātā sudharmātmā sarvakāmasamṛddhimān  || 52.3 ||
sā purī janasaṃkīrṇā nānāratnopaśobhitā  |
vārāṇasīti vikhyātā gaṅgātīramupāśritā  || 52.4 ||
śaraccandrapratīkāśā vidvajjanavibhūṣitā  |
indrayaṣṭisamākīrṇā gopagokulasaṃvṛtā  || 52.5 ||
bahudhvajasamākīrṇā vedadhvaninināditā  |
vaṇigjanairbahuvidhaiḥ krayavikrayaśālinī  || 52.6 ||
yantrādānaiḥ pratolībhiruccaiścānyaiḥ suśobhitā  |
devatāyatanairdivyairāśramairgahanairyutā  || 52.7 ||
nānāpuṣpaphalairramyā kadalīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitā  |
panasairbakulaistālairaśokairāmrakaistathā  || 52.8 ||
rājavṛkṣakapitthaiśca dāḍimairupaśobhitā  |
vedādhyayananirghoṣaiḥ pavitrīkṛtamaṅgalā  || 52.9 ||
tasyā uttaradigbhāge āśramo'bhūtsuśobhanaḥ  |
tanmandāravanaṃ nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 52.10 ||
bahumandārasaṃyuktaṃ tena mandārakaṃ viduḥ  |
vipro dīrghatapā nāma sarvadā tatra tiṣṭhati  || 52.11 ||
tapastapati so'tyarthaṃ tena dīrghatapāḥ smṛtaḥ  |
sa tiṣṭhati sapatnīkaḥ sasutaḥ sasnuṣastathā  || 52.12 ||
śuśrūṣanti sadā tasya putrāḥ pañca prayatnataḥ  |
tasya putraḥ kanīyāṃstu ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 52.13 ||
vedādhyayanasampanno brahmacārī guṇānvitaḥ  |
yogābhyāsarato nityaṃ kandamūlaphalāśanaḥ  || 52.14 ||
tiṣṭhate mṛgarūpeṇa mṛgayūthacarastadā  |
dinānte ca dinānte ca mātāpitroḥ samīpagaḥ  || 52.15 ||
abhivādayate nityaṃ bhaktimānmuniputrakaḥ  |
punargacchati tatraiva kānane girigahvare  || 52.16 ||
krīḍanbālamṛgaiḥ sārddhaṃ pratyahaṃ sa muneḥ sutaḥ  |
kadāciddaivayogena ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mamāra saḥ  || 52.17 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamāhātmye ṛkṣaśṛṅgacaritre dīrghatapomunyākhyānavarṇanaṃ nāma dvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 53

uttānapāda uvāca -
āśrame vasatastasya sa dīrghatapaso muneḥ  |
kanīyāṃstanayo deva kathaṃ mṛtyumupāgataḥ  || 53.1 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā kathāṃ divyāṃ mahīpate  |
śravaṇādeva yasyāstu mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 53.2 ||
kāśīrājo mahāvīryo mahābalaparākramaḥ  |
citrasena iti khyātāṃ dharaṇyāṃ sa narādhipa  || 53.3 ||
tasya rājye sadā dharmo nādharmo vidyate kvacit |
vedadharmarato nityaṃ prajā dharmeṇa pālayan  || 53.4 ||
svadharmanirataścaiva yuddhātithyapriyaḥ sadā  |
kṣatradharmaṃ samāśritya bhogānbhuṅkte sa kāmataḥ  || 53.5 ||
kośasyānto na vidyeta hastyaśvarathapattimān  |
itihāsapurāṇajñaiḥ paṇḍitaiḥ saha saṃkathām  || 53.6 ||
kathayanrājate rājā kailāsa iva śaṅkaraḥ  |
evaṃ sa pālayanrājyaṃ rājā mantriṇamabravīt || 53.7 ||
mṛgayāyāṃ gamiṣyāmi tiṣṭhadhvaṃ rājyapālane  |
gamyatāṃ sacivaiḥ prokte gato'sau vasudhādhipaḥ  || 53.8 ||
aśvārūḍhāśca dhāvanto rājāno maṇḍalādhipāḥ  |
chatraiśchatrāṇi ghṛṣyanto'nujagmuḥ kānanaṃ prati  || 53.9 ||
rajastatrotthitaṃ bhaumaṃ gajavājipadāhatam  |
tenaitacchāditaṃ sarvaṃ sadiṅmārtaṇḍamaṃlam  || 53.10 ||
na tatra dṛśyate sūryo na kāṣṭhā na ca candramāḥ  |
pādapāśca na dṛśyante giriśṛṅgāṇi sarvataḥ  || 53.11 ||
parasparaṃ na paśyanti niśārddhe vārṣike yathā  |
tatrāsau sumahadyūthaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ samalakṣyata  || 53.12 ||
adhāvatsahitaḥ sarvaiḥ sa rājā rājaputrakaiḥ  |
vṛndāsphoṭo'bhavatteṣāṃ śīghraṃ jagmurdiśo daśa  || 53.13 ||
ekamārgagato rājā citraseno mahīpatiḥ  |
ekākī sa gatastatra yatra yatra ca te mṛgāḥ  || 53.14 ||
praviṣṭo'sau tato durgaṃ kānanaṃ girigahvaram  |
vallīgulmasamākīrṇaṃ sthito yatra na lakṣyate  || 53.15 ||
adṛśyāṃstu mṛgānmatvā diśo rājā vyalokayat |
kāṃ diśaṃ nu gamiṣyāmi kva me sainyasamāgamaḥ  || 53.16 ||
evaṃ kaṣṭaṃ gato rājā citraseno narādhipaḥ  |
vṛkṣacchāyāṃ samāśritya viśrāmamakaronnṛpaḥ  || 53.17 ||
kṣuttṛṣārto bhramandurge kānane girigahvare  |
tato'paśyatsaro divyaṃ padminīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitam  || 53.18 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇaṃ cakravākopaśobhitam  |
tato dṛṣṭvā sa rājendraḥ samprahṛṣṭatanūruhaḥ  || 53.19 ||
kamalāni gṛhītvā tu tataḥ snānaṃ samācarat |
tarpayitvā pitṛdevānmanuṣyāṃśca yathāvidhi  || 53.20 ||
ācchādya śatapatraiśca pūjayāmāsa śaṅkaram  |
yayau pānīyamamalaṃ yathāvatsa samāhitaḥ  || 53.21 ||
uttīrya salilāttīre dṛṣṭvā vṛkṣaṃ samīpagam  |
uttarīyamadhaḥ kṛtvopaviṣṭo dharaṇītale  || 53.22 ||
cintayannupaviṣṭo'sau kimadya prakaromyaham  |
tatrāsīno dadarśātha vanoddeśe mṛgānbahūn  || 53.23 ||
kecitpūrvamukhāstatra cāpare dakṣiṇāmukhāḥ  |
vāruṇyamimukhāḥ kecitkecitkauberadiṅmukhāḥ  || 53.24 ||
kecinnidrāparāḥ kecidūrdhvakarṇāḥ sthitāḥ pare  |
mṛgamadhye sthito yogī ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 53.25 ||
mṛgāndṛṣṭvā tato rājā āhārārthamacintayat |
hatvaiteṣu mṛgaṃ kaṃcidbhakṣayāmi yadṛcchayā  || 53.26 ||
svasthāvastho bhaviṣyāmi mṛgamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt |
kāśīṃ prati gamiṣyāmi mārgamanviṣya yatnataḥ  || 53.27 ||
vicintyaivaṃ tato rājā vṛkṣamūlamupāśritaḥ  |
cāpaṃ gṛhya karāgreṇa sa śaraṃ saṃdadhe tataḥ  || 53.28 ||
vicikṣepa śaraṃ tatra yatra te bahavo mṛgāḥ  |
teṣāṃ madhye sa vai viddha ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 53.29 ||
jagmustrastāstu te sarve śabdaṃ kṛtvā vanaukasaḥ  |
sa ṛṣiḥ patitastatra kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti cābravīt || 53.30 ||
hāhā kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ tena yenāhaṃ ghātito'dhunā  |
kasyaiṣā durmatirjātā pāpabuddhermamopari  || 53.31 ||
mṛgamadhye sthitaścāhaṃ na kaṃciduparodhaye  |
tāṃ vācaṃ mānuṣīṃ śrutvā sa rājā vismayānvitaḥ  || 53.32 ||
śīghraṃ gatvā tato'paśyadbrāhmaṇaṃ brahmatejasā  |
hāhā kaṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ me'dya yenāsau ghātito dvijaḥ  || 53.33 ||

citrasena uvāca -
akāmādghātitastvaṃ tu mṛgabhrāntyā mayānagha  |
gṛhītvā bahudārūṇi svatanuṃ dāhayāmyaham  || 53.34 ||
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaṃ tu yatkiṃcinna samaṃ brahmahatyayā  |
anyathā brahmahatyāyāḥ śuddhirme na bhaviṣyati  || 53.35 ||

ṛkṣaśṛṅga uvāca -
na te siddhirbhavetkācinmayi pañcatvamāgate  |
bahvyo hatyā bhaviṣyanti vināśe mama sāmpratam  || 53.36 ||
jananī me pitā vṛddho bhrātaraśca tapasvinaḥ  |
bhrātṛjāyā mariṣyanti mayi pañcatvamāgate  || 53.37 ||
etā hatyā bhaviṣyanti kathaṃ śuddhirbhavettava  |
upāyaṃ kathayiṣyāmi taṃ kartuṃ yadi manyase  || 53.38 ||

citrasena uvāca -
upāyaḥ kathyatāṃ me'dya yaste manasi vartate  |
kariṣye tamahaṃ sarvaṃ yatnenāpi mahāmune  || 53.39 ||

ṛkṣaśṛṅga uvāca -
pṛcchāmi tvāṃ kathaṃ ko vā kutastvamiha cāgataḥ  |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ madhye ko bhavānuta śūdrajaḥ  || 53.40 ||

citrasena uvāca -
nāhaṃ śūdro'smi bhostāta na vaiśyo brāhmaṇo na vā  |
na cāntyajo'smi viprendra kṣatriyo'smi mahāmune  || 53.41 ||
dharmajñaśca kṛtajñaśca sarvasattvahite rataḥ  |
akāmātpātakaṃ jātaṃ kathaṃ śuddhirbhaviṣyati  || 53.42 ||

ṛkṣaśṛṅga uvāca -
māṃ gṛhītvā āśramaṃ gaccha yatra tau pitarau mama  |
āvedayasva cātmānaṃ putraghātinamāturam  || 53.43 ||
te dṛṣṭvā māṃ kariṣyanti kāruṇyaṃ ca tavopari  |
upāyaṃ kathayiṣyanti yena śāntirbhaviṣyati  || 53.44 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā citraseno nṛpottama  |
skandhe kṛtvā tu taṃ vipraṃ jagāmāśramasannidhau  || 53.45 ||
na śaknoti yadā voḍhuṃ viśrāmyati punaḥpunaḥ  |
tāvatpaśyati taṃ vipraṃ mūrchitaṃ vikalendriyam  || 53.46 ||
mumoca citrasenastaṃ chāyāyāṃ vaṭabhūruhaḥ  |
vastraṃ caturguṇaṃ kṛtvā cakre vātaṃ muhurmuhuḥ  || 53.47 ||
paśyatastasya rājendra ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  |
pañcatvamagamacchīghraṃ dhyānayogena yogavit || 53.48 ||
dāhayāmāsa taṃ vipraṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  |
snānaṃ kṛtvā sa śokārto vilalāpa muhurmuhuḥ  || 53.49 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamāhātmye ṛkṣaśṛṅgasvargagamanavarṇanaṃ nāma tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 54

īśvara uvāca -
tataścānantaraṃ rājā jagāmodvegamuttamam  |
kathaṃ yāmi gṛhaṃ tvadya vārāṇasyāmahaṃ punaḥ  || 54.1 ||
brahmahatyāsamāviṣṭo juhomyagnau kalevaram  |
athavā tasya vākyena taṃ gacchāmyāśramaṃ prati  || 54.2 ||
kathayāmi yathāvṛttaṃ gatvā tasya mahāmuneḥ  |
evaṃ saṃcintya rājāsau jagāmāśramasannidhau  || 54.3 ||
ṛkṣaśṛṅgasya cāsthīni gṛhītvā sa nṛpottamaḥ  |
dṛṣṭimārge sthitastasya maharṣerbhāvitātmanaḥ  || 54.4 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca -
āgaccha svāgataṃ te'stu āsane'tropaviśyatām  |
arghaṃ dadāmyahaṃ yena madhuparkaṃ saviṣṭaram  || 54.5 ||

citrasena uvāca -
arghasyāsya na yogyo'haṃ maharṣe nāsmi bhāṣaṇe  |
mṛgamadhyasthito viprastava putro mayā hataḥ  || 54.6 ||
putraghnaṃ viddhi māṃ vipra tīvradaṇḍena daṇḍaya  |
mṛgabhrāntyā hato vipra ṛkṣaśṛṅgo mahātapāḥ  || 54.7 ||
iti matvā muniśreṣṭha kuru me tvaṃ yathocitam  |
mātā tadvacanaṃ śrutvā gṛhānniṣkramya vihvalā  || 54.8 ||
hā hatāsmītyuvācedaṃ papāta dharaṇītale  |
vilalāpa suduḥkhārtā putraśokena pīḍitā  || 54.9 ||
hā hatā putra putreti karuṇaṃ kurarī yathā  |
vilalāpāturā mātā kva gato māṃ vihāya vai  |
mukhaṃ darśaya cātmīyaṃ mātaraṃ māṃ hi mānaya  || 54.10 ||
śrutādhyayanasampannaṃ japahomaparāyaṇam  |
āgataṃ tvāṃ gṛhadvāre kadā drakṣyāmi putraka  || 54.11 ||
lokoktyā śrūyate caitaccandanaṃ kila śītalam  |
putragātrapariṣvaṅgaścandanādapi śītalaḥ  || 54.12 ||
kiṃ candanena pīyūpabindunā kiṃ kimindunā  || 54.13 ||
putragātrapariṣvaṅgapātraṃ gātraṃ bhavedyadi  || 54.14 ||
pariṣvajitumicchāmi tvāmahaṃ putra supriya  |
pañcatvamanuyāsyāmi tvadvihīnādya duḥkhitā  || 54.15 ||
evaṃ vilapatī dīnā putraśokena pīḍitā  |
mūrchitā vihvalā dīnā nipapāta mahītale  || 54.16 ||
bhāryāṃ ca patitāṃ dṛṣṭvā putraśokena pīḍitām  |
cukopa sa munistatra citrasenāya bhūbhṛte  || 54.17 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca -
yāhi yāhi mahāpāpa mā mukhaṃ darśayasva me  |
kiṃ tvayā ghātito vipro hyakāmācca suto mama  || 54.18 ||
brahmahatyā bhaviṣyanti bahvyaste vasudhādhipa  |
sakuṭumbasya me tvaṃ hi mṛtyureṣa upasthitaḥ  || 54.19 ||
evamuktvā tato vipro vicintya ca punaḥpunaḥ  |
parityajya tadā krodhaṃ munibhāvājjagāda ha  || 54.20 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca -
udvegaṃ tyaja bho vatsa duruktaṃ gadito mayā  |
putraśokābhibhūtena duḥkhataptena mānada  || 54.21 ||
kiṃ karoti naraḥ prājñaḥ preryamāṇaḥ svakarmabhiḥ  |
prāgeva hi manuṣyāṇāṃ buddhiḥ karmānusāriṇī  || 54.22 ||
anenaiva vidhānena pañcatvaṃ vihitaṃ mama  |
hatyāstava bhaviṣyanti pūrvamuktā na saṃśayaḥ  || 54.23 ||
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ madhye śūdracaṇḍālajātiṣu  |
kastvaṃ kathaya satyaṃ me kasmācca nihato dvijaḥ  || 54.24 ||

citrasena uvāca -
vijñāpayāmi viprarṣe kṣantavyaṃ te mamopari  |
nāhaṃ vipro'smi vai tāta na vaiśyo na ca śūdrajaḥ  || 54.25 ||
na vyādhaścāntyajāto vā kṣatriyo'haṃ mahāmune  |
kāśīrājo mṛgān hantumāgato vanamuttamam  || 54.26 ||
bhrāntyā nipātito hyeṣa mṛgarūpadharo muniḥ  |
idānīṃ tava pādānte saṃśritaḥ pātakānvitaḥ  || 54.27 ||
kiṃ kartavyaṃ mayā vipra upāyaṃ kathayasva me  || 54.28 ||

dīrghatapā uvāca -
brahmahatyā na śakyetāpyekā nistarituṃ prabho  |
daśaikā ca kathaṃ śakyāstāḥ śṛṇuṣva nareśvara  || 54.29 ||
catvāro me sutā rājan sabhāryā mātṛpūrvakāḥ  |
mayā saha na jīvanti ṛkṣaśṛṅgasya kāraṇe  || 54.30 ||
upāyaṃ śobhanaṃ tāta kathayiṣye śṛṇuṣva tam  |
śakro'pi yadi taṃ kartuṃ sukhopāyaṃ nareśvara  || 54.31 ||
sakuṭumbaṃ samastaṃ māṃ dāhayitvānale nṛpa  |
asthīni narmadātoye śūlabhede vinikṣipa  || 54.32 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle śūlabhedaṃ hi viśrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  || 54.33 ||
śucirbhūtvā mamāsthīni tatra tīrthe vinikṣipa  |
mokṣyase sarvapāpaistvaṃ mama vākyānna saṃśayaḥ  || 54.34 ||

rājovāca -
ādeśo dīyatāṃ tāta kariṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ  |
samastaṃ me'sti yatkiṃcidrājyaṃ kośaḥ suhṛtsutāḥ  || 54.35 ||
tavādhīnaṃ mahāvipra prayacchāmi prasīda me  |
parasparaṃ vivadatorvipra rājñostadā nṛpa  || 54.36 ||
sphuṭitvā hṛdayaṃ śīghraṃ munibhāryā mṛtā tadā  |
putraśokasamāviṣṭā nirjīvā patitā kṣitau  || 54.37 ||
putrāśca mātṛśokena sarve pañcatvamāgatāḥ  |
snuṣāścaiva tadā sarvā mṛtāśca saha bhartṛbhiḥ  || 54.38 ||
pañcatvaṃ ca gatāḥ sarve munimukhyā nṛpottama  |
viprānāhvāpayāmāsa ye tatrāśramavāsinaḥ  || 54.39 ||
tebhyo nivedayāmāsa yathāvṛttaṃ nṛpottamaḥ  |
sa taistadābhyanujñātaḥ kāṣṭhānyādāya yatnataḥ  || 54.40 ||
dāhaṃ saṃcayanaṃ cakre citraseno mahīpatiḥ  |
ṛkṣaśṛṅgādisarveṣāṃ gṛhītvāsthīni yatnataḥ  || 54.41 ||
yāmyāśāṃ prasthito rājā pādacārī mahīpate  |
na śaknoti yadā gantuṃ chāyāmāśritya tiṣṭhati  || 54.42 ||
viśramya ca punargacchedbhārākrānto mahīpatiḥ  |
sacailaṃ kurute snānaṃ muktvāsthīni pade pade  || 54.43 ||
pibejjalaṃ nirāhāraḥ sa gacchan dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
acireṇaiva kālena saṃgato narmadātaṭam  || 54.44 ||
āśramasthān dvijān dṛṣṭvā papraccha pṛthivīpatiḥ  || 54.45 ||

citrasena uvāca -
kathyatāṃ śūlabhedasya mārgaṃ me dvijasattamāḥ  |
yena yāmi mahābhāgāḥ svakāryārthasya siddhaye  || 54.46 ||
munaya ūcuḥ  |
itaḥ krośāntarādarvāktīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tato drakṣyasi nānyathā  || 54.47 ||
ṛṣivākyena rājāsau śīghraṃ gatvā nareśvaraḥ  |
sa dadarśa tataḥ śīghraṃ bahudvijasamākulam  || 54.48 ||
bahudrumalatākīrṇaṃ bahupuṣpopaśobhitam  |
ṛkṣasiṃhasamākīrṇaṃ nānāvratadharaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 54.49 ||
ekapādāsthitāḥ kecidapare sūryadṛṣṭayaḥ  |
ekāṅguṣṭha sthitāḥ kecidūrdhvabāhusthitāḥ pare  || 54.50 ||
dinaikabhojanāḥ kecitkecitkandaphalāśanāḥ  |
trirātrabhojanāḥ kecitparākavratino'pare  || 54.51 ||
cāndrāyaṇaratāḥ kecitkecitpakṣopavāsinaḥ  |
māsopavāsinaḥ kecitkecidṛtvantapāraṇāḥ  || 54.52 ||
yogābhyāsaratāḥ kecitkeciddhyāyanti tatpadam  |
śīrṇaparṇāśinaḥ kecitkecicca kaṭukāśanāḥ  || 54.53 ||
śaivālabhojanāḥ kecitkecinmārutabhojanāḥ  |
gārhasthye ca sthitāḥ kecitkeciccaivāgnihotriṇaḥ  || 54.54 ||
evaṃvidhān dvijān dṛṣṭvā jānubhyāmavaniṃ gataḥ  |
praṇamya śirasā rājanrājā vacanamabravīt || 54.55 ||

citrasena uvāca -
kasmindeśe ca tattīrthaṃ satyaṃ kathayata dvijāḥ  |
yenābhivāñchitā siddhiḥ saphalā me bhaviṣyati  || 54.56 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
dhanvantaraśataṃ gaccha bhṛgutuṅgasya mūrdhani  |
kuṇḍaṃ drakṣyasi tatpūrṇaṃ vistīrṇaṃ payasā śivam  || 54.57 ||
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā gataḥ kuṇḍasya sannidhau  |
dṛṣṭvā caiva tu tattīrthaṃ bhrāntirjātā nṛpasya vai  || 54.58 ||
tato vismayamāpannaścintayanvai muhurmuhuḥ  |
ākāśasthaṃ dadarśāsau sāmiṣaṃ kuraraṃ nṛpaḥ  || 54.59 ||
bhramamāṇaṃ gṛhītāhiṃ vadhyamānaṃ nirāmiṣaiḥ  |
parasparaṃ ca yuyudhuḥ sarve'pyāmiṣakāṅkṣayā  || 54.60 ||
hataścañcuprahāreṇa sa tataḥ patito'ṃbhasi  |
śūlena śūlinā yatra bhūbhāgo bheditaḥ purā  || 54.61 ||
tattīrthasya prabhāveṇa sa sadyaḥ puruṣo'bhavat |
vimānasthaṃ dadarśāsau pumāṃsaṃ divyarūpiṇam  || 54.62 ||
gandharvāpsaraso yakṣāstaṃ yāntaṃ tuṣṭuvurdivi  |
apsarogīyamāne tu gate sūryasya mūrdhani  |
citrasenastatastasminnāścaryaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  || 54.63 ||
ṛṣiṇā kathitaṃ yadvattadvattīrthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
hṛṣṭaromābhavaddṛṣṭvā prabhāvaṃ tīrthasambhavam  || 54.64 ||
mamādya divaso dhanyo yasmādatra samāgataḥ  |
asthīni bhūmau nikṣipya snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 54.65 ||
tilamiśreṇa toyenātarpayatpitṛdevatāḥ  |
gṛhyāsthīni tato rājā cikṣepāntarjale tadā  || 54.66 ||
kṣaṇamekaṃ tato vīkṣya rājorddhvavadanaḥ sthitaḥ  |
tān dadarśa punaḥ sarvān divyarūpadharāñchubhān  || 54.67 ||
divyavastraiśca saṃvītān divyābharaṇabhūṣitān  |
vimānairvividhairdivyairapsarogaṇasevitaiḥ  || 54.68 ||
pṛthagbhūtāṃśca tān sarvān vimāneṣu vyavasthitān  |
utpattivatsamālokya rājā saṃharṣī so'bhavat || 54.69 ||
ṛṣirvimānamārūḍhaścitrasenamathābravīt |
bhobhoḥ sādho mahārāja citrasena mahīpate  || 54.70 ||
tvatprasādānnṛpaśreṣṭha gatirdivyā mamedṛṣī  |
jāteyaṃ yattvayā kāryaṃ kṛtaṃ paramaśobhanam  || 54.71 ||
svasuto'pi na śaknoti pitḥṇāṃ kartumīdṛśam  |
madīyavacanāttāta niṣpāpastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 54.72 ||
phalaṃ prāpsyasi rājendra kāmikaṃ manasepsitam  |
āśīrvādāṃstato dattvā citrasenāya dhīmate  |
svargaṃ jagāma sasutastato dīrghatapā muniḥ  || 54.73 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcamāvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dīrghatapasaḥ svargārohaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 55

uttānapāda uvāca -
māhātmyaṃ tīrthajaṃ dṛṣṭvā citraseno nareśvaraḥ  |
kiṃ cakāra kva vā vāsaṃ kimāhāro babhūva ha  || 55.1 ||

īśvara uvāca -
bhṛgutuṅgaṃ samāruhya aiśānīṃ diśamāśritaḥ  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ kuṇḍe tatra nṛpottamaḥ  || 55.2 ||
sarvān devān hṛdi dhyātvā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān  |
vicikṣepa yadātmānaṃ pratyakṣau rudrakeśavau  |
kare gṛhītvā rājānaṃ rudro vacanamabravīt || 55.3 ||

īśvara uvāca -
prāṇatyāgaṃ mahārāja mā kāle tvaṃ kṛthā vṛthā  |
adyāpyasi yuvā tvaṃ vai na yuktaṃ maraṇaṃ tava  || 55.4 ||
svasthānaṃ gaccha śīghraṃ tvaṃ bhuktvā bhogānyathepsitān  |
kuru niṣkaṇṭakaṃ rājyaṃ nāke śakra ivāparaḥ  || 55.5 ||

citrasena uvāca -
na rājyaṃ kāmaye deva na putrānna ca bāndhavān  |
na bhāryāṃ na ca kośaṃ ca na gajānna turaṃgamān  || 55.6 ||
muñca muñca mahādeva mā vighnaḥ kriyatāṃ mama  |
svargaprāptirmamādyaiva tvatprasādānmaheśvara  || 55.7 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yasyāgrato bhavedbrahmā viṣṇuḥ śambhustathaiva ca  |
svargeṇa tasya kiṃ kāryaṃ sa gataḥ kiṃ kariṣyati  || 55.8 ||
tuṣṭā vayaṃ trayo devā vṛṇīṣva varamuttamam  |
yathepsitaṃ mahārāja satyametadasaṃśayam  || 55.9 ||

citrasena uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭāstrayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
adyaprabhṛti yuṣmābhiḥ sthātavyamiha sarvadā  || 55.10 ||
gayāśiro yathā puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ yuṣmābhireva ca  |
tathaivedaṃ prakartavyaṃ śūlabhedaṃ ca pāvanam  || 55.11 ||
yatrayatra sthitā yūyaṃ tatratatra vasāmyaham  |
gaṇānāṃ caiva sarveṣāmādhipatyamathāstu me  || 55.12 ||

īśvara uvāca -
adyaprabhṛti tiṣṭhāmaḥ śūlabhede nareśvara  |
trikālāṃ hi trayo devāḥ kalāṃśena vasāmahe  || 55.13 ||
nandisaṃjño gaṇādhīśo bhaviṣyati bhavāndhruvam  |
matsamīpe tu bhavata ādau pūjā bhaviṣyati  || 55.14 ||
prakṣipya tāni cāsthīni yatra dīrghatapā yayau  |
sakuṭumbo vimānasthaḥ svargatastvaṃ tathā kuru  || 55.15 ||
evaṃ devā varaṃ dattvā citrasenāya pārthiva  |
kuṇḍamūrdhani yāmyāyāṃ trayo devāstadā sthitāḥ  || 55.16 ||
parasparaṃ vadantyevaṃ puṇyatīrthamidaṃ param  |
yathā hi gayāśiraḥ puṇyaṃ pūrvameva paṭhyate  |
tathā revātaṭe puṇyaṃ śūlabhedaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 55.17 ||

īśvara uvāca -
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ yathā puṇyaṃ gayāśiraḥ  |
sakṛtpiṇḍodakenaiva naro nirmalatāṃ vrajet || 55.18 ||
ekaṃ gayāśiro muktvā sarvatīrthāni bhūpate  |
śūlabhedasya tīrthasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 55.19 ||
kuṇḍamudīcyāṃ yāmyāyāṃ daśahastapramāṇataḥ  |
raudravāruṇakāṣṭhāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ caikaviṃśati  || 55.20 ||
etatpramāṇaṃ tattīrthaṃ piṇḍadānādikarmasu  |
nādharmaniratā dātuṃ labhante dānamatra hi  || 55.21 ||
viṣṇustu pitṛrūpeṇa brahmarūpī pitāmahaḥ  |
prapitāmaho rudro'bhūdevaṃ tripuruṣāḥ sthitāḥ  || 55.22 ||
kadā paśyati tīrthaṃ vai kadā nastārayiṣyati  |
iti pratīkṣāṃ kurvanti putrāṇāṃ satataṃ nṛpa  |
śūlabhede naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā śūladharaṃ sakṛt || 55.23 ||
nāputro nādhano rogī saptajanmasu jāyate  |
ekaviṃśatiṃ pituḥ pakṣe mātuśvaivekaviṃśatim  || 55.24 ||
bhāryāpakṣe daśaiveha kulānyetāni tārayet |
śūlabhedavane rājañchākamūlaphalairapi  || 55.25 ||
ekasminbhojite vipre koṭīrbhavati bhojitā  |
pañcasthāneṣu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute bhaktimānnaraḥ  || 55.26 ||
kulāni pretabhūtāni sarvāṇyapi hi tārayet |
dvijadevaprasādena pitḥṇāṃ ca prasādataḥ  || 55.27 ||
śrāddhado nivasettatra yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
syurātmaghātino ye ca gobrāhmaṇahanāśca ye  || 55.28 ||
daṃṣṭribhirjalapāte ca vidyutpāteṣu ye mṛtāḥ  |
na yeṣāmagnisaṃskāro nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā  || 55.29 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yasteṣāṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta bhaktitaḥ  |
mokṣāvāptirbhavetteṣāṃ yugamekaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 55.30 ||
ajñānādyatkṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bālabhāvācca yatkṛtam  |
tatsarvaṃ nāśayetpāpaṃ snānamātreṇa bhūpate  || 55.31 ||
rajakena yathā dhautaṃ vastraṃ bhavati nirmalam  |
tathā pāpo'pi tattīrthe snāto bhavati nirmalaḥ  || 55.32 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yo'tra tīrthe vidhisamanvitam  |
dhyāyannityaṃ mahādevaṃ sa gacchetparamaṃ padam  || 55.33 ||
krīḍitvā sa yathākāmaṃ svecchayā śivamandire  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajño jāyate'sau śubhe kule  || 55.34 ||
rūpavānsubhagaścaiva sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
rājā vā rājaputro vācārasamanvitaḥ  || 55.35 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājaṃstīrthasya phalamuttamam  |
yacchrutvā mānavo nityaṃ mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 55.36 ||
ya idaṃ śrāvayennityamākhyānaṃ dvijapuṃgavān  |
śrāddhe devakule vāpi paṭhetparvṇi parvṇi  || 55.37 ||
gīrvāṇāstasya tuṣyanti manuṣyāḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva naśyate sarvapātakam  || 55.38 ||
likhitvā tīrthamāhātmyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadāti yaḥ  |
jātismaratvaṃ labhate prāpnotyabhimataṃ phalam  || 55.39 ||
rudraloke vasettāvadyāvadakṣaramanvitam  || 55.40 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedamāhātmye kāśīrājamokṣagamanaṃ nāma pañcapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 56

uttānapāda uvāca -
anyacca śrotumicchāmi kena gaṅgāvatāritā  |
rudraśīrṣe sthitā devī puṇyā kathamihāgatā  || 56.1 ||
puṇyā devāśilā nāma tasyā māhātmyamuttamam  |
etadākhyāhi me sarvaṃ prasanno yadi śaṅkara  || 56.2 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā yathā gaṅgāvatāritā  |
devaiḥ sarvairmahābhāgā sarvalokahitāya vai  || 56.3 ||
asti vindhyo nago nāma yāmyāśāyāṃ mahīpate  |
gīrvāṇāstu gatāḥ sarve tasya mūrdhni nareśvara  || 56.4 ||
tatra cāhvānitā gaṅgā brahmādyairakhilaiḥ suraiḥ  |
abhyarcyeśaṃ jagannāthaṃ devadevaṃ jagadgurum  || 56.5 ||
jaṭāmadhyasthitāṃ gaṅgāṃ mocayasveti bhūtale  |
bhāsvantī sā tato muktā rudreṇa śirasā bhuvi  || 56.6 ||
tatra sthāne mahāpuṇyā devairutpāditā svayam  |
tato devanadī jātā sā hitāya nṛṇāṃ bhuvi  || 56.7 ||
vasanti ye taṭe tasyāḥ snānaṃ kurvanti bhaktitaḥ  |
pibanti ca jalaṃ nityaṃ na te yānti yamālayam  || 56.8 ||
yatra sā patitā kuṇḍe śūlabhede narādhipa  |
devanadyāḥ pratīcyāṃ tu tatra prācī sarasvatī  || 56.9 ||
yāmyāyāṃ śūlabhedasya tatra tīrthamanuttamam  |
tatra devaśilā puṇyā svayaṃ devena nirmitā  || 56.10 ||
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 56.11 ||
tatra snātvā tu yo bhaktyā brāhmaṇān bhojayennṛpa  |
svalpānnenāpi dattena tasya cānto na vidyate  || 56.12 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
kāni dānāni dattāni śastāni dharaṇītale  |
yāni dattvā naro bhaktyā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 56.13 ||
devaśilāyā māhātmyaṃ snānadānādijaṃ phalam  |
vratopavāsaniyamairyatprāpyaṃ tadvadasva me  || 56.14 ||

īśvara uvāca -
āsītpurā mahāvīryaścedinātho mahābalaḥ  |
vīrasena iti khyāto maṇḍalādhipatirnṛpa  || 56.15 ||
rāṣṭre tasya ripurnāsti na vyādhirna ca taskarāḥ  |
na cādharmo'bhavattatra dharma eva hi sarvadā  || 56.16 ||
sadā mudānvito rājā sabhāryo bahuputrakaḥ  |
ekāsīdduhitā tasya surūpā girijā yathā  || 56.17 ||
iṣṭā sā pitṛmātṛbhyāṃ bandhuvargajanasya ca  |
kṛtaṃ vaivāhikaṃ karma kāle prāpte yathāvidhi  || 56.18 ||
anantaraṃ cedipatirdvādaśābdamakhe sthitaḥ  |
tatastasyāstu yo bhartā sa mṛtyuvaśamāgataḥ  || 56.19 ||
vidhavāṃ tāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā rājā śokasamanvitaḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ tatra svabhāryāṃ duḥkhapīḍitām  || 56.20 ||
priye duḥkhamidaṃ jātaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ suduḥsaham  |
naiṣā rakṣayituṃ śakyā rūpayauvanagarvitā  || 56.21 ||
dūṣayeta kulaṃ kvāpi kathaṃ rakṣyā hi bālikā  |
nopāyo vidyate kvāpi bhānumatyāśca rakṣaṇe  |
parasparaṃ vivadatoḥ śrutvā tatkanyakābravīt || 56.22 ||

bhānumatyuvāca -
na lajjāmi tavāgre'haṃ jalpantī tāta karhicit |
satyaṃ notpadyate doṣo madarthe te narādhipa  || 56.23 ||
adyaprabhṛtyahaṃ tāta dhārayiṣye na mūrdhajān  |
sthūlavastrapaṭārddhaṃ tu dhārayiṣyāmi te gṛhe  || 56.24 ||
kariṣyāmi vratānyāśu purāṇavihitāni ca  |
ātmānaṃ śoṣayiṣyāmi toṣayiṣye janārdanam  || 56.25 ||
mamaiṣā vartate buddhiryadi tvaṃ tāta manyase  |
bhānumatyā vacaḥ śrutvā rājā saṃharṣito'bhavat || 56.26 ||
tīrthayātrāṃ samuddiśya kośaṃ dattvā supuṣkalam  |
visṛjya puruṣānvṛddhān kṛtvā tasyāḥ surakṣaṇe  || 56.27 ||
puruṣān sāyudhāṃścāpi brāhmaṇānsapurohitān  |
dāsīdāsānpadātīṃśca cāsyāḥ saṃrakṣaṇakṣamān  || 56.28 ||
tataḥ piturmatenaiva gaṅgātīraṃ gatā satī  |
avagāhya taṭe dve tu gaṅgāyāḥ sa narādhipa  || 56.29 ||
nityaṃ sampūjya sadviprāngandhamālyādibhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
dvādaśābdāni sā tīre gaṅgāyāḥ samavasthitā  || 56.30 ||
tyaktvā gaṅgāṃ tadā rājñī gatā kāṣṭhāṃ tu dakṣiṇām  |
prāptā sā sacivaiḥ sārddhaṃ yatra revā mahānadī  || 56.31 ||
samāḥ pañca sthitā tatra oṅkāre'marakaṇṭake  |
udagyāmyeṣu tīrtheṣu tīrthāttīrthaṃ jagāma sā  || 56.32 ||
snātvā snātvā pūjya viprān bhaktipūrvamatandritā  |
vāruṇīṃ sā diśaṃ gatvā devanadyāśca saṅgame  || 56.33 ||
dadarśa cāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ munisaṅghaiḥ samākulam  |
dṛṣṭvā munisamūhaṃ sā praṇipatyedamabravīt || 56.34 ||
māhātmyamasya tīrthasya nāma caivāsya kīdṛśam  |
kathayantu mahābhāgāḥ prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mama  || 56.35 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
cakratīrthaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ cakraṃ dattaṃ purā hareḥ  |
maheśvareṇa tuṣṭena devadevena śūlinā  || 56.36 ||
atra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
anivartikā gatistasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 56.37 ||
dvitīye'hni tato gacchecchūlabhede tapasvini  |
pūrvoktena vidhānena snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 56.38 ||
janmatrayakṛtaiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
jalena tilamātreṇa pradadyādañjalitrayam  || 56.39 ||
tṛpyanti pitarastasya dvādaśābdānyasaṃśayam  |
yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute bhaktyā śrotriyairbrāhmaṇairnṛpa  || 56.40 ||
vārddhuṣyādyāstu varjyante pitḥṇāṃ dattamakṣayam  |
apare'hṇi tato gacchetpuṇyāṃ devaśilāṃ śubhām  || 56.41 ||
vīkṣyate jāhnavī puṇyā devairutpāditā purā  |
snātvā tatra jalaṃ dadyāttilamiśraṃ narādhipa  || 56.42 ||
sakṛtpiṇḍapradānena mucyate brahmahatyayā  |
ekādaśyāmupoṣitvā pakṣayorubhayorapi  || 56.43 ||
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ kuryātpaṭhetpaurāṇikīṃ kathām  |
viṣṇupūjāṃ prakurvīta puṣpadhūpanivedanaiḥ  || 56.44 ||
prabhāte bhojayedviprān dānaṃ dadyātsaśaktitaḥ  |
caturthe'hni tato gacchedyatra prācī sarasvatī  || 56.45 ||
brahmadehādviniṣkrāntā pāvanārthaṃ śarīriṇām  |
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 56.46 ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyamanindyān bhojayeddvijān  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti dvādaśābdānyasaṃśayam  || 56.47 ||
sarvadevamayaṃ sthānaṃ sarvatīrthamayaṃ tathā  |
devakoṭisamākīrṇaṃ koṭiliṅgottamottamam  || 56.48 ||
trirātraṃ kurute yo'tra śuciḥ snātvā jitendriyaḥ  |
pakṣaṃ māsaṃ ca ṣaṇmāsamabdamekaṃ kadācana  || 56.49 ||
na tasya sambhavo martye tasya vāso bhaveddivi  |
niyamastho vimucyeta trijanmajanitādaghāt || 56.50 ||
vinā puṃsā tu yā nārī dvādaśābdaṃ śucivratā  |
tiṣṭhate sākṣayaṃ kālaṃ rudraloke mahīyate  || 56.51 ||
munīnāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā mudā paramayā yayau  |
tato'vagāhya tattīrthamaharniśamatandritā  || 56.52 ||
dṛṣṭvā tīrthaprabhāvaṃ tu punarvacanamabravīt |
śrūyatāṃ vacanaṃ me'dya brāhmaṇāḥ sapurohitāḥ  || 56.53 ||
na tyajāmīdṛśaṃ sthānaṃ yāvajjīvamaharniśam  |
matpituśca tathā mātuḥ kathayadhvamidaṃ vacaḥ  || 56.54 ||
tvatkanyā śūlabhedasthā niyatā vratacāriṇī  |
evamuktvā sthitā sā tu tatra bhānumatī nṛpaḥ  || 56.55 ||
ekāntaropavāsasthā śanairmāsopavāsitā  |
devaśilāsthitā nityaṃ dadhyau sā cakrapāṇinam  || 56.56 ||
aharniśaṃ daheddhūpaṃ candanaṃ ca sadīpakam  |
pādaśaucaṃ svayaṃ kṛtvā svayaṃ bhojayate dvijān  |
dvādaśābdāni sā rājñī suvratā tatra saṃsthitā  || 56.57 ||

īśvara uvāca -
anyaddevaśilāyāstu māhātmyaṃ śṛṇu bhūpate  |
kathayāmi mahābāho setihāsaṃ purātanam  || 56.58 ||
kaścidvanecaro vyādhaḥ śabaraḥ saha bhāryayā  |
durbhikṣapīḍitastatra āmiṣārthaṃ vanaṃ gataḥ  || 56.59 ||
nāpaśyatpakṣiṇastatra na mṛgānna phalāni ca  |
sarastato dadarśātha padminīkhaṇḍamaṇḍitam  || 56.60 ||
dṛṣṭvā sarovaraṃ tatra śabarī vākyamabravīt |
kumudāni gṛhāṇa tvaṃ divyānyāhārasiddhaye  || 56.61 ||
devasya pūjanārthaṃ tu śūlabhedasya yatnataḥ  |
vikrayo bhavitā tatra dharmaśīlo jano yataḥ  || 56.62 ||
bhāryāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā jagrāha kumudāni saḥ  |
uttīrṇastu taṭe yāvaddṛṣṭvā śrīvṛkṣamagrataḥ  || 56.63 ||
śrīphalāni gṛhītvā tu supakvāni viśeṣataḥ  |
śūlabhedaṃ sa samprāpto dadarśa subahūñjanān  || 56.64 ||
caitramāse site pakṣe ekādaśyāṃ narādhipa  |
tasminnahani nāśnīyurbālā vṛddhāstathā striyaḥ  || 56.65 ||
maṇḍapaṃ dadṛśe tatra kṛtaṃ devaśilopari  |
vastraiḥ saṃveṣṭitaṃ divyaṃ sraṅmālyairupaśobhitam  || 56.66 ||
ṛṣayaścāgatāstatra ye cāśramanivāsinaḥ  |
sopavāsāḥ saniyamāḥ sarve sāgniparigrahāḥ  || 56.67 ||
devanadyāstaṭe ramye munisaṅghaiḥ samākule  |
āgacchadbhirnṛpaśreṣṭha mārgastatra na labhyate  || 56.68 ||
dṛṣṭvā janapadaṃ tatra tāṃ bhāryāṃ śabaro'bravīt |
gaccha pṛcchasva kimapi kimadya snānakāraṇam  || 56.69 ||
parvāṇi yāni śrūyante kiṃsvitsūryendusamplavaḥ  |
ayanaṃ kiṃ bhavedadya kiṃ vākṣayatṛtīyakā  || 56.70 ||
tataḥ svabharturvacanācchabarī prasthitā tadā  |
papraccha nārīṃ dṛṣṭvāgre dattvāgre kamale śubhe  || 56.71 ||
tithiradyaiva kā proktā kiṃ parva kathayasva me  |
kimayaṃ snāti loko'yaṃ kiṃ vā snānasya kāraṇam  || 56.72 ||

nāryuvāca -
adya caikādaśī puṇyā sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  |
upoṣitā sakṛdyena nākaprāptiṃ karoti sā  || 56.73 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā śabarī śābarāya vai  |
kathayāmāsa cāvyagrā strīvākyaṃ nṛpasattama  || 56.74 ||
adya tvekādaśī puṇyā bālavṛddhairupoṣitā  |
madanaikādaśī nāma sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  || 56.75 ||
niyatā śrūyate tatra rājaputrī suśobhanā  |
vratasthā niyatāhārā nāmnā bhānumatī satī  || 56.76 ||
naitayā sadṛśī kācittriṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  |
dṛśyate sā varārohā hyavatīrṇā mahītale  || 56.77 ||
bhāryāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā śabarastāṃ jagāda ha  |
kamalāni yathālābhaṃ dattvā bhuṅkṣva hi satvaram  || 56.78 ||
mamaiṣā vartate buddhirna bhoktavyaṃ mayā dhruvam  |
na mayopārjitaṃ bhadre pāpabuddhyā śubhaṃ kvacit || 56.79 ||

śabaryuvāca -
na pūrvaṃ tu mayā bhuktaṃ kasmiṃścaiva tu vāsare  |
bhuktaśeṣaṃ mayā bhuktaṃ yāvatkālaṃ smarāmyaham  || 56.80 ||
bhāryāyā niścayaṃ jñātvā snānaṃ kartuṃ jagāma ha  |
ardhottarīyavastreṇa snānaṃ kṛtvā tu bhaktitaḥ  || 56.81 ||
sarvān devānnamaskṛtya gato devaśilāṃ prati  |
tasthau sa śaṅkamāno'pi namaskṛtya janārdanam  || 56.82 ||
yasyāstu kumude datte tayā rājñyai niveditam  |
taddṛṣṭvā padmayugalaṃ tāṃ dāsīṃ sābravīttadā  || 56.83 ||
kutra padmadvayaṃ labdhaṃ kathyatāmagrato mama  |
śīghraṃ tatraiva gatvā ca padmānānaya cāparān  || 56.84 ||
dhānyena vasunā vāpi kamalāni samānaya  |
bhānumatyā vacaḥ śrutvā gatā sā śabaraṃ prati  || 56.85 ||
śrīphalāni ca puṣpāṇi bahūnyanyāni dehi me  || 56.86 ||

śabaryuvāca -
śrīphalāni sapuṣpāṇi dāsyāmi ca viśeṣataḥ  |
na lobho na spṛhā me'sti gatvā rājñīṃ nivedaya  || 56.87 ||
tayā ca satvaraṃ gatvā yathāvṛttaṃ niveditam  |
śabaryuktaṃ purastasyāḥ savistaraparaṃ vacaḥ  || 56.88 ||
tasyāstu vacanaṃ śrutvā rājñī tatra svayaṃ gatā  |
uvāca śabarīṃ prītyā dehi padmāni mūlyataḥ  || 56.89 ||

śabaryuvāca -
na mūlyaṃ kāmaye devi phalapuṣpasamudbhavam  |
śrīphalāni ca puṣpāṇi yatheṣṭaṃ mama gṛhyatām  || 56.90 ||
arcāṃ kuru yathānyāyaṃ vāsudeve jagatpatau  || 56.91 ||

rājñyuvāca -
vinā mūlyaṃ na gṛhṇāmi kamalāni tavādhunā  |
dhānyasya khārikāmekāṃ dadāmi pratigṛhyatām  || 56.92 ||
daśa viṃśatyatha triṃśaccatvāriṃśadathāpi vā  |
gṛhāṇa vā khāriśataṃ durbhikṣāṃ bodhimuttara  || 56.93 ||
vasu ratnaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca anyatte yadabhīpsitam  |
tatsarvaṃ sampradāsyāmi kamalārthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 56.94 ||

śabaryuvāca -
nāhāraṃ cintayāmyadya muktvā devaṃ varānane  |
devakāryaṃ vinā bhadre nānyā buddhiḥ pravartate  || 56.95 ||

rājñyuvāca -
na tvayānnaṃ parityājyaṃ sarvamanne pratiṣṭhitam  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena mamānnaṃ pratigṛhyatām  || 56.96 ||
tapasvino mahābhāgā ye cāraṇyanivāsinaḥ  |
gṛhasthadvāri te sarve yācante'nnamatandritāḥ  || 56.97 ||

śabaryuvāca -
niṣedhaśca kṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ sarvaṃ satye pratiṣṭhitam  |
satyena tapate sūryaḥ satyena jvalate'nalaḥ  || 56.98 ||
satyena tiṣṭhatyudadhirvāyuḥ satyena vāti hi  |
satyena pacyate sasyaṃ gāvaḥ kṣīraṃ sravanti ca  || 56.99 ||
satyādhāramidaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena satyaṃ satyena pālayet || 56.100 ||
devakāryaṃ tu me muktvā nānyā buddhiḥ pravartate  |
gṛhāṇa rājñi puṣpāṇi kuru pūjāṃ gadābhṛtaḥ  || 56.101 ||
śrūyate dvijavākyaistu na doṣo vidyate kvacit |
kuśāḥ śākaṃ payo matsyā gandhāḥ puṣpākṣatā dadhi  |
māṃsaṃ śayyāsanaṃ dhānāḥ pratyākhyeyā na vāri ca  || 56.102 ||

rājñyuvāca -
ārāmopahṛtaṃ puṣpamāraṇyaṃ puṣpameva ca  |
krītaṃ pratigrahe labdhaṃ puṣpamevaṃ caturvidham  || 56.103 ||
uttamaṃ puṣpamāraṇyaṃ gṛhītaṃ svayameva ca  |
madhyamaṃ phalamārāme tvadhamaṃ krītameva ca  |
pratigraheṇa yallabdhaṃ niṣphalaṃ tadvidurbudhāḥ  || 56.104 ||

purohita uvāca -
gṛhāṇa rājñi puṣpāṇi kuru pūjāṃ gadābhṛtaḥ  |
upakāraḥ prakartavyo vyapadeśena karhicit || 56.105 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śrīphalāni sapadmāni dattāni śabareṇa tu  |
gṛhītvā tāni rājñī sā pūjāṃ cakre suśobhanām  || 56.106 ||
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ cakre śrutvā paurāṇikīṃ kathām  |
śabarastu tato bhāryāmidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 56.107 ||
dīpārthaṃ gṛhyatāṃ sneho yathālābhena sundari  |
kṛtvā dīpaṃ tatastau tu kṛtvā pūjāṃ hareḥ śubhām  || 56.108 ||
cakraturjāgaraṃ rātrau dhyāyanto dharaṇīdharam  |
tataḥ prabhātasamaye dṛṣṭvā snānotsukaṃ janam  || 56.109 ||
snāti vai śūlabhede tu devanadyāṃ tathāpare  |
sarasvatyāṃ narāḥ kecinmārkaṇḍasya hrade'pare  || 56.110 ||
cakratīrthaṃ gatāścakruḥ snānaṃ kecidvidhānataḥ  |
śucayaste janāḥ sarve snātvā devāśilopari  || 56.111 ||
śrāddhaṃ cakruḥ prayatnena śraddhayā pūtacetasā  |
tāndṛṣṭvā śabaro bilvaiḥ piṇḍāṃścakre prayatnataḥ  || 56.112 ||
bhānumatyā tathā bhartuḥ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtam  |
anindyā bhojitā viprā dambhavārddhuṣyavarjitāḥ  || 56.113 ||
haviṣyānnaistathā dadhnā śarkarāmadhusarpiṣā  |
pāyasena tu gavyena kṛtānnena viśeṣataḥ  || 56.114 ||
bhojayitvā tathā rājñī dadau dānaṃ yathāvidhi  |
pādukopānahau chatraṃ śayyāṃ govṛṣameva ca  || 56.115 ||
vividhāni ca dānāni hemaratnadhanāni ca  |
cakratīrthe mahārāja kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
pṛthvī tena bhaveddattā saśailavanakānanā  || 56.116 ||

uttānapāda uvāca -
yāni yāni ca dattāni śastāni jagatīpateḥ  |
tāni sarvāṇi deveśa kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 56.117 ||

īśvara uvāca -
tilapradaḥ prajāmiṣṭāṃ dīpadaścakṣuruttamam  |
bhūmidaḥ svargamāpnoti dīrghamāyurhiraṇyadaḥ  || 56.118 ||
gṛhado rogarahito rūpyado rūpavān bhavet |
vāsodaścandrasālokyamarkasāyujyamaśvadaḥ  || 56.119 ||
vṛṣadastu śriyaṃ puṣṭāṃ godātā ca triviṣṭapam  |
yānaśayyāprado bhāryāmaiśvaryamabhayapradaḥ  || 56.120 ||
dhānyadaḥ śāśvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahma śāśvatam  |
vāryannapṛthivīvāsastilakāñcanasarpiṣām  || 56.121 ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ brahmadānaṃ viśiṣyate  |
yena yena hi bhāvena yadyaddānaṃ prayacchati  || 56.122 ||
tena tena sa bhāvena prāpnoti pratipūjitam  |
dṛṣṭvā dānāni sarvāṇi rājñī dattāni yāni ca  || 56.123 ||
uvāca śabaro bhāryāṃ yattacchṛṇu nareśvara  |
purāṇaṃ paṭhitaṃ bhadre brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  || 56.124 ||
śrutaṃ ca tanmayā sarvaṃ dānadharmaphalaṃ śubham  |
pūrvajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ snānadānavratādibhiḥ  || 56.125 ||
śarīraṃ dustyajaṃ muktvā labhate gatimuttamām  |
saṃsārasāgarādbhītaḥ satyaṃ bhadre vadāmi te  || 56.126 ||
anekāni ca pāpāni kṛtāni bahuśo mayā  |
ghātitā jantavo bhadre nirdagdhāḥ parvatāḥ sadā  || 56.127 ||
tena pāpena dagdho'haṃ dāridryaṃ na nivartate  |
tīrthāvagāhanaṃ pūrvaṃ pāpena na kṛtaṃ mayā  || 56.128 ||
tenāhaṃ duḥkhito bhadre dāridryamanivartikam  |
māturgṛhaṃ prayāhi tvaṃ tyaja snehaṃ mamopari  |
nagaśṛṅgaṃ samāruhya moktumicchāmyahaṃ tanum  || 56.129 ||

śabaryuvāca -
mātrā pitrā na me kāryaṃ nāpi svajanabāndhavaiḥ  |
yā gatistava jīveśa sā mamāpi bhaviṣyati  || 56.130 ||
na strīṇāmīdṛśo dharmo vinā bhartrā svajīvitam  |
śrūyante bahavo doṣā dharmaśāstreṣvanekadhā  || 56.131 ||
pāraṇaṃ kuru bhojendra vrataṃ yena na naśyati  |
yatte'bhivāñchitaṃ kiṃcidviṣṇave kartumarhasi  || 56.132 ||
bhāryāyā vacanaṃ śrutvā mumude śabarastataḥ  |
gṛhītvā śrīphalaṃ śīghraṃ homaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 56.133 ||
sarvadevānnamaskṛtya bhukto'pi ca tayā saha  |
caitryāṃ tu viṣuvaṃ jñātvā tasthau tatra dinatrayam  || 56.134 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe vyādhavākyopadeśakathanapūrvakadānādiphalavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 57

īśvara uvāca -
bhānumatī dvijānbhojya bubhuje bhuktaśeṣataḥ  |
bhuktvā susukhamāsthāya tadannaṃ pariṇāmya ca  || 57.1 ||
trayodaśyāṃ tato gatvā madanākhyatithau tadā  |
mārkaṇḍasya hrade snātvānarcya devaṃ guhāśayam  || 57.2 ||
kṛtopavāsaniyamā snāpayitvā maheśvaram  |
pañcāmṛtasugandhena dhūpadīpanivedanaiḥ  || 57.3 ||
ārcayadvividhaiḥ puṣpairnaivedyaiśca suśobhanaiḥ  |
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śrutvā paurāṇikīṃ kathām  || 57.4 ||
nṛtyagītaistathā stotrairdadhyau devaṃ maheśvaram  |
annaṃ vistāritaṃ sarvaṃ devasyāgre yathāvidhi  || 57.5 ||
cāturvarṇyasutāḥ sarve bhojitāḥ saparicchadāḥ  |
caturdaśyāṃ dinaṃ yāvatsampūjya vṛṣabhadhvajam  || 57.6 ||
śaṅkhavāditrabherībhiḥ paṭahadhvanināditam  |
kṣapājāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhūtajanasaṃkulam  || 57.7 ||
nṛtyagītaistathā stotraiḥ preritā sā niśā tadā  |
prabhāte bhojitā viprāḥ pāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  || 57.8 ||
dattvā dānāni viprebhyaḥ śaktyā viprānusārataḥ  |
arcayitvā mahāpuṣpaiḥ sugandhairmadanena ca  || 57.9 ||
vicitraiḥ sūkṣmavastraiśca devaḥ sampūjya veṣṭitaḥ  |
sragdāmalambamānaiśca bahudīpasamujjvalaiḥ  || 57.10 ||
pakvānnairvividhairbhakṣyaiḥ suvṛttairmodakādibhiḥ  |
tataste brāhmaṇāḥ sarve vedādhyayanatatparāḥ  || 57.11 ||
tatparva kīrtayāṃścakruḥ padmakaṃ nāma nāmataḥ  |
ādityasya dinaṃ tvadya tithiḥ pañcadaśī tathā  || 57.12 ||
tvāṣṭrameva ca nakṣatraṃ saṃkrāntirviṣuvantathā  |
vyatīpātastathā yogaḥ karaṇaviṣṭireva ca  || 57.13 ||
padmakaṃ nāma parvaitadayanādicaturguṇam  |
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 57.14 ||
te dvijā bhānumatyātha śūlabhedaṃ gatāḥ saha  |
dadṛśuḥ śabaraṃ kuṇḍe bhāryayā saha saṃsthitam  || 57.15 ||
aiśānīṃ sa diśaṃ gatvā parvate bhṛgumūrdhani  |
patituṃ ca samārūḍho bhāryayā saha pārthiva  || 57.16 ||

bhānumatyuvāca -
tiṣṭha tiṣṭha mahāsattva śṛṇuṣva vacanaṃ mama  |
kimarthaṃ tyajasi prāṇānadyāpi ca yuvā bhavān  || 57.17 ||
kaḥ santāpaḥ ka udvegaḥ kiṃ duḥkhaṃ vyādhireva ca  |
śiśuḥ saṃdṛśyase'dyāpi kāraṇaṃ kathyatāmidam  || 57.18 ||

śabara uvāca -
kāraṇaṃ nāsti me kiṃcinna duḥkhaṃ kiṃcideva tu  |
saṃsārabhayabhīto'haṃ nānyā buddhiḥ pravartate  || 57.19 ||
duḥkhena labhyate yasmānmānuṣyaṃ janma bhāgyataḥ  |
mānuṣyaṃ janma cāsādya yā na dharmaṃ samācaret || 57.20 ||
sa gacchennirayaṃ ghoramātmadoṣeṇa sundari  |
tasmātpatitumicchāmi tīrthe'sminpāpanāśane  || 57.21 ||

rājñyuvāca -
adyāpi vartate kālo dharmasyopārjane tava  |
kṛtāpakṛtakarmā vai vratadānairviśudhyati  || 57.22 ||
ahaṃ dāsyāmi dhānyaṃ vā vāsāṃsi draviṇaṃ bahu  |
nityamācara dharmaṃ tvaṃ dhyāyannityaṃ maheśvaram  || 57.23 ||

śabara uvāca -
naivāhaṃ kāmaye vittaṃ na dhānyaṃ vastrameva ca  |
yo yasyaivānnamaśnāti sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam  || 57.24 ||

rājñyuvāca -
kandamūlaphalāhāro bhramitvā bhaikṣyamuttamam  |
avagāhya sutīrthāni sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 57.25 ||
tato vimuktapāpastu yatkiṃcitkurute śuciḥ  |
karmaṇā tena pūtastvaṃ sadgatiṃ prāpsyasi dhruvam  || 57.26 ||

śabara uvāca -
annamadya mayā tyaktaṃ prāṇebhyo'pi mahattaram  |
satyaṃ na lopayeddevi niścitātra matirmama  || 57.27 ||
prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ devi kṣamasvādya janaiḥ saha  |
ardhottarīyavastreṇa saṃyamyātmānamudyataḥ  || 57.28 ||
bhāryayā sahito vyādho hariṃ dhyātvā papāta ha  |
nagārdhātpatito yāvadgatajīvo narādhipa  || 57.29 ||
cūrṇībhūtau hi tau dṛṣṭvā kuṇḍasyopari bhūmipa  |
trimuhūrte gate kāle śabaro bhāryayā saha  || 57.30 ||
divyaṃ vimānamārūḍho gataścānuttamāṃ gatim  || 57.31 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vyādhasvargagamanavarṇanaṃ nāma saptapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 58

uttānapāda uvāca -
athāto devadeveśa bhānumatyakarocca kim  |
eṣa me saṃśayo deva kathayasva prasādataḥ  || 58.1 ||

īśvara uvāca -
cintayitvā muhūrtaṃ sā gatā kuṇḍasya sannidhau  |
dṛṣṭvā kuṇḍasya māhātmyaṃ rājñī harṣeṇa pūritā  || 58.2 ||
viprān bahūn samāhūya pūjayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt |
dattvā tu vidhivaddānaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nṛpātmaja  || 58.3 ||
niścayaṃ paramaṃ kṛtvā sthitā śāntena cetasā  |
tataḥ sampūjya vidhivatpitḥndevānnarādhipa  || 58.4 ||
kṣapayitvā pakṣamekaṃ madhumāsasya sā sthitā  |
amāvāsyāṃ tato rājñī gatā parvatasannidhau  || 58.5 ||
nagaśṛṅgaṃ samāruhya kṛtvā mukulitau karau  |
vijñāpya brāhmaṇān sarvānidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 58.6 ||
mama mātā pitā bhrātā ye cānye sakhibāndhavāḥ  |
kṣamāpayitvā sarvāṃstānvacanaṃ mama kathyatām  || 58.7 ||
tvatputrī śūlabhede tu tapaḥ kṛtvā svaśaktitaḥ  |
visṛjya caiva sātmānaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe divaṃ yayau  || 58.8 ||
brāhmaṇā ūcuḥ  |
saṃdeśaṃ kathayiṣyāmastvayoktaṃ śobhanavrate  |
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ suśroṇi mā te'bhūdatra saṃśayaḥ  || 58.9 ||
tato visṛjya tāṃllokān sthitā parvatamūrdhani  |
ardhottarīyavastreṇa gāḍhaṃ baddhā punaḥpunaḥ  || 58.10 ||
tataścikṣepa sātmānamekacittā narādhipa  |
nagārddhe patitā yāvattāvaddṛṣṭāḥ surāṅganāḥ  || 58.11 ||
bhobho vatse mahābhāge bhānumatyatitāpasi  |
divyaṃ vimānamāruhya kailāsaṃ prati gamyatām  || 58.12 ||
tataḥ sā paśyatāṃ teṣāṃ janānāṃ tridivaṃ gatā  || 58.13 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
iti te kathitaḥ sarvaḥ śūlabhedasya vistaraḥ  |
yaḥ śrutaḥ śaṅkarātpūrvamṛṣidevasamāgame  || 58.14 ||
ya idaṃ paṭhate bhaktyā tīrthe devakule'pi vā  |
sa mucyate mahāpāpādapi janmaśatārjitāt || 58.15 ||
brahmahā ca surāpī ca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ  |
goghātī strīvighātī ca devabrahmasvahārakaḥ  || 58.16 ||
svāmidrohī mitraghātī tathā viśvāsaghātakaḥ  |
paranyāsāpahārī ca paranikṣepalopakaḥ  || 58.17 ||
rasabhedī tulābhedī tathā vārddhuṣikastu yaḥ  |
yaḥ kanyāvighnakartā ca tathā vikrayakārakaḥ  || 58.18 ||
parabhāryā bhrātṛbhāryā gauḥ snuṣā kanyakā tathā  |
abhigāmī paradveṣī tathā dharmapradūṣakaḥ  || 58.19 ||
mucyante sarve evaite śūlabhedaprabhāvataḥ  || 58.20 ||
ya idaṃ śrāvayecchrāddhe viprāṇāṃ bhuñjatāṃ nṛpa  |
mudaṃ prayānti saṃhṛṣṭāḥ pitarastasya sarvaśaḥ  || 58.21 ||
yaścedaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā paṭhyamānaṃ naro vaśī  |
sa muktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ sarvakalyāṇabhāgbhavet || 58.22 ||
idaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyamidaṃ pāvanamuttamam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ nṛṇāmāyuḥkīrtivivardhanam  || 58.23 ||
iti  kathitamidaṃ te śūlabhedasya puṇyaṃ mahimana hi manuṣyaiḥ śrūyate yatsapāpaiḥ  |
madanariputaṭinyā yāmyakūlasthitasya prabaladuritakandocchedakuddālakalpam  || 58.24 ||

|| iti śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūlabhedatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ||

śūlabhedamāhātmyaṃ samāptam


ṛKV adhyāya 59

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ puṣkariṇīṃ gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  |
śrute yasyāḥ prabhāve tu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 59.1 ||
revāyā uttare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
yatrāste sarvadā devo vedamūrtirdivākaraḥ  || 59.2 ||
kurukṣetraṃ yathā puṇyaṃ sārvakāmikamuttamam  |
idaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 59.3 ||
kurukṣetre yathā vṛddhirdānasya jagatīpate  |
puṣkariṇyāṃ tathā dānaṃ vardhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 59.4 ||
yavamekaṃ tu yo dadyātsauvarṇaṃ mastake nṛpa  |
puṣkariṇyāṃ tathā sthānaṃ yathā sthānaṃ nare smṛtam  || 59.5 ||
sūryagrahe tu yaḥ snātvā dadyāddānaṃ yathāvidhi  |
hastyaśvaratharatnādi gṛhaṃ gāśca yugaṃdharān  || 59.6 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi brāhmaṇebhyo dadāti yaḥ  |
trayodaśa dinaṃ yāvattrayodaśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 59.7 ||
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
dvādaśābde bhavetprītistatra tīrthe mahīpate  || 59.8 ||
yastatra kurute śrāddhaṃ pāyasairmadhusarpiṣā  |
śrāddhado labhate svargaṃ pitḥṇāṃ dattamakṣayam  || 59.9 ||
akṣatairbadarairbilvairiṅgudairvā tilaiḥ saha  |
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti tasmiṃstīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 59.10 ||
tatra snātvā tu yo devaṃ pūjayecca divākaram  |
ādityahṛdayaṃ japtvā punarādityamarcayet |
sa gacchetparamaṃ lokaṃ tridaśairapi vanditam  || 59.11 ||
ṛcamekāṃ japedyastu yajurvā sāma eva ca  |
sa samagrasya vedasya phalamāpnoti vai nṛpa  || 59.12 ||
yastryakṣaraṃ japenmantraṃ dhyāyamāno divākaram  |
ādityahṛdayaṃ japtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 59.13 ||
yastatra vidhivatprāṇāṃstyajate nṛpasattama  |
sa gacchetparamaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo divākaraḥ  || 59.14 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe puṣkariṇyāmādityatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 60

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhūyo'pyahaṃ pravakṣyāmi ādityeśvaramuttamam  |
sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ pārtha sarvavighnavināśanam  || 60.1 ||
āyuḥśrīvarddhanaṃ nityaṃ putradaṃ svargadaṃ śivam  |
yasya tīrthasya cānyāni tīrthāni kurunandana  || 60.2 ||
nālabhanta śriyaṃ nāke martye pātālagocare  |
kurukṣetraṃ gayā gaṅgā naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  || 60.3 ||
vārāṇasī ca kedāraṃ prayāgaṃ rudranandanam  |
mahākālaṃ sahasrākṣaṃ śuklatīrthaṃ nṛpottama  || 60.4 ||
ravitīrthasya sarvāṇi kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  |
ravitīrthe hi yadvṛttaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpottama  || 60.5 ||
snehātte kathayiṣyāmi vārddhakenātipīḍitaḥ  |
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve taponiṣṭhā mahaujasaḥ  || 60.6 ||
śrutaṃ me rudrasāṃnidhye nandiskandagaṇaiḥ saha  |
pārvatyā pṛṣṭaḥ śambhuśca ravitīrthasya yatphalam  || 60.7 ||
śambhunā ca yadākhyātaṃ girijāyāḥ sasambhramam  |
tatsarvamekacittena rudrodgītaṃ śrutaṃ mayā  || 60.8 ||
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇu yatnena pāṇḍava  |
durbhikṣopahatā viprā narmadāṃ tu samāśritāḥ  || 60.9 ||
uddālako vaśiṣṭhaśca māṇḍavyo gautamastathā  |
yājñavalkyo'tha gargaśca śāṇḍilyo gālavastathā  || 60.10 ||
nāciketo vibhāṇḍaśca vālakhilyādayastathā  |
śātātapaśca śaṅkhaśca jaiminirgobhilastathā  || 60.11 ||
jaigīṣavyaḥ śatānīkaḥ sarva eva samāgatāḥ  |
tīrthayātrā kṛtā taistu narmadāyāḥ samantataḥ  || 60.12 ||
ādityeśvaramāyātāḥ prasaṅgādṛṣipuṃgavāḥ  |
vṛkṣaiḥ saṃchāditaṃ śubhraṃ dhavatindukapāṭalaiḥ  || 60.13 ||
jambīrairarjunaiḥ kubjaiḥ śamīkesarakiṃśukaiḥ  |
tasmiṃstīrthe mahāpuṇye sugandhikusumākule  || 60.14 ||
punnāganālikeraiśca khadiraiḥ kalpapādapaiḥ  |
anekaśvāpadākīrṇaṃ mṛgamārjārasaṃkulam  || 60.15 ||
ṛkṣahastisamākīrṇaṃ citrakaiścopaśobhitam  |
praviṣṭā ṛṣayaḥ sarve vane puṣpasamākule  || 60.16 ||
vanānte ca striyo dṛṣṭvā raktā raktāmbarānvitāḥ  |
raktamālyānuśobhāḍhyā raktacandanacarcitāḥ  || 60.17 ||
raktābharaṇasaṃyuktāḥ pāśahastā bhayāvahāḥ  |
tāsāṃ samīpagā dṛṣṭāḥ kṛṣṇajīmūtasannibhāḥ  || 60.18 ||
mahākāyā bhīmavaktrāḥ pāśahastā bhayāvahāḥ  |
anāvṛṣṭyupamā dṛṣṭā āturāḥ piṅgalocanāḥ  || 60.19 ||
dīrghajihvā karālāsyā tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrā durāsadā  |
vṛddhā nārī kuruśreṣṭha dṛṣṭānyā ṛṣipuṃgavaiḥ  || 60.20 ||
tataḥ samīpagā vṛddhā tasya vṛndasya bhārata  |
svādhyāyaniratā viprā dṛṣṭāstaiḥ pāpakarmabhiḥ  || 60.21 ||
ūcuste tu samūhena brāhmaṇāṃstapasi sthitān  |
asmākaṃ svāminaḥ sarve tiṣṭhante tīrthamadhyataḥ  |
te prasthāpyā mahābhāgāḥ sarvathaiva tvarānvitāḥ  || 60.22 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ teṣāṃ sarve caiva tvarānvitāḥ  |
jagmuste narmadākakṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā revāṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 60.23 ||
tataḥ kecitstuvantyanye jaya devi namo'stu te  || 60.24 ||
namo'stu te siddhagaṇairniṣevite namo'stu te sarvapavitramaṅgale  |
namo'stu te viprasahasrasevite namo'stu rudrāṅgasamudbhave vare  || 60.25 ||
namo'stu te sarvapavitrapāvane namo'stu te devi varaprade śive  |
namāmi te śītajale sukhaprade saridvare pāpahare vicitrite  || 60.26 ||
anekabhūtaughasusevitāṅge gandharvayakṣoragapāvitāṅge  |
mahāgajaughairmahiṣairvarāhairāpīyase toyamahormimāle  || 60.27 ||
namāmi te sarvavare sukhaprade vimocayāsmānaghapāśabaddhān  || 60.28 ||
bhramanti tāvannarakeṣu martyā yāvattavāmbho nahi saṃśrayanti  |
spṛṣṭaṃ karaiścandramaso raveścettaddevi dadyātparamaṃ padaṃ tu  || 60.29 ||
anekasaṃsārabhayārditānāṃ pāpairanekairabhiveṣṭitānām  |
gatistvamambhojasamānavaktre dvandvairanekairabhisaṃvṛtānām  || 60.30 ||
nadyaśca pūtā vimalā bhavanti tvāṃ devi samprāpya na saṃśayo'tra  |
duḥkhāturāṇāmabhayaṃ dadāsi śiṣṭairanekairabhipūjitāsi  || 60.31 ||
viṇmūtradehāśca nimagnadehā bhramanti tāvannarakeṣu martyāḥ  |
mahābaladhvastataraṅgabhaṅgaṃ jalaṃ na yāvattava saṃspṛśanti  || 60.32 ||
mlecchāḥ pulindāstvatha yātudhānāḥ pibanti ye'ṃbhastava devi puṇyam  |
te'pi pramucyanti bhayācca ghorātkimatra viprā bhavapāśabhītāḥ  || 60.33 ||
sarāṃsi nadyaḥ kṣayamabhyupetā ghore yuge'sminkalināvasṛṣṭe  |
tvaṃ bhrājase devi jalaughapūrṇā divīva nakṣatrapathe ca gaṅgā  || 60.34 ||
tava prāsādādvarade viśiṣṭe kālaṃ yathemaṃ paripālayitvā  |
yāsyāma mokṣaṃ tava suprasādādvayaṃ yathā tvaṃ kuru naḥ prasādam  || 60.35 ||
tvāmāśritā ye śaraṇaṃ gatāśca gatistvamambeva piteva putrān  |
tvatpālitā yāvadimaṃ sughoraṃ kālaṃ tvanāvṛṣṭihataṃ kṣipāmaḥ  || 60.36 ||
evaṃ stutā tadā devī narmadā saritāṃ varā  |
pratyakṣā sā parā mūrtirbrāhmaṇānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 60.37 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
paṭhanti ye stotramidaṃ narendra śṛṇvanti bhaktyā parayā praśāntāḥ  |
te yānti rudraṃ vṛṣasaṃyutena yānena divyāmbarabhūṣitāṅgāḥ  || 60.38 ||
ye stotrametatsatataṃ japanti snātvā ca toyena tu narmadāyāḥ  |
tebhyo'ntakāle sariduttameyaṃ gatiṃ viśuddhāmacirāddadāti  || 60.39 ||
prātaḥ samutthāya tathā śayāno yaḥ kīrtayetānudinaṃ stavendram  |
dehakṣayaṃ sve salile dadāti samāśrayaṃ tasya mahānubhāva  || 60.40 ||
pāpairvimuktā divi modamānāḥ sambhoginaścaiva tu nānyathā ca  || 60.41 ||
prasannā narmadā devī stotreṇānena bhārata  |
jalenāpyāyitān viprān dakṣiṇāpathavāhinī  || 60.42 ||
amṛtatvaṃ tu vo dadmi yogibhiryanna gamyate  |
durlabhaṃ yatsuraiḥ sarvairmatprasādāllabhiṣyatha  || 60.43 ||
iti te brāhmaṇā rājaṃllabdhā varamanuttamam  |
gamiṣyantaḥ prītacittā dadṛśuścitramadbhutam  || 60.44 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dṛṣṭāstaiḥ puruṣāḥ pārtha narmadātaṭasaṃsthitāḥ  |
snānadevārcanāsaktāḥ pañca eva mahābalāḥ  || 60.45 ||
te dṛṣṭā brāhmaṇaiḥ sarvairvedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ  |
saṃpṛṣṭāstairmahārāja yathā tadavadhāraya  || 60.46 ||
viprā ūcuḥ  |
vanānte strīyugaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahāraudraṃ bhayāvaham  |
vṛddhāśca puruṣāstatra pāśahastā bhayāvahāḥ  || 60.47 ||
durdharṣā durnirīkṣyāśca itaścetaśca cañcalāḥ  |
vyāharantaḥ śubhāṃ vācaṃ na tatra gatirasti vai  || 60.48 ||
aparasparayoḥ sarve nirīkṣantaḥ punaḥpunaḥ  |
taistu yadvacanaṃ proktaṃ tatsarvaṃ kathyatāmiti  || 60.49 ||
asmākaṃ puruṣāḥ pañca tiṣṭhanti tatra sattamāḥ  |
te prasthāpyā mahābhāgāḥ sarvathaiva tvarānvitāḥ  || 60.50 ||
atha te puruṣāḥ pañca śrutvā vākyamidaṃ śubham  |
parasparaṃ nirīkṣanto vadanti ca punaḥpunaḥ  || 60.51 ||
kva te kasya kuto yātāḥ kimuktaṃ tairbhayāvahaiḥ  || 60.52 ||
puruṣā ūcuḥ  |
tīrthāvagāhanaṃ sarvaiḥ pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimaiḥ  |
uttaraiśca kṛtaṃ bhaktyā na pāpaṃ tairvyapohitam  || 60.53 ||
niṣpāpāścātha saṃjātāstīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve vahnikālopamā dvijāḥ  || 60.54 ||
pātakāni ca ghorāṇi yānyacintyāni dehinām  |
pāpiṣṭhena tu caikena gurudārā niṣevitā  || 60.55 ||
hṛtaṃ cānyena mitrasvaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca dhanaṃ tathā  |
brahmahatyā mahāraudrā kṛtā cānyena pātakam  || 60.56 ||
surāpānaṃ tu cānyasya saṃjātaṃ cāpyakāmataḥ  |
govadhyā cāpyakāmena kṛtā caikena pāpinā  || 60.57 ||
akāmato'pi sarveṣāṃ pātakāni narādhipa  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ tu te śrutvā vākyaṃ tadvismayānvitāḥ  || 60.58 ||
sadya eva tadā jātāḥ pāpiṣṭhā gatakalmaṣāḥ  |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāvena narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  || 60.59 ||
na kvacitpātakānāṃ tu praveśaścātra jāyate  |
evaṃ saṃcitya te sarve pāpiṣṭhāśca parasparam  || 60.60 ||
citrabhānuḥ smṛtastaistu vicintya hṛdaye harim  |
snātvā revājale puṇye tarpitāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ  || 60.61 ||
natvā tu bhāskaraṃ devaṃ hṛdi dhyātvā janārdanam  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu taṃ bhaktyā jvalantaṃ jātavedasam  || 60.62 ||
patitāḥ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha pāpodvignā mahīpate  |
sāttvikīṃ vāsanāṃ kṛtvā tyaktvā rajastamastathā  || 60.63 ||
hataṃ taiḥ pāvake sarvaṃ revāyā uttare taṭe  |
vimānasthāstadā dṛṣṭā brāhmaṇaiste yudhiṣṭhira  || 60.64 ||
āścaryamatulaṃ dṛṣṭamṛṣibhirnarmadātaṭe  |
tadāprabhṛti te sarve rāgadveṣavivarjitāḥ  || 60.65 ||
ravitīrthaṃ dvijā hṛṣṭāḥ sevante mokṣakāṅkṣayā  |
tīrthasyāsya ca yatpuṇyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 60.66 ||
pīḍito vṛddhabhāvena bhaktyā prīto nareśvara  |
uddeśaṃ kathayiṣyāmi dvikrośābhyantare sthitaḥ  || 60.67 ||
kurukṣetraṃ yathā puṇyaṃ ravitīrthaṃ śrutaṃ mayā  |
īśvareṇa purā khyātaṃ ṣaṇmukhasya narādhipa  || 60.68 ||
śrutaṃ rudrācca taiḥ sarvairahaṃ tatra samīpagaḥ  |

īśvara uvāca -
mārtaṇḍagrahaṇe prāpte ye vrajanti ṣaḍānana  |
ravitīrthe kurukṣetre tulyametatphalaṃ labhet || 60.69 ||
snāne dāne tathā japye home caiva viśeṣataḥ  |
kurukṣetre samaṃ puṇyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 60.70 ||
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  |
ravitīrthe viśeṣeṇa revā puṇyaphalapradā  || 60.71 ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ sūryadine bhaktyā vyatīpāte ca vai dhṛtau  |
saṃkrāntau grahaṇe'māyāṃ ye vrajanti jitendriyāḥ  || 60.72 ||
kāmakrodhairvimuktāśca rāgadveṣaistathaiva ca  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā devasyāgre narādhipa  || 60.73 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpaṃ devasya bodhayet |
kathāṃ vai vaiṣṇavīṃ pārtha vedābhyasanameva ca  || 60.74 ||
ṛgvedaṃ vā yajurvedaṃ sāmavedamatharvaṇam  |
ṛcamekāṃ japedyastu sa vedaphalamāpnuyāt || 60.75 ||
gāyatryā ca caturvedaphalamāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
prabhāte pūjayedviprānannadānahiraṇyataḥ  || 60.76 ||
bhūmidānena vastreṇa annadānena śaktitaḥ  |
chatropānahaśayyādigṛhadānena pāṇḍava  || 60.77 ||
grāmadhūrvahadānena gajakanyāhayena ca  |
vidyāśakaṭadānena sarveṣāmabhayaṃ bhavet || 60.78 ||
śatruśca mitratāṃ yāti viṣaṃ caivāmṛtaṃ bhavet |
grahā bhavanti suprītāḥ prītastasya divākaraḥ  || 60.79 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ ravitīrthaphalaṃ nṛpa  |
ye śṛṇvanti narā bhaktyā ravitīrthaphalaṃ śubham  || 60.80 ||
te'pi pāpavinirmuktā raviloke vasanti hi  |
godānena ca yatpuṇyaṃ yatpuṇyaṃ bhṛgudarśane  || 60.81 ||
kedāra udakaṃ pītvā tatpuṇyaṃ jāyate nṛṇām  |
abdamaśvatthasevāyāṃ tilapātraprado bhavet || 60.82 ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti ādityeśvarakīrtanāt |
śrute yasya prabhāve na jāyate yannṛpātmaja  || 60.83 ||
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi bhaktyā tava mahīpate  |
pāpāni ca pralīyante bhinnapātre yathā jalam  || 60.84 ||
tīrthasyābhimukho nityaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ kathitaṃ tava pāṇḍava  || 60.85 ||
pāpiṣṭhānāṃ kṛtaghnānāṃ svāmimitrāvaghātinām  |
tīrthākhyānaṃ śubhaṃ teṣāṃ gopitavyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ  || 60.86 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ādityeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 61

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchetparaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
śakratīrthaṃ suvikhyātamaśeṣāghavināśanam  || 61.1 ||
purā śakreṇa tatraiva tapo vai duratikramam  |
prārabdhaṃ parayā bhaktyā devaṃ prati maheśvaram  || 61.2 ||
tataḥ saṃtoṣito deva umāpatirnarādhipa  |
devendratvaṃ varaṃ rājyaṃ dānavānāṃ vadhaṃ dadau  || 61.3 ||
labdhaṃ śakreṇa nṛpate narmadātīrthabhāvataḥ  |
tataḥ puṇyatamaṃ tīrthaṃ saṃjātaṃ vasudhātale  || 61.4 ||
kārttikasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśīm  |
upoṣya vai naro bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 61.5 ||
duḥsvapnasambhavaiḥ pāpairdurnimittasamudbhavaiḥ  |
grahaśākinisambhūtairmucyate pāṇḍunandana  || 61.6 ||
śakreśvaraṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha ye prapaśyanti bhaktitaḥ  |
teṣāṃ janmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ naśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 61.7 ||
agamyāgamane caiva avāhye caiva vāhite  |
svāmimitravighāte yannaśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 61.8 ||
gopradānaṃ prakartavyaṃ śubhaṃ brāhmaṇapuṃgave  |
dhuryaṃ vā dāpayettasmin sarvāṅgaruciraṃ nṛpa  || 61.9 ||
dātavyaṃ parayā bhaktyā svarge vāsamabhīpsatā  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ śakreśvaraphalaṃ nṛpa  || 61.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śakreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 62

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra karoḍīśvaramuttamam  |
yatra vai nihatāstāta dānavāḥ sapadānugāḥ  || 62.1 ||
indrādidevaiḥ saṃhṛṣṭaiḥ satataṃ jayabuddhibhiḥ  |
teṣāṃ ye putrapautrāśca pūrvavairamanusmaram  || 62.2 ||
kruddhairdevasamūhaiśca dānavā nihatā raṇe  |
teṣāṃ śirāṃsi saṃgṛhya sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 62.3 ||
nikṣipya narmadātoye bandhubhāvamanusmaram  |
tatra snātvā surāḥ sarve sthāpayitvā umāpatim  || 62.4 ||
indreṇa sahitāḥ sarve'pūjayaṃllokasiddhaye  |
hṛṣṭacittāḥ surāḥ sarve jagmurākāśamaṇḍalam  || 62.5 ||
dānavānāṃ mahābhāga sūditā koṭiruttamā  |
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ karoḍīti mahītale  || 62.6 ||
vikhyātaṃ tu tadā loke pāpaghnaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāmubhau pakṣau ca bhaktitaḥ  |
upoṣya śūlinaścāgre rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 62.7 ||
satkathāpāṭhasaṃyukto vedādhyayanasaṃyutaḥ  |
prabhāte vimale prāpte pūjayettridaśeśvaram  || 62.8 ||
pañcāmṛtena saṃsnāpya śrīkhaṇḍena ca guṇṭhayet |
śastaiḥ pallavapuṣpaiśca pūjayettu prayatnataḥ  || 62.9 ||
bahurūpaṃ japanmantraṃ dakṣiṇāśāṃ vyavasthitaḥ  |
yathoktena vidhānena nābhimātre jale kṣipet || 62.10 ||
tilāñjaliṃ tu pretāya dakṣiṇāśāmupasthitaḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva viprāya kārayedvijitendriyaḥ  || 62.11 ||
viṣamairagrajātaiśca vedābhyasanatatparaiḥ  |
gohiraṇyena sampūjya tāmbūlairbhojanaistathā  || 62.12 ||
bhūṣaṇaiḥ pādukābhiśca brāhmaṇānpāṇḍunandana  |
bhavetkoṭiguṇaṃ tasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 62.13 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ kaścittyajeddehaṃ vidhānataḥ  |
tasya bhavati yatpuṇyaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 62.14 ||
yāvadasthīni tiṣṭhanti martyasya narmadājale  |
tāvadvasati dharmātmā śivaloke sudurlabhe  || 62.15 ||
tataḥ kālāccyutastasmādiha mānuṣatāṃ gataḥ  |
koṭidhanapatiḥ śrīmāñjāyate rājapūjitaḥ  || 62.16 ||
sarvadharmasamāyukto medhāvī bījaputrakaḥ  |
vikhyāto vasudhāpṛṣṭhe dīrghāyurmānavo bhavet || 62.17 ||
punaḥ smarati tattīrthaṃ tatra gatvā nṛpottama  |
karoḍeśvaramabhyarcya prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 62.18 ||
indracandrayamairrudrairādityairvasubhistathā  |
viśvedevaistathā sarvaiḥ sthāpitastridaśeśvaraḥ  || 62.19 ||
revāyā uttare kūle lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
mānavo bhaktisaṃyuktaḥ prāsādaṃ kārayettu yaḥ  || 62.20 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe naraśreṣṭha sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāt |
nyāyopāttadhanenaiva dārupāṣāṇakeṣṭakaiḥ  || 62.21 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdraiḥ strībhiśca śaktitaḥ  |
te'pi yānti narā loke śāṃkare surapūjite  || 62.22 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti sadā bhaktyā māhātmyaṃ tīrthajaṃ nṛpa  |
tasya pāpaṃ praṇaśyeta ṣaṇmāsābhyantaraṃ ca yat || 62.23 ||


|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe karoḍīśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ||



ṛKV adhyāya 63

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra kumāreśvaramuttamam  |
prasiddhaṃ sarvatīrthānāmagastyeśvarasannidhau  || 63.1 ||
ṣaṇmukhena purā tāta sarvapātakanāśanam  |
ārādhya parayā bhaktyā siddhiḥ prāptā narādhipa  || 63.2 ||
devasainyādhipo jātaḥ sarvaśatrunibarhaṇaḥ  |
ugratejā mahātmāsau saṃjātastīrthasevanāt || 63.3 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ saṃjātaṃnarmadātaṭe  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo gatvā ekacitto jitendriyaḥ  || 63.4 ||
kārttikasya caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
snāpayedgirijānāthaṃ dadhidugdhena sarpiṣā  || 63.5 ||
gītaṃ tatra prakartavyaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ yathāvidhi  |
brāhmaṇaiḥ śrotriyaiḥ pārtha ṣaṭkarmanirataiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 63.6 ||
yatkiṃciddīyate tatra akṣayaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
sarvatīrthamayaṃ tīrtha nirmitaṃ śikhinā nṛpa  || 63.7 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kumāreśvarajaṃ phalam  |
kumāradarśanātpuṇyaṃ prāpyate pāṇḍunandana  || 63.8 ||
mṛtaḥ svargamavāpnoti satyamīśvarabhāṣitam  || 63.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kumāreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 64

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
narāṇāṃ pāpanāśāya agastyeśvaramuttamam  || 64.1 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājanmucyate brahmahatyayā  |
kārttikasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  || 64.2 ||
ghṛtena snāpayeddevaṃ samādhistho jitendriyaḥ  |
ekaviṃśatikulopeto cyavedaiśvarātpadāt || 64.3 ||
dhanaṃ copānahau chatraṃ dadyācca ghṛtakambalam  |
bhojanaṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 64.4 ||
