|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe agastyeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 65

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra ānandeśvaramuttamam  |
tattīrthaṃ kathayiṣyāmi sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 65.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ānandaścaiva saṃjāto rudrasya dvijasattama  |
kathyatāṃ me ca tatsarvaṃ saṃkṣepātsaha bāndhavaiḥ  || 65.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kathayāmi nṛpaśreṣṭha ānandeśvaramuttamam  |
dānavānāṃ vadhaṃ kṛtvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 65.3 ||
pūjito daivataiḥ sarvaiḥ kinnarairyakṣapannagaiḥ  |
ānandasaṃyuto devo nanarta vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 65.4 ||
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsthāya gauryā cārddhāṅgasaṃsthitaḥ  |
bhūtavetālakaṅkālairbhairavairbhairavo vṛtaḥ  || 65.5 ||
nanarta narmadātīre dakṣiṇe pāṇḍunandana  |
tuṣṭairmarudgaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ sthāpitaḥ kamalāsanaḥ  || 65.6 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthamānandeśvaramucyate  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ narādhipa  || 65.7 ||
vidhivaccārcayeddevaṃ sugandhena vilepayet |
brāhmaṇānpūjayettatra yathāśaktyā yudhiṣṭhira  || 65.8 ||
godānaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vastradānaṃ śubhāvaham  |
vasantasya trayodaśyāṃ śrāddhaṃ tatraiva kārayet || 65.9 ||
iṅgudairbadarairbilvairakṣataiśca jalena vā  |
pretānāṃ kārayecchrāddhamānandeśvara uttame  || 65.10 ||
ānanditā bhaveyuste yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
santatervai na vicchedaḥ saptajanmasu jāyate  |
ānando hi bhavatteṣāṃ pratijanmani bhārata  || 65.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ānandeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 66

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra mātṛtīrthamanuttamam  |
saṅgamasya samīpasthaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 66.1 ||
mātarastatra rājendra saṃjātā narmadātaṭe  |
umārdhanārirdeveśo vyālayajñopavītadhṛk || 66.2 ||
uvāca yoginīvṛndaṃ kaṣṭaṃkaṣṭamaho hara  |
ajeyāḥ sarvadevānāṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara  || 66.3 ||
tīrthamatra vidhānena prakhyātaṃ vasudhātale  |
evaṃ bhavatu yoginya ityuktvāntaradhācchivaḥ  || 66.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā navamyāṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā pūjayenmātṛgocaram  || 66.5 ||
tasya syurmātaraḥ prītāḥ prīto'yaṃ vṛṣavāhanaḥ  |
vandhyāyā mṛtavatsāyā aputrāyā yudhiṣṭhira  || 66.6 ||
snāpanaṃ cārabhettatra mantraśāstraviduttamaḥ  |
sahiraṇyena kumbhena pañcaratnaphalānvitaḥ  || 66.7 ||
snāpayetputrakāmāyāḥ kāṃsyapātreṇa deśikaḥ  |
putraṃ sā labhate nārī vīryavantaṃ guṇānvitam  || 66.8 ||
yo yaṃ kāmamabhidhyāyettataḥ sa labhate nṛpa  |
mātṛtīrthātparaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati  || 66.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mātṛtīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 67

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ tāta jalamadhye vyavasthitam  |
luṅkeśvaramiti khyātaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 67.1 ||
idaṃ tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ nānāścaryaṃ mahītale  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyamutpattiṃ śṛṇu bhārata  || 67.2 ||
āsītpurā mahāvīryo dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
kālapṛṣṭha iti khyātaḥ suto brahmasutasya ca  || 67.3 ||
gaṅgātaṭaṃ samāśritya cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
adhomukho'pi saṃsthitvāpibaddhūmamaharniśam  || 67.4 ||
tataścānantaraṃ devastiṣṭhate hyumayā saha  |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ pārvatī sā tu tapasyugre vyavasthitam  || 67.5 ||
paśya paśya mahādeva dhūmāśī tiṣṭhate naraḥ  |
prasīda taṃ kuruṣvādya dehi śīghraṃ varaṃ vibho  || 67.6 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yaduktaṃ vacanaṃ devi na tanme rocate priye  |
svakāryaṃ ca sadā cintyaṃ parakāryaṃ visarjayet || 67.7 ||
mūrkhastrībālaśatrūṇāṃ yaśchandenānuvartate  |
vyasane patate ghore satyametadudīritam  || 67.8 ||

devyuvāca -
bhāryayābhyarthito bhartā kāraṇaṃ bahu bhāṣate  |
laghutvaṃ yāti sā nārī evaṃ śāstreṣu paṭhyate  || 67.9 ||
prāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyāmi yadi māṃ tvaṃ na manyase  |
pārvatyā prerito devo gato'sau dānavaṃ prati  || 67.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kimarthaṃ pibase dhūmaṃ kimarthaṃ tapyase tapaḥ  |
kiṃ duḥkhaṃ kiṃ nu santāpo vada kāryamabhīpsitam  || 67.11 ||
yuvā tvaṃ dṛśyase'dyāpi varṣaviṃśatireva ca  |
tadācakṣva hi me sarvaṃ tapasaḥ kāraṇaṃ mahat || 67.12 ||

dānava uvāca -
acalā dīyatāṃ bhaktirmama sthairyaṃ tavopari  |
aparaṃ varṣasāhasraṃ nirvighnaṃ me gataṃ vibho  || 67.13 ||
divasānāṃ sahasre dve pūrṇe tvattapasā mama  || 67.14 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yācayābhīpsitaṃ kāryaṃ tuṣṭo'haṃ tava suvrata  |
devasya vacanaṃ śrutvā cintayāmāsa dānavaḥ  || 67.15 ||
kiṃ nākaṃ yācayāmyadya kimadya sakalāṃ mahīm  |
evaṃ saṃcintayāmāsa kāmabāṇena pīḍitaḥ  || 67.16 ||

dānava uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dāsyasi me prabho  |
saṅgrāmaistu na tuṣṭo'haṃ balaṃ nāstīti kiṃcana  || 67.17 ||
yasya mūrdhanyahaṃ deva pāṇinā samupaspṛśe  |
devadānavagandharvo bhasmasādyātu tatkṣaṇāt || 67.18 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yattvayā cintitaṃ kiṃcittatsarvaṃ saphalaṃ tava  |
uttiṣṭha gaccha śīghraṃ tvaṃ bhavanaṃ prati dānava  || 67.19 ||

dānava uvāca -
sthīyatāṃ devadeveśa yāvajjñāsyāmi te varam  |
yuṣmanmūrdhni nyase pāṇiṃ pratyayo me bhavedyathā  || 67.20 ||
tataścānantaraṃ devaścintayāno maheśvaraḥ  |
na skando na harirbrahmā yaḥ kāryeṣu kṣamo'dhunā  || 67.21 ||
jñātvā caivāpadaṃ prāptāṃ devaḥ prārthayate vṛṣam  |
anena saha pāpena yudhyasva sāmprataṃ kṣaṇam  || 67.22 ||
karaṃ prāsārayaddaityo devaṃ mūrdhni kila spṛśet |
lāṅgūlenāhato daityo viṣaṇṇaḥ patito bhuvi  || 67.23 ||
devastu dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ gataścaivomayā saha  |
bhayabhīto nirīkṣeta grīvāṃ bhajya punaḥpunaḥ  || 67.24 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve yuyudhe vṛṣabheṇa saḥ  |
dvāvetau balināṃ śreṣṭhau yuyudhāte mahābalau  || 67.25 ||
prahārairvajrasadṛśaiḥ kopena ghaṭikātrayam  |
pāṇibhyāṃ na spṛśedyo vai vṛṣabhasya śirastathā  || 67.26 ||
hatvā lāṅgūlapātena āgato vṛṣabhastadā  |
utthitaścāpyasau daityo vrajate vṛṣapṛṣṭhataḥ  || 67.27 ||
vāyuvegena samprāpto yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
āgataṃ dānavaṃ dṛṣṭvā vṛṣo vacanamabravīt || 67.28 ||
āruhya pṛṣṭhe me deva śīghrameva hi gamyatām  |
āruhya vṛṣabhaṃ devo jagāma comayā saha  || 67.29 ||
nākaṃ prāptastato devo gataḥ śakrasya mandiram  |
nātyajaddevapṛṣṭhaṃ tu dānavo baladarpitaḥ  || 67.30 ||
indralokaṃ parityajya brahmalokaṃ gatastadā  |
yatrayatra vrajeddevo bhayātsaha divaukasaiḥ  || 67.31 ||
apaśyattatra tatraiva pṛṣṭhe lagnaṃ tu dānavam  |
sarvāṃllokān bhramitvā tu devo vismayamāgataḥ  || 67.32 ||
na sthānaṃ vidyate kiṃcidyatra viśramyate kṣaṇam  |
devadānavayostatra yuddhaṃ jñātvā sudāruṇam  || 67.33 ||
harṣitātmā munistatra ciraṃ nṛtyati nāradaḥ  |
dhanyo'hamadya me janma jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam  || 67.34 ||
mahāntaṃ ca kaliṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃtoṣaḥ paramo'bhavat |
devadānavayostatra yuddhaṃ tyaktvā ca nāradaḥ  || 67.35 ||
ājagāma tato vipro yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā devo'tha taṃ vipraṃ pratipūjyābravīdidam  || 67.36 ||
bho nārada muniśreṣṭha jānīṣe keśavaṃ kvacit |
gatvā tatra ca śīghraṃ tvaṃ keśavāya nivedaya  || 67.37 ||

nārada uvāca -
devadānavasiddhānāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām  |
sarveṣāmeva deveśo harate dhruvamāpadam  || 67.38 ||
asaṃbhāvyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ manasāpi na cintayet |
īdṛśīṃ naiva budhyāmi āpadaṃ ca vibho tava  || 67.39 ||

īśvara uvāca -
gaccha nārada śīghraṃ tvaṃ yatra devo janārdanaḥ  |
viditaṃ ca tvayā sarvaṃ yatkṛtaṃ dānavena tu  || 67.40 ||
avadhyo dānavo hyeṣa sendrairapi marudgaṇaiḥ  |
gatvā tu keśavaṃ devaṃ nivedaya mahāmune  || 67.41 ||

nārada uvāca -
na tu gacchāmyahaṃ deva suptaḥ kṣīrodadhau sukhī  |
keśavaḥ preraṇe hyeṣāmādeśo dīyatāṃ prabho  || 67.42 ||
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā rājānaṃ ca tathā prabhum  |
guruṃ caivāditaḥ kṛtvā śayānaṃ na prabodhayet || 67.43 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yadi kvacidagāreṣu vahnirutpadyate mahān  |
nidhanaṃ yānti tatrasthā yadbudhyerannasūrayaḥ  || 67.44 ||

nārada uvāca -
śīghraṃ gaccha mahādeva ātmānaṃ rakṣa suprabho  |
gacchāmyahaṃ na sandeho yatra devo janārdanaḥ  || 67.45 ||
tato nandimahākālau stambhahastau bhayānakau  |
jaghnaturdānavaṃ tatra mudgarādibhirāyudhaiḥ  || 67.46 ||
trayo'pi ca mahākāyāḥ saptatālapramāṇakāḥ  |
na śamo jāyate teṣāṃ yudhyatāṃ ca parasparam  || 67.47 ||
tataścānantaraṃ vipro'gacchattaṃ keśavaṃ prati  |
suptaṃ kṣīrārṇave'paśyaccheṣaparyaṅkasaṃsthitam  || 67.48 ||
lakṣmyā pādayugaṃ gṛhya ūrūpari niveśitam  |
apsarogīyamānaṃ tu bhaktyānamya ca keśavam  || 67.49 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam  |
utthāpayasva deveśaṃ lakṣmi tvamaviśaṅkitā  || 67.50 ||
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā padāṅguṣṭhaṃ vyamardayat |
nāradastiṣṭhate dvāri uttiṣṭha madhusūdana  || 67.51 ||
devo'pi nāradaṃ dṛṣṭvā paraṃ harṣamupāgataḥ  |
svāgataṃ tu muniśreṣṭha suprabhātādya śarvarī  || 67.52 ||

nārada uvāca -
adya me saphalaṃ deva prabhātaṃ tava darśanāt |
kuśalaṃ ca na devānāṃ śīghramuttiṣṭha gamyatām  || 67.53 ||

śrīviṣṇuruvāca -
brahmā cendraśca rudraśca ye cānye tu marudgaṇāḥ  |
āpadaḥ kāraṇaṃ yacca tatsamākhyātumarhasi  || 67.54 ||

nārada uvāca -
dānavena mahātīvraṃ tapastaptaṃ sudāruṇam  |
rudreṇa ca varo datto bhasmatvaṃ manasepsitam  || 67.55 ||
varadānabalenaiva sa devaṃ hantumarhati  |
īdṛśaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ jñātvā nīto devo'maraiḥ saha  || 67.56 ||
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā jagāma samunirhariḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā devastamīśānaṃ gacchantaṃ diśamuttarām  || 67.57 ||
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ ca rudro'tha pariṣvajya punaḥpunaḥ  |
namaskṛtya jagannāthaṃ devaṃ ca madhusūdanaḥ  || 67.58 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
bhayasya kāraṇaṃ deva kathyatāṃ ca maheśvara  |
devadānavayakṣāṇāṃ preṣayeyaṃ yamālayam  || 67.59 ||
lalāṭe ca kṛto dharmo yuṣmākaṃ ca maheśvara  |
chittvā śirastathāṅgāni indriyāṇi na saṃśayaḥ  || 67.60 ||

īśvara uvāca -
nāsti saukhyaṃ ca mūrkheṣu nāsti saukhyaṃ ca rogiṣu  |
parādhīnena saukhyaṃ tu strījite ca viśeṣataḥ  || 67.61 ||
strījitena mayā viṣṇo varo dattastu dānave  |
yasya mūrdhni nyasetpāṇiṃ sa bhavedbhasmapuṃjavat || 67.62 ||
ajeyaścāmaraścaiva mayā hyuktaḥ sa keśava  |
hantumicchati māṃ pāpa upāyastava vidyate  || 67.63 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
gacchantu amarāḥ sarve yuṣmābhiḥ saha śaṅkara  |
upāyaṃ sarjayāmyadya vadhārthaṃ dānasya ca  || 67.64 ||
revāyāśca taṭe tiṣṭha deva tvamamaraiḥ saha  |
kālakṣepo na kartavyo gamyatāṃ tvaritaṃ prabho  || 67.65 ||
dakṣiṇā yatra gaṅgā ca revā caiva mahānadī  |
yatrayatra ca dṛśyeta prācī caiva sarasvatī  || 67.66 ||
tatsamaṃ ca mahātīrthaṃ na martye caiva dṛśyate  |
snānaṃ ye tatra kurvanti dānaṃ caiva tu bhaktitaḥ  || 67.67 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ naśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
etattīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  || 67.68 ||
gamyatāṃ tatra deveśa luṅkeśaṃ tvaṃ sahāmaraiḥ  |
viṣṇostu vacanādeva praviṣṭo hradamuttamam  || 67.69 ||
ratiṃ sumahatīṃ cakre saha tatra marudgaṇaiḥ  |
tataścānantaraṃ devo māyāṃ kṛtvā hyanekadhā  || 67.70 ||
vasantamāsaṃ saṃsṛjya udyānavanaśobhitam  |
aśokairbakulaiścaiva brahmavṛkṣaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  || 67.71 ||
śrīvṛkṣaiśca kapitthaiśca śirīṣairrājacampakaiḥ  |
śrīphalaiśca tathā tālaiḥ kadambodumbaraistathā  || 67.72 ||
aśvatthādidrumaiścaiva nānāvṛkṣairanekaśaḥ  |
nānāpuṣpaiḥ sugandhāḍhyairbhramaraiśca nināditam  || 67.73 ||
tasminmadhye mahāvṛkṣo nyagrodhaśca suśobhanaḥ  |
bahupakṣisamāyuktaḥ kokilārāvanāditaḥ  || 67.74 ||
kṛṣṇena ca kṛtaṃ tasminkanyārūpaṃ ca tatkṣaṇāt |
na tasyāḥ sadṛśī kanyā trailokye sacarācare  || 67.75 ||
anyāśca kanyakāḥ sapta surūpāḥ śubhalocanāḥ  |
divyarūpadharāḥ sarvā divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ  || 67.76 ||
pumāṃsamabhikāṅkṣantyo yadyekaḥ kāmayetstriyaḥ  |
mauktikairratnamāṇikyairvaiḍūryaiśca suśobhanaiḥ  || 67.77 ||
kāmahāraiśca vaṃśaiśca baddho hindolakaḥ kṛtaḥ  |
ārūḍhāśca mahākanyā gāyante susvaraṃ tadā  || 67.78 ||
mārutaḥ śītalo vāti vanaṃ spṛṣṭvā suśobhanam  |
vātena prerito gandho dānavo ghrāṇapīḍitaḥ  || 67.79 ||
tataḥ kusumagandhena vismayaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  |
āghrāya cedṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ mayā  || 67.80 ||
vane cintayataḥ kiṃciddhvanigītaṃ suśobhanam  |
gītasya ca dhvaniṃ śrutvā mohito māyayā hareḥ  || 67.81 ||
vyādhasyaiva mahākūṭe patanti ca yathā mṛgāḥ  |
kālaspṛṣṭastathā kṛṣṇe patitaśca narādhipa  || 67.82 ||
dṛṣṭvā kanyāṃ ca tāṃ daityo mūrcchayā patito bhuvi  |
patitena tu dṛṣṭaikā kanyā vaṭatale sthitā  || 67.83 ||
āsyaṃ dṛṣṭvā tu nārīṇāṃ punaḥ kāmena pīḍitaḥ  |
gṛhītvā hemadaṇḍaṃ tu tāṃ pātayitumicchati  || 67.84 ||

kanyovāca -
mā mānusparśayatvaṃ hi kumāryahaṃ kulottama  |
bho muñca muñca māṃ śīghraṃ yāvadgacchāmyahaṃ gṛham  || 67.85 ||

dānava uvāca -
ahaṃ vivāhamicchāmi tvayā saha suśobhane  |
bhūpṛṣṭhe sakale rājñī bhavasyevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 67.86 ||

kanyovāca -
pitā rakṣati kaumārye bhartā rakṣati yauvane  |
putro rakṣati vṛddhatve na strī svātantryamarhati  || 67.87 ||
na svātantryaṃ mamaivāsti utpannāhaṃ mahatkule  |
yācyastu matpitā bhrātā mātāpi hi tathaiva ca  || 67.88 ||

dānava uvāca -
yadi māṃ necchase tvadya svātantryaṃ nāvalambase  |
mamāpi ca tadā hatyā satyaṃ ca śubhalocane  || 67.89 ||

kanyovāca -
viśvāso naiva kartavyo yādṛśe tādṛśe nare  |
narāḥ strīṣu vicitrāśca lampaṭāḥ kāmamohitāḥ  || 67.90 ||
pariṇīya tu māṃ tvaṃ hi bhuṅkṣva bhogānmayā saha  |
janmanāśo bhavetpaścānna tvaṃ nānyo bhavenmama  || 67.91 ||
brāhmaṇī kṣatriṇī vaiśī śūdrī yāvattathaiva ca  |
dvitīyo na bhavedbhartā ekākī ceha janmani  || 67.92 ||

dānava uvāca -
yattvayā gaditaṃ vākyaṃ tanmayā dhāritaṃ hṛdi  |
pratyayaṃ me kuruṣvādya yatte manasi rocate  || 67.93 ||

kanyovāca -
jānīṣva gopakanyāṃ māṃ krīḍāmi sakhibhiḥ saha  |
asmatkuleṣu yaddivyaṃ tatkuruṣva yathāvidhi  || 67.94 ||
na taddivyaṃ kule'smākaṃ viṣaṃ kośaṃ na tattulā  |
gopānvayeṣu sarveṣu hastaḥ śirasi dīyate  || 67.95 ||
kāmāndhenaiva rājendra nikṣipto mastake karaḥ  |
tatkṣaṇādbhasmasādbhūto dagdhastṛṇacayo yathā  || 67.96 ||
keśavopari devaistu puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ śubhā kṛtā  |
hṛṣṭāḥ sarve'gamandevāḥ svasthānaṃ vigatajvarāḥ  || 67.97 ||
kṣīrodaṃ keśavo gacchatkālapṛṣṭhe nipātite  |
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā caritaṃ dānavasya ca  || 67.98 ||
sa jayī jāyate nityaṃ śaṅkarasya vaco yathā  |
etasmātkāraṇādrājaṃlliṅgeśvaramiti śrutam  || 67.99 ||
līnaṃ ca pātakaṃ yasmātsnānamātreṇa naśyati  |
tvagasthi śoṇitaṃ māṃsaṃ medaḥsnāyustathaiva ca  || 67.100 ||
majjāśukragataṃ pāpaṃ naśyate janmakoṭijam  |
luṅkeśvare mahārāja toyaṃ pibati bhaktitaḥ  || 67.101 ||
tribhiḥ prasṛtimātrābhiḥ pāpaṃ yāti sahasradhā  |
viśeṣeṇa caturdaśyāmubhau pakṣau tu cāṣṭamī  || 67.102 ||
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā pitḥṇāṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
uddhṛtāstena te sarve nārakīyāḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 67.103 ||
kākiṇīṃ caiva yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage  |
tena dānaphalaṃ sarvaṃ kurukṣetrādikaṃ ca yat || 67.104 ||
prāptaṃ tu nānyathā rājañchaṅkaro vadate tvidam  |
sparśaliṅgamidaṃ rājañchaṅkareṇa tu nirmitam  || 67.105 ||
sparśamātre manuṣyāṇāṃ rudravāso'bhijāyate  |
tena dānaphalaṃ sarvaṃ kurukṣetrādikaṃ ca yat || 67.106 ||
etasmātkāraṇādrājaṃllokapālāśca rakṣakāḥ  |
durgā ca rakṣaṇe sṛṣṭā caturhastadharā śubhā  || 67.107 ||
dhanado lokapāleśo rakṣakaśceśvarasya ca  |
rakṣati ca sadā kālaṃ grahavyāpārarūpataḥ  || 67.108 ||
putrabhrātṛsamārūpaiḥ svāmisambandharūpibhiḥ  |
laṅkeśvaraṃ ca rājendra devairnādyāpi mucyate  || 67.109 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe luṅkeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 68

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dhanadasya tu tattīrthaṃ tato gacchedyudhiṣṭhira  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 68.1 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ tatra prāpyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
caitramāsatrayodaśyāṃ śuklapakṣe jitendriyaḥ  || 68.2 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  |
pañcāmṛtena rājendra snāpayeddhanadaṃ budhaḥ  || 68.3 ||
dīpaṃ ghṛtena dātavyaṃ gītaṃ vādyaṃ ca kārayet |
prabhāte pūjayedviprānātmanaḥ śreya icchati  || 68.4 ||
pratigrahasamarthāṃśca vidyāsiddhāntavādinaḥ  |
śrautasmārtakriyāyuktān paradāraparāṅmukhān  || 68.5 ||
pūjayedgohiraṇyena vastropānahabhojanaiḥ  |
chatraśayyāpradānena sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 68.6 ||
trijanmajanitaṃ pāpaṃ varadasya prabhāvataḥ  |
svargadaṃ durvinītānāṃ vinītānāṃ ca mokṣadam  || 68.7 ||
annadaṃ ca daridrāṇāṃ bhavejjanmanijanmani  |
kulīnatvaṃ duḥkhahāniḥ svabhāvājāyate nare  || 68.8 ||
vyādhidhvaṃso bhavetteṣāṃ narmadodakasevanāt |
dhanadasya tu yastīrthe vidyādānaṃ prayacchati  || 68.9 ||
sa yāti bhāskare loke sarvavyādhivivarjite  |
devadroṇīṃ ca tatraiva svaśaktyā pāṇḍunandana  || 68.10 ||
ye prakurvanti bhūyiṣṭhāṃ revāyā dakṣiṇe taṭe  |
te yānti śāṃkare loke sarvaduḥkhavivarjite  || 68.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dhanadatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 69

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra maṅgaleśvaramuttamam  |
sthāpitaṃ bhūmiputreṇa lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 69.1 ||
toṣitaḥ parayā bhaktyā śaṅkaraḥ śaśiśekharaḥ  |
caturdaśyāṃ gururdevaḥ pratyakṣo maṅgaleśvaraḥ  || 69.2 ||
brūhi putra varaṃ śubhraṃ taṃ te dāsyāmi maṅgala  || 69.3 ||

maṅgala uvāca -
prasādaṃ kuru me śambho pratijanmani śaṅkara  |
tvadaṅgasvedasambhūto grahamadhye vasāmyaham  || 69.4 ||
tvatprasādena īśāna pūjyo'haṃ sarvadaivataiḥ  |
kṛtārtho hyadya saṃjātastava darśanabhāṣaṇāt || 69.5 ||
sthāne'smin devadeveśa mama nāmnā maheśvaraḥ  |
evaṃ bhavatu te putretyuktvā cāntaradhīyata  || 69.6 ||
maṅgalo'pi mahātmā vai sthāpayitvā maheśvaram  |
ātmayogabalenaiva śūlināpūjayattataḥ  || 69.7 ||
sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ liṅgaṃ nāmnā vai maṅgaleśvaram  |
tatra tīrthe tu vai rājanbrāhmaṇānprīṇayetsudhīḥ  || 69.8 ||
sapatnīkānnṛpaśreṣṭha caturthyaṅgārake vrate  |
patnībhartārasaṃyuktaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ śrotriyaṃ dvijam  || 69.9 ||
vratānte caiva gaurdhuryaiḥ śivamuddiśya dīyate  |
prīyatāṃ me mahādevaḥ sapatnīko vṛṣadhvajaḥ  || 69.10 ||
vastrayugmaṃ pradātavyaṃ lohitaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
dhūrvahau raktavarṇau ca śubhraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathaiva ca  || 69.11 ||
chatraṃ śayyāṃ śubhāṃ caiva raktamālyānulepanam  |
dātavyaṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha viśuddhenāntarātmanā  || 69.12 ||
caturthyāṃ tu tathāṣṭamyāṃ pakṣayoḥ śuklakṛṣṇayoḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva kartavyaṃ vittaśāṭhyena varjitaḥ  || 69.13 ||
pretā bhavanti suprītā yugamekaṃ mahīpate  |
saputro jāyate martyaḥ pratijanma nṛpottama  || 69.14 ||
tasya tīrthasya bhāvena sarvāṅgaruciro nṛpa  |
maṅgalaṃ bhavate vaṃśo nāśubhaṃ vidyate kvacit || 69.15 ||
bhaktyā yaḥ kīrtayennityaṃ tasya pāpaṃ vyapohati  || 69.16 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe maṅgaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 70

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
revāyā uttare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
raviṇā nirmitaṃ pārtha sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 70.1 ||
svāṃśena bhāskarastatra tiṣṭhate cottare taṭe  |
sarvavyādhiharaḥ puṃsāṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ  || 70.2 ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃṣaṣṭhyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha hyaṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśīm  |
snānaṃ yaḥ kārayenmartyaḥ śrāddhaṃ preteṣu bhaktitaḥ  |
tasya pāpakṣayaḥ pārtha sūryaloke mahīyate  || 70.3 ||
tataḥ svargāccyutaḥ so'pi jāyate vimale kule  |
dhanāḍhyo vyādhinirmukto jīvejjanmanijanmani  || 70.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ravitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 71

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kāmeśvaraṃ tataścānyacchṛṇu pāṇḍavasattama  |
siddho yatra gaṇādhyakṣo gaurīputro mahābalaḥ  || 71.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā bhaktiyukto jitendriyaḥ  |
pañcāmṛtena saṃsnāpya dhūpanaivedyapūjanaiḥ  || 71.2 ||
prasādya jagatāmīśaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
aṣṭamyāṃ mārgaśīrṣasya tatra snātvā yudhiṣṭhira  || 71.3 ||
yo yena yajate tatra sa taṃ kāmamavāpnuyāt || 71.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kāmeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 72

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra maṇināgeśvaraṃ śubham  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
sthāpitaṃ maṇināgena lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 72.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āśīviṣeṇa sarpeṇa īśvarastoṣitaḥ katham  |
kṣudrāḥ sarvasya lokasya bhayadā viṣaśālinaḥ  || 72.2 ||
kathyatāṃ tāta me sarvaṃ pātakasyopaśāntidam  |
mama santāpajaṃ duḥkhaṃ duryodhanasamudbhavam  || 72.3 ||
karṇabhīṣmodbhavaṃ raudraṃ duḥkhaṃ pāñcālisambhavam  |
tava vaktrāmbujaughena plāvitaṃ nirvṛtiṃ gataḥ  || 72.4 ||
śrutvā tava mukhodgītāṃ kathāṃ vai pāpanāśinīm  |
ayuktamidamasmākaṃ dvija kleśo na śāmyati  || 72.5 ||
athavā prāpsyate tāta vidyādānasya yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ prāpyate nityaṃ kathāśravaṇato hareḥ  || 72.6 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
yathāyathā tvaṃ nṛpa bhāṣase ca tathātathā me sukhameti bhāratī  |
śaithilyatā vā jarayānvitasya tvatsauhṛdaṃ naśyati naiva tāta  |
śṛṇuṣva tasmātsaha bāndhavaiśca kathāmimāṃ pāpaharāṃ praśastām  || 72.7 ||
kathayāmi yathāvṛttamitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 72.8 ||
kathitaṃ pūrvato vṛttaiḥ pāramparyeṇa bhārata  || 72.9 ||
dve bhārye kaśyapasyāstāṃ sarvalokeṣvanuttame  |
garutmantaṃ ca vinatāsūta kadrūrahīnatha  || 72.10 ||
saṃtoṣeṇa ca te tāta tiṣṭhataḥ kāśyape gṛhe  |
kadrūśca vinatā nāma hṛṣṭe ca vanite sadā  || 72.11 ||
tābhyāṃ sārddhaṃ krīḍate ca kaśyapo'pi prajāpatiḥ  |
tatastvekadine prāpte āśramasthā śubhānanā  || 72.12 ||
uccaiḥśravaṃ hayaṃ dṛṣṭvā manovegasamanvitam  |
paśya paśya hi tanvaṅgī hayaṃ sarvatra pāṇḍuram  || 72.13 ||
dhāvamānamaviśrāntaṃ javena manasopamam  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasā cāśvamīrṣyābhāvena cābravīt || 72.14 ||

kadrūruvāca -
brūhi bhadre sahasrāṃśoraśvaḥ kiṃvarṇako bhavet |
ahaṃ bravīmi kṛṣṇo'yaṃ tvaṃ kiṃ vadasi tadvada  || 72.15 ||

vinatovāca -
paśyase nanu netraiśca kṛṣṇaṃ śvetaṃ na paśyasi  |
asatyabhāṣaṇādbhadre yamalokaṃ gamiṣyasi  || 72.16 ||
satyānṛte tu vacane paṇastava mamaiva tu  |
sahasraṃ caiva varṣāṇāṃ dāsyahaṃ tava mandire  || 72.17 ||
asatyā yadi me vāṇī kṛṣṇa uccaiḥśravā yadi  |
tadāhaṃ tvadgṛhe dāsī bhavāmi sarpamātṛke  || 72.18 ||
yadi uccaiḥśravāḥ śveto'haṃ dāsī ca tavaiva tu  |
evaṃ parasparaṃ dvābhyāṃ saṃvādo'yaṃ vyavardhata  || 72.19 ||
āśrameṣu gatā bālā rātrau cintāparā sthitā  |
bandhuvargasya kathitaṃ samastaṃ tadviceṣṭitam  || 72.20 ||
putrāṇāṃ kathitaṃ pārtha paṇaṃ caiva mayā kṛtam  |
hāhākāraḥ kṛtaḥ sarpaiḥ śrutvā mātrā paṇaṃ kṛtam  || 72.21 ||
jātā dāsī na sandehaḥ śveto bhāskaravāhanaḥ  |
uccaiḥśravā hayaḥ śveto na kṛṣṇo vidyate kvacit || 72.22 ||

kadrūruvāca -
yathāhaṃ na bhave dāsī tatkāryaṃ ca vicintyatām  |
viśadhvaṃ romakūpeṣu hyuccaiḥśravahayasya tu  || 72.23 ||
ekaṃ muhūrtamātraṃ tu yāvatkṛṣṇaḥ sa dṛśyate  |
kṣaṇamātreṇa caikena dāsī sā bhavate mama  || 72.24 ||
dāsīṃ kṛtvā tu tāṃ tanvīṃ vinatāṃ satyagarvitām  |
tataḥ svasthānagāḥ sarve bhaviṣyatha yathāsukham  || 72.25 ||
sarpā ūcuḥ  |
yathā tvaṃ jananī cāmba sarveṣāṃ bhuvi pūjitā  |
tathā sāpi viśeṣeṇa vañcitavyā na mātaraḥ  || 72.26 ||
mātā ca pitṛbhāryā ca mātṛmātā pitāmahī  |
karmaṇā manasā vācā hitaṃ tāsāṃ samācaret || 72.27 ||
sā tatastena vākyena kruddhā kālānalopamā  |
mama vākyamakurvāṇā ye kecidbhuvi pannagāḥ  || 72.28 ||
havyavāhamukhe sarve te yāsyantyavicāritam  |
mātustadvacanaṃ śrutvā sarve caiva bhujaṅgamāḥ  || 72.29 ||
kecitpraviṣṭā romeṣu uccaiḥśravahayasya ca  |
naṣṭāḥ keciddaśadiśaṃ kadrūśāpabhayāttataḥ  || 72.30 ||
kecidgaṅgājale naṣṭāḥ kecinnaṣṭāḥ sarasvatīm  |
kecinmahodadhau līnāḥ praviṣṭā vindhyakandare  || 72.31 ||
āśritya narmadātoye maṇināgottamo nṛpa  |
tapaścacāra vipulamuttare narmadātaṭe  || 72.32 ||
mātṛśāpabhayātpārtha dhyāyate kāmanāśanam  |
acchedyamapratarkyaṃ ca vināśotpattivarjitam  || 72.33 ||
vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ sāgraṃ tadardhaṃ ravivīkṣakaḥ  |
evaṃ dhyānaratasyaiva pratyakṣastripurāntakaḥ  || 72.34 ||
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga sattvavāṃstu bhujaṃgama  |
tvayā bhaktyā gṛhīto'haṃ prītaste hyurageśvara  |
varaṃ yācaya me kṣipraṃ yaste manasi vartate  || 72.35 ||

maṇināga uvāca -
mātṛśāpabhayānnātha kliṣṭo'haṃ narmadātaṭe  |
tvatprasādena me nātha mātṛśāpo bhavedvṛthā  || 72.36 ||

īśvara uvāca -
havyavāhamukhaṃ vatsa na prāpsyasi mamājñayā  |
mama loke nivāsaśca tava putra bhaviṣyati  || 72.37 ||

maṇināga uvāca -
atra sthāne mahādeva sthīyatāmaṃśabhāgataḥ  |
sahasrāṃśena bhāgena sthīyatāṃ narmadājale  |
upakārāya lokānāṃ mama nāmnaiva śaṅkara  || 72.38 ||

īśvara uvāca -
sthāpayasva paraṃ liṅgamājñayā mama pannaga  |
ityuktvāntarhito devo jagāma hyumayā saha  || 72.39 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tatra tīrthe tu ye gatvā śuciprayatamānasāḥ  |
pañcamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ śuklakṛṣṇayoḥ  || 72.40 ||
arcayanti sadā pārtha nopasarpanti te yamam  |
dadhnā ca madhunā caiva ghṛtena kṣīrayogataḥ  || 72.41 ||
snāpayanti virūpākṣamumādehārdhadhāriṇam  |
kāmāṅgadahanaṃ devamaghāsuraniṣūdanam  || 72.42 ||
snāpyamānaṃ ca ye bhaktvā paśyanti parameśvaram  |
te yānti ca pare loke sarvapāpavivarjitaiḥ  || 72.43 ||
śrāddhaṃ preteṣu ye pārtha cāṣṭamyāṃ pañcamīṣu ca  |
brāhmaṇaiśca sadā yogyairvedapāṭhakacintakaiḥ  || 72.44 ||
svadāranirataiḥ ślakṣṇaiḥ paradāravivarjitaiḥ  |
ṣaṭkarmanirataistāta śūdrapreṣaṇavarjitaiḥ  || 72.45 ||
khañjāśca dardurāḥ ṣaṇḍhā vārddhuṣyāśca kṛṣīvalāḥ  |
bhinnavṛttikarāḥ putra niyojyā na kadācana  || 72.46 ||
vṛṣalīmandire yasya mahiṣīṃ yastu pālayet |
sa vipro dūratastyājyo vrate śrāddhe narādhipa  || 72.47 ||
kāṇāṣṭuṃṭāśca maṇṭāśca vedapāṭhavivarjitāḥ  |
na te pūjyā dvijāḥ pārtha maṇināgeśvare śubhe  || 72.48 ||
yadīcchedūrdhvagamanamātmanaḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha  |
sarvāṅgarucirāṃ dhenuṃ yo dadyādagrajanmane  || 72.49 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ lokaṃ yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
tataḥ svargāccyutaḥ so'pi jāyate vimale kule  || 72.50 ||
ye paśyanti paraṃ bhaktyā maṇināgeśvaraṃ nṛpa  |
na teṣāṃ jāyate vaṃśe pannagānāṃ bhayaṃ nṛpa  || 72.51 ||
pannagaḥ śaṅkate teṣāṃ maṇināgapradarśanāt |
sauparṇarūpiṇaste vai dṛśyante nāgamaṇḍale  || 72.52 ||
phalāni caiva dānānāṃ śṛṇuṣvātha nṛpottama  |
annaṃ saṃskārasaṃyuktaṃ ye dadante narottamāḥ  || 72.53 ||
toyaṃ śayyāṃ tathā chatraṃ kanyāṃ dāsīṃ subhāṣiṇīm  |
pātre deyaṃ yato rājan yadīcchecchreya ātmanaḥ  || 72.54 ||
surabhīṇi ca puṣpāṇi gandhavastrāṇi dāpayet |
dīpaṃ dhānyaṃ gṛhaṃ śubhraṃ sarvopaskarasaṃyutam  || 72.55 ||
ye dadante paraṃ bhaktyā te vrajanti triviṣṭapam  |
maṇināge nṛpaśreṣṭha yacca dānaṃ pradīyate  || 72.56 ||
tasya dānasya bhāvena svarge vāso bhaveddhruvam  |
pātakāni pralīyante āmapātre yathā jalam  || 72.57 ||
narmadātoyasaṃsiddhaṃ bhojyaṃ vipre dadāti yaḥ  |
so'pi pāpairvinirmuktaḥ krīḍate daivataiḥ saha  || 72.58 ||
tataḥ svargacyutānāṃ hi lakṣaṇaṃ pravadāmyaham  |
dīrghāyuṣo jīvaputrā dhanavantaḥ suśobhanāḥ  || 72.59 ||
sarvavyādhivinirmuktāḥ sutabhṛtyaiḥ samanvitāḥ  |
tyāgino bhogasaṃyuktā dharmākhyānaratāḥ sadā  || 72.60 ||
devadvijagurorbhaktāstīrthasevāparāyaṇāḥ  |
mātāpitṛvaśā nityaṃ drohakrodhavivarjitāḥ  || 72.61 ||
ebhireva guṇairyuktā ye narāḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
satyaṃ te svargādāyātāḥ svarge vāsaṃ vrajanti te  || 72.62 ||
sarvatīrthavaraṃ tīrthaṃ maṇināgaṃ nṛpottama  |
tīrthākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi  || 72.63 ||
so'pi pāpairvinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  |
na viṣaṃ kramate teṣāṃ vicaranti yathecchayā  || 72.64 ||
bhādrapadyāṃ ca yatṣaṣṭhyāṃ puṇyaṃ sūryasya darśane  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti ākhyānaśravaṇena tu  || 72.65 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe maṇināgeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 73

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ pārtha gopāreśvaramuttamam  |
godehānniḥsṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ puṇyaṃ bhūmitale nṛpa  || 73.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
godehānniḥsṛtaṃ kasmālliṅgaṃ pāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
dakṣiṇe narmadākūle maṇināgasamīpataḥ  |
saṃkṣepātkathyatāṃ vipra gopāreśvarasambhavam  || 73.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kāmadhenustapastatra purā pārtha cakāra ha  |
dhyāyate parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 73.3 ||
tuṣṭastasyā jagannātha kapilāya maheśvaraḥ  |
niḥsṛto dehamadhyāttu acchedyaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 73.4 ||
tuṣṭo devi jaganmātaḥ kapile parameśvari  |
ārādhanaṃ kṛtaṃ yasmāttadvadāśu śubhānane  || 73.5 ||

surabhyuvāca -
lokānāmupakārāya sṛṣṭāhaṃ parameṣṭhinā  |
lokakāryāṇi sarvāṇi sidhyanti matprasādataḥ  || 73.6 ||
lokāḥ svargaṃ prayāsyanti matprasādena śaṅkara  |
tīrthe tvaṃ bhava me śambho lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 73.7 ||
tatheti bhagavānuktvā tīrthe tatrāvasanmudā  |
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ vikhyātaṃ vasudhātale  |
snānenaikena rājendra pāpasaṅghaṃ vyapohati  || 73.8 ||
gopāreśvaragodānaṃ yastu bhaktyā ca kārayet |
yogye dvijottame deyā yogyā dhenuḥ sakāñcanā  || 73.9 ||
savatsā taruṇī śubhrā bahukṣīrā savastrakā  |
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ vā pradāpayet || 73.10 ||
sarveṣu caiva māseṣu kārttike ca viśeṣataḥ  |
dāpayetparayā bhaktyā dvije svādhyāyatatpare  || 73.11 ||
vidhinā ca pradadyādyo vidhinā yastu gṛhṇate  |
tāvubhau puṇyakarmāṇau prekṣakaḥ puṇyabhājanam  || 73.12 ||
piṇḍadānaṃ prakuryādyaḥ pretānāṃ bhaktisaṃyutaḥ  |
piṇḍenaikena rājendra pretā yānti parāṃ gatim  || 73.13 ||
bhaktyā praṇāmaṃ rudrasya ye kurvanti dine dine  |
teṣāṃ pāpaṃ pralīyeta bhinnapātre jalaṃ yathā  || 73.14 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājanvṛṣabhaṃ ca samutsṛjet |
pitaraścoddhṛtāstena śivaloke mahīyate  || 73.15 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
vṛṣotsarge kṛte tāta phalaṃ yajjāyate nṛṇām  |
tatsarvaṃ kathayasvāśu prayatnena dvijottama  || 73.16 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇe vṛṣe caiva tu yatphalam  |
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva dharmanandana  || 73.17 ||
kārttike caiva vaiśākhe pūrṇimāyāṃ narādhipa  |
rudrasya sannidhau bhūtvā śuciḥ snāto jitendriyaḥ  || 73.18 ||
vṛṣasyaiva samutsargaṃ kārayetprīyatāṃ haraḥ  |
sāṃnidhye kārayetputra catasro vatsikāḥ śubhāḥ  || 73.19 ||
dattvā tu vipramukhyāya sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ  |
prīyatāṃ ca mahādevo brahmā viṣṇurmaheśvaraḥ  || 73.20 ||
vṛṣabhe romasaṃkhyā yā sarvāṅgeṣu narādhipa  |
tāvadvarṣapramāṇaṃ tu śivaloke mahīyate  || 73.21 ||
śivaloke vasitvā tu yadā martyeṣu jāyate  |
kule mahati sambhūtirdhanadhānyasamākule  || 73.22 ||
nīrogo rūpavāṃścaiva vidyāḍhyaḥ satyavākśuciḥ  |
gopāreśvaramāhātmyaṃ mayā khyātaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
godehānniḥsṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 73.23 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gopāreśvaramāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 74

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
revāyā uttare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ martye nāmnā vai gautameśvaram  || 74.1 ||
sthāpitaṃ gautamenaiva lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
svargasopānarūpaṃ tu tīrthaṃ puṃsāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 74.2 ||
tatra gaccha paraṃ bhaktyā yatra devo jagadguruḥ  |
pātakasya vināśārthaṃ svargavāsapradastathā  || 74.3 ||
saubhāgyavarddhanaṃ tīrthaṃ jayadaṃ duḥkhanāśanam  |
piṇḍadānena caikena kulānāmuddharettrayam  || 74.4 ||
yatkiṃciddīyate bhaktyā svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu  |
tatsarvaṃ śatasāhasramājñayā gautamasya hi  || 74.5 ||
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ svayaṃ rudreṇa bhāṣitam  || 74.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcamāvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gautameśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 75

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
śaṅkhacūḍasya nāmnā vai prasiddhaṃ bhūmimaṇḍale  || 75.1 ||
śaṅkhacūḍaḥ svayaṃ tatra saṃsthitaḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
vainateyabhayātpārtha sukhadanarmadātaṭe  || 75.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ  |
snāpayecchaṅkhacūḍaṃ tu kṣīrakṣaudreṇa sarpiṣā  || 75.3 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryāddevasyāgre narādhipa  |
dadhibhaktena sampūjya brāhmaṇāñchaṃsitavratān  |
gopradāne dvijendro'yaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaraḥ  || 75.4 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ pārtha sarpadaṣṭaṃ pratarpayet |
sa yāti paramaṃ lokaṃ śaṅkarasya vaco yathā  || 75.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śaṅkhacūḍatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 76

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra pāreśvaramanuttamam  |
parāśaro mahātmā vai narmadāyāstaṭe śubhe  || 76.1 ||
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ putrārthaṃ pāṇḍunandana  |
himavadduhitā tena gaurī nārāyaṇī nṛpa  || 76.2 ||
toṣitā parayā bhaktyā narmadottarake taṭe  |
tasya tuṣṭā mahādevī śaṅkarārdhāṅgadhāriṇī  || 76.3 ||
bhobho ṛṣivara śreṣṭha tuṣṭāhaṃ tava bhaktitaḥ  |
varaṃ yācaya me vipra parāśara mahāmate  || 76.4 ||

parāśara uvāca -
parituṣṭāsi me devi yadi deyo varo mama  |
dehi putraṃ bhagavati satyaśaucaguṇānvitam  || 76.5 ||
vedābhyasanaśīlaṃ hi sarvaśāstraviśāradam  |
tīrthe cātra bhaveddevi sannidhānavareṇa tu  || 76.6 ||
lokopakārahetośca sthīyatāṃ girinandini  |
parāśarābhidhānena narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 76.7 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
evaṃ bhavatu te vipra tatraivāntaradhīyata  |
parāśaro mahātmā vai sthāpayāmāsa pārvatīm  || 76.8 ||
śaṅkaraṃ sthāpayāmāsa surāsuranamaskṛtam  |
acchedyamapratarkyaṃ ca devānāṃ tu durāsadam  || 76.9 ||
parāśaro mahātmā vai kṛtārtho hyabhavannṛpa  || 76.10 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ  |
stryathavā puruṣo vāpi kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  || 76.11 ||
māghe caitre'tha vaiśākhe śrāvaṇe nṛpanandana  |
māsi mārgaśire caiva śuklapakṣe tu sarvadā  || 76.12 ||
tatra gatvā śubhe sthāne narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 76.13 ||
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā vratametatsamācaret |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpadānaṃ svaśaktitaḥ  || 76.14 ||
gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ tathā vādyaṃ kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
prabhāte vimale prāpte dvijāḥ pūjyāḥ svaśaktitaḥ  || 76.15 ||
sampūjya brāhmaṇān pārtha dhanadānahiraṇyataḥ  |
vastreṇa chatradānena śayyātāmbūlabhojanaiḥ  || 76.16 ||
prīṇayennarmadātīre brāhmaṇāñchaṃsitavratān  |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha āmaiḥ pakvairjalena ca  || 76.17 ||
strīṇāṃ caiva tu śūdrāṇāmāmaśrāddhaṃ praśasyate  |
āmaṃ caturguṇaṃ deyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 76.18 ||
vedoktena vidhānena dvijāḥ pūjyāḥ prayatnataḥ  |
hastamātraiḥ kuśaiścaiva tilaiścaivākṣatairnṛpa  || 76.19 ||
viprā udaṅmukhāḥ kāryāḥ svayaṃ vai dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
darbheṣu nikṣipedannamityuccārya dvijāgrataḥ  || 76.20 ||
pretā yāntu pare loke tīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  |
pāpaṃ me praśamaṃ yātu etu vṛddhiṃ śubhaṃ sadā  || 76.21 ||
vṛddhiṃ yātu sadā vaṃśo jñātivargo dvijottama  |
evamuccārya viprāya dānaṃ deyaṃ svaśaktitaḥ  || 76.22 ||
gobhūtisahiraṇyādi cānnaṃ vastraṃ svaśaktitaḥ  |
dātavyaṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha pāreśvaravarāśrame  || 76.23 ||
ye śṛṇvanti paraṃ bhaktyā mucyante sarvapātakaiḥ  || 76.24 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe pāreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 77

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhīmeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  |
sevitaṃ ṛṣisaṅghaiśca bhīmavratadharaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 77.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
japedekākṣaraṃ mantramūrdhvabāhurdivākare  || 77.2 ||
tasya janmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  |
saptajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ gāyatryā naśyate dhruvam  || 77.3 ||
daśabhirjanmabhirjātaṃ śatena tu purā kṛtam  |
sahasreṇa trijanmotthaṃ gāyatrī hanti kilbiṣam  || 77.4 ||
vaidikaṃ laukikaṃ vāpi jāpyaṃ japtaṃ nareśvara  |
tatkṣaṇāddahate sarvaṃ tṛṇaṃ tu jvalano yathā  || 77.5 ||
na devabalamāśritya kadācitpāpamācaret |
ajñānānnaśyate kṣipraṃ nottaraṃ tu kadācana  || 77.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo dānaṃ śaktimāśritya cācaret |
tadakṣayyaphalaṃ sarvaṃ jāyate pāṇḍunandana  || 77.7 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhīmeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 78

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra naradeśvaramuttamam  |
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ nāradena tu  || 78.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
nāradena muniśreṣṭha kasmāttīrthaṃ vinirmitam  |
etadākhyāhi me sarvaṃ prasanno yadi sattama  || 78.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
parameṣṭhisutaḥ pārtha nārado munisattamaḥ  |
revāyāścottare kūle tapastena purā kṛtam  || 78.3 ||
navanāḍīnirodhena kāṣṭhāvatyāṃ gatena ca  |
toṣitaḥ paśubhartā vai nāradena yudhiṣṭhira  || 78.4 ||

īśvara uvāca -
tuṣṭo'haṃ tava viprendra yoginātha ayonija  |
varaṃ prārthaya me vatsa yaste manasi vartate  || 78.5 ||

nārada uvāca -
tvatprasādena me śambho yogaścaiva prasidhyatu  |
acalā te bhavedbhaktiḥ sarvakālaṃ mamaiva tu  || 78.6 ||
svecchācārī bhave deva vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
trikālajño jagannātha gītajño'haṃ sadā bhave  || 78.7 ||
dine dine yathā yuddhaṃ devadānavamānuṣaiḥ  |
pātāle martyaloke vā svarge vāpi maheśvara  || 78.8 ||
paśyeyaṃ tvatprasādena bhavantaṃ pārvatīṃ tathā  |
tīrthaṃ lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 78.9 ||

īśvara uvāca -
evaṃ nārada sarvaṃ tu bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ  |
cintitaṃ matprasādena sidhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 78.10 ||
svecchācāro bhavervatsa svarge pātālagocare  |
martye vā bhrama vai yoginna kenāpi nivāryase  || 78.11 ||
sapta svarāstrayo grāmā mūrcchanāścaikaviṃśatiḥ  |
tānā ekonapañcāśatprasādānme tava dhruvam  || 78.12 ||
mama priyaṃkaraṃ divyaṃ nṛtyagītaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
kaliṃ ca paśyase nityaṃ devadānavakinnaraiḥ  || 78.13 ||
tvattīrthaṃ bhūtale puṇyaṃ matprasādādbhaviṣyati  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajño hyaśeṣajñānakovidaḥ  |
ekastvamasi niḥsaṅgo matprasādena nārada  || 78.14 ||
ityuktvāntardadhe devo nāradastatra śūlinam  |
sthāpayāmāsa rājendra sarvasattvopakārakam  || 78.15 ||
pṛthivyāmuttamaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ nāradena tu  |
tatra tīrthe nṛpaśreṣṭha yo gacchedvijitendriyaḥ  || 78.16 ||
māsi bhādrapade pārtha kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 78.17 ||
chatraṃ tatra pradātavyaṃ brāhmaṇe śubhalakṣaṇe  |
śastreṇa tu hatā ye vai teṣāṃ śrāddhaṃ pradāpayet |
te yānti paramaṃ lokaṃ piṇḍadānaprabhāvataḥ  || 78.18 ||
kapilā tatra dātavyā pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  |
ityuccārya dvije deyā yāntu te paramāṃ gatim  || 78.19 ||
asya śrāddhasya bhāvena brāhmaṇasya prasādataḥ  |
narmadātoyabhāvena nyāyārjitadhanasya ca  |
teṣāṃ caiva prabhāvena pretā yāntu parāṃ gatim  || 78.20 ||
ityuccārya dvije deyā dakṣiṇā ca svaśaktitaḥ  |
haviṣyānnaṃ viśālākṣa dvijānāṃ caiva dāpayet || 78.21 ||
dīpaṃ bhaktyā pradātavyaṃ nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ ca kārayet |
avāptaṃ tena vai sarvaṃ yaḥ karotīśvarālaye  || 78.22 ||
sa yāti rudrasāṃnidhyamiti rudraḥ svayaṃ jagau  |
vidyādānena caikena akṣayāṃ gatimāpnuyāt || 78.23 ||
dhūrvahāstatra dātavyā bhūmiḥ sasyavatī nṛpa  |
citrabhānuṃ śubhairmantraiḥ prīṇayettatra bhaktitaḥ  || 78.24 ||
ājyena suprabhūtena homadravyeṇa bhārata  |
ye yajanti sadā bhaktyā trikālaṃ nṛtyameva ca  || 78.25 ||
tīrthe nāradanāmākhye revāyāścottare taṭe  |
citrabhānumukhā devāḥ sarvadevamaya ṛṣiḥ  || 78.26 ||
ṛṣiṇā prīṇitāḥ sarve tasmātprītyo hutāśanaḥ  |
pūjite havyavāhe tu dāridryaṃ naiva jāyate  || 78.27 ||
dhanena vipulā prītirjāyate pratijanmani  |
kulīnāśca suveṣāśca sarvakālaṃ dhanena tu  || 78.28 ||
plavo nadīnāṃ patiraṅganānāṃ rājā ca sadvṛttarataḥ prajānām  |
dhanaṃ narāṇāmṛtavastarūṇāṃ gataṃ gataṃ yauvanamānayanti  || 78.29 ||
dhanadatvaṃ dhaneśena tasmiṃstīrthe hyupārjitam  |
yamena ca yamatvaṃ hi indratvaṃ caiva vajriṇā  || 78.30 ||
anyairapi mahīpālaiḥ pārthivatvamupārjitam  |
nāradeśvaramāhātmyāddhruvo niścalatāṃ gataḥ  || 78.31 ||
sarvatīrthavaraṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ nāradena tu  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ revāyāścottare taṭe  |
tadvaraṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  || 78.32 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nāradeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 79

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthadvayamanuttamam  |
dadhiskandaṃ madhuskandaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 79.1 ||
dadhiskande naraḥ snātvā yastu dadyāddvije dadhi  |
upatiṣṭhettatastasya saptajanmani bhārata  || 79.2 ||
na vyādhirna jarā tasya na śoko naiva matsaraḥ  |
daśacandraśataṃ yāvajjāyate vimale kule  || 79.3 ||
madhuskande'pi madhunā miśritānyastilāndadet |
nāsau vaivasvataṃ devaṃ paśyedvai janmasaptatim  || 79.4 ||
madhunā saha sammiśraṃ piṇḍaṃ yastu pradāpayet |
tasya pautraprapautrebhyo dāridryaṃ naiva jāyate  || 79.5 ||
dadhibhiḥ saha saṃmiśraṃ piṇḍaṃ yastu pradāpayet |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā vidhivaddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 79.6 ||
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  |
dvādaśābdāni tuṣyanti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 79.7 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dadhiskandamadhuskandatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 80

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra nandikeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahānandī tatte sarvaṃ vadāmyaham  || 80.1 ||
revāyāṃ purataḥ kṛtvā purā nandī gaṇeśvaraḥ  |
tapastapañjayaṃ kurvaṃstīrthāttīrthaṃ jagāma ha  || 80.2 ||
dadhiskandaṃ madhuskandaṃ yāvattyaktvā tu gacchati  |
tāvattuṣṭo mahādevo nandināthamuvāca ha  || 80.3 ||

īśvara uvāca -
bhobhoḥ prasanno nandīśa varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  |
tapasā tena tuṣṭo'haṃ tīrthayātrākṛtena te  || 80.4 ||

nandīśvara uvāca -
na cāhaṃ kāmaye vittaṃ na cāhaṃ kulasantatim  |
muktvā na kāmaye kāmaṃ tava pādāmbujātparam  || 80.5 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgeṣu tiryagyoniṃ gatasya vā  |
janma janmāntare'pyastu bhaktistvayi mamācalā  || 80.6 ||
tathetyuktvā mahādevaḥ parayā kṛpayā nṛpa  |
gṛhītvā taṃ kare siddhaṃ jagāma nilayaṃ haraḥ  || 80.7 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhaktyā tryakṣaṃ prapūjayet |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 80.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti cet |
śivasyānucaro bhūtvā modate kalpamakṣayam  || 80.9 ||
tataḥ kālena mahatā jāyate vimale kule  |
vedavedāṅgatattvajño jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 80.10 ||
etatte kathitaṃ tāta tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
durlabhaṃ martyasaṃjñasya sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 80.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nandikeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 81

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja varuṇeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahādevo varuṇo nṛpasattama  || 81.1 ||
piṇyākaśākaparṇaiśca kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ  |
ārādhya girijānāthaṃ tataḥ siddhiṃ parāṃ gataḥ  || 81.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjayecchaṅkaraṃ bhaktyā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 81.3 ||
kuṇḍikāṃ vardhanīṃ vāpi mahadvā jalabhājanam  |
annena sahitaṃ pārtha tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 81.4 ||
yatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ satre dvādaśavārṣike  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 81.5 ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāmannadānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam  |
sadyaḥ prītikaraṃ toyamannaṃ ca nṛpasattama  || 81.6 ||
tatratīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
varuṇasya pure vāso yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam  || 81.7 ||
paścātpūrṇe tataḥ kāle martyaloke prajāyate  |
annadānaprado nityaṃ jīvedvarṣaśataṃ naraḥ  || 81.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe varuṇeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 82

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla vahnitīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahātejāstapaḥ kṛtvā hutāśanaḥ  || 82.1 ||
sarvabhakṣyaḥ kṛto yo'sau daṇḍake muninā purā  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya pūto jāto hutāśanaḥ  || 82.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
agnipraveśaṃ kurute sa gacchedagnisāmyatām  || 82.3 ||
bhaktyā snātvā tu yastatra tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalamāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 82.4 ||
tasyaivānantaraṃ rājankauberaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
kubero yatra saṃsiddho yakṣāṇāmadhipaḥ purā  || 82.5 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā samabhyarcya jagadgurum  |
umayā sahitaṃ bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 82.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā dadyādviprāya kāṃcanam  |
nābhimātre jale tiṣṭhansa labhetārbudaṃ phalam  || 82.7 ||
dadhiskande madhuskande nandīśe varuṇālaye  |
āgneye yatphalaṃ tāta snātvā tatphalamāpnuyāt || 82.8 ||
te vandyā mānuṣe loke dhanyāḥ pūrṇamanorathāḥ  |
yaistu dṛṣṭaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ narmadātīrthapañcakam  || 82.9 ||
te yānti bhāskare loke parame duḥkhanāśane  |
bhāskarādaiśvare loke caiśvarādanivartake  || 82.10 ||
nīyate sa pare loke yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  |
tataḥ svargāccyuto martyo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 82.11 ||
sarvarogavinirmukto bhunakti sacarācaram  |
viṣṇuśca devatā yeṣāṃ narmadātīrthasevinām  || 82.12 ||
akhaṇḍitapratāpāste jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
gaṅgā kanakhale puṇyā kurukṣetre sarasvatī  || 82.13 ||
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  |
revātīre vasennityaṃ revātoyaṃ sadā pibet || 82.14 ||
sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu somapānaṃ dine dine  |
gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāśca sarāṃsi ca  |
kalpānte saṃkṣayaṃ yānti na mṛtā tena narmadā  || 82.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dadhiskandādipañcatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvyaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 83

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
brahmahatyāharaṃ proktaṃ revātaṭasamāśrayam  |
hanūmatābhidhaṃ hyatra vidyate liṅgamuttamam  || 83.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
hanūmanteśvaraṃ nāma kathaṃ jātaṃ vadasva me  |
brahmahatyāharaṃ tīrthaṃ revādakṣiṇasaṃsthitam  || 83.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābāho somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇa  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 83.3 ||
tava snehātpravakṣyāmi pīḍito vārddhakena tu  |
pūrvaṃ jātaṃ mahadyuddhaṃ rāmarāvaṇayorapi  || 83.4 ||
pulastyo brahmaṇaḥ putro viśravāstasya vai sutaḥ  |
rāvaṇastena saṃjāto daśāsyo brahmarākṣasaḥ  || 83.5 ||
trailokyavijayī bhūtaḥ prasādācchūlinaḥ sa ca  |
gīrvāṇā vijitāḥ sarve rāmasya gṛhiṇī hṛtā  || 83.6 ||
vāritaḥ kumbhakarṇena sītāṃ mocaya mocaya  |
vibhīṣaṇena vai pāpo mandodaryā punaḥpunaḥ  || 83.7 ||
tvaṃ jitaḥ kārtavīryeṇa raiṇukeyena so'pi ca  |
sa rāmo rāmabhadreṇa tasya saṃkhye kathaṃ jayaḥ  || 83.8 ||

rāvaṇa uvāca -
vānaraiśca narairṛkṣairvarāhaiśca nirāyudhaiḥ  |
devāsurasamūhaiśca na jito'haṃ kadācana  || 83.9 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sugrīvahanumadbhyāṃ ca kumudenāṅgadena ca  |
etairanyaiḥ sahāyaiśca rāmacandreṇa vai jitaḥ  || 83.10 ||
rāmacandreṇa paulastyo hataḥ saṃkhye mahābalaḥ  |
vanaṃ bhagnaṃ hatāḥ śūrāḥ prabhañjanasutena ca  || 83.11 ||
rāvaṇasya suto janye hataścākṣakumārakaḥ  |
āyāmo rakṣasāṃ bhīmaḥ sampiṣṭo vānareṇa tu  || 83.12 ||
evaṃ rāmāyaṇe vṛtte sītāmokṣe kṛte sati  |
ayodhyāṃ tu gate rāme hanumānsa mahākapiḥ  || 83.13 ||
kailāsākhyaṃ gataḥ śailaṃ praṇāmāya maheśituḥ  |
tiṣṭha tiṣṭhetyasau prokto nandinā vānarottamaḥ  || 83.14 ||
brahmahatyāyutastvaṃ hi rākṣasānāṃ vadhena hi  |
bhairavasya sabhā nūnaṃ na draṣṭavyā tvayā kape  || 83.15 ||

hanumānuvāca -
nandinātha haraṃ pṛccha pātakasyopaśāntidam  |
pāpo'haṃ plavago yasmātsaṃjātaḥ kāraṇāntarāt || 83.16 ||

nandyuvāca -
rudradehodbhavā kiṃ te na śrutā bhūtale sthitā  |
śravaṇājjanmajanitaṃ dviguṇaṃ kīrtanādvrajet || 83.17 ||
triṃśajjanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ naśyedrevāvagāhanāt |
tasmāttvaṃ narmadātīraṃ gatvā cara tapo mahat || 83.18 ||
gandharvāhasuto'pyevaṃ nandinoktaṃ niśamya ca  |
prayāto narmadātīramaurvyādakṣiṇasaṅgamam  || 83.19 ||
dadhyau sudakṣiṇe devaṃ virūpākṣaṃ triśūlinam  |
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃyuktaṃ vyālayajñopavītinam  || 83.20 ||
bhasmopacitasarvāṅgaṃ ḍamarusvaranāditam  |
umārddhāṅgaharaṃ śāntaṃ gonāthāsanasaṃsthitam  || 83.21 ||
vatsarān subahūn yāvadupāsāṃcakra īśvaram  |
tāvattuṣṭo mahādeva ājagāma sahomayā  || 83.22 ||
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ meghagambhīranisvanām  |
sādhu sādhvityuvāceśaḥ kaṣṭaṃ vatsa tvayā kṛtam  || 83.23 ||
na ca pūrvaṃ tvayā pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ rāvaṇasaṃkṣaye  |
svāmikāryaratastvaṃ hi siddho'si mama darśanāt || 83.24 ||
hanumāṃśca haraṃ dṛṣṭvā umārddhāṅgaharaṃ sthiram  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato'vocajjaya śambho namo'stu te  |
jayāndhakavināśāya jaya gaṅgāśirodhara  || 83.25 ||
evaṃ stuto mahādevo varado vākyamabravīt |
varaṃ prārthaya me vatsa prāṇasambhavasambhava  || 83.26 ||

śrīhanumānuvāca -
brahmarakṣovadhājjātā mama hatyā maheśvara  |
na pāpo'haṃ bhavedeva yuṣmatsambhāṣaṇe kṣaṇāt || 83.27 ||

īśvara uvāca -
narmadātīrthamāhātmyāddharmayogaprabhāvataḥ  |
manmūrtidarśanātputra niṣpāpo'si na saṃśayaḥ  || 83.28 ||
anyaṃ ca te prayacchāmi varaṃ vānarapuṃgava  |
upakārāya lokānāṃ nāmāni tava mārute  || 83.29 ||
hanūmānaṃ janisuto vāyuputro mahābalaḥ  |
rāmeṣṭaḥ phālguno gotraḥ piṅgākṣo'mitavikramaḥ  || 83.30 ||
udadhikramaṇaśreṣṭho daśagrīvasya darpahā  |
lakṣmaṇaprāṇadātā ca sītāśokanivartanaḥ  || 83.31 ||
ityuktvāntardadhe deva umayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
hanūmānīśvaraṃ tatra sthāpayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ  || 83.32 ||
ātmayogabalenaiva brahmacaryaprabhāvataḥ  |
īśvarasya prasādena liṅgaṃ kāmapradaṃ hi tat |
acchedyamapratarkyaṃ ca vināśotpattivarjitam  || 83.33 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
hanūmanteśvare putra pratyakṣapratyayaṃ śṛṇu  |
yadvṛttaṃ dvāparasyādau tretānte pāṇḍunandana  || 83.34 ||
suparvā nāma bhūpālo babhūva vasudhātale  |
tasya rājñaḥ sadā saukhyaṃ narā dīrghāyuṣaḥ sadā  || 83.35 ||
sa putradhanasaṃyuktaścauropadravavarjitaḥ  |
śatabāhurbabhūvāsya putro bhīmaparākramaḥ  || 83.36 ||
āsakto'sau sadā kālaṃ pāpadharmairnareśvara  |
aṭāṭyata dharāṃ sarvāṃ parvatāṃśca vanāni ca  || 83.37 ||
vadhārthaṃ mṛgayūthānāmāgato vindhyaparvatam  |
tarujātisamākīrṇe hastiyūthasamācite  || 83.38 ||
siṃhacitrakaśobhāḍhye mṛgavārāhasaṃkule  |
krīḍitvā sa vane rājā narmadāmānataḥ kvacit || 83.39 ||
hanūmantavane prāptaḥ śatakrośapramāṇake  |
ciñciṇīvanaśobhāḍhye kadambatarusaṃkule  || 83.40 ||
nityaṃ pālāśajambīraiḥ karaṃjakhadiraistathā  |
pāṭalairbadarairyuktaiḥ śamītindukaśobhitam  || 83.41 ||
mṛgayūthaiḥ samāchannaśikhaṇḍisvaranāditam  |
pārāvatakasaṅghānāṃ samantātsvaraśobhitam  || 83.42 ||
śaratkāle'ramadrājā bahule cāśvinasya saḥ  |
vanamadhyaṃ gato'drākṣīdbhramantaṃ piṅgaladvijam  || 83.43 ||
pustikākarasaṃsthaṃ ca papraccha capalaṃ dvijam  || 83.44 ||

śatabāhuruvāca -
ekākī tvaṃ vane kasmādbhramase pustikākaraḥ  |
itastato'pi sampaśyan kathayasva dvijottama  || 83.45 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
kānyakubjātsamāyātaḥ preṣito rājakanyayā  |
asthikṣepāya vai rājanhanūmanteśvare jale  || 83.46 ||

rājovāca -
asthikṣepo jale kasmāddhanūmanteśvare dvija  |
kriyate kena kāryeṇa sāścaryaṃ kathyatāṃ mama  || 83.47 ||
suparvaṇaḥ suto yānaṃ tyaktvā bhūmau praṇamya ca  |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā brāhmaṇāya nareśvara  |
samastaṃ kathayāmāsa vṛttāntaṃ svaṃ purātanam  || 83.48 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
śikhaṇḍī nāma rājāsti kanyakubje pratāpavān  |
aputro'sau mahīpālaḥ kanyā jātā manorathaiḥ  || 83.49 ||
jātismarā sucārvaṅgī narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  |
pitrā ca saikadā kanyā vivāhāya prajalpitā  || 83.50 ||
anitye putri saṃsāre kanyādānaṃ dadāmyaham  |
śvaḥkṛtyamadya kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇikam  |
na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtaṃ cāsya na cākṛtam  || 83.51 ||

kanyovāca -
iccheyaṃ yatra kale hi tatra deyā tvayā pituḥ  |
putrīvākyādasau rājā vismito vākyamabravīt || 83.52 ||

śikhaṇḍyuvāca -
kathyatāṃ me mahābhāge sāścaryaṃ bhāṣitaṃ tvayā  |
piturvākyena sā bālottamā hyāgatāntikam  || 83.53 ||
kathayāmāsa yadvṛttaṃ hanūmanteśvare nṛpa  |
kalāpinī hyahaṃ tāta yutā bhartrāvasaṃ tadā  || 83.54 ||
revaurvyāsaṅgamantisthā revāyā dakṣiṇe taṭe  |
hanūmantavane puṇye cikrīḍāhaṃ yadṛcchayā  || 83.55 ||
bhartṛyuktā ca saṃsuptā rajanyāṃ sarale nage  |
āgatā lubdhakāstatra kṣudhārtā vanamuttamam  || 83.56 ||
bhartṛyogayutā pāpairdṛṣṭāhaṃ vadhacintakaiḥ  |
pāśabandhaṃ samādāya baddhāhaṃ svāminā saha  || 83.57 ||
grīvāṃ te moṭayāmāsuḥ picchāchoṭanakaṃ kṛtam  |
hutāśanamukhe taistu saha kāntena lubdhakaiḥ  || 83.58 ||
paribharjyāvayormāṃsaṃ bhakṣayitvā yatheṣṭataḥ  |
suptāḥ svasthendriyā rātrau sā gatā śarvarī kṣayam  || 83.59 ||
prabhāte māṃsaśeṣaṃ ca jambukairgṛdhraghātibhiḥ  |
maccharīrodbhavaṃ cāsthi snāyumāṃsena cāvṛtam  || 83.60 ||
gṛhītaṃ ghātinaikena cākāśātpatitaṃ tadā  |
taṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā pare pakṣiṇa āgatāḥ  || 83.61 ||
dṛṣṭvā pakṣisamūhaṃ tu asthikhaṇḍaṃ vyasarjayat |
vihagānāṃ samastānāṃ dhāvatāṃ caiva paśyatām  || 83.62 ||
patitaṃ narmadātoye hanūmanteśvare nṛpa  |
madīyamasthikhaṇḍaṃ ca patitaṃ narmadājale  || 83.63 ||
tasya tīrthasya puṇyena jātāhaṃ putrikā tava  |
bhūpakanyā tvahaṃ jātā pūrṇacandranibhānanā  || 83.64 ||
jātismarā narendrasya saṃjātā bhavataḥ kule  |
tasmādvivāhaṃ necchāmi mama bhartā nṛpottama  || 83.65 ||
viṣame vartate'dyāpi śakuntamṛgajātiṣu  |
tasyāsthiśeṣaṃ rājendra tasmiṃstīrthe bhaviṣyati  || 83.66 ||
tatkṣepaṇārthaṃ vai tāta preṣayādya dvijottamam  |
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kāraṇaṃ nṛpasattama  || 83.67 ||
madbhartā viṣame sthāne śakuntamṛgajātiṣu  |
yadi preṣayase tāta kaṃcittvaṃ narmadātaṭe  || 83.68 ||
tasyāhaṃ kathayiṣyāmi sthānaiścihnaiśca lakṣitam  |
śikhaṇḍināpyahaṃ tatra hyāhūto hyavanīpate  || 83.69 ||
dāsyāmi viṃśatigrāmāngaccha tvaṃ narmadātaṭe  |
preṣaṇaṃ me pratijñātamalakṣmyā pīḍitena tu  || 83.70 ||

kanyovāca -
gaccha tvaṃ narmadāṃ puṇyāṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarīm  |
āgneyyāṃ somanāthasya hanūmanteśvaraḥ paraḥ  || 83.71 ||
ardhakrośena revāyā vistīrṇo vaṭapādapaḥ  |
karaṃjaḥ kaṭahaścaiva sannidhāne vaṭasya ca  || 83.72 ||
nyagrodhamūlasāṃnidhye sūkṣmānyasthīni drakṣyasi  |
samūhya tāni saṃgṛhya gaccha revāṃ dvijottama  || 83.73 ||
āśvinasyāsite pakṣe tripurāristu vai tithau  |
snāpya triśūlinaṃ bhaktyā rātrau tvaṃ kuru jāgaram  || 83.74 ||
kṣipeḥ prabhāte tāni tvaṃ nābhimātrajalasthitaḥ  |
ityuccārya dvijaśreṣṭha vimuktistasya jāyatām  || 83.75 ||
kṣiptvāsthīni punaḥ snānaṃ kartavyaṃ tvaghanāśanam  |
evaṃ kṛte tu rājendra gatistasya bhaviṣyati  || 83.76 ||
kathitaṃ kanyayā yacca tatsarvaṃ pustikākṛtam  |
āgato'haṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha tīrthe'tra duritāpahe  || 83.77 ||
so'bhijñānaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā nītvāsthīni nareśvara  |
pūrvoktena vidhānena prākṣipaṃ nārmadā masipuṣpavṛṣṭiḥ'śu sādhu sādhviti pāṇḍava  |
vimānaṃ ca tato divyamāgataṃ barhiṇastadā  || 83.78 ||
divyarūpadharo bhūtvā gato nāke kalāpavān  |
evaṃ tu pratyayaṃ dṛṣṭvā hanūmanteśvare nṛpa  || 83.79 ||
cakārānaśanaṃ vipraḥ śatabāhuśca bhūpatiḥ  |
śoṣayāmāsatustau svamīśvarārādhane ratau  || 83.80 ||
dhyāyantau tasthaturdevaṃ śatabāhudvijottamau  |
māsārdhena mṛto rājā śatabāhurmahāmanāḥ  || 83.81 ||
kiṅkaṇījālaśobhāḍhyaṃ vimānaṃ tatra cāgatam  |
sādhu sādhu nṛpaśreṣṭha vimānārohaṇaṃ kuru  || 83.82 ||

śatabāhuruvāca -
nāyāmi svargamārgāgraṃ vipro yāvanna saṃsthitaḥ  |
upadeśaprado mahyaṃ gururūpī dvijottamaḥ  || 83.83 ||
apsarasa ūcuḥ  |
lobhāvṛto hyayaṃ vipro lobhātpāpasya saṃgrahaḥ  |
hanūmanteśvare rājanye mṛtāḥ sattvamāsthitāḥ  || 83.84 ||
te yānti śāṃkare loke sarvapāpakṣayaṃkare  |
naiva pāpakṣayaścāsya brāhmaṇasya nareśvara  || 83.85 ||
gṛhaṃ ca gṛhiṇī citte brāhmaṇasya pravartate  |
śatabāhustato vipramuvāca vinayānvitaḥ  || 83.86 ||
tyaja mūlamanarthasya lobhamenaṃ dvijottama  |
ityuktvā svaryayau rājā svargakanyāsamāvṛtaḥ  || 83.87 ||
dinaiḥ kaiścidgato vipraḥ svargaṃ vaitālikairvṛtaḥ  |
barhī ca kāśīrājasya putrastīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 83.88 ||
ātmānaṃ kanyayā dattaṃ pūrvajanma vyacintayan  |
sā ca taṃ prauḍhamālokya piturājñāmavāpya ca  |
svayaṃvare svabhartāraṃ lebhe sādhvī nṛpātmajam  || 83.89 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etadvṛttāntamabhavattasmiṃstīrthe nṛpottama  |
etasmātkāraṇānmedhyaṃ tīrthametatsadā nṛpa  || 83.90 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ sarvakālaṃ nareśvara  |
viśeṣāccāśvine māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  || 83.91 ||
snāpayedīśvaraṃ bhaktyā kṣaudrakṣīreṇa sarpiṣā  |
dadhnā ca khaṇḍayuktena kuśatoyena vai punaḥ  || 83.92 ||
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena guṇṭhayecca maheśvaram  |
tataḥ sugandhapuṣpaiśca bilvapatraiśca pūjayet || 83.93 ||
mucakundena kadena jātīkāśakuśodbhavaiḥ  |
unmattamunipuṣpaughaiḥ puṣpaistatkālasambhavaiḥ  || 83.94 ||
arcayetparayā bhaktyā hanūmanteśvaraṃ śivam  |
ghṛtena dāpayeddīpaṃ tailena tadabhāvataḥ  || 83.95 ||
śrāddhaṃ ca kārayettatra brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaiḥ kulīnairgṛhapālakaiḥ  || 83.96 ||
tarpayedbrāhmaṇān bhaktyā vasanānnahiraṇyataḥ  |
narakasthā divaṃ yāntu procyeti praṇameddvijān  || 83.97 ||
patitān varjayedviprān vṛṣalī yasya gehinī  |
svavṛṣaṃ cāparityajya vṛṣairanyairvṛṣāyate  || 83.98 ||
vṛṣalīṃ tāṃ vidurdevā na śūdrī vṛṣalī bhavet |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ gurudāraniṣevaṇam  || 83.99 ||
suvarṇaharaṇanyāsamitradrohodbhavaṃ tathā  |
naśyate pātakaṃ sarvamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 83.100 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
vākpralāpena bho vatsa bahunoktena kiṃ mayā  |
sarvapātakasaṃyukto dadyāddānaṃ dvijanmane  || 83.101 ||
godānaṃ ca prakartavyamasmiṃstīrthe viśeṣataḥ  |
godānaṃ hi yataḥ pārtha sarvadānādhikaṃ smṛtam  || 83.102 ||
sarvadevamayā gāvaḥ sarve devāstadātmakāḥ  |
śṛṅgāgreṣu mahīpāla śakro vasati nityaśaḥ  || 83.103 ||
uraḥ skandaḥ śiro brahmā lalāṭe vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ  |
candrārkau locane devau jihvāyāṃ ca sarasvatī  || 83.104 ||
marudgaṇāḥ sadā sādhyā yasyā dantā nareśvara  |
huṅkāre caturo vedān vidyātsāṅgapadakramān  || 83.105 ||
ṛṣayo romakūpeṣu hyasaṃkhyātāstapasvinaḥ  |
daṇḍahasto mahākāyaḥ kṛṣṇo mahiṣavāhanaḥ  || 83.106 ||
yamaḥ pṛṣṭhasthito nityaṃ śubhāśubhaparīkṣakaḥ  |
catvāraḥ sāgarāḥ puṇyāḥ kṣīradhārāḥ staneṣu ca  || 83.107 ||
viṣṇupādodbhavā gaṅgā darśanātpāpanāśanī  |
prasrāve saṃsthitā yasmāttasmādvandyā sadā budhaiḥ  || 83.108 ||
lakṣmīśca gomaye nityaṃ pavitrā sarvamaṅgalā  |
gomayālepanaṃ tasmātkartavyaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 83.109 ||
gandharvāpsaraso nāgāḥ khurāgreṣu vyavasthitāḥ  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ yāni tīrthāni bhārata  |
tāni sarvāṇi jānīyādgaurgavyaṃ tena pāvanam  || 83.110 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
sarvadevamayī dhenurgīrvāṇādyairalaṃkṛtā  |
etatkathaya me tāta kasmādgoṣu samāśritāḥ  || 83.111 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sarvadevamayo viṣṇurgāvo viṣṇuśarīrajāḥ  |
devāstadubhayāttasmātkalpitā vividhā janaiḥ  || 83.112 ||
śvetā vā kapilā vāpi kṣīriṇī pāṇḍunandana  |
savatsā ca suśīlā ca sitavastrāvaguṇṭhitā  || 83.113 ||
kāṃsyadohanikā deyā svarṇaśṛṅgī subhūṣitā  |
hanūmanteśvarasyāgre bhaktyā viprāya dāpayet || 83.114 ||
niyamasthena sā deyā svargamānantyamicchatā  |
asamarthāya ye dadyurviṣṇuloke prayānti te  || 83.115 ||
asau loke cyuto rājanbhūtale dvijamandire  |
kuśalo jāyate putro guṇavidyādhanarddhimān  || 83.116 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ hanūmanteśvaraṃ nṛpa  |
śṛṇvanvimucyate pāpādvarṇasaṃkarasaṃbhavāt || 83.117 ||
dūrasthaścintayan paśyanmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 83.118 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe hanūmanteśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tryaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 84

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
atraivodāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
kailāse pṛcchate bhaktyā ṣaṇmukhāya śivoditam  || 84.1 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pūrvaṃ tretāyuge skanda hato rāmeṇa rāvaṇaḥ  |
caturdaśa tadā koṭyo nihatā brahmarakṣasām  || 84.2 ||
hateṣu teṣu vai tatra rakṣaṇāya divaukasām  |
mahānandastadā jātastriṣu lokeṣu putraka  || 84.3 ||
tataḥ sītāṃ samāsādya samaṃ vānarapuṃgavaiḥ  |
rāmo'pyayodhyāmāyāto bharatena kṛtotsavaḥ  |
tasmai samarpayāmāsa sa rājyaṃ lakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ  || 84.4 ||
tasminpraśāsati tato rājyaṃ nihatakaṇṭakam  |
kṛtakāryo'tha hanumānkailāsamagātpurā  || 84.5 ||
tato nandī pratīhāro rudrāṃśamapi taṃ kapim  |
na ca saṃgamayāmāsa rudreṇāghaughahāriṇā  || 84.6 ||
tena pṛṣṭastadā nandī kiṃ mayā pātakaṃ kṛtam  |
yena rudravapuḥ puṇyaṃ na paśyāmyambikānvitam  || 84.7 ||

nandyuvāca -
tvayāvataraṇaṃ cakre kapīndrāmarahetunā  |
tathāpi hi kṛtaṃ pāpamupabhogena śāmyati  || 84.8 ||

hanumānuvāca -
kiṃ mayākāri tatpāpaṃ nandindevārthakāriṇā  |
rākṣasāśca hatā duṣṭā viprayajñāṅgaghātinaḥ  || 84.9 ||
tatastadālāpakutūhalī haro nijāṃśabhājaṃ kapimugratejasam  |
uvāca dvārāntaradattadṛṣṭiḥ puraḥsthitaṃ prekṣya kapīśvaraṃ punaḥ  || 84.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
gaṅgā gayā kape revā yamunā ca sarasvatī  |
sarvapāpaharā nadyastāsu snānaṃ samācara  || 84.11 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
somanāthasamīpasthaṃ tatra tvaṃ gaccha vānara  || 84.12 ||
tatra snātvā mahāpāpaṃ gamiṣyati mamājñayā  |
utpatya vegāddhanumāñchrīrevādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 84.13 ||
jagāma sumahānādastapaścakre suduṣkaram  |
tasya vai tapyamānasya rakṣovadhakṛtaṃ tamaḥ  || 84.14 ||
vilīnaṃ pārtha kālena kiyateśaprasādataḥ  |
tato devaiḥ samaṃ devastattīrthamagamaddharaḥ  || 84.15 ||
kapimāliṅgayāmāsa varaṃ tasmai pradattavān  |
adyaprabhṛti te tīrthaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ  || 84.16 ||
kapitīrthaṃ tato jātaṃ tasthau tatra svayaṃ haraḥ  |
hanūmanteśvaro nāmnā sarvahatyāharastadā  || 84.17 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhaktyā liṅgaṃ prapūjayet |
sarvapāpāni naśyanti harasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 84.18 ||
tatrāsthīni vilīyante piṇḍadāne'kṣayā gatiḥ  |
yatkiṃciddīyate tatra taddhi koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 84.19 ||
hanumānapyayodhyāyāṃ rāmaṃ draṣṭumathāgamat |
cakāra kuśalapraśnaṃ svasvarūpaṃ nyavedayat || 84.20 ||

śrīrāma uvāca -
kurvato devakāryaṃ te mama kāryaṃ ca kurvataḥ  |
tato'hamapi pāpīyāṃstapastapsyāmyasaṃśayam  || 84.21 ||
tatraiva dakṣiṇe kūle revāyāḥ pāpahāriṇi  |
caturviṃśativarṣāṇi tapastepe'tha rāghavaḥ  || 84.22 ||
jyotiṣmatīpurīsaṃsthaḥ śrīrevāsnānamācaran  |
tasya śuśrūṣaṇaṃ cakre lakṣmaṇo'pi tadājñayā  || 84.23 ||
sthāpayāmāsaturliṅge tau tadā rāmalakṣmaṇau  |
prabhāvātsatyatapaso revātīre mahāmatī  |
niṣpāpatāṃ tadā vīrau jagmatū rāmalakṣmaṇau  || 84.24 ||
tatastadā devapurogamo haro gato hi vai puṇyamunīśvaraiḥ saha  |
āgatya tīrthaṃ ca varaṃ dadau tadā nijāṃ kalāṃ tatra vimucya tīrthe  || 84.25 ||
munibhiḥ sarvatīrthānāṃ kṣiptaṃ kumbhodakaṃ bhuvi  |
ekasthaṃ liṅganāmātha kalākumbhastathābhavat || 84.26 ||
kumbheśvara iti khyātastadā devagaṇārcitaḥ  |
rāmo'pi pūjayāmāsa talliṅgaṃ devasevivatam  || 84.27 ||
tato varaṃ dadau devo rāmakīrtyabhivṛddhaye  |
caturviṃśatime varṣe rāmo niṣpāpatāṃ gataḥ  || 84.28 ||
yadā kanyāgataḥ paṅgurguruṇā sahito bhavet |
tadeva devayātreyamiti devā jagurmudā  || 84.29 ||
yathā godāvarītīrthe sarvatīrthaphalaṃ bhavet |
tathātra revāsnānena liṅgānāṃ darśanairnḥṇām  || 84.30 ||
kariṣyantyatra ye śrāddhaṃ pitḥṇāṃ narmadātaṭe  |
kumbheśvarasamīpasthāstatphalaṃ śṛṇu ṣaṇmukha  || 84.31 ||
yāvanto romakūpāḥ syuḥ śarīre sarvadehinām  |
tāvadvarṣapramāṇena pitḥṇāmakṣayā gatiḥ  || 84.32 ||
pṛthivyāṃ devatāḥ sarvāḥ sarvatīrthāni yāni tu  |
labhante tatphalaṃ martyā liṅgatrayavilokanāt || 84.33 ||
aputro labhate putraṃ nirdhano dhanamāpnuyāt |
sarogo mucyate rogānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 84.34 ||
siṃharāśiṃ gate jīve yatsyādgodāvarīphalam  |
taddvādaśaguṇaṃ skanda kumbheśvarasamīpataḥ  || 84.35 ||
ye jānanti na paśyanti kumbhaśambhumumāpatim  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle teṣāṃ janma nirarthakam  || 84.36 ||
yathā godāvarīyātrā kartavyā muniśāsanāt |
caturviṃśatime varṣe tatheyaṃ devabhāṣitam  || 84.37 ||
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca yāvadvai divi tārakaḥ  |
tāvattadakṣayaṃ dānaṃ revākumbheśvarāntike  || 84.38 ||
mahādānāni deyāni tatra laukairvicakṣaṇaiḥ  |
godānamatra śaṃsanti sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tathā  || 84.39 ||
yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa naśyate pāpasañcayaḥ  |
snānena kiṃ punaḥ skanda brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 84.40 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā śrāddhaṃ kuryādyudhiṣṭhira  |
ekottaraṃ kulaśatamuddharecchivaśāsanāt || 84.41 ||
yāni kāni ca tīrthāni cāsamudrasarāṃsi ca  |
śivaliṅgārcanasyeha kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 84.42 ||
evaṃ devā varaṃ dattvā harīśvarapurogamāḥ  |
svasthānamagaman pūrvaṃ muktvā tannāma cottamam  || 84.43 ||
tīrthasyāsya varaṃ dattvā sa rāmo lakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ  |
ayodhyāṃ praviveśāsau niṣpāpo narmadājalāt || 84.44 ||
sauvarṇīṃ ca tataḥ kṛtvā sītāṃ yajñaṃ cakāra saḥ  |
anumantrya munīṃllokāndevatāśca nijaṃ kulam  || 84.45 ||
purā tretāyuge jātaṃ tattīrthaṃ skandanāmakam  |
niyamena tato lokaiḥ kartavyaṃ liṅgadarśanam  || 84.46 ||
tāvatpāpāni deheṣu mahāpātakajānyapi  |
yāvanna prekṣate jantustattīrthaṃ devasevitam  || 84.47 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
jyotiṣmatīpurīsaṃsthaṃ ye drakṣyanti haraṃ param  || 84.48 ||
tasmānmohaṃ parityajya janairgantavyamādarāt |
tīrthāśeṣaphalāvāptyai tīrthaṃ kumbheśvarāhvayam  || 84.49 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śrutveti śambhuvacasā sa ṣaḍānano'tha natvā pituḥ padayugāmbujamādareṇa  |
samprāpya dakṣiṇataṭaṃ giriśasravantyāḥ kīśāgryarāmakalaśākhyaśivān dadarśa  || 84.50 ||
|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kapitīrtharāmeśvaralakṣmaṇeśvarakumbheśvaramāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 85

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra narmadāyāḥ purātanam  |
brahmahatyāharaṃ tīrthaṃ vārāṇasyā samaṃ hi tat || 85.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryaṃ kathyatāṃ brahmanyadvṛttaṃ narmadātaṭe  |
vārāṇasyā samaṃ kasmādetatkathaya me prabho  || 85.2 ||
nimagno duḥkhasaṃsāre hṛtarājyo dvijottama  |
yuṣmadvāṇījalasnāto nirduḥkhaḥ saha bāndhavaiḥ  || 85.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābāho somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇa  |
pṛṣṭo'smi durlabhaṃ tīrthaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ param  || 85.4 ||
ādau pitāmahastāvatsamastajagataḥ prabhuḥ  |
manasā tasya saṃjātā daśaiva ṛṣipuṃgavāḥ  || 85.5 ||
marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum  |
pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradameva ca  || 85.6 ||
jajñe prācetasaṃ dakṣaṃ mahātejāḥ prajāpatiḥ  |
dakṣasyāpi tathā jātāḥ pañcāśadduhitāḥ kila  || 85.7 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa  |
tathaiva sa mahābhāgaḥ saptaviṃśatimindave  || 85.8 ||
rohiṇī nāma yā tāsāmabhīṣṭā sābhavadvidhoḥ  |
śeṣāsu karuṇāṃ kṛtvā śapto dakṣeṇa candramāḥ  || 85.9 ||
kṣayarogyabhavaccandro dakṣasyāyaṃ prajāpateḥ  |
sa ca śāpaprabhāveṇa nistejāḥ śarvarīpatiḥ  || 85.10 ||
gataḥ pitāmahaṃ somo vepamāno'mṛtāṃśumān  |
padmayone namastubhyaṃ vedagarbha namo'stu te  |
śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ prasanno'smi pāhi māṃ kamalāsana  || 85.11 ||

brahmovāca -
nistejāḥ śarvarīnātha kalāhīnaśca dṛśyase  |
udvignamānasastāta saṃjātaḥ kena hetunā  || 85.12 ||

soma uvāca -
dakṣaśāpena me brahmannistejastvaṃ jagatpate  |
nirhāraścāsya śāpasya kathyatāṃ me pitāmaha  || 85.13 ||

brahmovāca -
sarvatra sulabhā revā triṣu sthāneṣu durlabhā  |
oṅkāre'tha bhṛgukṣetre tathā caivaurvasaṃgame  || 85.14 ||
tatra gaccha kṣapānātha yatra revāntaraṃ taṭam  |
tvarito'sau gatastatra yatra revaurvisaṃgamaḥ  || 85.15 ||
kāṣṭhāvasthaḥ sthitaḥ somo dadhyau tripuravairiṇam  |
yāvadvarṣaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ tāvattuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 85.16 ||
pratyakṣaḥ somarājasya vṛṣāsana umāpatiḥ  |
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatyoccairjaya śambho namo'stu te  || 85.17 ||
jaya śaṅkara pāpaharāya namo jaya īśvara te jagadīśa namaḥ  |
jaya vāsukibhūṣaṇadhāra namo jaya śūlakapāladharāya namaḥ  || 85.18 ||
jaya andhakadehavināśa namo jaya dānavavṛndavadhāya namaḥ  |
jaya niṣkalarūpa sakalāya namo jaya kāla kāmadahāya namaḥ  || 85.19 ||
jaya mecakakaṇṭhadharāya namo jaya sūkṣmanirañjanaśabda namaḥ  |
jaya ādiranādirananta namo jaya śaṅkara kiṃkaramīśa bhaja  || 85.20 ||
evaṃ stuto mahādevaḥ somarājena pāṇḍava  |
tuṣṭastasya nṛpaśreṣṭha śivayā śaṅkaro'bravīt || 85.21 ||

īśvara uvāca -
varaṃ prārthaya me bhadra yatte manasi vartate  |
sādhu sādhu mahāsattva tuṣṭo'haṃ tapasā tava  || 85.22 ||

soma uvāca -
dakṣaśāpena dagdho'haṃ kṣīṇasattvo maheśvara  |
śāpasyopaśamaṃ deva kuru śarma mama prabho  || 85.23 ||

īśvara uvāca -
tava bhaktigṛhīto'hamumayā saha toṣitaḥ  |
niṣpāpaḥ somanāthastvaṃ saṃjātastīrthasevanāt || 85.24 ||
ityūce devadeveśaḥ kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvendunā tataḥ  |
sthāpitaṃ paramaṃ liṅgaṃ kāmadaṃ prāṇināṃ bhuvi  |
sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ tattu brahmahatyāvināśanam  || 85.25 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
somanāthaprabhāvaṃ me saṃkṣepātkathaya prabho  |
duḥkhārṇavanimagnānāṃ trātā prāpto dvijottama  || 85.26 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇu tīrthaprabhāvaṃ te saṃkṣepātkathayāmyaham  |
yadvṛttamuttare kūle revāyā urisaṃgame  || 85.27 ||
śambaro nāma rājābhūttasya putrastrilocanaḥ  |
trilocanasutaḥ kaṇvaḥ sa pāparddhiparo'bhavat || 85.28 ||
vane nityaṃ bhramanso'tha mṛgayūthaṃ dadarśa ha  |
mṛgayūthaṃ hataṃ tattu trilocanasutena ca  || 85.29 ||
mṛgarūpī dvijo madhye carate nirjane vane  |
sa hatastena saṅgena kaṇvena munisattama  || 85.30 ||
brahmahatyānvitaḥ kaṇvo nistejā vyacaranmahīm  |
vyacaraṃścaiva samprāpto narmadāmurisaṃgame  || 85.31 ||
kiṃśukāśokabahale jambīrapanasākule  |
kadambapāṭalākīrṇe bilvanāraṅgaśobhite  || 85.32 ||
ciñciṇīcampakopete hyagastitaruchādite  |
prabhūtabhūtasaṃyuktaṃ vanaṃ sarvatra śobhitam  || 85.33 ||
citrakairmṛgamārjārairhiṃsraiḥ śambaraśūkaraiḥ  |
śaśairgavayasaṃyuktaiḥ śikhaṇḍikharamaṇḍitam  || 85.34 ||
praviṣṭastu vane kaṇvastṛṣārtaḥ śramapīḍitaḥ  |
snāto revājale puṇye saṅgame pāpanāśane  || 85.35 ||
arcitaḥ parayā bhaktyā somanātho yudhiṣṭhira  |
papau suvimalaṃ toyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 85.36 ||
phalāni ca vicitrāṇi cakhāda saha kiṃkaraiḥ  |
suptaḥ pādapacchāyāyāṃ śrānto mṛgavadhena ca  || 85.37 ||
tāvattīrthavaraṃ vipraḥ snānārthaṃ saṅgamaṃ gataḥ  |
mārgago brāhmaṇo harṣodyuktastadgatamānasaḥ  || 85.38 ||
abalā tamuvācedaṃ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha dvijottama  |
trasto nirīkṣate yāvaddiśaḥ sarvā nareśvara  || 85.39 ||
tāvadvṛkṣasamārūḍhāṃ striyaṃ raktāmbarāvṛtām  |
raktamālyāṃ tadā bālāṃ raktacandanacarcitām  |
raktābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāṃ pāśahastāṃ dadarśa ha  || 85.40 ||

stryuvāca -
saṃdeśaṃ śrūyatāṃ vipra yadi gacchasi saṅgame  |
madbhartā tiṣṭhate tatra śīghrameva visarjaya  || 85.41 ||
ekākinī ca te bhāryā tiṣṭhate vanamadhyagā  |
ityākarṇya gato vipraḥ saṅgamaṃ suradurlabhe  || 85.42 ||
vṛkṣacchāyānvitaḥ kaṇvo brāhmaṇenāvalokitaḥ  |
uvāca taṃ prati tadā vacanaṃ brāhmaṇottamaḥ  || 85.43 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
vanāntare mayā dṛṣṭā bālā kamalalocanā  |
raktāmbaradharā tanvī raktacandanacarcitā  || 85.44 ||
raktamālyā suśobhāḍhyā pāśahastā mṛgekṣaṇā  |
vṛkṣārūḍhāvadadvākyaṃ madbhartā preṣyatāmiti  || 85.45 ||

kaṇva uvāca -
kasminsthāne tu viprendra vidyate mṛgalocanā  |
kasya sā kena kāryeṇa sarvametadvadāśu me  || 85.46 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
saṅgamādardhakrośe sā udyānānte hi vidyate  |
vacanādbrahmaṇasyaiṣā na jñātā pārthivena tu  || 85.47 ||
tadā sa kaṇvabhūpālaḥ svakaṃ dūtaṃ samādiśat |

kaṇva uvāca -
gaccha tvaṃ pṛcchatāṃ tāṃ kvāgatā kvaca gamiṣyasi  |
preṣitastvarito dūto gato nārīsamīpataḥ  || 85.48 ||
vṛkṣasthāṃ dadṛśo bālāmuvāca nṛpasattama  |
mannāthaḥ pṛcchati tvāṃ tu kāsi tvaṃ kva gamiṣyasi  || 85.49 ||

kanyovāca -
gururātmavatāṃ śāstā rājā śāstā durātmanām  |
iha pracchanna pāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ  || 85.50 ||
brahmahatyā ca saṃjātā mṛgarūpadharadvijāt |
mayā yukto'pi te rājā muktastīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 85.51 ||
ardhakrośāntarānmadhye brahmahatyā na saṃviśet |
somanāthaprabhāvo'yaṃ vārāṇasyāḥ samaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 85.52 ||
gaccha tvaṃ preṣyatāṃ rājā śīghramatra na saṃśayaḥ  |
gato bhṛtyastataḥ śīghraṃ vepamānaḥ suvihvalaḥ  || 85.53 ||
samastaṃ kathayāmāsa yadvṛttaṃ hi purātanam  |
tasya vākyādasau rājā patito dharaṇītale  || 85.54 ||

bhṛtya uvāca -
kasmāttvaṃ śocase nātha pūrvopāttaṃ śubhāśubham  |
ityākarṇya vacastasya rājā vacanamabravīt || 85.55 ||
prāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyāmi somanāthasamīpataḥ  |
śīghramānīyatāṃ vahnirindhanāni bahūni ca  || 85.56 ||
ānītaṃ tatkṣaṇātsarvaṃ bhṛtyaistadvaśavartibhiḥ  |
snānaṃ kṛtvā śubhe toye saṅgame pāpanāśane  || 85.57 ||
arcitaḥ parayā bhaktyā somanātho mahībhṛtā  |
triḥpradakṣiṇataḥ kṛtvā jvalantaṃ jātavedasam  || 85.58 ||
praviṣṭaḥ kaṇvarājāsau hṛdi dhyātvā janārdanam  |
pītāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇam  || 85.59 ||
śriyā yuktaṃ suparṇasthaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  |
surārisūdanaṃ dadhyau sugatirme bhavatviti  || 85.60 ||
papāta puṣpavṛṣṭistu sādhu sādhu nṛpātmaja  |
āścaryamatulaṃ dṛṣṭvā nirīkṣya ca parasparam  || 85.61 ||
mṛtaṃ taiḥ pāvake bhṛtyairhṛdi dhyātvā gadādharam  |
vimānasthāstataḥ sarve saṃjātāḥ pāṇḍunandana  || 85.62 ||
niṣpāpāste divaṃ yātāḥ somanāthaprabhāvataḥ  |
brāhmaṇe saṅgame tatra dhyāyamāne vṛṣadhvajam  || 85.63 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
somanāthaprabhāvo'yaṃ śṛṇuṣvaikamanā vidhim  |
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ sarvakālaṃ raverdine  || 85.64 ||
viśeṣācchuklapakṣe cetsūryavāreṇa saptamī  |
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  || 85.65 ||
pañcāmṛtena gavyena snāpayetparameśvaram  |
śrīkhaṇḍena tato guṇṭhya puṣpadhūpādikaṃ dadet || 85.66 ||
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpaṃ nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ ca kārayet |
somavāre tathāṣṭamyāṃ prabhāte pūjayeddvijān  || 85.67 ||
jitakrodhānātmavataḥ paranindāvivarjitān  |
sarvāṅgarucirāñchastān svadāraparipālakān  || 85.68 ||
gāyatrīpāṭhamātrāṃśca vikarmaviratān sadā  |
punarbhūvṛṣalīśūdrī careyuryasya mandire  || 85.69 ||
dūrato'sau dvijastyājya ātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  |
hīnāṅgānatiriktāṅgān yeṣāṃ pūrvāparaṃ na hi  || 85.70 ||
vrate śrāddhe tathā dāne dūratastān vivarjayet |
āyasī taruṇī tulyā dvijāḥ svādhyāyavarjitāḥ  || 85.71 ||
ātmānaṃ saha yājyena pātayanti na saṃśayaḥ  |
śālmalīnāvatulyāḥ syuḥ ṣaṭkarmaniratā dvijāḥ  || 85.72 ||
dātāraṃ ca tathātmānaṃ tārayanti taranti ca  |
śrāddhaṃ someśvare pārtha yaḥ kuryādgatamatsaraḥ  || 85.73 ||
pretāstasya hi suprītā yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
annaṃ vastraṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca yo dadyādagrajanmane  || 85.74 ||
sa yāti śāṅkare loka iti me satyabhāṣitam  |
hayaṃ yo yacchate tatra sampūrṇaṃ taruṇaṃ sitam  || 85.75 ||
raktaṃ vā pītavarṇaṃ vā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam  |
kuṅkumena viliptāṅgāvagrajanmahayāvapi  || 85.76 ||
sragdāmabhūṣitau kāryau sitavastrāvaguṇṭhitau  |
aṅghriḥ pradīyatāṃ skandhe madīye hayamāruha  || 85.77 ||
ārūḍhe brāhmaṇe brūyādbhāskaraḥ prīyatāmiti  |
sa yāti śāṃkaraṃ lokaṃ sarvapāpavivarjitaḥ  || 85.78 ||
uparāge tu somasya tīrthaṃ gatvā jitendriyaḥ  |
satyalokāccyutaścāpi rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 85.79 ||
tasya vāsaḥ sadā rājanna naśyati kadācana  |
dīrghāyurjāyate putro bhāryā ca vaśavartinī  || 85.80 ||
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ  |
sopavāso jitakrodho dhenuṃ dadyāddvijanmane  || 85.81 ||
savatsāṃ kṣīrasaṃyuktāṃ śvetavastrāvalokitām  |
śabalāṃ pītavarṇāṃ ca dhūmrāṃ vā nīlakarburām  || 85.82 ||
kapilāṃ vā savatsāṃ ca ghaṇṭābharaṇabhūṣitām  |
rūpyakhurāṃ kāṃsyadohāṃ svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ nareśvara  || 85.83 ||
śvetayā vardhate vaṃśo raktā saubhāgyavardhinī  |
śabalā pītavarṇā ca duḥkhaghnyau saṃprakīrtite  || 85.84 ||
kapilā nāśayetpāpaṃ saptajanmasamudbhavam  |
satyalokamavāpnoti gopradāyī nareśvara  || 85.85 ||
pakṣānte'tha vyatīpāte vai dhṛtau ravisaṃkrame  |
dinakṣaye gajacchāyāṃ grahaṇe bhāskarasya ca  || 85.86 ||
ye vrajanti mahātmānaḥ saṅgame suradurlabhe  |
mṛdāvaguṇṭhayitvā tu cātmānaṃ saṅgame viśet || 85.87 ||
hṛdayāntarjale jāpyā prāṇāyāmo'thavā nṛpa  |
gāyatrī vaiṣṇavī caiva saurī śaivī yadṛcchayā  |
te'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyanta ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 85.88 ||
jagatīṃ somanāthasya yastu kuryātpradakṣiṇām  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasuṃdharā  || 85.89 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ gurudāraniṣevaṇam  |
bhrūṇahā svarṇahartā ca mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 85.90 ||
tīrthākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti jitendriyaḥ  |
vyādhito mucyate rogī cārogī sukhamāpnuyāt || 85.91 ||
yatte saṃdahyate cetaḥ śṛṇu tanme yudhiṣṭhira  |
naikāpi nṛpa loke'smin bhrūṇahatyā sudustyajā  || 85.92 ||
kimu ṣaḍviṃśatiṃ pārtha prāpa yāḥ kṣaṇadākaraḥ  |
so'pi tīrthamidaṃ prāpya tapastaptvā suduścaram  || 85.93 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ śītaraśmirabhūtsukhī  |
śrūyate nṛpa paurāṇī gāthā gītā maharṣibhiḥ  || 85.94 ||
liṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ hyekaṃ daśabhrūṇahanaṃ bhavet |
ato liṅgatrayaṃ somaḥ sthāpayāmāsa bhārata  || 85.95 ||
revaurisaṃgame hyādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ bhṛgukacchake  |
tataḥ siddhiṃ parāṃ prāpya prabhāse tu tṛtīyakam  || 85.96 ||
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
dharmyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ saṃśuddhikṛnnṛṇām  || 85.97 ||
putrārthī labhate putrānniṣkāmaḥ svargamāpnuyāt |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyastīrthaṃ kṛtvā paraṃ nṛpa  || 85.98 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ somanāthasya yatphalam  |
śrutvā putramavāpnoti snātvā cāṣṭau na saṃśayaḥ  || 85.99 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe somanāthatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 86

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja piṅgalāvartamuttamam  |
saṅgamasya samīpasthaṃ revāyā uttare taṭe  |
havyavāhena rājendra sthāpitaḥ piṅgaleśvaraḥ  || 86.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
havyavāhena bhagavannīśvaraḥ sthāpitaḥ katham  |
etadākhyāhi me sarvaṃ prasādādvaktumarhasi  || 86.2 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śambhunā retasā rājaṃstarpito havyavāhanaḥ  |
prāptasaukhyena raudreṇa gauryākrīḍanacetasā  || 86.3 ||
havyavāhamukhe kṣiptaṃ rudreṇāmitatejasā  |
rudrasya retasā dagdhastīrthayātrākṛtādaraḥ  || 86.4 ||
sāgarāṃśca nadīrgatvā kramādrevāṃ samāgataḥ  |
cacāra parayā bhaktyā dhyānamugraṃ hutāśanaḥ  || 86.5 ||
vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ sāgraṃ yāvattepe hutāśanaḥ  |
tāvattuṣṭo mahādevo varado jātavedasaḥ  |
saṃnidhau samupetyātha vacanaṃ cedamabravīt || 86.6 ||

īśvara uvāca -
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva havyāśa yaste manasi vartate  || 86.7 ||

vahniruvāca -
namaste sarvalokeśa ugramūrte namo'stu te  |
retasā tava saṃdagdhaḥ kuṣṭhī jāto maheśvara  |
kṛpāṃ kuru mahādeva mama rogaṃ vināśaya  || 86.8 ||

īśvara uvāca -
havyavāha bhavārogo matprasādācca satvaram  |
atra tīrthe kṛtasnānaḥ svarūpaṃ pratipatsyase  || 86.9 ||
ityuktvā ca mahādevastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
anantaraṃ havyavāhaḥ sasnau revājale tvaran  || 86.10 ||
tadaiva roganirmukto'bhavaddivyasvarūpavān  |
sthāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ sa vahniḥ piṅgaleśvaram  || 86.11 ||
nāmnā saṃpūjayāmāsa tuṣṭāva stutibhirmudā  |
tato jagāma deśaṃ svaṃ devānāṃ havyavāhanaḥ  || 86.12 ||
havyavāhena bhūpaivaṃ sthāpitaḥ piṅgaleśvaraḥ  |
jitakrodho hi yastatra upavāsaṃ samācaret || 86.13 ||
atirāntraphalaṃ tasya ante rudratvamāpnuyāt |
guṇānvitāya viprāya kapilāṃ tatra bhārata  || 86.14 ||
alaṃkṛtya savatsāṃ ca śaktyālaṅkārabhūṣitām  |
yaḥ prayacchati rājendra sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  || 86.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe piṅgaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 87

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sthāpitaṃ munisaṅghairyadbrahmavaṃśasamudbhavaiḥ  || 87.1 ||
ṛṇamocanamityākhyaṃ revātaṭasamāśritam  |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ manujo bhaktyā tarpayan pitṛdevatāḥ  || 87.2 ||
devaiḥ pitṛmanuṣyaiśca ṛṇamātmakṛtaṃ ca yat |
mucyate tatkṣaṇānmartyaḥ snāto vai narmadājale  || 87.3 ||
pratyakṣaṃ duritaṃ tatra dṛśyate phalarūpataḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājannekacitto jitendriyaḥ  || 87.4 ||
snātvā dānaṃ ca vai dadyādarcayedgirijāpatim  |
ṛṇatrayavinirmukto nāke dīpyati devavat || 87.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ṛṇatrayamocanatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 88

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ pārtha kāpilaṃ tīrthamāśrayet |
sthāpitaṃ kapilenaiva sarvapātakanāśanam  || 88.1 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca site pakṣe caturdaśyāṃ nareśvara  |
snāpayetparayā bhaktyā kapilākṣīrasarpiṣā  || 88.2 ||
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena guṇṭhayeta maheśvaram  |
tataḥ sugandhapuṣpaiśca śvetaiśca nṛpasattama  || 88.3 ||
ye'rcayanti jitakrodhā na te yānti yamālayam  |
asipattravanaṃ ghoraṃ yamaculhī sudāruṇā  || 88.4 ||
dṛśyate naiva vidvadbhiḥ kapileśvarapūjanāt |
snātvā revājale puṇye bhojayedbrāhmaṇāñchubhān  || 88.5 ||
gopradānena vastreṇa tiladānena bhārata  |
chatraśayyāpradānena rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 88.6 ||
tīvratejā vighoraśca jīvatputraḥ priyaṃvadaḥ  |
śatruvargo na tasya syātkadācitpāṇḍunandana  || 88.7 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kapileśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 89

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra pūtikeśvaramuttamam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 89.1 ||
sthāpitaṃ jāmbuvantena lokānāṃ tu hitārthinā  |
rājā prasenajinnāma tasyāṃ vakṣasthalānmaṇau  || 89.2 ||
samutkṣipte tu tenaiva sapūtirabhavadvraṇaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tapastaptvā nirvraṇaḥ samajāyata  || 89.3 ||
tena tatsthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ pūtikeśvaramuttamam  |
yastatra manujo bhaktyā snāyādbharatasattama  || 89.4 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti sampūjya parameśvaram  |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ sarvakālaṃ narādhipa  |
ye'rcayanti sadā devaṃ te na yānti yamālayam  || 89.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe pūtikeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 90

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
revāyā uttare kūle vaiṣṇavaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
jalaśāyīti vai nāma vikhyātaṃ vasudhātale  || 90.1 ||
dānavānāṃ vadhaṃ kṛtvā suptastatra janārdanaḥ  |
cakraṃ prakṣālitaṃ tatra devadevena cakriṇā  |
sudarśanaṃ ca niṣpāpaṃ revājalasamāśrayāt || 90.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
cakratīrthaṃ samācakṣva munisaṃghaiśca vanditam  |
viṣṇoḥ prabhāvamatulaṃ revāyāścaiva yatphalam  || 90.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāprājña viraktastvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ cakriṇā svayam  || 90.4 ||
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm  |
āsītpurā mahādaityastālamegha iti śrutaḥ  || 90.5 ||
tena devā jitāḥ sarve hṛtarājyā narādhipa  |
yajñabhāgān svayaṃ bhuṅkte ahaṃ viṣṇurna saṃśayaḥ  || 90.6 ||
dhanadasya hṛtaṃ cittaṃ hṛtaḥ śakrasya vāraṇaḥ  |
indrāṇīṃ vāñchate pāpo hayaratnaṃ raverapi  || 90.7 ||
tālameghabhayātpārtha ravirudrāḥ savāsavāḥ  |
yamaḥ skando jaleśo'gnirvāyurdevo dhaneśvaraḥ  || 90.8 ||
savākpatimaheśāśca naṣṭacittāḥ pitāmaham  |
gatā devā brahmalokaṃ tatra dṛṣṭvā pitāmaham  || 90.9 ||
tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stotrairvāgīśapramukhāḥ surāḥ  |
guṇatrayavibhāgāya paścādbhedamupeyuṣe  || 90.10 ||
dṛṣṭvā devānnirutsāhān vivarṇānavanīpate  |
prasādābhimukho devaḥ pratyuvāca divaukasaḥ  || 90.11 ||

brahmovāca -
svāgataṃ surasaṅghasya kāntirnaṣṭā purātanī  |
himakliṣṭaprabhāveṇa jyotīṃṣīva mukhāni vaḥ  || 90.12 ||
praśamādarciṣāmetadanudgīrṇaṃ surāyudham  |
vṛtrasya hantuḥ kuliśaṃ kuṇṭhitaśrīva lakṣyate  || 90.13 ||
kiṃ cāyamaridurvāraḥ pāṇau pāśaḥ pracetasaḥ  |
mantreṇa hatavīryasya phaṇino dainyamāśritaḥ  || 90.14 ||
kuberasya manaḥśalyaṃ śaṃsatīva parābhavam  |
apaviddhagato vāyurbhagnaśākha iva drumaḥ  || 90.15 ||
yamo'pi vilikhanbhūmiṃ daṇḍenāstamitatviṣā  |
kurute'sminnamogho'pi nirvāṇālātalāghavam  || 90.16 ||
amī ca kathamādityāḥ pratāpakṣatiśītalāḥ  |
