citranyastā iva gatāḥ prakāmālokanīyatām  || 90.17 ||
tadbrūta vatsāḥ kimitaḥ prārthayadhvaṃ samāgatāḥ  |
kimāgamanakṛtyaṃ vo brūta niḥsaṃśayaṃ surāḥ  || 90.18 ||
mayi sṛṣṭirhi lokānāṃ rakṣā yuṣmāsvavasthitā  |
tato mandānilodbhūtakamalākaraśobhinā  || 90.19 ||
guruṃ netrasahasreṇa prerayāmāsa vṛtrahā  |
sa dvinetraṃ hareścakṣuḥ sahasranayanādhikam  || 90.20 ||
vācaspatiruvācedaṃ prāñjalirjalajāsanam  |
yuṣmadvaṃśodbhavastāta tālamegho mahābalaḥ  || 90.21 ||
upatāpayate devāndhūmaketurivocchritaḥ  |
tena devagaṇāḥ sarve duḥkhitā dānavena ca  || 90.22 ||
tālamegho daityapatiḥ sarvānno bādhate balī  |
tasmāttvāṃ śaraṇaṃ prāptāḥ śaraṇaṃ no vidhe bhava  || 90.23 ||
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān vedhāstānabravīdvacaḥ  || 90.24 ||

brahmovāca -
tālameghena vo madhye balī tena samaḥ surāḥ  |
vinā mādhavadevena sādhyo me naiva dānavaḥ  || 90.25 ||
tataḥ suragaṇāḥ sarve viriñcipramukhā nṛpa  |
kṣīrodaṃ prasthitāḥ sarve duḥkhitāstena vairiṇā  || 90.26 ||
tvaritāḥ prasthitā devāḥ keśavaṃ draṣṭukāmyayā  |
kṣīrodaṃ sāgaraṃ gatvāstuvaṃste jalaśāyinam  || 90.27 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
jagadādiranādistvaṃ jagadanto'pyanantakaḥ  |
jaganmūrtiramūrtistvaṃ jaya gīrvāṇapūjita  || 90.28 ||
jaya kṣīrodaśayana jaya lakṣmyā sadā vṛta  |
jaya dānavanāśāya jaya devakinandana  || 90.29 ||
jaya śaṅkhagadāpāṇe jaya cakradhara prabho  |
iti devastutiṃ śrutvā prabuddho jalaśāyyatha  || 90.30 ||
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ meghagambhīranisvanām  |
kimarthaṃ bodhito brahman samarthairvaḥ surāsuraiḥ  || 90.31 ||

brahmovāca -
tālameghabhayātkṛṣṇa samprāptāstava mandiram  |
na vadhyaḥ kasyacitpāpastālamegho janārdana  || 90.32 ||
tvameva jahi taṃ duṣṭaṃ mṛtyuṃ yāsyati nānyathā  || 90.33 ||

śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca -
svasthānaṃ gamyatāṃ devāḥ svakīyāṃ labhata prajām  |
duṣṭātmānaṃ haniṣyāmi tālameghaṃ mahābalam  || 90.34 ||
sthānaṃ bruvantu me devā vasedyatra sa dānavaḥ  || 90.35 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
himācalaguhāyāṃ sa vasate dānaveśvaraḥ  |
caturviṃśatisāhasraiḥ kanyābhiḥ parivāritaḥ  || 90.36 ||
turaṅgaiḥ syandanaiḥ kṛṣṇa saṃkhyā tasya na vidyate  |
naṭā nānāvidhāstatra asaṃkhyātaguṇā hare  || 90.37 ||
dviradāḥ parvatākārā hayāśca dviradopamāḥ  |
mahābalo vasettatra gīrvāṇabhayadāyakaḥ  || 90.38 ||
śrutvā devo vacasteṣāṃ devānāmāturātmanām  |
acintayadgarutmantaṃ śatrusaṅghavināśanam  || 90.39 ||
cakraṃ kareṇa saṃgṛhya gadācakradharaḥ prabhuḥ  |
śārṅgaṃ ca muśalaṃ sīraṃ karairgṛhya janārdanaḥ  || 90.40 ||
ārūḍhaḥ pakṣirājendraṃ vadhārthaṃ dānavasya ca  |
dānavasya pure peturutpātā ghorarūpiṇaḥ  || 90.41 ||
gomāyurgṛdhramadhye tu kapotaiḥ samamāviśat |
vinā vātena tasyaiva dhvajadaṇḍaḥ papāta ha  || 90.42 ||
sarpasūṣakayoryuddhaṃ tathā kesarināgayoḥ  |
unmārgāḥ saritastatrāvahanraktavimiśritāḥ  |
akālatarupuṣpāṇi dṛśyante sma samantataḥ  || 90.43 ||
tataḥ prāpto jagannātho himavantaṃ nageśvaram  |
pāñcajanyaśvasahasā pūritaḥ purasannidhau  || 90.44 ||
tena śabdena mahatā hyārūḍho dānaveśvaraḥ  |
uvāca ca tadā vākyaṃ tālamegho mahābalaḥ  || 90.45 ||

tālamegha uvāca -
ko'yaṃ mṛtyuvaśaṃ prāpto hyajñātvā mama vikramam  |
dhundhumārājñayā hyāśu svasainyaparivāritaḥ  || 90.46 ||
balādānaya taṃ baddhvā mamāgre bahuśālinam  || 90.47 ||

dhundhumāra uvāca -
ānayāmi na sandehaḥ suro yakṣo'tha kinnaraḥ  |
syandanaughaiḥ samāyukto gajavājibhaṭaiḥ saha  || 90.48 ||
hṛṣṭastato jagadyoniḥ suparṇastho mahābalaḥ  |
gṛhyatāṃ gṛhyatāmeṣa ityuktāstena kiṃkarāḥ  || 90.49 ||
caturdikṣu pradhāvanta itaścetaśca sarvataḥ  |
suparṇenāgnirūpeṇa dagdhāste śalabhā yathā  || 90.50 ||
dhundhumāro'pi kṛṣṇena śaraghātena tāḍitaḥ  |
hato vakṣaḥsthale pāpo mṛtāvastho rathopari  || 90.51 ||
hāhākāraṃ tataḥ sarve dānavāścakrurāturāḥ  |
tālameghastataḥ kruddho rathārūḍho vinirgataḥ  |
dadṛśe keśavaṃ pārtha śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 90.52 ||

tālamegha uvāca -
anye te dānavāḥ kṛṣṇa ye hatāḥ samare tvayā  |
hiraṇyakaśipuprakhyānapumāṃso hi te'cyuta  || 90.53 ||
ityuktvā dānavaḥ pārtha varṣayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ  |
dānavasya śarānmuktān chedayāmāsa keśavaḥ  || 90.54 ||
garutmānavadhītsainyamavadhyaṃ yatsurāsuraiḥ  |
kṛṣṇena dviguṇāstasya preṣitāḥ svaśilīmukhāḥ  || 90.55 ||
dviguṇaṃ dviguṇīkṛtya preṣayāmāsa dānavaḥ  |
tānapyaṣṭaguṇaiḥ kṛṣṇaśchādayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ  || 90.56 ||
tataḥ kruddhena daityena hyāgneyaṃ bāṇamuttamam  || 90.57 ||
vāruṇaṃ preṣayāmāsa tvāgneyaṃ śamitaṃ tataḥ  |
vāruṇenaiva vāyavyaṃ tālamegho vyasarjayat || 90.58 ||
sārpaṃ caiva hṛṣīkeśo vāyavyasya praśāntaye  |
nārasiṃhaṃ nṛsiṃho'pi preṣayāmāsa pāṇḍava  || 90.59 ||
nārasiṃhaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā tālamegho mahābalaḥ  |
uttīrya syandanācchīghraṃ gṛhītvā khaḍgacarmaṇī  || 90.60 ||
kṛṣṇa tvāṃ preṣayiṣyāmi yamamārgaṃ sudāruṇam  |
ityuktvā dānavaḥ pārtha āgataḥ keśavaṃ prati  || 90.61 ||
khaḍgenātāḍayaddaityo gadāpāṇiṃ janārdanam  |
maṇḍalāgraṃ tato gṛhya keśavo hṛṣṭamānasaḥ  || 90.62 ||
jaghanoraḥsthale pārtha tālameghaṃ mahāhave  |
janārdanastadā daityaṃ daityo harimahanmṛdhe  || 90.63 ||
janārdanastataḥ kruddhastālameghāya bhārata  |
amoghaṃ cakramādāya muktaṃ tasya ca mūrdhani  || 90.64 ||
nipapāta śirastasya parvatāśca cakampire  |
samudrāḥ kṣubhitāḥ pārtha nadya unmārgagāminīḥ  || 90.65 ||
puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ tato devā mumucuḥ keśavopari  |
avadhyaḥ surasaṅghānāṃ sūditaḥ keśava tvayā  || 90.66 ||
svasthāścaiva tato devāstālameghe nipātite  |
janārdano'pi kaunteya narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 90.67 ||
kṣīrodāṃ narmadāṃ matvā anantabhujagopari  |
lakṣmyā samanvitaḥ kṛṣṇo nilīnaścottare taṭe  || 90.68 ||
cakraṃ vibhīṣaṇaṃ martye jvālāmālāsamanvitam  |
patitaṃ narmadātoye jalaśāyisamīpataḥ  || 90.69 ||
nirdhūtakalmaṣaṃ jātaṃ narmadātoyayogataḥ  |
tālameghavadhotpannaṃ yatpāpaṃ nṛpanandana  || 90.70 ||
tatsravaṃ kṣālitaṃ sadyo narmadāṃbhasi bhārata  |
tadāprabhṛti loke'smiñjalaśāyī mahīpate  || 90.71 ||
cakratīrthaṃ vadantyanye kecitkālāghanāśanam  |
vikhyātaṃ bhārate varṣe narmadāyāṃ mahīpate  || 90.72 ||
tattīrthasya prabhāvo'yaṃ śrūyatāmavanīpate  |
yathā'nanto hi nāgānāṃ devānāṃ ca janārdanaḥ  || 90.73 ||
māsānāṃ mārgaśīrṣo'sti nadīnāṃ narmadā yathā  |
māsi mārgaśire pārtha hyekādaśyāṃ site'hani  || 90.74 ||
gatvā yo manujo bhaktyā kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
vaiṣṇavīṃ bhāvanāṃ kṛtvā jaleśaṃ tu vrajeta vai  || 90.75 ||
ekabhuktaṃ ca naktaṃ ca tathaivāyācitaṃ nṛpa  |
upavāsaṃ tathā dānaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam  || 90.76 ||
karoti ca kuruśreṣṭha na sa yāti yamālayam  |
yamalokabhayādbhītā ye lokāḥ pāṇḍunandana  || 90.77 ||
te paśyantu śriyaḥ kāntaṃ nāgaparyaṅkaśāyinam  |
gopījanasamāvṛttaṃ yoganidrāṃ samāśritam  |
viśvarūpaṃ jagannāthaṃ saṃsārabhayanāśanam  || 90.78 ||
snāpayetparayā bhaktyā kṣaudrakṣīreṇa sarpiṣā  |
khaṇḍena toyamiśreṇa jagadyoniṃ janārdanam  || 90.79 ||
snāpyamānaṃ ca paśyanti ye lokā gatamatsarāḥ  |
te yānti paramaṃ lokaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam  || 90.80 ||
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpamathavā tailapūritam  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā daivasyāgre vimatsarāḥ  || 90.81 ||
ye kathāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ bhaktyā śṛṇvanti ca nṛpottama  |
brahmahatyādipāpāni naśyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 90.82 ||
pradakṣiṇanti ye martyā jalaśāyijagadgurum  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā taistu saptadvīpā vasuṃdharā  || 90.83 ||
tataḥ prabhāte vimale pitḥn saṃtarpayejjalaiḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ ca brāhmaṇaistatra yogyaiḥ pāṇḍava mānavāḥ  || 90.84 ||
svadāranirataiḥ śāntaiḥ paradāravivarjakaiḥ  |
vedābhyasanaśīlaiśca svakarmanirataiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 90.85 ||
nityaṃ yajanaśīlaiśca trisandhyāparipālakaiḥ  |
śraddhayā kārayecchrāddhaṃ yadīcchecchreya ātmanaḥ  || 90.86 ||
te dhanyā mānuṣe loke vandyā hi bhuvi mānavāḥ  |
ye vasanti sadākālaṃ pādapadmāśrayā hareḥ  || 90.87 ||
jalaśāyaṃ prapaśyanti pratyakṣaṃ suranāyakam  |
pakṣopavāsaṃ pārākaṃ vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ śubham  || 90.88 ||
māsopavāsamugraṃ ca ṣaṣṭhānnaṃ pañcamaṃ vratam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kuryātso'kṣayāṃ gatimāpnuyāt || 90.89 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi tiladhenośca yatphalam  |
yathā yasminyadā deyā dāne tasyāḥ śubhaṃ phalam  || 90.90 ||
etatkathāntaraṃ puṇyamṛṣerdvaipāyanātpurā  |
śrutaṃ hi naimiṣe puṇye nāradādyairanekadhā  || 90.91 ||
idaṃ paramamāyuṣyaṃ maṅgalyaṃ kīrtivardhanam  |
viprāṇāṃ śrāvayanvidvānphalānantyaṃsamaśnute  || 90.92 ||
bahubhyo na pradeyāni gaurgṛhaṃ śayanaṃ striyaḥ  |
vibhaktadakṣiṇā hyetā dātāraṃ nāpnuvanti ca  || 90.93 ||
ekametatpradātavyaṃ na bahūnāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
sā ca vikrayamāpannā dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam  || 90.94 ||
yathālābhā tu sarveṣāṃ caturdroṇā tu gauḥ smṛtā  |
droṇasya vatsakaḥ kāryo bahūnāṃ vāpi kāmataḥ  || 90.95 ||
yasmindeśe tu yanmānaṃ viṣaye vā vicāritam  |
tena mānena tāṃ kurvannakṣayaṃ phalamaśnute  || 90.96 ||
sukhapūrvaṃ śucau bhūmau puṣpadhūpākṣataistathā  |
karṇābhyāṃ ratne dātavye dīpau netradvaye tathā  || 90.97 ||
śrīkhaṇḍamurasi sthāpyaṃ tābhyāṃ caiva tu kāñcanam  |
ūrdhve madhu ghṛtaṃ deyaṃ kuryātsarṣaparomakam  || 90.98 ||
kambale kambalaṃ dadyācchroṇyāṃ madhu ghṛtaṃ tathā  |
yavasaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyādghṛtaṃ kṣaudrasamanvitam  || 90.99 ||
svarṇaśṛṅgī rūpyaśiphārukmalāṅgūlasaṃyutā  |
ratnapṛṣṭhī tu dātavyā kāṃsyapātrāvadohinī  || 90.100 ||
yatsyādbālyakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yadvā kṛtamajānatā  |
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā yadvicintitam  || 90.101 ||
jale niṣṭhīvitaṃ caiva muśalaṃ vāpi laṅghitam  |
vṛṣalīgamanaṃ caiva gurudāraniṣevaṇam  || 90.102 ||
kanyāyā gamanaṃ caiva suvarṇasteyameva ca  |
surāpānaṃ tathā cānyattiladhenuḥ punāti hi  || 90.103 ||
ahorātropavāsena vidhivattāṃ visarjayet |
yā sā yamapure ghore nadī vaitaraṇī smṛtā  || 90.104 ||
vālukāyo'śmasthalā ca pacyate yatra duṣkṛtī  |
avīcirnarako yatra yatra yāmalaparvatau  || 90.105 ||
yatra lohamukhāḥ kākā yatra śvāno bhayaṃkarāḥ  |
asipattravanaṃ caiva yatra sā kūṭaśālmalī  || 90.106 ||
tānsukhena vyatikramya dharmarājālayaṃ vrajet |
dharmarājastu taṃ dṛṣṭvā sūnṛtaṃ vakti bhārata  || 90.107 ||
vimānamuttamaṃ yogyaṃ maṇiratnavibhūṣitam  |
atrāruhya naraśreṣṭha prayāhi paramāṃ gatim  || 90.108 ||
mā ca cāṭu bhaṭe dehi maiva dehi purohite  |
mā ca kāṇe virūpe ca nyūnāṅge na ca devale  || 90.109 ||
avedaviduṣe naiva brāhmaṇe sarvavikraye  |
mitraghne ca kṛtaghne ca mantrahīne tathaiva ca  || 90.110 ||
vedāntagāya dātavyā śrotriyāya kuṭumbine  |
vedāntagasute deyā śrotriye gṛhapālake  || 90.111 ||
sarvāṅgarucire vipre sadvṛtte ca priyaṃvade  |
pūrṇimāyāṃ tu māghasya kārttikyāmatha bhārata  || 90.112 ||
vaiśākhyāṃ mārgaśīrṣyāṃ vāṣāḍhyāṃ caitryāmathāpi vā  |
ayane viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte ca sarvadā  || 90.113 ||
ṣaḍaśītimukhe puṇye chāyāyāṃ kuṃjarasya vā  |
eṣa te kathitaḥ kalpastiladhenormayānagha  || 90.114 ||
vrajanti vaiṣṇavaṃ lokaṃ dattvā pādaṃ yamopari  |
prāṇatyāgātparaṃ lokaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
bhittvāśu bhāskaraṃ yānti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 90.115 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ cakratīrthaphalaṃ nṛpa  |
yacchrutvā mānavo bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 90.116 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe jalaśāyitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma navatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 91

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthaparamapāvanam  |
caṇḍādityaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha sthāpitaṃ caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥ  || 91.1 ||
āstāṃ purā mahādaityau caṇḍamuṇḍau sudāruṇau  |
narmadātīramāśritya ceraturvipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 91.2 ||
dhyāyantau bhāskaraṃ devaṃ tamonāśaṃ jagattraye  |
tuṣṭastattapasā devaḥ sahasrāṃśuruvāca ha  || 91.3 ||
sādhu sādhviti tau pārtha narmadāyāḥ śubhe taṭe  |
varaṃ prārthayataṃ vīrau yatheṣṭaṃ cetasecchitam  || 91.4 ||
caṇḍamuṇḍāvūcatuḥ  |
ajeyau sarvadevānāṃ bhūyāsvāvāṃ samāhitau  |
sarvarogaiḥ parityaktau sarvakālaṃ divākara  || 91.5 ||
evamastviti tau prāha bhāskaro vāritaskaraḥ  |
ityuktvāntardadhe bhānurdaityābhyāṃ tatra bhāskaraḥ  || 91.6 ||
sthāpitaḥ parayā bhaktyā taṃ gacchedātmasiddhaye  |
gīrvāṇāṃśca manuṣyāṃśca pitḥṃstatrāpi tarpayet || 91.7 ||
sa vasedbhāskare loke viriñcidivasaṃ nṛpa  |
ghṛtena bodhayeddīpaṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ sa ca nareśvara  |
mucyate sarvapāpaistu pratiyāti puraṃ raveḥ  || 91.8 ||
utpattiṃ caṇḍabhānoryaḥ śṛṇoti bharatarṣabha  |
vijayī sa sadā nūnamādhivyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 91.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe caṇḍādityatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 92

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra yamahāsyamanuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritam  || 92.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
yamahāsyaṃ kathaṃ jātaṃ pṛthivyāṃ dvijapuṃgava  |
etatsarvaṃ mamākhyāhi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 92.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāprājña pṛṣṭo'haṃ nṛpanandana  |
snānārthaṃ narmadāṃ puṇyāmāgataste pitā purā  || 92.3 ||
rajakena yathā dhautaṃ vastraṃ bhavati nirmalam  |
tathāsau nirmalo jāto dharmarājo yudhiṣṭhira  || 92.4 ||
sa paśyannirmalaṃ dehaṃ hasanprovāca vismitaḥ  || 92.5 ||

yama uvāca -
matpuraṃ kathamāyānti manujāḥ pāpabṛṃhitāḥ  |
snānenaikena revāyāḥ prāpyate vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  || 92.6 ||
samarthā ye na paśyanti revāṃ puṇyajalāṃ śubhām  |
jātyandhaiste samā jñeyā mṛtaiḥ paṅgubhireva vā  || 92.7 ||
samarthā ye na paśyanti revāṃ puṇyajalāṃ nadīm  |
etasmātkāraṇādrājanhasito lokaśāsanaḥ  || 92.8 ||
sthāpayitvā yamastatra devaṃ svargaṃ jagāma ha  |
yamahāseśvare rājañjitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  || 92.9 ||
viśeṣāccāśvine māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
upoṣya parayā bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 92.10 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryāddīpaṃ devasya bodhayet |
ghṛtena caiva rājendra śṛṇu tatrāsti yatphalam  || 92.11 ||
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvairagamyāgamanodbhavaiḥ  |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇodbhūtairapeyāpeyajairapi  || 92.12 ||
avāhyavāhite yatsyādadohyādohane yathā  |
snānamātreṇa tasyaivaṃ yānti pāpānyanekadhā  || 92.13 ||
yamalokaṃ na vīkṣeta manujaḥ sa kadācana  |
pitḥṇāṃ paramaṃ guhyamidaṃ bhūmau nareśvara  || 92.14 ||
dadatāmakṣayaṃ sarvaṃ yamahāsye na saṃśayaḥ  |
amāvāsyāṃ jitakrodho yastu pūjayate dvijān  || 92.15 ||
hiraṇyabhūmidānena tiladānena bhūyasā  |
kṛṣṇājinapradānena tiladhenupradānataḥ  || 92.16 ||
vidhānoktadvijāgryāya ye pradāsyanti bhaktitaḥ  |
hayaṃ vā kuṃjaraṃ vātha dhūrvahau sīrasaṃyutau  || 92.17 ||
kanyāṃ vasumatīṃ gāṃ ca mahiṣīṃ vā payasvinīm  |
dadate ye nṛpaśreṣṭha nopasarpanti te yamam  || 92.18 ||
yamo'pi bhavati prītaḥ pratijanma yudhiṣṭhira  |
yamasya vāho mahiṣo mahiṣyastasya mātaraḥ  || 92.19 ||
tāsāṃ dānaprabhāveṇa yamaḥ prīto bhaveddhruvam  |
nāsau yamamavāpnoti yadi pāpaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  || 92.20 ||
etasmātkāraṇādatra mahiṣīdānamuttamam  |
tasyāḥ śṛṅge jalaṃ kāryaṃ dhūmravastrānuveṣṭitā  || 92.21 ||
āyasasya khurāḥ kāryāstāmrapṛṣṭhāḥ subhūṣitāḥ  |
lavaṇācalaṃ pūrvasyāmāgneyyāṃ guḍaparvatam  || 92.22 ||
kārpāsaṃ yāmyabhāgaṃ tu navanītaṃ tu nairṛte  |
paścime saptadhānyāni vāyavye taṃdulāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 92.23 ||
saumye tu kāñcanaṃ dadyādīśāne ghṛtameva ca  |
pradadyādyamarājo me prīyatāmityudīrayan  || 92.24 ||
ityuccārya dvijasyāgre yamalokaṃ mahābhayam  |
asipattravanaṃ ghoraṃ yamacullī sudāruṇā  || 92.25 ||
raudrā vaitaraṇī caiva kumbhīpāko bhayāvahaḥ  |
kālasūtro mahābhīmastathā yamalaparvatau  || 92.26 ||
krakacaṃ tailayantraṃ ca śvāno gṛdhrāḥ sudāruṇāḥ  |
nirucchvāsā mahānādā bhairavo rauravastathā  || 92.27 ||
ete ghorā yāmyaloke śrūyante dvijasattama  |
tvatprasādena te somyāstīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 92.28 ||
dānasyāsya prabhāveṇa yamarājaprasādataḥ  |
narake'haṃ na yāsyāmi dvija janmani janmani  || 92.29 ||
yamahāsyasya cākhyānamidaṃ śṛṇvanti ye narāḥ  |
te'pi pāpavinirmuktā na paśyanti yamālayam  || 92.30 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe yamahāsyatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 93

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra kalhoḍītīrthamuttamam  |
vikhyātaṃ bhārate loke gaṅgāyāḥ pāpanāśanam  || 93.1 ||
durlabhaṃ manujaiḥ pārtha revātaṭasamāśritam  |
prāṇināṃ pāpanāśāya ūṣaraṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā  || 93.2 ||
tattu tīrthamidaṃ puṇyamityevaṃ śūlino vacaḥ  |
jāhnavī paśurūpeṇa tatra snānārthamāgatā  || 93.3 ||
atastadviśrutaṃ loke kalhoḍītīrthamuttamam  |
trirātraṃ kārayettatra pūrṇimāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 93.4 ||
rajastamastathā krodhaṃ dambhaṃ mātsaryameva ca  |
etāṃstyajati yaḥ pārtha tenāptaṃ mokṣajaṃ phalam  || 93.5 ||
payasā snāpayeddevaṃ trisandhyaṃ ca tryahaṃ tathā  |
payo gosambhavaṃ sadyaḥ savatsā jīvaputriṇī  || 93.6 ||
kṛtvā tattāmraje pātre kṣaudreṇa caiva yojite  |
oṃ namaḥ śrīśivāyeti snānaṃ devasya kārayet || 93.7 ||
sa yāti tridaśasthānaṃ nākastrībhiḥ samāvṛtaḥ  |
yastatra vidhivatsnātvā dānaṃ preteṣu yacchati  || 93.8 ||
śuklāṃ gāṃ dāpayettatra prīyatāṃ me pitāmahāḥ  |
brāhmaṇe śaucasampanne svadāranirate sadā  || 93.9 ||
savatsāṃ vastrasaṃyuktāṃ hiraṇyopari saṃsthitām  |
sattvayukto dadadrājañchāmbhavaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 93.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kalhoḍītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 94

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ rājannanditīrthaṃ vrajecchubham  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṃsāṃ nandinā nirmitaṃ purā  || 94.1 ||
pāpaughahatajantūnāṃ mokṣadaṃ narmadātaṭe  |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā nandināthe yudhiṣṭhira  || 94.2 ||
pañcopacārapūjāyāmarcayennandikeśvaram  |
ratnāni caiva viprebhyo yo dadyāddharmanandana  || 94.3 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra vāsaḥ pinākinaḥ  |
sarvasaukhyasamāyukto'psarobhiḥ saha modate  || 94.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nandikeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturnavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 95

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra badaryāśramamuttamam  |
sarvatīrthavaraṃ puṇyaṃ kathitaṃ śaṃbhunā purā  || 95.1 ||
yaścaiṣa bhāratasyārthe tatra siddhaḥ kirīṭabhṛt |
bhrātā te phālguno nāma viddhyenaṃ naradaivatam  || 95.2 ||
naranārāyaṇau dvau tāvāgatau narmadātaṭe  |
jñānaṃ tasyaiva yo rājanbhaktimānvai janārdane  || 95.3 ||
samaṃ paśyati sarveṣu sthāvareṣu careṣu ca  |
brāhmaṇaṃ śvapacaṃ caiva tatra prīto janārdanaḥ  || 95.4 ||
aikātmyaṃ paśya kaunteya mayi cātmani nāntaram  |
naranārāyaṇābhyāṃ hi kṛtaṃ badarikāśramam  || 95.5 ||
sthāpitaḥ śaṅkarastatra lokānugrahakāraṇāt |
trimūrtisthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ svargamārgānumuktidam  || 95.6 ||
tatra gatvā śucirbhūtvā hyekarātropavāsakṛt |
rajastamastathā tyaktvā sāttvikaṃ bhāvamāśrayet || 95.7 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā madhumāsāṣṭamīdine  |
athavā ca caturdaśyāmubhau pakṣau ca kārayet || 95.8 ||
āśvinasya viśeṣeṇa kathitaṃ tava pāṇḍava  |
snāpayetparayā bhaktyā kṣīreṇa madhunā saha  || 95.9 ||
dadhnā śarkarayā yuktaṃ ghṛtena samalaṃkṛtam  |
pañcāmṛtamidaṃ puṇyaṃ snāpayedvṛṣabhadhvajam  || 95.10 ||
snāpyamānaṃ śivaṃ bhaktyā vīkṣate yo vimatsaraḥ  |
tasya vāsaḥ śivopānte śakraloke na saṃśayaḥ  || 95.11 ||
śāṭhyenāpi namaskāraḥ prayuktaḥ śūlapāṇine  |
saṃsāramūlabaddhānāmudveṣṭanakaro hi yaḥ  || 95.12 ||
tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ tena tena sarvamanuṣṭhitam  |
yenauṃ namaḥ śivāyeti mantrābhyāsaḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ  || 95.13 ||
yaḥ punaḥ snāpayedbhaktyā ekabhakto jitendriyaḥ  |
tasyāpi yatphalaṃ pārtha vakṣye talleśatastava  || 95.14 ||
pīḍito vṛddhabhāvena tava bhaktyā vadāmyaham  |
te yānti paramaṃ sthānaṃ bhittvā bhāskaramaṇḍalam  || 95.15 ||
saṃsāre sarvasaukhyānāṃ nilayāste bhavanti ca  |
āścaryaṃ jñātivargāṇāṃ dharmāṇāṃ nilayāstu te  || 95.16 ||
sampannāḥ sarvakāmaiste pṛthivyāṃ pṛthivīpate  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yaḥ kuryānnarmadodakamiśritam  || 95.17 ||
yogyaiśca brāhmaṇairrājankulīnairvedapāragaiḥ  |
surūpaiśca suśīlaiśca svadāranirataiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 95.18 ||
āryadeśaprasūtaiśca ślakṣṇaiścaiva surūpibhiḥ  |
kārayetpiṇḍadānaṃ vai bhāskare kutapasthite  || 95.19 ||
pitḥṇāṃ paramaṃ lokaṃ yadīccheddharmanandana  |
varjayettānprayatnena kāṇānduṣṭāṃśca dāmbhikān  || 95.20 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena yogyaṃ vipraṃ samāśrayet |
narakānmocayetpretānkumbhīpākapurogamān  || 95.21 ||
mokṣo bhavati sarveṣāṃ pitḥṇāṃ nṛpanandana  |
viprebhyaḥ kāñcanaṃ dadyātprīyatāṃ me pitāmahaḥ  || 95.22 ||
annaṃ ca dāpayettatra bhaktyā vastraṃ ca bhārata  |
gāṃ vṛṣaṃ medinīṃ dadyācchatraṃ śastaṃ nṛpottama  || 95.23 ||
sa pumānsvargamāpnoti ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt |
prāṇatyāgaṃ tu yaḥ kuryācchikhinā salilena vā  || 95.24 ||
anāśakena vā bhūyaḥ sa gacchecchivamandiram  |
naranārāyaṇītīre devadroṇyāṃ ca yo nṛpa  || 95.25 ||
sa vasedīśvarasyāgre yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  |
punaḥ svargāccyutaḥ so'pi rājā bhavati vīryavān  || 95.26 ||
sarvaiśvaryaguṇairyuktaḥ prajāpālanatatparaḥ  |
tataḥ smarati tattīrthaṃ punarevāgamiṣyati  || 95.27 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nārāyaṇītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 96

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ koṭīśvaraṃ param  |
ṛṣikoṭiḥ samāyātā yatra vai kurunandana  || 96.1 ||
kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasyaiva kṣemārthaṃ munipuṃgavāḥ  |
mantrayitvā dvijaiḥ sarvairvedamaṅgalapāṭhakaiḥ  || 96.2 ||
sthāpitaḥ śaṅkarastatra kāraṇaṃ bandhanāśanam  |
saṃsāracchedakaraṇaṃ prāṇināmārtināśanam  || 96.3 ||
koṭīśvaramiti proktaṃ pṛthivyāṃ nṛpanandana  |
snāpayettaṃ tu yo bhaktyā pūrṇimāyāṃ nṛpottama  || 96.4 ||
pitḥṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍadānaṃ yathāvidhi  |
śrāvaṇasya viśeṣeṇa pūrṇimāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 96.5 ||
pitḥṇāmakṣayā tṛptiryāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
pitḥṇāṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ revātaṭasamāśritam  |
mokṣadaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ nirmitaṃ munisattamaiḥ  || 96.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe koṭīśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṇṇavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 97

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla vyāsatīrthamanuttamam  |
durlabhaṃ manujaiḥ puṇyamantarikṣe vyavasthitam  || 97.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kasmādvai vyāsatīrthaṃ tadantarikṣe vyavasthitam  |
etadākhyāhi saṃkṣepāttyaja granthasya vistaram  || 97.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahābāho dharmavānsādhuvatsala  |
svakarmanirataḥ pārtha tīrthayātrākṛtādaraḥ  || 97.3 ||
durlabhaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ vyāsatīrthaṃ nareśvara  |
pīḍito vṛddhabhāvena akalpo'haṃ nṛpātmaja  || 97.4 ||
visaṃjño gatavittastu saṃjātaḥ smṛtivarjitaḥ  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ tīrthaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 97.5 ||
kalistatraiva rājendra na viśedvyāsasaṃśrayāt |
antarikṣe tu saṃjātaṃ revāyāśceṣṭitena tu  || 97.6 ||
viriñcirnaiva śaknoti revāyā guṇakīrtanam  |
kathaṃ jñāsyāmyahaṃ tāta revāmāhātmyamuttamam  || 97.7 ||
vyāsatīrthaṃ viśeṣeṇa lavamātraṃ bravīmyataḥ  |
pratyakṣaḥ pratyayo yatra dṛśyate'dya kalau yuge  || 97.8 ||
vihaṅgo gacchate naiva bhittvā śūlaṃ sudāruṇam  |
tasyotpattiṃ samāsena kathayāmi nṛpātmaja  || 97.9 ||
āsītpūrvaṃ mahīpāla munirmānyaḥ parāśaraḥ  |
tenātyugraṃ tapaścīrṇaṃ gaṅgāmbhasi mahāphalam  || 97.10 ||
prāṇāyāmena saṃtasthau praviṣṭo jāhnavījale  |
pūrṇe dvādaśame varṣe niṣkrānto jalamadhyataḥ  || 97.11 ||
bhikṣārthī saṃcaredgrāmaṃ nāvā yatraiva tiṣṭhati  |
tatra tena parā dṛṣṭā bālā caiva manoharā  || 97.12 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa ca kāmārta uvāca madhuraṃ tadā  |
māṃ nayasva paraṃ pāraṃ kāsi tvaṃ mṛgalocane  || 97.13 ||
nāvārūḍhe nadītīre mama cittapramāthini  |
evamuktā tu sā tena praṇamya ṛṣipuṃgavam  || 97.14 ||
kathayāmāsa cātmānaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ kāmamohitam  |
kaivartānāṃ gṛhe dāsī kanyāhaṃ dvijasattama  || 97.15 ||
nāvāsaṃrakṣaṇārthāya ādiṣṭā svāminā vibho  |
mayā vijñāpitaṃ vṛttamaśeṣaṃ jñātumarhasi  || 97.16 ||
evamuktastayā so'tha kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvābravīdidam  || 97.17 ||

parāśara uvāca -
ahaṃ jñānabalādbhadre tava jānāmi sambhavam  |
kaivartaputrikā na tvaṃ rājakanyāsi sundari  || 97.18 ||

kanyovāca -
kaḥ pitā kathyatāṃ brahmankasyā vā hyudarodbhavā  |
kasminvaṃśe prasūtāhaṃ kaivartatanayā katham  || 97.19 ||

parāśara uvāca -
kathayāmi samastaṃ yattvayā pṛṣṭamaśeṣataḥ  |
vasurnāmeti bhūpālaḥ somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇaḥ  || 97.20 ||
jambūdvīpādhipo bhadre śatrūṇāṃ bhayavardhanaḥ  |
śatāni sapta bhāryāṇāṃ putrāṇāṃ ca daśaiva tu  || 97.21 ||
dharmeṇa pālayellokānīśavatpūjyate sadā  |
mlecchāstasyāvidheyāśca kṣīradvīpanivāsinaḥ  || 97.22 ||
teṣāmutsādanārthāya yayāvullaṅghya sāgaram  |
saṃyuktaḥ putrabhṛtyaiśca pauruṣe mahati sthitaiḥ  || 97.23 ||
samaraṃ taiḥ samārabdhaṃ mlecchaiśca vasunā saha  |
jitā mlecchāḥ samastāste vasunā mṛgalocane  || 97.24 ||
karadāste kṛtāstena saputrabalavāhanāḥ  |
pradhānā tasya sā rājñī tava mātā mṛgekṣaṇe  || 97.25 ||
pravāsasthe mahīpāle saṃjātā sā rajasvalā  |
nārīṇāṃ tu sadākālaṃ manmatho hyadhiko bhavet || 97.26 ||
viśeṣeṇa ṛtoḥ kāle bhidyante kāmasāyakaiḥ  |
manmathena tu saṃtaptācintayatsā śubhekṣaṇā  || 97.27 ||
dūtaṃ vai preṣayāmyadya vasurājñaḥ samīpataḥ  |
āhūtaḥ satvaraṃ dūta gaccha tvaṃ nṛpasannidhau  || 97.28 ||

dūta uvāca -
paratīraṃ gato devi vasurājāriśāsanaḥ  |
tatra gantumaśakyeta jalayānairvinā śubhe  || 97.29 ||
tāni yānāni sarvāṇi gṛhītāni pare taṭe  |
dūtavākyena sā rājñī viṣaṇṇā kāmapīḍitā  || 97.30 ||
tatsakhī tāmuvācātha kasmāttvaṃ paritapyase  |
svalekhaḥ preṣyatāṃ devi śukahaste yathārthataḥ  || 97.31 ||
samudraṃ laṅghayitvā tu śakuntā yānti sundari  |
sakhivākyena sā rājñī svasthā jātā narādhipa  || 97.32 ||
vyāhṛto lekhakastatra likha lekhaṃ mamājñayā  |
tvaddhīnā satyabhāmādya vaso rājanna jīvati  || 97.33 ||
ṛtukālo'dya saṃjāto likha lekhaṃ tu lekhakaṃ  |
likhite bhūrjapatre tu lekhe vai lekhakena tu  || 97.34 ||
śukaḥ pañjaramadhyastha ānītoddhaiva sannidhau  || 97.35 ||

satyabhāmovāca -
nītvā lekhaṃ gaccha śīghraṃ vasurājñaḥ samīpataḥ  |
śakuniḥ praṇato bhūtvā gṛhītvā lekhamuttamam  || 97.36 ||
utpatya sahasā rājañjagāmākāśamaṇḍalam  |
tataḥ pakṣī gataḥ śīghraṃ vasurājasamīpataḥ  || 97.37 ||
kṣipte lekhe śukenaiva satyabhāmāvisarjite  |
vasurājñā tato lekho gṛhya haste'vadhāritaḥ  || 97.38 ||
lekhārthaṃ cintayitvā tu gṛhya vīryaṃ nareśvaraḥ  |
amoghaṃ puṭikāṃ kṛtvā pratilekhena miśritam  || 97.39 ||
śukasya so'payāmāsa gaccha rājñīsamīpataḥ  |
praṇamya vasurājānaṃ bījaṃ gṛhyotpapāta ha  || 97.40 ||
samudropari samprāptaḥ śukaḥ śyenena vīkṣitaḥ  |
sāmiṣaṃ taṃ śukaṃ jñātvā śyenastamabhyadhāvata  || 97.41 ||
hataścañcuprahāreṇa śukaḥ śyenena bhārata  |
mūrcchayā tasya tadbījaṃ patitaṃ sāgarāmbhasi  || 97.42 ||
matsyena gilitaṃ tacca bījaṃ vasumahīpateḥ  |
kanyā matsyodare jātā tena bījena sundari  || 97.43 ||
prāpto'sau lubdhakairmatsya ānītaḥ svagṛhaṃ tataḥ  |
yāvadvidārito matsyastāvaddṛṣṭā tvamuttame  || 97.44 ||
śaśimaṇḍalasaṅkāśā sūryatejaḥsamaprabhā  |
dṛṣṭvā tvāṃ harṣitāḥ sarve kaivartā jāhnavītaṭe  || 97.45 ||
harṣitāste gatāḥ sarve pradhānasya ca mandiram  |
strīratnaṃ kathayāmāsurgṛhāṇa tvaṃ mahāprabham  || 97.46 ||
gṛhītā tena tanvaṅgī hyaputreṇa mṛgekṣaṇā  |
bhāryāṃ svāmāha tanvaṅgi pālayasva mṛgekṣaṇe  || 97.47 ||
tataḥ sā cintayāmāsa parāśaravacastadā  |
evamuktvā tu sā tena dattātmānaṃ nareśvara  || 97.48 ||
uvāca sādhu me brahmanmatsyagandho'nu vartate  |
tatastena tu sā bālā divyagandhādhivāsitā  || 97.49 ||
kṛtā yogabalenaiva jvālayitvā vibhāvasum  |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ vahnimūḍhā tena rasāttadā  || 97.50 ||
jalayānasya madhye tu kāmasthānānyasaṃspṛśat |
jñātvā kāmotsukaṃ vipraṃ bhītā sā dharmanandana  || 97.51 ||
hasantī tamuvācātha deva tvaṃ lokasannidhau  |
na lajjase kathaṃ dhīmankurvāṇaḥ pāmarocitam  || 97.52 ||
tatastena kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā saṃsmṛtā hṛdi tāmasī  |
āgatā tāmasī māyā yayā vyāptaṃ carācaram  || 97.53 ||
tataḥ sā vismitā tena karmaṇaiva tu rañjitā  |
brahmacaryābhitaptena strīsaukhyaṃ krīḍitaṃ tadā  || 97.54 ||
tataḥ sā tatkṣaṇādeva garbhabhāreṇa pīḍitā  |
prasūtā bālakaṃ tatra jaṭilaṃ daṇḍadhāriṇam  || 97.55 ||
kamaṇḍaludharaṃ śāntaṃ mekhalākaṭibhūṣitam  |
uttarīyakṛtaskandhaṃ viṣṇumāyāvivarjitam  || 97.56 ||
tato'pi śaṅkitā pārtha dṛṣṭvā taṃ kalabālakam  |
vepamānā tato bālā jagāma śaraṇaṃ muneḥ  || 97.57 ||
rakṣa rakṣa muniśreṣṭha parāśara mahāmate  |
jātaṃ me'tyadbhutaṃ putraṃ kaupīnavaramekhalam  |
daṇḍahastaṃ jaṭāyuktamuttarīyavibhūṣitam  || 97.58 ||

parāśara uvāca -
mā bhaiṣīḥ svasute jāte kumārī tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  |
nāmnā yojanagandheti dvitīyaṃ satyavatyapi  || 97.59 ||
śaṃtanurnāma rājā yaḥ sa te bhartā bhaviṣyati  |
prathamā mahiṣī tasya somavaṃśavibhūṣaṇā  || 97.60 ||
gaccha tvaṃ svāśrayaṃ śubhre pūrvarūpeṇa saṃsthitā  |
mā viṣādaṃ kuruṣvātra dṛṣṭaṃ jñānasya me balam  || 97.61 ||
ityuktvā prayayau vipraḥ sā bālā putramāśritā  |
natvoce mātaraṃ bhaktyā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ vinayānataḥ  || 97.62 ||
kṣamyatāṃ mātaruktaṃ me prasādaḥ kriyatāmapi  |
īśvarārādhane yatnaṃ kariṣyāmyahamambike  || 97.63 ||
tataḥ sā putravākyena viṣaṇṇā vākyamabravīt || 97.64 ||

yojanagandhovāca -
mā tyaktvā gaccha vatsādya mātaraṃ māmanāgasam  |
tvadviyogena me putra pañcatvaṃ bhāvyasaṃśayam  || 97.65 ||
nāsti putrasamaḥ sneho nāsti bhrātṛsamaṃ kulam  |
nāsti satyaparo dharmo nānṛtātpātakaṃ param  || 97.66 ||
bālabhāve mayā jāta ādhāraḥ kila jāyase  |
na me bhartā na me putraḥ paśya karmaviḍambanam  || 97.67 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
mā viṣādaṃ kuruṣvāntaḥ satyametanmayoritam  |
āpatkāle'smi te devi smartavyaḥ kāryasiddhaye  || 97.68 ||
āpadastārayiṣyāmi kṣamyatāṃ me duruttaram  |
ityuktvā prayayau vyāsaḥ kanyā sāpi gatā gṛham  || 97.69 ||
parāśarasutastatra viṣaṣṇo vanamadhyataḥ  |
tretāyugāvasāne tu dvāparādau nareśvara  || 97.70 ||
vyāsārthaṃ cintayāmāsurdevāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ  |
ākhyāto nāradenaiva putraḥ parāśarasya saḥ  || 97.71 ||
kaivartaputrikājāto jñānī jahnusutātaṭe  |
tato nāradavākyena āgatāḥ surasattamāḥ  || 97.72 ||
rāmaḥ pitāmahaḥ śakro munisaṅghaiḥ samāvṛtāḥ  |
āsyādikaṃ pṛthagdattvā sādhu sādhvityudīrayan  || 97.73 ||
pitāmahena vai bālo garbhādhānādisaṃskṛtaḥ  |
dvīpāyano dvīpajanmā pārāśaryaḥ parāśarāt || 97.74 ||
kṛṣṇāṃśātkṛṣṇanāmāyaṃ vyāso vedānvyasiṣyati  |
virañcinābhiṣikto'sau munisaṅghaiḥ punaḥpunaḥ  || 97.75 ||
vyāsastvaṃ sarvalokeṣu ityuktvā prayayuḥ surāḥ  |
tīrthayātrā samārabdhā kṛṣṇadvaipāyanena tu  || 97.76 ||
gaṅgāvagāhitā tena kedāraśca sapuṣkaraḥ  |
gayā ca naimiṣaṃ tīrthaṃ kurukṣetraṃ sarasvatī  || 97.77 ||
ujjayinyāṃ mahākālaṃ somanāthaṃ prabhāsake  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ snātvā yāto mahāmuniḥ  || 97.78 ||
amṛtāṃ narmadāṃ prāpto rudradehodbhavāṃ śubhām  |
sāhlādo narmadāṃ dṛṣṭvā cittaviśrāntimāpa ca  || 97.79 ||
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ  || 97.80 ||
sārdravāsāśca hemante tiṣṭhandadhyau maheśvaram  |
svāntarhṛtkamale sthāpya dhyāyate parameśvaram  || 97.81 ||
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakartāramachedyaṃ varadaṃ śubham  |
nityaṃ siddheśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ pūjayeddhyānatatparaḥ  || 97.82 ||
arcanātsiddhaliṅgasya dhyānayogaprabhāvataḥ  |
pratyakṣaḥ śaṅkaro jātaḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasya saḥ  || 97.83 ||

īśvara uvāca -
toṣito'haṃ tvayā vatsa varaṃ varaya śobhanam  || 97.84 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva yadi deyo varo mama  |
pratyakṣo narmadātīre svayameva bhaviṣyasi  |
atītānāgatajño'haṃ tvatprasādādumāpate  || 97.85 ||

īśvara uvāca -
evaṃ bhavatu te putra matprasādādasaṃśayam  |
tvayi bhaktigṛhīto'haṃ pratyakṣo narmadātaṭe  || 97.86 ||
sahasrāṃśārdhabhāvena pratyakṣo'haṃ tvadāśrame  |
ityuktvā prayayau devaḥ kailāsaṃ nagamuttamam  || 97.87 ||
patnīsaṃgrahaṇaṃ jātaṃ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasya tu  |
śāstroktena vidhānena patnī pālayatastathā  || 97.88 ||
putro jāto hyaputrasya parāśarasutasya ca  |
devairvardhāpitaḥ sarvairiñcendrapurogamaiḥ  || 97.89 ||
putrajanmanyathājagmurvaśiṣṭhādyā munīśvarāḥ  |
tīrthayātrāprasaṅgena parāśarapurogamāḥ  || 97.90 ||
manvatriviṣṇuhārītayājñavalkyośano'ṅgirāḥ  |
yamāpastambasaṃvartāḥ kātyāyanabṛhaspatī  || 97.91 ||
evamādisahasrāṇi lakṣakoṭiśatāni ca  |
saśiṣyāśca mahābhāgā narmadātaṭamāśritāḥ  || 97.92 ||
vyāsāśrame śubhe ramye saṃtuṣṭā āyayurnṛpa  |
dṛṣṭvā tānso'pi viprendrānabhyutthānakṛtodyamaḥ  || 97.93 ||
pituḥ pūrvaṃ praṇamyādau sarveṣāṃ ca yathāvidhi  |
āsanāni dadau bhaktyā pādyamarghaṃ nyavedayat || 97.94 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vākyametaduvāca ha  |
uddhṛto'haṃ na sandeho yuṣmatsambhāṣaṇārcanāt || 97.95 ||
āraṇyāni ca śākāni phalānyāraṇyajāni ca  |
tāni dāsyāmi yuṣmākaṃ sarveṣāṃ prītipūrvakam  || 97.96 ||
nyamantrayata tānsarvānpratyekaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
tataste praṇataṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaṃ munim  || 97.97 ||
vardhayitvā jayāśīrbhiravalokya parasparam  |
parāśaraḥ samastaiśca vīkṣito munipuṃgavaiḥ  || 97.98 ||
uttaraṃ dīyatāṃ tāta kṛṣṇadvaipāyanasya ca  |
evamuktastu taiḥ sarvairbhagavānsa parāśaraḥ  |
provāca svātmajaṃ vyāsamṛṣīṇāṃ yaccikīrṣitam  || 97.99 ||

śrīparāśara uvāca -
necchanti dakṣiṇe kūle vratabhaṅgabhayādatha  |
bhojanaṃ bhoktukāmāste śrāddhe caiva viśeṣataḥ  || 97.100 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
karomi bhavatāmuktamatraiva sthīyatāṃ kṣaṇam  |
yāvatprasādya saritaṃ karomi vidhimuttamam  || 97.101 ||
evamuktvā śucirbhūtvā narmadātaṭamāsthitaḥ  |
stotraṃ jagāda sahasā tannibodha nareśvara  || 97.102 ||
jaya bhagavati devi namo varade jaya pāpavināśinī bahuphalade  |
jaya śumbhaniśumbhakapāladhare praṇamāmi tu devanarārtihare  || 97.103 ||
jaya candradivākaranetradhare jaya pāvakabhūṣitavaktravare  |
jaya bhairavadehanilīnapare jaya andhakaraktaviśoṣakare  || 97.104 ||
jaya mahiṣavimardini śūlakare jaya lokasamastakapāpahare  |
jaya devi pitāmaharāmanate jaya bhāskaraśakraśiro'vanate  || 97.105 ||
jaya ṣaṇmukhasāyudha īśanute jaya sāgaragāmini śambhunute  |
jaya duḥkhadaridravināśakare jaya putrakalatravivṛddhikare  || 97.106 ||
jaya devi samastaśarīradhare jaya nākavidarśini duḥkhahare  |
jaya vyādhivināśini mokṣakare jaya vāñchitadāyini siddhavare  || 97.107 ||
etadvyāsakṛtaṃ stotraṃ yaḥ paṭhecchivasannidhau  |
gṛhe vā śuddhabhāvena kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  || 97.108 ||
tasya vyāso bhavetprītaḥ prītaśca vṛṣavāhanaḥ  |
prītā syānnarmadā devī sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  || 97.109 ||
na te yānti yamālokaṃ yaiḥ stutā bhuvi narmadā  |
pitāmaho'pi muhyeta devi tvadguṇakīrtanāt || 97.110 ||
vākpatirnaiva te vaktuṃ svarūpaṃ veda narmade  |
kathaṃ guṇānahaṃ devi tvadīyāñjñātumutsahe  || 97.111 ||
iti jñātvā śuciṃ bhāvaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhiḥ  |
prasannā narmadādevī tato vacanamabravīt || 97.112 ||
satyavādena tuṣṭāhaṃ bhobho vyāsa mahāmune  |
yadīcchasi varaṃ kiṃcittaṃ te sarvaṃ dadāmyaham  || 97.113 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭāsi me devi yadi deyo varo mama  |
ātithyamuttare kūle ṛṣīṇāṃ dātumarhasi  || 97.114 ||

narmadovāca -
ayuktaṃ yācitaṃ vyāsa vimārge yatpravartanam  |
indracandrayamaiḥ śakyamunmārge na pravartitum  || 97.115 ||
yācasvānyaṃ varaṃ putra yatkiṃcidbhuvi durlabham  |
etacchrutvā vaco devyā vyāso mūrcchāṃ yatastadā  || 97.116 ||
vṛthā kleśo'dya me jāta iti matvā papāta ha  |
dharaṇī calitā sarvā saśailavanakānanā  || 97.117 ||
mūrcchāpannaṃ tato vyāsaṃ dṛṣṭvā devāḥ savāsavāḥ  |
hāhākāramukhāḥ sarve tatrājagmuḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 97.118 ||
vyāsamutthāpayāmāsurvedavyasanatatparam  |
brāhmaṇārthe ca saṃkliṣṭo nātmahetoḥ saridvare  || 97.119 ||
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe ca sadyaḥ prāṇānparityajet |
evaṃ sā narmadā proktā brahmādyaiḥ surasattamaiḥ  || 97.120 ||
suśītalaistaṃ bahubhiśca vātairrevābhyaṣiñcatsvajalena bhītā  |
sacetanaḥ satyavatīsuto'pi praṇamya devānsaritaṃ jagāda  || 97.121 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
tīrthaiḥ samastaiḥ kila sevanāya phalaṃ pradiṣṭaṃ mama mandabhāgyāt |
yaddevi puṇyā viphalā mamāśā āraṇyapuṣpāṇi yathā janānām  || 97.122 ||

narmadovāca -
yato yato māṃ hi mahānubhāva ninīṣate cittamilātale'tra  |
vindhyena sārddhaṃ tava mārgamadya yāsyāmyahaṃ daṇḍadharasya pṛṣṭhe  || 97.123 ||
evamukto mahātejā vyāsaḥ satyavatīsutaḥ  |
dakṣiṇe cālayāmāsa svāśramasya saridvarām  || 97.124 ||
daṇḍahasto mahātejā huṅkāramakaronmuniḥ  |
vyāsahuṅkārabhītā sā calitā rudranandinī  || 97.125 ||
daṇḍena darśayanmārgaṃ devī tatra pravartitā  |
vyāsamārgaṃ gatā devī dṛṣṭā śakrapurogamaiḥ  || 97.126 ||
puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ tato devā vyamuñcan saha kiṃkaraiḥ  |
kiṃ kurmo brūhi me putra karmaṇā te sma rañjitāḥ  || 97.127 ||

vyāsa uvāca -
tapaśca vipulaṃ kṛtvā dānaṃ dattvā mahāphalam  |
etadeva naraiḥ kāryaṃ sādhūnāṃ yatsukhāvaham  || 97.128 ||
yadi tuṣṭā mahābhāgā anugrāhyo hyahaṃ yadi  |
tasmānmamāśrame sarvaiḥ sthīyatāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 97.129 ||
ātithyaṃ śākaparṇena revāmṛtavimiśritam  |
pratipannaṃ samastairvaḥ parāśaramukhairmama  |
sthātavyaṃ svāśrame sarvairrevāyā uttare taṭe  || 97.130 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
snānatarpaṇanityāni kṛtāni dvijasattamaiḥ  |
vyāsakuṇḍe tato gatvā homaḥ sarvaiḥ prakalpitaḥ  || 97.131 ||
śrīphalairbilvapatraiśca juhuvurjātavedasam  |
gautamo bhṛgurmāṇḍavyo nārado lomaśastathā  || 97.132 ||
parāśarastathā śaṅkhaḥ kauśikaścyavano muniḥ  |
pippalādo vasiṣṭhaśca nāciketo mahātapāḥ  || 97.133 ||
viśvāmitro'pyagastyaśca uddālakayamau tathā  |
śāṇḍilyo jaiminiḥ kaṇvo yājñavalkyośano'ṅgirāḥ  || 97.134 ||
śātātapo dadhīciśca kapilo gālavastathā  |
jaigīṣavyastathā dakṣo bharato mudgalastathā  || 97.135 ||
vātsyāyano mahātejāḥ saṃvartaḥ śaktireva ca  |
jātūkarṇyo bharadvājo vālakhilyāruṇistathā  || 97.136 ||
evamādisahasrāṇi juhvate jātavedasam  |
akṣamālākarotkīrṇā dhyānayogaparāyaṇāḥ  || 97.137 ||
ekacittā dvijāḥ sarve cakrurhomakriyāṃ tadā  |
tataḥ samutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ mokṣadaṃ vyādhināśanam  || 97.138 ||
acchedyaṃ paramaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā vyāsastutoṣa ca  |
puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ dadurdevā āśīrvādāndvijottamāḥ  || 97.139 ||
sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇato vyāso devaṃ dṛṣṭvā trilocanam  |
brāhmaṇānpūjayāmāsa śākamūlaphalena ca  || 97.140 ||
pitṛpūrvaṃ dvijāḥ sarve bhojitāḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
āśīrvādāṃstataḥ puṇyān dattvā viprā yayuḥ punaḥ  || 97.141 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ vyāsākhyaṃ procyate budhaiḥ  || 97.142 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
vyāsatīrthasya yatpuṇyaṃ tatsarvaṃ kathayasva me  |
snānadānavidhānaṃ ca yasminkāle mahāphalam  || 97.143 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kathayāmi samastaṃ te bhrātṛbhiḥ saha pāṇḍava  |
kārttikasya site pakṣe caturdaśyāṃ jitendriyaḥ  || 97.144 ||
upoṣya yo naro bhaktyā rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  |
snāpayedīśvaraṃ bhaktyā kṣaudrakṣīreṇa sarpiṣā  || 97.145 ||
dadhnā ca khaṇḍayuktena kuśatoyena vai punaḥ  |
śrīkhaṇḍena sugandhena guṇṭhayetparameśvaram  || 97.146 ||
tataḥ sugandhakusumairbilvapatraiśca pūjayet |
mucukundena kundena kuśajātīprasūnakaiḥ  || 97.147 ||
unmattamunipuṣpaiśca tathānyaiḥ kālasambhavaiḥ  |
arcayetparayā bhaktyā dvīpeśvaramanuttamam  || 97.148 ||
ikṣugaḍukadānena tuṣyate parameśvaraḥ  |
gaḍukāṣṭakadānena pātakaṃ yātyahorjitam  || 97.149 ||
māsarjitaṃ ca naśyeta gaḍukāṣṭaśatena ca  |
ṣāṇmāsikaṃ sahasreṇa dviguṇairabdikaṃ tathā  || 97.150 ||
ājanmajanitaṃ pāpamayutena praṇaśyati  |
dviguṇairnaśyate vyādhistriguṇaiḥ syāddhanāgamaḥ  || 97.151 ||
ṣaḍguṇairjāyate vāgmī siddhastaddviguṇaistathā  |
rudratvaṃ daśalakṣaiśca jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 97.152 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha snānaṃ kurvīta bhaktitaḥ  |
mantroktena vidhānena sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 97.153 ||
vāruṇaṃ ca tathāgneyaṃ brāhmayaṃ caivākṣayaṃkaram  |
devānpitḥnmanuṣyāṃśca vidhivattarpayedbudhaḥ  || 97.154 ||
ṛcā ṛgvedajaṃ puṇyaṃ sāmnā sāmaphalaṃ labhet |
yajurvedasya yajuṣā gāyatryā sarvamāpnuyāt || 97.155 ||
akṣaraṃ ca japenmantraṃ sauraṃ vā śivadaivatam  |
athavā vaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ dvādaśākṣarasaṃjñitam  || 97.156 ||
pūjayedbrāhmaṇānbhaktyā sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitān  |
svadāraniratānviprāndambhalobhavivarjitān  || 97.157 ||
bhinnavṛttikarān pāpān patitāñchūdrasevanān  |
śūdrīgrahaṇasaṃyuktānvṛṣalī yasya mandire  || 97.158 ||
parokṣavādino duṣṭāngurunindāparāyaṇān  |
vedadveṣaṇaśīlāṃśca haitukān bakavṛttikān  || 97.159 ||
īdṛśānvarjayecchrāddhe dāne sarvavrateṣu ca  |
gāyatrīsāramātro'pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ  || 97.160 ||
nāyantritaścaturvedī sarvāśī sarvavikrayī  |
īdṛśānpūjayedviprānannadānahiraṇyataḥ  || 97.161 ||
upānahau ca vastrāṇi śayyāṃ chatramathāsanam  |
yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe bhaktyā so'pi svarge mahīyate  || 97.162 ||
pratyakṣā surabhī tatra jaladhenustathāghṛtā  |
tiladhenuḥ pradātavyā mahiṣyaśca tathaiva ca  || 97.163 ||
kṛṣṇājinapradātā yo dātā yastilasarpiṣoḥ  |
kanyāpustakayordātā so'kṣayaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 97.164 ||
dhūrvāhau khurasaṃyuktau dhānyopaskarasaṃyutau  |
dāpayetsvargakāmastu iti me satyabhāṣitam  || 97.165 ||
sūtreṇa veṣṭayeddvīpamathavā jagatīṃ śubham  |
mandiraṃ parayā bhaktyā parameśamathāpi vā  || 97.166 ||
pradakṣiṇāṃ vidhānena yaḥ karotyatra mānavaḥ  |
jambūplākṣāhvayau dvīpau śālmaliścāparo nṛpa  || 97.167 ||
kuśaḥ krauñcastathā kāśaḥ puṣkaraścaiva saptamaḥ  |
saptasāgaraparyantā veṣṭitā tena bhārata  || 97.168 ||
dvīpeśvare mahārāja vṛṣotsargaṃ ca kārayet |
vṛṣeṇāruṇavarṇena māheśaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 97.169 ||
yastu vai pāṇḍuro vaktre lalāṭe pādayostathā  |
lāṅgūle yastu vai śubhraḥ sa vai nākasya darśakaḥ  || 97.170 ||
nīlo'yamīdṛśaḥ prokto yastu dvīpeśvare tyajet |
sa samāḥ romasaṃkhyātā nāke vasati bhārata  || 97.171 ||
sauraṃ ca śāṃkaraṃ lokaṃ vairañcaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ kramāt |
bhunakti svecchayā rājanvyāsatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 97.172 ||
sapatnīkaṃ tato vipraṃ pūjayettatra bhaktitaḥ  |
sitaraktāni vastrāṇi yo dadyādagrajanmane  || 97.173 ||
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ yugmaṃ prīyatāṃ me jagadguruḥ  |
nāsti viprasamo bandhuriha loke paratra ca  || 97.174 ||
yamaloke mahāghore patantaṃ yo'bhirakṣati  |
itihāsapurāṇajñaṃ viṣṇubhaktaṃ jitendriyam  || 97.175 ||
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā sāmagaṃ vā viśeṣataḥ  |
dvīpeśvaraṃ ca ye bhaktyā saṃsmaranti gṛhe sthitāḥ  || 97.176 ||
na teṣāṃ jāyate śoko na hānirna ca duṣkṛtam  |
prathamaṃ pūjayettatra liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ tataḥ  || 97.177 ||
yatra siddho mahābhāgo vyāsaḥ satyavatīsutaḥ  |
asyaiva pūjanātsiddho dhārāsarpo mahāmatiḥ  || 97.178 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājanprāṇatyāgaṃ karoti ca  |
sūryalokamasau bhittvā prayāti śivasannidhau  || 97.179 ||
samāḥ sahasrāṇi ca sapta vai jale daśaikamagnau patane ca ṣoḍaśa  |
mahāhave ṣaṣṭiraśīti gograhe hyanāśake bhārata cākṣayā gatiḥ  || 97.180 ||
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  |
vāyubhūtaṃ nirīkṣante hyāgacchantaṃ svagotrajam  || 97.181 ||
asmadgotre'sti kaḥ putro yo no dadyāttilodakam  |
kārttikyāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa veśākhyāṃ vā tathaiva ca  || 97.182 ||
svargatiṃ ca prayāsyāmastatra tīrthopasevanāt |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ dvīpeśvaramanuttamam  || 97.183 ||
yaḥ paṭhetparayā bhaktyā śṛṇuyāttadgato nṛpa  |
so'pi pāpavinirmukto modate śivamandire  || 97.184 ||
ūṣaraṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ nirmitaṃ munipuṃgavaiḥ  |
kāmapradaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha vyāsatīrthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 97.185 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vyāsatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 98

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra prabhāseśvaramuttamam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu svargasopānamuttamam  || 98.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
prabhāsaṃ tāta me brūhi kathaṃ jātaṃ mahāphalam  |
svargasopānadaṃ dṛśyaṃ saṃkṣepātkathayasva me  || 98.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
durbhagā ravipatnī ca prabhānāmeti viśrutā  |
tayā cārādhitaḥ śambhurugreṇa tapasā purā  || 98.3 ||
vāyubhakṣā sthitā varṣaṃ varṣaṃ dhyānaparāyaṇā  |
tatastuṣṭo mahādevaḥ prabhāyāḥ pāṇḍunandana  || 98.4 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kasmātsaṃkliśyase bāle kathyatāṃ yadvivakṣitam  |
ahaṃ hi bhāskaro'pyeko nānātvaṃ naiva vidyate  || 98.5 ||

prabhovāca -
nānyo devaḥ striyaḥ śambho vinā bhartrā kvacitprabho  |
saguṇo nirguṇo vāpi dhanāḍhyo vāpyakiṃcanaḥ  || 98.6 ||
priyo vā yadi vā dveṣyaḥ strīṇāṃ bhartaiva daivatam  |
durbhagatvena dagdhāhaṃ sakhīmadhye sureśvara  |
bharttaryallabdhasaukhyāsmi tena kliśyāmyahaṃ bhṛśam  || 98.7 ||

īśvara uvāca -
vallabhā bhāskarasyaiva matprasādādbhaviṣyasi  || 98.8 ||

pārvatyuvāca -
apramāṇaṃ bhavadvākyaṃ bhāskaro'pi kariṣyati  |
vṛthā kleśo bhavedasyāḥ prabhāyāḥ parameśvara  || 98.9 ||
umāvākyānmaheśānadhyātastimiranāśanaḥ  |
āgato gaganādbhānurnarmadottararodhasi  || 98.10 ||

bhānuruvāca -
āhūto'smi kathaṃ deva hyaghāsuraniṣūdana  || 98.11 ||

īśvara uvāca -
prabhāṃ pālaya bho bhāno saṃtoṣeṇa pareṇa hi  || 98.12 ||

umovāca -
prabhāyā mandire nityaṃ sthīyatāṃ himanāśana  |
agrapatnī samastānāṃ bhāryāṇāṃ kriyatāṃ rave  || 98.13 ||

bhānuruvāca -
evaṃ devi kariṣyāmi tava vākyaṃ varānane  |
etacchrutvā prabhāhūtā pratyuvāca maheśvaram  || 98.14 ||

prabhovāca -
svāṃśena sthīyatāṃ deva manmathāre umāpate  |
ekāṃśaḥ sthāpyatāmatra tīrthasyonmīlanāya ca  || 98.15 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sarvadevamayaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ tatra pāṇḍava  |
prabhāseśa iti khyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu durlabham  || 98.16 ||
anyāni yāni tīrthāni kāle tāni phalanti vai  |
prabhāseśastu rājendra sadyaḥ kāmaphalapradaḥ  || 98.17 ||
māghamāse site pakṣe saptamyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
aśvaṃ yaḥ sparśayettatra yathoktabrāhmaṇe nṛpa  || 98.18 ||
indratvaṃ prāpyate tena bhāskarasyāthavā padam  |
snātvā paramayā bhaktyā dānaṃ dadyāddvijātaye  || 98.19 ||
gopradātā labhetsvargaṃ satyalokaṃ vareśvara  |
sarvāṅgasundarīṃ śubhrāṃ kṣīriṇīṃ taruṇīṃ śubhām  || 98.20 ||
savatsāṃ ghaṇṭāsaṃyuktāṃ kāṃsyapātrāvadohinīm  |
dadate ye nṛpaśreṣṭha na te yānti yamālayam  || 98.21 ||
atha yaḥ parayā bhaktyā snānaṃ devasya kārayet |
sa prāpnoti paraṃ lokaṃ yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 98.22 ||
daurbhāgyaṃ nāśamāyāti snānamātreṇa pāṇḍava  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā kanyādānaṃ prayacchati  || 98.23 ||
brāhmaṇāya vivāhena dāpayetpāṇḍunandana  |
samānavayase deyā kulaśīladhanaistathā  || 98.24 ||
ye dadante mahārāja hyapi pātakasaṃyutāḥ  |
teṣāṃ pāpāni līyante hyudake lavaṇaṃ yathā  || 98.25 ||
svāmidrohakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ nikṣepasyāpahāriṇi  |
mitraghne ca kṛtaghne ca kūṭasākṣyasamudbhavam  || 98.26 ||
tadgrāmodyānabhedotthaṃ paradāraniṣevaṇam  |
vārddhuṣikasya yatpāpaṃ yatpāpaṃ steyasambhavam  || 98.27 ||
kūpabhedodbhavaṃ yacca baiḍālavratadhāriṇaḥ  |
dāmbhikaṃ vṛkṣacchedotthaṃ vivāhasya niṣedhajam  || 98.28 ||
ārāmasthatarucchedamagamyāgamanodbhavam  |
svabhāryātyajane yacca parabhāryāsamīhanāt || 98.29 ||
brahmasvaharaṇe yacca garade govighātini  |
vidyāvikrayaṇotthaṃ ca saṃsargādyacca pātakam  || 98.30 ||
śvabiḍālavadhādghoraṃ sarpaśūdrodbhavaṃ tathā  |
bhūmihartuśca yatpāpaṃ bhūmihāriṇi caiva hi  || 98.31 ||
mā dadasveti yatpāpaṃ govahnibrāhmaṇeṣu ca  |
tatpāpaṃ yāti vilayaṃ kanyādānena pāṇḍava  || 98.32 ||
sa gatvā bhāskaraṃ lokaṃ rudraloke śubhe vrajet |
krīḍate rudralokastho yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 98.33 ||
sarvapāpakṣaye jāte śive bhavati bhāvanā  |
etadvrajati yastīrthaṃ prabhāsaṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 98.34 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ prāpya so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
gopradānaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃ param  |
praśastaṃ sarvakālaṃ hi caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 98.35 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe prabhāsatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 99

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  |
sthāpitaṃ vāsukīśaṃ tu samastāghaughanāśanam  || 99.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kasmācca kāraṇāttāta revāyā dakṣiṇe taṭe  |
vāsukīśasthāpito vai vistarādvada me guro  || 99.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etatsarvaṃ samāsthāya nṛtyaṃ śambhuścakāra vai  || 99.3 ||
śramādajāyata svedo gaṅgātoyavimiśritam  |
patantamurago'śnāti haramaulivinirgatam  || 99.4 ||
mandākinī tataḥ kruddhā vyālasyopari bhārata  |
prāpnuhyajagarattvaṃ hi bhujaṅga kṣudrajantuka  || 99.5 ||

vāsukiruvāca -
anugrāhyo'smi te pāpo durnayo'haṃ harādṛte  |
trailokyapāvanī puṇyā sarittvaṃ śubhalakṣaṇā  || 99.6 ||
saṃsāracchedanakarī hyārtānāmārtināśanī  |
svargadvāre sthitā tvaṃ hi dayāṃ kuru mayīśvari  || 99.7 ||

gaṅgovāca -
kuruṣva vipulaṃ vindhyaṃ tapastvaṃ śaṅkaraṃ prati  |
tataḥ prāpsyasi svaṃ sthānaṃ pannagatvaṃ mamājñayā  || 99.8 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato'sau tvarito vindhyaṃ nāgo gatvā nagaṃ śubham  |
tapastaptuṃ samārebhe śaṅkarārādhanodyataḥ  || 99.9 ||
nityaṃ dadhyau mahādevaṃ tryakṣaṃ ḍamarukodyatam  |
tato varṣaśate pūrṇa uparuddho jagadguruḥ  |
āgatastatsamīpaṃ tu ślakṣṇāṃ vāṇīmudāharat || 99.10 ||
varaṃ varaya me vatsa pannaga tvaṃ kṛtādara  || 99.11 ||

vāsukiruvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva varaṃ dāsyasi śaṅkara  |
prasādāttava deveśa bhūyānniṣpāpatā mama  |
tīrthaṃ kiṃcitsamākhyāhi sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 99.12 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pannaga tvaṃ mahābāho revāṃ gaccha śubhaṃkarīm  |
yāmye tasyāstaṭe puṇye snānaṃ kuru yathāvidhi  || 99.13 ||
ityuktvāntardadhe devo vāsukistvarayānvitaḥ  |
rūpeṇājagareṇaiva praviṣṭo narmadājalam  || 99.14 ||
mārgeṇa tasya saṃjātaṃ jāhnavyāḥ srota uttamam  |
nirdhūtakalmaṣaḥ sarpaḥ saṃjāto narmadājale  || 99.15 ||
sthāpitaḥ śaṅkarastatra narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
tato nāgeśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ prasiddhaṃ pāpanāśanam  || 99.16 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ snāpayenmadhunā śivam  |
vimuktakalmaṣaḥ sadyo jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 99.17 ||
aputrā ye narāḥ pārtha snānaṃ kurvanti saṅgame  |
te labhante sutāñchreṣṭhān kārttavīryopamāñchubhān  || 99.18 ||
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yaḥ kuryādupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
kurvanpramocayetpretānnarakānnṛpanandana  || 99.19 ||
sarpāṇāṃ ca bhayaṃ vaṃśe jñātivarge na jāyate  |
nirdoṣaṃ nandate tasya kulaṃ nāgaprasādataḥ  || 99.20 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ tava snehānnṛpottama  || 99.21 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nāgeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 100

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthaṃ paramarocanam  |
mārkaṇḍeśamiti khyātaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 100.1 ||
uttamaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ gīrvāṇairvanditaṃ śivam  |
guhyādguhyataraṃ putra nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  || 100.2 ||
sthāpitaṃ tu mayā pūrvaṃ svargasopānasaṃnibham  |
jñānaṃ tatraiva me jātaṃ prasādācchaṅkarasya ca  || 100.3 ||
anyastatraiva yo gatvā drupadāmantarjale japet |
sa pātakairaśeṣaśca mucyate pāṇḍunandana  || 100.4 ||
vācikairmānasaiśca vā karmajairapi pātakaiḥ  |
piṇḍikāṃ cāpyavaṣṭabhya yāmyāmāśāṃ ca saṃsthitaḥ  || 100.5 ||
yojayecchūlinaṃ bhaktyā dvātriṃśadbahurūpiṇam  |
dehapāte śivaṃ gacchediti me niścayo nṛpa  || 100.6 ||
ājyena bodhayeddīpamaṣṭamyāṃ niśi bhārata  |
svargalokamavāpnoti ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 100.7 ||
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yo bhaktyā kurvīta nṛpanandana  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 100.8 ||
iṅgudairbadarairbilvairakṣatena jalena vā  |
tarpayettatra yo vaṃśyānāpnuyājjanmanaḥ phalam  || 100.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 101

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
uttare narmadākūle yajñavāṭasya madhyataḥ  || 101.1 ||
saṃkarṣaṇamiti khyātaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pāpanāśanam  |
tapaścīrṇaṃ purā rājanbalabhadreṇa tatra vai  || 101.2 ||
gīrvāṇā api tatraiva saṃnidhau nṛpanandana  |
umayā sahitaḥ śambhuḥ sthitastatraiva keśavaḥ  || 101.3 ||
balabhadreṇa rājendra prāṇināmupakārataḥ  |
sthāpitaḥ parayā bhaktyā śaṅkaraḥ pāpanāśanaḥ  || 101.4 ||
yastatra snāti vai bhaktyā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  |
ekādaśyāṃ site pakṣe madhunā snāpayecchivam  || 101.5 ||
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yo bhaktyā pitḥṇāmatha dāpayet |
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ balabhadravaco yathā  || 101.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe saṃkarṣaṇatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 102

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
manmatheśaṃ tato gacchetsarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
snānamātrānnaro rājanyamalokaṃ na paśyati  || 102.1 ||
anapatyā yā ca nārī snāyādvai pāṇḍunandana  |
putraṃ sā labhate pārtha satyasaṅghaṃ dṛḍhavratam  || 102.2 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājañchuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ  |
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 102.3 ||
kāmikaṃ tīrtharājaṃ tu tādṛśaṃ na bhaviṣyati  |
trirātraṃ kurute rājansa golakṣaphalaṃ labhet || 102.4 ||
tatra nṛtyaṃ prakartavyaṃ tuṣyate parameśvaraḥ  |
gītavāditranirghoṣai rātrau jāgaraṇena ca  || 102.5 ||
eraṇḍyāṃ ca mahādevo dṛṣṭo me manmatheśvaraḥ  |
kiṃ samartho yamo ruṣṭo bhadro bhadrāṇi paśyati  || 102.6 ||
kāmena sthāpitaḥ śambhuretasmātkāmado nṛpa  |
sopānaḥ svargamārgasya pṛthivyāṃ manmatheśvaraḥ  || 102.7 ||
viśeṣaścātra sandhyāyāṃ śrāddhadāne ca bhārata  |
annadānena rājendra kīrtitaṃ phalamuttamam  || 102.8 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ tava bhaktyā tu bhārata  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ prakhyāto manmatheśvaraḥ  || 102.9 ||
godānaṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha trayodaśyāṃ prakārayet |
caitre māsi site pakṣe tatra gatvā jitendriyaḥ  || 102.10 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā devasyāgre nṛpottama  |
dīpaṃ bhaktyā ghṛtenaiva devasyāgre nivedayet || 102.11 ||
stryatha vā puruṣo vāpi samametatphalaṃ smṛtam  || 102.12 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe manmatheśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 103

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla eraṇḍīsaṅgamaṃ param  |
yacchrutaṃ vai mayā rājañchivasya vadataḥ purā  || 103.1 ||
etadeva purā praśnaṃ gauryā pṛṣṭastu śaṅkaraḥ  |
provāca nṛpaśārdūla guhyādguhyataraṃ śubham  || 103.2 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ nākhyātaṃ kasyacinmayā  |
revāyāścottare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
bhrūṇahatyāharaṃ devi kāmadaṃ putravardhanam  || 103.3 ||

pārvatyuvāca -
kathayasva mahādeva tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
bhrūṇahatyāharaṃ kasmātkāmadaṃ svargadarśanam  || 103.4 ||

īśvara uvāca -
atrirnāma mahādevi mānaso brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ  |
agnihotrarato nityaṃ devatātithipūjakaḥ  || 103.5 ||
somasaṃsthāśca saptaiva kṛtā vipreṇa pārvati  |
anasūyeti vikhyātā bhāryā tasya guṇānvitā  || 103.6 ||
pativratā patiprāṇā patyuḥ kāryahite ratā  |
evaṃ yāti tataḥ kāle na putrā na ca putrikā  || 103.7 ||
aparāhṇe mahādevi sukhāsīnau tu sundari  |
vadantau sukhaduḥkhāni pūrvavṛttāni yāni ca  || 103.8 ||

atriruvāca -
saumye śubhe priye kānte cārusarvāṅgasundari  |
vidyāvinayasampanne padmapatranibhekṣaṇe  || 103.9 ||
pūrṇacandranibhākāre pṛthuśroṇibharālase  |
na tvayā sadṛśī nārī trailokye sacarācare  || 103.10 ||
ratiputraphalā nārī paṭhyate vedavādibhiḥ  |
putrahīnasya yatsaukhyaṃ tatsaukhyaṃ mama sundari  || 103.11 ||
yathāhaṃ na tathā putraḥ samarthaḥ sarvakarmasu  |
punnāmanarakādbhadre jātamātreṇa sundari  || 103.12 ||
patantaṃ rakṣayeddevi mahāpātakinaṃ yadi  |
mahāghore gatā vāpi duṣṭakarmapitāmahāḥ  || 103.13 ||
taddharanti suputrāśca vaitaraṇyāṃ gatānapi  |
putreṇa lokāñjayati pautreṇa paramā gatiḥ  || 103.14 ||
atha putrasya pautreṇa pragacchedbrahma śāśvatam  |
nāsti putrasamo bandhuriha loke paratra ca  || 103.15 ||
ahaśca madhyarātre ca cintayānasya sarvadā  |
śuṣyanti mama gātrāṇi grīṣme nadyudakaṃ yathā  || 103.16 ||

anasūyovāca -
yattvayā śocitaṃ vipra tatsarvaṃ śocayāmyaham  |
tavodvegakaraṃ yacca tanme dahati cetasi  || 103.17 ||
yena putrā bhaviṣyanti āyuṣmanto guṇānvitāḥ  |
tatkāryaṃ ca samīkṣasva yena tuṣyetprajāpatiḥ  || 103.18 ||

atriruvāca -
tapastaptaṃ mayā bhadre jātamātreṇa duṣkaram  |
vratopavāsaniyamaiḥ śākāhāreṇa sundari  || 103.19 ||
kṣīṇadehastu tiṣṭhāmi hyaśakto'haṃ mahāvrate  |
tena śocāmi cātmānaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ mayā  || 103.20 ||

anasūyovāca -
bhartuḥ pativratā nārī ratiputravivardhinī  |
trivargasādhanā sā ca ślāghyā ca viduṣāṃ jane  || 103.21 ||
japastapastīrthayātrā mṛḍejyāmantrasādhanam  |
devatārādhanaṃ caiva strīśūdrapatanāni ṣaṭ || 103.22 ||
īdṛśaṃ tu mahādoṣaṃ strīṇāṃ tu vratasādhane  |
vadanti munayaḥ sarve yathoktaṃ vedabhāṣitam  || 103.23 ||
anujñātā tvayā brahmaṃstapastapsyāmi duṣkaram  |
putrārthitvaṃ samuddiśya toṣayāmi surottamān  || 103.24 ||

atriruvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe mama saṃtoṣakāriṇi  |
ājñātā tvaṃ mayā bhadre putrārthaṃ tapa āśraya  || 103.25 ||
devatānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ pitḥṇāmanṛṇo bhave  |
na bhāryāsadṛśo bandhustriṣu lokeṣu vidyate  || 103.26 ||
tena devāḥ praśaṃsanti na bhāryāsadṛśaṃ sukham  |
sanmukhe manmukhāḥ putrāḥ vilome tu parāṅmukhāḥ  || 103.27 ||
tena bhāryāṃ praśaṃsanti sadevāsuramānuṣāḥ  |
mahāvrate mahāprājñe sattvavati śubhekṣaṇe  || 103.28 ||
tapastapasva śīghraṃ tvaṃ putrārthaṃ tu mamājñayā  |
etadvākyāvasāne tu sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇatābravīt || 103.29 ||
tvatprasādena viprendra sarvānkāmānavāpnuyām  |
haṃsalīlāgatiḥ sā ca mṛgākṣī varavarṇinī  || 103.30 ||
niyamasthā tato bhūtvā samprāptā narmadāṃ nadīm  |
śivasvedodbhavāṃ devīṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanīm  || 103.31 ||
yasyā darśanamātreṇa naśyate pāpasañcayaḥ  |
snānamātreṇa vai yasyā aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 103.32 ||
ye pibanti mahādevi śraddadhānāḥ payaḥ śubham  |
somapānena tattulyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 103.33 ||
ye smaranti divā rātrau yojanānāṃ śatairapi  |
mucyante sarvapāpebhyo rudralokaṃ prayānti te  || 103.34 ||
narmadāyāḥ samīpe tu tāvubhau yojanadvaye  |
na paśyanti yamaṃ tatra ye mṛtā varavarṇini  || 103.35 ||
tatastaduttare kūle eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame śubhe  |
niyamasthā viśālākṣī śākāhāreṇa sundari  || 103.36 ||
toṣayantī trīṃśca devāñchubhaiḥ stotrairvrataistathā  |
grīṣmeṣu ca mahādevi pañcāgniṃ sādhayettataḥ  || 103.37 ||
varṣākāle cārdravāsāścareccāndrāyaṇāni ca  |
hemante tu tataḥ prāpte toyamadhye vasetsadā  || 103.38 ||
prātaḥsnānaṃ tataḥ sandhyāṃ kuryāddevarṣitarpaṇam  |
devānāmarcanaṃ kṛtvā homaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 103.39 ||
yajate vaiṣṇavāṃllokān snānajāpyahutena ca  |
evaṃ varṣaśate prāpte rudraviṣṇupitāmahāḥ  || 103.40 ||
samprāptā dvijarūpaistu eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame priye  |
purataḥ saṃsthitāstasyā vedamabhyuddharanti ca  || 103.41 ||
anasūyā japaṃ tyaktvā nirīkṣya tānmuhurmuhuḥ  |
utthitā sā viśālākṣī arghaṃ dattvā yathāvidhi  || 103.42 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ  |
darśanena tu viprāṇāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 103.43 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇatābravīt |
kandamūlaphalaṃ śākaṃ nīvārānapi pāvanān  |
prayacchāmyahamadyaiva munīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 103.44 ||
viprā ūcuḥ  |
tapasā tu vicitreṇa tapaḥsatyena suvrate  |
tṛptāḥ sma sarvakāmaistu suvrate tava darśanāt || 103.45 ||
asmākaṃ kautukaṃ jātaṃ tāpasena vratena yat |
svargamokṣasutasyārthe tapastapasi duṣkaram  || 103.46 ||

anasūyovāca -
tapasā sidhyate svargastapasā paramā gatiḥ  |
tapasā cārthakāmau ca tapasā guṇavānsutaḥ  |
tapa eva ca me viprāḥ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 103.47 ||
viprā ūcuḥ  |
tanvī śyāmā viśālākṣī snigdhāṅgī rūpasaṃyutā  |
haṃsalīlāgatigamā tvaṃ ca sarvāṅgasundarī  || 103.48 ||
kiṃ ca te tapasā kāryamātmānaṃ śocyase katham  || 103.49 ||

anasūyovāca -
yadi rudraśca viṣṇuśca svayaṃ sākṣātpitāmahaḥ  |
gūḍharūpadharāḥ sarve taccihnamupalakṣaye  || 103.50 ||
tasyā vākyāvasāne tu svarūpaṃ darśayanti te  |
svasvarūpaiḥ sthitā devāḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhāḥ  || 103.51 ||
caturbhujo mahādevi śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
atasīpuṣpavarṇastu pītavāsā janārdanaḥ  || 103.52 ||
garutmānvāhanaṃ yasya śriyā ca sahito hariḥ  |
prasannavadanaḥ śrīmānsvayaṃrūpo vyavasthitaḥ  || 103.53 ||
pītavāsā mahādevi caturvadanapaṅkajaḥ  |
haṃsopari samārūḍho hyakṣamālākarodyataḥ  || 103.54 ||
āgato narmadātīre brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
yo'sau sarvajagadvyāpī svayaṃ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ  || 103.55 ||
vṛṣabhaṃ tu samārūḍho daśabāhusamanvitaḥ  |
bhasmāṅgarāgaśobhāḍhyaḥ pañcavaktrastrilocanaḥ  || 103.56 ||
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃyuktaḥ kṛtacandrārddhaśekharaḥ  |
evaṃrūpadharo devaḥ sarvavyāpī maheśvaraḥ  || 103.57 ||
anasūyā nirīkṣyaitaddevānāṃ darśanaṃ param  |
vepamānā tataḥ sādhvī surāndṛṣṭvā muhurmuhuḥ  || 103.58 ||

anasūyovāca -
kiṃ vyāpārasvarūpāstu viṣṇurudrapitāmahāḥ  |
etadvai śrotumicchāmi hyaśeṣaṃ kathayantu me  || 103.59 ||

brahmovāca -
prāvṛṭkālo hyahaṃ brahmā āpaścaiva prakīrtitāḥ  |
megharūpo hyahaṃ prokto varṣayāmi ca bhūtale  || 103.60 ||
ahaṃ sarvāṇi bījāni prāksandhyāsūdite ravau  |
etadvai kāraṇaṃ sarvaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ param  || 103.61 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
hemantaśca bhavedviṣṇurviśvarūpaṃ carācaram  |
pālanāya jagatsarvaṃ viṣṇormāhātmyamuttamam  || 103.62 ||

rudra uvāca -
grīṣmakālo hyahaṃ proktaḥ sarvabhūtakṣayaṃkaraḥ  |
karṣayāmi jagatsarvaṃ rudrarūpastapasvini  || 103.63 ||
evaṃ brahmā ca viṣṇuśca rudraścaiva mahāvrate  |
trayo devāstrayaḥ sandhyāstrayaḥ kālāstrayo'gnayaḥ  || 103.64 ||
tathā brahmā ca viṣṇuśca rudraścaikātmatāṃ gataḥ  |
varaṃ dadyuśca te bhadre yastvayā manasīpsitam  || 103.65 ||

anasūyovāca -
dhanyā puṇyā hyahaṃ loke ślāghyā vandyā ca sarvadā  |
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca prasannavadanāḥ śubhāḥ  || 103.66 ||
yadi tuṣṭāstrayo devā dayāṃ kṛtvā mamopari  |
asmiṃstīrthe tu sāṃnidhyādvaradāḥ santu me sadā  || 103.67 ||

rudra uvāca -
evaṃ bhavatu te vākyaṃ yattvayā prārthitaṃ śubhe  |
pratyakṣā vaiṣṇavī māyā eraṇḍīnāma nāmataḥ  || 103.68 ||
yasyā darśanamātreṇa naśyate pāpasañcayaḥ  |
caitramāse tu samprāpte ahorātroṣito bhavet || 103.69 ||
eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame snātvā brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryātprabhāte bhojayeddvijān  || 103.70 ||
yathoktena vidhānena piṇḍaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi  |
pradakṣiṇāṃ tato dadyāddhiraṇyaṃ vastrameva ca  || 103.71 ||
rajataṃ ca tathā gāvo bhūmidānamathāpi vā  |
sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ proktamiti svāyambhuvo'bravīt || 103.72 ||
ye mriyanti narā devi eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame śubhe  |
yāvadyugasahasraṃ tu rudraloke vasanti te  || 103.73 ||
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā japedrudrāṃśca vaidikān  |
ekādaśaikasaṃjñāṃśca sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 103.74 ||
vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam  |
putrārthī labhate putrāṃllabhetkāmān yathepsitān  || 103.75 ||
eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgame snātvā revāyā vimale jale  |
mahāpātakino vāpi te yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 103.76 ||

anasūyovāca -
yadi tuṣṭāstrayo devā mama bhaktipracoditāḥ  |
mama putrā bhavantveva harirudrapitāmahāḥ  || 103.77 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
pūjyā yatputratāṃ yānti na kadācicchrutaṃ mayā  |
śubhe dadāmi putrāṃste devatulyaparākramān  |
rūpavanto guṇopetānyajvinaśca bahuśrutān  || 103.78 ||

anasūyovāca -
īpsitaṃ tacca dātavyaṃ yanmayā prārthitaṃ hare  |
nānyathā caiva kartavyā mama putraiṣaṇā tu yā  || 103.79 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
pūrvaṃ tu bhṛgusaṃvāde garbhavāsa upārjitaḥ  |
tasyāhaṃ caiva pāraṃ tu naiva paśyāmi śobhane  || 103.80 ||
smaramāṇaḥ purāvṛttaṃ cintayāmi punaḥpunaḥ  |
evaṃ saṃcintya te devāḥ pitāmahamaheśvarāḥ  || 103.81 ||
ayonijā bhaviṣyāmastava putrā varānane  |
yonivāse mahāprājñi devā naiva vrajanti ca  || 103.82 ||
sāṃnidhyātsaṅgame devi lokānāṃ tu varapradāḥ  |
eraṇḍī vaiṣṇavī māyā pratyakṣā tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 103.83 ||
trayo devāḥ sthitāḥ pātha revāyā uttare taṭe  |
varaprāptā tu sā devī gatā māhendraparvatam  || 103.84 ||
kṣīṇāṅgī śukladehā ca rūkṣakeśī sudāruṇā  |
kṛtayajñopavītā sā taponiṣṭhā śubhekṣaṇā  || 103.85 ||
śilātalaniviṣṭo'sau dṛṣṭaḥ kānto mahāyaśāḥ  |
hṛṣṭacitto'bhavaddevi uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha sābravīt || 103.86 ||

atriruvāca -
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe hyanasūye mahāvrate  |
acintyaṃ gālavādīnāṃ varaṃ prāptāsi durlabham  || 103.87 ||

anasūyovāca -
tvatprasādena devarṣe varaṃ prāptāsmi durlabham  |
tena devāḥ praśaṃsanti siddhāśca ṛṣayo'malāḥ  || 103.88 ||
evamuktā tu sā devī harṣeṇa mahatā yutā  |
ālokayettataḥ kāntaṃ tenāpi śubhadarśanā  || 103.89 ||
īkṣaṇāccaiva saṃjātaṃ lalāṭe maṇḍalaṃ śubham  |
navayojanasāhasraṃ maṇḍalaṃ raśmibhirvṛtam  || 103.90 ||
kadambagolakākāraṃ triguṇaṃ parimaṇḍalam  |
tasya madhye tu deveśi puruṣo divyarūpadhṛk || 103.91 ||
hemavarṇo'mṛtamayaḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ  |
ādyaḥ putro'nasūyāyāḥ svayaṃ sākṣātpitāmahaḥ  || 103.92 ||
candramā iti vikhyātaḥ somarūpo nṛpātmaja  |
iṣṭāpūrte ca saṃpāti kalāṣoḍaśakena tu  || 103.93 ||
pratipacca dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā ca maheśvari  |
caturthī pañcamī caiva avyayā ṣoḍaśī kalā  || 103.94 ||
caturvidhasya lokasya sūkṣmo bhūtvā varānane  |
āprīṇāti jagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 103.95 ||
sarve te hyupajīvanti hutaṃ dattaṃ śaśisthitam  |
vanaspatigate some dhanavāṃśca varānane  || 103.96 ||
bhuñjan paragṛhe mūḍho dadedabdakṛtaṃ śubham  |
vanaspatigate some yastu chindyādvanaspatīn  |
tena pāpena deveśi narā yānti yamālayam  || 103.97 ||
vanaspatigate some maithunaṃ yo niṣevate  |
brahmahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 103.98 ||
vanaspatigate some manthānaṃ yo'dhivāhayet |
gāvastasya praṇaśyanti yāśca vai pūrvasaṃcitāḥ  || 103.99 ||
vanaspatigate some hyadhvānaṃ yo'dhigacchati  |
bhavanti pitarastasya taṃ māsaṃ reṇubhojanāḥ  || 103.100 ||
amāvasyāṃ mahādevi yastu śrāddhaprado bhavet |
abdamekaṃ viśālākṣi tṛptāstatpitaro dhruvam  || 103.101 ||
hiraṇyaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ yo dadāti dvijātiṣu  |
sarvaṃ lakṣaguṇaṃ devi labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 103.102 ||
evaṃ guṇaviśiṣṭo'sau somarūpaḥ prajāpatiḥ  |
saṃjātaḥ prathamaḥ putro hyanasūyāsunandanaḥ  || 103.103 ||
dvitīyastu mahādevi durvāsā nāma nāmataḥ  |
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakartā ca svayaṃ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ  || 103.104 ||
ṛṣimadhyagato devi tapastapati duṣkaram  |
so'pi rudratvamāyāti samprāpte bhūtaviplave  || 103.105 ||
indro'pi śaptastenaiva durvāsasā varānane  |
dvitīyasya tu putrasya sambhavaḥ kathito mayā  || 103.106 ||
dattātreyasvarūpeṇa bhagavānmadhusūdanaḥ  |
jagadvyāpī jagannāthaḥ svayaṃ sākṣājjanārdanaḥ  || 103.107 ||
ete devāstrayaḥ putrā anasūyāyā maheśvari  |
varadānena te devā hyavatīrṇā mahītale  || 103.108 ||
putraprāptikaraṃ tīrthaṃ revāyāścottare taṭe  |
anasūyākṛtaṃ pārtha sarvapāpakṣayaṃ param  || 103.109 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
āścaryabhūtaṃ loke'sminnarmadāyāṃ purātanam  |
bhrūṇahatyā gatā tatra brāhmaṇasya narādhipa  || 103.110 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
itihāsaṃ dvijaśreṣṭha kathayasva mamānagha  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ loke duḥkhārtasya ca kathyatām  || 103.111 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
suvarṇaśilake grāme gautamānvayasambhavaḥ  |
kṛṣīvalo mahādevi bhāryāputrasamanvitaḥ  || 103.112 ||
vasate tatra govindaḥ saṃjāto vipule kule  |
putradārasamopeto gṛhakṣetrarataḥ sadā  || 103.113 ||
śakaṭaṃ pūrayitvā tu kāṣṭhānāmagamadguham  |
prakṣiptāni ca kāṣṭhāni hyekākī kṣudhayānvitaḥ  || 103.114 ||
riṅgamāṇastadā putraḥ pituḥ śabdātsamāgataḥ  |
na dṛṣṭastena vai putraḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ saṃchādito'vaśaḥ  || 103.115 ||
āgatastvarito gehe pipāsārto narādhipa  |
śakaṭaṃ mocya taddvāri savṛṣaṃ rajjusaṃyutam  || 103.116 ||
bhāryā tasyaiva yā dṛṣṭā cittajñā vaśavartinī  |
dṛṣṭvā nipātitaṃ putraṃ kāṣṭhairnirbhinnamastakam  || 103.117 ||
ajalpamānākaruṇaṃ nikṣiptaṃ jñolikāṃ śiśum  |
śuśrūṣaṇe ratā sādhvī priyasya ca narādhipa  || 103.118 ||
tataḥ snānādikaṃ kṛtvā bhojanācchayanaṃ śubham  |
putraṃ putravatāṃ śreṣṭhā hyutthāpayati śāsanaiḥ  || 103.119 ||
yadā ca notthitaḥ suptaḥ putraḥ pañcatvamāgataḥ  |
tadā sā dīnavadanā ruroda ca mumoha ca  || 103.120 ||
tacchrutvā ruditaṃ śabdaṃ govindastrastamānasaḥ  |
kimetaditi coktvā tu patito dharaṇītale  || 103.121 ||
dvāvetau muktakeśau tu bhūmau nipatitau nṛpa  |
vilepāte ca rājendra niḥśvāsocchvāsitena ca  || 103.122 ||
kaṃ paśye prāṅgaṇe putraṃ dṛṣṭvā krīḍantamāturam  |
saṃdhārayiṣye hṛdayaṃ sphuṭitaṃ tava kāraṇe  || 103.123 ||
tvajjanmāntaṃ yaśo nityamakṣayāṃ kulasantatim  |
dṛṣṭvā kimanṛṇībhūto yāsyāmi paramāṃ gatim  || 103.124 ||
mama vṛddhasya dīnasya gatistvaṃ kila putraka  |
ete manorathāḥ sarve cintitā viphalā gatāḥ  || 103.125 ||
imāṃ tu vikalāṃ dīnāṃ vihīnāṃ sutabāndhavaiḥ  |
rudantīṃ patitāṃ pāhi mātaraṃ dharaṇītale  || 103.126 ||
punnāmno narakādyasmātpitaraṃ trāyate sutaḥ  |
tena putra iti proktaḥ svayameva svayambhuvā  || 103.127 ||
aputrasya gṛhaṃ śūnyaṃ diśaḥ śūnyā hyabāndhavāḥ  |
mūrkhasya hṛdayaṃ śūnyaṃ sarvaśūnyaṃ daridratā  || 103.128 ||
mṛṣāyaṃ vadate lokaścandanaṃ kila śītalam  |
putragātrapariṣvaṅgaścandanādapi śītalaḥ  || 103.129 ||
śmaśrugrahaṇakrīḍantaṃ dhūlidhūsaritānanam  |
puṇyahīnā na paśyanti nijotsaṅgasamāsthitam  || 103.130 ||
digambaraṃ gatavrīḍaṃ jaṭilaṃ dhūlidhūsaram  |
puṇyahīnā na paśyanti gaṅgādharamivātmajam  || 103.131 ||
vīṇāvādyasvaro loke susvaraḥ śrūyate kila  |
ruditaṃ bālakasyaiva tasmādāhlādakārakam  || 103.132 ||
mṛgapakṣiṣu kākeṣu paśūnāṃ svarayoniṣu  |
putraṃ teṣu samasteṣu vallabhaṃ bruvate budhāḥ  || 103.133 ||
matsyāśvaprakarāścaiva kūrmagrāhādayo'pi vā  |
putrotpattau ca hṛṣyanti vipattau yānti duḥkhitām  || 103.134 ||
devagandharvayakṣāśca hṛṣyante putrajanmani  |
pañcatve te'pi śocanti mandabhāgyo'smi putraka  || 103.135 ||
ṛṣimelāpakaṃ cakre putrārthe rāghavo nṛpa  |
indrasthāne sthitastasya prokṣate hyāsanaṃ yataḥ  || 103.136 ||
svargavāsaṃ sutādbāhyaṃ vidyate na tu pāṇḍava  |
cakre daśarathastasmātputrārthaṃ yajñamuttamam  || 103.137 ||
rāmo lakṣmaṇaśatrughnau bharatastatra sambhavāt |
kārtavīryo jito yena rāmeṇāmitatejasā  || 103.138 ||
sa rāmo rāmacandreṇa aṣṭavarṣeṇa nirjitaḥ  |
ekākinā hato vālī plavagaḥ śatrudurjayaḥ  || 103.139 ||
rāvaṇo brahmaputro yastrailokyaṃ yasya śaṅkate  |
hataḥ sa rāmacandreṇa saputraḥ sahabāndhavaḥ  || 103.140 ||
evaṃ putraṃ vinā saukhyaṃ martyaloke na vidyate  |
vaṃśārthe maithunaṃ yasya svargārthe yasya bhāratī  || 103.141 ||
mṛṣṭānnaṃ brāhmaṇasyārthe svarge vāsaṃ tu yānti te  |
brahmahatyāśvamedhābhyāṃ na paraṃ pāpapuṇyayoḥ  || 103.142 ||
putrotpattivipattibhyāṃ na paraṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ  |
kiṃ bravīmīti bho vatsa na tu saukhyaṃ sutaṃ vinā  || 103.143 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ duḥkhaṃ pralapitvā punaḥpunaḥ  |
janaiścāśvāsito vipro bālaṃ gṛhya bahirgataḥ  || 103.144 ||
tataḥ saṃskṛtya taṃ bālaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  |
samavetau tu duḥkhārtāvāgatau svagṛhaṃ punaḥ  || 103.145 ||
evaṃ gṛhāgate vipre rātrirjātā yudhiṣṭhira  |
bhūmau prasupto govindaḥ putraśokena pīḍitaḥ  || 103.146 ||
yāvannirīkṣate bhāryā bhartāraṃ duḥkhapīḍitam  |
kṛmirāśigataṃ sarvaṃ govindaṃ samapaśyata  || 103.147 ||
duḥkhādduḥkhatare magnā dṛṣṭvā taṃ pātakānvitam  |
evaṃ duḥkhanimagnāyāḥ śarvarī vigatā tadā  || 103.148 ||
paśupālastu mahiṣīmuktvāraṇye'gamadgṛhāt |
araṇye mahiṣīḥ sarvā rakṣayitvā gṛhāgataḥ  || 103.149 ||
vijñaptaḥ paśupālena govindo brāhmaṇottamaḥ  |
yāvadbhokṣyāmyahaṃ svāminmahiṣīstvaṃ ca rakṣase  || 103.150 ||
tataḥ sa tvarito vipro jagāma mahiṣīḥ prati  |
na tatra mahiṣīḥ paśyetpaścātkṣetrābhisammukham  || 103.151 ||
dhāvamānaśca viprastu eraṇḍīsaṅgame gataḥ  |
tataḥ praviṣṭastu jale revairaṇḍyostu saṅgame  || 103.152 ||
tajjalaṃ pītamātraṃ tu tvarayā cātitarṣitaḥ  |
akāmātsalilaṃ pītvā prakṣālya nayane śubhe  || 103.153 ||
ājagāma tataḥ paścādbhavanaṃ divasakṣaye  |
bhuktvā duḥkhānvito rātrau govindaḥ śayanaṃ yayau  || 103.154 ||
nidrābhibhūtaḥ śokena śrameṇaiva tu kheditaḥ  |
punastaccārdharātre tu tasya bhāryā yudhiṣṭhira  || 103.155 ||
kṛmibhirveṣṭitaṃ gāntraṃ kvacitpaśyatyaveṣṭitam  |
punaḥ sā vismayāviṣṭā tasya bhāryā guṇānvitā  |
uvāca duṣkṛtaṃ tasya sādhvasāviṣṭacetasā  || 103.156 ||

bhāryovāca -
atīte pañcame cāhni tvindhanaṃ kṣipatastu te  |
gṛhapaścādgato bālo hyajñānādghātitastvayā  || 103.157 ||
mayā tatpātakaṃ ghoraṃ rahasyaṃ na prakāśitam  |
tena pracchannapāpena dahyamānā divāniśam  || 103.158 ||
na sukhaṃ tava gātrasya paśyāmi na hi cātmanaḥ  |
nidrā mama śamaṃ yātā ratiścaiva tvayā saha  || 103.159 ||
śrūyate mānave śāstre śloko gīto maharṣibhiḥ  |
smṛtvā smṛtvā tu taṃ citte paritāpo na śāmyati  || 103.160 ||
kīrtanānnaśyate dharmo vardhate'sau nigūhanāt |
iha loke pare caiva pāpasyāpyevameva ca  || 103.161 ||
evaṃ saṃcityamānāhaṃ sthitā rātrau bhayāturā  |
kṛmirāśigataṃ tvāṃ hi kasyāhaṃ kathayāmi kim  || 103.162 ||
punastvaṃ cādya me dṛṣṭo bhrūṇahatyākṛmiśritaḥ  |
kvacidbhindanti te gātraṃ kvacinnaṣṭāḥ samantataḥ  || 103.163 ||
etatsaṃsmṛtya saṃsmṛtya vimṛśāmi punaḥpunaḥ  |
na jāne kāraṇaṃ kiṃcitpṛcchantyāḥ kathayasva me  || 103.164 ||
taḍāgaṃ vā saridvāpi tīrthaṃ vā devatārcanam  |
yaṃ gato'si prabhāvo'yaṃ tasya nānyasya me sthitam  || 103.165 ||
evamuktastu vipro'sau kathayāmāsa bhārata  |
bhāryāyā yaddivā vṛttaṃ śaṅkamāno nṛpottama  || 103.166 ||
adyāhaṃ mahiṣīsārthaṃ eraṇḍīsaṅgamaṃ gataḥ  |
nābhimātre jale gatvā pītavānsalilaṃ bahu  || 103.167 ||
nānyattīrthaṃ vijānāmi saritaṃ sara eva vā  |
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ kathitaṃ tava bhāmini  || 103.168 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu sā sarvamupavāsakṛtakṣaṇā  |
sapatnīko gatastatra saṅgame varavarṇini  || 103.169 ||
snātvā tatra jale ramye natvā devaṃ tu bhāskaram  |
snāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ śaṅkaraṃ comayā saha  || 103.170 ||
pañcagavyaghṛtakṣīrairdadhikṣaudraghṛtairjalaiḥ  |
gandhamālyādidhūpaiśca naivedyaiśca suśobhanaiḥ  || 103.171 ||
pūjya trayīmayaṃ liṅgaṃ devīṃ kātyāyanīṃ śubhām  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā patyāsi pativratā  || 103.172 ||
tataḥ prabhāte vimale dvijānsampūjya yatnataḥ  |
godānena hiraṇyena vastreṇānnena bhārata  || 103.173 ||
govindaḥ pūjayāmāsa svaśaktyā brāhmaṇāñchubhān  |
muktapāpo gṛhāyātaḥ svabhāryāsahito nṛpa  || 103.174 ||
evaṃ yaḥ śṛṇute bhaktyā govindākhyānamuttamam  |
paṭhate parayā bhaktyā bhrūṇahatyā praṇaśyati  || 103.175 ||
krīḍate śāṃkare loke yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
yaścaivāśvayuje māsi caitre vā nṛpasattama  || 103.176 ||
saptamyāṃ ca site pakṣe sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
sāttvikīṃ vāsanāṃ kṛtvā yo vasecchivamandire  || 103.177 ||
dhyāyamāno virūpākṣaṃ triśūlakarasaṃsthitam  |
kaṃsāsuranihantāraṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam  || 103.178 ||
pakṣirājasamārūḍhaṃ trailokyavaradāyakam  |
pitāmahaṃ tato dhyāyeddhaṃsasthaṃ caturānanam  || 103.179 ||
sargapradaṃ samastasya kamalākaraśobhitam  |
yo hyevaṃ vasate tatra triyame sthāna uttame  || 103.180 ||
tataḥ prabhāte vimale hyaṣṭamyāṃ ca narādhipa  |
brāhmaṇān pūjayedbhaktyā sarvadoṣavivarjitān  || 103.181 ||
sarvāvayavasampūrṇānsarvaśāstraviśāradān  |
vedābhyāsaratānnityaṃ svadāraniratānsadā  || 103.182 ||
śrāddhe dāne vrate yogyān brāhmaṇān pāṇḍunandana  |
pretānāṃ pūjanaṃ tatra devapūrvaṃ samārabhet || 103.183 ||
pretatvānmucyate śīghrameraṇḍyāṃ piṇḍatarpaṇaiḥ  |
dānāni tatra deyāni hyannamukhyāni sarvadā  || 103.184 ||
hiraṇyabhūmikanyāśca dhūrvāhau śubhalakṣaṇau  |
sīreṇa sahitau pārtha dhānyaṃ droṇakasaṃkhyayā  || 103.185 ||
alaṃkṛtāṃ savatsāṃ ca kṣīriṇīṃ taruṇīṃ sitām  |
raktāṃ vā kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ vā pāṭalāṃ kapilāṃ tathā  || 103.186 ||
kāṃsyadohanasaṃyuktāṃ rukmakhuravibhūṣaṇām  |
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ savatsāṃ ca brāhmaṇāyopapādayet || 103.187 ||
prīyatāṃ me jagannāthā harakṛṣṇapitāmahāḥ  |
saṃsārarakṣaṇī devī surabhī māṃ samuddharet || 103.188 ||
putrārthaṃ yāḥ striyaḥ pārtha hyeraṇḍīsaṅgame nṛpa  |
snāpyante rudrasūktaiśca caturvedodbhavaistathā  || 103.189 ||
caturbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ śastaṃ dvābhyāṃ yogyaiśca kārayet |
ekena sārdrakumbhena dāmpatyamabhiṣecayet || 103.190 ||
daivajñenaiva caikena athavā sāmagena vā  |
pañcaratnasamāyuktaṃ kumbhe tatraiva kārayet || 103.191 ||
gandhatoyasamāyuktaṃ sarvauṣadhivimiśritam  |
āmrapallavasaṃyuktamaśvatthamadhukaṃ tathā  || 103.192 ||
guṇṭhitaṃ sitavastreṇa sitacandanacarcitam  |
sitapuṣpaistu saṃchannaṃ siddhārthakṛtamadhyamam  || 103.193 ||
kāṃsyapātre tu saṃsthāpya putrārthī deśikottamaḥ  |
aṅgalagnaṃ tu yadvastraṃ kaṭakābharaṇaṃ tathā  || 103.194 ||
tatsarvaṃ maṇḍale tyājyaṃ siddhyarthaṃ cātmanastadā  |
praṇamya bhāskaraṃ paścādācāryaṃ rudrarūpiṇam  || 103.195 ||
madhuraṃ ca tato'śnīyāddevyā bhuvana uttame  |
phaladānaṃ ca viprāya chatraṃ tāmbūlameva ca  || 103.196 ||
upānahau ca yānaṃ ca sa bhavedduḥkhavarjitaḥ  |
bhāskare krīḍate loke yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 103.197 ||
dānaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ sarvaṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  |
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃkṣayam  || 103.198 ||
evaṃ pāpāni naśyanti hyeraṇḍīsaṅgame nṛṇām  |
samantācchastrapātena hyeraṇḍīsaṅgame nṛpa  || 103.199 ||
bhrūṇahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ naśyate śaṅkaro'bravīt |
prāṇatyāgaṃ ca yo bhaktyā jātavedasi kārayet || 103.200 ||
anāśakaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha jale vā tadanantaram  |
pañcasāhasrikaṃ mānaṃ varṣāṇāṃ jātavedasi  || 103.201 ||
jale trīṇi sahasrāṇyanāśake ṣaṣṭiṃ bhuñjate  |
kākā bakāḥ kapotāśca hyulūkāḥ paśavastathā  || 103.202 ||
saṃgamodakasaṃspṛṣṭāste yānti paramāṃ gatim  |
vṛkṣāśca tatpadaṃ jñātvā yāṃ gatiṃ yānti yoginaḥ  || 103.203 ||
eraṇḍikā mayā devī dṛṣṭo me manmatheśvaraḥ  |
kiṃ samartho yamo ruṣṭo bhadro bhadrāṇi paśyati  || 103.204 ||
mṛttikāṃ saṅgamodbhūtāṃ ye ca guṇṭhanti nityaśaḥ  |
bhrūṇahatyādipāpāni naśyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 103.205 ||
eraṇḍīsaṅgame martyo luṇṭhyamāno narādhipa  |
sarvapāpairvinirmuktaḥ padaṃ gacchatyanāmayam  || 103.206 ||
eraṇḍyāḥ saṅgamaṃ martyāḥ kīrtayantyāśramasthitāḥ  |
vimuktapāpā jāyante satyaṃ śaṅkarabhāṣitam  || 103.207 ||
eraṇḍīpādapāgraistu dṛṣṭaiḥ pāpaṃ vyapohati  || 103.208 ||
tīrthākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ ye paṭhiṣyanti mānavāḥ  |
śṛṇvanti cāpare bhaktyā muktapāpā bhavanti te  || 103.209 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātameraṇḍīsaṅgamaṃ nṛpa  |
bhūyaścānyatpravakṣyāmi sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 103.210 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe eraṇḍīsaṅgamatīrthaphalamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tryadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 104

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpālaṃ sauvarṇaśilamuttamam  |
prakhyātamuttare kūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 104.1 ||
samantācchatapātena munisaṅghaiḥ purā kṛtam  |
revāyāṃ durlabhaṃ sthānaṃ saṅgamasya samīpataḥ  || 104.2 ||
vibhaktaṃ hastamātraṃ ca puṇyakṣetraṃ narādhipa  |
suvarṇaśilake snātvā pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 104.3 ||
natvā tu bhāskaraṃ devaṃ hotavyaṃ ca hutāśane  |
bilvenājyavimiśreṇa bilvapatrairathāpi vā  || 104.4 ||
prīyatāṃ me jagannātho vyādhirnaśyatu me dhruvam  |
dvijāya kāñcane datte yatphalaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva me  || 104.5 ||
bahusvarṇasya yatproktaṃ yāgasya phalamuttamam  |
tathāsau labhate sarvaṃ kāñcanaṃ yaḥ prayacchati  || 104.6 ||
tena dānena pūtātmā mṛtaḥ svargamavāpnuyāt |
rudrasyānucarastāvadyāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 104.7 ||
tataḥ svargāvatīrṇastu jāyate viśade kule  |
dhanadhānyasamopetaḥ punaḥ smarati tajjalam  || 104.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe suvarṇaśilātīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 105

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
karañjākhyaṃ tato gacchetsopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
tatra snātvā tu rājendra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 105.1 ||
arcayitvā mahādevaṃ dattvā dānaṃ tu bhaktitaḥ  |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi maṇimauktikavidrumān  || 105.2 ||
pādukopānahau chatraṃ śayyāṃ prāvaraṇāni ca  |
koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ sarvaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 105.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe karañjatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 106

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
saubhāgyakaraṇaṃ divyaṃ naranārīmanoramam  || 106.1 ||
tatra yā durbhagā nārī naro vā nṛpasattama  |
snātvārcayedumārudrau saubhāgyaṃ tasya jāyate  || 106.2 ||
tṛtīyāyāmahorātraṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
nimantrayeddvijaṃ bhaktyā sapatnīkaṃ surūpiṇam  || 106.3 ||
gandhamālyairalaṃkṛtya vastradhūpādivāsitam  |
bhojayetpāyasānnena kṛsareṇātha bhaktitaḥ  || 106.4 ||
bhojayitvā yathānyāyaṃ pradakṣiṇamudāharet |
prīyatāṃ me mahādevaḥ sapatnīko vṛṣadhvajaḥ  || 106.5 ||
yathā te devadeveśa na viyogaḥ kadācana  |
mamāpi karuṇāṃ kṛtvā tathāstviti vicintayet || 106.6 ||
evaṃ kṛte tatastasya yatpuṇyaṃ samudāhṛtam  |
tatte sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā devena bhāṣitam  || 106.7 ||
daurbhāgyaṃ durgatiścaiva dāridryaṃ śokabandhanam  |
vandhyatvaṃ saptajanmāni jāyate na yudhiṣṭhira  || 106.8 ||
jyeṣṭhamāse site pakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
tatra gatvā tu yo bhaktyā pañcāgniṃ sādhayettataḥ  || 106.9 ||
so'pi pāpairaśeṣaistu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
guggulaṃ dahate yastu dvidhā cittavivarjitaḥ  || 106.10 ||
śarīraṃ bhedayedyastu gauryāścaiva samīpataḥ  |
tasminkarmapraviṣṭasya utkrāntirjāyate yadi  || 106.11 ||
dehapāte vrajetsvargamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt |
sitaraktaistathā pītairvastraiśca vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 106.12 ||
brāhmaṇīṃ brāhmaṇaṃ caiva pūjayitvā yathāvidhi  |
puṣpairnānāvidhaiścaiva gandhadhūpaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  || 106.13 ||
kaṇṭhasūtrakasindūraiḥ kuṅkumena vilepayet |
kalpayeta striyaṃ gaurīṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śivarūpiṇam  || 106.14 ||
teṣāṃ tadrūpakaṃ kṛtvā dānamutsṛjyate tataḥ  |
kaṅkaṇaṃ karṇaveṣṭaṃ ca kaṇṭhikāṃ mudrikāṃ tathā  || 106.15 ||
saptadhānyaṃ tathā caiva bhojanaṃ nṛpasattama  |
anyānyapi ca dānāni tasmiṃstīrthe dadāti yaḥ  || 106.16 ||
sarvadānaiśca yatpuṇyaṃ prāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
sahasraguṇitaṃ sarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 106.17 ||
śaṅkareṇa samaṃ tasmādbhogaṃ bhuṅkte hyanuttamam  |
saubhāgyaṃ tasya vipulaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 106.18 ||
aputro labhate putramadhano dhanamāpnuyāt |
rājendra kāmadaṃ tīrthaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 106.19 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kāmadatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||


ṛKV adhyāya 107

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheta rājendra bhaṇḍārītīrthamuttamam  |
daridracchedakaraṇaṃ yugānyekonaviṃśatiḥ  || 107.1 ||
dhanadena tapastaptvā prasanne padmasambhave  |
tatraiva svalpadānena prāptaṃ vittasya rakṣaṇam  || 107.2 ||
tatra gatvā tu yo bhaktyā snātvā vittaṃ prayacchati  |
tasya vittaparicchedo na kadācidbhaviṣyati  || 107.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhaṇḍārītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 108

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla rohiṇītīrthamuttamam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu sarvapāpaharaṃ param  || 108.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
rohiṇītīrthamāhātmyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
śrotumicchāmi tattvena tanme tvaṃ vaktumarhasi  || 108.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasminnekārṇave ghore naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame  |
udadhau ca śayānasya devadevasya cakriṇaḥ  || 108.3 ||
nābhau samutthitaṃ padmaṃ ravimaṇḍalasannibham  |
karṇikākesaropetaṃ patraiśca samalaṃkṛtam  || 108.4 ||
tatra brahmā samutpannaścaturvadanapaṅkajaḥ  |
kiṃ karomīti deveśa ājñā me dīyatāṃ prabho  || 108.5 ||
evamuktastu deveśaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ  |
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ tadā devaṃ pitāmaham  || 108.6 ||
sarasvatyāṃ mahābāho lokaṃ kuru mamājñayā  |
bhūtagrāmamaśeṣasya utpādanavidhikṣayam  || 108.7 ||
etacchrutaṃ tu vacanaṃ padmanābhasya bhārata  |
cintayāmāsa bhagavānsaptarṣīnhitakāmyayā  || 108.8 ||
kramātte cintitāḥ prājñāḥ pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  |
prācetaso vasiṣṭhaśca bhṛgurnārada eva ca  || 108.9 ||
yajñe prācetaso dakṣo mahātejāḥ prajāpatiḥ  |
dakṣasyāpi tathā jātāḥ pañcāśadduhitaro'nagha  || 108.10 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa  |
tathaiva sa mahābhāgaḥ saptaviṃśatimindave  || 108.11 ||
rohiṇīnāma yā tāsāṃ madhye tasya narādhipa  |
aniṣṭā sarvanārīṇāṃ bhartuścaiva viśeṣataḥ  || 108.12 ||
tataḥ sā paramaṃ kṛtvā vairāgyaṃ nṛpasattama  |
