āgatya narmadātīre cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 108.13 ||
ekarātraistrirātraiśca ṣaḍdvādaśabhireva ca  |
pakṣamāsopavāsaiśca karśayanti kalevaram  || 108.14 ||
ārādhayantī satataṃ mahiṣāsuranāśinīṃ  |
devīṃ bhagavatīṃ tāta sarvārtivinivāraṇīm  || 108.15 ||
snātvā snātvā jale nityaṃ narmadāyāḥ śucismitā  |
tatastuṣṭā mahābhāgā devī nārāyaṇī nṛpa  || 108.16 ||
prasannā te mahābhāge vratena niyamena ca  |
etacchrutvā tu vacanaṃ rohiṇī śaśinaḥ priyā  || 108.17 ||
yathā bhavāmi na cirāttathā bhavatu mānade  |
evamastviti sā coktvā bhavānī bhaktavatsalā  || 108.18 ||
stūyamānā munigaṇaistatraivāntaradhīyata  |
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ rohiṇī śaśinaḥ priyā  || 108.19 ||
saṃjātā sarvakālaṃ tu vallabhā nṛpasattama  |
tatra tīrthe tu yā nārī naro vā snāni bhaktitaḥ  || 108.20 ||
vallabhā jāyate sā tu bharturvai rohiṇī yathā  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitprāṇatyāgaṃ karoti vai  || 108.21 ||
saptajanmāni dāmpatyaviyogo na bhavetkvacit || 108.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe rohiṇīsomanāthatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 109

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla cakratīrthamanuttamam  |
senāpuramitikhyātaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 109.1 ||
saināpatyābhiṣekāya devadevena cakriṇā  |
ānītaśca mahāseno devaiḥ sendrapurogamaiḥ  || 109.2 ||
dānavānāṃ vadhārthāya jayāya ca divaukasām  |
bhūmidānena viprendrāṃstarpayitvā yathāvidhi  || 109.3 ||
śaṅkhabherīninādaiśca paṭahānāṃ ca nisvanaiḥ  |
vīṇāveṇumṛdaṅgaiśca jhallarīsvaramaṅgalaiḥ  || 109.4 ||
tataḥ kṛtvā svanaṃ ghoraṃ dānavo baladarpitaḥ  |
rururnāma vighātārthamabhiṣekasya cāgataḥ  || 109.5 ||
hastyaśvarathapattyoghaiḥ pūrayanvai diśo daśa  |
tatra tena mahadyuddhaṃ pravṛttaṃ kila bhārata  || 109.6 ||
śaktyṛṣṭipāśamuśalaiḥ khaḍgaistomaraṭaṅkanaiḥ  |
bhallaiḥ karṇikanārācaiḥ kabandhapaṭasaṃkulaiḥ  || 109.7 ||
tatastu tāṃ śatrubalasya senāṃ kṣaṇena cāpancyutabāṇaghātaiḥ  |
vidhvastahastyaśvarathānmahātmā jagrāha cakraṃ ripusaṅghanāśanaḥ  || 109.8 ||
jvalacca cakraṃ niśitaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ surāsurāṇāṃ ca sudarśanaṃ raṇe  |
cakarta daityasya śirastadānīṃ karātpramuktaṃ madhughātinaśca tat || 109.9 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasā vighnamabhiṣeke ṣaḍānanaḥ  |
tyaktvā tu tatra saṃsthānaṃ cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 109.10 ||
muktaṃ cakraṃ vināśāya hariṇā lokadhāriṇā  |
dvidalaṃ dānavaṃ kṛtvā papāta vimale jale  || 109.11 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ cakratīrthamiti śrutam  |
sarvapāpavināśāya nirmitaṃ viśvamūrtinā  || 109.12 ||
cakratīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayeddevamacyutam  |
puṇḍarīkasya yajñasya phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 109.13 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayedbrāhmaṇāñchubhān  |
śāntadāntajitakrodhānsa labhetkoṭijaṃ phalam  || 109.14 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā tyajate dehamātmanaḥ  |
viṣṇulokaṃ mṛto yāti jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  || 109.15 ||
krīḍayitvā yathākāmaṃ devagandharvapūjitaḥ  |
ihāgatya ca bhūyo'pi jāyate vipule kule  || 109.16 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥkhapraṇāśanam  |
kathitaṃ te mahābhāga bhūyaścānyacchṛṇuṣva me  || 109.17 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe senāpure cakratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma navottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 110

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dhautapāpaṃ tato gacchenmahāpātakanāśanam  |
samīpe cakratīrthasya viṣṇunā nirmitaṃ purā  || 110.1 ||
nihatairdānavairghorairdevadevo janārdanaḥ  |
tatpāpasya vināśārthaṃ dānavāntodbhavasya ca  || 110.2 ||
tatra tīrthe jitakrodhaścacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
duścaraṃ maunamāsthāya hyaśakyaṃ devadānavaiḥ  || 110.3 ||
snātvā dattvā dvijātibhyo dānāni vividhāni ca  |
tatkṣaṇātsupāpastu gatastadvaiṣṇavaṃ padam  || 110.4 ||
evaṃ yuktastu yastatra pāpaṃ kṛtvā sudāruṇam  |
snātvā japtvā vidhānena mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 110.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dhautapāpatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma daśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 111

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
skandena nirmitaṃ pūrvaṃ tapaḥ kṛtvā sudāruṇam  || 111.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
skandasya caritaṃ sarvamājanma dvijasattama  |
tīrthasya ca vidhiṃ puṇyaṃ kathayasva yathārthataḥ  || 111.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
devadevena vai taptaṃ tapaḥ pūrvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
vijñaptena suraiḥ sarvairumādevī vivāhitā  || 111.3 ||
nāsti senāpatiḥ kaściddevānāṃ surasattama  |
nīyante dānavairghoraiḥ sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ  || 111.4 ||
yathā niśā vinā candraṃ divaso bhāskaraṃ vinā  |
na śobhate muhūrtaṃ vai tathā senā vināyakā  || 111.5 ||
evaṃ jñātvā mahādeva parayā dayayā vibho  |
senānīrdīyatāṃ kaścittriṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 111.6 ||
etacchrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ devānāṃ parameśvaraḥ  |
kāmayāna umāṃ devīṃ sasmāra manasā smaram  || 111.7 ||
tena mūrchitasarvāṅgaḥ kāmarūpo jagadguruḥ  |
kāmayāmāsa rudrāṇīṃ divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ kila  || 111.8 ||
devarājastato jñātvā mahāmaithunagaṃ haram  |
saṃmantrya daivataiḥ sārddhaṃ praiṣayajjātavedasam  || 111.9 ||
tena gatvā mahādevaḥ paramānandasaṃsthitaḥ  |
sahasā tena dṛṣṭo'sau hāhetyuktvā samutthitaḥ  || 111.10 ||
tataḥ kruddhā mahādevī śāpavācamuvāca ha  |
vepamānā mahārāja śṛṇu yatte vadāmyaham  || 111.11 ||
ahaṃ yasmātsuraiḥ sarvairyācitā putrajanmani  |
kṛtā ratiśca viphalā saṃpreṣya jātavedasam  || 111.12 ||
tasmātsarve putrahīnā bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ  |
hareṇoktastato vahnirasmākaṃ bījamāvaha  || 111.13 ||
yathā bhavati lokeṣu tathā tvaṃ kartumarhasi  |
mama tejastvayā śakyaṃ gṛhītuṃ surasattama  |
devakāryārthasiddhyarthaṃ nānyaḥ śakto jagattraye  || 111.14 ||

agniruvāca -
tejasastava me deva kā śaktirdhāraṇe vibho  |
karoti bhasmasātsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 111.15 ||

īśvara uvāca -
udarasthena bījena yadi te jāyate rujā  |
tadā kṣipasva tattejo gaṅgātoye hutāśana  || 111.16 ||
evamuktvā mahādevo'moghaṃ bījamuttamam  |
havyavāhamukhe sarvaṃ prakṣipyāntaradhīyata  || 111.17 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve dahyamāno hutāśanaḥ  |
gaṅgātoye vinikṣipya jagāma svaṃniveśanam  || 111.18 ||
asahantī tu tattejo gaṅgāpi saritāṃ varā  |
śarastambe vinikṣipya jagāmāśu yathāgatam  || 111.19 ||
tatra jātaṃ tu taddṛṣṭvā sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ  |
kṛttikāṃ preṣayāmāsuḥ stanyaṃ pāyayituṃ tadā  || 111.20 ||
dṛṣṭvā tā āgatāḥ sarvā gaṅgāgarbhe mahāmateḥ  |
ṣaṇmukhaiḥ ṣaṇmukho bhūtvā pipāsurapibatstanam  || 111.21 ||
jātakarmādisaṃskārānvedoktānpadmasambhavaḥ  |
cakāra sarvāndājendra vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  || 111.22 ||
ṣaṇmukhātṣaṇmukho nāma kārttikeyastu kṛttikāt |
kumāraśca kumāratvādgaṅgāgarbho'gnijo'paraḥ  || 111.23 ||
evaṃ kumāraḥ sambhūto hyanadhītya sa vedavit |
śāstrāṇyanekāni veda cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 111.24 ||
devāraṇyeṣu sarveṣu nadīṣu ca nadeṣu ca  |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni samudrādyāni bhārata  || 111.25 ||
tataḥ paryāyayogena narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 111.26 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmavihitaṃ japañjāpyamaharniśam  |
dhyāyamāno mahādevaṃ śucirdhamanisaṃtataḥ  || 111.27 ||
tato varṣasahasrānte pūrṇe devo maheśvaraḥ  |
umayā sahitaḥ kāle tadā vacanamabravīt || 111.28 ||

īśvara uvāca -
ahaṃ te varadastāta gaurī mātā pitā hyaham  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yacceṣṭaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham  || 111.29 ||

ṣaṇmukha uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva umayā saha śaṅkara  |
vṛṇomi mātāpitarau nānyā gatirmatirmama  || 111.30 ||
etacchrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ putrasya vadanāccyutam  |
tathetyuktvā tu snehena premṇā taṃ pariṣasvaje  || 111.31 ||
tatastaṃ mūrdhnyupāghrāya hyumayovāca śaṅkaraḥ  || 111.32 ||

īśvara uvāca -
akṣayaścāvyayaścaiva senānīstvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  || 111.33 ||
śikhī ca te vāhanaṃ divyarūpo datto mayā śaktidharasya saṃkhye  |
surāsurādīṃśca jayeti coktvā jagāma kailāsavaraṃ mahātmā  || 111.34 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve tadā sa śikhivāhanaḥ  |
sthāpayitvā mahādevaṃ jagāma surasannidhau  || 111.35 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ skandatīrthamiti śrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ martyānāṃ bhuvi durlabham  || 111.36 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājanbhaktyā snātvārcayecchivam  |
gandhamālyābhiṣekaiśca yājñikaṃ sa labhetphalam  || 111.37 ||
skandatīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
tilamiśreṇa toyena tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 111.38 ||
piṇḍadānena caikena vidhiyuktena bhārata  |
dvādaśābdāni tuṣyanti pitaro nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 111.39 ||
tatra tīrthe tu rājendra śubhaṃ vā yādi vāśubham  |
iha loke pare caiva tatsarvaṃ jāyate'kṣayam  || 111.40 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitprāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyati  |
śāstrayuktena vidhinā sa gacchecchivamandiram  || 111.41 ||
kalpamekaṃ vasitvā tu devagandharvapūjitaḥ  |
atra bhāratavarṣe tu jāyate vimale kule  || 111.42 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajñaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ putrapautrasamanvitaḥ  || 111.43 ||
idaṃ te kathitaṃ rājanskandatīrthasya sambhavam  |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ devadevena bhāṣitam  || 111.44 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe skandatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 112

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthamāṅgirasasya tu  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvapāpavināśanam  || 112.1 ||
purāsīdaṅgirānāma brāhmaṇo vedapāragaḥ  |
putrahetoryugasyādau cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 112.2 ||
nityaṃ triṣavaṇasnāyī japandevaṃ sanātanam  |
pūjayaṃśca mahādevaṃ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ  || 112.3 ||
dvādaśābde tataḥ pūrṇe tutoṣa parameśvaraḥ  |
vareṇa chandayāmāsa dvijamāṅgirasaṃ varam  || 112.4 ||
vavre sa tu mahādevaṃ putraṃ putravatāṃ varam  |
vedavidyāvratasnātaṃ sarvaśāstraviśāradam  || 112.5 ||
devānāṃ mantriṇaṃ rājan sarvalokeṣu pūjitam  |
brahmalakṣmyāḥ sadāvāsamakṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ sutam  || 112.6 ||
tathābhilaṣitaḥ putraḥ sarvavidyāviśāradaḥ  |
bhaviṣyati na sandehaścaivamuktvā yayau haraḥ  || 112.7 ||
varairaṅgirasaścāpi bṛhaspatirajāyata  |
yathābhilaṣitaḥ putro vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  || 112.8 ||
jāte putre'ṅgirāstatra sthāpayāmāsa śaṅkaram  |
hṛṣṭatuṣṭamanā bhūtvā jagāmottaraparvatam  || 112.9 ||
tatra cāṅgirase tīrthe yaḥ snātvā pūjayecchivam  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 112.10 ||
aputro labhate putramadhano dhanamāpnuyāt |
icchate yaśca yaṃ kāmaṃ sa taṃ labhati mānavaḥ  || 112.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe'gnirasatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvādaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 113

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra koṭitīrthamanuttamam  |
ṛṣikoṭirgatā tatra parāṃ siddhimupāgatā  || 113.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhojayedbrāhmaṇāñchuciḥ  |
ekasminbhojite vipre koṭirbhavati bhojitā  || 113.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjite tu mahādeve vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet || 113.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe koṭitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayodaśādhikaśātatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 114

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
ayonijaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 114.1 ||
ayonije naraḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
pitṛdevārcanaṃ kṛtvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 114.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu vidhinā prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
sa kadācinmahārāja yonidvāraṃ na paśyati  || 114.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ayonisambhavatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 115

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja tīrthamaṅgārakaṃ param  |
rūpadaṃ sarvalokānāṃ viśrutaṃ narmadātaṭe  || 115.1 ||
aṅgārakeṇa rājendra purā taptaṃ tapaḥ kila  |
arbudaṃ ca nikharvaṃ ca prayutaṃ varṣasaṃkhyayā  || 115.2 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevaḥ parayā kṛpayā nṛpa  |
pratyakṣadarśī bhagavānuvāca kṣitinandanam  || 115.3 ||
varado'smi mahābhāga durlabhaṃ tridaśairapi  |
varaṃ dāsyāmyahaṃ vatsa brūhi yatte vivakṣitam  || 115.4 ||

aṅgāraka uvāca -
tava prasādāddeveśa sarvalokamaheśvara  |
grahamadhyagato nityaṃ vicarāmi nabhastale  || 115.5 ||
yāvaddharādharo loke yāvaccandradivākarau  |
nadyo nadāḥ samudrāśca varo me cākṣayo bhavet || 115.6 ||
evamastviti deveśo dattvā varamanuttamam  |
jagāmākāśamāviśya vandyamānaḥ surāsuraiḥ  || 115.7 ||
bhūmiputrastatastasminsthāpayāmāsa śaṅkaram  |
gataḥ surālaye loke grahabhāve niveśitaḥ  || 115.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
hutahomo jitakrodhaḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 115.9 ||
caturthyaṅgārake yastu snātvā cābhyarcayedgraham  |
aṅgārakaṃ vidhānena saptajanmāni bhārata  || 115.10 ||
daśayojanavistīrṇe maṇḍale rūpavān bhavet |
tatraiva tā mṛto jantuḥ kāmato'kāmato'pi vā  |
rudrasyānucaro bhūtvā tenaiva saha modate  || 115.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe aṅgārakatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcadaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 116

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
pāṇḍutīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpavināśanam  |
tatra snātvā naro rājanmucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 116.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā dāpayetkāñcanaṃ śuciḥ  |
bhrūṇahatyādipāpāni naśyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 116.2 ||
piṇḍodakapradānena vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet |
pitaraḥ pitāmahāśca nṛtyante ca praharṣitāḥ  || 116.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe pāṇḍutīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

ṛKV adhyāya 117

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra puṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ trilocanam  |
tatra tiṣṭhati deveśaḥ sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ  || 117.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhaktyārcayati śaṅkaram  |
rudrasya bhavanaṃ yāti mṛto nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ  || 117.2 ||
kalpakṣaye tataḥ pūrṇe krīḍitvā ca ihāgataḥ  |
āviyogena tiṣṭheta pūjyamānaḥ śataṃ samāḥ  || 117.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe trilocanatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptadaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 118

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
indratīrthetivikhyātaṃ narmadādakṣiṇe taṭe  || 118.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle indratīrthaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
śrotumicchāmi viprendra hyādimadhyāntavistaraiḥ  || 118.2 ||
etacchrutvā tu vacanaṃ dharmaputrasya dhīmataḥ  |
kathayāmāsa tadvattamitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 118.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
viśvāsayitvā suciraṃ dharmaśatruṃ mahābalam  |
vṛtraṃ jitvātha hatvā tu gacchamānaṃ śacīpatim  || 118.4 ||
niṣkrāmamāṇaṃ mārgeṇa brahmahatyā durāsadā  |
ahorātramaviśrāntā jagāma bhuvanatrayam  || 118.5 ||
yatoyato brahmahaṇaṃ yāti yānena śobhanam  |
diśo bhāgaṃ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ tato hatyā na muñcati  || 118.6 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaganāgamaḥ  |
pātakānāṃ gatirdṛṣṭā na tu viśvāsaghātinām  || 118.7 ||
pāpakarmamukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā snānadānairviśudhyati  |
nārī vā puruṣo vāpi naiva viśvāsaghātinaḥ  || 118.8 ||
evamādīni cānyāni śrutvā vākyāni devarāṭ |
vacanaṃ tadvidhairuktaṃ viṣādamagamatparam  || 118.9 ||
tyaktvā rājyaṃ suraiḥ sārdhaṃ jagāma tapa uttamam  |
putradāragṛhaṃ rājyaṃ vasūni vividhāni ca  || 118.10 ||
phalānyetāni dharmasya śobhayanti janeśvaram  |
phalaṃ dharmasya bhuñjeti suhṛtsvajanabāndhavāḥ  || 118.11 ||
paśyatāṃ sarvameteṣāṃ pāpamekena bhujyate  |
paraṃ hi sukhamutsṛjya karśayanvai kalevaram  || 118.12 ||
devarājo jagāmāsau tīrthānyāyatanāni ca  |
gaṅgātīrtheṣu sarveṣu yāmuneṣu tathaiva ca  || 118.13 ||
sārasvateṣu sarveṣu sāmudreṣu pṛthakpṛthak |
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca  || 118.14 ||
pāpaṃ na muñcate sarve paścāddevasamāgame  |
revāprabhavatīrtheṣu kūlayorubhayorapi  || 118.15 ||
pūjayanvai mahādevaṃ skandatīrthaṃ samāsadat |
tava sthitvopavāsaiśca kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ  || 118.16 ||
karśayanvai svakaṃ dehaṃ na lebhe śarma vai kvacit |
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ  || 118.17 ||
ārdravāsāstu hemante cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
evaṃ tu tapatastasya indrasya viditātmanaḥ  || 118.18 ||
vatsarāṇāṃ sahasrāṇi gatāni daśa bhārata  |
tatastvekādaśe prāpte varṣe tu nṛpasattama  || 118.19 ||
sahasā bhagavāndevastu tutoṣa parameśvaraḥ  |
tathā brahmarṣayaḥ siddhā brahmaviṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 118.20 ||
tatrājagmuḥ surāḥ sarve yatra devaḥ śatakratuḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā samāgatān devānṛṣīṃścaiva mahāmatiḥ  || 118.21 ||
uvāca praṇato bhūtvā sarvadevapurohitaḥ  |
viditaṃ sarvameteṣāṃ yathā vṛtravadhaḥ kṛtaḥ  || 118.22 ||
yuṣmākaṃ cājñayā pūrvaṃ brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
tathāpyevaṃ brahmahaṇaṃ matvā pāpasya kāriṇam  || 118.23 ||
bhramantaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu brahmahatyā na muñcati  |
na nandati jagatsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 118.24 ||
yathā vihīnacandrārkaṃ tathā rājyamanāyakam  |
tasmātsarve suraśreṣṭhā vijñāpyaṃ mama samprati  || 118.25 ||
kurvantu śakraṃ nirdoṣaṃ tathā sarve maharṣayaḥ  |
bṛhaspatimukhodgīrṇaṃ śrutvā tadvacanaṃ śubham  || 118.26 ||
tataḥ provāca bhagavānbrahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
etatpāpaṃ mahāghoraṃ brahmahatyāsamudbhavam  || 118.27 ||
daivatebhyo'tha bhūtebhyaścaturbhāgaṃ kṣipāmyaham  |
evaṃ muktvā kṣipaccaino jalopari mahāmatiḥ  || 118.28 ||
avagāhya tataḥ peyā āpo vai nānyathā budhaiḥ  |
dharāyāmakṣipadbhāgaṃ dvitīyaṃ padmasaṃbhavaḥ  || 118.29 ||
abhakṣyā tena saṃjātā sadākālaṃ vasuṃdharā  |
tadārdhamarddhaṃ nārīṇāṃ dvitīye'hni yudhiṣṭhira  || 118.30 ||
nikṣipya bhagavāndevaḥ punaranyajjagāda ha  |
asaṃgrāhyā tvasaṃgrāhyā tena jātā rajasvalā  || 118.31 ||
caturdināni sā prājñaiḥ pāpasya mahato mahāt |
caturthaṃ tu tato bhāgaṃ vibhajya parameśvaraḥ  || 118.32 ||
kṛṣigorakṣyavāṇijyaiḥ śūdrasevākare dvije  |
tato'bhinandayāmāsuḥ sarve devā maharṣayaḥ  || 118.33 ||
devendraṃ vāgbhiriṣṭābhirnarmadājalasaṃsthitam  |
vareṇa chandayāmāsa tatastuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 118.34 ||
varaṃ dāsyāmi deveśa varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 118.35 ||

indra uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
atra saṃsthāpayiṣyāmi sadā saṃnihito bhava  || 118.36 ||
evamastviti coktvā taṃ brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
jagmurākāśamāviśya stūyamānā maharṣibhiḥ  || 118.37 ||
gateṣu devadeveṣu devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ  |
sthāpayitvā mahādevaṃ jagāma tridaśālayam  || 118.38 ||
indratīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
mahāpātakayukto'pi mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 118.39 ||
indratīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
so'śvamedhasya yajñasya puṣkalaṃ phalamaśnute  || 118.40 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
śrutamātreṇa yenaiva mucyante pātakairnarāḥ  || 118.41 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe indratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 119

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra kahloḍītīrthamuttamam  |
revāyāścottare kūle sarvapāpavināśanam  || 119.1 ||
hitārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāmṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitaṃ purā  |
tapasā tu samuddhṛtya narmadāyāṃ mahāmbhasi  || 119.2 ||
snātvā tu kapilātīrthe kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
śrutvā cākhyānakaṃ divyaṃ brāhmaṇāñchṛṇu yatphalam  || 119.3 ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ kapilādānamuttamam  |
brāhmaṇānveṣitaṃ pūrvamṛṣidevasamāgame  || 119.4 ||
sadyaḥ prasūtāṃ kapilāṃ śobhanāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
sopavāso jitakrodhastasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 119.5 ||
sasamudraguhā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
dattā caiva mahābāho pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 119.6 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā yatpurā kṛtam  |
naśyate kapilāṃ dattvā saptajanmārjitaṃ nṛpa  || 119.7 ||
bhūmidānaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ hastyaśvakanakādikam  |
kapilādānasyaikasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 119.8 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
mṛto viṣṇupuraṃ yāti gīyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 119.9 ||
yāvanti tasyā romāṇi savatsāyāstu bhārata  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi sa svarge krīḍate ciram  || 119.10 ||
tato'vakīrṇakālena tviha mānuṣyatāṃ gataḥ  |
dhanadhānyasamopeto jāyate vipule kule  || 119.11 ||
vedavidyā vratasnātaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ  |
vyādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 119.12 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kalhoḍītīrthamuttamam  |
yatkṛtvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 119.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kahloḍītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 120

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kambukeśvaramuttamam  |
hiraṇyakaśipurdaityo dānavo baladarpitaḥ  || 120.1 ||
avadhyaḥ sarvalokānāṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  |
tasya putro mahātejāḥ prahlādo nāma nāmataḥ  || 120.2 ||
viṣṇuprasādādbhaktyā ca tasya rājye pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
virocanastasya sutastasyāpi balireva ca  || 120.3 ||
baliputro'bhavadbāṇastasmādapi ca śambaraḥ  |
śambarasyānvaye jātaḥ kamburnāma mahāsuraḥ  || 120.4 ||
jñātvā viṣṇumayaṃ ghoraṃ mahadbhayamupasthitam  |
dānavānāṃ vināśāya nānyo hetuḥ kadācana  || 120.5 ||
sa tyaktvā putradārāṃśca suhṛdbandhuparigrahān  |
cacāra maunamāsthāya tapaḥ kamburmahāmatiḥ  || 120.6 ||
akṣasūtrakaro bhūtvā daṇḍī muṇḍī ca mekhalī  |
śākayāvakabhakṣaśca valkalājinasaṃvṛtaḥ  || 120.7 ||
snātvā nityaṃ dhṛtiparo narmadājalamāśritaḥ  |
pūjayaṃstu mahādevamarbudaṃ varṣasaṃkhyayā  || 120.8 ||
tatastutoṣa bhagavāndevadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
uvāca dānavaṃ kāle meghagambhīrayā girā  || 120.9 ||
bhobhoḥ kambo mahābhāga tuṣṭo'haṃ tava suvrata  |
iṣṭaṃ vratānāṃ paramaṃ maunaṃ sarvārthasādhanam  || 120.10 ||
caritaṃ ca tvayā loke devadānavaduścaram  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yatte manasi rocate  || 120.11 ||

kamburuvāca -
yadi prasanno deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
akṣayyaścāvyayaścaiva svecchayā vicarāmyaham  || 120.12 ||
daityadānavasaṅghānāṃ saṃyugeṣvapalāyitā  |
bhayaṃ cānyanna vidyeta muktvā devaṃ gadādharam  || 120.13 ||
tasyāhaṃ saṃyuge sādhyo yenopāyena śaṅkara  |
bhavāmi na sadā kālaṃ taṃ vadasva varaṃ mama  || 120.14 ||

īśvara uvāca -
mama saṃnihito yatra tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi dānava  |
tatra viṣṇubhayaṃ nāsti vasātra vigatajvaraḥ  || 120.15 ||
tasya devādhidevasya vedagarbhasya saṃyuge  |
śaṅkhacakradharasyeśā nāhaṃ sarve surāsurāḥ  || 120.16 ||
kiṃ punaryo dviṣatyenaṃ lokālokaprabhuṃ harim  |
sa sukhī vartate kālaṃ na nimeṣaṃ mataṃ mama  || 120.17 ||
tasmāttvaṃ parayā bhaktyā sarvabhūtahite rataḥ  |
vasiṣyasi ciraṃ kālamityuktvādarśanaṃ gataḥ  || 120.18 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve tatra tīrthe mahāmatiḥ  |
sthāpayāmāsa deveśaṃ śivaṃ śāntamanāmayam  || 120.19 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe mahādevaṃ sthāpayitvā divaṃ gataḥ  |
tadāprabhṛti tatpārtha kambutīrthamiti śrutam  |
vikhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu mahāpātakanāśanam  || 120.20 ||
kambutīrthe naraḥ snātvā vidhinābhyarcya bhāskaram  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmamantraiśca stūyamāno nṛpottama  || 120.21 ||
tasya puṇyaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ tu śṛṇuṣvādya mamaiva gadato nṛpa  || 120.22 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmagīteṣu sāṅgopāṅgeṣu yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti gāyatrīmātramantravit || 120.23 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjayeddevamīśānaṃ so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 120.24 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā tatra tīrthe kalevaram  |
yastyajennātra sandeho rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 120.25 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kambukeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma viṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 121

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla candrahāsamataḥ param  |
yatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāptaḥ somarājaḥ surottamaḥ  || 121.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ siddhiṃ parāṃ prāptaḥ somanātho jagatpatiḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva mamānagha  || 121.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā śapto munīndreṇa dakṣeṇa kila bhārata  |
asevanāddhi dārāṇāṃ kṣayarogī bhaviṣyasi  || 121.3 ||
udvāhitānāṃ patnīnāṃ ye na kurvanti sevanam  |
yā niṣṭhā jāyate nṛṇāṃ tāṃ śṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 121.4 ||
ṛtāvṛtau hi nārīṇāṃ sevanājjāyate sutaḥ  |
sutātsvargaśca mokṣaśca ityevaṃ śrutibhāṣitam  || 121.5 ||
tatkālocitadharmeṇa veṣṭito raurave patet |
tasyāstadrudhiraṃ pāpaḥ pibate kālamīpsitam  || 121.6 ||
tato'vatīrṇaḥ kālena yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ prayāsyati  |
tasyāṃ tasyāṃ sa duṣṭātmā durbhago jāyate sadā  || 121.7 ||
nārīṇāṃ tu sadā kāmo'bhyadhikāḥ parivartate  |
viśeṣeṇa ṛtau kāle pīḍyate kāmasāyakaiḥ  || 121.8 ||
paribhūtā hitā bhartrā dhyāyante'nyaṃ patiṃ striyaḥ  |
tataḥ putraḥ samutpanno hyaṭate kulamuttamam  || 121.9 ||
svargasthāstena pitaraḥ pūrvajāste pitāmahāḥ  |
patanti jātamātreṇa kulaṭastena cocyate  || 121.10 ||
tena karmavipākena kṣayarogyabhavacchaśī  |
tyaktvā lokaṃ surendrāṇāṃ martyalokamupāgataḥ  || 121.11 ||
tatastīrthānyanekāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca  |
bhramanvai narmadāṃ prāptaḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanīm  || 121.12 ||
upavāsaṃ ca dānāni vratāni niyamāṃstathā  |
cacāra dvādaśābdāni tato muktaḥ sa kilbiṣaiḥ  || 121.13 ||
snāpayitvā mahādevaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  |
jagāma prabhayā pūrṇaḥ sa ca lokamanuttamam  || 121.14 ||
yenaiva sthāpito devaḥ pūjyate varṣasaṃkhyayā  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi rudraloke sa pūjyate  || 121.15 ||
tena devānvidhānoktānsthāpayanti narā bhuvi  |
akṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ yasmātkālaṃ bhuñjanti mānavāḥ  || 121.16 ||
somatīrthe naraḥ snātvā pūjayeddevamīśvaram  |
sa bhrājate naro loke somavatpriyadarśanaḥ  || 121.17 ||
candrahāse tu yo gatvā grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
snānaṃ samācaredbhaktyā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 121.18 ||
tatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca candrahāse śubhāśubham  |
kṛtaṃ nṛpavaraśreṣṭha sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 121.19 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
candrahāse tu ye snātvā paśyanti grahaṇaṃ narāḥ  || 121.20 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurākṛtam  |
snānamātreṇa rājendra tatra tīrthe praṇaśyati  || 121.21 ||
bahavastaṃ na jānanti mahāmohasamanvitāḥ  |
dehasthamiva sarveṣāṃ paramānandarūpiṇam  || 121.22 ||
paścime sāgare gatvā somatīrthe tu yatphalam  |
tatsamagramavāpnoti candrahāse na saṃśayaḥ  || 121.23 ||
saṃkrāntau ca vyatīpāte ayane viṣuve tathā  |
candrahāse naraḥ snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 121.24 ||
te mūḍhāste durācārāsteṣāṃ janma nirarthakam  |
candrahāsaṃ na jānanti ye revāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 121.25 ||
candrahāse tu yaḥ kaścitsaṃnyāsaṃ kurute dvijaḥ  |
anivartikā gatistasya somalokānna saṃśayaḥ  || 121.26 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe somatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 122
śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla kohanasveti viśrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ mṛtyuvināśanam  || 122.1 ||
purā tatra dvijaḥ kaścidvedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
patnīputrasuhṛdvargaiḥ svakarmanirato'vasat || 122.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
brāhmaṇasya tu yatkarma utpattiḥ kṣatriyasya tu  |
vaiśyasyāpi ca śūdrasya tatsarvaṃ kathayasva me  || 122.3 ||
dharmasyārhasya kāmasya mokṣasya ca paraṃ vidhim  |
nikhilaṃ jñātumicchāmi nānyo vettā matirmama  || 122.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
utpattikāraṇaṃ brahmā devadevaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
prathamaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ carācarajagadguruḥ  || 122.5 ||
dvijātayo mukhājjātāḥ kṣatriyā bāhuyantrataḥ  |
ūrupradeśādvaiśyāstu śūdrāḥ pādeṣvathābhavan  || 122.6 ||
tatastvanye pṛthagvarṇāḥ pṛthagdharmān samācaran  |
paryāyeṇa samutpannā hyanulomavilomataḥ  || 122.7 ||
teṣāṃ dharmaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrutismṛtyarthacoditam  |
yena samyakkṛtenaiva sarve yānti parāṃ gatim  || 122.8 ||
gatirdhyānaṃ vinā bhaktairbrāhmaṇaiḥ prāpyate nṛpa  |
adhyāpayanyato vedānvedaṃ vāpi yathāvidhi  || 122.9 ||
kulajāṃ rūpasampannāṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitām  |
udvāhayettataḥ patnīṃ guruṇānumate tadā  || 122.10 ||
tataḥ smārtaṃ vivāhāgniṃ śrautaṃ vā pūjayetkramāt |
pratigrahadhano bhūtvā dambhalobhavivarjitaḥ  || 122.11 ||
pañcayajñavidhānāni kārayedvai yathāvidhi  |
vanaṃ gacchettataḥ paścāddvitīyāśramasevanāt || 122.12 ||
putreṣu bhāryāṃ nikṣipya sarvasaṅgavivarjitaḥ  |
iṣṭāṃllokānavāpnoti na ceha jāyate punaḥ  || 122.13 ||
kṣatriyastu sthito rājye pālayitvā vasuṃdharām  |
śaśvaddharmamanāścaiva prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 122.14 ||
vaiśyadharmo na sandehaḥ kṛṣigorakṣaṇe rataḥ  |
satyaśaucasamopeto gacchate svargamuttamam  || 122.15 ||
na śūdrasya pṛthagdharmo vihitaḥ parameṣṭhinā  |
na mantro na ca saṃskāro na vidyāparisevanam  || 122.16 ||
na śabdavidyāsamayo devatābhyarcanāni ca  |
yathā jātena satataṃ vartitavyamaharniśam  || 122.17 ||
sa dharmaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ purā sṛṣṭaḥ svayambhuvā  |
mantrasaṃskārasampannāstrayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ  || 122.18 ||
teṣāṃ matamanādṛtya yadi varteta kāmataḥ  |
sa mṛto jāyate śvā vai gatirūrdhvā na vidyate  || 122.19 ||
na teṣāṃ preṣaṇaṃ nityaṃ teṣāṃ matamanusmaran  |
yaśobhāgī svadharmasthaḥ svargabhāgī sa jāyate  || 122.20 ||
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇo'vasadvipraḥ sa bhārata  |
hanasveti hanasveti śṛṇoti vākyamīdṛśam  || 122.21 ||
tato nirīkṣate cordhvamadhaścaiva diśo daśa  |
vepamānaḥ sa bhītaśca praskhalaṃśca pade pade  || 122.22 ||
śṛṅkhalāyudhahastaiśca pāśaiścaiva sudāruṇaiḥ  |
veṣṭitaṃ mahiṣārūḍhaṃ naraṃ paśyati manmukham  || 122.23 ||
kṛṣṇāṃjanacayaprakhyaṃ kṛṣṇāmbaravibhūṣitam  |
raktākṣamāyatabhujaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam  || 122.24 ||
dṛṣṭvā taṃ tu samāyāntaṃ nirīkṣyātmānamātmanā  |
japañjāpyaṃ ca paramaṃ śatarudrīyasaṃstavam  || 122.25 ||
tataḥ provāca bhagavānyamaḥ saṃyamano mahān  |
śṛṇu vākyamato brahmanyamo'haṃ sarvajantuṣu  || 122.26 ||
saṃharasva mahābhāga rudrajāpyaṃ sudurbhidam  |
yenāhaṃ kālapāśaistvāṃ saṃyamāmi gatavyathaḥ  || 122.27 ||
tacchrutvā niṣṭhuraṃ vākyaṃ yamasya mukhanirgatam  |
mahābhayasamopeto brāhmaṇaḥ prapalāyitaḥ  || 122.28 ||
tasya mārge gatāḥ sarve yamena saha kiṃkarāḥ  |
tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti taṃ vipramūcuste so'pyadhāvata  || 122.29 ||
tvaramāṇaḥ pariśrānto hā hato'haṃ durātmabhiḥ  |
rakṣa rakṣa mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala  || 122.30 ||
evamuktvāpatadbhūmau liṅgamāliṅgya bhārata  |
gatasattvaḥ sa viprendraḥ samāśritya sureśvaram  || 122.31 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā patitaṃ bhūmau devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
ko haniṣyati mābhaistvaṃ huṅkāramakarottadā  || 122.32 ||
tena te kiṃkarāḥ sarve yamena saha bhārata  |
huṅkāreṇa gatāḥ sarve meghā vātahatā yathā  || 122.33 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ kohanasveti viśrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  || 122.34 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalamāpnotyanuttamam  || 122.35 ||
tatra tīrthe tu rājendra prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
na paśyati yamaṃ devamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 122.36 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryājjale vā nṛpasattama  |
agniloke vasettāvadyāvatkalpaśatatrayam  || 122.37 ||
evaṃ varuṇaloke'pi vasitvā kālamīpsitam  |
iha lokamanuprāpto mahādhanapatirbhavet || 122.38 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kohanatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 123

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra karmadītīrthamuttamam  |
yatra tiṣṭhati vighneśo gaṇanātho mahābalaḥ  || 123.1 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā caturthyāṃ vā hyupoṣitaḥ  |
vighnaṃ na vidyate tasya saptajanmani bhārata  || 123.2 ||
tatra tīrthe hi yatkiṃciddīyate nṛpasattama  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 123.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe karmadeśvaratīrthamāhatmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||


ṛKV adhyāya 124

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla narmadeśvaramuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 124.1 ||
agnipraveśaśca jale'thavā mṛtyuranāśake  |
anivartikā gatistasya yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt || 124.2 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 125

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla ravitīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra devaḥ sahasrāṃśustapastaptvā divaṃ gataḥ  || 125.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ devo jagaddhātā sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ  |
tapastapati deveśastāpaso bhāskaro raviḥ  || 125.2 ||
ārādhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvadevaiśca pūjitaḥ  |
pratyakṣo dṛśyate loke sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ  || 125.3 ||
ādityatvaṃ kathaṃ prāptaḥ kathaṃ bhāskara ucyate  |
sarvametatsamāsena kathayasva mamānagha  || 125.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
mahāpraśno mahārāja yastvayā paripṛcchitaḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ sampravakṣyāmi namaskṛtya svayambhuvam  || 125.5 ||
āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam  |
apratarkyamavijñeyaṃ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ  || 125.6 ||
tatastejaśca divyaṃ ca taptapiṇḍamanuttamam  |
ākāśāttu yathaivolkā sṛṣṭihetoradhomukhī  || 125.7 ||
tattejaso'ntaḥ puruṣaḥ saṃjātaḥ sarvabhūṣitaḥ  |
sa śivo'pāṇipādaśca yena sarvamidaṃ tatam  || 125.8 ||
tasyotpannasya bhūtasya tejo rūpasya bhārata  |
paścātprajāpatirbhūyaḥ kālaḥ kālāntareṇa vai  || 125.9 ||
agnirjātaḥ sa bhūtānāṃ manuṣyāsurarakṣasām  |
sarvadevādhidevaśca ādityastena cocyate  || 125.10 ||
ādau tasya namaskāro'nyeṣāṃ ca tadanantaram  |
kriyate daivataiḥ sarvaistena sarvairmaharṣibhiḥ  || 125.11 ||
tisraḥ sandhyāstrayo devāḥ sāṃnidhyāḥ sūryamaṇḍale  |
namaskṛtena sūryeṇa sarve devā namaskṛtāḥ  || 125.12 ||
na divā na bhavedrātriḥ ṣaṇmāsā dakṣiṇāyanam  |
ayanaṃ cottaraṃ cāpi bhāskareṇa vinā nṛpa  || 125.13 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  |
na vartate vinā sūryaṃ tena pūjyatamo raviḥ  || 125.14 ||
śabdagāḥ śrutimukhyāśca brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
pratyakṣo bhagavāndevo dṛśyate lokapāvanaḥ  || 125.15 ||
utpattiḥ pralayasthānaṃ nidhānaṃ bījamavyayam  |
hetureko jagannātho nānyo vidyeta bhāskarāt || 125.16 ||
evamātmabhavaṃ kṛtvā jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam  |
lokānāṃ tu hitārthāya sthāpayeddharmapaddhatim  || 125.17 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya sthāpayitvātmanastanum  |
sahasrāṃśuṃ nidhiṃ dhāmnāṃ jagāmākāśamavyayam  || 125.18 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
sahasrakiraṇaṃ devaṃ nāmamantravidhānataḥ  || 125.19 ||
tena taptaṃ hutaṃ tena tena sarvamanuṣṭhitam  |
tena samyagvidhānena samprāptaṃ paramaṃ padam  || 125.20 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
snātvā ye narmadātoye devaṃ paśyanti bhāskaram  || 125.21 ||
tathā devasya rājendra ye kurvanti pradakṣiṇam  |
ananyabhaktyā satataṃ trirakṣarasamanvitāḥ  || 125.22 ||
tena pūtaśarīrāste mantreṇa gatapātakāḥ  |
yatpuṇyaṃ ca bhavetteṣāṃ tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu  || 125.23 ||
sasamudraguhā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā sarvā pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 125.24 ||
mantramūlamidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
tena mantravihīnaṃ tu kāryaṃ loke na sidhyati  || 125.25 ||
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ  |
kāryārthaṃ naiva sidhyeta tathā karma hyamantrakam  || 125.26 ||
bhasmahutaṃ pārtha yathā toyavivarjitam  |
niṣphalaṃ jāyate dānaṃ tathā mantravivarjitam  || 125.27 ||
kāṣṭhapāṣāṇaloṣṭeṣu mṛnmayeṣu viśeṣataḥ  |
mantreṇa loke pūjāṃ tu kurvanti na hyamantrataḥ  || 125.28 ||
dvādaśābdānnamaskārādbhaktyā yallabhate phalam  |
mantrayuktanamaskārātsakṛttallabhate phalam  || 125.29 ||
saṃkrāntau ca vyatīpāte ayane viṣuve tathā  |
narmadāyā jale snātvā yastu pūjayate ravim  || 125.30 ||
dvādaśābdena yatpāpamajñānajñānasaṃcitam  |
tatkṣaṇānnaśyate sarvaṃ vahninā tu tuṣaṃ yathā  || 125.31 ||
candrasūryagrahe snātvā sopavāso jitendriyaḥ  |
tatrādityamukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 125.32 ||
māghamāse tu samprāpte saptamyāṃ nṛpasattama  |
sopavāso jitakrodha uṣitvā sūryamandire  || 125.33 ||
prātaḥ snātvā vidhānena dadātyarghaṃ divākare  |
vidhinā mantrayuktena sa labhetpuṇyamuttamam  || 125.34 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ kṛtvā hyudakatarpaṇam  |
mandire devadevasya tataḥ pūjāṃ samācaret || 125.35 ||
gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathā dhūpairdīpanaivedyaśobhanaiḥ  |
pūjayitvā jagannāthaṃ tato mantramudīrayet || 125.36 ||
viṣṇuḥ śakro yamo dhātā mitro'tha varuṇastathā  |
vivasvānsavitā pūṣā caṇḍāṃśurbharga eva ca  || 125.37 ||
iti dvādaśanāmāni japankṛtvā pradakṣiṇām  |
yatphalaṃ labhate pārtha tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu  || 125.38 ||
daridro vyādhito mūko badhiro jaḍa eva ca  |
na bhavetsapta janmāni ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 125.39 ||
evaṃ jñātvā vidhānena japanmantraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
ārādhayedraviṃ bhaktyā ya icchetpuṇyamuttamam  || 125.40 ||
mantrahīnāṃ tu yaḥ kuryādbhaktiṃ devasya bhārata  |
sa viḍambati cātmānaṃ paśukīṭapataṅgavat || 125.41 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścittyajate dehamuttamam  |
sa gatastatra devaistu pūjyamāno maharṣibhiḥ  || 125.42 ||
svecchayā suciraṃ kālamiha loke nṛpo bhavet || 125.43 ||
putrapautrasamāyukto hastyaśvarathasaṅkulaḥ  |
dāsīdāsaśatopeto jāyate vipule kule  || 125.44 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ravitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 126

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra paraṃ tīrthamayonijam  |
snātamātro narastatra na paśyedyonisaṅkaṭam  || 126.1 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā pūjayeddevamīśvaram  |
ayonijo mahādeva yathā tvaṃ parameśvara  || 126.2 ||
tathā mocaya māṃ deva sambhavādyonisaṅkaṭāt |
gandhapuṣpādidhūpaiśca sa mucyetsarvapātakaiḥ  || 126.3 ||
tasya devasya yo bhaktyā kurute liṅgapūraṇam  |
sa vaseddevadevasya yāvatsikthasya saṃkhyayā  || 126.4 ||
ayonije mahādevaṃ snāpayedgandhavāriṇā  |
madhukṣīreṇa dadhnā vā sa labhedvipulāṃ śriyam  || 126.5 ||
aṣṭabhyāṃ ca site pakṣe asitāṃ vā caturdaśīm  |
pūjayitvā mahādevaṃ prīṇayedgītavādyakaiḥ  || 126.6 ||
vasetsa ca śive loke ye kurvanti manoharam  |
te vasanti śive loke yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 126.7 ||
tasya devasya bhaktyā tu yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇām  |
vijñāpayaṃśca satataṃ mantreṇānena bhārata  || 126.8 ||
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ pāramparyeṇa mānavaiḥ  |
sakāśāddevadevasya tacchṛṇuṣva samādhinā  || 126.9 ||
ayonijo mahādeva yathā tvaṃ parameśvara  |
tathā mocaya māṃ śarva sambhavādyonisaṅkaṭāt || 126.10 ||
kiṃ tasya bahubhirmantraiḥ kaṃṭhaśoṣaṇatatparaiḥ  |
yenauṃnamaḥ śivāyeti proktaṃ devasya saṃnidhau  || 126.11 ||
tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ tena tena sarvamanuṣṭhitam  |
yenauṃnamaḥ śivāyeti mantrābhyāsaḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ  || 126.12 ||
na tatphalamavāpnoti sarvadeveṣu vai dvijaḥ  |
yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti ṣaḍakṣara udīraṇāt || 126.13 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayecchivayoginam  |
dvijānāmayutaṃ sāgraṃ sa labhetphalamuttamam  || 126.14 ||
athavā bhaktiyuktastu teṣāṃ dānte jitendriye  |
saṃskṛtya dadate bhikṣāṃ phalaṃ tasya tato'dhikam  || 126.15 ||
yatihaste jalaṃ dadyādbhikṣāṃ dattvā punarjalam  |
sā bhikṣā meruṇā tulyā tajjalaṃ sāgaropamam  || 126.16 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ayoniprabhavatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 127

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra agnitīrthamanuttamam  |
tatra snātvā tu pakṣādau mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 127.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kanyāṃ dadyātsvayamalaṃkṛtām  |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narottama  || 127.2 ||
agniṣṭomātirātrābhyāṃ śataṃ śataguṇīkṛtam  |
prāpnoti puruṣo dattvā yathāśaktyā hyalaṃkṛtām  || 127.3 ||
tasyāḥ putraprapautrāṇāṃ yā bhavedromasaṃgatiḥ  |
sa yāti tena mānena śivaloke parāṃ gatim  || 127.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe agnitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 128

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra bhṛkuṭeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahābhāgo bhṛguḥ paramakopanaḥ  || 128.1 ||
tena varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ tapaścīrṇaṃ purānagha  |
putrārthaṃ varayāmāsa putraṃ putravatāṃ varaḥ  || 128.2 ||
varo datto mahābhāga devenāndhakaghātinā  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  || 128.3 ||
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ labhet |
bhṛkuṭeśaṃ tu yaḥ kaścidghṛtena madhunā saha  || 128.4 ||
putrārthī snāpayedbhaktyā sa labhetputramīpsitam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā dadyādviprāya kāñcanam  || 128.5 ||
godānaṃ vā mahīṃ vāpi tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 128.6 ||
sasamudraguhā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
dattā pṛthvī na sandehastena sarvā nṛpottama  || 128.7 ||
tena dānena sa svarge krīḍayitvā yathāsukham  |
martye bhavati rājendro brāhmaṇo vā supūjitaḥ  || 128.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhṛkuṭeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 129

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla brahmatīrthamanuttamam  |
anyeṣāṃ caiva tīrthānāṃ parātparataraṃ mahat || 129.1 ||
tatra tīrthe suraśreṣṭho brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 129.2 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurākṛtam  |
tatkṣālayati deveśo darśanādeva pātakam  || 129.3 ||
śrutismṛtyuditānyeva tatra snātvā dvijarṣabhāḥ  |
prāyaścittāni kurvanti teṣāṃ vāsastriviṣṭape  || 129.4 ||
ye punaḥ śāstramutsṛjya kāmalobhaprapīḍitāḥ  |
prāyaścittaṃ vadiṣyanti te vai nirayagāminaḥ  || 129.5 ||
snātvādau pātakī brahmannatvā tu kīrtayedagham  |
tasya tannaśyate kṣipraṃ tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā  || 129.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya sa labhetphalamuttamam  || 129.7 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaddānaṃ brahmoddiśya prayacchati  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 129.8 ||
gāyatrīsāramātro'pi tatra yaḥ kriyate japaḥ  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmasahitaḥ sa bhavennātra saṃśayaḥ  || 129.9 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā tyajeddehaṃ sudustyajam  |
anivartikā gatistasya brahmalokānna saṃśayaḥ  || 129.10 ||
yāvadasthīni tiṣṭhanti brahmatīrthe ca dehinām  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi devaloke mahīyate  || 129.11 ||
avatīrṇastato loke brahmajño jāyate kule  |
uttamaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ devānāmiva devatā  || 129.12 ||
vidyāsthānāni sarvāṇi vetti vedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
jāyate pūjito loke rājabhiḥ sa na saṃśayaḥ  || 129.13 ||
putrapautrasamopetaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ brahmatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 129.14 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ jñānavatāṃ varam  |
ye paśyanti mahātmāno hyamṛtatvaṃ prayānti te  || 129.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe brahmatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||


ṛKV adhyāya 130

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle devatīrthamanuttamam  |
tatra devaiḥ samāgatya toṣitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 130.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ  |
sa labhennātra sandeho gosahasraphalaṃ dhruvam  || 130.2 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe devatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma triṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 131

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle nāgatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahānāgā bhaye jāte tato nṛpa  || 131.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
mahābhayānāṃ lokasya nāgānāṃ dvijasattama  |
kathaṃ jātaṃ bhayaṃ tīvraṃ yena te tapasi sthitāḥ  || 131.2 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyacca yatsurāsuramānave  |
tāta te viditaṃ sarvaṃ tena me kautukaṃ mahat || 131.3 ||
mama saṃtāpajaṃ duḥkhaṃ duryodhanasamudbhavam  |
tava vaktrāmbujaughena plāvitaṃ nirvṛtiṃ gatam  || 131.4 ||
śrutvā tava mukhodgītāṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśanīm  |
bhūyo bhūyaḥ smṛtirjātā śravaṇe mama suvrata  || 131.5 ||
na kleśatvaṃ dvije yuktaṃ na cānyo jānate phalam  |
vidyādānasya mahataḥ śrāvitasya sutasya ca  || 131.6 ||
evaṃ jñātvā yathānyāyaṃ yaḥ praśnaḥ pṛcchito mayā  |
kathā tu kathyatāṃ vipra dayāṃ kṛtvā mamopari  || 131.7 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
yathā yathā tvaṃ nṛpa bhāṣase ca tathā tathā me sukhameti bhāratī  |
śaithilyabhāvājjarayānvitasya tvatsauhṛdaṃ naśyati naiva tāta  || 131.8 ||
kathayāmi yathāvṛttamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
kathitaṃ pūrvato vṛddhaiḥ pāramparyeṇa bhārata  || 131.9 ||
dve bhārye kaśyapasyāstāṃ sarvalokeṣvanuttame  |
garutmato vai vinatā sarpāṇāṃ kadrureva ca  || 131.10 ||
aśvasaṃdarśanāttābhyāṃ kalirūpaṃ vyavasthitam  |
prabhātakāle rājendra bhāskarākāravarcasam  || 131.11 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā vinatā rūpamaśvaṃ sarvatra pāṇḍuram  |
atha tāṃ kadrūmavocatsā paśya paśya varānane  || 131.12 ||
uccaiḥśravasaḥ sādṛśyaṃ paśya sarvatra pāṇḍuram  |
dhāvamānamaviśrāntaṃ javena pavanopamam  || 131.13 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasā yāntamīrṣyābhāvena mohitā  |
kṛṣṇaṃ matvā tathājalpattayā saha nṛpottama  || 131.14 ||
vinate tvaṃ mṛṣā loke nṛśaṃse kulapāṃsani  |
kṛṣṇaṃ cainaṃ vada śvetaṃ narakaṃ yāsyase param  || 131.15 ||

vinatovāca -
satyānṛte tu vacane paṇo'yaṃ te mamaiva tu  |
sahasraṃ vatsarāndāsī bhaveyaṃ tava veśmani  || 131.16 ||
tatheti te pratijñāya rātrau gatvā svakaṃ gṛham  |
parityajya ubhe te tu krodhamūrchitamūrchite  || 131.17 ||
bandhugarvasya gatvā tu kathayāmāsa taṃ paṇam  |
kadrūrvinatayā sārddhaṃ yadvṛttaṃ pramadālaye  || 131.18 ||
tacchrutvā bāndhavāḥ sarve kadrūputrāstathaiva ca  |
na manyante hitaṃ kāryaṃ kṛtaṃ mātrā vigarhitam  || 131.19 ||
akṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇatāmamba kathaṃ gaccheddhayottamaḥ  |
dāsatvaṃ prāpsyase tvaṃ hi paṇenānena suvrate  || 131.20 ||

kadrūruvāca -
bhaveyaṃ na yathādāsī tatkurudhvaṃ hi satvaram  |
viśadhvaṃ romakūpeṣu tasyāśvasya matirmama  || 131.21 ||
kṣaṇamātraṃ kṛte kārye sā dāsī ca bhavenmama  |
tataḥ svasthoragāḥ sarve bhaviṣyatha yathāsukham  || 131.22 ||
sarpā ūcuḥ  |
yathā tvaṃ jananī devi pannagānāṃ matā bhuvi  |
tathāpi sā viśeṣeṇa vañcitavyā na karhicit || 131.23 ||

kadrūruvāca -
mama vākyamakurvāṇā ye kecidbhuvi pannagāḥ  |
havyavāhamukhaṃ sarve te yāsyantyavicāritāḥ  || 131.24 ||
etacchrutvā tu vacanaṃ ghoraṃ mātṛmukhodbhavam  |
kecitpraviṣṭā romāṇi tathānye girisaṃsthitāḥ  || 131.25 ||
kecitpraviṣṭā jāhnavyāmanye ca tapasi sthitāḥ  || 131.26 ||
tato varṣasahasrānte tutoṣa parameśvaraḥ  |
mahādevo jagaddhātā hyuvāca parayā girā  || 131.27 ||
bho bhoḥ sarpā nivartadhvaṃ tapaso'sya mahatphalam  |
yamicchatha dadāmyadya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 131.28 ||
sarpā ūcuḥ  |
kadrūśāpabhayādbhītā devadeva maheśvara  |
tava pārśve vasiṣyāmo yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 131.29 ||

devadeva uvāca -
ekaścāyaṃ mahābāhurvāsukirbhujagottamaḥ  |
mama pārśve vasennityaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhayarakṣakaḥ  || 131.30 ||
anyeṣāṃ caiva sarpāṇāṃ bhayaṃ nāsti mamājñayā  |
āplutya narmadātoye bhujagāste ca rakṣitāḥ  || 131.31 ||
nāsti mṛtyubhayaṃ teṣāṃ vasadhvaṃ yatra cepsitam  |
kadrūśāpabhayaṃ nāsti hyeṣa me vistaraḥ paraḥ  || 131.32 ||
evaṃ dattvā varaṃ teṣāṃ devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
jagāmākāśamāviśya kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  || 131.33 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve vāsukipramukhā nṛpa  |
sthāpayitvā tathā jagmurdevadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 131.34 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitpañcamyāmarcayecchivam  |
tasya nāgakulānyaṣṭau na hiṃsanti kadācana  || 131.35 ||
mṛtaḥ kālena mahatā tatra tīrthe nareśvara  |
śivasyānucaro bhūtvā vasate kālamīpsitam  || 131.36 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nāgeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikatriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 132

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra uttare narmadātaṭe  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ vārāhaṃ nāma nāmataḥ  || 132.1 ||
tatra devo jagaddhātā vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthitaḥ  |
sthito lokahitārthāya saṃsārārṇavatārakaḥ  || 132.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayeddharaṇīdharam  |
gandhamālyaviśeṣaiśca jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  || 132.3 ||
upavāsaparo bhūtvā dvādaśyāṃ nṛpasattama  |
vṛṣalāḥ pāpakarmāṇastathaivāndhapiśācinaḥ  || 132.4 ||
ālāpādgātrasaṃparkānniḥśvāsātsahabhojanāt |
pāpaṃ saṃkramate yasmāttasmāttān parivarjayet || 132.5 ||
brāhmaṇān pūjayedbhaktyā yathāśaktyā yathāvidhi  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ kathāyāṃ tatra bhārata  || 132.6 ||
prabhāte vimale snātvā tatra tīrthe jagadgurum  |
ye paśyanti jitakrodhāste muktāḥ sarvapātakaiḥ  || 132.7 ||
yathā tu dṛṣṭvā bhujagāḥ suparṇaṃ naśyanti muktvā viṣamugratejaḥ  |
naśyanti pāpāni tathaiva śīghraṃ dṛṣṭvā mukhaṃ śūkararūpiṇastu  || 132.8 ||
nabhogataṃ naśyati cāndhakāraṃ dṛṣṭvā raviṃ devavaraṃ tathaiva  |
naśyanti pāpāni sudustarāṇi dṛṣṭvā mukhaṃ pārtha dharādharasya  || 132.9 ||
kiṃ tasya bahubhirmantrairbhaktiryasya janārdane  |
namo nārāyaṇāyeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ  || 132.10 ||
eko'pi kṛṣṇasya kṛtaḥ praṇāmo daśāśvamedhāvabhṛthena tulyaḥ  |
daśāśvamedhī punareti janma kṛṣṇapraṇāmī na punarbhavāya  || 132.11 ||
dhyāyamānā mahātmāno rūpaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ hareḥ  |
ye tyajanti svakaṃ dehaṃ tatra tīrthe jitendriyāḥ  || 132.12 ||
te gacchantyamalaṃ sthānaṃ yatsurairapi durlabham  |
kṣarākṣaravinirmuktaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam  || 132.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ādivārāhatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 133

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla paraṃ tīrthacatuṣṭayam  |
yeṣāṃ darśanamātreṇa sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 133.1 ||
kauberaṃ vāruṇaṃ yāmyaṃ vāyavyaṃ tu tataḥ param  |
yatra siddhā mahāprājñā lokapālā mahābalāḥ  || 133.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kimarthaṃ lokapālaiśca tapaścīrṇaṃ purānagha  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya hyetanme vaktumarhasi  || 133.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
adhiṣṭhānaṃ samicchanti hyacalaṃ nirbale sati  |
saṃsāre sarvabhūtānāṃ tṛṇabinduvadasthire  || 133.4 ||
kadalīsāraniḥsāre mṛgatṛṣṇeva cañcale  |
sthāvare jaṅgame sarve bhūtagrāme caturvidhe  || 133.5 ||
dharmo mātā pitā dharmo dharmo bandhuḥ suhṛttathā  |
ādhāraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ trailokye sacarācare  || 133.6 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu te sarve lokapālāḥ kṛtakṣaṇāḥ  |
tapaste cakruratulaṃ mārutāhāratatparāḥ  || 133.7 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevaḥ kṛtasyārddhe gate tadā  |
anurūpeṇa rājendra yugasya parameśvaraḥ  || 133.8 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa lokapālānmahābalān  |
yo yamicchati kāmaṃ vai taṃ taṃ tasya dadāmyaham  || 133.9 ||
etacchrutvā vacastasya lokapālā jagaddhitāḥ  |
varadaṃ prārthayāmāsurdevaṃ varamanuttamam  || 133.10 ||

kubera uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva yadi deyo varo mama  |
yakṣāṇāmīśvaraścāhaṃ bhavāmi dhanadastviti  || 133.11 ||
tataḥ provāca deveśaṃ yamaḥ saṃyamane rataḥ  |
tatra pradhāno bhagavān bhaveyaṃ sarvajantuṣu  || 133.12 ||
varuṇo'nantaraṃ prāha praṇamya tu maheśvaram  |
krīḍeyaṃ vāruṇe loke yādogaṇasamanvitaḥ  || 133.13 ||
jagādāśu tato vāyuḥ praṇamya tu maheśvaram  |
vyāpakatvaṃ trilokeṣu prārthayāmāsa bhārata  || 133.14 ||
teṣāṃ yadīpsitaṃ kāmamumayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
sarveṣāṃ lokapālānaḥ dattvā cādarśanaṃ gataḥ  || 133.15 ||
gate maheśvare deve yathāsthānaṃ tu te sthitāḥ  |
sthāpanā ca kṛtā sarvaiḥ svanāmnaiva pṛthakpṛthak || 133.16 ||
kuberaśca kubereśaṃ yamaścaiva yameśvaram  |
varuṇo varuṇeśaṃ tu vāto vāteśvaraṃ nṛpa  || 133.17 ||
tarpaṇaṃ vidadhuḥ sarve mantraiśca vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
sarve sarveśvaraṃ deva pūjayitvā yathāvidhi  || 133.18 ||
āhvayāmāsustān viprānsarve sarveśvarā iva  |
kṣāntadāntajitakrodhānsarvabhūtābhayapradān  || 133.19 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātān sarvaśāstraviśāradān  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃyuktāṃstathātharvavibhūṣitān  || 133.20 ||
cāturvidhyaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ dānaṃ dāsyāma gṛhṇata  |
evamuktvā tu sarveṣāṃ viprāṇāṃ dānamuttamam  || 133.21 ||
tatra sthāne dadusteṣāṃ bhūmidānamanuttamam  |
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca yāvattiṣṭhati medinī  || 133.22 ||
tāvaddānaṃ tu yuṣmākaṃ paripanthī na kaścana  |
rājā vā rājatulyo vā lokapālairanuttamam  || 133.23 ||
dattaṃ lopayate mūḍhaḥ śrūyatāṃ tasya yo vidhiḥ  |
śoṣayeddhanado vittaṃ tasya pāpasya bhārata  || 133.24 ||
śarīraṃ varuṇo devaḥ saṃtatīṃ śvasanastathā  |
āyurnayati tasyāśu yamaḥ saṃyamano mahān  || 133.25 ||
niḥśeṣaṃ bhasmasātkṛtvā hutabhugyāti bhārata  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena brāhmaṇebhyo yudhiṣṭhira  |
bhaktiḥ kāryā nṛpaiḥ sarvairicchadbhiḥ śreya ātmanaḥ  || 133.26 ||
rājā vṛkṣo brāhmaṇāstasya mūlaṃ bhṛtyāḥ parṇā mantriṇastasya śākhāḥ  |
tasmānmūlaṃ yatnato rakṣaṇīyaṃ mūle gupte nāsti vṛkṣasya nāśaḥ  || 133.27 ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi svarge tiṣṭhati bhūmidaḥ  |
ācchettā cāvamantā ca tānyeva narake vaset || 133.28 ||
svadattā paradattā vā pālanīyā vasuṃdharā  |
yasya yasya yadā bhūmistasya tasya tadā phalam  || 133.29 ||
devatājñāmanusmṛtya rājāno ye'pi tāṃ nṛpa  |
pālayiṣyanti satataṃ teṣāṃ vāsastriviṣṭape  || 133.30 ||
svadattā paradattā vā yatnādrakṣyā yudhiṣṭhira  |
mahī mahīkṣitā nityaṃ dānācchreyo'nupālanam  || 133.31 ||
āyuryaśo balaṃ vittaṃ saṃtatiścākṣayā nṛpa  |
teṣāṃ bhaviṣyate nūnaṃ ye prajāpālane ratāḥ  || 133.32 ||
evamuktvā tu tān sarvāṃllokapālān dvijottamān  |
pūjayitvā vidhānena praṇipatya vyasarjayan  || 133.33 ||
gateṣu vipramukhyeṣu snātvā hutahutāśanāḥ  |
lokapālāḥ kṣudhāviṣṭāḥ paryaṭanbhaikṣamātmanaḥ  || 133.34 ||
asthicarmāvaśeṣāṅgāḥ kapāloddhṛtapāṇayaḥ  |
alabdhagrāsamarddhārdhaṃ niryayurnagarādbahiḥ  || 133.35 ||
śāpaṃ dattvā tadā krodhādbrāhmaṇāya yudhiṣṭhira  |
daridrāḥ satataṃ mūrkhā bhaveyuśca yayurgṛhān  || 133.36 ||
tadāprabhṛti te sarve brāhmaṇā dhanavarjitāḥ  |
śāpadoṣeṇa kauberyāṃ saṃjātā duḥkhabhājanāḥ  || 133.37 ||
na dhanaṃ paitṛkaṃ putrairna pitā putrapautrikam  |
bhuñjate sakalaṃ kālamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 133.38 ||
kubereśo naraḥ snātvā yastu pūjayate śivam  |
gandhadhūpanamaskāraiḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 133.39 ||
yamatīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā sampaśyati yameśvaram  |
sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyeta saptajanmāntarārjitaiḥ  || 133.40 ||
pūrṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāṃ snātvā tu pitṛtarpaṇam  |
yaḥ karoti tilaiḥ snānaṃ tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 133.41 ||
sutṛptāstena toyena pitaraśca pitāmahāḥ  |
svargasthā dvādaśābdāni krīḍanti prapitāmahāḥ  || 133.42 ||
varuṇeśe naraḥ snātvā hyarcayitvā maheśvaram  |
vājapeyasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti puṣkalam  || 133.43 ||
mṛtāṃ kālena mahatā loke yatra jaleśvaraḥ  |
sa gacchettatra yānena gīyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 133.44 ||
vāteśvare naraḥ snātvā sampūjya ca maheśvaram  |
jāyate kṛtakṛtyo'sau lokapālānavekṣayan  || 133.45 ||
kiṃ tasya bahubhiryajñairdānairvā bahudakṣiṇaiḥ  |
snātvā catuṣṭaye loke avāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam  || 133.46 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
nityaṃ vasanti kaurilyāṃ lokapālānnimantrya ye  || 133.47 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ dhanyamāyurvivardhanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 133.48 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kuberāditīrthacatuṣṭayamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayastriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 134

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle rāmeśvaramanuttamam  |
tīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  || 134.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu ye snātvā pūjayanti maheśvaram  |
mahādevaṃ mahātmānaṃ mucyante sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 134.2 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe rāmeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catustriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 135

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ cānyatsiddheśvaramanuttamam  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ sarvalokeṣu pūjitam  || 135.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā hyumārudraṃ prapūjayet |
vājapeyasya yajñasya sa labhetphalamuttamam  || 135.2 ||
tena puṇyena mahatā mṛtaḥ svargamavāpnuyāt |
apsarogaṇasaṃvīto jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  || 135.3 ||
sahasravatsarāṃstatra krīḍayitvā yathāsukham  |
dhanadhānyasamopete kule mahati jāyate  || 135.4 ||
pūjyamāno naraśreṣṭha vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
vyādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 135.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe siddheśvaramāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 136

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla cāhalyeśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgā tvahalyā tāpasī purā  || 136.1 ||
gautamo brāhmaṇastvāsītsākṣādbrahmeva cāparaḥ  |
satyadharmasamāyukto vānaprasthāśrame rataḥ  || 136.2 ||
tasya patnī mahābhāgā hyahalyā nāma viśrutā  |
rūpayauvanasampannā triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā  || 136.3 ||
asyā apyatirūpeṇa devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ  |
mohito lobhayāmāsa hyahalyāṃ balasūdanaḥ  || 136.4 ||
māṃ bhajasva varārohe devarājamanindite  |
krīḍayasva mayā sārddhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu pūjitā  || 136.5 ||
kiṃ kariṣyasi vipreṇa śaucācārakṛśena tu  |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaśīlena kliśyantīva sulocane  || 136.6 ||
evamuktā varārohā strīsvabhāvātsucañcalā  |
manasādhyāya śakraṃ sā kāmena kaluṣīkṛtā  || 136.7 ||
tasyā viditvā taṃ bhāvaṃ sa devaḥ pākaśāsanaḥ  |
gautamaṃ vañcayāmāsa duṣṭabhāvena bhāvitaḥ  || 136.8 ||
viditvā cāntaraṃ tasya gṛhītvā veṣamuttamam  |
ahalyāṃ ramayāmāsa viśvastāṃ mandirāntike  || 136.9 ||
kṣaṇamātrāntare tatra devarājasya bhārata  |
ājagāma muniśreṣṭho mandiraṃ tvarayānvitaḥ  || 136.10 ||
āgataṃ gautamaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhītabhītaḥ puraṃdaraḥ  |
nirgataḥ sa tato dṛṣṭvā śakro'yamiti cintayan  || 136.11 ||
tataḥ śaśāpa devendraṃ gautamaḥ krodhamūrchitaḥ  |
ajitendriyo'si yasmāttvaṃ tasmādbahubhago bhava  || 136.12 ||
evamuktastu devendrastatkṣaṇādeva bhārata  |
bhagānāṃ tu sahasreṇa tatkṣaṇādeva veṣṭitaḥ  || 136.13 ||
tyaktvā rājyaṃ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ gataśrīko jagāma ha  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ gautamena mahītale  || 136.14 ||
ahalyāpi tataḥ śaptā yasmāttvaṃ duṣṭacāriṇī  |
prekṣya māṃ ramase śakraṃ tasmādaśmamayī bhava  || 136.15 ||
gate varṣasahasrānte rāmaṃ dṛṣṭvā yaśasvinam  |
tīrthayātrāprasaṅgena dhautapāpā bhaviṣyasi  || 136.16 ||
evaṃ gate tataḥ kāle dṛṣṭā rāmeṇa dhīmatā  |
viśvāmitrasahāyena tyaktvā sāśmamayīṃ tanum  || 136.17 ||
pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṃ gatapāpā vimatsarā  |
āgatā narmadātīre tīrthe snātvā yathāvidhi  || 136.18 ||
kṛtaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ māsaṃ kṛcchraṃ cānyaṃ tataḥ param  |
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo dattvā varamanuttamam  || 136.19 ||
jagāmādarśanaṃ bhūyo reme comāpatiściram  |
ahalyā tu gate deve sthāpayitvā jagadgurum  || 136.20 ||
ahalyeśvaranāmānaṃ svagṛhe cāgamatpunaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  || 136.21 ||
sa mṛtaḥ svargamāpnoti yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  |
krīḍayitvā yathākāmaṃ tatra loke mahātapāḥ  || 136.22 ||
gate varṣasahasrānte mānuṣyaṃ labhate punaḥ  |
dhanadhānyacayopetaḥ putrapautrasamanvitaḥ  || 136.23 ||
vedavidyāśrayo dhīmāñjāyate vimale kule  |
rūpasaubhāgyasampannaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgramahalyātīrthasevanāt || 136.24 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ahalyātīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 137

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dharmaputra tato gacchetkarkaṭeśvaramuttamam  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 137.1 ||
tatra snātvā vidhānena yastu pūjayate śivam  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 137.2 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ purāṇe yacchrutaṃ mayā  |
na tadvarṇayituṃ śakyaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa vadāmyataḥ  || 137.3 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kuryātkiṃcitkarma śubhāśubham  |
harṣānmadānmahārāja tatsarvaṃ jāyate'kṣayam  || 137.4 ||
tatra tīrthe tapastaptvā vālakhilyā marīcayaḥ  |
ramante'dyāpi lokeṣu svecchayā kurunandana  || 137.5 ||
tatrasthāstanna jānanti narājñānabahiṣkṛtāḥ  |
śarīrasthamivātmānamakṣayaṃ jyotikhyayam  || 137.6 ||
tatra tīrthe nṛpaśreṣṭha devī nārāyaṇī purā  |
adyāpi tapate ghoraṃ tapo yāvatkilārbudam  || 137.7 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni tṛptiṃ yānti pitāmahāḥ  || 137.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe karkaṭeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptatriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 138

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchetpāṇḍuputra śakratīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddho mahābhāgo devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ  || 138.1 ||
gautamena purā śaptaṃ jñātvā devāḥ sureśvaram  |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarva ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ  || 138.2 ||
gautamaṃ prārthayāmāsurvākyaiḥ sānunayaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
gatarājyaṃ gataśrīkaṃ śakraṃ prati munīśvara  || 138.3 ||
indrena rahitaṃ rājyaṃ na kaścitkāmayeddvija  |
devo vā mānavo vāpi etatte viditaṃ prabho  || 138.4 ||
tasya tvaṃ bhagayuktasya dayāṃ kuru dvijottama  |
gataścādarśanaṃ śakro dūṣitaḥ svena pāpmanā  || 138.5 ||
devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā gautamo vedavittamaḥ  |
tatheti kṛtvā śakrasya varaṃ dātuṃ pracakrame  || 138.6 ||
etadbhagasahasraṃ tu purā jātaṃ śatakrato  |
tallocanasahasraṃ tu matprasādādbhaviṣyati  || 138.7 ||
evamuktaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ praṇamya munisattamam  |
brāhmaṇāṃstānmahābhāgānnarmadāṃ pratyagāttataḥ  || 138.8 ||
snātvā sa vimale toye saṃsthāpya tripurāntakam  |
jagāma tridaśāvāsaṃ pūjyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 138.9 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
paradārābhigamanānmucyate pātakānnaraḥ  || 138.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śakratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭatriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 139

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja somatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra somastapastaptvā nakṣatrapathamāsthitaḥ  || 139.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snāyādācamya vidhipūrvakam  |
kṛtajāpyo raviṃ dhyāyettasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 139.2 ||
ṛgvedayajurvedābhyāṃ sāmavedena bhārata  |
japato yatphalaṃ proktaṃ gāyatryā cātra tatphalam  || 139.3 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā brāhmaṇān bhojayecchuciḥ  |
tena samyagvidhānena koṭirbhavati bhojitā  || 139.4 ||
pādukopānahau chatraṃ vastrakambalavājinaḥ  |
yo datte vipramukhyāya tasya tatkoṭisaṃmitam  || 139.5 ||
sahasraṃ tu sahasrāṇāmanṛcāṃ yastu bhojayet |
ekasya mantrayuktasya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm  || 139.6 ||
evaṃ tu bhojayettatra bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam  |
śākhāntargamathādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ vā samāptigam  || 139.7 ||
agnihotrasahasrasya yatphalaṃ prāpyate budhaiḥ  |
samaṃ tadvedaviduṣā tīrthe somasya tatphalam  || 139.8 ||
bhojayedyaḥ śataṃ teṣāṃ sahasraṃ labhate naraḥ  |
ekasya yogayuktasya tatphalaṃ kavayo viduḥ  || 139.9 ||
saṃnirudhyendriyagrāmaṃ yatrayatra vasenmuniḥ  |
tatratatra kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca  || 139.10 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
saṃkrāntau ca vyatīpāte yogī bhojyo viśeṣataḥ  || 139.11 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yastu tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
vimānena mahābhāgāḥ sa yāti tridivaṃ naraḥ  || 139.12 ||
somasyānucaro bhūtvā tenaiva saha modate  || 139.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe somatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonacatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 140

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja nandāhradamanuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgā nandā devī varapradā  || 140.1 ||
mahiṣāsure mahākāye purā devabhayaṃkare  |
śūlinyā śūlabhinnāṅge kṛte dānavasattame  || 140.2 ||
yenaikādaśarudrāśca hyādityāḥ samarudgaṇāḥ  |
vasavo vāyunā sārddhaṃ candrādityau sureśvara  || 140.3 ||
balinā nirjitā yena brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
saṅgrāme sumahāghore kṛte devabhayaṃkare  || 140.4 ||
kṛtvā tatkadanaṃ ghoraṃ nandā devī sureśvarī  |
yasmātsnātā viśālākṣī tena nandāhradaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 140.5 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā nandāmuddiśya bhārata  |
dadāti dānaṃ viprebhyaḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 140.6 ||
bhairavaṃ caiva kedāraṃ tathā rudraṃ mahālayam  |
nandāhradaścaturthaḥ syātpañcamaṃ bhuvi durlabham  || 140.7 ||
bahavastaṃ na jānanti kāmarāgasamanvitāḥ  |
narmadāyā hradaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  || 140.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā nandāṃ devīṃ prapūjayet |
kiṃ tasya himavanmadhyagamanena prayojanam  || 140.9 ||
paramārthamavijñāya paryaṭanti tamovṛtāḥ  |
teṣāṃ samāgame pārtha śrama eva hi kevalam  || 140.10 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ snānadānena yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti snātvā nandāhrade nṛpa  || 140.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nandāhradatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 141

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tāpeśvaramanuttamam  |
yatra sā hariṇī siddhā vyādhabhītā nareśvara  || 141.1 ||
jale prakṣipya gātrāṇi hyantarikṣaṃ gatā tu sā  |
vyādho vismitacittastu tāṃ mṛgīmavalokya ca  || 141.2 ||
vimucya saśaraṃ cāpaṃ prārebhe tapa uttamam  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu vyādhenācaritaṃ tapaḥ  || 141.3 ||
atīte tu tataḥ kāle parituṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  |
varaṃ brūhi mahāvyādha yatte manasi rocate  || 141.4 ||

vyādha uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
tava pārśve mahādeva vāso me pratidīyatām  || 141.5 ||

īśvara uvāca -
evaṃ bhavatu te vyādha yastvayā kāṅkṣito varaḥ  |
daivadevo mahādeva ityuktvāntaradhīyata  |
gate cādarśanaṃ deve sthāpayitvā maheśvaram  || 141.6 ||
pūjayitvā vidhānena gato vyādhastato divam  |
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 141.7 ||
vyādhānutāpasaṃjātaṃ tāpeśvaramiti śrutam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā sampūjayati śaṅkaram  || 141.8 ||
śivalokamavāpnoti māmuvāca maheśvaraḥ  |
ye snātā narmadātoye tīrthe tāpeśvare narāḥ  || 141.9 ||
tāpatrayavimuktāste nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 141.10 ||
snānaṃ samācarennityaṃ sarvapātakaśāntaye  || 141.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tāpeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 142

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja rukmiṇītīrthamuttamam  |
yatraiva snānamātreṇa rūpavānsubhago bhavet || 142.1 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
snānaṃ samācarettatra na ceha jāyate punaḥ  || 142.2 ||
yaḥ snātvā rukmiṇītīrthe dānaṃ dadyāttu kāṃcanam  |
tattīrthasya prabhāvena śokaṃ nāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 142.3 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
tīrthasyāsya kathaṃ jāto mahimedṛṅmunīśvara  |
rūpasaubhāgyadaṃ yena tīrthametadbravīhi me  || 142.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kathayāmi yathāvṛttamitihāsaṃ purātanam  |
kathitaṃ pūrvato vṛddhaiḥ pāramparyeṇa bhārata  || 142.5 ||
taṃ te'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasaḥ  |
nagaraṃ kuṇḍinaṃ nāma bhīṣmakaḥ paripāti hi  || 142.6 ||
hastyaśvarathasampanno dhanāḍhyo'ti pratāpavān  |
strīsahasrasya madhyasthaḥ kurute rājyamuttamam  || 142.7 ||
tasya bhāryā mahādevī prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasī  |
tasyāmutpādayāmāsa putramekaṃ ca rukmakam  || 142.8 ||
dvitīyā tanayā jajñe rukmiṇī nāma nāmataḥ  |
tadāśarīriṇī vācā rājānaṃ tamuvāca ha  || 142.9 ||
caturbhujāya dātavyā kanyeyaṃ bhuvi bhīṣmaka  |
evaṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jaharṣa priyayā saha  || 142.10 ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ saha vidvadbhiḥ praviṣṭaḥ sūtikāgṛham  |
svastikaṃ vācayitvāsyāścakre nāmeti rukmiṇī  || 142.11 ||
yataḥ suvarṇatilako janmanā saha bhārata  |
tataḥ sā rukmiṇīnāma brāhmaṇaiḥ kīrtitā tadā  || 142.12 ||
tataḥ sā kālaparyāyādaṣṭavarṣā vyajāyata  |
pūrvoktaṃ caiva tadvākyamaśarīriṇyudīritam  || 142.13 ||
smṛtvā smṛtvātha nṛpatiścintayāmāsa bhūpatiḥ  |
kasmai deyā mayā bālā bhavitā kaścaturbhujaḥ  || 142.14 ||
etasminnantare tāvadraivatātparvatottamāt |
mukhyaścedipatistatra damaghoṣaḥ samāgataḥ  || 142.15 ||
praviṣṭo rājasadanaṃ yatra rājā sa bhīṣmakaḥ  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā cāgataṃ gehe pūjayāmāsa bhūpatiḥ  || 142.16 ||
āsanaṃ vipulaṃ dattvā sabhāṃ gatvā niveśitaḥ  |
kuśalaṃ tava rājendra damaghoṣa śriyāyuta  || 142.17 ||
puṇyāhamadya saṃjātamahaṃ tvaddarśanotsukaḥ  |
kanyā madīyā rājendra hyaṣṭavarṣā vyajāyata  || 142.18 ||
caturbhujāya dātavyā vāguvācāśarīriṇī  |
bhīṣmakasya vacaḥ śrutvā damaghoṣo'bravīdidam  || 142.19 ||
caturbhujo mama sutastriṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  |
tasyeyaṃ dīyatāṃ kanyā śiśupālasya bhīṣmaka  || 142.20 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā damaghoṣasya bhūmipa  |
bhīṣmakena tato dattā śiśupālāya rukmiṇī  || 142.21 ||
prārabdhaṃ maṅgalaṃ tatra bhīṣmakeṇa yudhiṣṭhira  |
dikṣu deśāntareṣveva ye vasanti svagotrajāḥ  || 142.22 ||
nimantritāstu te sarve samājagmuryathākramam  |
tato yādavavaṃśasya tilakau balakeśavau  || 142.23 ||
nimantritau samāyātau kuṇḍinaṃ bhīṣmakasya tu  |
bhīṣmakeṇa yathānyāyaṃ pūjitau tau yadūttamau  || 142.24 ||
tataḥ pradoṣasamaye rukmiṇī kāmamohinī  |
sakhībhiḥ sahitā yātā pūrbahiścāmbikārcane  || 142.25 ||
sāpaśyattatra deveśaṃ gopaveṣadharaṃ harim  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mohamāpannā kāmena kaluṣīkṛtā  || 142.26 ||
keśavo'pi ca tāṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃkarṣaṇamuvāca ha  |
strīratnapravaraṃ tāta hartavyamiti me matiḥ  || 142.27 ||
keśavasya vacaḥ śrutvā saṃkarṣaṇa uvāca ha  |
gaccha kṛṣṇa mahābāho strīratnaṃ cāśu gṛhyatām  || 142.28 ||
ahaṃ ca tava mārgeṇa hyāgamiṣyāmi pṛṣṭhataḥ  |
dānavānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kurvaṃśca kadanaṃ mahat || 142.29 ||
saṃkarṣaṇamataṃ prāpya keśavaḥ keśisūdanaḥ  |
yayau kanyāṃ gṛhītvā tu rathamāropya satvaram  || 142.30 ||
nirgataḥ sahasā rājanvegenaivānilo yathā  |
hāhākārastadā jāto bhīṣmakasya pure mahān  || 142.31 ||
nirgatā dānavāḥ kruddhā velā iva mahodadheḥ  |
garjantaḥ sāyudhāḥ sarve dhāvanto rathavartmani  || 142.32 ||
baladevaṃ tataḥ prāptā rathamārgānugāminam  |
teṣāṃ yuddhaṃ balasyāsītsarvalokakṣayaṃkaram  || 142.33 ||
yathā tārāmaye pūrvaṃ saṅgrāme lokaviśrute  |
gadāhasto mahābāhustrailokye'pratimo balaḥ  || 142.34 ||
halenākṛṣya sahasā gadāpātairapātayat |
aśakyo dānavairhantuṃ balabhadro mahābalaḥ  || 142.35 ||
babhañja dānavānsarvāṃstasthau giririvācalaḥ  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā ca balaṃ kruddhaṃ durdharṣaṃ tridaśairapi  || 142.36 ||
bhīṣmaputro mahātejā rukmīnāṃ mahayaśāḥ  |
narāṇāmatiśūrāṇāmakṣauhiṇyā samanvitaḥ  || 142.37 ||
balabhadramatikramya tato yuddhe nirākarot |
tadyuddhaṃ vañcayitvā tu rathamārgeṇa satvaram  || 142.38 ||
keśavo'pi tadā devo rukmiṇyā sahito yayau  |
vindhyaṃ tu laṅghayitvāgre trailokyagururavyayaḥ  || 142.39 ||
narmadātaṭamāpede yatra siddhaḥ purā punaḥ  |
ajeyo yena saṃjātastīrthasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 142.40 ||
etasmātkāraṇāttāta yodhanīpuramucyate  |
rukmo'pi dānavendro'sau prāptaḥ  || 142.41 ||
pratyuvācācyutaṃ kruddhastiṣṭha tiṣṭheti mā vraja  |
adya tvāṃ niśitairbāṇairneṣyāmi yamasādanam  || 142.42 ||
evaṃ parasparaṃ vīrau jagarjaturubhāvapi  |
tayoryuddhamabhūdghoraṃ tārakāgnijasannibham  || 142.43 ||
cikṣepa śarajālāni keśavaṃ prati dānavaḥ  |
nānucintya śarāṃstasya keśavaḥ keśisūdanaḥ  || 142.44 ||
tato viṣṇuḥ svayaṃ kruddhaścakraṃ gṛhya sudarśanam  |
sampraharatyamuṃ yāvadrukmiṇyātra nivāritaḥ  || 142.45 ||
tvāṃ na jānāti deveśaṃ caturbāhuṃ janārdanam  |
darśayasva svakaṃ rūpaṃ dayāṃ kṛtvā mamopari  || 142.46 ||
evamuktastu rukmiṇyā darśayāmāsa bhārata  |
devā dṛṣṭvāpi tadrūpaṃ stuvantyākāśasaṃsthitāḥ  |
divyaṃ cakṣustadā devo dadau rukmasya bhārata  || 142.47 ||

rukma uvāca -
yanmayā pāpaniṣṭhena mandabhāgyena keśava  |
sāyakairāhataṃ vakṣastatsarvaṃ kṣantumarhasi  || 142.48 ||
pūrvaṃ dattā svayaṃ deva jānakī janakena vai  |
mayā pradattā deveśa rukmiṇī tava keśava  || 142.49 ||
udvāhaya yathānyāyaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  |
rukmasya vacanaṃ śrutvā tatastuṣṭo jagadguruḥ  || 142.50 ||
babhāṣe devadeveśo rukmiṇaṃ bhīṣmakātmajam  |
gaccha svakaṃ puraṃ mā bhaiḥ kuru rājyamakaṇṭakam  || 142.51 ||
keśavasya vacaḥ śrutvā rukmo dānavapuṃgavaḥ  |
taṃ praṇamya jagannāthaṃ jagāma bhavanaṃ pituḥ  || 142.52 ||
gate rukme tadā kṛṣṇaḥ samāmantrya dvijottamān  |
marīcimatryaṅgirasaṃ pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum  || 142.53 ||
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahābhāgamityete sapta mānasāḥ  |
ityete brāhmaṇāḥ sapta purāṇe niścayaṃ gatāḥ  || 142.54 ||
kṣamāvantaḥ prajāvanto maharṣibhiralaṃkṛtāḥ  |
ityevaṃ brahmaputrāśca satyavanto mahāmate  || 142.55 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya nivasanti jitendriyāḥ  |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaniratā japahomaparāyaṇāḥ  || 142.56 ||
nimantritāstu rājendra keśavena mahātmanā  |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ brahmoktavidhinā tataḥ  || 142.57 ||
haristānpūjayāmāsa saptabrahmarṣipuṃgavān  |
pradadau dvādaśa grāmāṃstebhyastatra janārdanaḥ  || 142.58 ||
yāvaccandraśca sūryaśca yāvattiṣṭhati medinī  |
tāvaddānaṃ mayā dattaṃ paripanthī na kaścana  || 142.59 ||
maddattaṃ pālayiṣyante ye nṛpā gatakalmaṣāḥ  |
tebhyaḥ svasti kariṣyāmi dāsyāmi paramāṃ gatim  || 142.60 ||
yāvaddhi yānti lokeṣu mahābhūtāni pañca ca  |
tāvatte divi modante maddattaparipālakāḥ  || 142.61 ||
yastu lopayate mūḍho dattaṃ vaḥ pṛthivītale  |
narake tasya vāsaḥ syādyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 142.62 ||
svadattā paradattā vā pālanīyā vasuṃdharā  |
yasya yasya yadā bhūmistasya tasya tadā phalam  || 142.63 ||
svadattāṃ paradattāṃ vā yo hareta vasuṃdharām  |
sa viṣṭhāyāṃ kṛmirbhūtvā pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati  || 142.64 ||
anyāyena hṛtā bhūmiranyāyena ca hāritā  |
hartā hārayitā caiva viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ  || 142.65 ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi svarge tiṣṭhati bhūmidaḥ  |
ācchettā cānumantā ca tānyeva narake vaset || 142.66 ||
yānīha dattāni purā narendrairdānāni dharmārthayaśaskarāṇi  |
nirmālyarūpapratimāni tāni ko nāma sādhuḥ punarādadāti  || 142.67 ||
evaṃ tānpūjayitvā tu samyaṅnyāyena pāṇḍava  |
rukmiṇyā vidhivatpāṇiṃ jagrāha madhusūdanaḥ  || 142.68 ||
muśalī ca tataḥ sarvāñjitvā dānavapuṃgavān  |
svasthānamagamattatra kṛtvā kāryaṃ suśobhanam  || 142.69 ||
prayātau dvāravatyāṃ tau kṛṣṇasaṃkarṣaṇāvubhau  |
gacchamānaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā keśavaṃ kleśanāśanam  || 142.70 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ satyavantaśca nirgatāḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
āgacchamānāṃstau vīkṣya rathamārgeṇa brāhmaṇān  || 142.71 ||
muhūrtaṃ tatra viśramya keśavo vākyamabravīt |
kimāgamanakāryaṃ vo brūta sarvaṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 142.72 ||
kurvāṇāḥ svīyakarmāṇi mama kṛtyaṃ tu tiṣṭhate  |
devasya vacanaṃ śrutvā munayo vākyamabruvan  || 142.73 ||
kalpakoṭisahasreṇa satyabhāvāttu vanditaḥ  |
duṣprāpyo'si manuṣyāṇāṃ prāptaḥ kiṃ tyajase hi naḥ  || 142.74 ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā bhagavānidamabravīt |
mathurāyāṃ dvāravatyāṃ yodhanīpura eva ca  || 142.75 ||
trikālamāgamiṣyāmi satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
evaṃ te brāhmaṇāḥ śrutvā yodhanīpuramāgatāḥ  || 142.76 ||
avatīrṇastribhāgena prādurbhāve tu māthure  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ tīrthasyotpattikāraṇam  || 142.77 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyacca vartamānaṃ tathāparam  |
yaṃ śrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 142.78 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayedbalakeśavau  |
tena devo jagaddhātā pūjitastriguṇātmavān  || 142.79 ||
upavāsī naro bhūtvā yastu kuryātpradakṣiṇam  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 142.80 ||
tatra tīrthe tu ye vṛkṣāstānpaśyantyapi ye narāḥ  |
te'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyante bhrūṇahatyāsamairapi  || 142.81 ||
prātarutthāya ye kecitpaśyanti balakeśavau  |
tena te sadṛśāḥ syurvai devadevena cakriṇā  || 142.82 ||
te pūjyāste namaskāryāsteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
ye namanti jagannāthaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ harim  || 142.83 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaddānaṃ snānaṃ devārcanaṃ nṛpa  |
tatsarvamakṣayaṃ tasya ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 142.84 ||
praviśyāgnau mṛtānāṃ ca yatphalaṃ samudāhṛtam  |
tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpaśreṣṭha procyamānamaśeṣataḥ  || 142.85 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṃkiṇījālamālinā  |
āgneye bhavate tatra modate kālamīpsitam  || 142.86 ||
jale caivā mṛtānāṃ tu yodhanīpuramadhyataḥ  |
vasanti vāruṇe loke yāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 142.87 ||
anāśake mṛtānāṃ tu tatra tīrthe narādhipa  |
anivartikā gatirnṛṇāṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 142.88 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo dadyātkapilādānamuttamam  |
vidhānena tu saṃyuktaṃ śṛṇu tasyāpi yatphalam  || 142.89 ||
yāvanti tasyā romāṇi tatprasūteśca bhārata  |
tāvanti divi modante sarvakāmaiḥ supūjitāḥ  || 142.90 ||
yāvanti romāṇi bhavanti dhenvāstāvanti varṣāṇi mahīyate saḥ  |
svargāccyutaścāpi tatastrilokyāṃ kule samutpatsyati gomatāṃ saḥ  || 142.91 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo dadyādrūpyaṃ kāñcanameva vā  |
kāñcanena vimānena viṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 142.92 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yo dadyātpāduke vastrameva ca  |
dānasyāsya prabhāvena labhate svargamīpsitam  || 142.93 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmavedānāṃ paṭhanādyatphalaṃ bhavet |
tatra tīrthe tu rājendra gāyatryā tatphalaṃ labhet || 142.94 ||
prayāge yadbhavetpuṇyaṃ gayāyāṃ ca tripuṣkare  |
kurukṣetre tu rājendra rāhugraste divākare  || 142.95 ||
someśvare ca yatpuṇyaṃ somasya grahaṇe tathā  |
tatphalaṃ labhate tatra snānamātrānna saṃśayaḥ  || 142.96 ||
dvādaśyāṃ tu naraḥ snātvā namaskṛtya janārdanam  |
uddhṛtāḥ pitarastena avāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam  || 142.97 ||
saṃkrāntau ca vyatīpāte dvādaśyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayedekaṃ koṭirbhavati bhojitā  || 142.98 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni hyāsamudrāṇi pāṇḍava  |
tāni sarvāṇi tatraiva dvādaśyāṃ pāṇḍunandana  || 142.99 ||
kṣayaṃ yānti ca dānāni yajñahomabalikriyāḥ  |
na kṣīyate mahārāja tatra tīrthe tu yatkṛtam  || 142.100 ||
yadbhūtaṃ yadbhaviṣyacca tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
kathitaṃ te mayā sarvaṃ pṛthagbhāvena bhārata  || 142.101 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe rukmiṇītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvicatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 143

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja yojaneśvaramuttamam  |
yatra siddhau purā kalpe naranārāyaṇāvṛṣī  || 143.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tapastaptvā saṅgrāme devadānavaiḥ  |
jayaṃ prāptau mahātmānau naranārāyaṇāvubhau  || 143.2 ||
punastretāyuge prāpte tau devau rāmalakṣmaṇau  |
tatra tīrthe punaḥ snātvā rāvaṇo durjayo hataḥ  || 143.3 ||
punaḥ pārtha kalau prāpte tau devau balakeśavau  |
vasudevakule jātau duṣkaraṃ karma cakratuḥ  || 143.4 ||
narakaṃ kālanemiṃ ca kaṃsaṃ cāṇūramuṣṭikau  |
śiśupālaṃ jarāsaṃdhaṃ jaghnaturbalakeśavau  || 143.5 ||
tatastatra ripūnsaṃkhye bhīṣmadroṇapuraḥsarān  |
karṇaduryodhanādīṃśca nihaniṣyati sa prabhuḥ  || 143.6 ||
dharmakṣetre kurukṣetre tatra yudhyanti te kṣaṇam  |
bhīmārjunanimittena śiṣyau kṛtvā parasparam  || 143.7 ||
tatra tīrthe punargatvā tapaḥ kṛtvā suduṣkaram  |
pūjayitvā dvijānbhaktyā yāsyete dvārakāṃ punaḥ  || 143.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayedbalakeśavau  |
tena devo jagaddhātā pūjitastriguṇātmavān  || 143.9 ||
upavāsī naro bhūtvā yastu kuryātprajāgaram  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo gāyaṃstasya śubhāṃ kathām  || 143.10 ||
yāvatastatra tīrthe tu vṛkṣān paśyanti mānavāḥ  |
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ tāvadeṣāṃ praṇaśyati  || 143.11 ||
prātarutthāya ye kecitpaśyanti balakeśavau  |
tenaiva sadṛśāḥ sarve devadevena cakriṇā  || 143.12 ||
te pūjyāste namaskāryāsteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
ye namanti jagatpūjyaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ harim  || 143.13 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaddānaṃ snānaṃ devārcanaṃ nṛpa  |
kriyate tatphalaṃ sarvamakṣayāyopakalpate  || 143.14 ||
agnerapatyaṃ prathamaṃ suvarṇaṃ bhūrvaiṣṇavī sūryasutāśca gāvaḥ  |
lokāstrayastena bhavanti dattā yaḥ kāñcanaṃ gāṃ ca bhuvaṃ ca dadyāt || 143.15 ||
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
atītaṃ ca bhaviṣyacca vartamānaṃ mahābalam  || 143.16 ||
śrutvā vāpi paṭhitvedaṃ śrāvayipatvātha dhārmikān  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 143.17 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe yojaneśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tricatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 144

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja dvādaśītīrthamuttamam  |
kṣaranti sarvadānāni japahomabalikriyāḥ  || 144.1 ||
na kṣīyate tu rājendra cakratīrthe tu yatkṛtam  |
yadbhūtaṃ yadbhaviṣyacca tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  || 144.2 ||
kathitaṃ tanmayā sarvaṃ pṛthagbhāvena bhārata  || 144.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dvādaśītīrthamahātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 145
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharāpāla śīvatīrthamanuttamam  |
darśanādyasya devasya mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 145.1 ||
śivatīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  |
pūjayeta mahādevaṃ so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 145.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā sopavāso'rcayecchivam  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 145.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śivatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcacatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 146

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
asmāhakaṃ tato gacchetpitṛtīrthamanuttamam  |
pretatvādyatra mucyante piṇḍenaikena pūrvajāḥ  || 146.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
asmāhakasya māhātmyaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  |
snānadānena yatpuṇyaṃ tathā piṇḍodakena ca  || 146.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā kalpe nṛpaśreṣṭha ṛṣidevasamāgame  |
praśnaḥ pṛṣṭo mayā tāta yathā tvamanupṛcchasi  || 146.3 ||
ekatra sāgarāḥ sapta saprayāgāḥ sapuṣkarāḥ  |
nāsya sāmyaṃ labhante te nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 146.4 ||
somanāthaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ yatsomena pratiṣṭhitam  |
tatra somagrahe puṇyaṃ tatpuṇyaṃ labhate naraḥ  || 146.5 ||
māsānte pitaro nṛṇāṃ vīkṣante santatiṃ svakām  |
kaścidasmatkule'smākaṃ piṇḍamatra pradāsyati  || 146.6 ||
prapitāmahāstathādityāḥ śrutireṣā sanātanī  |
evaṃ bruvanti devāśca ṛṣayaḥ satapodhanāḥ  || 146.7 ||
sakṛtpiṇḍodakenaiva śṛṇu pārthiva yatphalam  |
dvādaśābdāni rājendra yogaṃ bhuktvā suśobhanam  || 146.8 ||
yuge yuge mahārāja asmāhake pitāmahāḥ  |
sarvadā hyavalokanta āgacchantaṃ svagotrajam  || 146.9 ||
bhaviṣyati kimasmākamamāvāsyāpyamāhake  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ ca ye kuryuḥ pitḥṇāṃ tilatarpaṇam  || 146.10 ||
te sarvapāpanirmuktāḥ sarvānkāmāṃllabhati vai  |
jalamadhye'tra bhūpāla agnitīrthaṃ ca tiṣṭhati  || 146.11 ||
darśanāttasya tīrthasya pāparāśirvilīyate  |
snānamātreṇa rājendra brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 146.12 ||
śuklāmbaradharo nityaṃ niyataḥ sa jitendriyaḥ  |
ekakālaṃ tu bhuñjāno māsaṃ tīrthasya sannidhau  || 146.13 ||
suvarṇālaṃkṛtānāṃ tu kanyānāṃ śatadānajam  |
phalamāpnoti sampūrṇaṃ pitṛloke mahīyate  || 146.14 ||
pṛthivyāmāsamudrāyāṃ mahābhogapatirbhavet |
dhanadhānyasamāyukto dātā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 146.15 ||
upavāsī śucirbhūtvā brahmalokamavāpnuyāt |
asmāhakaṃ samāsādya yastu prāṇān parityajet || 146.16 ||
koṭivarṣasahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate  |
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā divaścyutaḥ  || 146.17 ||
suvarṇamaṇimuktāḍhye kule jāyeta rūpavān  |
kṛtvābhiṣekavidhinā hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 146.18 ||
dhanāḍhyo rūpavāndakṣo dātā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  |
caturvedeṣu yatpuṇyaṃ satyavādiṣu yatphalam  || 146.19 ||
tatphalaṃ labhate nūnaṃ tatra tīrthe'bhiṣecanāt |
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ nirmitaṃ śambhunā purā  || 146.20 ||
hṛdayeśaḥ svayaṃ viṣṇurjapeddevaṃ maheśvaram  |
gandharvāpsarasaścaiva maruto mārutāstathā  || 146.21 ||
viśvedevāśca pitaraḥ sacandrāḥ sadivākarāḥ  |
marīciratryaṅgirasau pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  || 146.22 ||
pracetāśca vasiṣṭhaśca bhṛgurnārada eva ca  |
cyavano gālavaścaiva vāmadevo mahāmuniḥ  || 146.23 ||
vālakhilyāśca gandhārāstṛṇabinduśca jājaliḥ  |
uddālakaścarṣyaśṛṅgo vasiṣṭhaśca sanandanaḥ  || 146.24 ||
śukraścaiva bharadvājo vātsyo vātsyāyanastathā  |
agastirmitrāvaruṇau viśvāmitro munīśvaraḥ  || 146.25 ||
gautamaśca pulastyaśca paulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  |
sanātanastu kapilo vāhniḥ pañcaśikhastathā  || 146.26 ||
