anye'pi bahavastatra munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ  |
krīḍanti devatāḥ sarva ṛṣayaḥ satapodhanāḥ  || 146.27 ||
manuṣyāścaiva yogīndrāḥ pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ  |
asmāhake'tra tiṣṭhanti sarva eva na saṃśayaḥ  || 146.28 ||
pitaraḥ pitāmahāścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahāḥ  |
yeṣāṃ dattamupasthāyi sukṛtaṃ vāpi duṣkṛtam  || 146.29 ||
akṣayaṃ tatra tatsarvaṃ yatkṛtaṃ yodhanīpure  |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ tyaktvā sarvabandhusuhṛjjanān  || 146.30 ||
dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ priyānputrāṃstathā dehaṃ nṛpottama  |
gacchate vāyubhūtastu śubhāśubhasamanvitaḥ  || 146.31 ||
adṛśyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ paramātmā mahattaraḥ  |
śubhāśubhagatiṃ prāptaḥ karmaṇā svena pārthiva  || 146.32 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
śubhāśubhaṃ na bandhūnāṃ jāyate kena hetunā  |
ekaḥ prasūyate jantureka eva pralīyate  || 146.33 ||
eko hi bhuṅkte sukṛtameka eva hi duṣkṛtam  || 146.34 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
eṣa tvayokto nṛpate mahāpraśnaḥ smṛto mayā  || 146.35 ||
pitāmahamukhodgītaṃ śrutaṃ te kathayāmyaham  |
yanme pitāmahātpūrvaṃ vijñātamṛṣisaṃsadi  || 146.36 ||
na mātā na pitā bandhuḥ kasyacinna suhṛtkvacit |
kasya na jñāyate rūpaṃ vāyubhūtasya dehinaḥ  || 146.37 ||
yadyevaṃ na bhavettāta lokasya tu nareśvara  |
amaryādaṃ bhavennūnaṃ vinaśyati carācaram  || 146.38 ||
evaṃ jñātvā pūrā rājansamastairlokakartṛbhiḥ  |
maryādā sthāpitā loke yathā dharmo na naśyati  || 146.39 ||
dharme naṣṭe manuṣyāṇāmadharmo'bhibhavetpunaḥ  |
tataḥ svadharmacalanānnarake gamanaṃ dhruvam  || 146.40 ||
loko niraṅkuśaḥ sarvo maryādālaṅghane rataḥ  |
maryādā sthāpitā tena śāstraṃ vīkṣya maharṣibhiḥ  || 146.41 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  |
piṇḍodakapradānaṃ ca tathaivātithipūjanam  || 146.42 ||
pitaraḥ pitāmahāścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahāḥ  |
trayo devāḥ smṛtāstāta brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 146.43 ||
pūjitaiḥ pūjitāḥ sarve tathā mātāmahāstrayaḥ  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena śrutismṛtyarthanoditān  || 146.44 ||
dharmaṃ samācarannityaṃ pāpāṃśena na lipyate  |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmaṃ manasāpi na laṅghayet || 146.45 ||
iha loke pare caiva yadīcchecchreya ātmanaḥ  |
pitāputrau sadāpyekau bimbādbimbamivoddhṛtau  || 146.46 ||
vibhaktau vāvibhaktau vā śrutismṛtyarthatastathā  |
uddharedātmanātmānamātmānamavasādayet || 146.47 ||
piṇḍodakapradānābhyāmṛte pārtha na saṃśayaḥ  |
evaṃ jñātvā prayatnena piṇḍodakaprado bhavet || 146.48 ||
āyurdharmo yaśastejaḥ santatiścaiva vardhate  |
pṛthivyāṃ sāgarāntāyāṃ pitṛkṣetrāṇi yāni ca  || 146.49 ||
tāni te sampravakṣyāmi yeṣu dattaṃ mahāphalam  |
gayāyāṃ puṣkare jyeṣṭhe prayāge naimiṣe tathā  || 146.50 ||
saṃnihatyāṃ kurukṣetre prabhāse kurunandana  |
piṇḍodakapradānena yatphalaṃ kathitaṃ budhaiḥ  || 146.51 ||
asmāhake tadāpnoti narmadāyāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
tatra brahmā murāriśca rudraśca umayā saha  || 146.52 ||
indrādyā devatāḥ sarve pitaro munayastathā  |
sāgarāḥ saritaścaiva parvatāśca balāhakāḥ  || 146.53 ||
tiṣṭhanti pitaraḥ sarve sarvatīrthādhikaṃ tataḥ  |
sthitā brahmaśilā tatra gajakumbhanibhā nṛpa  || 146.54 ||
kalau na dṛśyā bhavati pradhānaṃ yadgayāśiraḥ  |
vaiśākhe māsi samprāpte'māvāsyāṃ nṛpottama  || 146.55 ||
vyāpya sā tiṣṭhate tīrthaṃ gajakumbhanibhā śilā  |
tacca gavyūtimātraṃ hi tīrthaṃ tataḥ pravakṣate  || 146.56 ||
tasmindine tatra gatvā yastu śrāddhaprado bhavet |
pitḥṇāmakṣayā tṛptirjāyate śatavārṣikī  || 146.57 ||
anyasyāmapyamāvāsyāṃ yaḥ snātvā vijitendriyaḥ  |
karoti manujaḥ śrāddhaṃ vidhivanmantrasaṃyutam  || 146.58 ||
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ yatsyāttacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  |
agniṣṭomāśvamedhābhyāṃ vājapeyasya yatphalam  || 146.59 ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti yathā me śaṅkaro'bravīt |
rauravādiṣu sarveṣu narakeṣu vyavasthitāḥ  || 146.60 ||
pitā pitāmahādyāśca pitṛke mātṛke tathā  |
piṇḍodakena caikena tarpaṇena viśeṣataḥ  || 146.61 ||
krīḍanti pitṛlokasthā yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
ye karmasthā vikarmasthā ye jātāḥ pretakalmaṣāḥ  || 146.62 ||
piṇḍenaikena mucyante te'pi tatra na saṃśayaḥ  |
asmāhake śilā divyā tiṣṭhate gajasannibhā  || 146.63 ||
brahmaṇā nirmitā pūrvaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarī  |
uparyasyā yathānyāyaṃ pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  || 146.64 ||
dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu dadyātpiṇḍānvicakṣaṇaḥ  |
bhūmau cānnena siddhena śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 146.65 ||
śrāddhibhyo vastrayugmāni chatropānatkamaṇḍalu  |
dakṣiṇā vividhā deyā pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  || 146.66 ||
yo dadāti dvijaśreṣṭha tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni tṛptiṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ  || 146.67 ||
asmāhake mahārāja pitaraśca pitāmahāḥ  |
vāyubhūtā nirīkṣante āgacchantaṃ svagotrajam  || 146.68 ||
atra tīrthe suto'bhyetya snātvā toyaṃ pradāsyati  |
śrāddhaṃ vā piṇḍadānaṃ vā tena yāsyāma sadgatim  || 146.69 ||
snāne kṛte tu ye kecijjāyante vastravipluṣaḥ  |
prīṇayennarakasthāṃstu taiḥ pitḥnnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 146.70 ||
keśodabindavastasya ye cānye lepabhājinaḥ  |
tṛpyantyanagninasaṃskārā yaṃ mṛtāḥ syuḥ svagotrajāḥ  || 146.71 ||
tatra tīrthe tu ye kecicchrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  |
narakāduddharantyāśu japantaḥ pitṛsaṃhitām  || 146.72 ||
vanaspatigate some yadā somadinaṃ bhavet |
akṣayāllabhate lokānpiṇḍenaikena mānavaḥ  || 146.73 ||
akṣayaṃ tatra vai sarvaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
narakāduddharantyāśu japante pitṛsaṃhitām  || 146.74 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tvamāvāsyāṃ pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  |
nīlaṃ sarvāṅgasampūrṇaṃ yo'bhiṣicya samutsṛjet || 146.75 ||
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ vaktuṃ na tu vācaspatiḥ kṣamaḥ  |
asmāhake vṛṣotsargādyatpuṇyaṃ samavāpyate  || 146.76 ||
tava śuśrūṣaṇātsarvaṃ tatpravakṣyāmi bhārata  |
rauravādiṣu ye kiṃcitpacyante tasya pūrvajāḥ  || 146.77 ||
vṛṣotsargeṇa tānsarvāṃstārayedekaviṃśatim  |
lohito yastu varṇena mukhe pucche ca pāṇḍuraḥ  || 146.78 ||
piṅgaḥ khuraviṣāṇābhyāṃ sa nīlo vṛṣa ucyate  |
yastu sarvāṅgapiṅgaśca śvetaḥ pucchakhureṣu ca  || 146.79 ||
sa piṅgo vṛṣa ityāhuḥ pitḥṇāṃ prītivardhanaḥ  |
pārāvatasavarṇaśca lalāṭe tilako bhavet || 146.80 ||
taṃ vṛṣaṃ babhrumityāhuḥ pūrṇaṃ sarvāṅgaśobhanam  |
sarvāṅgeṣvekavarṇo yaḥ piṅgaḥ pucchakhureṣu ca  || 146.81 ||
khurapiṅgaṃ tamityāhuḥ pitḥṇāṃ sadgatipradam  |
nīlaṃ sarvaśarīreṇa svāraktanayanaṃ dṛḍham  || 146.82 ||
tameva nīlamityāhurnīlaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
yastu vaiśyagṛhe jātaḥ sa vai nīlo viśiṣyate  || 146.83 ||
na vāhayedgṛhe jātaṃ vatsakaṃ tu kadācana  |
tenaiva ca vṛṣotsarge pitḥṇāmanṛṇo bhavet || 146.84 ||
jātaṃ tu svagṛhe vatsaṃ dvijanmā yastu vāhayet |
patanti pitarastasya brahmakokagatā api  || 146.85 ||
yathāyathā hi pibati pītvā dhūnāti mastakam  |
pibanpitḥn prīṇayati narakāduddhareddhunan  || 146.86 ||
yathā pucchābhighātena skandhaṃ gacchanti bindavaḥ  |
narakāduddharantyāśu patitān gotriṇastathā  || 146.87 ||
garjanprāvṛṣi kāle tu viṣāṇābhyāṃ bhuvaṃ likhan  |
khurebhyo yā mṛdudbhūtā tayā saṃprīṇayedṛṣīn  || 146.88 ||
pibanpitḥn prīṇayate khādanollekhane surān  |
garjannṛṣimanuṣyāṃśca dharmarūpo hi dharmaja  || 146.89 ||
bhūtairvāpi piśācairvā cāturthikajvareṇa vā  |
gṛhīto'smāhakaṃ gacchetsarveṣāmādhināśanam  || 146.90 ||
snātvā tu vimale toye darbhagranthiṃ nibandhayet |
mastake bāhumūle vā nābhyāṃ vā galake'pi vā  || 146.91 ||
gatvā devasamīpaṃ ca prādakṣiṇyena keśavam  |
tataḥ samuccaranmantraṃ gāyatryā vātha vaiṣṇavam  || 146.92 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ śaraṇyeśaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam  |
namo yajñāṅgasambhūta sarvavyāpinnamo'stu te  || 146.93 ||
namo namaste deveśa padmagarbha sanātana  |
dāmodara jayānanta rakṣa māṃ śaraṇāgatam  || 146.94 ||
tvaṃ kartā tvaṃ ca hartā ca jagatyasmiṃścarācare  |
tvaṃ pālayasi bhūtāni bhuvanaṃ tvaṃ bibharṣi ca  || 146.95 ||
prasīda devadeveśa suptamaṅgaṃ prabodhaya  |
tvaddhyānanirato nityaṃ tvadbhaktiparamo hare  || 146.96 ||
iti stuto mayā deva prasādaṃ kuru me'cyuta  |
māṃ rakṣa rakṣa pāpebhyastrāyasva śaraṇāgatam  || 146.97 ||
evaṃ stutvā ca deveśaṃ dānavāntakaraṃ harim  |
punaruktena vai snātvā tato viprāṃstu bhojayet || 146.98 ||
vedoktena vidhānena snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā vācayetsvastikaṃ tataḥ  || 146.99 ||
evaṃ stutvā ca deveśaṃ dānavāntakaraṃ harim  |
punaruktena vai snātvā tato viprāṃstu bhojayet || 146.100 ||
vedoktena vidhānena snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
evaṃ tānvācayitvā tu tato viprānvisarjayet || 146.101 ||
yattatroccaritaṃ kiṃcittadviprebhyo nivedayet |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā nārī vā bhaktitatparā  |
śaktito dakṣiṇāṃ dadyātkṛtvā śrāddhaṃ yathāvidhi  || 146.102 ||
tatra tīrthe naro yāvatsnāpayedvidhipūrvakam  |
kṣīreṇa madhunā vāpi dadhnā vā śītavāriṇā  || 146.103 ||
tāvatpuṣkarapātreṣu pibanti pitaro jalam  |
ayane viṣuve caiva yugādau sūryasaṃkrame  || 146.104 ||
puṣpaiḥ sampūjya deveśaṃ naivedyaṃ yaḥ pradāpayet |
so'śvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti puṣkalam  || 146.105 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājan sūryagrahaṇamācaret |
sūryatejonibhairyānairviṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 146.106 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ samprayacchati  |
satputreṇa ca tenaiva samprāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam  || 146.107 ||
iti śrutvā tato devāḥ sarve śakrapurogamāḥ  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśāśca sthāpayāṃcakrurīśvaram  || 146.108 ||
sarvarogopaśamanaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  |
yastu saṃvatsaraṃ pūrṇamamāvāsyāṃ tu bhāvitaḥ  || 146.109 ||
pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ ca kuryādasmāhake nṛpa  |
tripuṣkare gayāyāṃ ca prabhāse naimiṣe tathā  || 146.110 ||
yatpuṇyaṃ śrāddhakartḥṇāṃ tadihaiva bhaveddhruvam  |
tilodakaṃ kuśairmiśraṃ yo dadyāddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 146.111 ||
manvādau ca yugādau ca vyatīpāte dinakṣaye  |
yo dadyātpitṛmātṛbhyaḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 146.112 ||
asmāhake naro yastu snātvā sampūjayeddharim  |
brahmāṇaṃ śaṅkaraṃ bhaktyā kuryājjāgaraṇakriyām  || 146.113 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śakrātithyamavāpnuyāt |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā yaḥ paśyati janārdanam  || 146.114 ||
viśeṣavidhinābhyarcya praṇamya ca punaḥpunaḥ  |
saputreṇa ca tenaiva pitḥṇāṃ vihitā gatiḥ  || 146.115 ||
ekamūrtistrayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
satkāryakāraṇopetāḥ susūkṣmāḥ sumahāphalāḥ  || 146.116 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājanmahāpātakanāśanam  |
asmāhakasya māhātmyaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi  || 146.117 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe asmāhakatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 147

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla siddheśvaramanuttamam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  || 147.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayedvṛṣabhadhvajam  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto gatiṃ yātyaśvamedhinām  || 147.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā śrāddhaṃ kuryātprayatnataḥ  |
pitḥṇāṃ prīṇanārthāya sarvaṃ tena kṛtaṃ bhavet || 147.3 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu jantūnāṃ nṛpasattama  |
garbhavāse matisteṣāṃ na jāyeta kadācana  || 147.4 ||
garbhavāso hi duḥkhāya na sukhāya kadācana  |
tattīrthavāriṇā snāturna punarbhavasambhavaḥ  || 147.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe siddheśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 148

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthamaṅgārakaṃ śivam  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 148.1 ||
caturthyaṅgārakadine saṃkalpya kṛtaniścayaḥ  |
snāyādastaṃ gate sūrye sandhyopāsanatatparaḥ  || 148.2 ||
pūjayellohitaṃ bhaktyā gandhamālyavibhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
saṃsthāpyasthaṇḍile devaṃ raktacandanacarcitam  || 148.3 ||
aṅgārakāyeti namaḥ karṇikāyāṃ prapūjayet |
kujāya bhūmiputrāya raktāṅgāya suvāsase  || 148.4 ||
harakopodbhavāyeti svedajāyātibāhave  |
sarvakāmapradāyeti pūrvādiṣu daleṣu ca  || 148.5 ||
evaṃ sampūjya vidhivaddadyādarghyaṃ vidhānataḥ  |
bhūmiputra mahāvīrya svedodbhava pinākinaḥ  || 148.6 ||
aṅgāraka mahātejā lohitāṅga namo'stu te  |
karakaṃ vārisaṃyuktaṃ śālitaṃdulapūritam  || 148.7 ||
sahiraṇyaṃ savastraṃ ca modakopari saṃsthitam  |
brāhmaṇāya nivedyaṃ tatkujo me prīyatāmiti  || 148.8 ||
arghaṃ dattvā vidhānena raktacandanavāriṇā  |
raktapuṣpasamākīrṇaṃ tilataṃdulamiśritam  || 148.9 ||
kṛtvā tāmramaye pātre maṇḍale vartule śubhe  |
kṛtvā śirasi tatpātraṃ jānubhyāṃ dharaṇīṃ gataḥ  || 148.10 ||
mantrapūtaṃ mahābhāga dadyādarghyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
tato bhuñjīta maunena kṣāratilāmlavarjitam  || 148.11 ||
snigdhaṃ mṛdusamadhuramātmanaḥ śreya icchatā  |
evaṃ caturthe samprāpte caturthyaṅgārake nṛpa  || 148.12 ||
sauvarṇaṃ kārayeddevaṃ yathāśakti surūpiṇam  |
sthāpayettāmrake pātre guḍapīṭhasamanvite  || 148.13 ||
gandhapuṣpādibhirdevaṃ pūjayedguḍasaṃsthitam  |
īśānyāṃ sthāpayeddevaṃ guḍatoyasamanvitam  || 148.14 ||
kāsāreṇa tathāgneyyāṃ sthāpayetkarakaṃ param  |
raktatandulasaṃmiśraṃ nairṛtyāṃ vāyugocare  || 148.15 ||
sthāpayenmodakaiḥ sārdhaṃ caturthaṃ karakaṃ budhaḥ  |
sūtreṇa veṣṭitagrīvaṃ gandhamālyairalaṃkṛtam  || 148.16 ||
śaṅkhatūryaninādena jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  |
raktāmbaradharaṃ vipraṃ raktamālyānulepanam  || 148.17 ||
vedimadhyagataṃ vāpi mahadāsanasaṃsthitam  |
surūpaṃ subhagaṃ śāntaṃ sarvabhūtahite ratam  || 148.18 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātaṃ sarvaśāstraviśāradam  |
pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṃ vācayetpāṇḍunandana  || 148.19 ||
raktāṃ gāṃ ca tato dadyādraktenānaḍuhā saha  |
prīyatāṃ bhūmijo devaḥ sarvadaivatapūjitaḥ  || 148.20 ||
vipraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya patnīputrasamanvitaḥ  |
pitṛmātṛsuhṛtsārddhaṃ kṣamāpya ca visarjayet || 148.21 ||
evaṃ kṛtasya tasyātha tasmiṃstīrthe viśeṣataḥ  |
yatpuṇyaṃ phalamuddiṣṭaṃ tatte sarvaṃ vadāmyaham  || 148.22 ||
sapta janmāni rājendra surūpaḥ subhago bhavet |
tīrthasyāsya prabhāvena nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 148.23 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā tatra tīrthe mṛto naraḥ  |
aṅgārakapuraṃ yāti devagandharvapūjitaḥ  || 148.24 ||
upabhujya yathānyāyaṃ divyānbhogānanuttamān  |
iha mānuṣyaloke vai rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 148.25 ||
surūpaḥ subhagaścaiva sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ  || 148.26 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe maṅgaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭācatvāriṃśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 149

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ tīrthaṃ liṅgeśvaramiti śrutam  |
darśanāddevadevasya yatra pāpaṃ praṇaśyati  || 149.1 ||
kṛtvā tu kadanaṃ ghoraṃ dānavānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthāya narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ  || 149.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā devaṃ namasyati  |
sa mucyate nṛpaśreṣṭha mahāpāpaiḥ purākṛtaiḥ  || 149.3 ||
dvādaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasya śukle ca samupoṣitaḥ  |
gandhamālyairjagannāthaṃ pūjayetpāṇḍunandana  || 149.4 ||
brāhmaṇāṃśca mahābhāga dānasaṃmānabhojanaiḥ  |
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 149.5 ||
satrayājiphalaṃ janturlabhate dvādaśābdakaiḥ  |
brāhmaṇānbhojayaṃstatra tadeva labhate phalam  || 149.6 ||
tarpayitvā pitḥn devān snātvā tadgatamānasaḥ  |
japeddvādaśanāmāni devasya purataḥ sthitaḥ  || 149.7 ||
māsi māsi nirāhāro dvādaśyāṃ kurunandana  |
keśavaṃ pūjayennityaṃ māsi mārgaśire budhaḥ  || 149.8 ||
pauṣe nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ māghamāse tu mādhavam  |
govindaṃ phālgune māsi viṣṇuṃ caitre samarcayet || 149.9 ||
vaiśākhe madhuhantāraṃ jyeṣṭhe devaṃ trivikramam  |
vāmanaṃ tu tathāṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe śrīdharaṃ smaret || 149.10 ||
hṛṣīkeśaṃ bhādrapade padmanābhaṃ tathāśvine  |
dāmodaraṃ kārttike tu kīrtayannāvasīdati  || 149.11 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurā kṛtam  |
tannaśyati na sandeho māsanāmānukīrtanāt || 149.12 ||
svayaṃ vinuddhaḥ satatamunmiṣannimiṣaṃstathā  |
śīghraṃ prapaśya bhuñjāno mantrahīnaṃ samudgiret || 149.13 ||
paramāpadgatasyāpi jantoreṣā pratikriyā  |
yanmāsādhipaterviṣṇormāsanāmānukīrtanam  || 149.14 ||
tā niśāste ca divasāste māsāste ca vatsarāḥ  |
narāṇāṃ saphalā yeṣu cintito bhagavānhariḥ  || 149.15 ||
paramāpadgatasyāpi yasya devo janārdanaḥ  |
nāvasarpati hṛtpadmātsa yogī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 149.16 ||
te bhāgyahīnā manujāḥ suśocyāste bhūmibhārāya kṛtāvatārāḥ  |
acetanāste paśubhiḥ samānā ye bhaktihīnā bhagavatyanante  || 149.17 ||
te pūrṇakāryāḥ puruṣāḥ pṛthivyāṃ te svāṅgapātādbhuvanaṃ punanti  |
vicakṣaṇā viśvavibhūṣaṇāste ye bhaktiyuktā bhagavatyanante  || 149.18 ||
sa eva sukṛtī tena labdhaṃ janmataroḥ phalam  |
citte vacasi kāye ca yasya devo janārdanaḥ  || 149.19 ||
etattīrthavaraṃ puṇyaṃ liṅgo yatra janārdanaḥ  |
vañcayitvā ripūnsaṃkhye krodho bhūtvā sanātanaḥ  || 149.20 ||
upaplave candramaso raveśca yo hyaṣṭakānāmayanadvaye ca  |
pānīyamapyatra tilairvimiśraṃ dadyātpitṛbhyaḥ prayato manuṣyaḥ  || 149.21 ||
ghoṇonmīlitamerurandhranivaho duḥkhābdhimajjatplavaḥ prādurbhūtarasātalodarabṛhatpaṅkārdhamagnakṣuraḥ  |
phūtkārotkaranunnavātavidaladdigdantinādaśrutinyastastabdhavapuḥ śrutirbhavatu vaḥ kroḍo hariḥ śāntaye  || 149.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe liṅgavārāhatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 150

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja kusumeśvaramuttamam  |
dakṣiṇe narmadākūle upapātakanāśanam  || 150.1 ||
kāmena sthāpito devaḥ kusumeśvarasaṃjñitaḥ  |
khyātaḥ sarveṣu lokeṣu devadevaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 150.2 ||
kāmo manobhavo viśvaḥ kusumāyudhacāpabhṛt |
sa kāmān dadāti sarvān pūjito mīnaketanaḥ  || 150.3 ||
tena nirdagdhakāyena cārādhya parameśvaram  |
anaṅgena tathā prāptamaṅgitvaṃ narmadātaṭe  || 150.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
aṅgibhṛtasya nāśatvamanaṅgasya tu me vada  |
na śrutaṃ na ca me dṛṣṭaṃ bhūtapūrvaṃ kadācana  || 150.5 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathā vṛttamācakṣva dvijasattama  |
śrotumicchāmi viprendra bhīmārjunayamaiḥ saha  || 150.6 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ādau kṛtayuge tāta devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṃsthitaḥ  || 150.7 ||
tena sampāditā lokāstapasā sasurāsurāḥ  |
jagmuste śaraṇaṃ sarve devadevaṃ śacīpatim  || 150.8 ||
vyāpakaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
saṃtāpayati lokāṃstrīṃstannivāraya gopate  || 150.9 ||
śrutvā tadvacanaṃ teṣāṃ devānāṃ balavṛtrahā  |
cintayāmāsa manasā tapovighnāyacādiśat || 150.10 ||
apsarāṃ menakāṃ rambhāṃ ghṛtācīṃ ca tilottamām  |
vasantaṃ kokilaṃ kāmaṃ dakṣiṇānilamuttamam  || 150.11 ||
gatvā tatra mahādevaṃ tapaścaraṇatatparam  |
kṣobhayadhvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ gaṅgāsāgaravāsinam  || 150.12 ||
evamuktāstu te sarve devarājena bhārata  |
devāpsaraḥsamopetā jagmuste harasannidhau  || 150.13 ||
vasantamāse kusumākarākule mayūradātyūhasukokilākule  |
pranṛtya devāpsaragītasaṃkule pravāti vāte yamanairṛtākule  || 150.14 ||
tena saṃmūrchitāḥ sarve saṃsargācca khagottamāḥ  |
madhumādhavagandhena sakinnaramahoragāḥ  || 150.15 ||
yāvadālokate tāvattadvanaṃ vyākulīkṛtam  |
vīkṣate madanāviṣṭaṃ daśāvasthāgataṃ janam  || 150.16 ||
devadevo'pi devānāmavasthātritayaṃ gataḥ  |
sāttvikīṃ rājasīṃ rājaṃstāmasīṃ tāṃ śṛṇuṣva me  || 150.17 ||
ekaṃ yogasamādhinā mukulitaṃ cakṣurdvitīyaṃ punaḥ pārvatyā jaghanasthalastanataṭe śṛṅgārabhārālasam  |
anyaddūranirastacāpamadanakrodhānaloddīpitaṃ śambhorbhinnarasaṃ samādhisamaye netratrayaṃ pātu vaḥ  || 150.18 ||
evaṃ dṛṣṭaḥ sa devena saśaraḥ saśarāsanaḥ  |
bhasmībhūto gataḥ kāmo vināśaḥ sarvadehinām  || 150.19 ||
kāmaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣayaṃ yātaṃ tatra devāpsarogaṇāḥ  |
bhītā yathāgataṃ sarve jagmuścaiva diśo daśa  || 150.20 ||
kāmena rahitā lokāḥ sasurāsuramānavāḥ  |
brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ jagmurdevā indrapurogamāḥ  || 150.21 ||
sīdamānaṃ jagaddṛṣṭvā tamūcuḥ parameṣṭhinam  |
jānāsi tvaṃ jagaccheṣaṃ prabho maithunasambhavāt || 150.22 ||
prajāḥ sarvā viśuṣyanti kāmena rahitā vibho  || 150.23 ||
etacchrutvā vacasteṣāṃ devānāṃ prapitāmahaḥ  |
jagāma sahitastatra yatra devo maheśvaraḥ  || 150.24 ||
atoṣayajjagannāthaṃ sarvabhūtamaheśvaram  |
stutibhistaṇḍakaiḥ stotrairvedavedāṅgasambhavaiḥ  || 150.25 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo devānāṃ parameśvaraḥ  |
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ devānbrahmapurogamān  || 150.26 ||
kiṃ kāryaṃ kaśca santāpaḥ kiṃ vāgamanakāraṇam  |
devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca kathyatāṃ mama māciram  || 150.27 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
kāmanāśājjagannāśo bhavitāyaṃ carācare  |
trailokyaṃ tvaṃ punaḥ śambho utpādayitumarhasi  || 150.28 ||
etacchrutvā vacasteṣāṃ vimṛśya parameśvaraḥ  |
cintayāmāsa kāmasya vigrahaṃ bhuvi durlabham  || 150.29 ||
ājagāma tataḥ śīghramanaṅgo hyaṅgatāṃ gataḥ  |
prāṇadaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ paśyatāṃ nṛpasattama  || 150.30 ||
tataḥ śaṅkhaninādena bherīṇāṃ niḥsvanena ca  |
abhyanandaṃstato devaṃ surāsuramahoragāḥ  || 150.31 ||
namaste devadeveśa kṛtārthāḥ surasattamāḥ  |
visarjitāḥ punarjagmuryathāgatamarindama  || 150.32 ||
gateṣu sarvadeveṣu kāmadevo'pi bhārata  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 150.33 ||
tapojapakṛśībhūto divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ kila  |
mahābhūtairvighnakaraiḥ pīḍyamānaḥ samantataḥ  || 150.34 ||
ātmavighnavināśārthaṃ saṃsmṛtaḥ kuṇḍaleśvaraḥ  |
cakāra rakṣāṃ sarvatra śarapāte nṛpottama  || 150.35 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo dṛḍhabhaktyā varapradaḥ  |
vareṇa chandayāmāsa kāmaṃ kāmavināśanaḥ  || 150.36 ||
jñātvā tuṣṭaṃ mahādevamuvāca jhaṣaketanaḥ  |
praṇataḥ prāñjalirbhūtvā devadevaṃ trilocanam  || 150.37 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
atra tīrthe jagannātha sadā saṃnihito bhava  || 150.38 ||
tatheti coktvā vacanaṃ devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
jagāmākāśamāviśya stūyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 150.39 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve kāmadevo jagadgurum  |
sthāpayāmāsa rājendra kusumeśvarasaṃjñitam  || 150.40 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā hyupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
caitramāse caturdaśyāṃ madanasya dine'thavā  || 150.41 ||
prabhāte vimale prāpte snātvā pūjya divākaram  |
tilamiśreṇa toyena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 150.42 ||
kṛtvā snānaṃ vidhānena pūjayitvā ca taṃ nṛpa  |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuryāttasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 150.43 ||
sattrayājiphalaṃ yacca labhate dvādaśābdikam  |
piṇḍadānātphalaṃ tacca labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 150.44 ||
aṅkullamūle yaḥ piṇḍaṃ pitḥnuddiśya dāpayet |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni tṛptiṃ yānti pitāmahāḥ  || 150.45 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgā ye tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
prāpnuvanti mṛtāḥ svargaṃ kiṃ punarye narā mṛtāḥ  || 150.46 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yo'tra jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  |
kusumeśe naro bhaktyā sa gacchecchivamandiram  || 150.47 ||
tatra divyāpsarobhiśca devagandharvagāyanaiḥ  |
krīḍate sevyamānastu kalpakoṭiśataṃ nṛpa  || 150.48 ||
pūrṇe caiva tataḥ kāla iha mānuṣyatāṃ gataḥ  |
jāyate rājarājendraiḥ pūjyamāno nṛpo mahān  || 150.49 ||
surūpaḥ subhago vāgmī vikrānto matimāñchuciḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 150.50 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ tīrthakoṭiśatādhikam  |
kusumeśeti vikhyātaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam  || 150.51 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kusumeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 151

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
uttare narmadākūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
jayavārāhamāhātmyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 151.1 ||
uddhṛtā jagatī yena sarvadevanamaskṛtā  |
lokānugrahabuddhyā ca saṃsthito narmadātaṭe  || 151.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vīkṣate madhusūdanam  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo daśajanmānukīrtanāt || 151.3 ||
matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca narasiṃho'tha vāmanaḥ  |
rāmo rāmaśca kṛṣṇaśca buddhaḥ kalkiśca te daśa  || 151.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
matsyena kiṃ kṛtaṃ tāta kūrmeṇa munisattama  |
varāheṇa ca kiṃ karma narasiṃhena kiṃ kṛtam  || 151.5 ||
vāmanena ca rāmeṇa rāghaveṇa ca kiṃ kṛtam  |
buddharūpeṇa kiṃ vāpi kalkinā kiṃ kṛtaṃ vada  || 151.6 ||
evamuktastu viprendro dharmaputreṇa dhīmatā  |
uvāca madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ tadā dharmasutaṃ prati  || 151.7 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
mīno bhūtvā purā kalpe prītyarthaṃ brahmaṇo vibhuḥ  |
samarpayatsamuddhṛtya vandānmagnānmahārṇave  || 151.8 ||
amṛtotpādane rājankūrmo bhūtvā jagadguruḥ  |
mandaraṃ dhārayāmāsa tathā devīṃ vasuṃdharām  || 151.9 ||
ujjahāra dharāṃ magnāṃ pātālatalavāsinīm  |
vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthāya devadevo janārdanaḥ  || 151.10 ||
narasyārddhatanuṃ kṛtvā siṃhasyārddhatanuṃ tathā  |
hiraṇyakaśiporvakṣo vidadāra nakhāṅkuśaiḥ  || 151.11 ||
jaṭī vāmanarūpeṇa stūyamāno dvijottamaiḥ  |
taddivyaṃ rūpamāsthāya kramitvā medinīṃ kramaiḥ  || 151.12 ||
kṛtavāṃśca baliṃ paścātpātālatalavāsinam  |
sthāpayitvā surān sarvān gato viṣṇuḥ svakaṃ puram  || 151.13 ||
jamadagnisuto rāmo bhūtvā śastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  |
kṣatriyān pṛthivīpālānavadhīddhaihayādikān  || 151.14 ||
kaśyapāya mahīṃ dattvā saparvatavanākarām  |
tapastapati deveśo mahendre'dyāpi bhārata  || 151.15 ||
tato dāśarathī rāmo rāvaṇaṃ devakaṇṭakam  |
sagaṇaṃ samare hatvā rājyaṃ dattvā vibhīṣaṇe  || 151.16 ||
pālayitvā nayādbhūmiṃ makhaiḥ saṃtarpya devatāḥ  |
svargaṃ gato mahātejā rāmo rājīvalocanaḥ  || 151.17 ||
vasudevagṛhe bhūyaḥ saṃkarṣaṇasahāyavān  |
avatīrṇo jagannātho vāsudevo yudhiṣṭhira  || 151.18 ||
so'vadhīttava sāmarthyādvadhārthaṃ duṣṭabhūbhṛtām  |
cāṇūrakaṃsakeśīnāṃ jarāsaṃdhasya bhārata  || 151.19 ||
tena tvaṃ susahāyena hatvā śatrūnnareśvara  |
bhokṣyase pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha saṃbhṛtām  || 151.20 ||
tathā buddhatvamaparaṃ navamaṃ prāpsyate'cyutaḥ  |
śāntimāndevadeveśo madhuhantā madhupriyaḥ  || 151.21 ||
tena buddhasvarūpeṇa devena parameṣṭhinā  |
bhaviṣyati jagatsarvaṃ mohitaṃ sacarācaram  || 151.22 ||
na śroṣyanti pituḥ putrāstadāprabhṛti bhārata  |
na gurorbāndhavāḥ śiṣyā bhaviṣyatyadharottaram  || 151.23 ||
jito dharmo hyadharmeṇa cāsatyena ṛtaṃ jitam  |
jitāścauraiśca rājānaḥ strībhiśca puruṣā jitāḥ  || 151.24 ||
sīdanti cāgnihotrāṇi gurau pūjā praṇaśyati  |
sīdanti mānavā dharmāḥ kalau prāpte yudhiṣṭhira  || 151.25 ||
dvādaśe daśame varṣe nārī garbhavatī bhavet |
kanyāstatra prasūyante brāhmaṇo haripiṅgalaḥ  || 151.26 ||
bhaviṣyati tataḥ kalkirdaśame janmani prabhuḥ  || 151.27 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājandevasya parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
kāraṇaṃ daśa janmanāṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 151.28 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śvetavārāhatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 152

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharāpāla bhārgaleśvaramuttamam  |
śaṅkaraṃ jagataḥ prāṇaṃ smṛtamātrāghanāśanam  || 152.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 152.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitprāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyati  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 152.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhārgaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvipañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 153

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ cānyadravitīrthamanuttamam  |
yasya saṃdarśanādeva mucyante pātakairnarāḥ  || 153.1 ||
ravitīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā naraḥ paśyati bhāskaram  |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ svayaṃ devena tacchṛṇu  || 153.2 ||
nāndho na mūko badhiraḥ kule bhavati kaścana  |
kurūpaḥ kunakhī vāpi tasya janmāni ṣoḍaśa  || 153.3 ||
dadrucitrakakuṣṭhāni maṇḍalāni vicarcikā  |
naśyanti devabhaktasya ṣaṇmāsānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 153.4 ||
caritaṃ tasya devasya purāṇe yacchrutaṃ mayā  |
na tatkathayituṃ śakyaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa nṛpottama  || 153.5 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaddānaṃ ravimuddiśya dīyate  |
vidhinā pātraviprāya tasyānto nāsti karhicit || 153.6 ||
ayane viṣuve caiva candrasūryagrahe tathā  |
ravitīrthe pradattānāṃ dānānāṃ phalamuttamam  || 153.7 ||
saṃkrāntau yāni dānāni havyakavyāni bhārata  |
apāmiva samudrasya teṣāmanto na labhyate  || 153.8 ||
yena yena yadā dattaṃ yena yena yadā hutam  |
tasya tasya tadā kāle savitā pratidāyakaḥ  || 153.9 ||
sapta janmāni tānyeva dadātyarkaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
śatamindukṣaye dānaṃ sahasraṃ tu dinakṣaye  || 153.10 ||
saṃkrāntau śatasāhasraṃ vyatīpāte tvanantakam  || 153.11 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ravitīrthaṃ kathaṃ tāta puṇyātpuṇyataraṃ smṛtam  |
vistareṇa mamākhyāhi śravaṇau mama lampaṭau  || 153.12 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā hyādityeśvaramuttamam  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvavyādhivināśanam  || 153.13 ||
purā kṛtayugasyādau jābālirbrāhmaṇo'bhavat |
vasiṣṭhānvayasambhūto vedaśāstrārthapāragaḥ  || 153.14 ||
pativratā sādhuśīlā tasya bhāryā manasvinī  |
ṛtukāle tu sā gatvā bhartāramidamabravīt || 153.15 ||
vartate ṛtukālo me bhartāraṃ tvāmupasthitā  |
bhaja māṃ prītisaṃyuktaḥ putrakāmāṃ tu kāminīm  || 153.16 ||
evamukto dvijaḥ prāha priye'dyāhaṃ vratānvitaḥ  |
gacchedānīṃ varārohe dāsya ṛtvantare punaḥ  || 153.17 ||
punardvitīye samprāpte ṛtukāle'pyupasthitā  |
punaḥ sā chanditā tena vratastho'dyeti bhārata  || 153.18 ||
itthaṃ vā bahuśastena chanditā ca punaḥ punaḥ  |
nirāśā cābhavattatra bhartāraṃ prati bhāminī  || 153.19 ||
duḥkhena mahatāviṣṭā vidhāyānaśanaṃ mṛtā  |
tena bhrūṇahatenaiva pāpena sahasā dvijaḥ  || 153.20 ||
śīrṇaghrāṇāṅghrirabhavattapaḥ sarvaṃ nanāśa ca  |
dṛṣṭvātmānaṃ sa kuṣṭhena vyāptaṃ brāhmaṇasattamaḥ  || 153.21 ||
viṣādaṃ paramaṃ gatvā narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
apṛcchadbhāskaraṃ tīrthaṃ dvijebhyo dvijasattamaḥ  || 153.22 ||
ārogyaṃ bhāskarādicchediti saṃcintya cetasi  |
kutastadbhāskaraṃ tīrthaṃ bho dvijāḥ kathyatāṃ mama  || 153.23 ||
tapastapyāmyahaṃ gatvā tasmiṃstīrthe subhāvitaḥ  || 153.24 ||
dvijā ūcuḥ  |
revāyā uttare kūle ādityeśvaranāmataḥ  |
vidyate bhāskaraṃ tīrthaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam  || 153.25 ||
tatra yāhyavicāreṇa gantuṃ cecchakyate tvayā  |
evamukto dvijairvipro gantuṃ tatra pracakrame  || 153.26 ||
vyādhinā paribhūtastu ghoreṇa prāṇahāriṇā  |
yadā gantuṃ na śaknoti tadā tena vicintitam  || 153.27 ||
sāmarthyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hi vidyate bhuvanatraye  |
liṅgapātaḥ kṛto viprairdevadevasya śūlinaḥ  || 153.28 ||
samudraḥ śoṣito viprairvindhyaścāpi nivāritaḥ  |
ahamapyatra saṃsthastu hyānayiṣyāmi bhāskaram  || 153.29 ||
tapobalena mahatā hyādityeśvarasaṃjñitam  |
iti niścitya manasā hyugre tapasi saṃsthitaḥ  || 153.30 ||
vāyubhakṣo nirāhāro grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyagaḥ  |
śiśire toyamadhyastho varṣāsvaprāvṛtākṛtiḥ  || 153.31 ||
sāgre varṣaśate pūrṇe ravistuṣṭo'bravīdidam  || 153.32 ||

sūrya uvāca -
varaṃ varaya bhadraṃ te kiṃ te manasi vāñchitam  |
adeyamapi dāsyāmi brūhi māṃ tvaṃ ciraṃ kṛthāḥ  || 153.33 ||
kimasādhyaṃ hi te vipra idānīṃ tapasi sthitaḥ  || 153.34 ||

jābāliruvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
mama pratijñā deveśa hyādityeśvaradarśane  || 153.35 ||
kṛtā tāṃ pārituṃ deva na śakto vyādhinā vṛtaḥ  |
śuklatīrthe'tra tiṣṭha tvamādityeśvaramūrtidhṛk || 153.36 ||
evamukte tu deveśo bahurūpo divākaraḥ  |
uttare narmadākūle kṣaṇādeva vyadṛśyata  || 153.37 ||
tadāprabhṛti bhūpāla taddhi tīrthaṃ pracakṣate  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ proktaṃ sarvaduḥkhavināśanam  || 153.38 ||
yastu saṃvatsaraṃ pūrṇaṃ nityamādityavāsare  |
snātvā pradakṣiṇāḥ sapta dattvā paśyati bhāskaram  || 153.39 ||
yatphalaṃ labhate tena tacchṛṇuṣva mayoditam  |
prasuptaṃ maṇḍalānīha dadrukuṣṭhavicarcikāḥ  || 153.40 ||
naśyanti satvaraṃ rājaṃstūlarāśirivānale  |
dhanaputrakalatrāṇāṃ pūrayedvatsaratrayāt || 153.41 ||
yastu śrāddhapradastatra pitḥnuddiśya saṃkrame  |
tṛpyanti pitarastasya pitṛdevo hi bhāskaraḥ  || 153.42 ||
iti te kathitaṃ sarvamādityeśvaramuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ sarvarogavināśanam  || 153.43 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ādityeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tripañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 154

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ kalakaleśvaram  |
vikhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu svayaṃ devena nirmitam  || 154.1 ||
andhakaṃ samare hatvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
sahito devagandharvaiḥ kinnaraiśca mahoragaiḥ  || 154.2 ||
śaṅkhatūryaninādaiśca mṛdaṅgapaṇavādibhiḥ  |
vīṇāveṇuravaiścānyaiḥ stutibhiḥ puṣkalādibhiḥ  || 154.3 ||
gāyanti sāmāni yajūṃṣi cānye chandāṃsi cānye ṛcamudgiranti  |
stotrairanekairapare gṛṇanti maheśvaraṃ tatra mahānubhāvāḥ  || 154.4 ||
pramathānāṃ ninādena kalkalena ca bandinām  |
yasmātpratiṣṭhitaṃ liṅgaṃ tasmājjātaṃ tadākhyayā  || 154.5 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vīkṣetkalakaleśvaram  |
vājapeyātparaṃ puṇyaṃ sa labhenmānavo bhuvi  || 154.6 ||
tena puṇyena pūtātmā prāṇatyāgāddivaṃ vrajet |
ārūḍhaḥ paramaṃ yānaṃ gīyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 154.7 ||
upabhujya mahābhogānkālena mahatā tataḥ  |
martyaloke mahātmāsau jāyate vimale kule  || 154.8 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ subhago loke vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
vyādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 154.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kalakaleśvaratīrthaphalamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥpañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 155

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvatīrthādanuttamam  |
uttare narmadākūle śuklatīrthaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 155.1 ||
tasya tīrthasya cānyāni puṇyatvācchubhadarśanāt |
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrthāni kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 155.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ  |
bhrātṛbhiḥ sahitaḥ sarvaistathānyairdvijasattamaiḥ  || 155.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śuklatīrthasya cotpattimākarṇaya nareśvara  |
yasya saṃdarśanādeva brahmahatyā pralīyate  || 155.4 ||
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī  |
yacca bālyaṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ darśanādeva naśyati  || 155.5 ||
mokṣadāni na sarvatra śuklatīrthamṛte nṛpa  |
śuklatīrthasya māhātmyaṃ purāṇe yacchrutaṃ mayā  || 155.6 ||
samāgame munīnāṃ tu devānāṃ hi tathaiva ca  |
kathitaṃ devadevena śitikaṇṭhena bhārata  |
kailāse parvataśreṣṭhe tatte saṃkathayāmyaham  || 155.7 ||
purā kṛtayugasyādau toṣituṃ girijāpatim  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ viṣṇurvarṣasahasrakam  |
vāyubhakṣo nirāhāraḥ śuklatīrthe vyavasthitaḥ  || 155.8 ||
tataḥ pratyakṣatāmāgāddevadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
prādurbhūtastu sahasā tatra tīrthe narādhipa  || 155.9 ||
krośadvayamidaṃ cakre bhuktimuktipradāyakam  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ  || 155.10 ||
gaṅgā kanakhale puṇyā kurukṣetre sarasvatī  |
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā  || 155.11 ||
sarvauṣadhīnāmaśanaṃ pradhānaṃ sarveṣu peyeṣu jalaṃ pradhānam  |
nidrā sukhānāṃ pramadā ratīnāṃ sarveṣu gātreṣu śiraḥ pradhānam  || 155.12 ||
snātasyāpi yathā puṇyaṃ lalāṭaṃ nṛpasattama  |
śuklatīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 155.13 ||
saritāṃ ca yathā gaṅgā devatānāṃ janārdanaḥ  |
śuklatīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 155.14 ||
catuṣpadānāṃ surabhirvarṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo yathā  |
pradhānaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ śuklatīrthaṃ tathā nṛpa  || 155.15 ||
grahāṇāṃ tu yathādityo nakṣatrāṇāṃ yathā śaśī  |
śiro vā sarvagātrāṇāṃ dharmāṇāṃ satyamiṣyate  || 155.16 ||
tathaiva pārtha tīrthānāṃ śuklatīrthamanuttamam  |
durvijñeyo yathā loke paramātmā sanātanaḥ  || 155.17 ||
susūkṣmatvādanirdeśyaḥ śuklatīrthaṃ tathā nṛpa  |
mandaprajñatvamāpanne mahāmohasamanvitaḥ  || 155.18 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ nā jānāti narmadātaṭasaṃsthitam  |
bahunātra kimuktena dharmaputra punaḥ punaḥ  || 155.19 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ samprāptaṃ kalmaṣakṣayāt |
yo'tra datte śucirbhūtvā ekaṃ revājalāñjalim  || 155.20 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi pitarastena tarpitāḥ  || 155.21 ||
ekaḥ putro dharāpṛṣṭhe pitḥṇāmārtināśanaḥ  |
cāṇakyo nāma rājābhūcchuklatīrthaṃ ca veda saḥ  || 155.22 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ko'sau dvijavaraśreṣṭha cāṇakyo nāma nāmataḥ  |
śuklatīrthasya yo vettā nānyo vettā hi kaścana  || 155.23 ||
kenopāyena tattīrthaṃ tena jñātaṃ dharātale  |
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 155.24 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ikṣvākuprabhavo rājā naptā śuddhodanasya ca  |
cāṇakyo nāma rājarṣirbubhuje pṛthivīmimām  || 155.25 ||
vikrānto matimāñchūraḥ sarvalokairavañcitaḥ  |
vañcitaḥ sahasā dhūrtavāyasābhyāṃ nṛpottamaḥ  || 155.26 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ sa vañcito rājā vāyasābhyāṃ kuto'thavā  |
purā yena pratijñātaṃ dhīgarbheṇa mahātmanā  || 155.27 ||
na jīve vañcito'nyena prāṇāṃstyakṣye na saṃśayaḥ  |
etanme vada viprendra paraṃ kautūhalaṃ mama  || 155.28 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ātmānaṃ vañcitaṃ jñātvā tadā saṃgṛhya vāyasau  |
preṣayāmāsa tīvreṇa daṇḍena yamasādanam  || 155.29 ||
vāyasāvūcatuḥ  |
sundopasundayoḥ putrāvāvāṃ kākatvamāgatau  |
mā vadhīstvaṃ mahābhāga kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  || 155.30 ||
tāvāvāṃ kṛtasaṃkalpau tvayā kopena mānada  |
nirastāvanirastau vā yāsyāvaḥ paramāṃ gatim  || 155.31 ||
tadādeśaya rājendra kṛtvā tava mahatpriyam  |
muktaśāpau bhaviṣyāvo brahmaṇo vacanaṃ tathā  || 155.32 ||
tacchrutvā kākavacanaṃ cāṇakyo nṛpasattamaḥ  |
nāhaṃ jīve viditvaivaṃ vañcitaḥ kena karhicit || 155.33 ||
tasmāttīrthaṃ vijānītaṃ yamasya sadane dvijau  |
preṣayāmi yathānyāyaṃ śrutvā tatkathayiṣyathaḥ  || 155.34 ||
tenaiva muktau tau kākau srakcandanavibhūṣitau  |
śīghragau preṣayāmāsa yamasya sadanaṃ prati  || 155.35 ||

rājovāca -
tatra dharmapuraṃ gatvā vicarantāvitastataḥ  |
yadi pṛcchati dharmātmā yamaḥ saṃyamano mahān  || 155.36 ||
kuto vāmāgataṃ brūtaṃ kena vā bhūṣitāvubhau  |
madīyā bhāratī tasya kathanīyā hyaśaṅkitam  || 155.37 ||
ikṣvākusaṃbhavo rājā cāṇakyo nāma dhārmikaḥ  |
dvādaśāhe mṛtasyāsya tarpitāvaśanādinā  || 155.38 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ rājño gatau tau yamasādanam  |
krīḍitau prāṅgaṇe tasya srakcandanavibhūṣitau  |
dharmarājena tau dṛṣṭau pṛṣṭau dhṛṣṭau ca vāyasau  || 155.39 ||

yama uvāca -
kutaḥ sthānātsamāyātau kena vā bhūṣitāvubhau  |
vṛttaṃ vai kathyatāmetadvāyasāvaviśaṅkayā  || 155.40 ||
kākāvūcatuḥ  |
ikṣvākusambhavo rājā cāṇakyo nāma dhārmikaḥ  |
dvādaśāhe mṛtasyāsya tarpitāvaśanādibhiḥ  || 155.41 ||
tayostadvacanaṃ śrutvā sadā vaivasvato yamaḥ  |
citraguptaṃ kaliṃ kālaṃ vīkṣyatāmidamabravīt || 155.42 ||
aṇḍajasvedajātīnāṃ bhūtānāṃ sacarācare  |
vihitaṃ lokakartḥṇāṃ sānnidhyaṃ brahmaṇā mama  || 155.43 ||
gataḥ kutra durācāraścāṇakyo nāmatastviha  |
anviṣyatāṃ purāṇeṣu tvitihāseṣu yā gatiḥ  || 155.44 ||
tatastairdharmapālaistu dharmarājapracoditaiḥ  |
nirīkṣitā purāṇoktā karmajā gatirāgatiḥ  || 155.45 ||
tataḥ provāca vacanaṃ dharmo dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  |
śṛṇvatāṃ dharmapālānāṃ meghagambhīrayā girā  || 155.46 ||
śuklatīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narmadāvimale jale  |
aṇḍajasvedajātīnāṃ na gatirmama sannidhau  || 155.47 ||
tattīrthaṃ dhārmikaṃ loke brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraiḥ  |
nirmitaṃ parayā bhaktyā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 155.48 ||
pāpopapātakairyuktā ye narā narmadājale  |
śuklatīrthe mṛtāḥ śuddhā na te madviṣayāḥ kvacit || 155.49 ||
etacchrutvā tu vacanaṃ tau kākau yamabhāṣitam  |
āgatau śīghragau pārtha dṛṣṭvā yamapuraṃ mahat || 155.50 ||
pṛṣṭau tau praṇatau rājñā yathāvṛttaṃ yathāśrutam  |
kathayāmāsatuḥ pārtha dānavau kākatāṃ gatau  || 155.51 ||
asmātsthānādgatāvāvāṃ yamasya puramuttamam  |
pṛthivyā dakṣiṇe bhāge hyatītya bahuyonijam  || 155.52 ||
tatpuraṃ kāmagaṃ divyaṃ svarṇaprākāratoraṇam  |
anekagṛhasambādhaṃ maṇikāñcanabhūṣitam  || 155.53 ||
catuṣpathaiścatvaraiśca ghaṇṭāmārgopaśobhitam  |
udyānavanasaṃchannaṃ padminīkhaṇḍamanditam  || 155.54 ||
haṃsasārasasaṃghuṣṭaṃ kokilākulasaṃkulam  |
siṃhavyāghragajākīrṇamṛkṣavānarasevitam  || 155.55 ||
naranārīsamākīrṇaṃ nityotsavavibhūṣitam  |
śaṃkhadundubhirnirghoṣairvīṇāveṇunināditam  || 155.56 ||
yamamārge'pi vihitaṃ svargalokamivāparam  |
gatau tatra punaścānyairyamadūtairyamājñayā  || 155.57 ||
viditau preṣitau tatra yatra devo jagatprabhuḥ  |
prāṇasya bhītyā dṛṣṭo'sau siṃhāsanagataḥ prabhuḥ  || 155.58 ||
mahākāyo mahājaṅgho mahāskandho mahodaraḥ  |
mahāvakṣā mahābāhurmahāvaktrekṣaṇo mahān  || 155.59 ||
mahāmahiṣamārūḍho mahāmukuṭabhūṣitaḥ  |
tatrānyaśca kaliḥ kālaścitragupto mahāmatiḥ  || 155.60 ||
samāgatau tadā dṛṣṭau madhye jvalitapāvakau  |
puṇyapāpāni jantūnāṃ śrutismṛtyarthapāragau  || 155.61 ||
vicārayantau satataṃ tiṣṭhāte tau divāniśam  |
tato hyāvāṃ praṇāmānte yamena yamamūrtinā  || 155.62 ||
pṛṣṭāvāgamane hetuṃ tamabrūva śṛṇuṣva tat |
ujjayinyāṃ mahīpālaścāṇakyo'bhūtpratāpavān  || 155.63 ||
dvādaśāhe mṛtasyāsya bhuktvā prāptau yamālayam  |
tato'smākaṃ vacaḥ śrutvā kampayitvā śiro yamaḥ  || 155.64 ||
uvāca vacanaṃ satyaṃ sabhāmadhye hasanniva  |
asti tatkāraṇaṃ yena cāṇakyaḥ pāpapūruṣaḥ  || 155.65 ||
nāyāto mama loke tu sarvapāpabhayaṃkare  |
śuklatīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narmadāyāṃ paraṃ padam  || 155.66 ||
jāyate sarvajantūnāṃ nātra kācidvicāraṇā  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo vāpi jantustatkṣetramaṇḍale  || 155.67 ||
mṛtaḥ sa vai na sandeho rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
taddharmavacanaṃ śrutvā nirgatya nagarādbahiḥ  || 155.68 ||
paśyantau vividhāṃ ghorāṃ narake lokayātanām  |
triṃśatkoṭyo hi ghorāṇāṃ narakāṇāṃ nṛpottama  || 155.69 ||
dṛṣṭā bhītau parāmārtigatau tatra mahāpathi  |
narako rauravastatra mahāraurava eva ca  || 155.70 ||
peṣaṇaḥ śoṣaṇaścaiva kālasūtro'sthibhañjanaḥ  |
tāmisraścāndhatāmisraḥ kṛmipūtivahastathā  || 155.71 ||
dṛṣṭaścānyo mahājvālastatraiva viṣabhojanaḥ  |
narakau daṃśamaśakau tathā yamalaparvatau  || 155.72 ||
nadī vaitaraṇī dṛṣṭā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī  |
śītalaṃ salilaṃ yatra pibanti hyamṛtopamam  || 155.73 ||
tadeva nīraṃ pāpānāṃ śoṇitaṃ parivartate  |
asipatravanaṃ cānyaddṛṣṭānyā mahatī śilā  || 155.74 ||
agnipuṃjanibhākārā viśālā śālmalī parā  |
ityādayastathaivānye śatasāhasrasaṃjñitāḥ  || 155.75 ||
ghoraghoratarā dṛṣṭāḥ kliśyante yatra mānavāḥ  |
vācikairmānasaiḥ pāpaiḥ karmajaiśca pṛthagvidhaiḥ  || 155.76 ||
ahaṃkārakṛtairdoṣairmāyāvacanapūrvakaiḥ  |
pitā mātā gururbhrātā anāthā vikalendriyāḥ  || 155.77 ||
bhramanti noddhṛtā yeṣāṃ gatisteṣāṃ hi raurave  |
tatra te dvādaśābdāni kṣapitvā raurave'dhamāḥ  || 155.78 ||
iha mānuṣyake loke dīnāndhāśca bhavanti te  |
devabrahmasvahartḥṇāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakarmaṇām  || 155.79 ||
mahārauravamāśritya dhruvaṃ vāso yamālaye  |
tataḥ kālena mahatā pāpāḥ pāpena veṣṭitāḥ  || 155.80 ||
jāyante kaṇṭakairbhinnāḥ kośe vā kośakārakāḥ  |
mṛgapakṣivihaṅgānāṃ ghātakā māṃsabhakṣakāḥ  || 155.81 ||
peṣaṇaṃ narakaṃ yānti śoṣaṇaṃ jīvabandhanāt |
tatratyāṃ yātanāṃ ghorāṃ sahitvā śāstracoditām  || 155.82 ||
iha mānuṣyatāṃ prāpya paṅgvandhabadhirā narāḥ  |
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe ca hyanṛtaṃ vadatāmiha  || 155.83 ||
patanaṃ jāyate puṃsāṃ narake kālasūtrake  |
tatratyā yātanā ghorā vihitā śāstrakartṛbhiḥ  || 155.84 ||
bhuktvā samāgatā hyatra te yāsyantyantyajāṃ gatim  |
bandhayanti ca ye jīvāṃstyaktvātmakulasantatim  || 155.85 ||
patanti nātra sandeho narake te'sthibhañjane  |
tatra varṣaśatasyānta iha mānuṣyatāṃ gatāḥ  || 155.86 ||
kubjā vāmanakāḥ pāpā jāyante duḥkhabhāginaḥ  |
ye tyajanti svakāṃ bhāryāṃ mūḍhāḥ paṇḍitamāninaḥ  || 155.87 ||
te yānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tāmisraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
tatra varṣaśatasyānte iha mānuṣyatāṃ gatāḥ  || 155.88 ||
duścarmāṇo durbhagāśca jāyante mānavā hi te  |
mānakūṭaṃ tulākūṭaṃ kūṭakaṃ tu vadanti ye  || 155.89 ||
narake te'ndhatāmisre prapacyante narādhamāḥ  |
śatasāhasrikaṃ kālamuṣitvā tatra te narāḥ  || 155.90 ||
iha śatrugṛhe tvandhā bhramante dīnamūrtayaḥ  |
pitṛdevadvijebhyo'nnamadattvā ye'tra bhuñjate  || 155.91 ||
narake kṛmibhakṣye te patanti svātmapoṣakāḥ  |
tataḥ prasūtikāle hi kṛmibhuktaśca savraṇaḥ  || 155.92 ||
jāyate'śucigandho'tra parabhāgyopajīvakaḥ  |
svakarmavicyutāḥ pāpā varṇāśramavivarjitāḥ  || 155.93 ||
narake pūyasampūrṇe kliśyante hyayutaṃ samāḥ  |
pūrṇe tatra tataḥ kāle prāpya mānuṣyakaṃ bhavam  || 155.94 ||
udvejanīyā bhūtānāṃ jāyante vyādhibhirvṛtāḥ  |
agnido garadaścaiva lobhamohānvito naraḥ  || 155.95 ||
narake viṣasampūrṇe nimajjati durātmavān  |
tatra varṣaśatātkālādunmajjanamavasthitaḥ  || 155.96 ||
bhuvi mānuṣatāṃ prāpya kṛpaṇo jāyate punaḥ  |
pādukopānahau chatraṃ śayyāṃ prāvaraṇāni ca  || 155.97 ||
adattvā daṃśamaśakairbhakṣyante janyasaptatim  |
piturdravyāpahartārastāḍanakrośane ratāḥ  || 155.98 ||
pīḍanaṃ kriyate teṣāṃ yatra tau yugmaparvatau  |
yā sā vaitaraṇī ghorā nadī raktapravāhinī  || 155.99 ||
pibanti rudhiraṃ tatra ye'bhiyānti rajasvalām  |
asipatravane ghore pīḍyante pāpakāriṇaḥ  || 155.100 ||
parapīḍākarā nityaṃ ye naro'ntyajagāminaḥ  |
gurudāraratānāṃ tu mahāpātakināmapi  || 155.101 ||
śilāvagūhanaṃ teṣāṃ jāyate janmasaptatim  |
jvalantīmāyasīṃ ghorāṃ bahukaṇṭakasaṃvṛtām  || 155.102 ||
śālmalīṃ te'vagūhanti paradāraratā hi ye  |
parasya yoṣitaṃ hṛtvā brahmasvamapahṛtya ca  || 155.103 ||
araṇye nirjale deśe sa bhavetkrūrarākṣasaḥ  |
devasvaṃ brāhmaṇasvaṃ ca lobhenaivāharecca yaḥ  || 155.104 ||
sa pāpātmā pare loke gṛdhrocchiṣṭena jīvati  |
evamādīni pāpāni bhuñjante yamaśāsanāt || 155.105 ||
yeṣāṃ tu darśanādeva śravaṇājjāyate bhayam  |
tathā dānaphalaṃ cānye bhuñjānā yamamandire  || 155.106 ||
dṛṣṭāḥ śrutaṃ kathayatāṃ dūtānāṃ ca yamājñayā  |
rathairanye gajairanye kecidvājibhirāvṛtāḥ  || 155.107 ||
dṛṣṭāstatra mahābhāga tapaḥsaṃcayasaṃsthitāḥ  |
godātā svarṇadātā ca bhūmiratnapradā narāḥ  || 155.108 ||
śayyāśanagṛhādīnāṃ sa lokaḥ kāmado nṛṇām  |
annaṃ pānīyasahitaṃ dadate ye'tra mānavāḥ  || 155.109 ||
tatra tṛptāḥ susaṃtuṣṭāḥ krīḍante yamasādane  |
atra yaddīyate dānamapi vālāgramātrakam  || 155.110 ||
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvaṃ śuklatīrthe nṛpottama  |
etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yaddṛṣṭaṃ yacca vai śrutam  || 155.111 ||
kuruṣva yadabhipretaṃ yadi śaknoṣi mucyatām  |
tayostadvacanaṃ śrutvā cāṇakyo hṛṣṭamānasaḥ  || 155.112 ||
visarjayāmāsa khagāvabhinandya punaḥpunaḥ  |
tābhyāṃ gatābhyāṃ sarvasvaṃ dattvā vipreṣu bhārata  || 155.113 ||
kāmakrodhau parityajya jagāmāmaraparvatam  |
tatra baddhvoḍupaṃ gāḍhaṃ kṛṣṇarajjvāvalambitam  || 155.114 ||
plavamāno'śu dhyāyandevaṃ janārdanam  |
ārogyaṃ bhāskarādiccheddhanaṃ vai jātavedasaḥ  || 155.115 ||
prāpnoti jñānamīśānānmokṣaṃ prāpnoti keśavāt |
nīlaṃ raktaṃ tadabhavanmecakaṃ yaddhi sūtrakam  || 155.116 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ dṛṣṭvā rajjuṃ mahāmatiḥ  |
āplutya vimale toye gato'sau vaiṣṇavaṃ padam  || 155.117 ||
gāyanti yadvedavidaḥ purāṇaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ śāśvatamacyutāhvayam  |
prāptaḥ sa taṃ rājasuto mahātmā nikṣipya dehaṃ śubhaśuklatīrthe  || 155.118 ||
eṣā te kathitā rājansiddhiścāṇakyabhūbhṛtaḥ  |
tathānyattava vakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasaḥ  || 155.119 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe cāṇakyasiddhiprāptivarṇanaṃ nāma pañcapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 156

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nāsti lokeṣu tattīrthaṃ pṛthivyāṃ yannareśvara  |
śuklatīrthena sadṛśamupamānena gīyate  || 156.1 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ mahātīrthaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  |
prāgudakpravaṇe deśe munisaṅghaniṣevitam  || 156.2 ||
vaiśākhe ca tathā māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī  |
kailāsādumayā sārddhaṃ svayamāyāti śaṅkaraḥ  || 156.3 ||
madhyāhnasamaye snātvā paśyatyātmānamātmanā  |
brahmaviṣṇvindrasahitaḥ śuklatīrthe samāhitaḥ  || 156.4 ||
kārttikyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa vaiśākhyāṃ ca narottama  |
brahmaviṣṇumahādevān snātvā paśyati taddine  || 156.5 ||
devarājaḥ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ vāyumārgavyavasthitaḥ  |
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ snātvā paśyati śaṅkaram  || 156.6 ||
gandharvāpsaraso yakṣāḥ siddhavidyādharoragāḥ  |
taddine te'pi deveśaṃ dṛṣṭvā muñcanti kilbiṣam  || 156.7 ||
ardhayojanavistāraṃ tadarddhenaiva cāyatam  |
śuklatīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  || 156.8 ||
yatra sthitaiḥ pradṛśyante vṛkṣāgrāṇi narottamaiḥ  |
tatra sthitā mahāpāpairmucyante pūrvasaṃcitaiḥ  || 156.9 ||
pāpopapātakairyukto naraḥ snātvā pramucyate  |
upārjitā vinaśyeta bhrūṇahatyāpi dustyajā  || 156.10 ||
yasmāttatraiva deveśa umayā saha tiṣṭhati  |
vaiśākhyāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa kailāsādeti śaṅkaraḥ  || 156.11 ||
tena tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  |
kathitaṃ brahmaṇā pūrvaṃ mayā tava tathā nṛpa  || 156.12 ||
rajakena yathā dhautaṃ vastraṃ bhavati nirmalam  |
tathā tatra vapuḥsnānaṃ puruṣasya bhavecchuci  || 156.13 ||
pūrve vayasi pāpāni kṛtvā puṣṭāni mānavaḥ  |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā śuklatīrthe vyapohati  || 156.14 ||
śuklatīrthe mahārāja rākāṃ revājalāñjalim  |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi dattvā syuḥ pitaraḥ śivāḥ  || 156.15 ||
na mātā na pitā bandhuḥ patanaṃ narakārṇave  |
uddharanti yathā puṇyaṃ śuklatīrthe nareśvara  || 156.16 ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa na tāṃ gacchanti sadgatim  |
śuklatīrthe mṛto janturdehatyāgena yāṃ labhet || 156.17 ||
kārttikasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
ghṛtena snāpayeddevamupoṣya prayato naraḥ  || 156.18 ||
snātvā prabhāte revāyāṃ dadyātsaghṛtakambalam  |
sahiraṇyaṃ yathāśakti devamuddiśya śaṅkaraṃ  || 156.19 ||
devasya pūraṇaṃ kuryādghṛtena ghṛtakambalam  |
sa gacchati mahātejāḥ śivalokaṃ mṛto naraḥ  || 156.20 ||
ekaviṃśakulopeto yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
śuklatīrthe naraḥ snātvā hyumāṃ rudraṃ ca yo'rcayet || 156.21 ||
gandhapuṣpādidhūpaiśca so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
māsopavāsaṃ yaḥ kuryāttatra tīrthe nareśvara  || 156.22 ||
mucyate sa mahatpāpaiḥ saptajanmasusaṃcitaiḥ  |
uṣṭrīkṣīramavikṣīraṃ navaśrāddhe ca bhojanam  || 156.23 ||
vṛṣalīgamanaṃ caiva tathābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇam  |
avikraye'nṛte pāpaṃ māhiṣe'yājyayājake  || 156.24 ||
vārddhuṣye paṅktigarade devabrāhmaṇadūṣake  |
evamādīni pāpāni tathānyānyapi bhārata  || 156.25 ||
cāndrāyaṇena naśyanti śuklatīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  |
śuklatīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 156.26 ||
tasya te dvādaśābdāni tṛptiṃ yānti sutarpitāḥ  |
pādukopānahau chatraṃ śayyāmāsanameva ca  || 156.27 ||
suvarṇaṃ dhanadhānyaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ yuktahalaṃ tathā  |
annaṃ pānīyasaṃhitaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe dadanti ye  || 156.28 ||
hṛṣṭāḥ puṣṭā mṛtā yānti śivalokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā śivamuddiśya bhārata  || 156.29 ||
bhikṣāmātraṃ tathānnaṃ ye te'pi svaryānti vai narāḥ  |
yajvināṃ vratināṃ caiva tatra tīrthanivāsinām  || 156.30 ||
api vālāgramātraṃ hi dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  |
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryācchuklatīrthe samāhitaḥ  || 156.31 ||
rāgadveṣavinirmukto hṛdi dhyātvā janārdanam  |
sarvakāmasusampūrṇaḥ sa gacchedvāruṇaṃ puram  || 156.32 ||
na rogo na jarā tatra yatra devo'ṃbhasāṃ patiḥ  |
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  || 156.33 ||
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  |
avaśaḥ svavaśo vāpi jantustatkṣetramaṇḍale  || 156.34 ||
mṛtaḥ sa tu na sandeho rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
śuklatīrthe tu yaḥ kanyāṃ śaktyā dadyādalaṃkṛtām  || 156.35 ||
vidhinā yo nṛpaśreṣṭha kurute vṛṣamokṣaṇam  |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ purāṇe rudrabhāṣitam  || 156.36 ||
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  |
yāvanto romakūpāḥ syuḥ sarvāṅgeṣu pṛthakpṛthak || 156.37 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate  |
śuklatīrthe tu yaddattaṃ grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  || 156.38 ||
vardhate tadguṇaṃ tāvaddināni daśa pañca ca  |
śuklatīrthe śucirbhūtvā yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇam  || 156.39 ||
pṛthvī pradakṣiṇā tena kṛtā yattasya tatphalam  |
śobhanaṃ mithunaṃ yastu rudramuddiśya pūjayet || 156.40 ||
sapta janmāni tasyaiva viyogo na ca vai kvacit |
etatte kathitaṃ rājan saṃkṣepeṇa phalaṃ mahat || 156.41 ||
śuklatīrthasya yatpuṇyaṃ yathā devācchrutaṃ mayā  |
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā purāṇe vihitaṃ phalam  || 156.42 ||
sa labhennātra sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
putrārthī labhate putraṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam  || 156.43 ||
mokṣārthī labhate mokṣaṃ snānadānaphalaṃ mahat || 156.44 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śuklatīrthamahātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 157

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ rājañchuklatīrthasamīpataḥ  |
vāsudevasya tīrthaṃ tu sarvalokeṣu pūjitam  || 157.1 ||
taddhi puṇyaṃ suvikhyātaṃ narmadāyāṃ purātanam  |
yatra huṅkāramātreṇa revā krośaṃ jagāma sā  || 157.2 ||
yadā prabhṛti rājendra huṅkāreṇa gatā sarit |
tadāprabhṛti sa svāmī huṅkāraḥ śabdito budhaiḥ  || 157.3 ||
huṅkāratīrthe yaḥ snātvā paśyatyavyayamacyutam  |
sa mucyate naraḥ pāpaiḥ saptajanma kṛtairapi  || 157.4 ||
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakarmiṇām  |
naivoddhartā jagannāthaṃ vinā nārāyaṇaṃ paraḥ  || 157.5 ||
sā jihvā yā hariṃ stauti taccittaṃ yattadarpitam  |
tāveva kevalau ślāghyau yau tatpūjākarau karau  || 157.6 ||
sarvadā sarvakāryeṣu nāsti teṣāmamaṅgalam  |
yeṣāṃ hṛdistho bhagavānmaṅgalāyatano hariḥ  || 157.7 ||
yadanyaddevatārcāyāḥ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
sāṣṭāṅgapraṇipātena tatphalaṃ labhate hareḥ  || 157.8 ||
reṇuguṇṭhitagātrasya yāvanto'sya rajaḥkaṇāḥ  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate  || 157.9 ||
sammārjanābhyukṣaṇalepanena tadālaye naśyati sarvapāpam  |
nārī narāṇāṃ parayā tu bhaktyā dṛṣṭvā tu revāṃ narasattamasya  || 157.10 ||
yenārcito bhagavānvāsudevo janmārjitaṃ naśyati tasya pāpam  |
sa yāti lokaṃ garuḍadhvajasya vidhūtapāpaḥ surasaṅghapūjyatām  || 157.11 ||
śāṭhyenāpi namaskāraṃ prayuñjaṃścakrapāṇinaḥ  |
saptajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ gacchatyāśu na saṃśayaḥ  || 157.12 ||
pūjāyāṃ prīyate rudro japahomairdivākaraḥ  |
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiḥ praṇipātena tuṣyati  || 157.13 ||
bhavajaladhigatānāṃ dvandvavātāhatānāṃ sutaduhitṛkalatratrāṇabhārārditānām  |
viṣamaviṣayatoye majjatāmaplavānāṃ bhavati śaraṇameko viṣṇupoto narāṇām  || 157.14 ||
huṅkāratīrthe rājendra śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  |
yatkṛtaṃ puruṣavyāghra tannaśyati na karhicit || 157.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe huṅkārasvāmitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 158

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchetparaṃ tīrthaṃ saṅgameśvaramuttamam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpabhayāpaham  || 158.1 ||
dhanadastatra viśrānto muhūrtaṃ nṛpasattama  |
pitṛlokātsamāyātaḥ kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  || 158.2 ||
pratyayārthaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha hyadyāpi dharaṇītale  |
kṛṣṇavarṇā hi pāṣāṇā dṛśyante sphaṭikojjvalāḥ  || 158.3 ||
vindhyanirjharaniṣkrāntā puṇyatoyā saridvarā  |
praviṣṭā narmadātoye sarvapāpapraṇāśane  || 158.4 ||
saṅgame tatra yaḥ snātvā pūjayetsaṅgameśvaram  |
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 158.5 ||
ghaṇṭāpatākāvitanaṃ yo dadetsaṅgameśvare  |
haṃsayuktavimānastho divyastrīśatasaṃvṛtaḥ  || 158.6 ||
sa rudrapadamāpnoti rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
dadhi bhakte na devasya yaḥ kuryālliṅgapūraṇam  || 158.7 ||
sikthasaṃkhyaṃ śive loke sa vasetkālamīpsitam  |
śrīphalaiḥ pūrayelliṅgaṃ niḥsvo bhūtvā bhavasya tu  || 158.8 ||
so'pi tatphalamāpnoti gataḥ svarge nareśvara  |
akṣayā santatistasya jāyate saptajanmasu  || 158.9 ||
snapanaṃ devadevasya dadhnā madhughṛtena vā  |
yaḥ karoti vidhānena tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 158.10 ||
dhṛtakṣīravahā nadyo yatra vṛkṣā madhusravāḥ  |
tatra te mānavā yānti suprasanne maheśvare  || 158.11 ||
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ yastu dadyānmaheśvare  |
tatsarvaṃ saptajanmāni hyakṣayaṃ phalamaśnute  || 158.12 ||
sarveṣāmeva pātrāṇāṃ mahāpātraṃ maheśvaraḥ  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena pūjanīyo maheśvaraḥ  || 158.13 ||
brahmacaryasthito nityaṃ yastu pūjayate śivam  |
iha jīvansa deveśo mṛto gacchedanāmayam  || 158.14 ||
śive tu pūjite pārtha yatphalaṃ prāpyate budhaiḥ  |
yogīndre caiva tatpārtha pūjite labhate phalam  || 158.15 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
yeṣāṃ gṛheṣu bhuñjanti śivabhaktiratā narāḥ  || 158.16 ||
saṃnirudhyendriyagrāmaṃ yatrayatra vasenmuniḥ  |
tatra tatra kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca  || 158.17 ||
yatphalaṃ vedaviduṣi bhojite śatasaṃkhyayā  |
tatphalaṃ jāyate pārtha hyekena śivayoginā  || 158.18 ||
yatra bhuñjati bhasmāṅgī mūrkho vā yadi paṇḍitaḥ  |
tatra bhuñjati deveśaḥ sapatnīko vṛṣadhvajaḥ  || 158.19 ||
viprāṇāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ koṭiṃ saṃbhojya yatphalam  |
bhikṣāmātrapradānena tatphalaṃ śivayoginām  || 158.20 ||
saṅgameśvaramāsādya prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ  |
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ śivalokātkadācana  || 158.21 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe saṅgameśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 159

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja tīrthaṃ paramapāvanam  |
narmadāyāṃ suduṣprāpaṃ siddhaṃ hyanarakeśvaram  || 159.1 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā pāpakarmāpi bhārata  |
na paśyati mahāghoraṃ narakadvārasaṃjñikam  || 159.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
śubhāśubhaphalaistāta bhuktabhogā narāstviha  |
jāyante lakṣaṇairyaistu tāni me vada sattama  || 159.3 ||
yathā nirgacchate jīvastyaktvā dehaṃ na paśyati  |
tathā gacchanpunardehaṃ pañcabhūtasamanvitaḥ  || 159.4 ||
tvagasthimāṃsamedo'sṛkkeśasnāyuśataiḥ saha  |
viṇmūtraretaḥsaṅghāte kā saṃjñā jāyate nṛṇām  || 159.5 ||
evamuktaḥ sa mārkaṇḍaḥ kathayāmāsa yogavit |
dhyātvā sanātanaṃ sarvaṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram  || 159.6 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇu pārtha mahāpraśnaṃ kathayāmi yathāśrutam  |
sakāśādbrahmaṇaḥ pūrvamṛṣidevasamāgame  || 159.7 ||
gururātmavatāṃ śāstā rājā śāstā durātmanām  |
iha pracchannapāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ  || 159.8 ||
acīrṇaprāyaścittānāṃ yamaloke hyanekadhā  |
yātanābhirviyuktānāmanekāṃ jīvasantatim  || 159.9 ||
gatvā manuṣyabhāve tu pāpacihnā bhavanti te  |
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  || 159.10 ||
sahitvā yātanāṃ sarvāṃ gatvā vaivasvatakṣayam  |
vistīrṇayātanā ye tu lokamāyānti cihnitāḥ  || 159.11 ||
gadgado'nṛtavādī syānmūkaścaiva gavānṛte  |
brahmahā jāyate kuṣṭhī śyāvadantastu madyapaḥ  || 159.12 ||
kunakhī svarṇaharaṇādduḥścarmā gurutalpagaḥ  |
saṃyogī hīnayoniḥ syāddaridro'dattadānataḥ  || 159.13 ||
grāmaśūkaratāṃ yāti hyayājyayājako nṛpa  |
kharo vai bahuyājī syācchvānimantritabhojanāt || 159.14 ||
aparīkṣitabhojī syādvānaro vijane vane  |
vitarjako'tha mārjāraḥ khadyotaḥ kakṣadāhataḥ  || 159.15 ||
avidyāṃ yaḥ prayaccheta balīvardo bhaveddhi saḥ  |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vipre dadānaḥ klībatāṃ vrajet || 159.16 ||
mātsaryādatha jātyandho janmāndhaḥ pustakaṃ haran  |
phalānyāharato'patyaṃ mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 159.17 ||
mṛto vānaratāṃ yāti tanmukto'tha galāḍavān  |
adattvā bhakṣayaṃstāni hyanapatyo bhavennaraḥ  || 159.18 ||
haranvastraṃ bhavedgodhā garadaḥ pavanāśanaḥ  |
pravrājī gamanādrājan bhavenmarupiśācakaḥ  || 159.19 ||
vātako jalahartā ca dhānyahartā ca mūṣakaḥ  |
aprāptayauvanāṃ gacchan bhavetsarpa iti śrutiḥ  || 159.20 ||
gurudārābhilāṣī ca kṛkalāso bhavecciram  |
jalaprasravaṇaṃ yastu bhindyānmatsyo bhavennaraḥ  || 159.21 ||
avikreyān vikrayan vai vikaṭākṣo bhavennaraḥ  |
ayonigo vṛko hi syādulūkaḥ krayavañcanāt || 159.22 ||
mṛtasyaikādaśāhe tu bhuñjānaḥ śvopajāyate  |
pratiśrutya dvijāyārthamadadanmadhuko bhavet || 159.23 ||
rājñīgamādbhavedduṣṭataskaro viḍvarāhakaḥ  |
parivādī dvijātīnāṃ labhate kācchapīṃ tanum  || 159.24 ||
vrajeddevalako rājanyoniṃ cāṇḍālasaṃjñitām  |
durbhagaḥ phalavikretā vṛściko vṛṣalīpatiḥ  || 159.25 ||
mārjāro'gniṃ padā spṛṣṭvā rogavānparamāṃsabhuk |
sodaryāgamanātṣaṇḍho durgandhaśca sugandhahṛt || 159.26 ||
grāmabhaṭṭo divākīrtirdaivajño gardabho bhavet |
kupaṇḍitaḥ syānmārjāro bhaṣaṇo vyāsa eva ca  || 159.27 ||
sa eva dṛśyate rājanprakāśātparamarmaṇām  |
yadvā tadvāpi pārakyaṃ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu  || 159.28 ||
kṛtvā vai yonimāpnoti tairaścīṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
evamādīni cānyāni cihnāni nṛpasattama  || 159.29 ||
svakarmavihitānyeva dṛśyante yaistu mānavāḥ  |
tato janma tato mṛtyuḥ sarvajantuṣu bhārata  || 159.30 ||
jāyate nātra sandehaḥ samībhūte śubhāśubhe  |
strīpuṃsoḥ samprayogeṇa viṣuddhe śukraśoṇite  || 159.31 ||
pañcabhūtasamopetaḥ saṣaṣṭhaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
indriyāṇi manaḥ prāṇā jñānamāyuḥ sukhaṃ dhṛtiḥ  || 159.32 ||
dhāraṇaṃ preraṇaṃ duḥkhamicchāhaṅkāra eva ca  |
prayatna ākṛtirvarṇaḥ svaradveṣau bhavābhavau  || 159.33 ||
tasyedamātmanaḥ sarvamanāderādimicchataḥ  |
prathame māsi sa kledabhūto dhātuvimūrchitaḥ  || 159.34 ||
māsyarbudaṃ dvitīye tu tṛtīye cendriyairyutaḥ  |
ākāśāllāghavaṃ saukṣmyaṃ śabdaṃ śrotrabalādikam  |
vāyostu sparśanaṃ ceṣṭāṃ dahanaṃ raukṣyameva ca  || 159.35 ||
pittāttu darśanaṃ paktimauṣṇyaṃ rūpaṃ prakāśanam  |
salilādrasanāṃ śaityaṃ snehaṃ kledaṃ samārdavam  || 159.36 ||
bhūmergandhaṃ tathā ghrāṇaṃ gauravaṃ mūrtimeva ca  |
ātmā gṛhṇātyajaḥ pūrvaṃ tṛtīye spandate ca saḥ  || 159.37 ||
daurhṛdasyāpradānena garbho doṣamavāpnuyāt |
vairūpyaṃ maraṇaṃ vāpi tasmātkāryaṃ priyaṃ striyāḥ  || 159.38 ||
sthairyaṃ caturthe tvaṅgānāṃ pañcame śoṇitodbhavaḥ  |
ṣaṣṭhe balaṃ ca varṇaśca nakharomṇāṃ ca sambhavaḥ  || 159.39 ||
manasā cetanāyukto nakharomaśatāvṛtaḥ  |
saptame cāṣṭame caiva tvacāvān smṛtivānapi  || 159.40 ||
punargarbhaṃ punardhātrīmenastasya pradhāvati  |
aṣṭame māsyato garbho jātaḥ prāṇairviyujyate  || 159.41 ||
navame daśame vāpi prabalaiḥ sūtimārutaiḥ  |
nirgacchate bāṇa iva yantracchidreṇa sajvaraḥ  || 159.42 ||
śarīrāvayavairyukto hyaṅgapratyaṅgasaṃyutaḥ  |
aṣṭottaraṃ marmaśataṃ tatrāsthā tu śatatrayam  || 159.43 ||
sapta śiraḥkapālāni vihitāni svayambhuvā  |
tisraḥ koṭyo'rdhakoṭī ca romṇāmaṅgeṣu bhārata  || 159.44 ||
dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi hṛdayādabhinisṛtāḥ  |
hitānāma hi tā nāḍyastāsāṃ madhye śaśiprabhā  || 159.45 ||
evaṃ pravartate cakraṃ bhūtagrāme caturvidhe  |
utpattiśca vināśaśca bhavataḥ sarvadehinām  || 159.46 ||
gatirūrdhvā ca dharmeṇa hyadharmeṇa tvadhogatiḥ  |
jāyate sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmacalanānnṛpa  || 159.47 ||
devatve mānavatve ca dānabhogādikāḥ kriyāḥ  |
dṛśyante yā mahārāja tatsarvaṃ karmajaṃ phalam  || 159.48 ||
svakarma vihite ghore kāmaksodhārjite śubhe  |
nimajjennarake ghore yasyottāro na vidyate  || 159.49 ||
uttāraṇāya jantūnāṃ narmadātaṭasaṃsthitam  |
evametanmahātīrthaṃ narakeśvaramuttamam  || 159.50 ||
narakāpahaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  |
tattīrthaṃ sarvatīrthānāmuttamaṃ bhuvi durlabham  || 159.51 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayeta maheśvaram  |
mahāpātakayukto'pi narakaṃ naiva paśyati  || 159.52 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo dadyāddhenuṃ vaitaraṇīṃ śubhām  |
sa mucyate sukhenaiva vaitaraṇyāṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 159.53 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
yamadvāre mahāghore yā sā vaitaraṇī nadī  |
kiṃrūpā kiṃpramāṇā sā kathaṃ sā vahati dvija  || 159.54 ||
kathaṃ tasyāḥ pramucyante keṣāṃ vāsastu saṃtatam  |
keṣāṃ tu sānukūlā sā hyetadvistarato vada  || 159.55 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dharmaputra mahābāho śṛṇu sarvaṃ mayoditam  |
yā sā vaitaraṇī nāma yamadvāre mahāsarit || 159.56 ||
agādhā pārarahitā dṛṣṭamātrā bhayāvahā  |
pūyaśoṇitatoyā sā māṃsakardamanirmitā  || 159.57 ||
tattoyaṃ bhramate tūrṇaṃ tāpīmadhye ghṛtaṃ yathā  |
kṛmibhiḥ saṅkulaṃ pūyaṃ vajratuṇḍairayomukhaiḥ  || 159.58 ||
śiśumāraiśca makarairvajrakartarisaṃyutaiḥ  |
anyaiśca jalajīvaiḥ sā suhiṃsrairmarmabhedibhiḥ  || 159.59 ||
tapanti dvādaśādityāḥ pralayānta ivolbaṇāḥ  |
patanti tatra vai martyāḥ krandanto bhṛśadāruṇam  || 159.60 ||
hā bhrātaḥ putra hā mātaḥ pralapanti muhurmuhuḥ  |
asipattravane ghore patantaṃ yo'bhirakṣati  || 159.61 ||
prataranti nimajjanti glāniṃ gacchanti jantavaḥ  |
caturvidhaiḥ prāṇigaṇairdraṣṭavyā sā mahānadī  || 159.62 ||
taranti tasyāṃ saddānairanyathā tu patanti te  |
mātaraṃ ye na manyante hyācāryaṃ gurumeva ca  || 159.63 ||
avajānanti mūḍhā ye teṣāṃ vāsastu saṃtatam  |
pativratāṃ sādhuśīlāmūḍhāṃ dharmeṣu niścalām  || 159.64 ||
parityajanti ye pāpāḥ saṃtataṃ tu vasanti te  |
viśvāsapratipannānāṃ svāmimitratapasvinām  || 159.65 ||
strībālavṛddhadīnānāṃ chidramanveṣayanti ye  |
pacyante tatra madhye vai krandamānāḥ supāpinaḥ  || 159.66 ||
śrāntaṃ bubhukṣitaṃ vipraṃ yo vighnayati durmatiḥ  |
kṛmibhirbhakṣyate tatra yāvatkalpaśatatrayam  || 159.67 ||
brāhmaṇāya pratiśrutya yo dānaṃ na prayacchati  |
āhūya nāsti yo brūte tasya vāsastu saṃtatam  || 159.68 ||
agnido garadaścaiva rājagāmī ca paiśunī  |
kathābhaṅgakaraścaiva kūṭasākṣī ca madyapaḥ  || 159.69 ||
vajravidhvaṃsakaścaiva svayaṃdattāpahārakaḥ  |
sukṣetrasetubhedī ca paradārapradharṣakaḥ  || 159.70 ||
brāhmaṇo rasavikretā vṛṣalīpatireva ca  |
gokulasya tṛṣārtasya pālībhedaṃ karoti yaḥ  || 159.71 ||
kanyābhidūṣakaścaiva dānaṃ dattvā tu tāpakaḥ  |
śūdrastu kapilāpānī brāhmaṇo māṃsabhojanī  || 159.72 ||
ete vasanti satataṃ mā vicāraṃ kṛthā nṛpa  |
sānukūlā bhavedyena tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 159.73 ||
ayane viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte dinakṣaye  |
anyeṣu puṇyakāleṣu dīyate dānamuttamam  || 159.74 ||
kṛṣṇāṃ vā pāṭalāṃ vāpi kuryādvaitaraṇīṃ śubhām  |
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ rūpyakhurāṃ kāṃsyapātrasya dohinīm  || 159.75 ||
kṛṣṇavastrayugācchannāṃ saptadhānyasamanvitām  |
kuryātsadroṇaśikhara āsīnāṃ tāmrabhājane  || 159.76 ||
yamaṃ haimaṃ prakurvīta lohadaṇḍasamanvitam  |
ikṣudaṇḍamayaṃ baddhvā hyuḍupaṃ paṭṭabandhanaiḥ  || 159.77 ||
uḍupopari tāṃ dhenuṃ sūryadehasamudbhavām  |
kṛtvā prakalpayedvidvāñchattropānadyugānvitām  || 159.78 ||
aṅgulīyakavāsāṃsi brāhmaṇāya nivedayet |
imamuccārayenmantraṃ saṃgṛhyāsyāśca pucchakam  || 159.79 ||
oṃ yamadvāre mahāghore yā sā vaitaraṇī nadī  |
tartukāmo dadāmyenāṃ tubhyaṃ vaitaraṇi namaḥ  |
ityadhivāsanamantraḥ  || 159.80 ||
gāvo me cāgrataḥ santu gāvo me santu pṛṣṭhataḥ  |
gāvo me hṛdaye santu gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham  || 159.81 ||
oṃ viṣṇurūpa dvijaśreṣṭha bhūdeva paṅktipāvana  |
sadakṣiṇā mayā dattā tubhyaṃ vaitaraṇi namaḥ  |
iti dānamantraḥ  || 159.82 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ dharmarājaṃ ca dhenuṃ vaitaraṇīṃ śivām  |
sarvaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya brāhmaṇāya nivedayet || 159.83 ||
pucchaṃ saṃgṛhya surabheragre kṛtvā dvijaṃ tataḥ  || 159.84 ||
dhenuke tvaṃ pratīkṣasva yamadvāre mahābhaye  |
uttitīrṣurahaṃ dheno vaitaraṇyai namo'stu te  |
ityanuvrajamantraḥ  || 159.85 ||
anuvrajeta gacchantaṃ sarvaṃ tasya gṛhaṃ nayet |
evaṃ kṛte mahīpāla saritsyātsukhavāhinī  || 159.86 ||
tārayate tayā dhenvā sā sarijjalavāhinī  |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti ye divyā ye ca mānuṣāḥ  || 159.87 ||
rogī rogādvimuktaḥ syācchāmyanti paramāpadaḥ  |
svasthe sahasraguṇitamāture śatasaṃmitam  || 159.88 ||
mṛtasyaiva tu yaddānaṃ parokṣe tatsamaṃ smṛtam  |
svahastena tato deyaṃ mṛte kaḥ kasya dāsyati  |
iti matvā mahārāja svadattaṃ syānmahāphalam  || 159.89 ||
ityevamuktaṃ tava dharmasūno dānaṃ mayā vaitaraṇīsamuttham  |
śṛṇoti bhaktyā paṭhatīha samyaksa yāti viṣṇoḥ padamaprameyam  || 159.90 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
prāpte cāśvayuje māsi tasminkṛṣṇā caturdaśī  |
snātvā kṛtvā tataḥ śrāddhaṃ sampūjya ca maheśvaram  || 159.91 ||
pitṛbhyo dīyate dānaṃ bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaiḥ  |
paścājjāgaraṇaṃ kuryātsatkathāśravaṇādibhiḥ  || 159.92 ||
tataḥ prabhātasamaye snātvā vai narmadājale  |
tarpaṇaṃ vidhivatkṛtvā pitḥṇāṃ devapūrvakam  || 159.93 ||
sauvarṇe ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ dīpaṃ dadyāddvijātaye  |
paścātsaṃbhojayedviprān svayaṃ caiva vimatsaraḥ  || 159.94 ||
evaṃ kṛte naraśreṣṭha na janturnarakaṃ vrajet |
avaśyameva manujairdraṣṭavyā nārakī sthitiḥ  || 159.95 ||
anena vidhinā kṛtvā na paśyennarakānnaraḥ  |
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ vidhinā nṛpa  || 159.96 ||
manvantaraṃ śive loke vāso bhavati durlabhe  |
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṃkiṇīśataśobhinā  || 159.97 ||
sa gacchati mahābhāga sevyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  |
bhunakti vividhānbhogānuktakālaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 159.98 ||
pūrṇe caiva tataḥ kāla iha mānuṣyatāṃ gataḥ  |
sarvavyādhivinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 159.99 ||
prāpya cāśvayuje māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
mahāpātakayukto'pi mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 159.100 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatikoṭyo vai narakāṇāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
vimuktā narakairduḥkhaiḥ śivalokaṃ vrajanti te  || 159.101 ||
tatra bhuktvā mahābhogāndivyaiśvaryasamanvitān  |
labhante mānuṣaṃ janma durlabhaṃ bhuvi mānavāḥ  || 159.102 ||

|| iti  śrīskande mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe'narakeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 160

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchetpāṇḍuputra mokṣatīrthamanuttamam  |
sevitaṃ devagandharvairmunibhiśca tapodhanaiḥ  || 160.1 ||
bahavastanna jānanti viṣṇumāyāvimohitāḥ  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgā ṛṣayaḥ satapodhanāḥ  || 160.2 ||
pulastyaḥ pulaho vidvānkratuścaiva mahāmatiḥ  |
prācetaso vasiṣṭhaśca dakṣo nārada eva ca  || 160.3 ||
ete cānye mahābhāgāḥ saptasāhasrasaṃjñitāḥ  |
mokṣaṃ gatāḥ saha sutaistattīrthaṃ tena mokṣadam  || 160.4 ||
tatra pravāhamadhye tu patitā tamahā nadī  |
tatra tatsaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram  || 160.5 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃjñānāmabhyastānāṃ tu yatphalam  |
samyagjaptvā tu vidhinā gāyatrīṃ tatra tallabhet || 160.6 ||
tatra dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ tīrthasevārjitaṃ phalam  |
sarvamakṣayatāṃ yāti mokṣasādhanamuttamam  || 160.7 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu saṃnyāsena dvijanmanām  |
anivartikā gatisteṣāṃ mokṣatīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 160.8 ||
eṣa te vidhiruddiṣṭaḥ saṃkṣepeṇa mayānagha  |
vyuṣṭistīrthasya mahatī purāṇe yābhidhīyate  || 160.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mokṣatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 161

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja sarpatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahāsarpāstapastaptvā yudhiṣṭhira  || 161.1 ||
vāsukistakṣako ghoraḥ sārpa airāvatastathā  |
kāliyaśca mahābhāgaḥ karkoṭakadhanaṃjayau  || 161.2 ||
śaṅkhacūḍo mahātejā dhṛtarāṣṭro vṛkodaraḥ  |
kuliko vāmanaścaiva teṣāṃ ye putrapautriṇaḥ  || 161.3 ||
tatra tīrthe mahāpuṇye tapastaptvā suduṣkaram  |
bhuñjanti vividhānbhogānkrīḍanti ca yathāsukham  || 161.4 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
vājapeyaphalaṃ tasya purā provāca śaṅkaraḥ  || 161.5 ||
snātānāṃ sarpatīrthe tu narāṇāṃ bhuvi bhārata  |
sarpavṛścikajātibhyo na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit || 161.6 ||
mṛto bhogavatīṃ gatvā pūjyamāno mahoragaiḥ  |
nāgakanyāparivṛto mahābhogapatirbhavet || 161.7 ||
mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe ca yāṣṭamī  |
sopavāsaḥ śucirbhūtvā liṅgaṃ sampūrayettilaiḥ  |
yathāvibhavasāreṇa gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet || 161.8 ||
evaṃ vidhāya vidhivatpraṇipatya kṣamāpayet |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva nareśvara  || 161.9 ||
tilāstatra ca yatsaṃkhyāḥ patrapuṣpaphalāni ca  |
tāvatsvargapure rājanmodate kālamīpsitam  || 161.10 ||
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo jāyate vimale kule  |
surūpaḥ subhagaścaiva dhanakoṭipatirbhavet || 161.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe sarpatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 162

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gopeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarpakṣetrādanantaram  |
yatra snānena caikena mucyante pātakairnarāḥ  || 162.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā kurute prāṇasaṃkṣayam  |
sa gacchedyadi yukto'pi pāpena śivamandiram  || 162.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayeddevamīśvaram  |
mucyate sarvapāpaiśca rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 162.3 ||
krīḍitvā ca yathākāmaṃ rudraloke mahātapāḥ  |
iha mānuṣyatāṃ prāpya rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  || 162.4 ||
hastyaśvarathasampanno dāsīdāsasamanvitaḥ  |
pūjyamāno narendraiśca jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sukhī  || 162.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gopeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dviṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 163

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja nāgatīrthamanuttamam  |
āśvinasya site pakṣe pañcamyāṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ  || 163.1 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā gandhadhūpanivedanaiḥ  |
prabhāte vimale snātvā śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 163.2 ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo rājanprāṇatyāgaṃ kariṣyati  || 163.3 ||
anivartikā gatistasya provāceti śivaḥ svayam  || 163.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe nāgatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma triṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 164

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahārāja sāṃvauraṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
yatra saṃnihito bhānuḥ pūjyamānaḥ surāsuraiḥ  || 164.1 ||
tatra ye paṅgutāṃ prāptāḥ śīrṇaghrāṇanakhā narāḥ  |
dadrumaṇḍalabhinnāṅgā makṣikākṛmisaṃkulāḥ  || 164.2 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ rahitā bhrātṛbhāryāvivarjitāḥ  |
anāthā vikalā vyaṅgā magnā ye duḥkhasāgare  || 164.3 ||
teṣāṃ nātho jagadyonirnarmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  |
sāṃvauranātho lokānāmārtihā duḥkhanāśanaḥ  || 164.4 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā māsamekaṃ nirantaram  |
pūjayedbhāskaraṃ devaṃ tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 164.5 ||
yatphalaṃ cottare pārtha tathā vai pūrvasāgare  |
dakṣiṇe paścime snātvā tatra tīrthe tu tatphalam  || 164.6 ||
kaumāre yauvane pāpaṃ vārddhake yacca saṃcitam  |
tatpraṇaśyati sāṃvaure snānamātrānna saṃśayaḥ  || 164.7 ||
na vyādhirnaiva dāridryaṃ na caiveṣṭaviyojanam  |
saptajanmāni rājendra sāṃvauraparisevanāt || 164.8 ||
saptamyāmupavāsena taddine cāpyupoṣite  |
sa tatphalamavāpnoti tatra snātvā na saṃśayaḥ  || 164.9 ||
raktacandanamiśreṇa yadarghyeṇa phalaṃ smṛtam  |
tatra tīrthe nṛpaśreṣṭha snātvā tatphalamāpnuyāt || 164.10 ||
narmadāsalilaṃ ramyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  |
nirīkṣitaṃ viśeṣeṇa sāṃvaureṇa mahātmanā  || 164.11 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
snātvā paśyanti deveśaṃ sāṃvaureśvaramuttamam  || 164.12 ||
sūryaloke vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 164.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe sāṃvaureśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 165

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle siddheśvaramiti śrutam  |
tīrthaṃ paraṃ mahārāja siddhaiḥ kṛtamiti prabho  || 165.1 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu pāvanam  |
narmadāyā mahārāja dakṣiṇaṃ kūlamāśritam  || 165.2 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ tatraiva yo dadyātpitḥnuddiśya bhārata  || 165.3 ||
tṛpyanti pitarastasya dvādaśābdānna saṃśayaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā snātvā pūjayate śivam  || 165.4 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā paṭhetpaurāṇikīṃ kathām  |
tataḥ prabhāte vimale snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 165.5 ||
vīkṣate girijākāntaṃ sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  |
purā siddhā mahābhāgāḥ kapilādyā maharṣayaḥ  || 165.6 ||
japantaśca paraṃ brahma yogasiddhā mahāvratāḥ  |
siddhiṃ te paramāṃ prāptā narmadāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ  || 165.7 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe siddheśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 166

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ siddheśvarī devī vaiṣṇavī pāpanāśinī  |
ānandaṃ paramaṃ prāptā dṛṣṭvā sthānaṃ suśobhanam  || 166.1 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
devīṃ paśyati yo bhaktyā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 166.2 ||
mṛtavatsā tu yā nārī vandhyā strījananī tathā  |
putraṃ sā labhate nārī śīlavantaṃ guṇānvitam  || 166.3 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā paśyeddevīṃ subhaktitaḥ  |
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ sarvakāle'thavā nṛpa  || 166.4 ||
saṅgame tu tataḥ snātā nārī vā puruṣo'pi vā  |
putraṃ dhanaṃ tathā devī dadāti paritoṣitā  || 166.5 ||
gotrarakṣāṃ prakurute dṛṣṭā devī supūjitā  |
prajāṃ ca pāti satataṃ pūjyamānā na saṃśayaḥ  || 166.6 ||
navamyāṃ ca mahārāja snātvā devīmupoṣitaḥ  |
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā śraddhāpūtena cetasā  || 166.7 ||
sa gacchetparamaṃ lokaṃ yaḥ surairapi durlabhaḥ  || 166.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe siddheśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 167

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tvaccihnenopalakṣitam  |
tīrthametanmamākhyāhi sambhavaṃ ca mahāmune  || 167.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā kṛtayugasyādau dakṣiṇe girimuttamam  |
vindhyaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ niyato niyatāśanaḥ  || 167.2 ||
ṛṣisaṅghaiḥ kṛtātithyo daṇḍake nyavasaṃ ciram  |
uṣitvā suciraṃ kālaṃ varṣāṇāmayutaṃ sukhī  || 167.3 ||
tānṛṣīn samanujñāpya śiṣyairanugatastataḥ  |
nivṛttaḥ sumahābhāga narmadākūlamāgataḥ  || 167.4 ||
puṇyaṃ ca ramaṇīyaṃ ca sarvapāpavināśanam  |
kṛtvāhamāspadaṃ tatra dvijasaṃghasamāyutaḥ  || 167.5 ||
brahmacāribhirākīrṇaṃ gārhasthye supratiṣṭhitaiḥ  |
vānaprasthaiśca yatibhiryatāhārairyatātmabhiḥ  || 167.6 ||
tapasvibhirmahābhāgaiḥ kāmakrodhavivarjitaiḥ  |
tatrāhaṃ varṣamayutaṃ tapaḥ kṛtvā sudāruṇam  || 167.7 ||
ārādhayaṃ vāsudevaṃ prabhuṃ kartāramīśvaram  |
japaṃstapobhirniyamairnarmadākūlamāśritaḥ  || 167.8 ||
tatastau varadau devau samāyātau yudhiṣṭhira  |
pratyakṣau bhāskarau rājannumāśrībhyāṃ vibhūṣitau  || 167.9 ||
praṇamyāhaṃ tato devau bhaktiyukto vaco'bruvam  |
bhavantau prārthayāmi sma varārhau varadau śivau  || 167.10 ||
dharmasthitiṃ mahābhāgau bhaktiṃ vānuttamāṃ yuvām  |
ajaro vyādhirahitaḥ pañcaviṃśativarṣavat |
asminsthāne sadā stheyaṃ saha devairasaṃśayam  || 167.11 ||
evamuktau mayā pārtha tau devau kṛṣṇaśaṅkarau  |
māmūcatuḥ prahṛṣṭau tau nivāsārthaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  || 167.12 ||
devāvūcatuḥ  |
asminsthāne sthitau viddhi saha devaiḥ savāsavaiḥ  |
evamuktvā tato devau tatraivāntaradhīyatām  || 167.13 ||
ahaṃ ca sthāpayitvā tau śaṅkaraṃ kṛṣṇamavyayam  |
kṛtakṛtyastato jātaḥ sampūjya susamāhitaḥ  || 167.14 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
mārkaṇḍeśvaranāmnā vai viṣṇuṃ tribhuvaneśvaram  || 167.15 ||
sa gacchetparamaṃ sthānaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śaivameva ca  |
ghṛtena payasā vātha dadhnā ca madhunā tathā  || 167.16 ||
nārmadenodakenātha gandhadhūpaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ  |
puṣpopahāraiśca tathā naivedyairniyatātmavān  || 167.17 ||
evaṃ viṣṇoḥ prakurvīta jāgaraṃ bhaktitatparaḥ  |
snānādīni tathā rājanprayataḥ śucimānasaḥ  || 167.18 ||
jyeṣṭhe māsi site pakṣe caturdaśyāmupoṣitaḥ  |
dvādaśyāṃ kārayeddevapūjanaṃ vaiṣṇavo naraḥ  || 167.19 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā caturdaśyāmekādaśyāṃ narottama  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ lokamāpnoti viṣṇutulyo bhavennaraḥ  || 167.20 ||
māheśvare ca rājendra gaṇavanmodate pure  |
śrāddhaṃ ca kurute tatra pitḥnuddiśya susthiraḥ  || 167.21 ||
tasya te hyakṣayāṃ tṛptiṃ prāpnuvanti na saṃśayaḥ  |
narmadāyāṃ dvijaḥ snātvā maunī niyatamānasaḥ  || 167.22 ||
upāsya sandhyāṃ tatrastho japaṃ kṛtvā suśobhanam  |
tarpayitvā pitḥndevānmanuṣyāṃśca yathāvidhi  || 167.23 ||
kṛṣṇasya purataḥ sthitvā mārkaṇḍeśasya vā punaḥ  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmamantrāṃśca japedatra prayatnataḥ  || 167.24 ||
ṛcamekāṃ japedyastu ṛgvedasya phalaṃ labhet |
yajurvedasya yajuṣā sāmnā sāmaphalaṃ labhet || 167.25 ||
ekasminbhojite vipre koṭirbhavati bhojitā  |
mṛtaprajā tu yā nārī vandhyā strījananī tathā  || 167.26 ||
rudrāṃstu vidhivajjaptvā brāhmaṇo vedatattvavit |
liṅgasya dakṣiṇe pārśve sthāpayetkalaśaṃ śivam  || 167.27 ||
rudraikādaśabhirmantraiḥ snāpayetkalaśāmbhasā  |
putramāpnoti rājendra dīrghāyuṣamakalmaṣam  || 167.28 ||
mārkaṇḍeśvaravṛkṣānyo dūrasthānapi paśyati  |
brahmahatyādipāpebhyo mucyate śaṅkaro'bravīt || 167.29 ||
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā paṭhedvā nṛpasattama  |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 167.30 ||
idaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ dhanyaṃ duḥkhapraṇāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ vāpi sarvapāpapramocanam  || 167.31 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 168

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe rodhasyaṅkūreśvaramuttamam  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 168.1 ||
yatra siddhaṃ mahārakṣa ārādhya tu maheśvaram  |
śaṅkaraṃ jagataḥ prāṇaṃ smṛtimātrāvahāriṇam  || 168.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kiṃ tadrakṣo dvijaśreṣṭha kiṃnāma kasya vānvaye  |
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  || 168.3 ||
ajñānatimirāndhā ye pumāṃsaḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ  |
yuṣmadvidhairdīpabhūtaiḥ paśyanti sacarācaram  || 168.4 ||
dharmaputravacaḥ śrutvā mārkaṇḍeyo munīśvaraḥ  |
smitaṃ kṛtvā babhāṣe tāṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśanīm  || 168.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
mānaso brahmaṇaḥ putraḥ pulastyo nāma pārthiva  |
vedaśāstrapravaktā ca sākṣādvedhā ivāparaḥ  || 168.6 ||
tṛṇabindusutā tasya bhāryāsītparameṣṭhinaḥ  |
tasya dharmaprasaṅgena putro jāto mahāmanāḥ  || 168.7 ||
yasmādvedetihāsaiśca saṣaḍaṅgapadakramāḥ  |
viśrāntā brahmaṇā dattā nāma viśravaseti ca  || 168.8 ||
kasmiṃścidatha kāle ca bharadvājo mahāmuniḥ  |
svasutāṃ pradadau rājanmudā viśravase nṛpa  || 168.9 ||
sa tayā ramate sārdhaṃ paulomyā maghavā iva  |
mudā paramayā rājanbrāhmaṇo vedavittamaḥ  || 168.10 ||
kenacittvatha kālena putraḥ putraguṇairyutaḥ  |
jajñe viśravaso rājannāmnā vaiśravaṇaḥ śrutaḥ  || 168.11 ||
so'pi maunavrataṃ kṛtvā bālabhāvādyudhiṣṭhira  |
sarvabhūtābhayaṃ dattvā cacāra paramaṃ vratam  || 168.12 ||
tasya tuṣṭo mahādevo brahmā brahmarṣibhiḥ saha  |
sakhitvaṃ ceśvaro dattvā dhanadatvaṃ jagāma ha  || 168.13 ||
yamendravaruṇānāṃ ca caturthastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  |
brahmāpyuktvā jagāmāśu lokapālatvamīpsitam  || 168.14 ||
tatastvanantare kāle kaikasī nāma rākṣasī  |
pātālaṃ bhūtalaṃ tyaktvā viśravaṃ cakame patim  || 168.15 ||
putro'tha rāvaṇo jātastasyā bharatasattama  |
kumbhakarṇo mahārakṣo dharmātmā ca vibhīṣaṇaḥ  || 168.16 ||
kumbhaścaiva vikumbhaśca kumbhakarṇasutāvubhau  |
mahābalau mahāvīryau mahāntau puruṣottama  || 168.17 ||
aṅkūro rākṣasaśreṣṭhaḥ kumbhasya tanayo mahān  |
vibhīṣaṇaṃ ca guṇavaddṛṣṭvaivaṃ rākṣasottamaḥ  || 168.18 ||
tataḥ sa yauvanaṃ prāpya jñātvā rakṣaḥ pitāmaham  |
paraṃ nirvedamāpannaścacāra sumahattapaḥ  || 168.19 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ paścimaṃ gatvā sāgaraṃ pūrvamuttaram  |
narmadāyāṃ prasaṅgena hyaṅkūro rākṣaseśvaraḥ  || 168.20 ||
tapaścacāra sumahaddivyaṃ varṣaśataṃ kila  |
tatastuṣṭo mahādevaḥ sākṣātparapuraṃjayaḥ  || 168.21 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa rākṣasaṃ vṛṣaketanaḥ  |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te tava dāsyāmi suvrata  || 168.22 ||
provāca rākṣaso vākyaṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram  |
varadaṃ so'grato dṛṣṭvā praṇamya ca punaḥpunaḥ  || 168.23 ||
yadi tuṣṭo mahādeva varado'si sureśvara  |
durlabhaṃ sarvabhūtānāmamaratvaṃ prayaccha me  || 168.24 ||
mama nāmnā sthito'nena vareṇa tripurāntaka  |
sadā saṃnihito'pyatra tīrthe bhavitumarhasi  || 168.25 ||

īśvara uvāca -
yāvadvibhīṣaṇamataṃ yāvaddharmaniṣevaṇam  |
kariṣyasi dṛḍhātmā tvaṃ tāvadetadbhaviṣyati  || 168.26 ||
evamuktvā yayau devaḥ sarvadaivatapūjitaḥ  |
vimānenārkavarṇena kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  || 168.27 ||
gate cādarśanaṃ deve snātvācamya vidhānataḥ  |
sthāpayāmāsa rājendra hyaṅkūreśvaramuttamam  || 168.28 ||
gandhapuṣpaistathā dhūpairvastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
patākaiścāmaraiśchatrairjayaśabdādimaṃgalaiḥ  || 168.29 ||
pūjayitvā sureśānaṃ stotrairhṛdyaiḥ supuṣkalaiḥ  |
jagāma bhavanaṃ rakṣo yatra rājā vibhīṣaṇaḥ  || 168.30 ||
pūjitaḥ sa yathānyāyaṃ dānasanmānagauravaiḥ  |
saudarye sthāpito bhāve so'vātsītparayāmudā  || 168.31 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
aṅkūreśvaranāmānaṃ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 168.32 ||
māṇḍavyakhātamārabhya saṅgamaṃ vāpi yacchubham  |
revāyā āmalakyāśca devakṣetraṃ maheśvaram  || 168.33 ||
māṇḍavyakhātātpaścimatastīrthaṃ tadaṅkūreśvaram  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ  || 168.34 ||
sandhyāmācamya yatnena japaṃ kṛtvātha bhārata  |
tarpayitvā pitḥndevānmanuṣyān bharatarṣabha  || 168.35 ||
sacailaḥ klinnavasano maunamāsthāya saṃyataḥ  |
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāmupoṣya vidhivannaraḥ  || 168.36 ||
pūjāṃ yaḥ kurute rājaṃstasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
sāgraṃ tu yojanaśataṃ tīrthānyāyatanāni ca  || 168.37 ||
bhavanti tāni dṛṣṭāni tataḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaddānaṃ devamuddiśya dīyate  || 168.38 ||
