snātvā tu vidhivatpātre tadakṣayamudāhṛtam  |
homāddaśaguṇaṃ proktaṃ phalaṃ jāpye tato'dhikam  || 168.39 ||
triguṇaṃ copavāsena snānena ca caturguṇam  |
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yastu prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti vā  || 168.40 ||
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānāṃ tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
aṅkūreśvaranāmākhye mṛtānāṃ sugatirbhavet || 168.41 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājannaṅkūreśvarasambhavam  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ paramaṃ pāpanāśanam  || 168.42 ||
ye'pi śṛṇvanti bhaktyedaṃ kīrtyamānaṃ mahāphalam  |
labhante nātra sandehaḥ śivasya bhuvanaṃ hi te  || 168.43 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe aṅkūreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 169

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchetparaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
māṇḍavyo yatra saṃsiddha ṛṣirnārāyaṇastathā  || 169.1 ||
nārāyaṇena śuśrūṣā śūlasthena kṛtā purā  |
tatra snātvā mahārāja mucyate pāpakañcukāt || 169.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryametallokeṣu yattvayā kathitaṃ mune  |
na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ tāta śūlasthena tapaḥ kṛtam  || 169.3 ||
etatsarvaṃ kathaya me ṛṣibhiḥ sahitasya vai  |
asya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ māṇḍavyasya kutūhalāt || 169.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇu rājanyathāvṛttapurā tretāyuge kṣitau  |
lokapālopamo rājā devapanno mahāmatiḥ  || 169.5 ||
dharmajñaśca kṛtajñaśca yajvā dānarataḥ sadā  |
prajā rarakṣa yatnena pitā putrānivaurasān  || 169.6 ||
dātyāyanī priyā bhāryā tasya rājño vaśānugā  |
hāranūpuraghoṣeṇa jhaṅkāraravanāditā  || 169.7 ||
parasparaṃ tayoḥ prītirvardhate'nudinaṃ nṛpa  |
vaṃśastambe sthito rājā saṃśāsti pṛthivīmimām  || 169.8 ||
hastyaśvarathasampūrṇāṃ dhanavāhanasaṃyutām  |
alaṃkṛto guṇaiḥ sarvairanapatyo mahīpatiḥ  || 169.9 ||
duḥkhena mahatāviṣṭaḥ saṃtaptaḥ santatiṃ vinā  |
snānahomarato nityaṃ dvādaśābdāni bhārata  || 169.10 ||
vratopavāsaniyamaiḥ patnībhiḥ saha tasthivān  |
ārādhayadbhagavatīṃ cāmuṇḍāṃ muṇḍamardinīm  || 169.11 ||
stotrairanekairbhaktyā ca pūjāvidhisamādhinā  |
jaya vārāhi cāmuṇḍe jaya devi trilocane  || 169.12 ||
brāhmi raudri ca kaumāri kātyāyani namo'stu te  |
pracaṇḍe bhairave raudri yoginyākāśagāmini  || 169.13 ||
nāsti kiṃcittvayā hīnaṃ trailokye sacarācare  |
rājñā stutā ca saṃtuṣṭā devī vacanamabravīt || 169.14 ||
varayasva yathākāmaṃ yaste manasi vartate  |
ārādhitā tvayā bhaktyā tuṣṭā dāsyāmi te varam  || 169.15 ||

devapanna uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭāsi deveśi varārho yadi vāpyaham  |
putrasantānarahitaṃ saṃtaptaṃ māṃ samuddhara  || 169.16 ||
santānaṃ naya me vṛddhiṃ gotrarakṣāṃ kuruṣva me  |
aputriṇāṃ gṛhāṇīha śmaśānasadṛśāni hi  || 169.17 ||
pitarastasya nāśnanti devatā ṛṣibhiḥ saha  |
kriyamāṇe'pyaharahaḥ śrāddhe matpitaraḥ sadā  || 169.18 ||
darśayanti sadātmānaṃ svapne kṣutpīḍitaṃ mama  |
iti rājño vacaḥ śrutvā devī dhyānamupāgatā  || 169.19 ||
divyena cakṣuṣā dṛṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
prasannavadanā devī rājānamidamabravīt || 169.20 ||
santānaṃ nāsti te rājaṃstrailokye sacarācare  |
yajasva yajñapuruṣamapatyaṃ nāsti te'nyathā  || 169.21 ||
mayā dṛṣṭaṃ mahīpāla trailokyaṃ divyacakṣuṣā  |
evamuktvā gatā devī rājā svagṛhamāgamat || 169.22 ||
iyāja yajñapuruṣaṃ saṃjātā kanyakā tataḥ  |
tejasvinī rūpavatī sarvalokamanoharā  || 169.23 ||
devagandharvaloke'pi tādṛśī nāsti kāminī  |
tasyā nāma kṛtaṃ pitrā harṣātkāmapramodinī  || 169.24 ||
tataḥ kālena vavṛdhe rūpeṇāstambhayajjagat |
haṃsalīlāgatiḥ subhrūḥ stanabhārāvanāmitā  || 169.25 ||
raktamālyāmbaradharā kuṇḍalābharaṇojjvalā  |
divyānulepanavatī sakhībhiḥ sā surakṣitā  || 169.26 ||
kucamadhyagato hāro vidyunmāleva rājate  |
bhramarāñcitakeśī sā bimboṣṭhī cāruhāsinī  || 169.27 ||
karṇāntaprāptanetrābhyāṃ pibantīvātha kāminaḥ  |
candratāmbūlasaurabhyairākarṣantīva manmatham  || 169.28 ||
kambugrīvā cārumadhyā tāmrapādāṅgulīnakhā  |
nimnanābhiḥ sujaghanā rambhorū sudatī śubhā  || 169.29 ||
mātāpitṛsuhṛdvarge krīḍānandavivardhinī  |
ekasmindivase bālā sakhīvṛndasamanvitā  || 169.30 ||
candanāgarutāṃbūladhūpasaumanasāñcitā  |
gṛhītvā puṣpadhūpādi gatā devīprapūjane  || 169.31 ||
taḍāgataṭa utsṛjya bhūṣaṇānyaṅgaveṣṭakān  |
cakruḥ sarasitāḥ krīḍāṃ jalamadhyagatāstadā  || 169.32 ||
krīḍantīṃ tāmavekṣyātha sasakhīṃ vimale jale  |
rākṣasaḥ śambaro nāma śyenarūpeṇa cāgamat || 169.33 ||
gṛhītā jalamadhyasthā tena sā kāmamodinī  |
khamutpapāta duṣṭātmā gṛhītvābharaṇānyapi  || 169.34 ||
vāyumārgaṃ gataḥ so'tha kāminyā saha bhārata  |
apatankuṇḍalādīni yatra toye mahāmuniḥ  || 169.35 ||
māṇḍavyo narmadātīre kāṣṭhavatsaṃjitendriyaḥ  |
līno māheśvare sthāne nārāyaṇapade pare  || 169.36 ||
tasya cānucaro bhrātā bhrātuḥ śuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ  |
tapojapakṛśībhūto dadhyau devaṃ janārdanam  || 169.37 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kāmamodinīharaṇavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonasaptatyadhiśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 170

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kāmapramodinīsakhyo nīyamānāṃ ca tena tu  |
dṛṣṭvā tāścukruśuḥ sarvā niḥsṛtya jalamadhyataḥ  || 170.1 ||
gatā rājagṛhe sarvāḥ kathayanti suduḥkhitāḥ  |
kāmapramodinī rājanhṛtā śyenena pakṣiṇā  || 170.2 ||
krīḍantī ca jalasthāne taḍāge devasannidhau  |
anveṣyā ca tvayā rājaṃstasya mārgaṃ vijānatā  || 170.3 ||
tāsāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devapannaḥ suduḥkhitaḥ  |
hāhetyuktvā samutthāya rudamāno varāsanāt || 170.4 ||
mantribhiḥ sahitastasmiṃstaḍāge jalasannidhau  |
na cihnaṃ na ca panthānaṃ dṛṣṭvā duḥkhānmumoha ca  || 170.5 ||
tasya rājñastu duḥkhena duḥkhito nāgaro janaḥ  |
kṣaṇenāśvāsito rājā mantribhiḥ sapurohitaiḥ  || 170.6 ||
kiṃ kurma ityuvācedamasminkāle vidhīyatām  |
sarvaistatsaṃvidaṃ kṛtvā vāhinīṃ caturaṅgiṇīm  || 170.7 ||
preṣayāmi diśaḥ sarvā hastyaśvarathasaṃkulā  |
vāditrāṇi ca vādyante vyākulībhūtasaṃkule  || 170.8 ||
nārācaistomarairbhallaiḥ khaḍgaiḥ paraśvadhādibhiḥ  |
rājā saṃnāhabaddho'bhūdganaṃ grasate kila  || 170.9 ||
na devo na ca gandharvo na daityo na ca rākṣasaḥ  |
kiṃ kariṣyati rājādya na jāne roṣaniṣkṛtim  || 170.10 ||
nāgaro'pi janastatra dṛṣṭvā cakitamānasaḥ  |
caturdaśasahasrāṇi dantināṃ sṛṇidhāriṇām  || 170.11 ||
aśvārohasahasrāṇi hyaśītiḥ śastrapāṇinām  |
rathānāṃ trisahasrāṇi viṃśatirbharatarṣabha  || 170.12 ||
saṅgrāmabherīninadaiḥ khurareṇurnabhogatā  |
etasminnantare tāta rakṣako nagarasya hi  || 170.13 ||
gṛhītvābharaṇaṃ tasyāstvaṅgapratyaṅgikaṃ tathā  |
kuṇḍalāṅgadakeyūrahāranūpurajhallarīḥ  || 170.14 ||
nivedyākathayadrājñe mayā dṛṣṭaṃ tvavekṣaṇāt |
tāpasānāmāśrame tu māṇḍavyo yatra tiṣṭhati  || 170.15 ||
tāpasairveṣṭito yatra dadṛśe tatra sannidhau  |
daṇḍavāsivacaḥ śrutvā pratyakṣāṅgavibhūṣaṇam  || 170.16 ||
sa krodharaktanayano mantriṇo vīkṣya naigamān  |
īdṛgbhūtasamācāro brāhmaṇo nagare mama  || 170.17 ||
cauracaryāṃ vratacchannaḥ paradravyāpaharakaḥ  |
tena kanyā hṛtā me'dya tapasvipāpakarmiṇā  || 170.18 ||
śākuntaṃ rūpamāsthāya jalastho gaganaṃ yayau  |
pākhaṇḍino vikarmasthān biḍālavratikāñchaṭhān  || 170.19 ||
cāṭutaskaradurvṛttān hanyānnastyasya pātakam  |
na draṣṭavyo mayā pāpaḥ steyī kanyāpahārakaḥ  || 170.20 ||
śūlamāropyatāṃ kṣipraṃ na vicārastu tasya vai  |
sa ca vadhyo mayā duṣṭo rakṣorūpī tapodhanaḥ  || 170.21 ||
evaṃ bruvaṃścalankrodhādādiśya daṇḍavāsinam  |
kāryākāryaṃ na vijñāya śūlamāropayaddvijam  || 170.22 ||
paurā jānapadāḥ sarve aśrupūrṇamukhāstadā  |
hāhetyuktvā rudantyanye vadanti ca pṛthakpṛthak || 170.23 ||
kutsitaṃ ca kṛtaṃ karma rājñā caṇḍālacāriṇā  |
brāhmaṇo naiva vadhyo hi viśeṣeṇa tapovṛtaḥ  || 170.24 ||
yadi roṣasamācāro nirvāsyo nagarādbahiḥ  |
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyātsarvapāpe'pyavasthitam  || 170.25 ||
rāṣṭrādenaṃ bahiṣkuryātsamagradhanamakṣatam  |
nāśnāti ca gṛhe rājannāgnirnagaravāsinām  |
sarve'pyudvignamanaso gṛhavyāptivivarjitāḥ  || 170.26 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe māṇḍavyaśūlāropaṇavarṇanaṃ nāma saptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 171

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
kathitaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ draṣṭuṃ śūle kṣiptaṃ tapodhanaiḥ  |
nārāyaṇasamīpe tu gatāḥ sarve maharṣayaḥ  || 171.1 ||
nārado devalo raibhyo yamaḥ śātātapo'ṅgirāḥ  |
vasiṣṭho jamadagniśca yājñavalkyo bṛhaspatiḥ  || 171.2 ||
kaśyapo'trirbharadvājo viśvāmitro'ruṇirmuniḥ  |
vālakhilyādayo'nye ca sarve'pyṛṣigaṇānvayāḥ  || 171.3 ||
dadṛśuḥ śūlamārūḍhaṃ māṇḍavyamṛṣipuṃgavāḥ  |
procurnārāyaṇaṃ vipraṃ kiṃ kurmastava cepsitam  || 171.4 ||
sarve te tatra sāṃnidhyānmāṇḍavyasya mahātmanaḥ  |
saṃbhrāntā āgatā ūcuḥ kiṃ mṛtaḥ kiṃ nu jīvati  || 171.5 ||
avasthāṃ tasya te dṛṣṭvā viṣādamagamanparam  |
asahitvā tu tadduḥkhaṃ sarve te manasā dvijāḥ  || 171.6 ||
pṛcchayatāṃ yadi manyeta rājānaṃ bhasmasātkuru  |
teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vākyaṃ nārāyaṇo'bravīt || 171.7 ||
mayi jīvati madbhrātā hyavasthāmīdṛśīṃ gataḥ  |
dhigjīvitaṃ ca me kiṃtu tapaso vidyate phalam  || 171.8 ||
dṛṣṭvā śūlasthitaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ manmano nu vidīryate  |
paraṃ kiṃ tu kariṣyāmi yena rāṣṭraṃ sarājakam  || 171.9 ||
bhasmasācca karomyadya bhavadbhiḥ kṣamyatāmiha  |
evamuktvā gṛhītvāsau karasthamabhimantrayet || 171.10 ||
krodhena paśyate yāvattāvaddhuṃkārako'bhavat |
tena huṅkāraśabdena ṛṣayo vismitāstadā  || 171.11 ||
māṇḍavyasya samīpe tu hyapṛcchaṃste dvijottamāḥ  |
nivārayasi kiṃ vipra śāpaṃ nṛpajighāṃsanam  || 171.12 ||
apāpasya tu yeneha kṛtamasya jighāṃsanam  |
ṛṣīṇāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā kṛcchrānmāṇḍavyako'bravīt || 171.13 ||
abhivandāmi vo mūrdhnā svāgataṃ ṛṣayaḥ sadā  |
arghyasanmānapūjārhāḥ sarve'tropaviśantu te  || 171.14 ||
niviṣṭaikāgramanasā sarvānmāṇḍavyako'bravīt || 171.15 ||
prāptaṃ duḥkhaṃ mayā ghoraṃ pūrvajanmārjitaṃ phalam  |
mā viṣādaṃ kurudhvaṃ bhoḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tu bhujyate  || 171.16 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
kena karmavipākena iha jātyantaraṃ vrajet |
dānadharmaphalenaiva kena svargaṃ ca gacchati  || 171.17 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
adattadānā jāyante parabhāgyopajīvinaḥ  |
na snānaṃ na japo homo nātithyaṃ na surārcanam  || 171.18 ||
na parvaṇi pitṛśrāddhaṃ na dānaṃ dvijasattamāḥ  |
vrajanti narake ghore yānti te tvantyajāṃ gatim  || 171.19 ||
punardaridrāḥ punareva pāpāḥ pāpaprabhāvānnarake vasanti  |
tenaiva saṃsariṇi martyaloke jīvādibhūte kṛmayaḥ pataṅgāḥ  || 171.20 ||
ye snānaśīlā dvijadevabhaktā jitendriyā jīvadayānuśīlāḥ  |
te devalokeṣu vasanti hṛṣṭā ye dharmaśīlā jitamānaroṣāḥ  || 171.21 ||
vidyāvinītā na paropatāpinaḥ svadāratuṣṭāḥ paradāravarjitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ na loke bhayamasti kiṃcitsvabhāvaśuddhā gatakalmaṣā hi te  || 171.22 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
pūrvajanmani viprendra kiṃ tvayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam  |
yena kaṣṭamidaṃ prāptaṃ sandhānaṃ śūlagarhitam  || 171.23 ||
śūlasthaṃ tvāṃ samālakṣya hyāgatāḥ sarva eva hi  |
jīvantaṃ tvāṃ prapaśyāma tvantarannavatārayan  |
rujāsaṃtāpajaṃ duḥkhaṃ soḍhvāpi tvamavedanaḥ  || 171.24 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
svayameva kṛtaṃ karma svayamevopabhujyate  |
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ pūrve nānye bhuñjanti karhicit || 171.25 ||
yathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram  |
tathā pūrvakṛtaṃ karma kartāramupagacchati  || 171.26 ||
na mātā na pitā bhrātā na bhāryā na sutāḥ suhṛt |
na kasya karmaṇāṃ lepaḥ svayamevopabhujyate  || 171.27 ||
śrūyatāṃ mama vākyaṃ ca bhavadbhiḥ pṛcchito hyaham  |
pūrve vayasi bho viprā malasnānakṛtakṣaṇaḥ  || 171.28 ||
ajñānādbālabhāvena yūkā kaṇṭe'dhiropitā  |
tailābhyaktaśirogātre mayā yūkā ghṛtā na hi  || 171.29 ||
kaṅkatīṃ ropya keśeṣu sāsā kaṇṭe'dhiropitā  |
teṣu pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ sadyaḥ phalametanmamābhavat || 171.30 ||
kiṃcitkālaṃ kṣapitvāhaṃ prāpsye mokṣaṃ nirāmayam  |
bhavantastviha santāpaṃ māṃ kurudhvaṃ maharṣayaḥ  || 171.31 ||
imāmavasthāṃ bhuktvāhaṃ kaṃcicchape na coccare  |
ahani katicicchūle kṣapayiṣyāmi kilbiṣam  || 171.32 ||
prāktanaṃ karma bhuñjāmi yanmayā saṃcitaṃ dvijāḥ  |
kṣantavyamasya rājño'tha kopaścaiva visarjyatām  || 171.33 ||
śrutvā tu tasya tadvākyaṃ māṇḍavyasya maharṣayaḥ  |
praharṣamatulaṃ labdhvā sādhu sādhvityapūjayan  || 171.34 ||

nārāyaṇa uvāca -
idaṃ jalaṃ mantrapūtaṃ kasminsthāne kṣipāmyaham  |
yena rājā bhavedbhasma sarāṣṭraḥ sapurohitaḥ  || 171.35 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
idaṃ jalaṃ ca rakṣasva kālakūṭaviṣopamam  |
samudre kṣipayiṣyāmi devakāryaṃ samutthitam  || 171.36 ||
atha te munayaḥ sarve māṇḍavyaṃ praṇipatya ca  |
āmantrayitvā harṣācca kaśyapādyā gṛhānyayuḥ  || 171.37 ||
gacchamānāstu te coktāḥ pañcame'hani tāpasāḥ  |
āgantavyaṃ bhavadbhiśca matsakāśaṃ pratijñayā  || 171.38 ||
tatheti te pratijñāya nāradādyā adarśanam  |
gateṣu vipramukhyeṣu śāṇḍilī ca tapodhanā  || 171.39 ||
dvitīye'hni samāyātā na tu buddhvātha taṃ ṛṣim  |
bhartāraṃ śirasā dhārya rātrau paryaṭate sma sā  || 171.40 ||
na dṛṣṭaḥ śūlake vipro bharākrāntyā yudhiṣṭhira  |
skhalitā tasya jānubhyāṃ śūlasthasya pativratā  || 171.41 ||
sarvāṅgeṣu vyathā jātā tasyāḥ praskhalanānmuneḥ  |
īdṛśīṃ vartamānāṃ ca hyavasthāṃ pūrvadaivikīm  || 171.42 ||
punaḥ pāpaphalaṃ kiṃciddhā kaṣṭaṃ mama vartate  |
vyathito'haṃ tvayā pāpe kimarthaṃ sūnakarmaṇi  || 171.43 ||
svairiṇīṃ tvāṃ prapaśyāmi rākṣasī taskarī nu kim  |
evamuktvā kṣaṇaṃ mohātkrandamāno muhurmuhuḥ  || 171.44 ||
tapasvino'tha ṛṣayaḥ sarve saṃtrastamānasāḥ  |
paśyamānā muneḥ kaṣṭaṃ pṛcchante te yudhiṣṭhira  || 171.45 ||
paryaṭase kimarthaṃ tvaṃ niśīye vahanaṃ nu kim  |
kṣiptaṃ tu jholikābhāraṃ kiṃvāgamanakāraṇam  |
vyathāmutpādya ṛṣaye duḥkhādduḥkhavilāsini  || 171.46 ||

śāṇḍilyuvāca -
nāsurīṃ na ca gandharvīṃ na piśācīṃ na rākṣasīm  |
pativratāṃ tu māṃ sarve jānantu tapasi sthitām  || 171.47 ||
na me kāmo na me krodho na vairaṃ na ca matsaraḥ  |
ajñānāddṛṣṭimāndyācca skhalanaṃ kṣantumarhatha  || 171.48 ||
vahanaṃ bhartṛsaukhyāya divā sampīḍyate rujā  |
ayaṃ bhartā vijānītha jholikāsaṃsthitaḥ sadā  || 171.49 ||
bharaṇaṃ pānaṃ vastraṃ ca dadāmyetasya rogiṇaḥ  |
ṛṣiḥ śaunakamukhyo'sau śāṇḍilīṃ māṃ vijānata  || 171.50 ||
svabhartṛdharmiṇīṃ kopaṃ mā kuruṣvātithiṃ kuru  |
satāṃ samīpaṃ samprāptāṃ sarvaṃ me kṣantumarhatha  || 171.51 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
paravyathāṃ na jānīṣe vyacarantī yadṛcchayā  |
prabhāte'bhyudite sūrye tava bhartā mariṣyati  || 171.52 ||
ātmaduḥkhātparaṃ duḥkhaṃ na jānāsi kulādhame  |
tena vākyena ghoreṇa śāṇḍilī vimanābhavat || 171.53 ||
paraṃ viṣādamāpannā kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvābravīdvacaḥ  |
kopātsaṃraktanayanā nirīkṣantī munīṃstadā  || 171.54 ||
satāṃ gehe kila prāptā bhavatāṃ cāpakāriṇī  |
sāmenātithipūjāyāṃ śiṣṭe ca gṛhamāgate  || 171.55 ||
bhavadbhirīdṛgātithyaṃ kṛtaṃ caiva mamaiva tu  |
svargāpavargadharmaśca bhavadbhirna nirīkṣitam  || 171.56 ||
prājāpatyāmimāṃ dṛṣṭvā māṃ yathā prākṛtāḥ striyaḥ  |
bhavantaḥ strībalaṃ me'dya paśyantu divi devatāḥ  || 171.57 ||
mariṣyati na me bhartā hyādityo nodayiṣyati  |
andhakāraṃ jagatsarvaṃ kṣīyate nādya śarvarī  || 171.58 ||
evamukte tayā vākye stambhite'rke tamomayam  |
na ca prajāyate sarvaṃ nirvaṣaṭkārasatkriyam  || 171.59 ||
svāhākāraḥ svadhākāraḥ pañcayajñavidhirnahi  |
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo nāsti sandhyālopavyatikramaḥ  |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ ca tadā pārtha luptapiṇḍodakakriyam  || 171.60 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śāṇḍilīṛṣisaṃvādavarṇanaṃ nāmaikasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 172

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
atha te ṛṣayaḥ sarve devāścendrapurogamāḥ  |
māṇḍavyasyāśrame puṇye samīyurnarmadātaṭe  || 172.1 ||
śaṅkhadundubhinādena dīpikājvalanena ca  |
apsarogītanādena nṛtyantyo vārayoṣitaḥ  || 172.2 ||
kathānakaiḥ stuvatyanye tasya śūlāgradhāriṇaḥ  |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi snātakānāṃ tapasvinām  || 172.3 ||
samāje tridaśaiḥ sārddhaṃ tatra te ca didṛkṣayā  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāstatra harṣātsamāgatāḥ  || 172.4 ||
mātaro mallikādyāśca kṣetrapālā vināyakāḥ  |
dikpālā lokapālāśca gaṅgādyāśca saridvarāḥ  || 172.5 ||
ṛṣidevasamāje tu nityaṃ harṣapramodane  |
tatra rājā samāyātaḥ paurajānapadaiḥ saha  || 172.6 ||
dṛṣṭvā kautūhalaṃ tatra vyākulīkṛtamānasam  |
vitrastamanaso bhūtvā bhayātsarve samāsthitāḥ  || 172.7 ||
tasminsamāgame divye brahmaviṣṇvīśamabruvan  |
bho māṇḍavya mahāsattva varadāste'maraiḥ saha  || 172.8 ||
anekakaṣṭatapasā tava siddhirbhaviṣyati  |
prārthayasva yathākāmaṃ yaste manasi rocate  || 172.9 ||
anādityamayaṃ lokaṃ nirvaṣaṭkāramākulam  |
naṣṭadharmaṃ vijānīhi prakṛtisthaṃ kuruṣva ca  |
anugrahaṃ tu śāṇḍilyāḥ prārthayāma dvijottama  || 172.10 ||
eṣa te kaṣṭado rājā samāyātastavāgrataḥ  |
saṃbhūṣayasva viprarṣe janaṃ devāsuraṃ gaṇam  || 172.11 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
yadi prasannā me devāḥ samāyātāḥ suraiḥ saha  |
trikālamatra tīrthe ca sthātavyamṛṣibhiḥ saha  || 172.12 ||
bhavatāṃ tu prasādena rujā me śāmyatāṃ sadā  |
evamastviti deveśā yāvajjalpanti pāṇḍava  || 172.13 ||
tāvadrakṣo gṛhītvā'gre kanyāṃ kāmapramodinīm  |
uvāca bhagavañchāpaṃ purā dattvorvaśī mama  || 172.14 ||
yadā kanyāṃ hare rakṣaḥśāpāntaste bhaviṣyati  |
tena me garhitaṃ karma śāpenākṛtabuddhinā  || 172.15 ||
kṣantavyamiti coktvā ca gataścādarśanaṃ punaḥ  |
gate caiva tu sā kanyā dṛṣṭvā padmadalekṣaṇā  || 172.16 ||
mantrayitvā suraiḥ sarvairdattā māṇḍavyadhīmate  |
tāṃ vajraśūlikāṃ plāvya pavitrairnarmadodakaiḥ  || 172.17 ||
māṇḍavyamṛṣimuttārya jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  |
vivāhayitvā tāṃ kanyāṃ māṇḍavyarṣipuṃgavaḥ  || 172.18 ||
abhivādya ca tān sarvān dānasanmānagauravaiḥ  |
atha rājā samīpastho ratnaiśca vividhairapi  || 172.19 ||
dhigvādairninditaḥ sarvaistairjanairbhūṣitaḥ punaḥ  |
rājñā ca brāhmaṇāḥ sarve bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ  || 172.20 ||
suvarṇakoṭidānena tuṣṭānkṛtvā kṣamāpitāḥ  |
vṛtte vivāha āhūya śāṇḍilīṃ tāmathābravīt || 172.21 ||
mānayasva imān viprānmocayasva divākaram  |
apahṛtya tamo yena kṛpā sadyaḥ pravartate  || 172.22 ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā śāṇḍilī duḥkhitābravīt |
udite'rke tu me bhartā mṛtyuṃ yāsyati bho dvijāḥ  || 172.23 ||
taṃ kathaṃ mocayāmīha hyātmano'niṣṭasiddhaye  |
kriyāpravartanāccādya kiṃ kāryaṃ me maharṣayaḥ  || 172.24 ||
niḥpuṃsī strī hyanāthāhaṃ bhavāmi bhavato matam  |
tiṣṭha tvamandhakāre tu necchāmi raviṇodayam  || 172.25 ||
tena vākyena te sarve devāsuramaharṣayaḥ  |
śiraḥsaṃcālanāḥ sarve sādhu sādhviti cābruvan  || 172.26 ||
pativrate mahābhāge śṛṇu vākyaṃ tapodhane  |
manyase yadi naḥ sarvānkuruṣva vacanaṃ ca yat || 172.27 ||

śāṇḍilyuvāca -
yena me na maredbhartā yena satyaṃ munervacaḥ  |
tatkurudhvaṃ vicāryāśu yena saṃvardhate sukham  || 172.28 ||
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā svapnāvasthākṛto hṛṣiḥ  |
antarhito muhūrtaṃ ca śāṇḍilyāśca prapaśya tām  || 172.29 ||
punarādāya te sarve kṛtvā nirvraṇasattanuṃ snāpito narmadātoye śāṇḍilyāyai samarpitaḥ  || 172.30 ||
tataḥ sā hṛṣṭamanasā patiṃ dṛṣṭvā tu taijasam  |
praṇamya tānṛṣīn devān vimalārkaṃ jagatkṛtam  || 172.31 ||
kriyāpravartitāḥ sarve devagandharvamānuṣāḥ  |
hṛṣṭatuṣṭā gatāḥ sarve svamāśramapadaṃ mahat || 172.32 ||
pativratā svabhartrā sā māsamevāśrame sthitā  |
māṇḍavyenāpyanujñātā yayau natvā svamāśramam  || 172.33 ||
gateṣu teṣu sarveṣu sthāpayāmāsa cācyutam  |
māṇḍavyeśvaranāmānaṃ nārāyaṇa iti smṛtam  || 172.34 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu pūjayāmāsa bhārata  |
gato'sāvṛṣisaṅghaiśca sahito'maraparvatam  || 172.35 ||
tapastapantau tau tatra hyadyāpi kila bhārata  |
bhrātarau saṃyatātmānau dhyāyataḥ paramaṃ padam  || 172.36 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pitarastasya tṛpyanti piṇḍadānāddaśābdikam  || 172.37 ||
devagṛhe tu pakṣādau yaḥ karoti vilepanam  |
godānaśatasāhasre datte bhavati yatphalam  || 172.38 ||
upalepanena dviguṇamarcane tu caturguṇam  |
dīpaprajvalane puṇyamaṣṭadhā parikīrtitam  || 172.39 ||
divyanetradharo bhūtvā trailokye sacarācare  |
dadhnā madhughṛtairdevaṃ payasā narmadodakaiḥ  || 172.40 ||
snapanaṃ ye prakurvanti puṣpamālāvilepanaiḥ  |
ye'rcayanti virūpākṣaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ harim  || 172.41 ||
te'pi divyavimānena krīḍante kalpasaṃkhyayā  |
dīpāṣṭakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādaṣṭamīṃ ca caturdaśīm  || 172.42 ||
ekādaśyāṃ tu kṛṣṇasya na paśyanti yamaṃ tu te  |
phalairnānāvidhaiḥ śubhrairyaḥ kuryālliṅgapūraṇam  || 172.43 ||
te'pi yānti vimānena siddhacāraṇasevitāḥ  |
ghaṇṭā caiva patākā ca vimāne puṣpamālikā  || 172.44 ||
vāditrāṇi yathārhāṇi prānte ca gacchate śivam  |
devālayaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādvaiṣṇavaṃ māṇḍaveśvaram  || 172.45 ||
svarge vasati dharmātmā yāvadābhūtasamplavam  |
māṇḍavyanārāyaṇākhye viprān bhojayate'grataḥ  || 172.46 ||
ekasmin bhojite vipre koṭirbhavati bhojitā  |
āśvine māsi samprāpte śuklapakṣe caturdaśīm  || 172.47 ||
kṛtopavāsaniyamo rātrau jāgaraṇena ca  |
dīpamālāṃ caturdikṣu pūjāṃ kṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ  || 172.48 ||
nārī vā puruṣo vāpi nṛtyagītapravādanaiḥ  |
prabhāte vimale sūrye snānādikavidhiṃ nṛpa  || 172.49 ||
abhinirvartya maunena paśyate devamīdṛśam  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudraloke mahīyate  || 172.50 ||
athavā mārgaśīrṣe ca caitravaiśākhayorapi  |
śrāvaṇe vā mahārāja sarvakāle'thavāpi ca  || 172.51 ||
śivarātrisamaṃ puṇyamityevaṃ śivabhāṣitam  |
vājapeyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ bhavati nānyathā  || 172.52 ||
durbhagā duḥkhitā vandhyā daridrā ca mṛtaprajā  |
snāti rudraghaṭairyā strī sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 172.53 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgāśca tasmiṃstīrthe tu ye mṛtāḥ  |
svargaṃ prayānti te sarve divyarūpadharā nṛpa  || 172.54 ||
anāśake jale'gnau tu ye mṛtā vyādhipīḍitāḥ  |
anivartikā gatisteṣāṃ rudraloke hyasaṃśayam  || 172.55 ||
nityaṃ namati yo rāja śivanārāyaṇāvubhau  |
godānaphalamāpnoti tasya tīrthaprabhāvataḥ  || 172.56 ||
devālaye tu rājendra yaśca kuryātpradakṣiṇām  |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena sasāgaradharā dharā  || 172.57 ||
sārddhaṃ śataṃ ca tīrthāni mallikābhavanādbahiḥ  |
tasya tīrthapramāṇaṃ tu vistaraṃ rājasattama  || 172.58 ||
sūtreṇa veṣṭayetkṣetramathavā śivamandiram  |
athavā śivaliṅgaṃ ca tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 172.59 ||
jambūdvīpaśca kṛtasnaśca śālmalī kuśakrauñcakau  |
śākapuṣkaragomedaiḥ saptadvīpā vasuṃdharā  || 172.60 ||
bhūṣitā tena rājendra saśailavanakānanā  |
revāyāṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge śivakṣetrātsamīpataḥ  || 172.61 ||
devakhātaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ nirmitaṃ tridaśairapi  |
tasmin yaḥ kurute snānaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 172.62 ||
pūrṇimāyāmamāvasyāṃ vyatīpāte'rkasaṃkrame  |
śrāddhaṃ ca saṃgrahe kuryātsa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  || 172.63 ||
devakhāte trayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  |
tiṣṭhanti ṛṣibhiḥ sārddhaṃ pitṛdevagaṇaiḥ saha  || 172.64 ||
tatra tīrthe'śvine māsi caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
vāyumārge sthitaḥ śakrastiṣṭhate daivataiḥ saha  || 172.65 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni saritaḥ sāgarāstathā  |
viṃśati tāni sarvāṇi devakhāte dinadvayam  || 172.66 ||
gayāśire ca yatpuṇyaṃ prayāge makarakaṇṭake  |
prayāge somatīrthe ca tatpuṇyaṃ māṇḍaveśvare  || 172.67 ||
paṭṭabandhena yatpuṇyaṃ mātrāyāṃ lakuleśvare  |
āśvinyāmaśvinīyoge tatpuṇyaṃ māṇḍaveśvare  || 172.68 ||
ujjayinyāṃ mahākāle vārāṇasyāṃ tripuṣkare  |
saṃnihatyāṃ ravigraste māṇḍavyākhye sanātanam  || 172.69 ||
iti jñātvā mahārāja sarvatīrtheṣu cottamam  |
pitḥndevān samabhyarcya snānadānādipūjanaiḥ  || 172.70 ||
caturdaśyāṃ nirāhāraḥ sthito bhūtvā śucivrataḥ  |
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā rātrau jāgaraṇe śivam  || 172.71 ||
snānaiśca vividhairdevaṃ puṣpāgaruvilepanaiḥ  |
prabhāte paurṇamāsyāṃ tu snānādividhitarpaṇaiḥ  || 172.72 ||
śrāddhena havyakavyena śivapūjārcanena ca  |
agniṣṭomādiyajñaiśca vidhivaccāptadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 172.73 ||
dhautapāpo viśuddhātmā phalate phalamuttamam  |
gosahasrapradānena dattaṃ bhavati bhārata  || 172.74 ||
snānādyairvidhivattatra taddine śivasannidhau  |
hiraṇyaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ dhenuṃ bhūmiṃ gomithunaṃ hayam  || 172.75 ||
śivamuddiśya vai vastrayugme dadyātsurūpiṇe  |
pādukopānahau chatraṃ bhājanaṃ raktavāsasī  || 172.76 ||
homaṃ jāpyaṃ tathā dānamakṣayaṃ sarvameva tat |
ṛcamekāṃ tu ṛgvede yajurvede yajustathā  || 172.77 ||
sāmaikaṃ sāmavede tu japeddevāgrasaṃsthitaḥ  |
samyagvedaphalaṃ tasya bhavedvai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 172.78 ||
gāyatrījāpyamātrastu vedatrayaphalaṃ labhet |
kulakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ labhate tu śivārcanāt || 172.79 ||
snāne dāne tathā śrāddhe jāgare gītavādite  |
anivartikā gatistasya śivalokātkadācana  || 172.80 ||
kālena mahatāviṣṭo martyaloke samāviśet |
rājā bhavati medhāvī sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ  || 172.81 ||
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ putrapautradhanānvitaḥ  |
tacca tīrthaṃ punaḥ smṛtvā līyamāno maheśvare  || 172.82 ||
upāste yastu vai sandhyāṃ tasmiṃstīrthe ca parvaṇi  |
sāṅgopāṅgaiścaturvedairlabhate phalamuttamam  || 172.83 ||
tatra sarvaṃ śivakṣetrāccharapātaṃ samantataḥ  |
na saṃcaredbhayodvignā brahmahatyā narādhipa  || 172.84 ||
yatra tatra sthito vṛkṣān paśyate tīrthatatparaḥ  |
vividhaiḥ pātakairmukto mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 172.85 ||
śvabhrī tatra mahārāja jalamadhye pradṛśyate  |
kathānikā purāṇoktā vānarī tīrthasevanāt || 172.86 ||
tatra kūpo mahārāja tiṣṭhate devanirmitaḥ  |
śivasya paścime bhāge śivakṣetramanuttamam  || 172.87 ||
vṛṣotsargaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa  |
krīḍanti pitarastasya svargaloke yadṛcchayā  || 172.88 ||
agamyāgamane pāpamayājyayājane kṛte  |
steyācca brahmagohatyāgurughātācca pātakam  |
tatsarvaṃ naśyate pāpaṃ vṛṣotsarge kṛte tu vai  || 172.89 ||
māṇḍavyatīrthamāhātmyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti samādhinā  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 172.90 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye māṇḍavyatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvisaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 173

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 173.1 ||
siddheśvaramiti khyātaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  |
yatra śuddhiṃ parāṃ prāpto devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
purā hatyāyutaḥ pārtha devadevastriśūladhṛk || 173.2 ||
purā pañcaśirā āsīdbrahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
tenānṛtaṃ vacaścoktaṃ kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  || 173.3 ||
tacchrutvā sahasā tasmai cukopa parameśvaraḥ  |
chedayāmāsa bhagavānmūrdhānaṃ karajaistadā  || 173.4 ||
tasya tatkarasaṃlagnaṃ cyavate na kadācana  |
tato hi devadeveśaḥ paryaṭan pṛthivīmimām  || 173.5 ||
tato vārāṇasīṃ prāptastasyāṃ tadapatacchiraḥ  |
patite tu kapāle ca brahmahatyā na muñcati  || 173.6 ||
tatastu sāgare gatvā pūrve ca dakṣiṇe tathā  |
paścime cottare pārtha devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 173.7 ||
paryaṭansarvatīrtheṣu brahmahatyā na muñcati  |
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle sutīrthaṃ prāptavān prabhuḥ  || 173.8 ||
kulakoṭiṃ samāsādya prārthayāmāsa cātmavān  |
prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā babhūva gatakalmaṣaḥ  || 173.9 ||
tato niṣkalmaṣo jāto devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
hatvā surebhyastatsthānaṃ tataścāntardadhe prabhuḥ  || 173.10 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ śuddharudreti kīrtitam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu loke brahmahatyāharaṃ param  || 173.11 ||
māse māse site pakṣe'māvāsyāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
snātvā tatra vidhānena tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 173.12 ||
dadyātpiṇḍaṃ pitḥṇāṃ tu bhāvitenāntarātmanā  |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni sutṛptāḥ pitaro nṛpa  || 173.13 ||
gandhadhūpapradīpādyairabhyarcya parameśvaram  |
śuddheśvarābhidhānaṃ tu śivaloke mahīyate  || 173.14 ||
etatte kathitaṃ rājañchuddharudramanuttamam  |
mayā śrutaṃ yathā devasakāśācchūlapāṇinaḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 173.15 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śuddheśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trisaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 174

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gopeśvaraṃ tato gaccheduttare narmadātaṭe  |
yatra snānena caikena mucyante pātakairnarāḥ  || 174.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā kurute prāṇasaṃkṣayam  |
barhiyuktena yānena sa gacchecchivamandire  || 174.2 ||
krīḍitvā suciraṃ kālaṃ śivaloke narādhipa  |
iha mānuṣyatāṃ prāpya rājā bhavati vīryavān  || 174.3 ||
hastyaśvarathasampanno dāsīdāsasamanvitaḥ  |
pūjyamāno narendraiśca jīvedvarṣaśataṃ naraḥ  || 174.4 ||
samprāpte kārttike māsi navamyāṃ śuklapakṣataḥ  |
sopavāsaḥ śucirbhūtvā dīpakāṃstatra dāpayet || 174.5 ||
gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya rātrau kurvīta jāgaram  |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 174.6 ||
yāvatpuṇyaṃ phalaṃ saṃkhyā dīpakānāṃ tathaiva ca  |
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  || 174.7 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra liṅgapūraṇakaṃ vidhim  |
tathaiva padmakaiścaiva dadhibhaktaistathaiva ca  || 174.8 ||
yastu kuryānnaraśreṣṭha tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
yāvanti tilasaṃkhyāni dadhibhaktaṃ tathaiva ca  || 174.9 ||
padmasaṃkhyā śive loke modate kālamīpsitam  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra yatkiṃciddīyate nṛpa  || 174.10 ||
sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ tasya saṃkhyātuṃ vā na śakyate  |
evaṃ te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ sarvatīrthamanuttamam  || 174.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gopeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 175

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
uttare narmadākūle bhṛgukṣetrasya madhyataḥ  |
kapileśvaraṃ tu vikhyātaṃ viśeṣātpāpanāśanam  || 175.1 ||
yo'sau sanātano devaḥ purāṇe paripaṭhyate  |
vāsudevo jagannāthaḥ kapilatvamupāgataḥ  || 175.2 ||
pātālaṃ sutalaṃ nāma tasyaiva nitalaṃ hyadhaḥ  |
gabhastigaṃ ca tasyādho hyandhatāmisrameva ca  || 175.3 ||
pātālaṃ saptamaṃ yacca hyadhastātsaṃsthitaṃ mahat |
vasate tatra vai devaḥ purāṇaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 175.4 ||
sa brahmā sa mahādevaḥ sa devo garuḍadhvajaḥ  |
pūjyamānaḥ suraiḥ siddhaistiṣṭhate brahmavādibhiḥ  || 175.5 ||
vasatastasya rājendra kapilasya jagadguroḥ  |
vināśaṃ cāgrataḥ prāptāḥ kṣaṇena sagarātmajāḥ  || 175.6 ||
bhasmībhūtāṃstu tāndṛṣṭvā kapilo munisattamaḥ  |
jagāma paramaṃ śokaṃ cintyamāno'tha kilbiṣam  || 175.7 ||
sarvasaṅgaparityāge citte nirviṣayīkṛte  |
ayuktaṃ ṣaṣṭisahasrāṇāṃ kartaṃ mama vināśanam  || 175.8 ||
kṛtasya karaṇaṃ nāsti tasmātpāpavināśanam  |
gatvā tu kāpilaṃ tīrthaṃ mocayāmyaghamātmanaḥ  || 175.9 ||
pātālaṃ tu tato muktvā kapilo munisattamaḥ  |
tapaścacāra sumahannarmadātaṭamāsthitaḥ  || 175.10 ||
vratopavāsairvividhaiḥ snānadānajapādikaiḥ  |
paraṃ nirvāṇamāpannaḥ pūjayanrudramavyayam  || 175.11 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetparameśvaram  |
gosahasraphalaṃ tasya labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 175.12 ||
jyeṣṭhamāse tu samprāpte śuklapakṣe caturdaśī  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena bhaktyā dānaṃ prayacchati  || 175.13 ||
pātrabhūtāya viprāya svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu  |
akṣayaṃ tatphalaṃ proktaṃ śivena parameṣṭhinā  || 175.14 ||
aṅgārakadine prāpte caturthyāṃ navamīṣu ca  |
snānaṃ karoti puruṣo bhaktyopoṣya varāṅganā  || 175.15 ||
rūpamaiśvaryamatulaṃ saubhāgyaṃ saṃtatiṃ parām  |
labhate saptajanmāni nityaṃ nityaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 175.16 ||
paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāṃ snātvā piṇḍaṃ prayacchati  |
tasya te dvādaśābdāni tṛptā yānti surālayam  || 175.17 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā dadyāddīpaṃ suśobhanam  |
jāyate tasya rājendra mahādīptiḥ śārīrajā  || 175.18 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu jantūnāṃ sarvadā kila  |
anivartikā bhavetteṣāṃ gatistu śivamandirāt || 175.19 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kapileśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcasaptādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 176

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla piṅgalāvartamuttamam  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ kāmikaṃ bhuvi durlabham  || 176.1 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurā kṛtam  |
piṅgaleśvaramāsādya tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ vrajet || 176.2 ||
tatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca devakhāte kṛtaṃ nṛpa  |
akṣayaṃ tadbhavetsarvamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 176.3 ||
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrtheṣu samuddhṛtya śubhodakam  |
muktaṃ tatra suraiḥ khātvā devakhātaṃ tato'bhavat || 176.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ tu devakhātaṃ tatsaṃjātaṃ dvijasattama  |
surāḥ sarve kathaṃ tatra mumucurvāri tīrthajam  |
sarvaṃ kathaya me vipra śravaṇe lampaṭaṃ manaḥ  || 176.5 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
yadā tu śūlaśuddhyarthaṃ rudro devagaṇaiḥ saha  |
babhrāma pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ kamaṇḍaludharaḥ śubhām  || 176.6 ||
prabhāsādyeṣu tīrtheṣu snānaṃ cakruḥ surāstadā  |
sarvatīrthotthitaṃ toyaṃ pātre vai nihitaṃ tu taiḥ  || 176.7 ||
śūlabhedamanuprāpya śūlaṃ śuddhaṃ tu śūlinaḥ  |
tatrotthamudakaṃ gṛhya āgatā bhṛgukacchake  || 176.8 ||
tatrāpaśyaṃstato hyagniṃ ca piṅgalākṣaṃ ca rogiṇam  |
tapasyugre vyavasitaṃ dhyāyamānaṃ maheśvaram  || 176.9 ||
havirbhāgaistu viprāṇāṃ rājñāṃ caivāmayāvinām  |
dṛṣṭvā tu bahurogārtamagniṃ devamukhaṃ surāḥ  |
prāhuste sahitā devaṃ śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram  || 176.10 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ śambho piṅgalasyāmayāvinaḥ  |
yathā hi nīrujaḥ kāyo haviṣāṃ grahaṇakṣamaḥ  |
punarbhavati piṅgastu tathā kuru maheśvara  || 176.11 ||

īśvara uvāca -
bhobhoḥ surā hi tapasā tuṣṭo'haṃ vo viśeṣataḥ  |
vacanācca viśeṣeṇa dadāmyabhimataṃ varam  || 176.12 ||

piṅgala uvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa dīyate deva cepsitam  |
candrādityau ca nayane kṛtvātra kalayā sthitaḥ  || 176.13 ||
tathā punarnavaḥ kāyo bhavedvai mama śaṅkara  |
tathā kuru virūpākṣa namastubhyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 176.14 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchambhurmūrtimādityarūpiṇīm  |
kṛtvā tu tasya tadrogamapānudata śaṅkaraḥ  || 176.15 ||
tataḥ punarnavībhūtaḥ punaḥ provāca śaṅkaram  |
atraiva sthīyatāṃ śambho tathaiva bhāskaraḥ svayam  || 176.16 ||
prāṇināmupakārāya rogāṇāmupaśāntaye  |
pāpānāṃ dhvaṃsanārthāya śreyasāṃ caiva vṛddhaye  || 176.17 ||
evamuktastu bhagavānpiṅgalena mahātmanā  |
avatāraṃ ca kṛtavān gīrvāṇānidamabravīt || 176.18 ||

īśvara uvāca -
muñcadhvamudakaṃ devāstīrthebhyo yatsamāhṛtam  |
mama cottarataḥ kṛtvā khātaṃ devamayaṃ śubham  || 176.19 ||
tatra nikṣipyatāṃ vāri sarvarogavināśanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ sarvairapi surādibhiḥ  || 176.20 ||
evamuktāḥ surāḥ sarve khātaṃ kṛtvā tathottare  |
vayastriṃśatkoṭigaṇairmuktaṃ tattīrthajaṃ jalam  || 176.21 ||
procuste sahitāḥ sarve virūpākṣapurogamāḥ  |
yaḥ kaściddevakhāte'sminmṛdālambhanapūrvakam  || 176.22 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā ravidine saṃsnāya narmadājale  |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pitṛbhyo vai dānaṃ dattvā svaśaktitaḥ  || 176.23 ||
pūjayiṣyati piṅgeśaṃ tasya vāsastriviṣṭape  |
bhaviṣyati surairuktaṃ śṛṇoti sakalaṃ jagat || 176.24 ||
āmayā bhuvi martyānāṃ kṣayarogavicarcikāḥ  |
vyādhayo vikṛtākārāḥ kāsaśvāsajvarodbhavāḥ  || 176.25 ||
ekadvitricaturthāhā ye jvarā bhūtasambhavāḥ  |
ye cānye vikṛtā doṣā dadruśca kāmalaṃ tathā  || 176.26 ||
dinaiste saptabhiryānti nāśaṃ snānairraverdine  |
śatabhedaprabhinnā ye kuṣṭhā bahuvidhāstathā  || 176.27 ||
śatamādityavārāṇāṃ snāyādaṣṭottaraṃ tu yaḥ  |
sampūjya śaṅkaraṃ dadyāttilapātraṃ dvijātaye  || 176.28 ||
naśyanti tasya kuṣṭhāni garuḍeneva pannagāḥ  |
evamuktvā gatāḥ sarve tridaśāstridaśālayam  || 176.29 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saritsu ca  |
snānaṃ samācarennityaṃ naraḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 176.30 ||
ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasreṣu ṣaṣṭitīrthaśateṣu ca  |
yatphalaṃ snānadāneṣu devakhāte tato'dhikam  || 176.31 ||
devakhāteṣu yaḥ snātvā tarpayitvā pitḥnnṛpa  |
pūjayeddevadeveśaṃ piṅgaleśvaramuttamam  || 176.32 ||
so'śvamedhasya yajñasya vājapeyasya bhārata  |
dvayoḥ puṇyamavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 176.33 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe piṅgaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭsaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 177

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhūtīśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  |
darśanādeva rājendra yasya pāpaṃ praṇaśyati  || 177.1 ||
tatra sthāne purā pārtha devadevena śūlinā  |
uddhūlanaṃ kṛtaṃ gātre tena bhūtīśvaraṃ tu tat || 177.2 ||
puṣye vā janmanakṣatre amāvāsyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
bhūtīśvare naraḥ snātvā kulakoṭiṃ samuddharet || 177.3 ||
tatra sthāne tu yo bhaktyā kurute hyaṅgaguṇṭhanam  |
tasya yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva narādhipa  || 177.4 ||
yāvanto bhūtikaṇikā gātre lagnāḥ śivālaye  |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  || 177.5 ||
sarveṣāmeva snānānāṃ bhasmasnānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam  |
purāṇairṛṣibhiḥ proktaṃ sarvaśāstreṣvanuttamam  || 177.6 ||
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ cāpi yaḥ sadā  |
snānaṃ karoti cāgneyaṃ pāpaṃ tasya praṇaśyati  || 177.7 ||
divyasnānādvaraṃ snānaṃ vāyavyaṃ bharatarṣabha  |
vāyavyāduttamaṃ brāhmyaṃ varaṃ brāhmyāttu vāruṇam  || 177.8 ||
āgneyaṃ vāruṇācchreṣṭhaṃ yasmāduktaṃ svayambhuvā  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena hyāgneyaṃ snānamācaret || 177.9 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ brāhmyaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyameva ca  |
kimuktaṃ śrotumicchāmi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 177.10 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānamavagāhya ca vāruṇam  |
āpohiṣṭheti ca brāhmyaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam  || 177.11 ||
sūrye dṛṣṭe tu yatsnānaṃ gaṅgātoyena tatsamam  |
tatsnānaṃ pañcamaṃ proktaṃ divyaṃ pāṇḍavasattama  || 177.12 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena snātvā bhūtīśvare tu yaḥ  |
pūjayeddevamīśānaṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ  || 177.13 ||
tatra sthāne tu ye nityaṃ dhyāyanti paramaṃ padam  |
sūkṣmaṃ cātīndriyaṃ nityaṃ te dhanyā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 177.14 ||
muktitīrthaṃ tu tattīrthaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  |
darśanādeva yasyaiva pāpaṃ yāti mahatkṣayam  || 177.15 ||
jāyate pūjayā rājyaṃ tatra stutvā maheśvaram  |
japena pāpasaṃśuddhirdhyānenānantyamaśnute  || 177.16 ||
oṃ jyotiḥ svarūpamanādimadhyamanutpādyamānamanucāryamāṇākṣaram  |
sarvabhūtasthitaṃ śivaṃ sarvayogeśvaraṃ sarvalokeśvaraṃ mohaśokahīnaṃ mahājñānagamyam  || 177.17 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo gatvā snānaṃ kuryānnareśvara  |
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
evambhūtaṃ na jānanti mokṣāpekṣaṇikā narāḥ  || 177.18 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhūtīśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 178

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra gaṅgāvāhakamuttamam  |
narmadāyāṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ bhṛgutīrthasamīpataḥ  || 178.1 ||
tatra gaṅgā mahāpuṇyā cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  |
purā varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ paramaṃ vratamāsthitā  || 178.2 ||
dhyātvā devaṃ jagadyoniṃ nārāyaṇamakalmaṣam  |
ātmānaṃ paramaṃ dhāma saritsā jagatīpate  || 178.3 ||
tato janārdano deva āgatyedamuvāca ha  || 178.4 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
tapasā tava tuṣṭo'haṃ matpādāmbujasambhave  |
mattaḥ kimicchase devi brūhi kiṃ karavāṇi te  || 178.5 ||

gaṅgovāca -
tvatpādakamalādbhraṣṭā gaṅgā sahacarā vibho  |
yadṛcchayā trilokeśa vandyamānā divaukasaiḥ  || 178.6 ||
nṛpo bhagīrathastasmāttapaḥ kṛtvā suduṣkaram  |
samārādhya jagannāthaṃ śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram  || 178.7 ||
avatārayāmāsa hi māṃ pṛthivyāṃ dharaṇīdhara  |
mayā vai yuvayorvākyādavatāraḥ kṛto bhuvi  || 178.8 ||
vaiṣṇavīmiti māṃ matvā janaḥ sarvāpluto mayi  |
ye vai brahmaṇo loke ye ca vai gurutalpagāḥ  || 178.9 ||
tyāginaḥ pitṛmātṛbhyāṃ ye ca svarṇaharā narāḥ  |
goghnā ye manujā loke tathā ye prāṇihiṃsakāḥ  || 178.10 ||
agamyāgāmino ye ca hyabhakṣyasya ca bhakṣakāḥ  |
ye cānṛtapravaktāro ye ca viśvāsaghātakāḥ  || 178.11 ||
devabrāhmaṇavittānāṃ hartāro ye narādhamāḥ  |
devabrahmagurustrīṇāṃ ye ca nindākarā narāḥ  || 178.12 ||
brahmaśāpapradagdhā ye ye caivātmahano dvijāḥ  |
bhraṣṭānaśanasaṃnyāsaniyatavratacāriṇaḥ  || 178.13 ||
tathaivāpeyapeyāśca ye ca svagurunindakāḥ  |
niṣedhakā ye dānānāṃ pātradānaparāṅmukhāḥ  || 178.14 ||
ṛtughnā ye svapatnīnāṃ pitroḥ sehaparā na hi  |
bāndhaveṣu ca dīneṣu karuṇā yasya nāsti vai  || 178.15 ||
kṣetrasetuvibhedī ca pūrvamārgapralopakaḥ  |
nāstikaḥ śāstrahīnastu vipraḥ sandhyāvivarjitaḥ  || 178.16 ||
ahutāśī hyasaṃtuṣṭaḥ sarvāśī sarvavikrayī  |
kadaryā nāstikāḥ krūrāḥ kṛtaghnā ye dvijāyaḥ  || 178.17 ||
paiśunyā rasavikreyāḥ sarvakālavinākṛtāḥ  |
svagotrāṃ paragotrāṃ vā ye bhuñjanti dvijādhamāḥ  || 178.18 ||
te māṃ prāpya vimucyante pāpasaṅghaiḥ susaṃcitaiḥ  |
tatpāpakṣārataptāyā na śarma mama vidyate  || 178.19 ||
tathā kuru jagannātha yathāhaṃ śarma cāpnuyām  |
evamuktastu deveśastuṣṭaḥ provāca jāhnavīm  || 178.20 ||

viṣṇuruvāca -
ahamatra vasiṣyāmi gaṅgādharasahāyavān  |
praviśasva sadā revāṃ tvamatraiva ca mūrtinā  || 178.21 ||
mama pādatalaṃ prāpya vaha tripathagāmini  |
yadā bahūdakakāle narmadājalasaṃbhṛtā  || 178.22 ||
prāvṛṭkālaṃ samāsādya bhaviṣyati jalākulā  |
plāvyobhayataṭaṃ devī prāpya māmuttarasthitam  || 178.23 ||
plāvayiṣyati toyena yadā śaṅkhaṃ kare sthitam  |
tadā parvaśatodyuktaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ parvasaṃjñitam  || 178.24 ||
na tena sadṛśaṃ kiṃcidvyatīpātādisaṃkramam  |
ayane dve ca na tathā puṇyātpuṇyataraṃ yathā  || 178.25 ||
tasminparvaṇi deveśi śaṅkhaṃ saṃspṛśya mānavaḥ  |
snānamācarate toye miśre gāṅgeyanārmade  || 178.26 ||
puṇyaṃ tvaśeṣapuṇyānāṃ maṅgalānāṃ ca maṅgalam  |
viṣṇunā vidhṛto yena tasmācchāntiḥ pracakrame  || 178.27 ||
tatrāntaṃ pāpasaṅghasya dhruvamāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
śaṅkhoddhāre naraḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 178.28 ||
tṛptāste dvādaśābdāni siddhiṃ ca sārvakāmikīm  |
gaṅgāvahe tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ śaṅkhoddhāre pradāsyati  || 178.29 ||
tena piṇḍapradānena nṛtyanti pitarastathā  |
śaṅkhoddhāre naraḥ snātvā pūjayedbalakeśavau  || 178.30 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śuddho bhavati jāhnavi  |
yattvaṃ lokakṛtaṃ karma manyase bhuvi duḥsaham  || 178.31 ||
tasminparvaṇi tatsarvaṃ tatra snātvā vyapohaya  |
evamuktvā naraśreṣṭha viṣṇuścāntaradhīyata  || 178.32 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ gaṅgāvāhakamuttamam  |
brahmādyairṛṣibhistāta pāramparyakramāgataiḥ  || 178.33 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā bhaktibhāvena bhārata  |
gaṅgātīrthe tu sa snātaḥ samasteṣu na saṃśayaḥ  || 178.34 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
anivartikā gatisteṣāṃ viṣṇulokātkadācana  || 178.35 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gaṅgāvahakatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 179

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra gautameśvaramuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  || 179.1 ||
gautamena tapastaptaṃ tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tatastuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 179.2 ||
praṇamya śirasā tatra sthāpitaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
sthāpito gautameneśo gautameśvara ucyate  || 179.3 ||
tatra devaiśca gandharvairṛṣibhiḥ pitṛdaivataiḥ  |
samprāptā hyuttamā siddhirārādhya parameśvaram  || 179.4 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjayetparamīśānaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 179.5 ||
bahavastanna jānanti viṣṇumāyāvimohitāḥ  |
tatra saṃnihitaṃ devaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ maheśvaram  || 179.6 ||
brahmacārī tu yo bhūtvā tatra tīrthe nareśvara  |
snātvārcayenmahādevaṃ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 179.7 ||
brahmacārī tu yo bhūtvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
pūjayetparamīśānaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 179.8 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo dānaṃ bhaktyā dadyāddvijātaye  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 179.9 ||
māse cāśvayuje rājan kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
snātvā tatra vidhānena dīpakānāṃ śataṃ dadet || 179.10 ||
pūjayitvā mahādevaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhirnaraḥ  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mṛtaḥ śivapuraṃ vrajet || 179.11 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ kārttikyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ  |
upoṣya prayato bhūtvā ghṛtena snāpayecchivam  || 179.12 ||
pañcagavyena madhunā dadhnā vā śītavāriṇā  |
sa ca sarvasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 179.13 ||
bhaktyā tu pūjayetpaścātsa labhetphalamuttamam  |
bilvapatrairakhaṇḍaiśca puṣpairunmattakodbhavaiḥ  || 179.14 ||
kuśāpāmārgasahitaiḥ kadambadroṇajairapi  |
mallikākaravīraiśca raktapītaiḥ sitāsitaiḥ  || 179.15 ||
puṣpairanyairyathālābhaṃ yo naraḥ pūjayecchivam  |
nairantaryeṇa ṣaṇmāsaṃ yo'rcayedgautameśvaram  |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti mṛtaḥ śivapuraṃ vrajet || 179.16 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe gautameśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ekonāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 180

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla daśāśvamedhikaṃ param  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  || 180.1 ||
yatra gatvā mahārāja snātvā sampūjya ceśvaram  |
daśānāmaśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 180.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
aśvamedho mahāyajño bahusambhāradakṣiṇaḥ  |
aśakyaḥ prākṛtaiḥ kartuṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ phalaṃ labhet || 180.3 ||
atyāścaryamidaṃ tattvaṃ tvayoktaṃ vadatā satā  |
yathā me jāyate śraddhā dīrghāyustvaṃ tathā vada  || 180.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
idamāścaryabhūtaṃ hi gauryā pṛṣṭastriyambakaḥ  |
tatte'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi pṛcchate nipuṇāya vai  || 180.5 ||
purā vṛṣastho deveśa hyumayā saha śaṅkaraḥ  |
kadācitparyaṭanpṛthivīṃ narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 180.6 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ tīrthaṃ dṛṣṭvā devo maheśvaraḥ  |
tīrthaṃ pratyañjaliṃ baddhvā namaścakre trilocanaḥ  || 180.7 ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā devīdamabravīt || 180.8 ||

devyuvāca -
kimetaddevadeveśa carācaranamaskṛta  |
prahvanamrāñjaliṃ baddhvā bhaktyā paramayā yutaḥ  || 180.9 ||
etadāścaryamatulaṃ sarvaṃ kathaya me prabho  || 180.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pratyakṣaṃ paśya tīrthasya phalaṃ mā vismitā bhava  |
viyatsthā me bhuvisthasya kṣaṇaṃ devi sthirā bhava  || 180.11 ||
evamuktvā tu deveśo gauravarṇo dvijo'bhavat |
kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭho jaṭilaḥ śuṣko dhamanisaṃtataḥ  || 180.12 ||
upaviśya bhuvaḥ pṛṣṭhe susvaraṃ mantramuccaran  |
kramapriyo mahādevo mādhuryeṇa pramodayan  || 180.13 ||
śrutvā tāṃ madhurāṃ vāṇīṃ svayaṃ devena nirmitām  |
saṃbhrāntā brāhmaṇāḥ sarve snātuṃ ye tatra cāgatāḥ  || 180.14 ||
nityakriyā ca sarveṣāṃ vismṛtā śrutivibhramāt |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā paṭhamānaṃ tu kṣutpipāsābhipīḍitam  || 180.15 ||
dvijo'nyamantrayatkaścidbhaktyā taṃ bhojanāya vai  |
prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ brahmanbhojanāya gṛhe mama  || 180.16 ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma hyadya me saphalāḥ kriyāḥ  |
sarvānkāmānpradāsyanti prītā me'dya pitāmahāḥ  || 180.17 ||
tvayi bhukte dvijaśreṣṭha prasīda tvaṃ dhruvaṃ mama  |
evamukto mahādevo dvijarūpadharastadā  || 180.18 ||
prahasya pratyuvācedaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ślakṣṇayā girā  |
mayā varṣasahasraṃ tu nirāhāraṃ tapaḥ kṛtam  || 180.19 ||
idānīṃ tu gṛhe tasya kariṣye dvijasattama  |
daśabhirvājimedhaiśca yeneṣṭaṃ pāraṇaṃ tathā  || 180.20 ||
ityukto devadevena brāhmaṇo vismayānvitaḥ  |
uttamāṅgaṃ vidhunvanvai jagāma svagṛhaṃ prati  || 180.21 ||
evaṃ te bahavo viprāḥ pratyākhyāte nimantraṇe  |
purāṇārthamajānanto nāstikā bahavo gatāḥ  || 180.22 ||
atha kaściddvijo vidvānpurāṇārthasya tattvavit |
devaṃ nimantrayāmāsa dvijarūpadharaṃ śivam  || 180.23 ||
tathaiva so'pi devena proktaḥ sa prāha taṃ punaḥ  |
manasā cintayitvā tu purāṇoktaṃ dvijottamaḥ  || 180.24 ||
smṛtivedapurāṇeṣu yaduktaṃ tattathā bhavet |
iti niścitya taṃ vipramuvāca prahasanniva  || 180.25 ||
bhobho vipra pratīkṣasva yāvadāgamanaṃ punaḥ  |
ityuktvā tu dvijo gatvā daśāśvamedhikaṃ param  || 180.26 ||
snānaṃ mahālambhanādi kṛtaṃ tena dvijanmanā  |
japaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā dānaṃ kṛtvā dharmānusārataḥ  || 180.27 ||
saṃkalpya kapilāṃ tatra purāṇoktavidhānataḥ  |
samāyāttvaritaṃ tatra yatrāsau tiṣṭhate dvijaḥ  || 180.28 ||
athāgatya dvijaṃ prāha vājimedhaḥ kṛto mayā  |
uttiṣṭha me gṛhaṃ ramyaṃ bhojanārthaṃ hi gamyatām  || 180.29 ||
ityuktaḥ śaṅkarastena brāhmaṇenātivismitaḥ  |
uvāca brāhmaṇaṃ deva idānīṃ tvamito gataḥ  || 180.30 ||
dvijavarya kathaṃ ceṣṭā daśa yajñā mahādhanāḥ  || 180.31 ||

dvija uvāca -
na vicārastvayā kāryaḥ kṛtā yajñā na saṃśayaḥ  |
yadi vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ taṃ bhuvi devā dvijāstathā  || 180.32 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā satyaṃ dvijottama  |
yadi vedapurāṇoktaṃ vākyaṃ niḥsaṃśayaṃ bhavet || 180.33 ||
tadā prāptaṃ mayā sarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
evamuktastu deveśa āstikyaṃ tasya cetasaḥ  || 180.34 ||
vimṛśya bahubhiḥ kiṃciduttaraṃ na prapadyata  |
jagāma tadgṛhaṃ ramyaṃ paṭhanbrahma sanātanam  || 180.35 ||
samprāptaṃ taṃ dvijaṃ bhaktyā pādyārghyeṇa tamarcayat |
ṣaḍrasaṃ bhojanaṃ tena dattaṃ paścādyathāvidhi  || 180.36 ||
tato bhukte mahādeve sarvadevamaye śive  |
puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papātāśu gaganāttasya mūrdhani  |
tasyāstikyaṃ tu saṃlakṣya tuṣṭaḥ provāca śaṅkaraḥ  || 180.37 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kiṃ te'dya kriyatāṃ brūhi varado'haṃ dvijottama  |
adeyamapi dāsyāmi ekacittasya te dhruvam  || 180.38 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
yadi prīto'si me deva yadi deyo varo mama  |
asmiṃstīrthe mahādeva sthātavyaṃ sarvadaiva hi  || 180.39 ||
upakārāya deveśa eṣa me vara uttamaḥ  |
evamuktastu devena āruroha dvijottamaḥ  || 180.40 ||
gandharvāpsaraḥsambādhaṃ vimānaṃ sārvakāmikam  |
pūjyamāno gatastatra yatra lokā nirāmayāḥ  || 180.41 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etadāścaryamatulaṃ dṛṣṭvā devī suvismitā  |
vismayotphullanayanā punaḥ papraccha śaṅkaram  || 180.42 ||

pārvatyuvāca -
kathametadbhavetsatyaṃ yatredamasamañjasam  |
snānaṃ kurvanti bahavo lokā hyatra maheśvara  || 180.43 ||
teṣāṃ tu svargagamanaṃ yathaiṣa svargatiṃ gataḥ  |
kathametatsamācakṣva vismayaḥ paramo mama  || 180.44 ||
etacchrutvā tu deveśaḥ prahasanpratyuvāca tām  |
vedavākye purāṇārthe smṛtyarthe dvijabhāṣite  || 180.45 ||
vismayo hi na kartavyo hyanumānaṃ hi tattathā  |
asaṃbhāvyaṃ hi lokānāṃ purāṇe yatpragīyate  || 180.46 ||
yadi pakṣaṃ puraskṛtya lokāḥ kurvanti pārvati  |
tasmānna siddhireteṣāṃ bhavatyeko na vismayaḥ  || 180.47 ||
nāstikā bhinnamaryādā ye niścayabahiṣkṛtāḥ  |
teṣāṃ siddhirna vidyeta āstikyādbhavate dhruvam  || 180.48 ||
śrutvākhyānamidaṃ devī vavande tīrthamuttamam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 180.49 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
daśāśvamedhaṃ rājendra sarvatīrthottamottamam  |
tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  || 180.50 ||
tatrāgatā mahābhāgā snātukāmā sarasvatī  |
puṇyānāṃ paramā puṇyā nadīnāmuttamā nadī  || 180.51 ||
nāmamātreṇa yasyāstu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
snātāstatra divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ  || 180.52 ||
daśāśvamedhe sā rājanniyatā brahmacāriṇī  |
ārādhayitvā deveśaṃ paraṃ nirvāṇamāgatīḥ  || 180.53 ||
kāluṣyaṃ brahmasambhūtā saṃvatsarasamudbhavam  |
prakṣālayitumāyāti daśamyāmāśvinasya ca  || 180.54 ||
upoṣya rajanīṃ tāṃ tu sampūjya tripurāntakam  |
rājanniṣkalmaṣā yānti śvobhūte śāśvataṃ padam  || 180.55 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
sarasvatī mahāpuṇyā nadīnāmuttamā nadī  |

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
rājannāśvayuje māsi daśamyāṃ tadviśiṣyate  |
pārthiveṣu ca tīrthe tu sarveṣveva na saṃśayaḥ  || 180.56 ||
daśāśvamedhike rājannityaṃ hi daśamī śubhā  |
viśeṣādāśvine śuklā mahāpātakanāśinī  || 180.57 ||
tasyāsnātvārcayeddevānupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena paścātsampūjayecchivam  || 180.58 ||
tatrasthāṃ pūjayeddevīṃ snātukāmāṃ sarasvatīm  |
namo namaste deveśi brahmadehasamudbhave  || 180.59 ||
kuru pāpakṣayaṃ devi saṃsārānmāṃ samuddhara  |
gandhadhūpaiśca sampūjya hyarcayitvā punaḥpunaḥ  || 180.60 ||
daśa pradakṣiṇā dattvā sūtreṇa pariveṣṭayet |
kapilāṃ tu tato vipre dadyādvigatamatsaraḥ  || 180.61 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampannāṃ sarvopaskarasaṃyutām  |
dattvā viprāya kapilāṃ na śocati kṛtākṛte  || 180.62 ||
paścājjāgaraṇaṃ kuryādghṛtenājvālya dīpakam  |
purāṇapaṭhanenaiva nṛtyagītavivādanaiḥ  || 180.63 ||
vedoktaiścaiva pūjayecchaśiśekharam  |
prabhāte vimale paścātsnātvā vai narmadājale  || 180.64 ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayedbhaktyā śivabhaktāṃśca yoginaḥ  |
evaṃ kṛte tato rājan samyaktīrthaphalaṃ labhet || 180.65 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayecchaṅkaraṃ naraḥ  |
daśāśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ labhate puṇyamuttamam  || 180.66 ||
pūtātmā tena puṇyena rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  |
ārūḍhaḥ paramaṃ yānaṃ kāmagaṃ ca suśobhanam  || 180.67 ||
tatra divyāpsarobhistu vījyamāno'tha cāmaraiḥ  |
krīḍate suciraṃ kālaṃ jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  || 180.68 ||
tato'vatīrṇaḥ kālena iha rājā bhaveddhruvam  |
hastyaśvarathasampanno mahābhogī paraṃtapaḥ  || 180.69 ||
daśāśvamedhe yaddānaṃ dīyate śivayoginām  |
daśāśvamedhasadṛśaṃ bhavettannātra saṃśayaḥ  || 180.70 ||
sarveṣāmeva yajñānāmaśvamedho viśiṣyate  |
durlabhaḥ svalpavittānāṃ bhūriśaḥ pāpakarmaṇām  || 180.71 ||
tatra tīrthe tu rājendra durlabho'pi surāsuraiḥ  |
prāpyate snānadānena ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 180.72 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā mṛtastatra nareśvara  |
devatvaṃ prāpnuyātso'pi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 180.73 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttatra tīrthe narottama  |
agniloke vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 180.74 ||
jalapraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttatra tīrthe narādhipa  |
dhyāyamāno mahādevaṃ vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 180.75 ||
daśāśvamedhe yaḥ kaścicchūravṛttyā tanuṃ tyajet |
akṣayā nu gatistasya ityevaṃ śrutinodanā  || 180.76 ||
na tāṃ gatiṃ yānti bhṛguprapātino na daṇḍino naiva ca sāṃkhyayoginaḥ  |
dhvajākule dundubhiśaṅkhanādite kṣaṇena yāṃ yānti mahāhave mṛtāḥ  || 180.77 ||
yatra tatra hataḥ śūraḥ śatrubhiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ  |
akṣayāṃllabhate lokānyadi klībaṃ na bhāṣate  || 180.78 ||
daśāśvamedhe saṃnyāsaṃ yaḥ karoti vidhānataḥ  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokātkadācana  || 180.79 ||
daśāśvamedhe yatpuṇyaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa yudhiṣṭhira  |
kathitaṃ parayā bhaktyā sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 180.80 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe daśāśvamedhatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 181

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi bhṛgutīrthasya vistaram  |
yaṃ śrutvā brahmahā goghno mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 181.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu vikhyātaṃ vṛṣakhātamiti śrutam  |
bhṛguṇā tatra rājendra tapastaptaṃ purā kila  || 181.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
bhṛgukacche sa viprendro nivasan kena hetunā  |
tapastaptvā suvipulaṃ parāṃ siddhimupāgataḥ  || 181.3 ||
ko vā vṛṣa iti proktastatkhātaṃ yena khānitam  |
etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ kathayasva mamānagha  || 181.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
eṣa praśno mahārāja yastvayā paripṛcchitaḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  || 181.5 ||
ṣaṣṭhastu brahmaṇaḥ putro mānaso bhṛgusattamaḥ  |
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ śrīvṛte kṣetra uttame  || 181.6 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu saṃśuṣko munisattamaḥ  |
nirāhāro nirānandaḥ kāṣṭhapāṣāṇavatsthitaḥ  || 181.7 ||
tataḥ kadāciddeveśo vimānavaramāsthitaḥ  |
umayā sahitaḥ śrīmāṃstena mārgeṇa cāgataḥ  || 181.8 ||
dṛṣṭvā tatra mahābhāgaṃ bhṛguṃ valmīkavatsthitam  |
uvāca devī deveśaṃ kimidaṃ dṛśyate prabho  || 181.9 ||

īśvara uvāca -
bhṛgurnāma mahādevi tapastaptvā sudāruṇam  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu mama dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ  || 181.10 ||
jalabindu kuśāgreṇa māse māse pibecca saḥ  |
saṃvatsaraśataṃ sāgraṃ tiṣṭhate ca varānane  || 181.11 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ gaurī krodhasaṃvartitekṣaṇā  |
uvāca devī deveśaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ maheśvaram  || 181.12 ||
satyamugro'si loke tvaṃ khyāpito vṛṣabhadhvaja  |
niṣkāruṇyo durārādhyaḥ sarvabhūtabhayaṃkaraḥ  || 181.13 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu dhyāyamānasya śaṅkaram  |
brāhmaṇasya varaṃ kasmānna prayacchasi śaṃsa me  || 181.14 ||
evamukto'tha deveśaḥ prahasya girinandinīm  |
uvāca naraśārdūla meghagambhīrayā girā  || 181.15 ||
strī vinaśyati garveṇa tapaḥ krodhena naśyati  |
gāvo dūrapracāreṇa śūdrānnena dvijottamāḥ  || 181.16 ||
krodhānvito dvijo gaurī tena siddhirna vidyate  |
varṣāyutaistathā lakṣairna kiṃcitkāraṇaṃ priye  || 181.17 ||
evambhūtasya tasyāpi krodhasya caritaṃ mahat |
evamuktvā tataḥ śambhurvṛṣaṃ dadhyau ca tatkṣaṇe  || 181.18 ||
vṛṣo hi bhagavanbrahmā vṛṣarūpī maheśvaraḥ  |
dhyānaprāptaḥ kṣaṇādeva garjayan vai muhurmuhuḥ  || 181.19 ||
kiṃ karomi suraśreṣṭha dhyātaḥ kenaiva hetunā  |
karomi kasya nidhanamakāle parameśvara  || 181.20 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kopayasva dvijaśreṣṭhaṃ gatvā tvaṃ bhṛgusattamam  |
yena me śraddadhatyeṣā gaurī lokaikasundarī  || 181.21 ||
etacchrutvā vṛṣo gatvā dharṣaṇārthaṃ dvijottamam  |
narmadāyāstaṭe ramye samīpe cāśrame bhṛguḥ  || 181.22 ||
tataḥ śṛṅgairgṛhītvā tu prakṣipto narmadājale  |
tataḥ kruddho bhṛgustatra daṇḍahasto mahāmuniḥ  || 181.23 ||
paśuvatte vadhiṣyāmi daṇḍaghātena mastake  |
śikhāyajñopavīte ca paridhānaṃ varāsane  || 181.24 ||
susaṃvṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ tena dhāvanvai pṛṣṭhato bravīt || 181.25 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
pāpakarmandurācāra kathaṃ yāsyasi me vṛṣa  |
avamānaṃ samutpādya kṛtvā gartaṃ khuraistathā  || 181.26 ||
garjayitvā mahānādaṃ tato vipramapātayat |
ātmānaṃ pātitaṃ jñātvā vṛṣeṇa parameṣṭhinā  || 181.27 ||
bhṛguḥ krodhena jajvāla hutāhutirivānalaḥ  |
kare gṛhya mahādaṇḍaṃ brahmadaṇḍamivāparam  || 181.28 ||
hantukāmo vṛṣaṃ vipro'bhyadhāvata yudhiṣṭhira  |
dhāvamānaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā sa vṛṣaḥ pūrvasāgare  || 181.29 ||
jambūdvīpaṃ kuśāṃ krauñcaṃ śālmaliṃ śākameva ca  |
gomedaṃ puṣkaraṃ prāptaḥ pūrvato dakṣiṇāpatham  || 181.30 ||
uttaraṃ paścimaṃ caiva dvīpāddvīpaṃ nareśvara  |
pātālaṃ sutalaṃ paścādvitalaṃ ca talātalam  || 181.31 ||
tāmisramandhatāmisraṃ pātālaṃ saptamaṃ yayau  |
tato jagāma bhūrlokaṃ prāṇārthī sa vṛṣottamaḥ  || 181.32 ||
bhuvaḥ svaścaiva ca mahastapaḥ satyaṃ janastathā  |
anugamyamāno vipreṇa na śarma labhate kvacit || 181.33 ||
pāpaṃ kṛtvaiva puruṣaḥ kāmakrodhabalārditaḥ  |
tato jagāma śaraṇaṃ brahmāṇaṃ viṣṇumeva ca  || 181.34 ||
indraṃ candraṃ tathādityairyāmyavāruṇamārutaiḥ  |
yadā sarvaiḥ parityakto lokālokaiḥ sureśvaraiḥ  || 181.35 ||
tadā devaṃ namaskṛtvā rakṣa rakṣasva cābravīt |
vadhyamānaṃ mahādevo bhṛguṇā parameṣṭhinā  || 181.36 ||
sarvalokaiḥ parityaktamanāthamiva taṃ prabho  |
dṛṣṭvā śrāntaṃ vṛṣaṃ devaḥ patitaṃ caraṇāgrataḥ  || 181.37 ||
tataḥ provāca bhagavān smitapūrvamidaṃ vacaḥ  || 181.38 ||

īśvara uvāca -
paśya devi mahābhāge śamaṃ viprasya sundari  || 181.39 ||

pārvatyuvāca -
yāvadvipro na cāsmākaṃ kupyate parameśvara  |
tāvadvaraṃ prayacchāśu yadi cecchasi matpriyam  || 181.40 ||
tato bhasmī jaṭī śūlī candrārdhakṛtaśekharaḥ  |
umārddhadeho bhagavānbhūtvā vipramuvāca ha  || 181.41 ||
bhobho dvijavaraśreṣṭha krodhaste na śamaṃ gataḥ  |
yasmāttasmādidaṃ tāta krodhasthānaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 181.42 ||
tato dṛṣṭvā ca taṃ śambhuṃ bhṛguḥ śreṣṭhaṃ trilocanam  |
jānubhyāmavaniṃ gatvā idaṃ stotramudairayat || 181.43 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
praṇipatya bhūtanāthaṃ bhavodbhavaṃ bhūtidaṃ bhayātītam  |
bhavabhīto bhuvanapate vijñaptuṃ kiṃcidicchāmi  || 181.44 ||
tvadguṇanikarānvaktuṃ kā śaktirmānuṣasyāsya  |
vāsukirapi na tāvadvaktuṃ vadanasahasraṃ bhavedyasya  || 181.45 ||
bhaktyā tathāpi śaṅkara śaśidhara karajāladhavalitāśeṣa  |
stutimukharasya maheśvara prasīda tava caraṇaniratasya  || 181.46 ||
sattvaṃ rajastamastvaṃ sthityutpattivināśanaṃ deva  |
bhavabhīto bhuvanapate bhuvaneśa śaraṇaniratasya  || 181.47 ||
yamaniyamayajñadānaṃ vedābhyāsaśca dhāraṇāyogaḥ  |
tvadbhakteḥ sarvamidaṃ nārhanti vai kalāsahasrāṃśam  || 181.48 ||
utkṛṣṭarasarasāyanakhaḍgāṃ janavivarapādukāsiddhiḥ  |
cihnaṃ hi tava natānāṃ dṛśyata iha janmani prakaṭam  || 181.49 ||
śāṭhyena yadi praṇamati vitarasi tasyāpi bhūtimicchayā deva  |
bhavati bhavacchedakarī bhaktirmokṣāya nirmitā nātha  || 181.50 ||
paradāraparasvarataṃ paraparibhavaduḥkhaśokasaṃtaptam  |
paravadanavīkṣaṇaparaṃ parameśvara māṃ paritrāhi  || 181.51 ||
adhikābhimānamuditaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅguravibhavavilasantam  |
krūraṃ kupathābhimukhaṃ śaṅkara śaraṇāgataṃ paritrāhi  || 181.52 ||
dīnaṃ dvijaṃ varārthe bandhujane naiva pūritā hyāśā  |
chinddhi maheśvara tṛṣṇāṃ kiṃ mūḍhaṃ māṃ viḍambayasi  || 181.53 ||
tṛṣṇāṃ harasva śīghraṃ lakṣmīṃ dada hṛdayavāsinīṃ nityam  |
chinddhi madamohapāśaṃ māmuttāraya bhavācca deveśa  || 181.54 ||
karuṇābhyudayaṃ nāma stotramidaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ divyam  |
yaḥ paṭhati bhṛguṃ smarati ca śivalokamasau prayāti dehānte  || 181.55 ||
etacchrutvā mahādevaḥ stotraṃ ca bhṛgubhāṣitam  |
uvāca varado'smīti devyā saha varottamam  || 181.56 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
prasanno devadeveśa yadi deyo varo mama  |
siddhikṣetramidaṃ sarvaṃ bhavitā mama nāmataḥ  || 181.57 ||
bhavadbhiḥ sannidhānena sthātavyaṃ hi sahomayā  |
devakṣetramidaṃ puṇyaṃ yena sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 181.58 ||
atra sthāne mahāsthānaṃ karomi jagadīśvara  |
tava prasādāddeveśa pūryantāṃ me manorathāḥ  || 181.59 ||

īśvara uvāca -
śriyā kṛtamidaṃ pūrvaṃ kiṃ na jñātaṃ tvayā dvija  |
anumānya śriyaṃ devīṃ yadīyaṃ manyate bhavān  || 181.60 ||
kuruṣva yadabhipretaṃ tvatkṛtaṃ naḥ tadanyathā  |
evamuktvā gate deve snātvā gatvā bhṛguḥ śriyam  || 181.61 ||
kṛtvā ca pāraṇaṃ tatra vasanviprastayā saha  |
śriyā ca sahitaḥ kāla idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 181.62 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
yadi te rocate bhadre duḥkhāsīnaṃ ca te yadi  |
tvayā vṛte mahākṣetre svīyaṃ sthānaṃ karomyaham  || 181.63 ||

śrīruvāca -
mama nāmnā tu viprarṣe tava nāmnā tu śobhanam  |
sthānaṃ kuruṣvābhipretamavirodhena me matiḥ  || 181.64 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
kacchapādhiṣṭhitaṃ hyetattasya pṛṣṭhigataṃ rame  |
saṃmantrya sahitaṃ tena śobhanaṃ bhavatī kuru  || 181.65 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhṛgukacchotpattivarṇanaṃ nāmaikāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 182

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato bhṛguḥ śriyā caiva sametaḥ kacchapaṃ gataḥ  |
abhinandya yathānyāyamuvāca vacanaṃ śubham  || 182.1 ||
tvayā dhṛtā dharā sarvā tathā lokāścarācarāḥ  |
tathaiva puṇyabhāvatvātsthitastatra mahāmate  || 182.2 ||
cāturvidyasya saṃsthānaṃ karomi ramayā saha  |
yadi tvaṃ manyase deva tadādeśaya māṃ vibho  || 182.3 ||

kūrma uvāca -
evameva dvijaśreṣṭha mama nāmāṅkitaṃ puram  |
bhaviṣyati mahatkālaṃ mamopari susaṃsthitam  || 182.4 ||
acalaṃ susthiraṃ tāta na bhīḥ kāryā sulocane  |
etacchrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ kacchapasya mukhāccyutam  || 182.5 ||
hṛṣṭastuṣṭaḥ śriyā sārddhaṃ padmayonisuto bhṛguḥ  |
abhīci udaye prāpte kṛtakautukamaṅgalaḥ  || 182.6 ||
nandane vatsare māghe pañcamyāṃ bharatarṣabha  |
śaste tu hyuttarāyoge kumbhasthe śaśimaṇḍale  || 182.7 ||
revāyā uttare tīre gambhīre cābhivāruṇi  |
prāgudakpravaṇe deśe koṭitīrthasamanvitam  || 182.8 ||
krośapramāṇaṃ tatkṣetraṃ prāsādaśatasaṃkulam  |
acireṇaiva kālena tapobalasamanvitaḥ  |
vicintya viśvakarmāṇaṃ cakāra bhṛgusattamaḥ  || 182.9 ||
brāhmaṇā vedavidvāṃsaḥ kṣatriyā rājyapālakāḥ  |
vaiśyā vṛttiratāstatra śūdrāḥ śuśrūṣakāstriṣu  || 182.10 ||
evaṃ śriyā vṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ paramānandananditam  |
nirmitaṃ bhṛguṇā tāta sarvapātakanāśanam  |
iti bhṛgukacchotpattiḥ  || 182.11 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ kālena mahatā kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  |
devalokaṃ jagāmāśu lakṣmīrṛṣisamāgame  || 182.12 ||
samarpya kuñcikāṭṭālaṃ bhṛgave brahmavādine  |
pālayasva yathārthaṃ vai sthānakaṃ mama suvrata  || 182.13 ||
devakāryāṇyaśeṣāṇi kṛtvā śrīḥ punarāgatā  |
ājagāma ramā devī bhṛgukacchaṃ tvarānvitā  || 182.14 ||
prārthitaṃ kuñcikāṭṭālaṃ svagṛhaṃ saparigraham  |
bhṛguryadā tadā pārtha mithyā nāsti tadā vadata  || 182.15 ||
eva vivādaḥ sumahānsaṃjātaśca nareśvara  |
mameti mama caiveti parasparasamāgame  || 182.16 ||
tataḥ kālena mahatā bhṛguṇā paramarṣiṇā  |
cāturvidyapramāṇārthaṃ cakāra mahatīṃ sthitim  || 182.17 ||
asmadīyaṃ yathā sarvaṃ nagaraṃ mṛgalocane  |
cāturvidyā dvijāḥ sarve tathā jānanti sundari  || 182.18 ||

śrīruvāca -
pramāṇaṃ mama viprendra cāturvaṇyā na saṃśayaḥ  |
madīyaṃ vā tvadīyaṃ vā kathayantu dvijottamāḥ  || 182.19 ||
tataḥ samastairvibudhaiḥ sampradhārya parasparam  |
dvidhā tairvāksthalaṃ dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇā nṛpasaṃhitam  || 182.20 ||
aṣṭādaśasahasrāṇi nocurvai kiṃciduttaram  |
aṣṭādaśasahasreṣu bhṛgukopabhayānnṛpa  |
uktaṃ ca tālakaṃ haste yasya tasyedamuttaram  || 182.21 ||
etacchrutvā tu sā devī nigamaṃ naigamaiḥ kṛtam  |
krodhena mahatāviṣṭā śaśāpa dvijapuṃgavān  || 182.22 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
yasmātsatyaṃ samutsṛjya lobhopahatamānasaiḥ  |
madīyaṃ lopitaṃ sthānaṃ tasmācchṛṇvantu me giram  || 182.23 ||
tripauruṣā bhavedvidyā tripuruṣaṃ na bhaveddhanam  |
na dvitīyastu vo vedaḥ paṭhito bhavati dvijāḥ  || 182.24 ||
gṛhāṇi na dvibhaumāni na ca bhūtiḥ sthirā dvijāḥ  |
pakṣapātena vo dharmo na ca niḥśreyabhāvataḥ  || 182.25 ||
iṣṭo gotrajanaḥ kaścillobhenāvṛtamānasaḥ  |
na ca dvaidhaṃ parityajya hyekaṃ satyaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 182.26 ||
adyaprabhṛti sarveṣāmahaṅkāro dvijanmanām  |
na pitā putravākyena na putraḥ pitṛkarmaṇi  || 182.27 ||
ahaṅkārakṛtāḥ sarve bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ  |
iti śaptvā ramādevī tadaiva ca divaṃ yayau  || 182.28 ||
tato gatāyāṃ vai lakṣmyāṃ devā brahmarṣayo'malāḥ  |
krodhalobhamidaṃ sthānaṃ te'pi coktvā divaṃ yayuḥ  || 182.29 ||
gatāṃ dṛṣṭvā tato devīmṛṣīṃścaiva tapodhanān  |
bhṛguśca parameṣṭhī sa viṣādamagamatparam  |
prasādayāmāsa punaḥ śaṅkaraṃ tripurāntakam  || 182.30 ||
tapasā mahatā pārtha tatastuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  |
uvāca vacanaṃ kāle harṣayan bhṛgusattamam  || 182.31 ||
kiṃ viṣaṇṇo'si viprendra kiṃ vā santāpakāraṇam  |
mayi prasanne'pi tava hyetatkathaya me'nagha  || 182.32 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
śāpayitvā dvijānsarvānpurā lakṣmīrvinirgatā  |
apavitramidaṃ coktvā tato devā vinirgatāḥ  || 182.33 ||

īśvara uvāca -
purā mayā yathā proktaṃ tattathā na tadanyathā  |
krodhasthānamasaṃdehaṃ tathānyadapi tacchṛṇu  || 182.34 ||
tatra sthānasamudbhūtā mahadbhayavivarjitāḥ  |
brāhmaṇā matprasādena bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ  || 182.35 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātāḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradāḥ  |
ye'pi te śatasāhasrāstvaritā hyāgatāstviha  || 182.36 ||
apaṭhasyāpi mūrkhasya sarvāvasthāṃ gatasya ca  |
uttarāduttaraṃ śakro dātuṃ na tu bhṛgūttama  || 182.37 ||
koṭitīrthamidaṃ sthānaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
adyaprabhṛti viprendra bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ  || 182.38 ||
matprasādāddevagaṇaiḥ sevitaṃ ca bhaviṣyati  |
bhṛgukṣetre mṛtā ye tu kṛmikīṭapataṃgakāḥ  || 182.39 ||
vāsasteṣāṃ śive loke matprasādādbhaviṣyati  |
vṛṣakhāte naraḥ snātvā pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 182.40 ||
sarvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  |
bhṛgutīrthe naraḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 182.41 ||
tasya te dvādaśābdāni śāntiṃ gacchanti tarpitāḥ  |
dadhikṣīreṇa toyena ghṛtena madhunā saha  || 182.42 ||
ye snapanti virūpākṣaṃ teṣāṃ vāsastriviṣṭape  |
matprasādāddvijaśreṣṭha sarvadevānusevitam  || 182.43 ||
bhaviṣyati bhṛgukṣetraṃ kurukṣetrādibhiḥ samam  |
mārtaṇḍagrahaṇe prāpte yavaṃ kṛtvā hiraṇmayam  || 182.44 ||
dattvā śirasi yaḥ snāti bhṛgukṣetre dvijottama  |
avicāreṇa taṃ viddhi saṃsnātaṃ kurujāṅgale  || 182.45 ||
ahaṃ caiva vasiṣyāmi ambikā ca mama priyā  |
sarvaduḥkhāpahā devī nāmnā saubhāgyasundarī  || 182.46 ||
vasiṣyāmi tayā devyā sahito bhṛgukacchake  |
evamuktvā sthito devo bhṛgukacche'mbikā tathā  || 182.47 ||
bhṛgustu svapuraṃ prāyādbrahmaghoṣanināditam  |
ṛgyajuḥsāmaghoṣeṇa hyatharvaṇanināditam  || 182.48 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā vṛṣamutsṛjate naraḥ  |
sa yāti śivasāyujyamityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 182.49 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā caitre māsi samācaret |
dadyācca lavaṇaṃ vipre pūjya saubhāgyasundarīm  || 182.50 ||
gobhūhiraṇyaṃ viprebhyaḥ prīyetāṃ lalitāśivau  |
na duḥkhaṃ durbhagatvaṃ ca viyogaṃ patinā saha  || 182.51 ||
prāpnoti nārī rājendra bhṛgutīrthāplavena ca  |
yastu nityaṃ bhṛguṃ devaṃ paśyedvai pāṇḍunandana  || 182.52 ||
ā brahmasadanaṃ yāvattatrasthairdaivataiḥ saha  |
yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpottama  || 182.53 ||
suvarṇaśṛṅgīṃ kapilāṃ payasvinīṃ sādhvīṃ suśīlāṃ taruṇīṃ savatsām  |
dattvā dvije sarvavratopapanne phalaṃ ca yatsyāttadihaiva nūnam  || 182.54 ||
samāḥ sahasrāṇi tu sapta vai jale mriyellabheddvādaśavahnimadhye  |
tyajaṃstanuṃ śūravṛttyā narendra śakrātithyaṃ yāti vai martyadharmā  || 182.55 ||
ākhyānametacca sadā yaśasyaṃ svargyaṃ dhanyaṃ putryamāyuṣyakāri  |
śṛṇvaṃllabhetsarvametaddhi bhaktyā parvaṇi parvaṇyājamīḍhassadaiva  || 182.56 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yastu bhṛgutīrthe vidhānataḥ  |
sa mṛtaḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ gacchedvai yacca durlabham  || 182.57 ||
etacchrutvā bhṛguśreṣṭho devadevena bhāṣitam  |
prahṛṣṭavadano bhūtvā tatraiva saṃsthito dvijaḥ  || 182.58 ||
tirobhāvaṃ gate deve bhṛguḥ śreṣṭho dvijottamaḥ  |
svamūrti tatra muktvā tu brahmalokaṃ jagāma ha  || 182.59 ||
bhṛgukacchasya cotpattiḥ kathitā tava pāṇḍava  |
saṃkṣepeṇa mahārāja sarvapāmapraṇāśanī  || 182.60 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ kṣetraṃ devena kīrtitam  |
caturyugasahasreṇa pitāmahadinaṃ smṛtam  || 182.61 ||
prāpte brahmadine viprā jāyate yugasambhavaḥ  |
na paśyāmi tvidaṃ kṣetramiti rudraḥ svayaṃ jagau  || 182.62 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti tvidaṃ bhaktyā nārī vā puruṣo'pi vā  |
sa yāti paramaṃ lokamiti rudraḥ svayaṃ jagau  || 182.63 ||
devakhāte naraḥ snātvā piṇḍadānādisatkriyām  |
yāṃ karoti nṛpaśreṣṭha tāmakṣayaphalāṃ viduḥ  || 182.64 ||
ya imaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā bhṛgukacchasya vistaram  |
koṭitīrthaphalaṃ tasya bhavedvai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 182.65 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhṛgukacchatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvyaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 183

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ mahārāja gacchetkedārasaṃjñakam  |
yatra gatvā mahārāja śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pibejjalam  |
sampūjya devadeveśaṃ kedārotthaṃ phalaṃ labhet || 183.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathamatra suraśreṣṭha kedārākhyaḥ sthitaḥ svayam  |
uttare narmadākūle etadvistarato vada  || 183.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā kṛtayugasyādau śaṅkarastu maheśvaraḥ  |
bhṛguṇārādhitaḥ śaptaḥ śriyā ca bhṛgukacchake  || 183.3 ||
apavitramidaṃ kṣetraṃ sarvavedavivarjitam  |
bhaviṣyati nṛpaśreṣṭha gatetyuktvā haripriyā  || 183.4 ||
tapaścacāra vipulaṃ bhṛgurvarṣasahasrakam  |
vāyubhakṣo nirāhāraściraṃ dhamanisaṃtataḥ  || 183.5 ||
tataḥ pratyakṣatāmāgālliṅgībhūto maheśvaraḥ  |
prādurbhūtastu sahasā bhittvā pātālasaptakam  || 183.6 ||
dadarśātha bhṛgurdevamautpalīṃ kelikāmiva  |
stutiṃ cakre sa devāya sthāṇave tryambaketi ca  || 183.7 ||
evaṃ stutaḥ sa bhagavān provāca prahasanniva  |
punaḥ punarbhṛguṃ mattaḥ kiṃtu prārthayase mune  || 183.8 ||

bhṛguruvāca -
pañcakrośamidaṃ kṣetraṃ padmayā śāpitaṃ vibho  |
upavitramidaṃ kṣetraṃ sarvavedavivarjitam  |
bhaviṣyatīti ca procya gatā devī vidaṃ prati  || 183.9 ||
punaḥ pavitratāṃ yāti yathedaṃ kṣetramuttamam  |
tathā kuru maheśāna prasanno yadi śaṅkara  || 183.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
kedārākhyamidaṃ brahmaṃlliṅgamādyaṃ bhaviṣyati  |
kṛtvedamādiliṅgāni bhaviṣyanti daśaiva hi  || 183.11 ||
ekādaśamadṛśyaṃ hi kṣetramadhye bhaviṣyati  |
pāvayiṣyati tatkṣetramekādaśaḥ svayaṃ vibhuḥ  || 183.12 ||
tathā vai dvādaśādityā matprasādāttu mūrtitaḥ  |
vasiṣyanti bhṛgukṣetre rogaduḥkhanibarhaṇāḥ  || 183.13 ||
durgāḥ hyaṣṭādaśa tathā kṣetrapālāstu ṣoḍaśa  |
bhṛgukṣetre bhaviṣyanti vīrabhadrāśca mātaraḥ  || 183.14 ||
pavitrīkṛtametaddhi nityaṃ kṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati  |
nāghamāse hyuṣaḥkāle snātvā māsaṃ jitendriyaḥ  || 183.15 ||
yaḥ pūjayati kedāraṃ sa gacchecchivamandiram  |
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā pitḥnuddiśya bhārata  |
śrāddhaṃ dadāti vidhivattasya prītāḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 183.16 ||
iti te kathitaṃ samyakkedārākhyaṃ savistaram  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvaduḥkhapraṇāśanam  || 183.17 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kedāreśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tryaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 184

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
dhautapāpaṃ tato gacchedbhṛgutīrthasamīpataḥ  |
vṛṣeṇa tu bhṛgustatra bhūyobhūyo dhutastataḥ  || 184.1 ||
dhautapāpaṃ tu tattena nāmnā lokeṣu viśrutam  |
tatra sthito mahādevastuṣṭyarthaṃ bhṛgusattame  || 184.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā śāṭhyenāpi nareśvara  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 184.3 ||
yastu samyagvidhānena tatra snātvārcayecchivam  |
devānpitḥnsamabhyarcya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 184.4 ||
brahmahatyā gavāṃ vadhyā tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
praviśenna sadā bhītā praviṣṭāpi kṣayaṃ vrajet || 184.5 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryabhūtaṃ loke'sminkathayasva dvijottama  |
praviśenna brahmahatyā yathā vai dhautapāpmani  || 184.6 ||
brahmahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ bhavitā neha kiṃcana  |
kathaṃ vā dhautapāpe tu praviṣṭaṃ naśyate dvija  |
etadvistarataḥ sarvaṃ pṛcchāmi vada kautukāt || 184.7 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ādisarge purā śambhurbrahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
vikāraṃ pañcamaṃ dṛṣṭvā śiro'śvamukhasannibham  || 184.8 ||
aṅguṣṭhāṅguliyogena tacchirastena kṛntitam  |
kṛttamātre tu śirasi brahmahatyābhaktadā  || 184.9 ||
brahmahatyāyutaścāsīduttare narmadātaṭe  |
dhunitaṃ tu yato rājanvṛṣeṇa dharmamūrtinā  || 184.10 ||
tatra dhauteśvarīṃ devīṃ sthāpitāṃ vṛṣabheṇa tu  |
dadarśa bhagavāñchambhuḥ sarvadaivatapūjitām  || 184.11 ||
dṛṣṭvā dhauteśvarīṃ durgāṃ brahmahatyāvināśinīm  |
tatra viśramamāṇaśca śaṅkarastripurāntakaḥ  || 184.12 ||
sa śaṅkaro brahmahatyāvihīnaṃ mene ātmānaṃ tasya tīrthasya bhāvāt |
suvismito devadevo vareṇyo dṛṣṭvā dūre brahmahatyāṃ ca tīrthāt || 184.13 ||
vidhautapāpaṃ mahitaṃ dharmaśaktyā viśenna hatyā devībhayātprabhītā  |
raktāmbarā raktamālyopayuktā kṛṣṇā nārī raktadāmaprasaktā  || 184.14 ||
māṃ vāñchantī skandhadeśaṃ rahasye dūre sthitā tīrthavaryaprabhāvāt |
saṃcintya devo manasā smarārirvāsāya buddhiṃ tatra tīrthe cakāra  || 184.15 ||
vimṛśya devo bahuśaḥ sthitaḥ svayaṃ vidhautapāpaḥ prathitaḥ pṛthivyām  |
babhūva tatraiva nivāsakārī vidhūtapāpanikaṭapradeśe  || 184.16 ||
tadāprabhṛti rājendra brahmahatyāvināśanam  |
vidhautapāpaṃ tattīrthaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 184.17 ||
āśvayukśuklanavamī tatra tīrthe viśiṣyate  |
dinatrayaṃ tu rājendra saptamyādiviśeṣataḥ  || 184.18 ||
samupoṣyāṣṭamīṃ bhaktyā sāṅgaṃ vedaṃ paṭhettu yaḥ  |
ahorātreṇa caikena ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃjñakam  || 184.19 ||
abhyasanbrahmahatyāyā mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
vṛṣalīgamanaṃ caiva yaśca gurvaṅganāgamaḥ  || 184.20 ||
snātvā brahmarasotkṛṣṭe kumbhenaiva pramucyate  |
vandhyā strījananī yā tu kākavandhyā mṛtaprajā  || 184.21 ||
sāpi kumbhodakaiḥ snātā jīvatputrā prajāvatī  |
apaṭhastu nara upoṣya ṛgyajuḥsāmasambhavām  || 184.22 ||
ṛcamekāṃ japanviprastathā parvaṇi yo nṛpa  |
anṛcopoṣya gāyatrīṃ japedvai vedamātaram  || 184.23 ||
japannavamyāṃ viprendro mucyate pāpasañcayāt |
evaṃ tu kathitaṃ tāta purāṇoktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ  || 184.24 ||
dhautapāpaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ śivena kathitaṃ mama  |
prāṇatyāgaṃ tu yaḥ kuryājjale vāgnau sthale'pi vā  || 184.25 ||
sa gacchati vimānena jvalanārkasamaprabhaḥ  |
haṃsabarhiprayuktena sevyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 184.26 ||
śivasya paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatsurairapi durlabham  |
krīḍate svecchayā tatra yāvaccandrārkatārakam  || 184.27 ||
dhautapāpe tu yā nārī kurute prāṇasaṃkṣayam  |
tatkṣaṇādeva sā pārtha puruṣatvamavāpnuyāt || 184.28 ||
atha kiṃ bahunoktena śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvaṃ dhautapāpe kṛtaṃ nṛpa  || 184.29 ||
saṃnyasenniyamenānnaṃ saṃnyasedviṣayādikam  |
phalamūlādikaṃ caiva jalamekaṃ na saṃtyajet || 184.30 ||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute pārtha rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  |
tatra bhuktākhilānbhogāñjāyate bhuvi bhūpatiḥ  || 184.31 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dhautapāpatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 185

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla eraṇḍītīrthamuttamam  |
snānamātreṇa tatraiva brahmahatyā praṇaśyati  || 185.1 ||
māsi cāśvayuje tatra śuklapakṣe caturdaśīm  |
upoṣya prayataḥ snātastarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 185.2 ||
putrarddhirūpasampanno jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  |
śivalokaṃ mṛto yāti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 185.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe eraṇḍītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 186

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla tīrthaṃ kanakhalottamam  |
garuḍena tapastaptaṃ pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 186.1 ||
divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ yāvajjātamātreṇa bhārata  |
tapojapaiḥ kṛśībhūto dṛṣṭo devena śambhunā  || 186.2 ||
tatastuṣṭo mahādevo vainateyaṃ manojavam  |
uvāca paramaṃ vākyaṃ vinatānandavardhanam  || 186.3 ||
prasannaste mahābhāga varaṃ varaya suvrata  |
durlabhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu dadāmi tava khecara  || 186.4 ||

garuḍa uvāca -
icchāmi vāhanaṃ viṣṇordvijendratvaṃ sureśvara  |
prasanne tvayi me sarvaṃ bhavatviti matirmama  || 186.5 ||

śrīmaheśa uvāca -
durlabhaḥ prāṇināṃ tāta yo varaḥ prārthito'nagha  |
devadevasya vāhanaṃ dvijendratvaṃ sudurlabham  || 186.6 ||
nārāyaṇodare sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  |
tvayā sa kathamūhyeta devadevo jagadguruḥ  || 186.7 ||
tenaiva sthāpitaścendrastrailokye sacarācare  |
kathamanyasya cendratvaṃ bhavatīti sudurlabham  || 186.8 ||
tathāpi mama vākyena vāhanaṃ tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi  |
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇervahato'pi jagattrayam  || 186.9 ||
indrastvaṃ pakṣiṇāṃ madhye bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ  |
iti dattvā varaṃ tasmā antardhānaṃ gato haraḥ  || 186.10 ||
tato gate mahādeve hyuruṇasyānujo nṛpa  |
ārādhayāmāsa tadā cāmuṇḍāṃ muṇḍamaṇḍitām  || 186.11 ||
śmaśānavāsinīṃ devīṃ bahubhūtasamanvitām  |
yoginīṃ yogasaṃsiddhāṃ vasāmāṃsāsavapriyām  || 186.12 ||
dhyātamātrā tu tenaiva pratyakṣā hyabhavattadā  |
jālaṃdhare ca yā siddhiḥ kaulīne uḍḍiśe pare  || 186.13 ||
samagrā sā bhṛgukṣetre siddhakṣetre tu saṃsthitā  |
cāmuṇḍā tatra sā devī siddhakṣetre vyavasthitā  || 186.14 ||
saṃstutā ṛṣibhirdevairyogakṣemārthasiddhaye  |
vinatānandajananastatra tāṃ yoginīṃ nṛpa  |
bhaktyā prasādayāmāsa stotrairvaidikalaukikaiḥ  || 186.15 ||

garuḍa uvāca -
oṃ yā sā kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭhā navarudhiramukhā pretapadmāsanasthā bhūtānāṃ vṛndavṛndaiḥ pitṛvananilayā krīḍate śūlahastā  |
śastradhvastapravīravrajarudhiragalanmuṇḍamālottarīyā devī śrīvīramātā vimalaśaśinibhā pātu vaścarmamuṇḍā  || 186.16 ||
yā sā kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭhā vikṛtabhayakarī trāsinī duṣkṛtānāṃ muñcajjvālākalāpairdaśanakasamasaiḥ khādati pretamāṃsam  |
yā sā dordaṇḍacaṇḍairḍamaruraṇaraṇāṭopaṭaṃkāraghaṇṭaiḥ kalpāntotpātavātāhatapaṭupaṭahairvalgate bhūtamātā  |
kṣutkṣāmā śuṣkakukṣiḥ khavarataranaravaraiḥ kṣodati pretamāṃsaṃ muñcantī cāṭṭahāsaṃ ghuraghuritaravā pātu vaścarmamuṇḍā  || 186.17 ||
yā sā nimnodarābhā vikṛtabhavabhayatrāsinī śūlahastā cāmuṇḍā muṇḍaghātā raṇaraṇitaraṇajhallarīnādaramyā  |
trailokyaṃ trāsayanti kakahakahakahairghorarāvairanekairnṛtyantī mātṛmadhye pitṛvananilayā pātu vaścarmamuṇḍā  || 186.18 ||
yā dhatte viśvamakhilaṃ nijāṃśena mahojjvalā  |
kanakaprasave līnā pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.19 ||
himādrisambhavā devī dayādarśitavigrahā  |
śivapriyā śive saktā pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.20 ||
anādijagadādiryā ratnagarbhā vasupriyā  |
rathāṅgapāṇinā padmā pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.21 ||
sāvitrī yā ca gāyatrī mṛḍānī vāgathendirā  |
smartḥṇāṃ yā sukhaṃ datte pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.22 ||
saumyāsaumyaiḥ sadā rūpaiḥ sṛjatyavati yā jagat |
parā śaktiḥ parā buddhiḥ pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.23 ||
brahmaṇaḥ sargasamaye sṛjyaśaktiḥ parā tu yā  |
jaganmāyā jagaddhātrī pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.24 ||
viśvasya pālane viṣṇoryā śaktiḥ paripālikā  |
madanonmādinī mukhyā pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.25 ||
viśvasaṃlayane mukhyā yā rudreṇa samāśritā  |
raudrī śaktiḥ śivānantā pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.26 ||
kailāsasānusaṃrūḍha kanakaprasaveśayā  |
bhasmakābhihṛtā pūrvaṃ pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.27 ||
patiprabhāvamicchantī trasyantī yā vinā patim  |
abalā tvekabhāvā ca pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.28 ||
viśvasaṃrakṣaṇe saktā rakṣitā kanakena yā  |
ā brahmastambajananī pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.29 ||
brahmaviṣṇvīśvarāḥ śaktyā śarīragrahaṇaṃ yayā  |
prāpitāḥ prathamā śaktiḥ pātu māṃ kanakeśvarī  || 186.30 ||
śrutvā tu garuḍenoktaṃ devīvṛttacatuṣṭayam  |
prasannā saṃmukhī bhūtvā vākyametaduvāca ha  || 186.31 ||

śrīcāmuṇḍovāca -
prasannā te mahāsattva varaṃ varaya vāñchitam  |
dadāmi te dvijaśreṣṭha yatte manasi rocate  || 186.32 ||

garuḍa uvāca -
ajaraścāmaraścaiva adhṛṣyaśca surāsuraiḥ  |
tava prasādāccaivānyairajeyaśca bhavāmyaham  || 186.33 ||
tvayā cātra sadā devi sthātavyaṃ tīrthasannidhau  |

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ bhaviṣyatītyuktvā devī devairabhiṣṭutā  || 186.34 ||
jagāmākāśamāviśya bhūtasaṅghasamanvitā  |
yadā lakṣmyā nṛpaśreṣṭha sthāpitaṃ puramuttamam  || 186.35 ||
anumānya tadā devīṃ kṛtaṃ tasyāṃ samarpitam  |

lakṣmīruvāca -
rakṣaṇāya mayā devi yogakṣemārthasiddhaye  || 186.36 ||
mātṛvatpratipālyaṃ te sadā devi puraṃ mama  |
garuḍo'pi tataḥ snātvā sampūjya kanakeśvarīm  || 186.37 ||
tīrthaṃ tatraiva saṃsthāpya jagāmākāśamuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 186.38 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute  |
gandhapuṣpādibhiryastu pūjayetkanakeśvaram  || 186.39 ||
tasya yogaiśvaryasiddhiryogapīṭheṣu jāyate  |
mṛto yogeśvaraṃ lokaṃ jayaśabdādimaṅgalaiḥ  |
sa gacchennātra sandeho yoginīgaṇasaṃyutaḥ  || 186.40 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kanakhaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 187

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
jāleśvaraṃ tato gacchelliṅgamādyaṃ svayambhuvaḥ  |
kālāgnirudraṃ vikhyātaṃ bhṛgukacche vyavasthitam  || 187.1 ||
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam  |
kṣetrapāpavināśāya kṛpayā ca samutthitam  || 187.2 ||
purā kalpe'suragaṇairākrānte bhuvanatraye  |
vedoktakarmanāśe ca dharme ca vilayaṃ gate  || 187.3 ||
devarṣimunisiddheṣu viśvāsaparameṣu ca  |
kālāgnirudrādutpanno dhūmaḥ kālodbhavodbhavaḥ  || 187.4 ||
dhūmātsamutthitaṃ liṅgaṃ bhittvā pātālasaptakam  |
avaṭaṃ dakṣiṇe kṛtvā liṅgaṃ tatraiva tiṣṭhati  || 187.5 ||
tatra tīrthe nṛpaśreṣṭha kuṇḍaṃ jvālāsamudbhavam  |
yatra sā patitā jvālā śivasya dahataḥ puram  || 187.6 ||
tatrāvaṭaṃ samudbhūtaṃ dhūmāvartastato'bhavat |
tasminkuṇḍe tu yaḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā vai narmadājale  || 187.7 ||
kuryācchrāddhaṃ pitṛbhyo vai pūjayecca trilocanam  |
kālāgnirudranāmāni sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  || 187.8 ||
yatkiṃcitkāmikaṃ karma hyābhicārikameva vā  |
ripusaṃkṣayakṛdvāpi sāṃtānikamathāpi vā  |
atra tīrthe kṛtaṃ sarvamacirātsidhyate nṛpa  || 187.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kālāgnirudratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 188

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ paraṃ mahārāja catvāriṃśatkramāntare  |
śālagrāmaṃ tato gacchetsarvadaivatapūjitam  || 188.1 ||
yatrādidevo bhagavānvāsudevastrivikramaḥ  |
svayaṃ tiṣṭhati lokātmā sarveṣāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 188.2 ||
nāradena tapastaptvā kṛtā śālā dvijanmanām  |
siddhikṣetraṃ bhṛgukṣetraṃ jñātvā revātaṭe svayam  || 188.3 ||
śālagrāmābhidho devo viprāṇāṃ tvadhivāsitaḥ  |
sādhūnāṃ copakārāya vāsudevaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 188.4 ||
yogināmupakārāya yogidhyeyo janārdanaḥ  |
śālagrāmeti tenaiva narmadātaṭamāśritaḥ  || 188.5 ||
māsi mārgaśire śuklā bhavatyekādaśī yadā  |
snātvā revājale puṇye taddinaṃ samupoṣayet || 188.6 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryātsampūjya ca janārdanam  |
punaḥ prabhātasamaye dvādaśyāṃ narmadājale  || 188.7 ||
snātvā saṃtarpya devāṃśca pitḥnmātḥstathaiva ca  |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścātpitṛbhyo vidhipūrvakam  || 188.8 ||
śaktito brāhmaṇānpūjya svarṇavastrānnadānataḥ  |
kṣamāpayitvā tānviprāṃstathā devaṃ khagadhvajam  || 188.9 ||
evaṃ kṛte mahārāja yatpuṇyaṃ ca bhavennḥṇām  |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā tatpuṇyaṃ nṛpasattama  || 188.10 ||
na śokaduḥkhe pratipatsyatīha jīvanmṛto yāti murārisāmyam  |
mahānti pāpāni visṛjya dugdhaṃ punarna mātuḥ pibate stanodyat || 188.11 ||
