śālagrāmaṃ paśyate yo hi nityaṃ snātvā jale nārmade'ghaughahāre  |
sa mucyate brahmahatyādipāpairnārāyaṇānusmaraṇena tena  || 188.12 ||
vasanti ye saṃnyasitvā ca tatra nigṛhya duḥkhāni vimuktasaṅghāḥ  |
dhyāyanto vai sāṃkhyavṛttyā turīyaṃ padaṃ murāreste'pi tatraiva yānti  || 188.13 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śālagrāmatīrthamahātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 189

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
udīrṇo yatra vārāho hyabhavaddharaṇīdharaḥ  || 189.1 ||
dhanvadaṃṣṭrāṃ karālāgrāṃ bibhracca pṛthivīmimām  |
sa eva pañcamaḥ prokto vārāho muktidāyakaḥ  || 189.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathamudīrṇarūpo'bhūdvārāho dharaṇīdharaḥ  |
vārāhatvaṃ gataḥ kena pañcamaḥ kena saṃjñitaḥ  || 189.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ādikalpe purā rājankṣīrode bhagavān hariḥ  |
śete sa bhogiśayane yoganidrāvimohitaḥ  || 189.4 ||
babhūva nṛpatiśreṣṭha gatvā vai devasaṃnidhau  |
avocadbhārakhinnāhaṃ gamiṣyāmi rasātalam  || 189.5 ||
dṛṣṭvā devāḥ samudvignā gatā yatra janārdanaḥ  |
tuṣṭuvurvāgbhiriṣṭābhiḥ keśavaṃ jagatpatim  || 189.6 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
namo namaste deveśa surārtihara sarvaga  |
viśvamūrte namastubhyaṃ trāhi sarvānmahadbhayāt || 189.7 ||
ityukto daivatairdevo hyuvāca kimupasthitam  |
kāryaṃ vadadhvaṃ me devā yatkṛtyaṃ mā ciraṃ kṛthāḥ  || 189.8 ||
devā ūcuḥ  |
dharā dharitrī bhūtānāṃ bhārodvignā nimajjati  |
tāmuddhara hṛṣīkeśa lokānsaṃsthāpaya sthitau  || 189.9 ||
evamuktaḥ suraiḥ sarvaiḥ keśavaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthāya sarvayajñamayaṃ vibhuḥ  || 189.10 ||
daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ piṅgākṣaṃ samākuñcitamūrdhajam  |
kṛtvā'nantaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ daṃṣṭrāgreṇoddharanbhuvam  || 189.11 ||
saparvatavanāmurvīṃ samudraparimekhalām  |
uddhṛtya bhagavān viṣṇurudīrṇaḥ samajāyata  || 189.12 ||
darśayanpañcadhātmānamuttare narmadātaṭe  |
tathādyaṃ koralāyāṃ tu dvitīyaṃ yodhanīpure  || 189.13 ||
jayakṣetrābhidhāne tu jayeti parikīrtitam  |
asurānmohayalliṅgastṛtīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 189.14 ||
pāvanāya jagaddhetoḥ sthito yasmācchaśiprabhaḥ  |
atastu nṛpaśārdūla śveta ityābhidhīyate  || 189.15 ||
uddhṛtya jagatāṃ devīmudīrṇo bhṛgukacchake  |
tataḥ pañcama udīrṇo varāha iti saṃjñitaḥ  || 189.16 ||
iti pañcavarāhāste kathitaḥ pāṇḍunandana  |
yugapaddarśanaṃ caiṣāṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 189.17 ||
jyeṣṭhe māsi site pakṣa ekādaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
gatvā hyādivarāhaṃ tu samprāpte daśamīdine  || 189.18 ||
haviṣyamannaṃ bhuñjīyāllaghusāyaṃ gate ravau  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryādvārāhe hyādisaṃjñake  || 189.19 ||
tataḥ prabhāte hyuṣasi saṃsnātvā narmadājale  |
saṃtarpya pitṛdevāṃśca tilairyavavimiśritaiḥ  || 189.20 ||
dhenuṃ dadyāddvije yogye sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  |
nirmamo nirahaṅkāro dānaṃ dadyāddvijātaye  || 189.21 ||
gatvā sampūjayeddevaṃ vārāhaṃ hyādisaṃjñitam  |
anena vidhinā pūjya paścādgacchejjayaṃ tvaran  || 189.22 ||
tvaritaṃ tu jayaṃ gatvā pūrvakaṃ vidhimācaret |
aśvaṃ dadyāddvijāgryāya jayapūrvābhinirgatam  || 189.23 ||
liṅge caiva tilā deyāḥ śvete hiraṇyameva ca  |
udīrṇe ca bhuvaṃ dadyātpūrvakaṃ vidhimācaret || 189.24 ||
anastamita āditye varāhānpañca paśyataḥ  |
yatphalaṃ labhate pārtha tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu  || 189.25 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ  |
ebhistu saha saṃyogo viśvastānāṃ ca vañcanam  || 189.26 ||
svasṛduhitṛbhaginīkuladāropabṛṃhaṇam  |
ā janmamaraṇādyāvatpāpaṃ bharatasattama  || 189.27 ||
tīrthapañcakapūtasya vaiṣṇavasya viśeṣataḥ  |
yugapaccavinaśyeta tūlarāśirivānalāt || 189.28 ||
nārāyaṇānusmaraṇājjapadhyānādviśeṣataḥ  |
vipraṇaśyanti pāpāni girikūṭasamānyapi  || 189.29 ||
dṛṣṭvā pañca varāhānvai pauruṣe mahati sthitaḥ  |
āplavannarmadātoye śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 189.30 ||
udayāstamanādarvāgyaḥ paśyelloṭaṇeśvaram  |
kalevaravimuktaḥ sa ityevaṃ śaṅkaro'bravīt || 189.31 ||
muktiṃ prayāti sahasā duṣprāpāṃ parameśvarīm  |
pauruṣe kriyamāṇe'pi na siddhirjāyate yadi  || 189.32 ||
bruvanti svargagamanamapi pāpānvitasya ca  |
yatra tatra gatasyaiva bhavetpañcavarāhakī  || 189.33 ||
jyeṣṭhasyaikādaśītithau dhruvaṃ tatra vasennaraḥ  |
ādiṃ jayaṃ tathā śvetaṃ liṅgamudīrṇameva ca  || 189.34 ||
āśritya tasyā draṣṭavyā varāhāstu yatastataḥ  |
jyeṣṭhasyaikādaśītithau viṣṇunā prabhuviṣṇunā  || 189.35 ||
vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthāya uddhṛtā dharaṇī vibho  |
puṇyātpuṇyatamā tena hyaśeṣāghaughanāśinī  || 189.36 ||
dṛṣṭvā pañcavarāhānvai kroḍamudīrṇarūpiṇam  |
pūjayitvā vidhānena paścājjāgaraṇaṃ caret || 189.37 ||
sapañcavartikān dīpān ghṛtenojjvālya bhaktitaḥ  |
purāṇaśravaṇairnṛtyairgītavādyaiḥ sumaṅgalaiḥ  || 189.38 ||
vedajāpyaiḥ pavitraiśca kṣapayitvā ca śarvarīm  |
yatpuṇyaṃ labhate martyo hyājamīḍha śṛṇuṣva tat || 189.39 ||
revājalaṃ puṇyatamaṃ pṛthivyāṃ tathā ca devo jagatāṃ patirhariḥ  |
ekādaśī pāpaharā narendra bahvāyāsairlabhyate mānavānām  || 189.40 ||
ekaikaśo brahmahatyādikāni śaktāni hantuṃ pāpasaṅghāni rājan  |
naite sarve yugapadvai sametā hantuṃ śaktāḥ kiṃ na tadbrūhi rājan  || 189.41 ||
yathedamuktaṃ tava dharmasūno śrutaṃ ca yacchaṅkarāccandramauleḥ  |
śrutvedamicchanmucyate sarvapāpaiḥ paṭhanpadaṃ yāti hi vṛtraśatroḥ  || 189.42 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye udīrṇavarāhatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 190

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla somatīrthamanuttamam  |
candrahāseti vikhyātaṃ sarvadaivatapūjitam  || 190.1 ||
yatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāptaḥ somo rājā surottamaḥ  || 190.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ siddhimanuprāptaḥ somo rājā jagatpatiḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva mamānagha  || 190.3 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purā śapto munīndreṇa dakṣeṇa kila bhārata  |
asevanāddhi dārāṇāṃ kṣayarogī bhaviṣyasi  || 190.4 ||
udvāhitānāṃ patnīnāṃ ye na kurvanti sevanam  |
yā niṣṭhā jāyate teṣāṃ tāṃ śṛṇuṣva narottama  || 190.5 ||
ṛtukāle tu nārīṇāṃ sevanājjāyate sutaḥ  |
sutātsvargaśca mokṣaśca hītyevaṃ śrutinodanā  || 190.6 ||
tatkālocitadharmeṇa ye na sevanti tāṃ narāḥ  |
teṣāṃ brahmaghnajaṃ pāpaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 190.7 ||
tena pāpena ghoreṇa veṣṭato raurave patet |
tasya tadrudhiraṃ pāpāḥ pibante kālamīpsitam  || 190.8 ||
tato'vatīrṇakālena yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ prayāsyati  |
tasyāṃ tasyāṃ sa duṣṭātmā durbhago jāyate sadā  || 190.9 ||
nārīṇāṃ tu sadā kāmo hyadhikaḥ parivartate  |
viśeṣeṇa ṛtoḥ kāle bhidyate kāmasāyakaiḥ  || 190.10 ||
paribhūtā hi sā bhartrā dhyāyate'nyaṃ patiṃ tataḥ  |
tasyāḥ putraḥ samutpanno hyaṭate kulamuttamam  || 190.11 ||
svargasthāstena pitaraḥ pūrvaṃ jātā mahīpate  |
patanti jātamātreṇa kulaṭastena cocyate  || 190.12 ||
tena karmavipākena kṣayarogī śaśī hyabhūt |
tyaktvā lokaṃ surendrāṇāṃ martyalokamupāgataḥ  || 190.13 ||
tatra tīrthānyanekāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca  |
bhramitvā narmadāṃ prāptaḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm  || 190.14 ||
upavāsastu dānāni vratāni niyamāśca ye  |
cacāra dvādaśābdāni tato muktaḥ sa kilbiṣaiḥ  || 190.15 ||
sthāpayitvā mahādevaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  |
jagāma prabhayā pūrṇaḥ somalokamanuttamam  || 190.16 ||
yenaiva sthāpito devaḥ pūjyate varṣasaṃkhyayā  |
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi tasya lokaṃ samaśnute  || 190.17 ||
tena devān vidhānoktān sthāpayanti narā bhuvi  |
akṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ yasmātphalaṃ bhavati nānyathā  || 190.18 ||
somatīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā pūjayeddevamīśvaram  |
jāyate sa naro bhūtvā somavitpriyadarśanaḥ  || 190.19 ||
candraprabhāse yo gatvā snānaṃ vidhivadācaret |
vyādhinā nābhibhūtaḥ syātkṣayarogeṇa vā yutaḥ  || 190.20 ||
candrahāsye naraḥ snātvā dvādaśyāṃ tu nareśvara  |
caturdaśyāmupoṣyaiva kṣīrasya juhuyāccarum  || 190.21 ||
mantraiḥ pañcabhirīśānaṃ puruṣastryambakaṃ yajet |
haviḥśeṣaṃ svayaṃ prāśya candrahāsyeśamīkṣayet || 190.22 ||
anena vidhinā rājaṃstuṣṭo devo maheśvaraḥ  |
vidhinā tīrthayogena kṣayarogādvimucyate  || 190.23 ||
saptabhiḥ somavārairyaḥ snānaṃ tatra samācaret |
sa vai karṇakṛtādrogānmucyate pūjayañchivam  || 190.24 ||
akṣirogastathā rājaṃścandrahāsye vinaśyati  |
candrahāsye tu yo gatvā grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
snānaṃ samācaredbhaktyā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 190.25 ||
tatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca candrahāsye śubhaśubham  |
kṛtaṃ nṛpavaraśreṣṭha sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 190.26 ||
te dhanyāste mahātmānasteṣāṃ janma sujīvitam  |
candrahāsye tu ye snātvā paśyanti grahaṇaṃ narāḥ  || 190.27 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurā kṛtam  |
snānamātrāttu rājendra tatra tīrthe praṇaśyati  || 190.28 ||
bahavastanna jānanti mahāmohasamanvitāḥ  |
dehastha iva sarveṣāṃ paramātmeva saṃsthitam  || 190.29 ||
paścime sāgare gatvā somatīrthe tu yatphalam  |
tatsamagramavāpnoti candrahāsye na saṃśayaḥ  || 190.30 ||
saṃkrāntau ca vyatīpāte viṣuve cāyane tathā  |
candrahāsye naraḥ snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 190.31 ||
te mūḍhāste durācārāsteṣāṃ janma nirarthakam  |
candrahāsyaṃ na jānanti narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  || 190.32 ||
candrahāsye tu yaḥ kaścitsaṃnyāsaṃ kurute nṛpa  |
anivartikā gatistasya somalokātkadācana  || 190.33 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe candrahāsyatīrthamahātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma navatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 191

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
siddheśvaraṃ tato gacchettasyaiva tu samīpataḥ  |
amṛtasrāvi talliṅgamādyaṃ svāyambhuvaṃ tathā  || 191.1 ||
dṛṣṭamātreṇa yeneha hyanṛṇo jāyate naraḥ  |
purā varṣaśataṃ sāgramārādhya parameśvaram  || 191.2 ||
prāpnuyuḥ paramāṃ siddhimādityā dvādaśaiva tu  |
ataḥ siddheśvaraḥ proktaḥ siddhidaḥ siddhikāṅkṣiṇām  || 191.3 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ siddheśvare prāptāḥ siddhiṃ devā dvijottama  |
ādityā iti yaccoktaṃ tanme vismāpanaṃ kṛtam  || 191.4 ||
tapasyugre vyavasitā ādityāḥ kena hetunā  |
samprāptāstu dvijaśreṣṭha siddhiṃ caivābhilāṣikīm  || 191.5 ||
saṃkṣipya tu mayā pṛṣṭaṃ vistarāddvija śaṃsa me  || 191.6 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
aditerdvādaśādityā jātāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ  |
indro dhātā bhagastvaṣṭā mitro'tha varuṇo'ryamā  || 191.7 ||
vivasvānsavitā pūṣā hyaṃśumānviṣṇureva ca  |
ta ime dvādaśādityā icchanto bhāskaraṃ padam  || 191.8 ||
narmadātaṭamāśritya tapasyugre vyavasthitāḥ  |
siddheśvare mahārāja kāśyapeyairmahātmabhiḥ  || 191.9 ||
parā siddhiranuprāptā dvādaśādityasaṃjñitaiḥ  |
sthāpitaśca jagaddhātā tasmiṃstīrthe divākaraḥ  || 191.10 ||
svakīyāṃśavibhāgena dvādaśādityasaṃjñitaiḥ  |
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ rājankhyātiṃ gataṃ bhuvi  || 191.11 ||
pralaye samanuprāpte hyādityā dvādaśaiva te  |
dvādaśādityato rājan sambhavanti yugakṣaye  || 191.12 ||
indrastapati pūrveṇa dhātā caivāgnigocare  |
gabhastipatirvai yāmye tvaṣṭā nairṛtadiṅmukhaḥ  || 191.13 ||
varuṇaḥ paścime bhāge mitrastu vāyave tathā  |
viṣṇuśca saumyadigbhāge vivasvānīśagocare  || 191.14 ||
ūrdhvataścaiva savitā hyadhaḥ pūṣā viśoṣayan  |
aṃśumāṃstu tathā viṣṇurmukhato nirgataṃ jagat || 191.15 ||
pradahanvai naraśreṣṭha babhramuśca itastataḥ  |
yathaiva te mahārāja dahanti sakalaṃ jagat || 191.16 ||
tathaiva dvādaśādityā bhaktānāṃ bhāvasādhanāḥ  |
prātarutthāya yaḥ snātvā dvādaśādityasaṃjñitam  || 191.17 ||
paśyate devadeveśaṃ śṛṇu tasyaiva yatphalam  |
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmajaṃ yatpurākṛtam  || 191.18 ||
naśyate tatkṣaṇādeva dvādaśādityadarśanāt |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasya devasya bhārata  || 191.19 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
tatra tīrthe tu saptamyāmupavāsena yatphalam  || 191.20 ||
anyatra saptasaptamyāṃ labhanti na labhanti ca  |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ vāre dainakare dvādaśādityadarśanāt || 191.21 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasya pāpaṃ tu naśyati  |
arogī saptajanmāni bhavedvai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 191.22 ||
yastu pradakṣiṇaśataṃ dadyādbhaktyā dine dine  |
dadrūpiṭakakuṣṭhāni maṇḍalāni vicarcikāḥ  || 191.23 ||
naśyanti vyādhayaḥ sarve garuḍeneva pannagāḥ  |
putraprāptirbhavettasya ṣaṣṭyā vāsarasevanāt || 191.24 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe dvādaśādityatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 192

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ tāta devatīrthamanuttamam  |
dṛṣṭvā tu śrīpatiṃ pāpairmucyate mānavo bhuvi  || 192.1 ||
maharṣestasya jāmātā bhṛgordevo janārdanaḥ  || 192.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ko'yaṃ śriyaḥ patirdevo devānāmadhipo vibhuḥ  |
kathaṃ janmābhavattasya deveṣu triṣu vā mune  || 192.3 ||
sambandhī ca kathaṃ jāto bhṛguṇā saha keśavaḥ  |
etadvistarato brahman vaktumarhasi bhārgava  || 192.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
saṃkṣepātkathayiṣyāmi sādhyasya caritaṃ mahat |
na hi vistarato vaktuṃ śaktāḥ sarve maharṣayaḥ  || 192.5 ||
nārāyaṇasya nābhyabjājjāto devaścaturmukhaḥ  |
tasya dakṣo'ṅgajo rājan dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhasambhavaḥ  || 192.6 ||
dharmaḥ stanāntātsaṃjātastasya putro'bhavatkila  |
nārāyaṇasahāyo'sāvajo'pi bharatarṣabha  || 192.7 ||
marutvatī vasurjñānā lambā bhānumatī satī  |
saṃkalpā ca muhūrtā ca sādhyā viśvāvatī kakup || 192.8 ||
dharmapatnyo daśaivaitā dākṣāyaṇyo mahāprabhāḥ  |
tāsāṃ sādhyā mahābhāgā putrānajanayannṛpa  || 192.9 ||
naro nārāyaṇaścaiva hariḥ kṛṣṇastathaiva ca  |
viṣṇoraṃśāṃśakā hyete catvāro dharmasūnavaḥ  || 192.10 ||
tathā nārāyaṇanarau gandhamādanaparvate  |
ātmanyātmānamādhāya tepatuḥ paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 192.11 ||
dhyāyamānāvanaupamyaṃ svaṃ kāraṇamakāraṇam  |
vāsudevamanirdeśyamapratarkyamanantaram  || 192.12 ||
yogayuktau mahātmānāvāsthitāvurutāpasau  |
tayostapaḥprabhāveṇa na tatāpa divākaraḥ  || 192.13 ||
vavāha śaṅkito vāyuḥ sukhasparśo hyaśaṅkitaḥ  |
śiśiro'bhavadatyarthaṃ jvalannapi vibhāvasuḥ  || 192.14 ||
siṃhavyāghrādayaḥ saumyāśceruḥ saha mṛgairgirau  |
tayorgauriva bhārārtā pṛthivī pṛthivīpate  || 192.15 ||
ceruśca bhūdharāścaiva cukṣubhe ca mahodadhiḥ  |
devāśca sveṣu dhiṣṇyeṣu niṣprabheṣu hataprabhāḥ  |
babhūvuravanīpāla paramaṃ kṣobhamāgatāḥ  || 192.16 ||
devarājastathā śakraḥ saṃtaptastapasā tayoḥ  |
yuyojāpsarasastatra tayorvighnacikīrṣayā  || 192.17 ||

indra uvāca -
rambhe tilottame kubje ghṛtāci lalite śubhe  |
pramloce subhru sumloce saurabheyi mahoddhate  || 192.18 ||
alambuṣe miśrakeśi puṇḍarīke varūthini  |
vilokanīyaṃ bibhrāṇā vapurmanmathabodhanam  || 192.19 ||
gandhamādanamāsādya kurudhvaṃ vacanaṃ mama  |
naranārāyaṇau tatra tapodīkṣānvitau dvijau  || 192.20 ||
tepāte dharmatanayau tapaḥ paramaduścaram  |
tāvasmākaṃ varārohāḥ kurvāṇau paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 192.21 ||
karmātiśayaduḥkhārtipradāvāyatināśanau  |
tadgacchata na bhīḥ kāryā bhavatībhiridaṃ vacaḥ  || 192.22 ||
smaraḥ sahāyo bhavitā vasantaśca varāṅganāḥ  |
rūpaṃ vayaḥ samālokya madanoddīpanaṃ param  |
kandarpavaśamabhyeti vivaśaḥ ko na mānavaḥ  || 192.23 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ityuktvā devarājena madanena samaṃ tadā  |
jagmurapsarasaḥ sarvā vasantaśca mahīpate  || 192.24 ||
gandhamādanamāsādya puṃskokilakulākulam  |
cacāra mādhavo ramyaṃ protphullavanapādapam  || 192.25 ||
pravavau dakṣiṇāśāyāṃ malayānugato'nilaḥ  |
bhṛṅgamālārutaravai ramaṇīyamabhūdvanam  || 192.26 ||
gandhaśca surabhiḥ sadyo vanarājisamudbhavaḥ  |
kinnaroragayakṣāṇāṃ babhūva ghrāṇatarpaṇaḥ  || 192.27 ||
varāṅganāśca tāḥ sarvā naranārāyaṇāvṛṣī  |
vilobhayitumārabdhā vāgaṅgalalitasmitaiḥ  || 192.28 ||
jagau manoharaṃ kācinnanarta tatra cāpsarāḥ  |
avādayattathaivānyā manoharataraṃ nṛpa  || 192.29 ||
hāvairbhāvaiḥ sṛtairhāsyaistathānyā valgubhāṣitaiḥ  |
tayoḥ kṣobhāya tanvaṅgyaścakrurudyamamaṅganāḥ  || 192.30 ||
tathāpi na tayoḥ kaścinmanasaḥ pṛthivīpate  |
vikāro'bhavadadhyātmapārasamprāptacetasoḥ  || 192.31 ||
nivātasthau yathā dīpāvakampau nṛpa tiṣṭhataḥ  |
vāsudevārpaṇasvasthe tathaiva manasī tayoḥ  || 192.32 ||
pūryamāṇo'pi cāmbhobhirbhuvamanyāṃ mahodadhiḥ  |
yathā na yāti saṃkṣobhaṃ tathā tanmānasaṃ kvacit || 192.33 ||
sarvabhūtahitaṃ brahma vāsudevamayaṃ param  |
manyamānau na rāgasya dveṣasya ca vaśaṃgatau  || 192.34 ||
smaro'pi na śaśākātha praveṣṭuṃ hṛdayaṃ tayoḥ  |
vidyāmayaṃ dīpayutamandhakāra ivālayam  || 192.35 ||
puṣpojjvalāṃstaruvarān vasantaṃ dakṣiṇānilam  |
tāścaivāpsarasaḥ sarvāḥ kandarpaṃ ca mahāmunī  || 192.36 ||
yaccārabdhaṃ tapastābhyāmātmānaṃ gandhamādanam  |
dadarśāte'khilaṃ rūpaṃ brahmaṇaḥ puruṣarṣabha  || 192.37 ||
dāhāya nāmalo vahnernāpaḥ kledāya cāmbhasaḥ  |
taddravyameva taddravyavikārāya na vai yataḥ  || 192.38 ||
tato vijñāya vijñāya paraṃ brahma svarūpataḥ  |
madhukandarpayoṣitsu vikāro nābhavattayoḥ  || 192.39 ||
tato gurutaraṃ yatnaṃ vasantamadanau nṛpa  |
cakrāte tāśca tanvaṅgyastatkṣobhāya punaḥpunaḥ  || 192.40 ||
atha nārāyaṇo dhairyaṃ saṃdhāyodīrṇamānasaḥ  |
ūrorutpādayāmāsa varāṅgīmabalāṃ tadā  || 192.41 ||
trailokyasundarīratnamaśeṣamavanīpate  |
guṇairlāghavamabhyeti yasyāḥ saṃdarśanādanu  || 192.42 ||
tāṃ vilokya mahīpāla cakampe manasānilaḥ  |
vasanto vismayaṃ yātaḥ smaraḥ sasmāra kiṃcana  || 192.43 ||
rambhātilottamādyāśca vailakṣyaṃ devayoṣitaḥ  |
na rejuravanīpāla tallakṣyahṛdayekṣaṇāḥ  || 192.44 ||
tataḥ kāmo vasantaśca pārthivāpsarasaśca tāḥ  |
praṇamya bhagavantau tau tuṣṭuvurmunisattamau  || 192.45 ||
vasantakāmāpsarasa ūcuḥ  |
prasīdatu jagaddhātā yasya devasya māyayā  |
mohitāḥ sma vijānīmo nāntaraṃ vidyate dvayoḥ  || 192.46 ||
prasīdatu sa vāṃ devo yasya rūpamidaṃ dvidhā  |
dhāmabhūtasya lokānāmanāderapratiṣṭhataḥ  || 192.47 ||
naranārāyaṇau devau śaṅkhacakrāyudhāvubhau  |
āstāṃ prasādasumukhāvasmākamaparādhinām  || 192.48 ||
nidhānaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ sarvapāpavanānalaḥ  |
nārāyaṇo'to bhagavān sarvapāpaṃ vyapohatu  || 192.49 ||
śārṅgacihnāyudhaḥ śrīmānātmajñānamayo'naghaḥ  |
naraḥ samastapāpāni hatātmā sarvadehinām  || 192.50 ||
jaṭākalāpabaddho'yamanayornaḥ kṣamāvatoḥ  |
saumyāsyadṛṣṭiḥ pāpāni hantuṃ janmārjitāni vai  || 192.51 ||
tathātmavidyādoṣeṇa yo'parādhaḥ kṛto mahān  |
trailokyavandyau yau nāthau vilobhayitumāgatāḥ  || 192.52 ||
prasīda deva vijñānadhana mūḍhadṛśāmiva  |
bhavanti santaḥ satataṃ svadharmaparipālakāḥ  || 192.53 ||
dṛṣṭvaitannaḥ samutpannaṃ yathā strīratnamuttamam  |
tvayi nārāyaṇotpannā śreṣṭhā pāravatī matiḥ  || 192.54 ||
tena satyena satyātmanparamātmansanātana  |
nārāyaṇa prasīdeśa sarvalokaparāyaṇa  || 192.55 ||
prasannabuddhe śāntātmanprasannavadanekṣaṇa  |
prasīda yogināmīśa nara sarvagatācyuta  || 192.56 ||
namasyāmo naraṃ devaṃ tathā nārāyaṇaṃ harim  |
namo narāya namyāya namo nārāyaṇāya ca  || 192.57 ||
prasannānāmanāthānāṃ tathā nāthavatāṃ prabho  |
śaṃ karotu naro'smākaṃ śaṃ nārāyaṇa dehi naḥ  || 192.58 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evamabhyarcitaḥ stutyā rāgadveṣādivarjitaḥ  |
prāheśaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ madhye nārāyaṇo nṛpa  || 192.59 ||

nārāyaṇa uvāca -
svāgataṃ mādhave kāme bhavatvapsarasāmapi  |
yatkāryamāgatānāṃ ca ihāsmābhistaducyatām  || 192.60 ||
yūyaṃ saṃsiddhaye nūnamasmākaṃ balaśatruṇā  |
saṃpreṣitāstato'smākaṃ nṛtyayogādidarśanam  || 192.61 ||
na vayaṃ gītanṛtyena nāṅgaceṣṭādibhāṣitaiḥ  |
lubdhā vai viṣayairmanye viṣayā dāruṇātmakāḥ  || 192.62 ||
śabdādisaṅgaduṣṭāni yadā nākṣāṇi naḥ śubhāḥ  |
tadā nṛtyādayo bhāvāḥ kathaṃ lobhapradāyinaḥ  || 192.63 ||
te siddhāḥ sma na vai sādhyā bhavatīnāṃ smarasya ca  |
mādhavasya ca śākro'pi svāsthyaṃ yātvaviśaṅkitāḥ  || 192.64 ||
yo'sau paraśca paramaḥ puruṣaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
paramātmā samastasya sthāvarasya carasya ca  || 192.65 ||
utpattiheturete ca yasminsarvaṃ pralīyate  |
sarvāvāsīti devatvādvāsudevetyudāhṛtaḥ  || 192.66 ||
vayamaṃśāṃśakāstasya caturvyūhasya māninaḥ  |
tadādeśitavārtmānau jagadbodhāya dehinām  || 192.67 ||
tatsarvabhūtaṃ sarveśaṃ sarvatra samadarśinam  |
kutaḥ paśyantau rāgādīnkariṣyāmo vibhedinaḥ  || 192.68 ||
vasante mayi cendre ca bhavatīṣu tathā smare  |
yadā sa eva bhūtātmā tadā dveṣādayaḥ katham  || 192.69 ||
tanmayānyavibhaktāni yadā sarveṣu jantuṣu  |
sarveśvareśvaro viṣṇuḥ kuto rāgādayastataḥ  || 192.70 ||
brahmāṇamindramīśānamādityamaruto'khilān  |
viśvedevānṛṣīn sādhyānvasūnpitṛgaṇāṃstathā  || 192.71 ||
yakṣarākṣasabhūtādīnnāgānsarpānsarīsṛpān  |
manuṣyapakṣigorūpagajasiṃhajalecarān  || 192.72 ||
makṣikāmaśakāndaṃśāñchalabhāñjalajān kṛmīn  |
gulmavṛkṣalatāvallītvaksāratṛṇajātiṣu  || 192.73 ||
yacca kiṃcidadṛśyaṃ vā dṛśyaṃ vā tridaśāṅganāḥ  |
manyadhvaṃ jātamekasya tatsarvaṃ paramātmanaḥ  || 192.74 ||
jāyamānaḥ kathaṃ viṣṇumātmānaṃ paramaṃ ca yat |
rāgadveṣau tathā lobhaṃ kaḥ kuryādamarāṅganāḥ  || 192.75 ||
sarvabhūtamaye viṣṇau sarvage sarvadhātari  |
nipātya taṃ pṛthagbhūte kuto rāgādiko guṇaḥ  || 192.76 ||
evamasmāsu yuṣmāsu sarvabhūteṣu cābalāḥ  |
tanmathaikatvabhūteṣu rāgādyavasaraḥ kutaḥ  || 192.77 ||
samyagdṛṣṭiriyaṃ proktā samastaikyāvalokinī  |
pṛthagvijñānamātraiva lokasaṃvyavahāravat || 192.78 ||
bhūtendriyāntaḥ karaṇapradhānapuruṣātmakam  |
jagadvai hyetadakhilaṃ tadā bhedaḥ kimātmakaḥ  || 192.79 ||
bhavanti layamāyānti samudrasalilormayaḥ  |
na vāribhedato bhinnāstathaivaikyādidaṃ jagat || 192.80 ||
yathāgnerarciṣaḥ pītāḥ piṅgalāruṇadhūsarāḥ  |
tathāpi nāgnito bhinnāstathaitadbrahmaṇo jagat || 192.81 ||
bhavatībhiśca yatkṣobhamasmākaṃ sa puraṃdaraḥ  |
kārayatyasadetacca vivekācāracetasām  || 192.82 ||
bhavantyaḥ sa ca devendro lokāśca sasurāsurāḥ  |
samudrādrivanopetā maddehāntaragocarāḥ  || 192.83 ||
yatheyaṃ cārusarvāṅgī bhavatīnāṃ mayāgrataḥ  |
darśitā darśayiṣyāmi tathā caivākhilaṃ jagat || 192.84 ||
prayātu śakro mā garvamindratvaṃ kasya susthiram  |
yūyaṃ ca mā smayaṃ yāta santi rūpānvitāḥ striyaḥ  || 192.85 ||
kiṃ surūpaṃ kurūpaṃ vā yadā bhedo na dṛśyate  |
tāratamyaṃ surūpatve satataṃ bhinnadarśanāt || 192.86 ||
bhavatīnāṃ smayaṃ matvā rūpaudāryaguṇodbhavam  |
mayeyaṃ darśitā tanvī tatastu śamameṣyatha  || 192.87 ||
yasmānmadūrorniṣpannā tviyamindīvarekṣaṇā  |
urvaśī nāma kalyāṇī bhaviṣyati varāpsarāḥ  || 192.88 ||
tadiyaṃ devarājasya nīyatāṃ varavarṇinī  |
bhavatyastena cāsmākaṃ preṣitāḥ prītimicchatā  || 192.89 ||
vaktavyaśca sahasrākṣo nāsmākaṃ bhogakāraṇāt |
tapaścaryā na vāprāpyaphalaṃ prāptumabhīpsatā  || 192.90 ||
sanmārgamasya jagato darśayiṣye karomyaham  |
tathā nareṇa sahito jagataḥ pālanodyataḥ  || 192.91 ||
yadi kaścittavābādhāṃ karoti tridaśeśvara  |
tamahaṃ vārayiṣyāmi nivṛtto bhava vāsava  || 192.92 ||
kartāsi cettvamābādhāṃ na duṣṭasyeha kasyacit |
taṃ cāpi śāstā tadahaṃ pravartiṣyāmyasaṃśayam  || 192.93 ||
etajjñātvā na santāpastvayā kāryo hi māṃ prati  |
upakārāya jagatāmavatīrṇo'smi vāsava  || 192.94 ||
yā ceyamurvaśī mattaḥ samudbhūtā puraṃdara tretāgnihetubhūteyaṃ evaṃ prāpya bhaviṣyati  || 192.95 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe narmadāmāhātmye śrīpatyutpattivarṇanaṃ nāma dvinavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 193

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ityukte'psarasaḥ sarvāḥ praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ  |
ūcurnārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ taddarśanasamīhayā  || 193.1 ||
vasantakāmāpsarasa ūcuḥ  |
bhagavanbhavatā yo'yamupadeśo hitārthinā  |
proktaḥ sa sarvo vijñāto māhātmyaṃ viditaṃ ca te  || 193.2 ||
yattvetadbhavatā proktaṃ prasannenāntarātmanā  |
darśiteyaṃ viśālākṣī darśayiṣyāmi vo jagat || 193.3 ||
tatrārthe sarvabhāvena prapannānāṃ jagatpate  |
darśayātmānamakhilaṃ darśiteyaṃ yathorvaśī  || 193.4 ||
yadi devāparādhe'pi nāsmāsu kupitaṃ tava  |
namaste jagatāmīśa darśayātmānamātmanā  || 193.5 ||

nārāyaṇa uvāca -
paśyatehākhilāṃllokānmama dehe surāṅganāḥ  |
madhuṃ madanamātmānaṃ yaccānyaddraṣṭumicchatha  || 193.6 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ityuktvā bhagavāndevastadā nārāyaṇo nṛpa  |
uccairjahāsa svanavattatrābhūdakhilaṃ jagat || 193.7 ||
brahmā prajāpatiḥ śakraḥ saha rudraiḥ pinākadhṛk |
ādityā vasavaḥ sādhyā viśvedevā maharṣayaḥ  || 193.8 ||
nāsatyadasrāvanilaḥ sarvaśaśca tathāgnayaḥ  |
yakṣagandharvasiddhāśca piśācoragakinnarāḥ  || 193.9 ||
samastāpsaraso vidyāḥ sāṅgā vedāstaduktayaḥ  |
manuṣyāḥ paśavaḥ kīṭāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ pādapāstathā  || 193.10 ||
sarīsṛpāścātha sūkṣmā yaccānyajjīvasaṃjñitam  |
samudrāḥ sakalāḥ śailāḥ saritaḥ kānanāni ca  || 193.11 ||
dvīpānyaśeṣāṇi tathā tathā sarvasarāṃsi ca  |
nagaragrāmapūrṇā ca medinī medinīpate  |
devāṅganābhirdevasya dehe dṛṣṭaṃ mahātmanaḥ  || 193.12 ||
nakṣatragrahatārābhiḥ susampūrṇaṃ nabhastalam  |
dadṛśustāḥ sucārvaṅgyastasyāntarviśvaṃ rūpiṇaḥ  || 193.13 ||
ūrdhvaṃ na tiryaṅnādhastādyadāntastasya dṛśyate  |
tamanantamanādiṃ ca tatastāstuṣṭuvuḥ prabhum  || 193.14 ||
madanena samaṃ sarvā madhunā ca varāṅganāḥ  |
sasādhvasā bhaktiparāḥ paraṃ vismayamāgatāḥ  || 193.15 ||
vasantakāmāpsarasa ūcuḥ  |
paśyāma nādiṃ tava deva nāntaṃ na madhyamavyākṛtarūpapāram  |
parāyaṇaṃ tvāṃ jagatāmanantaṃ natāḥ sma nārāyaṇamātmabhūtam  || 193.16 ||
mahīnabhovāyujalāgnayastvaṃ śabdādirūpastu parāparātman  |
tvatto bhavatyacyute sarvametadbhedādirūpo'si vibho tvamātman  || 193.17 ||
draṣṭāsi rūpasya parasya vettā śrotā ca śabdasya hare tvamekaḥ  |
sraṣṭā bhavān sarvagato'khilasya ghrātā ca gandhasya pṛthakśarīrī  || 193.18 ||
sureṣu sarveṣu na so'sti kaścinmanuṣyalokeṣu na so'sti kaścit |
paśvādivargeṣu na so'sti kaścidyo nāṃśabhūtastava devadeva  || 193.19 ||
brahmāmbudhīndupramukhāni saumya śakrādirūpāṇi tavottamāni  |
samudrarūpaṃ tava dhairyavatsu tejaḥ svarūpeṣu ravistathāgniḥ  || 193.20 ||
kṣamādhaneṣu kṣitirūpamagryaṃ śīghro balavatsu vāyuḥ  |
manuṣyarūpaṃ tava rājaveṣo mūḍheṣu sarveśvara pādapo'si  || 193.21 ||
sarvānayeṣvacyuta dānavastvaṃ sanatsajātaśca vivekavatsu  |
rasasvarūpeṇa jalasthito'si gandhasvarūpaṃ bhavato dharitryām  || 193.22 ||
dṛśyasvarūpaśca hutāśanastvaṃ sparśasvarūpaṃ bhavataḥ samīre  |
śabdādikaṃ te nabhasi svarūpaṃ mantavyarūpo manasi prabho tvam  || 193.23 ||
bodhasvarūpaśca matau tvamekaḥ sarvatra sarveśvara sarvabhūta  |
paśyāmi te nābhisarojamadhye brahmāṇamīśaṃ ca haraṃ bhṛkuṭyām  || 193.24 ||
tavāśvinau karṇagatau samastāstavāsthitā bāhuṣu lokapālāḥ  |
ghrāṇo'nilo netragatau ravīndu jihvā ca te nātha sarasvatīyam  || 193.25 ||
pādau dharitrī jaṭharaṃ samastāṃllokān hṛṣīkeśa vilokayāmaḥ  |
jaṅghe vayaṃ pādatalāṅgulīṣu piśācayakṣoragasiddhasaṅghāḥ  || 193.26 ||
puṃstve prajānāṃ patiroṣṭhayugme pratiṣṭhitāste kratavaḥ samastāḥ  |
sarve vayaṃ te daśaneṣu deva daṃṣṭrāsu devā hyabhavaṃśca dantāḥ  || 193.27 ||
romāṇyaśeṣāstava devasaṅghā vidyādharā nātha tavāṅghrirekhāḥ  |
sāṅgāḥ samastāstava deva vedāḥ samāsthitāḥ sandhiṣu bāhubhūtāḥ  || 193.28 ||
varāhabhūtaṃ dharaṇīdharaste nṛsiṃharūpaṃ ca sadā karālam  |
paśyāma te vājiśirastathoccaistrivikrame yacca tadāprameyam  || 193.29 ||
amī samudrāstava deva dehe maurvālayaḥ śailadharāstathāmī  |
imāśca gaṅgāpramukhāḥ sravantyo dvīpāṇyaśeṣāṇi vanādideśāḥ  || 193.30 ||
stuvanti ceme munayastaveśa dehe sthitāstvanmahimānamagryam  |
tvāmīśitāraṃ jagatāmanantaṃ yajanti yajñaiḥ kila yajñino'mī  || 193.31 ||
tvattohi saumyaṃ jagatīha kiṃcittvatto na raudraṃ ca samastamūrte  |
tvatto na śītaṃ ca na keśavoṣṇaṃ sarvasvarūpātiśayī tvameva  || 193.32 ||
prasīda sarveśvara sarvabhūta sanātanātmaparameśvareśa  |
tvanmāyayā mohitamānasābhiryatte'parāddhaṃ tadidaṃ kṣamasva  || 193.33 ||
kiṃ vāparāddhaṃ tava devadeva yanmāyayā no hṛdayaṃ tavāpi  |
māyābhiśaṅkipraṇatārtihantarmano hi no vihvalatāmupaiti  || 193.34 ||
na te'parāddhaṃ yadi te'parāddhamasmābhirunmārgavivartinībhiḥ  |
tatkṣamyatāṃ sṛṣṭikṛtastavaiva devāparādhaḥ sṛjato vivekam  || 193.35 ||
namo namaste govinda nārāyaṇa janārdana  |
tvannāmasmaraṇātpāpamaśeṣaṃ naḥ praṇaśyatu  || 193.36 ||
namo'nanta namastubhyaṃ viśvātmanviśvabhāvana  |
tvannāmasmaraṇātpāpamaśeṣaṃ naḥ praṇaśyatu  || 193.37 ||
vareṇya yajñapuruṣa prajāpālana vāmana  |
tvannāmasmaraṇātpāpamaśeṣaṃ naḥ praṇaśyatu  || 193.38 ||
namo'stu te'bjanābhāya prajāpatikṛte hara  |
tvannāmasmaraṇātpāpamaśeṣaṃ naḥ praṇaśyatu  || 193.39 ||
saṃsārārṇavapotāya namastubhyamadhokṣaja  |
tvannāmasmaraṇātpāpamaśeṣaṃ naḥ praṇaśyatu  || 193.40 ||
namaḥ parasmai śrīśāya vāsudevāya vedhase  |
svecchayā guṇayuktāya sargasthityantakāriṇe  || 193.41 ||
upasaṃhara viśvātmanrūpametatsanātanam  |
vardhamānaṃ na no draṣṭuṃ samarthaṃ cakṣurīśvara  || 193.42 ||
pralayāgnisahasrasya samā dīptistavācyuta  |
pramāṇena diśo bhūmirgaganaṃ ca samāvṛtam  || 193.43 ||
na vidmaḥ kutra vartāmo bhavānnāthopalakṣyate  |
sarvaṃ jagadiaikasthaṃ piṇḍitaṃ lakṣayāmahe  || 193.44 ||
kiṃ varṇayāmo rūpaṃ te kiṃ pramāṇamidaṃ hare  |
māhātmyaṃ kiṃ nu te deva yajjihvāyā na gocare  || 193.45 ||
vaktāro vāyutenāpi buddhīnāmayutāyutaiḥ  |
guṇanirvarṇanaṃ nātha kartuṃ tava na śakyate  || 193.46 ||
tadetaddarśitaṃ rūpaṃ prasādaḥ paramaḥ kṛtaḥ  |
chandato jagatāmīśa tadetadupasaṃhara  || 193.47 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ityevaṃ saṃstutastābhirapsarobhirjanārdanaḥ  |
divyajñānopapannānāṃ tāsāṃ pratyakṣamīśvaraḥ  || 193.48 ||
viveśa sarvabhūtāni svairaṃśairbhūtabhāvanaḥ  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvabhūteṣu līyamānamadhokṣajam  || 193.49 ||
vismayaṃ paramaṃ cakruḥ samastā devayoṣitaḥ  |
sa ca sarveśvaraḥ śailānpādapānsāgarānbhuvam  || 193.50 ||
jalamagniṃ tathā vāyumākāśaṃ ca viveśa ha  |
kāle dikṣvatha sarvātma hyātmanaścānyathāpi ca  || 193.51 ||
ātmarūpasthitaṃ svena mahimnā bhāvayañjagat |
devadānavarakṣāṃsi yakṣīvidyādharoragāḥ  || 193.52 ||
manuṣyapaśukīṭādimṛgapaśvantarikṣagāḥ  |
ye'ntarikṣe tathā bhūmau divi ye ca jalāśrayāḥ  || 193.53 ||
tānviveśa sa viśvātmā punastadrūpamāsthitaḥ  |
nareṇa sārdhaṃ yattābhirdṛṣṭapūrvamarindama  || 193.54 ||
tāḥ paraṃ vismayaṃ jagmuḥ sarvāstridaśayoṣitaḥ  |
praṇemuḥ sādhvasātpāṇḍuvadanā nṛpasattama  || 193.55 ||
nārāyaṇo'pi bhagavānāha tāstridaśāṅganāḥ  || 193.56 ||

nārāyaṇa uvāca -
nīyatāmurvaśī bhadrā yatrāsau tridaśeśvaraḥ  |
bhavatīnāṃ hitārthāya sarvabhūteṣvasāviti  || 193.57 ||
jñānamutpāditaṃ bhūyo layaṃ bhūteṣu kurvatā  |
tadgacchadhvaṃ samasto'yaṃ bhūtagrāmo madaṃśakaḥ  || 193.58 ||
ahamadyātmabhūtasya vāsudevasya yoginaḥ  |
asmātparataraṃ nāsti yo'nantaḥ paripathyate  || 193.59 ||
tamajaṃ sarvabhūteśaṃ jānīta paramaṃ padam  |
ahaṃ bhavatyo devāśca manuṣyāḥ paśavaśca ye  |
etatsarvamanantasya vāsudevasya vai kṛtam  || 193.60 ||
evaṃ jñātvā samaṃ sarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam  |
sapaśvādiguṇaṃ caiva draṣṭavyaṃ tridaśāṅganāḥ  || 193.61 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
ityuktāstena devena samastāstāḥ surastriyaḥ  |
praṇamya tau samadanāḥ savasantāśca pārthiva  || 193.62 ||
ādāya corvaśīṃ bhūyo devarājamupāgatāḥ  |
ācakhyuśca yathāvṛttaṃ devarājāya tattathā  || 193.63 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tathā tvamapi rājendra sarvabhūteṣu keśavam  |
cintayansamatāṃ gaccha samataiva hi muktaye  || 193.64 ||
rājannevaṃ viśeṣeṇa bhūteṣu parameśvaram  |
vāsudeva kathaṃ doṣāṃllobhādīnna prahāsyasi  || 193.65 ||
sarvabhūtāni govindādyadā nānyāni bhūpate  |
tadā vairādayo bhāvāḥ kriyatāṃ na tu putraka  || 193.66 ||
iti paśya jagatsarvaṃ vāsudevātmakaṃ nṛpa  |
etadeva hi kṛṣṇena rūpamāviṣkṛtaṃ nṛpa  || 193.67 ||
parameśvareti yadrūpaṃ tadetatkathitaṃ tava  |
janmādibhāvarahitaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam  || 193.68 ||
saṃkṣepeṇātha bhūpāla śrūyatāṃ yadvadāmi te  |
yanmataṃ puruṣaḥ kṛtvā paraṃ nirvāṇamṛcchati  || 193.69 ||
sarvo viṣṇusamāso hi bhāvābhāvau ca tanmayau  |
sadasatsarvamīśo'sau mahādevaḥ paraṃ padam  || 193.70 ||
bhavajaladhigatānāṃ dvandvavātāhatānāṃ sutaduhitṛkalatratrāṇabhārārditānām  |
viṣamaviṣayatoye majjatāmaplavānāṃ bhavati śaraṇameko viṣṇupoto narāṇām  || 193.71 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīpatimāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trinavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 194

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tacchrutvānāntadevena viśvarūpamudāhṛtam  |
devarājastathā devāḥ paraṃ vismayamāgatāḥ  || 194.1 ||
dṛṣṭvā cāpsarasaṃ puṇyāmurvaśīṃ kamalānanām  |
saṃtrasto vismitaścābhūdindro rājaśriyā vṛtaḥ  || 194.2 ||
na kiṃciduttaraṃ vākyamuktavāñjoṣamāsthitaḥ  |
iti vṛttāntabhūtaṃ hi nārāyaṇaviceṣṭitam  || 194.3 ||
bhṛgoḥ khātyāṃ samutpannā lakṣmīḥ śrutvā tu vai nṛpa  |
vaiśvarūpaṃ paraṃ rūpaṃ vismitācintayattadā  || 194.4 ||
kenopāyena sa syānme bhartā nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  |
vratena tapasā vāpi dānena niyamena ca  || 194.5 ||
vṛddhānāṃ sevanenātha devatārādhanena vā  |
iti cintāparāṃ kanyāṃ satī jñātvā yudhiṣṭhira  || 194.6 ||
prāha prāpto mayā bhartā śaṅkarastapasā kila  |
prajāpatiśca gāyatryā hyanyābhirabhivāñchitāḥ  || 194.7 ||
tapasaiva hi te prāpyastasmāttaccara suvrate  |
tapastvaṃ hi mahaccograṃ sarvavāñchitadāyakam  || 194.8 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sāgarāntaṃ samāsādya lakṣmīḥ parapuraṃjaya  |
cacāra vipulaṃ kālaṃ tapaḥ paramaduścaram  || 194.9 ||
sthāṇuvatsaṃsthitā sābhūddivyaṃ varṣasahasrakam  |
tata indrādayo devāḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharāḥ  || 194.10 ||
bhūtvā jagmustadarthaṃ te sā tu pṛṣṭavatī surān  |
viśvarūpaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yattaddarśayata māciram  || 194.11 ||
vilakṣā vrīḍitā devā gatvā nārāyaṇaṃ tadā  |
abruvan vaiśvarūpaṃ no śaktā darśayituṃ vayam  || 194.12 ||
tato yatheṣṭaṃ te jagmuḥ sa ca viṣṇuracintayat |
ugrarūpā sthitā devī dehaṃ dahati bhārgavī  || 194.13 ||
tāṃ tasmāttatra gatvāhaṃ varaṃ dattvā tu vāñchitam  |
punastapaḥ kariṣyāmi darśayiṣyāmi vā punaḥ  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ viśvarūpaṃ yaddurdaśyaṃ devadānavaiḥ  || 194.14 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gatvā hṛṣīkeśaḥ sāgarāntasthitāṃ śriyam  |
prāha tuṣṭo'smi te devi varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam  || 194.15 ||

śrīruvāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva prapannāyā janārdana  |
tadā darśaya yaddṛṣṭamapsarobhistavānagha  || 194.16 ||
viśvarūpamanantaṃ ca bhūtabhāvana keśava  |
gandhamādanamāsādya kṛtaṃ yacca tapastvayā  || 194.17 ||
tadvadasva vibho viṣṇo na mithyā yadi keśava  |
śraddadhāmi na caivāhaṃ rūpasyāsya kathaṃcana  || 194.18 ||
bahubhiryakṣarakṣobhirmāyācāripracāribhiḥ  |
chanditā mama jānadbhirbhāvamantargataṃ harau  || 194.19 ||
bhūtvā viṣṇusvarūpāste cakriṇaśca caturbhujāḥ  |
suvrīḍitā gatāḥ sarve viśvarūpo sahāyataḥ  || 194.20 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nārāyaṇo'tha bhagavāñchaṅkhacakragadābhṛtam  |
tayā tathoktastadrūpaṃ muktvā vai surapūjitam  || 194.21 ||
rūpaṃ paraṃ yathoktaṃ vai viśvarūpamadarśayat |
darśayitvā vacaḥ prāha pañcarātravidhānataḥ  || 194.22 ||
yo'rcayiṣyati māṃ nityaṃ sa pūjyaḥ sa ca pūjitaḥ  |
dhanadhānyasamāyuktaḥ sarvabhogasamanvitaḥ  || 194.23 ||
mūlaṃ hi sarvadharmāṇāṃ brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ  |
tenāhaṃ tatra sthāsyāmi mūlaśrīpatisaṃjñitaḥ  || 194.24 ||
mūlaśrīḥ procyate brāhmī brahmacaryasvarūpiṇī  |
sarvayogamayī puṇyā sarvapāpaharī śubhā  || 194.25 ||
patistasyāḥ prabhurahaṃ varadaḥ prāṇināṃ priye  |
revājale naraḥ snātvā yo'rcayenmāṃ yatavrataḥ  || 194.26 ||
mūlaśrīpatināmānaṃ vāñchite prāpnuyātphalam  |
dānāni tatra yo dadyānmahādānāni ca priye  || 194.27 ||
sahasraguṇitaṃ puṇyamanyasthānādavāpyate  |
dṛṣṭaṃ tvayā tatra deśe samyakcaivāvadhāritam  |
tadarcitvā parān kāmānāpsyasi tvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 194.28 ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva deveśi vāñchitaṃ durlabhaṃ suraiḥ  |
durgasaṃsārakāntārapatitaiḥ parameśvari  || 194.29 ||

śrīruvāca -
nārāyaṇa jagaddhātarnārāyaṇa jagatpate  |
nārāyaṇa parabrahma nārāyaṇaparāyaṇa  || 194.30 ||
prasīda pāhi māṃ bhaktyā samyaksarge niyojaya  |
priyo hyasi priyāhaṃ te yathā syāṃ tattathā kuru  || 194.31 ||
gṛhaṃ dharmārthakāmānāṃ kāraṇaṃ deva saṃmatam  |
tadāsthāyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ māṃ śreyasi niyojaya  || 194.32 ||

nārāyaṇa uvāca -
nārāyaṇagirā devi vijñapto'smi yatastvayā  |
nārāyaṇagirirnāma tena me'tra bhaviṣyati  || 194.33 ||
nārāyaṇasmṛtau yāti duritaṃ janmakoṭijam  |
yasmādgirati tasmācca girirityeva śabditam  || 194.34 ||
tasmātsarvāśrayo devi giriḥ parvatarāṅbhavet |
surāsuramanuṣyāṇāṃ yathāhamapi cāśrayaḥ  || 194.35 ||
ya etatpūjayiṣyanti maṇḍalasthaṃ paraṃ mama  |
nārāyaṇagirirnāma devarūpaṃ śubhekṣaṇe  || 194.36 ||
te divyajñānasampannā divyadehaviceṣṭitāḥ  |
divyaṃ lokamavāpsyanti divyabhogasamanvitāḥ  || 194.37 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tayorevaṃ saṃvadatordevā indrapurogamāḥ  |
samāgatā vanoddeśaṃ sāgarānte maharṣayaḥ  || 194.38 ||
tato bhṛguṃ devarājo nārāyaṇavicintitam  |
vavre jñātvā tu tatkanyāṃ dharmātmā sa dadau ca tām  || 194.39 ||
dharmo'pi vidhivadvatsa vivāhaṃ samakārayat |
devadevasya rājarṣe devatārthe samāhitaḥ  || 194.40 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
dharmo vivāhamakarodvidhivadyattvayoditam  |
ko vidhistatra kā dattā dakṣiṇā bhṛguṇāpi ca  || 194.41 ||
vivāhayajñe samabhūtsruksruvagrahaṇe ca kaḥ  |
ṛtvijaḥ ke sadasyāśca tasyāsan dvijasattama  || 194.42 ||
kiṃ tasyāvabhṛthaṃ tvāsīttatsarvaṃ vada vistarāt |
tvadvākyāmṛtapānena tṛptirmama na vidyate  || 194.43 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
nārāyaṇavivāhasya yajñasya ca yudhiṣṭhira  |
tapasastasya devasya samyagācaraṇasya ca  || 194.44 ||
vaktuṃ samartho na guṇānbrahmāpi parameśvaraḥ  |
tathāpyuddeśato vacmi śṛṇu bhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 194.45 ||
brahmā saptarṣayastatra sruksruvagrahaṇe ratāḥ  |
agnīñjuhuvire rājanvedirdhātrī sasāgarā  || 194.46 ||
daduḥ samudrā ratnāni brahmarṣibhyo nṛpottama  |
dhanado'pi dadau vittaṃ sarvabrāhmaṇavāñchitam  || 194.47 ||
viśvakarmā'pi devānāṃ brahmarṣīṇāṃ paraṃtapa  |
veśmāni suvicitrāṇi sarvaratnamayāni ca  || 194.48 ||
kṛtvā pradarśayāmāsa devendrāya yaśasvine  |
śatakratustato viprānkāpiṣṭhalapurogamān  || 194.49 ||
śaunakādīṃśca papraccha baṣkalāñchāgalānapi  |
ātreyānapi rājendra vṛṇudhvamabhivāñchitam  || 194.50 ||
dṛṣṭvā te citraratnāni prāhuḥ sarveśvareśvaram  |
devānāṃ ca ṛṣīṇāṃ ca saṅgamo'yaṃ supuṇyakṛt || 194.51 ||
asminpuṇye sureśāna vastuṃ vāñchāmahe sadā  |
śatakratuḥ prāha punarvāso vātra bhaviṣyati  |
satyadharmaratā yūyaṃ yāvatkālaṃ bhaviṣyatha  || 194.52 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
pṛṣṭaṃ yadrājaśārdūla ke makhe hotriṇo'bhavan  |
tatprocyamānamadhunā śṛṇu bhūtvā samāhitaḥ  || 194.53 ||
sanatkumārapramukhāḥ sadasyāstasya cābhavan  |
audgātramatryaṅgirasau marīciśca cakāra ha  || 194.54 ||
hautraṃ dharmavasiṣṭhau ca brahmatvaṃ sanako muniḥ  |
ṣaṭtriṃśadgrāmasāhasraṃ prādāttebhyaḥ śatakratuḥ  || 194.55 ||
lakṣmīrbhartrā ca saṃyuktābhavattatkṛtavānprabhuḥ  |
brahmaṇo juhvato vahniṃ yāvaddeśasthitaiḥ suraiḥ  || 194.56 ||
dṛṣṭaṃ lalāṭaṃ deśo'sau lalāṭa iti saṃjñitaḥ  |
sa deśaḥ śrīpateḥ kṣetrapuṇyaṃ devarṣisevitam  || 194.57 ||
sarvāścaryamayaṃ divyaṃ divyasiddhisamanvitam  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ tataḥ paṅktiṃ niveśayitumudyatā  || 194.58 ||
lakṣmīḥ śrīpatināmānamāha devaṃ vacastadā  |

śrīruvāca -
ya ete brāhmaṇāḥ śiṣyā bhṛgvādīnāṃ yatavratāḥ  || 194.59 ||
tānniveśayitumicchāmi tvatprasādādadhokṣaja  |
marīcyādayaḥ surendreṇa sthāpitā garuḍadhvaja  || 194.60 ||
naiṣṭhikavratino viprā bahavo'tra yatavratāḥ  |
prājāpatye vrate brāhme kecidatra vyavasthitāḥ  |
tānahaṃ sthāpayiṣyāmi tvatprasādādadhokṣaja  || 194.61 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ kautūhaladharo bhagavānvṛṣabhadhvajaḥ  |
papraccha vratinaḥ sarvānvṛttibhede vyavasthitān  || 194.62 ||
nārado'pi mahādevamupetya ca satīpatim  |
prāha kṛṣṇājinadharo naiṣṭhikā brāhmaṇā hyamī  || 194.63 ||
amī kāryāḥ suvastreṇa channaguhyā dvijottamāḥ  |
prājāpatyāścaturviṃśasahasrāṇi nareśvara  || 194.64 ||
brahmacaryavratasthānāṃ vratabrahmavicāriṇām  |
dvādaśaiṣāṃ sahasrāṇi santi vai vṛṣabhadhvaja  || 194.65 ||
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā devā devarṣayo'pi ca  |
sādhu sādhvityamanyanta nocuḥ kecana kiṃcana  || 194.66 ||
samāhvayattato lakṣmīstān viprān bhaktisaṃyutā  |
uvāca caraṇāngṛhya prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ mayi  || 194.67 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśacca sahasrāṇi veśmanāmatra saṃsthitiḥ  |
viśvakarmakṛtānāṃ tu teṣu tiṣṭhantu vo'khilāḥ  || 194.68 ||
te tatheti pratijñāya sthitāḥ saṃprītamānasāḥ  |
dhanadhānyasamṛddhāśca vāñchitaprāptilakṣaṇāḥ  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhāśca hyanārambheṣu karmaṇām  || 194.69 ||
iti saṃsthāpya tān viprān sā sthitā paryapālayat |
caturdhā tu sthito viṣṇuḥ śriyā devyāḥ priye rataḥ  || 194.70 ||
evaṃ vaivāhikamakhe nivṛtte ṛṣayastu tam  |
ūcuścāvabhṛthasnānaṃ kutra kurmo janārdana  || 194.71 ||
iti śrutvā tu vacanaṃ śrīpatiḥ pādapaṅkajāt |
mumoca jāhnavītoyaṃ revāmadhyagamaṃ śuci  || 194.72 ||
hareḥ pādodakaṃ dṛṣṭvā niḥsṛtaṃ munayastu te  |
vismitāḥ samapadyanta jānantastasya gauravam  || 194.73 ||
rudreṇa sahitāḥ sarve devatā ṛṣayastathā  |
saṃkathā vismitāścakrurvidhunvantaḥ śirāṃsi ca  || 194.74 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ  |
brūhi śambho kimatrāyaṃ akasmādvārisambhavaḥ  |
viṣṇoḥ pādāmbujotthaśca sammohakaraṇaḥ paraḥ  || 194.75 ||

īśvara uvāca -
pādodakamidaṃ viṣṇorahaṃ jānāmi vai surāḥ  |
daśāśvamedhāvabhṛthaiḥ snānamatrātiricyate  || 194.76 ||
yuṣmābhiḥ śrīpatiḥ pūjyaḥ snānaṃ cāvabhṛthaṃ kutaḥ  |
bhaviṣyatīti tenāśu idaṃ vo'rthe vinirmitam  || 194.77 ||
snātvātra tridaśeśānā yatphalaṃ samprapadyate  |
vaktuṃ na kenacidyāti tataḥ kimuttaraṃ vacaḥ  || 194.78 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evamuktvā tu te sarve snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāgatam  |
jagmurdevā maheśānapurogā bharatarṣabha  || 194.79 ||
brāhmaṇāśca tataḥ sarve svaveśmānyeva bhejire  |
devatīrthe mahārāja sarvapāpapraṇāśane  || 194.80 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīpativivāhavarṇanaṃ nāma caturnavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 195

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
devatīrthe tu kiṃ nāma māhātmyaṃ samudāhṛtam  |
phalaṃ kiṃ snānadānādikāriṇāṃ jāyate mune  || 195.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni devairmunigaṇairapi  |
sevitāni mahābāho tāni dhyātāni viṣṇunā  || 195.2 ||
samāgatānyekatāṃ vai tatra tīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
tattīrthaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ puṇyaṃ devatīrthamiti śrutam  || 195.3 ||
kurukṣetraṃ bhuvi paramantarikṣe tripuṣkaram  |
puruṣottamaṃ divi paraṃ devatīrthaṃ parātparam  || 195.4 ||
devatīrthasamaṃ nāsti tīrthamatra paratra ca  |
yatprāpya manujastapyenna kadācidyudhiṣṭhira  || 195.5 ||
devairuktāni tīrthāni yo'tra snānaṃ samācaret |
devatīrthe sa sarvatra snāto bhavati mānavaḥ  || 195.6 ||
evamastviti tairuktā devā ṛṣigaṇā api  |
saṃtuṣṭāḥ śrīśamabhyarcya svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ tu bhejire  || 195.7 ||
sūryagrahe'tra vai kṣetre snātvā yatphalamaśnute  |
snātvā śrīśaṃ samabhyarcya samupoṣya yathāvidhi  || 195.8 ||
yaddadāti hiraṇyāni dānāni vidhivannṛpa  |
tadanantaphalaṃ sarvaṃ sūryasya grahaṇe yathā  || 195.9 ||
bhūmidānaṃ dhenudānaṃ svarṇadānamanantakam  |
vajradānamanantaṃ ca phalaṃ prāha śatakratuḥ  || 195.10 ||
somo vai vastradānena mauktikānāṃ ca bhārgavaḥ  |
suvarṇasya ravirdānaṃ dharmarājo hyanantakam  || 195.11 ||
devatīrthe tu yaddānaṃ śraddhāyuktena dīyate  |
tadanantaphalaṃ prāha bṛhaspatirudāradhīḥ  || 195.12 ||
devatīrthe bhṛgukṣetre sarvatīrthādhika nṛpa  |
devatīrthe naraḥ snātvā śrīpatiṃ yo'nupaśyati  || 195.13 ||
somagrahe kulaśataṃ sa samuddhṛtya nākabhāk |
dānāni dvijamukhyebhyo devatīrthe narādhipa  || 195.14 ||
yairdattāni narairbhogabhāginaḥ pretya ceha te  |
devatīrthe viprabhojyaṃ harimuddiśya yaścaret || 195.15 ||
sa sarvāhlādamāpnoti svargaloke yudhiṣṭhira  |
devatīrthe naro nārī snātvā niyatamānasau  || 195.16 ||
upoṣyaikādaśīṃ bhaktyā pūjayedyaḥ śriyaḥ patim  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtenodbodhya dīpakam  || 195.17 ||
dvādaśyāṃ prātarutthāya tathā vai narmadājale  |
vipradāmpatyamabhyarcya vidhivatkurunandana  || 195.18 ||
vastrābharaṇatāmbūlapuṣpadhūpavilepanaiḥ  |
akṣaye viṣṇuloke'sau modate caritavrataḥ  || 195.19 ||
yaḥ sadaikādaśītithau snātvopoṣyārcayeddharim  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryādvedaśāstravidhānataḥ  || 195.20 ||
dharmarājakṛtāṃ pāpāṃ na sa paśyati yātanām  |
pañcarātravidhānena śrīpatiṃ yo'rcayiṣyati  || 195.21 ||
dīkṣāmavāpya vidhivadvaiṣṇavīṃ pāpanāśinīm  |
svargamokṣapradāṃ puṇyāṃ bhogadāṃ vittadāmatha  || 195.22 ||
rājyadāṃ vā mahābhāga putradāṃ bhāgyadāmatha  |
sukalatrapradāṃ vāpi viṣṇorbhaktipradāmiti  || 195.23 ||
tariṣyati bhavāmbhodhiṃ sa naraḥ kurunandana  |
yo'rcayiṣyati tatraiva devatīrthe śriyaḥ patim  || 195.24 ||
viśvarūpamatho samyaṅmūlaśrīpatimeva vā  |
nārāyaṇagiriṃ vāpi gṛhe vaikādaśītithau  || 195.25 ||
bhaktimāñchraddhayā yuktaḥ kṣīraistīrthodakairapi  |
susūkṣmairahatairvastrairmahākauśeyakairnṛpa  || 195.26 ||
vicitrairnetrajairvāpi dhūpairagurucandanaiḥ  |
guggulairghṛtamiśraiśca naivedyairvividhairapi  || 195.27 ||
pāyasādyairmanuṣyendra payasā vā yudhiṣṭhira  |
piṣṭadīpaiḥ suvimalairvardhamānairmanoharaiḥ  || 195.28 ||
pūjayitvā naro yāti yathā tacchṛṇu bhārata  |
śaṅkhī cakrī gadī padmī bhūtvāsau garuḍadhvajaḥ  || 195.29 ||
devalokānatikramya viṣṇulokaṃ prapadyate  |
yastu vai parayā bhaktyā śrīpateḥ pādapaṅkajam  || 195.30 ||
caturdhādhiṣṭhitaṃ paśyecchriyaṃ trailokyamātaram  |
nṛtyagītavinodena mucyate pātakardhruvam  || 195.31 ||
nīrājane tu devasya prātarmadhye dine tathā  |
sāyaṃ ca niyato nityaṃ yaḥ paśyetpūjayeddharim  || 195.32 ||
sa tīrtvā hyāpadaṃ durgāṃ naivārtiṃ samavāpnuyāt |
āyuḥśrīvardhanaṃ puṃsāṃ cakṣuṣāmapi pūrakam  || 195.33 ||
upapāpaharaṃ caiva sadā nīrājanaṃ hareḥ  |
tadā nīrājanākāle yo hareḥ paṭhati stavam  || 195.34 ||
sa dhanyo devadevasya prasannenāntarātmanā  |
harernīrājanāśeṣaṃ pāṇibhyāṃ yaḥ prayacchati  || 195.35 ||
saṃgṛhya cakṣuṣī tena yojayenmārjayanmukham  |
timirādīnakṣirogānnāśayeddīptimanmukham  || 195.36 ||
bhavatyaśeṣaduṣṭānāṃ nāśāyālaṃ narottama  |
dīpaprajvalanaṃ yasya nityamagre śriyaḥ pateḥ  || 195.37 ||
snātvā revājale puṇye pradadyādadhikaṃ vratī  |
saptadvīpavatī tena sasāgaravanāpagā  || 195.38 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā syādvai dharaṇī śaṅkaro'bravīt |
idaṃ yaḥ paṭhyamānaṃ tu śṛṇuyātpaṭhate'pi vā  || 195.39 ||
smaraṇaṃ so'tasamaye vipāpmā prāpnuyāddhareḥ  |
idaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ pitṛguṇapriyam  || 195.40 ||
māhātmyaṃ śrāvayedviprāñchrīpateḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi  |
ghṛtena madhunā tena tarpitāḥ syuḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 195.41 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīpatimāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 196

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharādhīśa haṃsatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra haṃsastapastaptvā brahmavāhanatāṃ gataḥ  || 196.1 ||
haṃsatīrthe naraḥ snātvā dānaṃ dattvā ca kāñcanam  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati  || 196.2 ||
haṃsayuktena yānena taruṇādityavarcasā  |
sarvakāmasamṛddhena sevyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ  || 196.3 ||
tatra bhuktvā yathākāmaṃ sarvān bhogān yathepsitān  |
jātismaro hi jāyeta punarmānuṣyamāgataḥ  || 196.4 ||
saṃnyāsena tyajeddehaṃ mokṣamāpnoti bhārata  || 196.5 ||
etatte kathitaṃ pārtha haṃsatīrthasya yatphalam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvaduḥkhavināśanam  || 196.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe haṃsatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṇṇavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 197

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ gacchetsūryatīrthamanuttamam  |
mūlasthānamiti khyātaṃ padmajasthāpitaṃ śubham  || 197.1 ||
mūlaśrīpatinā devī proktā sthāpaya bhāskaram  |
śrutvā devoditaṃ devī sthāpayāmāsa bhāskaram  || 197.2 ||
procyate narmadātīre mūlasthānākhyabhāskaraḥ  || 197.3 ||
tatra tīrthe naro yastu snātvā niyatamānasaḥ  |
saṃtarpya pitṛdevāṃśca piṇḍena salilena ca  || 197.4 ||
mūlasthānaṃ tataḥ paśyetsa gacchetparamāṃ gatim  |
guhyādguhyatarastatra viśeṣastu śruto mayā  || 197.5 ||
samāgame munīnāṃ tu śaṅkarācchaśiśekharāt |
sadā vai śuklasaptamyāṃ mūlamādityavāsaraḥ  || 197.6 ||
tadā revājalaṃ gatvā snātvā saṃtarpya devatāḥ  |
pitḥṃśca bharataśreṣṭha dattvā dānaṃ svaśaktitaḥ  || 197.7 ||
karavīraistato gatvā raktacandanavāriṇā  |
saṃsthāpya bhāskaraṃ bhaktyā sampūjya ca yathāvidhi  || 197.8 ||
tataḥ sāgurukairdhūpaiḥ kundaraiśca viśeṣataḥ  |
dhūpayeddevadeveśaṃ dīpān bodhya diśo daśa  || 197.9 ||
upoṣya jāgaraṃ kuryādgītavādyaṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
evaṃ kṛte mahīpāla na bhavedugraduḥkhabhāk || 197.10 ||
sūryaloke vasettāvadyāvatkalpaśatatrayam  |
gandharvairapsarobhiśca sevyamāno nṛpottama  || 197.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mūlasthānatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 198

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla bhadrakālītisaṅgamam  |
śūlatīrthamiti khyātaṃ svayaṃ devena nirmitam  || 198.1 ||
pañcāyatanamadhye tu tiṣṭhate parameśvaraḥ  |
śūlapāṇirmahādevaḥ sarvadevatapūjitaḥ  || 198.2 ||
sa saṅgamo nṛpaśreṣṭha nityaṃ devairniṣevitaḥ  |
darśanāttasya tīrthasya snānadānādviśeṣataḥ  || 198.3 ||
daurbhāgyaṃ durnimittaṃ ca hyabhiśāpo nṛpagrahaḥ  |
yadanyadduṣkṛtaṃ karma naśyate śaṅkaro'bravīt || 198.4 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ śūleśvarī devī kathaṃ śūleśvaro haraḥ  |
prathito narmadātīre etadvistarato vada  || 198.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
babhūva brāhmaṇaḥ kaścinmāṇḍavya iti viśrutaḥ  |
vṛttimānsarvadharmajñaḥ satye tapasi ca sthitaḥ  || 198.6 ||
aśokāśramamadhyastho vṛkṣamūle mahātapāḥ  |
ūrdhvabāhurmahātejāstasthau maunavratānvitaḥ  || 198.7 ||
tasya kālena mahatā tīvre tapasi vartataḥ  |
tamāśramamanuprāptā dasyavo loptrahāriṇaḥ  || 198.8 ||
anusarpyamāṇā bahubhiḥ puruṣairbharatarṣabha  |
te tasyāvasathe loptraṃ nyadadhuḥ kurunandana  || 198.9 ||
nidhāya ca tadā līnāstatraivāśramamaṇḍale  |
teṣu līneṣvatho śīghraṃ tatastadrakṣiṇāṃ balam  || 198.10 ||
ājagāma tato'paśyaṃstamṛṣiṃ taskarānugāḥ  |
tamapṛcchaṃstadā vṛttaṃ rakṣiṇastaṃ tapodhanam  || 198.11 ||
vada kena pathā yātā dasyavo dvijasattama  |
tena gacchāmahe brahman yathā śīghrataraṃ vayam  || 198.12 ||
tathā tu vacanaṃ teṣāṃ bruvatāṃ sa tapodhanaḥ  |
na kiṃcidvacanaṃ rājannavadatsādhvasādhu vā  || 198.13 ||
tataste rājapuruṣā vicinvantastamāśramam  |
saṃyamyainaṃ tato rājñe sarvān dasyūnnyavedayan  || 198.14 ||
taṃ rājā sahitaiścorairanvaśādvadhyatāmiti  |
sambadhya taṃ ca tairrājañchūle proto mahātapāḥ  || 198.15 ||
tataste śūlamāropya taṃ muniṃ rakṣiṇastadā  |
pratijagmurmahīpāla dhanānyādāya tānyatha  || 198.16 ||
śūlasthaḥ sa tu dharmātmā kālena mahatā tadā  |
dhyāyandevaṃ trilokeśaṃ śaṅkaraṃ tamumāpatim  || 198.17 ||
bahukālaṃ maheśānaṃ manasādhyāya saṃsthitaḥ  |
nirāhāro'pi viprarṣirmaraṇaṃ nābhyapadyata  || 198.18 ||
dhārayāmāsa viprāṇāmṛṣabhaḥ sa hṛdā harim  |
śūlāgre tapyamānena tapastena kṛtaṃ tadā  || 198.19 ||
santāpaṃ paramaṃ jagmuḥ śrutvaitanmunayo'khilāḥ  |
te rātrau śakunā bhūtvā saṃnyavartanta bhārata  || 198.20 ||
darśayanto muneḥ śaktiṃ tamapṛcchan dvijottamam  |
śrotumicchāma te brahman kiṃ pāpaṃ kṛtavānasi  || 198.21 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ sa muniśārdūlastānuvāca tapodhanān  |
doṣataḥ kiṃ gamiṣyāmi na hi me'nyo parādhyati  || 198.22 ||
evamuktvā tataḥ sarvānācacakṣe tato muniḥ  |
munayaśca tato rājñe dvitīye'hni nyavedayan  || 198.23 ||
rājā tu tamṛṣiṃ śrutvā niṣkrāntaḥ saha bandhubhiḥ  |
prasādayāmāsa tadā śūlasthamṛṣisattamam  || 198.24 ||

rājovāca -
yanmayā'pakṛtaṃ tāta tavājñānavaśādbahu  |
prasādaye tvāṃ tatrāhaṃ na me tvaṃ kroddhumarhasi  || 198.25 ||
evamuktastato rājñā prasādamakaronmuniḥ  |
kṛtaprasādaṃ rājā taṃ tataḥ samavatārayat || 198.26 ||
avatīryamāṇastu muniḥ śūle māṃsatvamāgate  |
atisaṃpīḍito vipraḥ śaṅkaraṃ manasāgamat || 198.27 ||
saṃdhyātaḥ śaṅkarastena bahukālopavāsataḥ  |
prādurbhūto mahādevaḥ śūlaṃ tasya tathāchinat || 198.28 ||
śūlamūlasthitaḥ śambhustuṣṭaḥ prāha punaḥpunaḥ  |
brūhi kiṃ kriyatāṃ vipra sattvasthānaparāyaṇa  || 198.29 ||
adeyamapi dāsyāmi tuṣṭo'smyadyomayā saha  |
kiṃ tu satyavatāṃ loke siddhirna syācca bhūyasī  || 198.30 ||
svakarmaṇo'nurūpaṃ hi phalaṃ bhuñjanti jantavaḥ  |
śubhena karmaṇā bhūtirduḥkhaṃ syātpātakena tu  || 198.31 ||
bahubhedaprabhinnaṃ tu manuṣyeṣu vipacyate  |
keṣāṃ daridrabhāvena keṣāṃ dhanavipattijam  || 198.32 ||
santatyabhāvajaṃ keṣāṃ keṣāṃcittadviparyayaḥ  |
tathā durvṛttitasteṣāṃ phalamāvirbhavennṛṇām  || 198.33 ||
keṣāṃcitputramaraṇe viyogātpriyamitrayoḥ  |
rājacaurāgnitaḥ keṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ syāddaivanirmitam  || 198.34 ||
taccharīre tu keṣāṃcitkarmaṇā sampradṛśyate  |
jarāśca vividhāḥ keṣāṃ dṛśyante vyādhayastathā  || 198.35 ||
dṛśyante cābhiśāpāśca pūrvakarmānusaṃcitāḥ  |
kaṣṭāḥ kaṣṭatarāvasthā gatāḥ kecidanāgasaḥ  || 198.36 ||
pūrvakarmavipākena dharmeṇa tapasi sthitāḥ  |
dāntāḥ svadāraniratā bhūridāḥ paripūjakāḥ  || 198.37 ||
hrīmanto nayasaṃyuktā anye bahuguṇairyutāḥ  |
durgamāmāpadaṃ prāpya nijakarmasamudbhavām  || 198.38 ||
na saṃjvaranti ye martyā dharmanindāṃ na kurvate  |
idameva tapo matvā kṣipanti suvicetasaḥ  || 198.39 ||
hā bhrātarmātaḥ putreti kaṣṭeṣu na vadanti ye  |
smaranti māṃ maheśānamathavā puṣkarekṣaṇam  || 198.40 ||
duṣkṛtaṃ pūrvajaṃ bhoktuṃ dhruvaṃ tadupaśāmyati  || 198.41 ||
dināni yāvanti vasetsa kaṣṭe yathākṛtaṃ cintayaddevamīśam  |
tāvanti saumyāni kṛtāni tena bhavanti vipra śrutinodanaiṣā  || 198.42 ||
yasmāttvayā kaṣṭagatena nityaṃ smṛtaścāhaṃ manasā pūjitaśca  |
gaurīsahāyastena ihāgato'smi brūhyadya kṛtyaṃ kriyatāṃ kiṃ nu vipra  || 198.43 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
tuṣṭo yadyumayā sārdhaṃ varado yadi śaṅkara  |
tadā me śūlasaṃsthasya saṃśayaṃ paramaṃ vada  || 198.44 ||
na rujā mama kāpi syācchūlasaṃprotite'gake  |
amṛtasrāvi tacchūlaṃ prabhāvātkasya śaṃsa me  || 198.45 ||

śrīśūlapāṇiruvāca -
śūlasthena tvayā vipra manasā cintito'smi yat |
anayānāṃ nihantāhaṃ duḥkhānāṃ vinibarhaṇaḥ  || 198.46 ||
dhyātamātro hyahaṃ vipra pātāle vāpi saṃsthitaḥ  |
śūlamūle tvahaṃ śambhuragre devī svayaṃ sthitā  |
jaganmātāmbikā devī tvāmṛtenānvapūrayat || 198.47 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
pūrvameva sthito yasmācchūlaṃ vyāpyomayā saha  |
prasādapravaṇo mahyamidānīṃ cānayā saha  || 198.48 ||
yasyāḥ saṃsmaraṇādeva daurbhāgyaṃ pralayaṃ vrajet |
na daurbhāgyātparaṃ loke duḥkhādduḥkhataraṃ kila  || 198.49 ||
kilaivaṃ śrūyate gāthā purāṇeṣu surottama  |
trailokyaṃ dahatastubhyaṃ saubhāgyamekatāṃ gatam  || 198.50 ||
viṣṇorvakṣaḥsthalaṃ prāpya tatsthitaṃ ceti naḥ śrutam  |
pītaṃ tadvakṣasastrastadakṣeṇa parameṣṭhinā  || 198.51 ||
tasmātsatīti saṃjajña iyamindīvarekṣaṇā  |
yajatastasya deveśa tava mānāvakhaṇḍanāt || 198.52 ||
juhāvāgnau tu sā devī hyātmānaṃ prāṇasaṃjñikam  |
ātmānaṃ bhasmasātkṛtvā prāleyādrestataḥ sutā  || 198.53 ||
menakāyāṃ prabho jātā sāmprataṃ yā hyumābhidhā  |
anādinidhanā devī hyapratarkyā sureśvara  || 198.54 ||
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa hyumā me varadā yadi  |
ubhāvapyatra vai sthāne sthitau śūlāgramūlayoḥ  || 198.55 ||
avatāro yatra tatra saṃsthitiṃ vai tataḥ kuru  || 198.56 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tenaivamukte sahasā kṛtvā bhūmaṇḍalaṃ dvidhā  |
niḥsṛtau śūlamūlāgrālliṅgārcāpratirūpiṇau  || 198.57 ||
pradyotayaddiśaḥ sarvā liṅgaṃ mūle pradṛśyate  |
vāmataḥ pratimā devī tadā śūleśvarī sthitā  || 198.58 ||
vilobhayantī ca jagadbhāti pūrayatī diśaḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā kṛtāñjalipuṭaḥ stutiṃ cakre dvijottamaḥ  || 198.59 ||

māṇḍavya uvāca -
tvamasya jagato mātā jagatsaubhāgyadevatā  |
na tvayā rahitaṃ kiṃcidbrahmāṇḍe'sti varānane  || 198.60 ||
prasādaṃ kuru dharmajñe mama tvājñaptumarhasi  |
īdṛśenaiva rūpeṇa keṣu sthāneṣu tiṣṭhasi  |
prasādapravaṇā bhūtvā vada tāni maheśvari  || 198.61 ||

śrīdevyuvāca -
sarvagā sarvabhūteṣu draṣṭavyā sarvato bhuvi  |
sarvalokeṣu yatkiṃcidvihitaṃ na mayā vinā  || 198.62 ||
tathāpi yeṣu sthāneṣu draṣṭavyā siddhimīpsubhiḥ  |
smartavyā bhūtikāmena tāni vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ  || 198.63 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣī naimiṣe liṅgadhāriṇī  |
prayāge lalitā devī kāmukā gandhamādane  || 198.64 ||
mānase kumudā nāma viśvakāyā tathā'pare  |
gomante gomatī nāma mandare kāmacāriṇī  || 198.65 ||
madotkaṭā caitrarathe hayantī hāstine pure  |
kānyakubje sthitā gaurī rambhā hyamalaparvate  || 198.66 ||
ekāmrake kīrtimatī viśvāṃ viśveśvare viduḥ  |
puṣkare puruhūtā ca kedāre mārgadāyinī  || 198.67 ||
nandā himavataḥ prasthe gokarṇe bhadrakarṇikā  |
sthāneśvare bhavānī tu bilvake bilvapattrikā  || 198.68 ||
śrīśaile mādhavī nāma bhadre bhadreśvarīti ca  |
jayā varāhaśaile tu kamalā kamalālaye  || 198.69 ||
rudrakoṭyāṃ tu kalyāṇī kālī kālañjare tathā  |
mahāliṅge tu kapilā mākoṭe mukuṭeśvarī  || 198.70 ||
śāligrāme mahādevī śivaliṅge jalapriyā  |
māyāpuryāṃ kumārī tu saṃtāne lalitā tathā  || 198.71 ||
utpalākṣī sahasrākṣe hiraṇyākṣe mahotpalā  |
gayāyāṃ vimalā nāma maṅgalā puruṣottame  || 198.72 ||
vipāśāyāmamoghākṣī pāṭalā puṇḍravardhane  |
nārāyaṇī supārśve tu trikūṭe bhadrasundarī  || 198.73 ||
vipule vipulā nāma kalyāṇī malayācale  |
koṭavī koṭitīrtheṣu sugandhā gandhamādane  || 198.74 ||
godāśrame trisandhyā tu gaṅgādvāre ratipriyā  |
śivacaṇḍe sabhānandā nandinī devikātaṭe  || 198.75 ||
rukmiṇī dvāravatyāṃ tu rādhā vṛndāvane vane  |
devakī mathurāyāṃ tu pātāle parameśvarī  || 198.76 ||
citrakūṭe tathā sītā vindhye vindhyanivāsinī  |
sahyādrāvekavīrā tu hariścandre tu caṇḍikā  || 198.77 ||
ramaṇā rāmatīrthe tu yamunāyāṃ mṛgāvatī  |
karavīre mahālakṣmī rūpādevī vināyake  || 198.78 ||
ārogyā vaidyanāthe tu mahākāle maheśvarī  |
abhayetyuṣṇatīrthe tu mṛgī vā vindhyakandare  || 198.79 ||
māṇḍavye māṇḍukī nāma svāhā māheśvare pure  |
chāgaliṅge pracaṇḍā tu caṇḍikāmarakaṇṭake  || 198.80 ||
someśvare varārohā prabhāse puṣkarāvatī  |
vedamātā sarasvatyāṃ pārā pārātaṭe mune  || 198.81 ||
mahālaye mahābhāgā payoṣṇyāṃ piṅgaleśvarī  |
siṃhikā kṛtaśauce tu kartike caiva śāṃkarī  || 198.82 ||
utpalāvartake lolā subhadrā śoṇasaṅgame  |
matā siddhavaṭe lakṣmīstaraṃgā bhāratāśrame  || 198.83 ||
jālandhare viśvamukhī tārā kiṣkindhaparvate  |
devadāruvane puṣṭirmedhā kāśmīramaṇḍale  || 198.84 ||
bhīmādevī himādrau tu puṣṭirvastreśvare tathā  |
kapālamocane śuddhirmātā kāyāvarohaṇe  || 198.85 ||
śaṅkhoddhāre dhvanirnāma dhṛtiḥ piṇḍārake tathā  |
kālā tu candrabhāgāyāmacchode śaktidhāriṇī  || 198.86 ||
veṇāyāmamṛtā nāma badaryāmurvaśī tathā  |
oṣadhī cottarakurau kuśadvīpe kuśodakā  || 198.87 ||
manmathā hemakūṭe tu kumude satyavādinī  |
aśvatthe vandinīkā tu nidhirvaiśravaṇālaye  || 198.88 ||
gāyatrī vedavadane pārvatī śivasannidhau  |
devaloke tathendrāṇī brahmāsye tu sarasvatī  || 198.89 ||
sūryabimbe prabhā nāma mātḥṇāṃ vaiṣṇavī matā  |
arundhatī satīnāṃ tu rāmāsu ca tilottamā  || 198.90 ||
citre brahmakalā nāma śaktiḥ sarvaśarīriṇām  |
śūleśvarī bhṛgukṣetre bhṛgau saubhāgyasundarī  || 198.91 ||
etaduddeśataḥ proktaṃ nāmāṣṭaśatamuttamam  |
aṣṭottaraṃ ca tīrthānāṃ śatametadudāhṛtam  || 198.92 ||
idameva paraṃ vipra sarveṣāṃ tu bhaviṣyati  |
paṭhatyaṣṭottaraśataṃ nāmnāṃ yaḥ śivasannidhau  || 198.93 ||
sa mucyate naraḥ pāpaiḥ prāpnoti striyamīpsitām  |
snātvā nārī tṛtīyāyāṃ māṃ samabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ  || 198.94 ||
na sā syādduḥkhinī jātu matprabhāvānnarottama  |
nityaṃ maddarśane nārī niyatāyā bhaviṣyati  || 198.95 ||
patiputrakṛtaṃ duḥkhaṃ na sā prāpsyati karhicit |
madālaye tu yā nārī tulāpuruṣasaṃjñitam  || 198.96 ||
sampūjya maṇḍayeddevāṃllokapālāṃśca sāgnikān  |
sapatnīkāndvijānpūjya vāsobhirbhūṣaṇaistathā  || 198.97 ||
bhūtebhyastu baliṃ dadyādṛtvigbhiḥ saha deśikaḥ  |
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tulāmityabhimantrayet || 198.98 ||
śuciraktāmbaro vā syādgṛhītvā kusumāñjalim  |
namaste sarvadevānāṃ śaktistvaṃ paramā sthitā  || 198.99 ||
sākṣibhūtā jagaddhātrī nirmitā viśvayoninā  |
tvaṃ tule sarvabhūtānāṃ pramāṇamiha kīrtitā  || 198.100 ||
karābhyāṃ baddhamuṣṭibhyāmāste paśyannumāmukham  |
tato'pare tulābhāgenyaseyurdvijapuṃgavāḥ  || 198.101 ||
dravyamaṣṭavidhaṃ tatra hyātmavittānusārataḥ  |
mandaśabhūte viprendra pṛthivyāṃ yadadhiṣṭhitam  || 198.102 ||
suvarṇaṃ caiva niṣpāvāṃstathā rājikusumbhakam  |
tṛṇarājendulavaṇaṃ kuṅkumaṃ tu tathāṣṭamam  || 198.103 ||
eṣāmekatamaṃ kuryādyathā vittānusārataḥ  |
sāmyādabhyadhikaṃ yāvatkāñcanādi bhaveddvija  || 198.104 ||
tāvattiṣṭhennaro nārī paścādidamudīrayet |
namo namaste lalite tulāpuruṣasaṃjñite  || 198.105 ||
tvamume tārayasvāsmānasmātsaṃsārakardamāt |
tato'vatīrya murave pūrvamarddhaṃ nivedayet || 198.106 ||
ṛtvigbhyo'paramarddhaṃ ca dadyādudakapūrvakam  |
tebhyo labdhā tato'nujñāṃ dadyādanyeṣu cārthiṣu  || 198.107 ||
sapatnīkaṃ guruṃ raktavāsasī paridhāpayet |
anyāṃśca ṛtvijaḥ śaktyā guruṃ keyūrakaṅkaṇaiḥ  || 198.108 ||
śuklāṃ gāṃ kṣīriṇīṃ dadyāllalitā prīyatāmiti  |
anena vidhinā yā tu kuryānnārī mamālaye  || 198.109 ||
mattulyā sā bhavedrājñāṃ tejasā śrīrivāmalā  |
sāvitrīva ca saundarye janmāni daśa pañca ca  || 198.110 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ niśamya vacanaṃ gauryā dvijavarottamaḥ  |
namaskṛtya jagāmāśu dharmarāja niveśanam  || 198.111 ||
tadā prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ khyātaṃ śūleśvarīti ca  |
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 198.112 ||
brāhmaṇānannavāsobhiḥ piṇḍaiḥ pitṛpitāmahān  |
bhaktopahārairdeveśamumayā saha śaṅkaraṃ  || 198.113 ||
dhūpagugguladānaiśca dīpadānaiḥ subodhitaiḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa gacchecchivasannidhim  || 198.114 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu yaḥ kaścidabhiyukto nareśvara  |
ambhiśāpi tathā snātastridinaṃ mucyate naraḥ  || 198.115 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ rātrau jāgarti yo naraḥ  |
upavāsaparaḥ śuddhaḥ śivaṃ sampūjayennaraḥ  |
pramucya pāpasaṃmohaṃ rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 198.116 ||
trinetraśca caturbāhuḥ sākṣādrudro'paraḥ  |
krīḍate devakanyābhiryāvaccandrārkatārakam  || 198.117 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śūleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 199

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ rājannāśvinaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
kāmikaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ prāṇināṃ siddhidāyakam  || 199.1 ||
tatra tīrthe'śvinau devau surūpau bhiṣajāṃ varau  |
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ saṃjātau yajñabhāginau  || 199.2 ||
saṃmatau sarvadevānāmādityatanayāvubhau  |
nāsatyau sattvaṃ pannau sarvaduḥkhaghnasattamau  || 199.3 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
ādityasya sutau tāta nāsatyau yena hetunā  |
saṃjātau śrotumicchāmi nirṇayaṃ paramaṃ dvija  || 199.4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
purāṇe bhāskare tāta etadvistarato mayā  |
saṃśrutaṃ devadevasya mārtaṇḍasya mahātmanaḥ  || 199.5 ||
tatte saṃkṣepataḥ sarvaṃ bhaktiyuktasya bhārata  |
kathayāmi na sandeho vṛddhabhāvena karśitaḥ  || 199.6 ||
atitejoraverdṛṣṭvā rājñī devī narottama  |
cacāra merukāntāre vaḍavā tapa ulbaṇam  || 199.7 ||
tataḥ katipayāhasya kālasya bhagavānraviḥ  |
dṛṣṭvā tu rūpamutsṛjya paramaṃ teja ujjvalam  || 199.8 ||
manobhavavaśībhūto hayo bhūtvā laghukramaḥ  |
visphurantī yathāprāṇaṃ dhāvamānā itastataḥ  || 199.9 ||
heṣamāṇaḥ svareṇāsau maithunāyopacakrame  |
sammukhī tu tato devī nivṛttā laghuvikramā  || 199.10 ||
yathā tathā nāsikāyāṃ praviṣṭaṃ bījamuttamam  |
tato nāsāgate bīje saṃjāto garbha uttamaḥ  || 199.11 ||
jātau yataḥ sutau pārtha nāsatyau viśrutau tataḥ  |
susamau suvibhaktāṅgau bimbādbimbamivodyatau  || 199.12 ||
adhikau sarvadevānāṃ rūpaiścaryasamanvitau  |
narmadātaṭamāśritya bhṛgukacche gatāvubhau  |
parāṃ siddhimanuprāptau tapaḥ kṛtvā suduścaram  || 199.13 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  |
surūpaḥ subhagaḥ pārtha jāyate yatra tatra ca  || 199.14 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe āśvinatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 200

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ pārtha sāvitrītīrthamuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgā sāvitrī vedamātṛkā  || 200.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
sāvitrī kā dvijaśreṣṭha kathaṃ vārādhyate budhaiḥ  |
prasannā vā varaṃ kaṃ ca dadāti kathayasva me  || 200.2 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
padmā padmāsanasthenādhiṣṭhitā padmayoginī  |
sāvitratejaḥsadṛśī sāvitrī tena cocyate  || 200.3 ||
padmānanā padmavarṇā padmapatranibhekṣaṇā  |
dhyātavyā brāhmaṇairnityaṃ kṣatravaiśyairyathāvidhi  || 200.4 ||
brahmahatyābhayātsā hi na tu śūdraiḥ kadācana  |
uccāraṇāddhāraṇādvā narake patati dhruvam  || 200.5 ||
vedoccāraṇamātreṇa kṣatriyairdharmapālakaiḥ  |
jihvāchedo'sya kartavyaḥ śūdrasyeti viniścayaḥ  || 200.6 ||
bālā bālendusadṛśī raktavastrānulepanā  |
uṣaḥkāle tu dhyātavyā sandhyā sandhāna uttame  || 200.7 ||
uttuṅgapīvarakucā sumukhī śubhadarśanā  |
sarvābharaṇasampannā śvetamālyānulepanā  || 200.8 ||
śvetavastraparicchannā śvetayajñopavītinī  |
madhyāhnasandhyā dhyātavyā taruṇā bhuktimuktidā  || 200.9 ||
pradoṣe tu punaḥ pārtha śvetā pāṇḍuramūrdhajā  |
sumṛtā tu durgakāntāre mātṛvatparirakṣati  || 200.10 ||
viśeṣeṇa tu rājendra sāvitrītīrthamuttamam  |
snātvācamya vidhānena manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ  || 200.11 ||
prāṇāyāmairdaheddoṣān saptajanmārjitānbahūn  |
āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa prokṣayedātmanastanum  || 200.12 ||
navaṣaṭca tathā tisrastatra tīrthe nṛpottama  |
āpohiṣṭheti trirāvṛtya pratigrāhairna lipyate  || 200.13 ||
aghamarṣaṇaṃ tryṛcaṃ toyaṃ yathāvedamathāpi vā  |
upapāpairna lipyeta padmapatramivāmbhasā  || 200.14 ||
tryāpaṃ hi kurute vipra ullekhatrayamācaret |
caturthaṃ kārayedyastu brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 200.15 ||
drupadākhyaśca yo mantro vede vājasaneyake  |
antarjale sakṛjjaptaḥ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaraḥ  || 200.16 ||
udutyamiti mantreṇa pūjayitvā divākaram  |
gāyatrīṃ ca japeddevīṃ pavitrāṃ vedamātaram  || 200.17 ||
gāyatrīṃ tu japeddevīṃ yaḥ sandhyānantaraṃ dvijaḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati  || 200.18 ||
daśabhirjanmabhirlabdhaṃ śatena tu purākṛtam  |
triyugaṃ tu sahasreṇa gāyatrī hanti kilbiṣam  || 200.19 ||
gāyatrīsāramātro'pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ  |
nāyantritaścaturvedī sarvāśī sarvavikrayī  || 200.20 ||
sandhyāhīno'śucirnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu  |
yadanyatkurute kiṃcinna tasya phalabhāgbhavet || 200.21 ||
sandhyāṃ nopāsate yastu brāhmaṇo mandabuddhimān  |
sa jīvanneva śūdraḥ syānmṛtaḥ śvā samprajāyate  || 200.22 ||
sāvitrītīrthamāsādya sāvitrīṃ yo japeddvijaḥ  |
traividyaṃ tu phalaṃ tasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 200.23 ||
pitḥnuddiśya yaḥ snātvā piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ nṛpa  |
kurute dvādaśābdāni tṛpyanti tatpitāmahāḥ  || 200.24 ||
sāvitrītīrthamāsādya yaḥ kuryātprāṇasaṃkṣayam  |
brahmalokaṃ vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 200.25 ||
pūrṇe caiva tataḥ kāla iha mānuṣyatāṃ gataḥ  |
caturvedo dvijo rājañjāyate vimale kule  || 200.26 ||
dhanadhānyacayopetaḥ putrapautrasamanvitaḥ  |
vyādhiśokavinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 200.27 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe sāvitrītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 201

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchenmahīpāla devatīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgā devāḥ sendrā yudhiṣṭhira  || 201.1 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  |
tatra tīrthaprabhāvena kṛtamānantyamaśnute  || 201.2 ||
viśeṣādbhādrapade tu kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśīm  |
pradhānaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ devairadhyāsitaṃ purā  || 201.3 ||
snātvā trayodaśīdine śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  |
devaiḥ saṃsthāpitaṃ devaṃ sampūjya vṛṣabhadhvajam  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudralokamavāpnuyāt || 201.4 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe devatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 202

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ cānyacchikhitīrthamanuttamam  |
pradhānaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ pañcāyatanamuttamam  || 202.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tapastaptvā śikhārthaṃ havyavāhanaḥ  |
śikhāṃ prāpya śikhī bhūtvā śikhākhyaṃ sthāpayañchivam  || 202.2 ||
pratipacchuklapakṣe yā bhavedāśvayuje nṛpa  |
tadā tīrthavare gatvā snātvā vai narmadājale  || 202.3 ||
devānṛṣīn pitḥṃścānyāṃstarpayettilavāriṇā  |
hiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇe dadyātsaṃtarpya ca hutāśanam  || 202.4 ||
gandhamālyaistathā dhūpaistataḥ sampūjayecchivam  |
anena vidhinābhyarcya śikhitīrthe maheśvaram  || 202.5 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena hyapsarogaṇasaṃvṛtaḥ  |
gīyamānastu gandharvairrudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 202.6 ||
śatrukṣayamavāpnoti tejasvī jāyate bhuvi  || 202.7 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śikhitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 203

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharādhīśa koṭitīrthamanuttamam  |
yatra siddhā mahābhāgāḥ koṭisaṃkhyā maharṣayaḥ  || 203.1 ||
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulamṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitaḥ śivaḥ  |
tathā koṭīśvarī devī cāmuṇḍā mahiṣārdinī  || 203.2 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ māsi bhādrapade nṛpa  |
tīrthakoṭīḥ samāhūya munibhiḥ sthāpitaḥ śivaḥ  || 203.3 ||
tasyāṃ tithau ca hastarkṣaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  |
tatra tīrthe tadā gatvā snānaṃ kṛtvā samāhitaḥ  || 203.4 ||
narakāduddharatyāśu puruṣānekaviṃśatim  |
tilodakapradānena kimuta śrāddhado naraḥ  || 203.5 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam  |
tasya tīrthasya yogena sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 203.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe koṭitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma tryuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 204

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhṛgutīrthaṃ tato gacchettīrtharājamanuttamam  |
paitāmahaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam  || 204.1 ||
brahmaṇā tatra tīrthe tu purā varṣaśatatrayam  |
ārādhanaṃ kṛtaṃ śambhoḥ kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  || 204.2 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kimarthaṃ muniśārdūla brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
ārādhayaddevadevaṃ mahābhaktyā maheśvaram  || 204.3 ||
ārādhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ jagadbhartā jagadguruḥ  |
śrotavyaṃ śrotumicchāmi mahadāścaryamuttamam  || 204.4 ||
dharmaputravacaḥ śrutvā mārkaṇḍeyo munīśvaraḥ  |
kathayāmāsa tadvṛttamitihāsaṃ purātanam  || 204.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
svaputrikāmabhigantumicchanpūrvaṃ pitāmahaḥ  |
śaptastu devadevena kopāviṣṭena sattama  || 204.6 ||
vedāstava vinaśyanti jñānaṃ ca kamalāsana  |
apūjyaḥ sarvalokānāṃ bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ  || 204.7 ||
evaṃ datte tataḥ śāpe brahmā khedāvṛtastadā  |
revāyā uttare kūle snātvā varṣaśatatrayam  |
toṣayāmāsa deveśaṃ tuṣṭaḥ provāca śaṅkaraḥ  || 204.8 ||
pūjyastvaṃ bhavitā loke prāpte parvaṇi parvaṇi  |
ahamatra ca vatsyāmi devaiśca pitṛbhiḥ saha  || 204.9 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ khyātiṃ prāptaṃ pitāmahāt |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  || 204.10 ||
tatra bhādrapade māsi kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ  |
amāvāsyāṃ tu yaḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 204.11 ||
piṇḍadānena caikena tilatoyena vā nṛpa  |
tṛpyanti dvādaśābdāni pitaro nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 204.12 ||
kanyāgate tu yastatra nityaṃ śrāddhaprado bhavet |
avāpya tṛptiṃ tatpūrve valganti ca hasanti ca  || 204.13 ||
sarveṣu pitṛtīrtheṣu śrāddhaṃ kṛtvāsti yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti darśe tatra na saṃśayaḥ  || 204.14 ||
paitāmahe naraḥ snātvā pūjayanpārvatīpatim  |
mucyate nātra sandehaḥ pātakaiścopapātakaiḥ  || 204.15 ||
tatra tīrthe mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
anivartikā gatī rājanrudralokādasaṃśayam  || 204.16 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe paitāmahatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 205

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gacchettataḥ kṣoṇinātha tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
kurkurīnāma vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  || 205.1 ||
yaṃ yaṃ prārthayate kāmaṃ paśuputradhanādikam  |
taṃ taṃ dadāti deveśī kurkurī tīrthadevatā  || 205.2 ||
kṣetrapālo vasettatra ḍhauṇḍheśo nāma nāmataḥ  |
tasya cārādhanaṃ kṛtvā nārī vā puruṣo'pi vā  || 205.3 ||
vandanādapi rājendra daurbhāgyaṃ nāśamāpnuyāt |
aputro labhate putramadhano dhanamuttamam  || 205.4 ||
nārī narastathāpyevaṃ labhate kāmamuttamam  |
sparśanāddarśanāttasya tīrthasya vidhipūrvakam  || 205.5 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe kurkurītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||


ṛKV adhyāya 206

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gacchettataḥ kṣoṇinātha tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ daśakanyeti viśrutam  |
mahādevakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam  || 206.1 ||
tatra tīrthe mahādevo daśakanyā guṇānvitāḥ  |
brahmaṇo varayāmāsa hyudvāhena yuyoja ha  || 206.2 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ daśakanyeti viśrutam  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyamakṣayaṃ kīrtitaṃ phalam  || 206.3 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kanyāṃ dadāti samalaṃkṛtām  |
prāpnoti puruṣo dattvā yathāśaktyā svalaṃkṛtām  || 206.4 ||
tena dānotthapuṇyena pūtātmāno narādhipa  |
vasanti romasaṃkhyāni varṣāṇi śivasannidhau  || 206.5 ||
tataḥ kālena mahatā tviha loke nareśvara  |
mānuṣyaṃ prāpya duṣprāpyaṃ dhanakoṭīpatirbhavet || 206.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā snātvā viprāya kāñcanam  |
samprayacchati śāntāya so'tyantaṃ sukhamaśnute  || 206.7 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ vāpi karmajaṃ yatpurā kṛtam  |
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti svarṇadānena bhārata  || 206.8 ||
naro dattvā suvarṇaṃ cāpi vālāgramātrakam  |
tatra tīrthe divaṃ yāti mṛto nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ  || 206.9 ||
tatra vidyādharaiḥ siddhairvimānavaramāsthitaḥ  |
pūjyamāno vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 206.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe daśakanyātīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 207

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyāgre pāvanaṃ tīrthaṃ svarṇabindviti viśrutam  |
yatra snātvā divaṃ yānti mṛtāśca na punarbhavam  || 207.1 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā datte viprāya kāñcanam  |
tena yattu phalaṃ proktaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva mahīpate  || 207.2 ||
sarveṣāmeva ratnānāṃ kāñcanaṃ ratnamuttamam  |
agnitejaḥsamudbhūtaṃ tena tatparamaṃ bhuvi  || 207.3 ||
tenaiva dattā pṛthivī saśailavanakānanā  |
sapattanapurā sarvā kāñcanaṃ yaḥ prayacchati  || 207.4 ||
mānasaṃ vācikaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā yatpurā kṛtam  |
tatsarvaṃ naśyati kṣipraṃ svarṇadānena bhārata  || 207.5 ||
svarṇadānaṃ tu yo dattvā hyapi vālāgramātrakam  |
tatra tīrthe mṛto yāti divaṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ  || 207.6 ||
tatra vidyādharaiḥ siddhairvimānavaramāsthitaḥ  |
pūjyamāno vasettāvadyāvadābhūtasamplavam  || 207.7 ||
pūrṇe tatra tataḥ kāle prāpya mānuṣyamuttamam  |
suvarṇakoṭisahite gṛhe vai jāyate dvijaḥ  || 207.8 ||
sarvavyādhivinirmuktaḥ sarvalokeṣu pūjitaḥ  |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ rājasaṃ satsu viśrutaḥ  || 207.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe suvarṇabindutīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 208

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhūmipāla tato gacchettīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu pitḥṇāmṛṇamocanam  || 208.1 ||
tatra snātvā vidhānena saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
manuṣyaśca nṛpaśreṣṭha dānaṃ dattvānṛṇo bhavet || 208.2 ||
icchanti pitaraḥ sarve svārthahetoḥ sutaṃ yataḥ  |
punnāmno narakātputro'smānayaṃ mocayiṣyati  || 208.3 ||
piṇḍadānaṃ jalaṃ tāta ṛṇamuttamamucyate  |
pitḥṇāṃ taddhi vai proktamṛṇaṃ daivamataḥ param  || 208.4 ||
agnihotraṃ tathā yajñāḥ paśubandhāstatheṣṭayaḥ  |
iti devarṇaṃ proktaṃ śṛṇu mānuṣyakaṃ tataḥ  || 208.5 ||
brāhmaṇeṣu ca tīrtheṣu devāyatanakarmasu  |
pratiśrutya dadettattadvyavahāraḥ kṛto yathā  || 208.6 ||
ṛṇatrayamidaṃ proktaṃ putrāṇāṃ dharmanandana  |
satputrāste tu rājendra snātā ya ṛṇamocane  || 208.7 ||
ṛṇatrayādvimucyante hyaputrāḥ putriṇastathā  |
tasmāttīrthavaraṃ prāpya putreṇa niyatātmanā  |
pitṛbhyastarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 208.8 ||
tatra tīrthe hutaṃ dattaṃ guravastoṣitā yadi  |
mṛtānāṃ sapta janmāni phalamakṣayamaśnute  || 208.9 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ṛṇamocanatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 209

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ pārtha puṣkalītīrthamuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā hyaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 209.1 ||
kṣamānāthaṃ tato gacchettīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam  |
dānavagandharvairapsarobhiśca sevitam  || 209.2 ||
tatra tiṣṭhati deveśaḥ sākṣādrudro maheśvaraḥ  |
bhāreṇa mahatā jāto bhārabhūtiriti smṛtaḥ  || 209.3 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
bhārabhūtīti vikhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇānvitam  |
śrotumicchāmi viprendra paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me  || 209.4 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
bhārabhūtisamutpattiṃ śṛṇu pāṇḍavasattama  |
vistareṇa yathā proktā purā devena śambhunā  || 209.5 ||
āsītkṛtayuge vipro vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ  |
viṣṇuśarmeti vikhyātaḥ sarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ  || 209.6 ||
kṣamā damo dayā dānaṃ satyaṃ śaucaṃ dhṛtistathā  |
vidyā vijñānamāstikyaṃ sarvaṃ tasminpratiṣṭhitam  || 209.7 ||
īdṛgguṇā hi ye viprā bhavanti nṛpasattama  |
patitānnarake ghore tārayanti pitḥṃstu te  || 209.8 ||
indriyaṃ lolupā viprā ye bhavanti nṛpottama  |
patanti narake ghore raurave pāpamohitāḥ  || 209.9 ||
ye kṣāntadāntāḥ śrutipūrṇakarṇā jitendriyāḥ prāṇivadhānnivṛttāḥ  |
pratigrahe saṃkucitāgrahastāste brāhmaṇāstārayituṃ samarthāḥ  || 209.10 ||
evaṃ guṇagaṇākīrṇo brāhmaṇo narmadātaṭe  |
vasate brāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ śiloñchavṛttijīvanaḥ  || 209.11 ||
tādṛśaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jñātvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
dvijarūpadharo bhūtvā tasyāśramamagātsvayam  || 209.12 ||
dṛṣṭvā taṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhamuccarantaṃ padakramam  |
abhivādayate vipraṃ svāgatena ca pūjitaḥ  || 209.13 ||
provāca taṃ muhūrtena brāhmaṇo vismayānvitaḥ  |
kimatha tadbaṭo brūhi kiṃ karomi tavepsitam  || 209.14 ||

baṭuruvāca -
vidyārthinamanuprāptaṃ viddhi māṃ dvijasattama  |
dadāsi yadi me vidyāṃ tataḥ sthāsyāmi te gṛhe  || 209.15 ||

brāhmaṇa uvāca -
sarveṣāmeva viprāṇāṃ baṭo tvaṃ gotra uttame  |
dānānāṃ paramaṃ dānaṃ kathaṃ vidyā ca dīyate  || 209.16 ||
guruśuśrūṣayā vidyā puṣkalena dhanena vā  |
athavā vidyayā vidyā bhavatīha phalapradā  || 209.17 ||

baṭuruvāca -
yathānye bālakāḥ snātāḥ śuśrūṣanti hyaharniśam  |
tathāhaṃ baṭubhiḥ sārdhaṃ śuśrūṣāmi na saṃśayaḥ  || 209.18 ||
tatheti coktvā viprendraḥ pāṭhayaṃstaṃ dine dine  |
vartate saha śiṣyaiḥ sa śiloñchānupahārayan  || 209.19 ||
tataḥ katipayāhobhiḥ prokto baṭubhirīśvaraḥ  |
pacanādyaṃ baṭo karma kuru kramata āgatam  || 209.20 ||
tatheti cokto deveśo bhāragrāmamupāgataḥ  |
dhyātvā vanaspatīḥ sarvā idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 209.21 ||
yāvadāgacchate vipro baṭubhiḥ saha mandiram  |
adarśanābhiḥ kartavyaṃ tāvadannaṃ susaṃskṛtam  || 209.22 ||
evamuktvā tu tāḥ sarvā viśvarūpo maheśvaraḥ  |
krīḍanārthaṃ gatastatra baṭuveṣadharaḥ pṛthak || 209.23 ||
dṛṣṭvā samāgataṃ tatra baṭuveṣadharaṃ pṛthak |
dhiktvāṃ ca paruṣaṃ vākyamūcuste girisannidhau  || 209.24 ||
kṣutkṣāmakaṃṭhāḥ sarve ca gatvā tu kila mandiram  |
tvayā siddhena cānnena tṛptiṃ yāsyāmahe vayam  || 209.25 ||
tadvṛthā cintitaṃ sava tvayāgatya kṛtaṃ dvija  |
mithyāpratijñena satā duranuṣṭhitamadya te  || 209.26 ||

baṭuruvāca -
santāpamanutāpaṃ vā bhojanārthaṃ dvijarṣabhāḥ  |
mā kurudhvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ siddhe'gre gṛhameṣyathā  || 209.27 ||

baṭuruvāca -
dinaśeṣeṇa cāsmākaṃ pañcatāṃ ca dine dine  |
niṣpattiṃ yāti vā neti tadasiddhamaśeṣataḥ  || 209.28 ||
asiddhaṃ siddhamasmākaṃ yattvayā samudāhṛtam  |
dṛṣṭvānṛtaṃ gatāstatra tvāṃ baddhāmbhasi nikṣipe  || 209.29 ||

baṭuruvāca -
bhobhoḥ śṛṇudhva sarve'tra sopādhyāyā dvijottamāḥ  |
pratijñāṃ mama durdharṣāṃ yāṃ śrutvā vismayo bhavet || 209.30 ||
yadi siddhamidaṃ sarvamannaṃ syādāśrame guroḥ  |
yūyaṃ baddhvā mayā sarve kṣeptavyā narmadāmbhasi  || 209.31 ||
athavānnaṃ na siddhaṃ syādbhavadbhirdṛḍhabandhanaiḥ  |
gurostu paśyato baddhvā kṣeptavyo'haṃ narmadāhrade  || 209.32 ||
tatheti kṛtvā te sarve samayaṃ gurusannidhau  |
snātvā jāpyavidhānena bhūtagrāmaṃ tato yayuḥ  || 209.33 ||
dṛṣṭvā te vismayaṃ jagmurvistṛte bhakṣyabhojane  |
ṣaḍrasena nṛpaśreṣṭha bhuktvā hutvā pṛthakpṛthak || 209.34 ||
tataḥ provāca vacanaṃ hṛṣṭapuṣṭo dvijottamaḥ  |
varado'smi varaṃ vatsa vṛṇu yattava rocate  || 209.35 ||
sāṅgopāṅgāstu te vedāḥ śāstrāṇi vividhāni ca  |
pratibhāsyanti te vipra madīyo'stu varastvayam  || 209.36 ||
praṇamya baṭubhiḥ sārdhaṃ sa cikrīḍa yathāsukham  |
dvitīye tu tataḥ prāpte divase narmadājale  || 209.37 ||
krīḍanārthaṃ gatāḥ sarve sopādhyāyā yudhiṣṭhira  |
tataḥ smṛtvā paṇaṃ sarve bhāṣayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 209.38 ||
upādhyāyamathovāca natvā devaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ  |
jale prakṣepayāmyadya niṣpratijñān baṭūn prabho  || 209.39 ||
taddevasya vacaḥ śrutvā naṣṭāste baṭavo nṛpa  |
gurostu paśyato rājandhāvamānā diśo daśa  || 209.40 ||
vāyuvegena devena luñjitāste samantataḥ  |
bhāraṃ baddhvā tu sarveṣāṃ baṭūnāṃ ca nareśvara  || 209.41 ||
śāpānugrahako devo'kṣipattoye yathā gṛhe  |
tato viṣādamagamaddṛṣṭvā tānnarmadājale  || 209.42 ||
guruṇā baṭurukto'tha kimetatsāhasaṃ kṛtam  |
eteṣāṃ mātṛpitaro bālakānāṃ gṛhe'ṅganāḥ  || 209.43 ||
yadi pṛcchanti te bālān kva gatān kathayāmyaham  |
evaṃ sthite mahābhāga yadi kaścinmariṣyati  || 209.44 ||
tadā svakīyajīvena tvaṃ yojayitumarhasi  |
mṛteṣu teṣu vipreṣu na jīve niścayo mṛtaḥ  || 209.45 ||
brahmahatyāśca te bahvyo bhaviṣyanti mṛte mayi  |
dvijabandhanamātreṇa narako bhavati dhruvam  || 209.46 ||
maraṇādyāṃ gatiṃ yāsi na tāṃ vedmi dvijādhama  |
evamuktaḥ smitaṃ kṛtvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 209.47 ||
bhārabhūteśvare tīrtha ujjahāra jalāddvijān  |
muktvā bhāraṃ tu devena chādayitvā tu tāndvijān  || 209.48 ||
liṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tatra bhārabhūteti viśrutam  |
mṛtāṃstān vai dvijān dṛṣṭvā brahmahatyā nirākṛtā  || 209.49 ||
gatāni pañca vai dṛṣṭvā brahmahatyāśatāni vai  |
tataḥ sa vismayāviṣṭo dṛṣṭvā tānbālakān guruḥ  || 209.50 ||
nānyasya kasyacicchaktirevaṃ syādīśvaraṃ vinā  |
jñātvā taṃ devadeveśaṃ praṇāmamakaroddvijaḥ  || 209.51 ||
ajñānena mayā sava yaduktaṃ parameśvara  |
apriyaṃ yatkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ kṣantavyaṃ tanmama prabho  || 209.52 ||

deva uvāca -
bhagavangururbhavāndevo bhavānmama pitāmahaḥ  |
vedagarbha namaste'stu nāsti kaścidvyatikramaḥ  || 209.53 ||
janitā copanetā ca yastu vidyāṃ prayacchati  |
annadātā bhayatrātā pañcaite pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ  || 209.54 ||
evamuktvā jagannātho viṣṇuśarmāṇamānataḥ  |
tatra tīrthe jagāmāśu kailāsaṃ dharaṇīdharam  || 209.55 ||
tadāprabhṛti tattīrthaṃ bhārabhūtīti viśrutam  |
vikhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu mahāpātakanāśanam  || 209.56 ||
tatra tīrthe punarvṛttamitihāsaṃ bravīmi te  |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyamekāgrastvaṃ śṛṇuṣva tat || 209.57 ||
purā kṛtayugasyādau vaiśyaḥ kaścinmahāmanāḥ  |
sukeśa iti vikhyātastasya putro'tidhārmikaḥ  || 209.58 ||
somaśarmeti vikhyāto mṛtaḥ pṛthulalocanaḥ  |
sa sakhāyaṃ vaṇikputraṃ kaṃciccakre daridriṇam  || 209.59 ||
sudevamiti khyātaṃ sarvakarmasu kovidam  |
ekadā tu samaṃ tena vyavahāramacintayat || 209.60 ||
sakhe samudrayānena gacchāvottaraṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
bhāṇḍaṃ bahu samādāya madīye dravyasādhane  || 209.61 ||
paraṃ tīraṃ gamiṣyāva utkarṣastvāvayoḥ samaḥ  |
iti tau mantrayitvā tu mantravatsamabhīpsitam  || 209.62 ||
sarvaṃ prayāṇakaṃ gṛhya hyārūḍhau lavaṇodadhim  |
tau gatvā tu paraṃ bhāṇḍaṃ vikrīya puratastadā  || 209.63 ||
prāptau bahu suvarṇaṃ ca ratnāni vividhāni ca  |
nāvaṃ tāṃ saṃgatāṃ kṛtvā paścāttāvārurohatuḥ  || 209.64 ||
nāvamantarjale dṛṣṭvā niśīthe svarṇasaṃbhṛtām  |
dṛṣṭvā tu somaśarmāṇamutsaṅge kṛtamastakam  || 209.65 ||
śayānamativiśvastaṃ sahadevo vyacintayat |
eṣa nidrāvaśaṃ yāto mayi prāṇānnidhāya vai  || 209.66 ||
asyādhīnamidaṃ sarvaṃ dravyaratnamaśeṣataḥ  |
