utkarṣārddhaṃ tu me dadyāttatra gatveti vā na vā  || 209.67 ||
iti niścitya manasā pāpastaṃ lavaṇodadhau  |
cikṣepa somaśarmāṇaṃ pāpadhyātena cetasā  || 209.68 ||
uttīrya taraṇāttasmādgatvā saṃgṛhya taddhanam  |
tataḥ katipayāhobhiḥ saṃyuktaḥ kāladharmaṇā  || 209.69 ||
gato yamapuraṃ ghoraṃ gṛhīto yamakiṃkaraiḥ  |
sa nītastena mārgeṇa yatra saṃtapate raviḥ  || 209.70 ||
kṛtvā dvādaśadhātmānaṃ samprāpte pralaye yathā  |
sutīkṣṇāḥ kaṇṭakā yatra yatra śvānaḥ sudāruṇāḥ  || 209.71 ||
tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrā mahāvyālā vyāghrā yatra mahāvṛkāḥ  |
sutaptā vālukā yatra kṣudhā tṛṣṇā tamo mahat || 209.72 ||
pānīyasya kathā nāsti na chāyā nāśramaḥ kvacit |
annaṃ pānīyasahitaṃ yāvattaddīyate viṣam  || 209.73 ||
chāyāṃ saṃprārthamānānāṃ bhṛśaṃ jvalati pāvakaḥ  |
tairdahyamānā bahuśo vilapanti muhurmuhuḥ  || 209.74 ||
hā bhrātarmātaḥ putreti patanti pathi mūrchitāḥ  |
itthaṃbhūtena mārgeṇa sa gīto yamakiṃkaraiḥ  || 209.75 ||
yatra tiṣṭhati deveśaḥ prajāsaṃyamano yamaḥ  |
te dvāradeśe taṃ muktvācakṣuryamakiṃkarāḥ  || 209.76 ||
baddhvā taṃ galapāśena hyāsīnaṃ mitraghātinam  |
avadhāraya deveśa budhyasva yadanantaram  || 209.77 ||

yama uvāca -
na tu pūrvaṃ mukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mayā viśvāsaghātinām  |
ye mitradrohiṇaḥ pāpāsteṣāṃ kiṃ śāsanaṃ bhavet || 209.78 ||
ṛṣayo'tra vicārārthaṃ niyuktā nipuṇāḥ sthitāḥ  |
te yatra bruvate tatra kṣipadhvaṃ mā vicāryatām  || 209.79 ||
ityuktāste tamādāya kiṃkarāḥ śīghragāminaḥ  |
munīśāṃstatra tānūcustaṃ nivedya yamājñayā  || 209.80 ||
dvijā anena mitraṃ svaṃ prasuptaṃ niśi ghātitam  |
viśvastaṃ dhanalobhena ko daṇḍo'sya bhaviṣyati  || 209.81 ||
munaya ūcuḥ  |
adṛṣṭapūrvamasmābhirvadanaṃ mitraghātinām  |
kṛtvā paṭāntare hyenaṃ śṛṇvantu gatimasya tām  || 209.82 ||
te śāstrāṇi vicāryātha ṛṣayaśca parasparam  |
āhūya yamadūtāṃstānūcurbrāhmaṇapuṃgavāḥ  || 209.83 ||
ālokitāni śāstrāṇi vedāḥ sāṅgāḥ smṛtīrapi  |
purāṇāni ca mīmāṃsā dṛṣṭamasmābhiratra ca  || 209.84 ||
brahmaghne ca surāpe ca steye gurvaṅganāgame  |
niṣkṛtirvihitā śāstre kṛtaghne nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ  || 209.85 ||
ye strīghnāśca gurughnāśca ye bālabrahmaghātinaḥ  |
vihitā niṣkṛtiḥ śāstre kṛtaghne nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ  || 209.86 ||
vāpīkūpataḍāgānāṃ bhettāro ye ca pāpinaḥ  |
udyānavāṭikānāṃ ca chettāro ye ca durjanāḥ  || 209.87 ||
dāvāgnidāhakā ye ca satataṃ ye'suhiṃsakāḥ  |
nyāsāpahāriṇo ye ca garadāḥ svāmivañcakāḥ  || 209.88 ||
mātāpitṛgurūṇāṃ ca tyāgino doṣadāyinaḥ  |
svabhartṛvañcanaparā yā strī garbhapraghātinī  || 209.89 ||
vivekarahitā yā strī yāsnātā bhojane ratā  |
dvikālabhojanaratāstathā vaiṣṇavavāsare  || 209.90 ||
tāsāṃ strīṇāṃ gatirdṛṣṭā na tu viśvāsaghātinām  |
viśvāsaghātināṃ puṃsāṃ mitradrohakṛtāṃ tathā  || 209.91 ||
teṣāṃ gatirna vedeṣu purāṇeṣu ca kā kathā  |
iti sthiteṣu pāpeṣu gatireṣāṃ na vidyate  || 209.92 ||
nānyā gatirmitrahanane viśvastaghne ca naḥ śrutam  |
ito nītvā yamadūtā enaṃ viśvastaghātinam  || 209.93 ||
kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ paryāyeṇa pṛthakpṛthak |
narakeṣu ca sarveṣu triṃśatkoṭiṣu saṃkhyayā  || 209.94 ||
kṣipyatāmeṣa mitraghno vicāro mā vidhīyatām  |
iti te vacanaṃ śrutvā kiṃkarāstaṃ nigṛhya ca  || 209.95 ||
yatra te narakā ghorāstatra kṣeptuṃ gatāstataḥ  |
te tamādāya hi narake ghore rauravasaṃjñite  || 209.96 ||
cikṣipustatra pāpiṣṭhaṃ kṣipte rāvo'bhavanmahān  |
narakasthitabhūteṣu moktavyo naiṣa pāpakṛt || 209.97 ||
asya saṃsparśanādeva pīḍā śataguṇā bhavet |
yathā vyathāsikāṣṭhaiśca samiddhairdahanātmakaiḥ  || 209.98 ||
bhavati sparśanāttasya kimetena kṛtāmalam  |
yathā durjanasaṃsargātsujano yāti lāghavam  || 209.99 ||
sannidhānāttathāsyāśu kṣate kṣārāvasecanam  |
prasādaḥ kriyatāmāśu nīyatāṃ narake'nyataḥ  || 209.100 ||
evamuktāstatastaistu gatāste tvaśuciṃ prati  |
tatra te nārakāḥ santi pūrvavatte'pi cukruśuḥ  || 209.101 ||
evaṃ te kiṃkarāḥ sarve paryaṭannarakamaṇḍale  |
narake'pi sthitistasya nāsti pāpasya durmateḥ  || 209.102 ||
yadā tadā tu te sarve taṃ gṛhya yamasannidhau  |
gatvā nivedya tatsarvaṃ yaduktaṃ nārakairnaraiḥ  |
narake na sthitiryasya tasya kiṃ kriyatāṃ vada  || 209.103 ||

yama uvāca -
pāpiṣṭha eṣa vai yātu yoniṃ tiryaṅniṣevitām  |
kālaṃ munibhiruddiṣṭaḥ tiryagyoniṃ praveśyatām  || 209.104 ||
evamukte tu vacane prajāsaṃyamanena ca  |
sa gataḥ kṛmitāṃ pāpo viṣṭhāsu ca pṛthakpṛthak || 209.105 ||
tato'sau daṃśamaśakān pipīlikasamudbhavān  |
yūkāmatkuṇakāḍhyāṃśca gatvā pakṣitvamāgataḥ  || 209.106 ||
sthāvaratvaṃ gataḥ paścātpāṣāṇatvaṃ tataḥ param  |
sarīsṛpānajagaravarāhamṛgahastinaḥ  || 209.107 ||
vṛkaśvānakharoṣṭrāṃśca sūkarīṃ grāmajātikām  |
yonimāśvatarīṃ prāpya tathā mahiṣasambhavām  || 209.108 ||
etāścānyāśca bahvīrvai prāpa yonīḥ krameṇa vai  |
sa tā yonīranuprāpya dhuryo'bhūdbhāravāhakaḥ  || 209.109 ||
sa gṛhe pārthiveśasya dhārmikasya yaśasvinaḥ  |
sa dṛṣṭvā kārttikīṃ prāptāmekadā nṛpasattamaḥ  || 209.110 ||
purohitaṃ samāhūya brāhmaṇāṃśca tathā bahūn  |
na gṛhe kārttikīṃ kuryādetanme bahuśaḥ śrutam  || 209.111 ||
sametāḥ kutra yāsyāma iti brūta dvijottamāḥ  |
yo gṛhe kārttikīṃ kuryātsnānadānādivarjitaḥ  || 209.112 ||
saṃvatsarakṛtātpuṇyātsa bahirbhavati śrutiḥ  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tīrthaṃ sarvaguṇānvitam  || 209.113 ||
sahitāstatra gacchāmaḥ snātuṃ dātuṃ ca śaktitaḥ  |
evamukte tu vacane pārthivena dvijottamāḥ  || 209.114 ||
ūcuḥ śreṣṭhaṃ nṛpatheṣṭha revāyā uttare taṭe  |
bhāreśvareti vikhyātaṃ muktitīrthaṃ nṛpottama  || 209.115 ||
tatra yāmo vayaṃ sarve sarvapāpakṣayāvaham  |
evamuktaḥ sa nṛpatirgṛhītvā pracuraṃ vasu  || 209.116 ||
śakaṭaṃ saṃbhṛtaṃ kṛtvā tatra yuktaḥ sa dhūrvahaḥ  |
yaḥ kṛtvā mitrahananaṃ goyoniṃ samupāgataḥ  || 209.117 ||
itthaṃ sa narmadātīre samprāptastīrthamuttamam  |
gatvā caturdaśīdine hyupavāsakṛtakṣaṇaḥ  || 209.118 ||
gatvā sa narmadātīre nāma rudretyanusmaran  |
śucipradeśācca mṛdaṃ mantreṇānena gṛhyatām  || 209.119 ||
uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa rudreṇa śatabāhunā  |
ahamapyuddhariṣyāmi prajayā bandhanena ca  || 209.120 ||
sa evaṃ tāṃ mṛdaṃ nītvā muktvā tīre tathottare  |
dadarśa bhāskaraṃ paścānmantreṇānena cālabhet || 209.121 ||
aśvakrānte rathakrānte viṣṇukrānte vasuṃdhare  |
mṛttike hara me pāpaṃ janmakoṭiśatārjitam  || 209.122 ||
tata evaṃ vigāhyāpo mantrametamudīrayet |
tvaṃ narmade puṇyajale tavāmbhaḥ śaṅkarodbhavam  || 209.123 ||
snānaṃ prakurvato me'dya pāpaṃ haratu cārjitam  |
sa snātvānena vidhinā saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  || 209.124 ||
yayau devālayaṃ paścādupahāraiḥ samanvitaḥ  |
bhaktyā saṃcintya sānnidhye śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram  || 209.125 ||
purāṇoktavidhānena pūjāṃ samupacakrame  |
pūjācatuṣṭayaṃ devi śivarātryāṃ nigadyate  || 209.126 ||
saṃsnāpya prathame yāme pañcagavyena śaṅkaram  |
ghṛtena pūraṇaṃ paścātkṛtaṃ nṛpavareṇa tu  || 209.127 ||
dhūpadīpanaivedyādyaṃ saṃkalpya ca yathāvidhi  |
argheṇānena deveśaṃ mantreṇānena śaṅkaram  || 209.128 ||
namaste devadeveśa śambho paramakāraṇa  |
gṛhāṇārghamimaṃ deva saṃsārāghamapākuru  || 209.129 ||
vittānurūpato dattaṃ suvarṇaṃ mantrakalpitam  |
agnirhi devāḥ sarve suvarṇaṃ ca hutāśanāt || 209.130 ||
ataḥ suvarṇadānena prītāḥ syuḥ sarvadevatāḥ  |
tadarghaṃ sarvadā dātuḥ prīto bhavatu śaṅkaraḥ  || 209.131 ||
anena vidhinā tena pūjitaḥ prathame śivaḥ  |
yāme dvitīye tu punaḥ pūrvoktavidhinā caret || 209.132 ||
snāpayāmāsa dugdhena gavyena tripurāntakam  |
taṃdulaiḥ pūraṇaṃ paścātkṛtaṃ liṅgasya śūlinaḥ  || 209.133 ||
kṛtvā vidhānaṃ pūrvoktaṃ dattaṃ vastrayugaṃ sitam  |
śvetavastrayugaṃ yasmācchaṅkarasyātivallabham  || 209.134 ||
prīto bhavati vai śambhurdattena śvetavāsasā  |
yāmaṃ tṛtīyaṃ samprāptaṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛpatisattamaḥ  || 209.135 ||
devaṃ saṃsnāpya madhunā pūraṇaṃ cakrivāṃstilaiḥ  |
tiladroṇapradānaṃ ca kuryānmantramudīrayan  || 209.136 ||
tilāḥ śvetāstilāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ sarvapāpaharāstilāḥ  |
tiladroṇapradānenu saṃsāraśchidyatāṃ mama  || 209.137 ||
anena vidhinā rājā yāminīyāmapūjanam  |
ativāhya vinodena brahmaghoṣeṇa jāgaram  || 209.138 ||
cakāra pūjanaṃ śambhorbahupuṇyaprasādhakam  |
ye jāgare trinetrasya śivarātryāṃ śivasthitāḥ  || 209.139 ||
te yāṃ gatiṃ gatāḥ pārtha na tāṃ gacchanti yajvinaḥ  |
pāpāni yāni kāni syuḥ koṭijanmārjitānyapi  || 209.140 ||
harakeśavayoḥ snānti jāgare yānti saṃkṣayam  |
yāvanto nimiṣā nṛṇāṃ bhavanti niśi jāgratām  || 209.141 ||
nimiṣe nimiṣe rājannaśvamedhaphalaṃ dhruvam  |
upavāsaparāṇāṃ ca devāyatanavāsinām  || 209.142 ||
śṛṇvatāṃ dharmamākhyānaṃ dhyāyatāṃ harakeśavau  |
na tāṃ bahusuvarṇena kratunā gatimāpnuyuḥ  || 209.143 ||
śivarātristithiḥ puṇyā kārttikī ca viśeṣataḥ  |
revāyā uttaraṃ kūlaṃ tīraṃ bhāreśvareti ca  || 209.144 ||
jāgṛtaścātiduḥkhena kathaṃ pāpaṃ na hāsyati  |
itthaṃsa jāgaraṃ kṛtvā śivarātryāṃ nareśvaraḥ  || 209.145 ||
prabhāte vimale gatvā narmadātīramuttamam  |
snāpitāstena te sarve vāhanāni gajādayaḥ  || 209.146 ||
yaistu vāhairgatastīrthaṃ snāto'haṃ snāpayāmi tān  |
tatra madhyasthitaḥ snātastiryaktvānnirgato vaṇik || 209.147 ||
dānaṃ dadau tānuddiśya kiṃcicchaktyanurūpataḥ  |
tena vāhakṛtāddoṣānmukto bhavati mānavaḥ  || 209.148 ||
anyathāsau kṛto lābhaḥ kṛto vrajati tān prati  |
saṃsnāpya taṃ tato rājā svayaṃ snātvā vidhānataḥ  || 209.149 ||
saṃtarpya pitṛdevāṃśca kṛtvā śrāddhaṃ yathāvidhi  |
kṛtvā piṇḍānpitṛbhyaśca vṛṣamutsṛjya lakṣaṇam  || 209.150 ||
gatvā devālayaṃ paścāddevaṃ tīrthodakena ca  |
saṃsnāpya pañcagavyena tataḥ pañcāmṛtena ca  || 209.151 ||
sarvauṣadhijalenaiva tataḥ śuddhodakena ca  |
candanena sugandhena samālabhya ca śaṅkaram  || 209.152 ||
kuṅkumaiśca sakarpūrairgandhaiśca vividhaistathā  |
puṣpaughaiśca sugandhāḍhyaiścaturthaṃ liṅgapūraṇam  || 209.153 ||
kṛtaṃ nṛpavareṇātra kurvatā pūrvakaṃ vidhim  |
godānaṃ ca kṛtaṃ paścādvidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā  || 209.154 ||
dhenuke rudrarūpāsi rudreṇa parinirmitā  |
asminnagādhe saṃsāre patantaṃ māṃ samuddhara  || 209.155 ||
dhenuṃ svalaṃkṛtāṃ dadyādanena vidhinā tataḥ  |
kṣamāpya devadeveśaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayedbahūn  || 209.156 ||
ṣaḍvidhairbhojanairbhakṣyairvāsobhistān samarcayet |
dakṣiṇābhirvicitrābhiḥ pūjayitvā kṣamāpayet || 209.157 ||
sa svayaṃ bubhuje paścātparivārasamanvitaḥ  |
tāmeva rajanīṃ tatra nyavasajjagatīpatiḥ  || 209.158 ||
tasya tatroṣitasyaivaṃ niśīthe'tha nareśvara  |
ākāśe so'ti śuśrāva divyavāṇīsamīritam  || 209.159 ||

vāguvāca -
rājansamaṃ tato loke phalaṃ bhavati sāmpratam  |
saṃsārasāgare hyatra patitānāṃ durātmanām  || 209.160 ||
yadi saṃnidhimātreṇa phalaṃ tatrocyate katham  |
yadi śaṃtanuvaṃśasya tatronmādakaraṃ bhavet || 209.161 ||
ya eṣa tvadgṛhe voḍhā hyatibhāradhuraṃdharaḥ  |
anena mitrahananaṃ pāpaṃ viśvāsaghātanam  || 209.162 ||
kṛtaṃ janmasahasrāṇāmatīte parijanmani  |
gatena pāpmanātmānaṃ narakeṣu ca saṃsthitiḥ  || 209.163 ||
tato yonisahasreṣu gatistiryakṣu caiva hi  |
goyoniṃ samanuprāptastvadgṛhe sa sudurmatiḥ  || 209.164 ||
snāpitaśca tvayā tīrthe hyasmin parvasamāgame  |
dṛṣṭvā pūjāṃ tvayā kḷptāṃ kṛtā jāgaraṇakriyā  || 209.165 ||
tena niṣkalmaṣo jāto muktvā dehaṃ tavāgrataḥ  |
svargaṃ prati vimānasthaḥ so'dya rājangamiṣyati  || 209.166 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evamukte nipatito dhuryaḥ prāṇairvyayujyata  |
vimānavaramārūḍhastatkṣaṇātsamadṛśyata  || 209.167 ||
sa taṃ praṇamya rājendramuvāca prahasanniva  || 209.168 ||

vṛṣa uvāca -
bhobho nṛpavaraśreṣṭha tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam  |
yatra cāsmadvidhastīrthe mucyate pātakairnaraḥ  |
mayā jñātamaśeṣeṇa matsamo nāsti pātakī  || 209.169 ||
ataḥ paraṃ kiṃ tu kuryāṃ paraṃ tīrthānukīrtanam  |
bhavānmātā bhavanbhrātā bhavāṃścaiva pitāmahaḥ  || 209.170 ||
kṣantavyaṃ praṇato'smyadya yasmiṃstīrthe hi mādṛśāḥ  |
gatimīdṛgvidhāṃ yānti na jāne tava kā gatiḥ  || 209.171 ||
samārādhya maheśānaṃ sampūjya ca yathāvidhi  |
kā gatistava saṃbhāṣyā dehyanujñāṃ mama prabho  || 209.172 ||
tvarayanti ca māṃ hyete divisthāḥ praṇayādgaṇāḥ  |
svastyastu te gamiṣyāmītyuktvā so'ntardadhe kṣaṇāt || 209.173 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
gate cādarśanaṃ tatra sa rājā vismayānvitaḥ  |
tīrthamāhātmyamatulaṃ varṇayansvapuraṃ gataḥ  || 209.174 ||
itthaṃbhūtaṃ hi tattīrthaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam  |
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ sarvaduḥkhaghnamuttamam  || 209.175 ||
upapāpāni naśyanti snānamātreṇa bhārata  |
kārttikasya caturdaśyāmupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ  || 209.176 ||
caturdhā pūrayelliṅgaṃ tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ  || 209.177 ||
mahāpāpāni catvāri caturbhiryānti saṃkṣayam  |
so'śvamedhasya yajñasya labhate phalamuttamam  || 209.178 ||
kārttike śuklapakṣasya caturdaśyāmupoṣitaḥ  |
svarṇadānācca tattīrthe yajñasya labhate phalam  || 209.179 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ vaiśākhe māsi pūrvavat |
dīpaṃ piṣṭamayaṃ kṛtvā pitḥn sarvān vimokṣayet || 209.180 ||
tatra yaddīyate dānamapi vālāgramātrakam  |
tadakṣayaphalaṃ sarvamevamāha maheśvaraḥ  || 209.181 ||
bhārabhūtyāṃ mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
anivartikā gatī rājañchivalokānnirantaram  || 209.182 ||
athavā lokavṛttyarthaṃ martyalokaṃ jigīṣati  |
sāṅgavedajñaviprāṇāṃ jāyate vimale kule  || 209.183 ||
dhanadhānyasamāyukto vedavidyāsamanvitaḥ  |
sarvavyādhivinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃ śatam  || 209.184 ||
punastattīrthamāsādya hyakṣayaṃ padamāpnuyāt || 209.185 ||
etatpuṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ kathitaṃ te nṛpottama  |
bhāratedaṃ mahākhyānaṃ śṛṇu caiva tataḥ param  || 209.186 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe bhārabhūtitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma navādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 210

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tasyaivānantaraṃ tāta puṅkhilaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
tatra tīrthe purā puṅkhaḥ pārtha siddhimupāgataḥ  || 210.1 ||
jāmadagnyo mahātejāḥ kṣatriyāntakaraḥ prabhuḥ  |
tapaḥ kṛtvā suvipulaṃ narmadottaratīrabhāk || 210.2 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vikhyātaṃ puṅkhatīrthaṃ nareśvara  |
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ snātvā hyārādhya parameśvaram  || 210.3 ||
ihaloke balairyuktaḥ pare mokṣamavāpnuyāt |
devānpitḥn samabhyarcya pitḥṇāmanṛṇī bhavet || 210.4 ||
tatra tīrthe naro yastu prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti vai  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 210.5 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 210.6 ||
tatra tīrthe naro yastu brāhmaṇān bhojayennṛpa  |
ekasmin bhojite vipre koṭirbhavati bhojitā  || 210.7 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitpūjayedvṛṣabhadhvajam  |
vājapeyasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 210.8 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe puṅkhilatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma daśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 211

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
āścaryabhūtaṃ lokasya devadevena yatkṛtam  |
tatte sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi narmadātaṭavāsinām  || 211.1 ||
dvijān sukṛtpaṇān devaḥ kuṣṭhī bhūtvā yayāca ha  |
śrāddhakāle tu samprāpte raktagandhānulepanaḥ  || 211.2 ||
sravadbudbudagātrastu makṣikākṛmisaṃvṛtaḥ  |
duścarmā durmukho gandhī praskhalaṃśca pade pade  || 211.3 ||
brāhmaṇāvasathaṃ gatvā skhalandvāre'bravīdidam  |
bhobho gṛhapate tvadya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhojanam  || 211.4 ||
tvadgṛhe kartumicchāmi hyebhiḥ saha susaṃskṛtam  |
tatastaṃ brahmāṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā yajamānasamanvitāḥ  || 211.5 ||
sravantaṃ sarvagātreṣu dhigdhigityevamabruvan  |
nirgacchasvāśu durgandha gṛhācchīghraṃ dvijādhama  || 211.6 ||
abhojyametatsarveṣāṃ darśanāttava satkṛtam  |
evameva tathetyuktvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 211.7 ||
jagāmākāśamamalaṃ dṛśyamāno dvijottamaiḥ  |
gate cādarśanaṃ deve snātvābhyukṣya samantataḥ  || 211.8 ||
bhuñjate'sma dvijā rājanyāvatpātre pṛthakpṛthak |
yatrayatra ca paśyanti tatratatra kṛmirbahuḥ  || 211.9 ||
dṛṣṭvā vismayamāpannāḥ sarve kimiti cābruvan  |
tataḥ kaściduvācedaṃ brāhmaṇo guṇavānajaḥ  || 211.10 ||
yogīndraḥ śaṅkayā tatra bahuviprasamāgame  |
yo'tra pūrvaṃ samāyātaḥ sa yogī parameśvaraḥ  || 211.11 ||
tasyedaṃ krīḍitaṃ manye bhartsitasya vipākajam  |
phalaṃ bhavati nānyasya hyatitheḥ śāstraniścayāt || 211.12 ||
sampūjya paramātmā vai hyatithiśca viśeṣataḥ  |
śrāddhakāle tu samprāptamatithiṃ yo na pūjayet || 211.13 ||
piśācā rākṣasāstasya tadvilumpantyasaṃśayam  |
rūpānvitaṃ virūpaṃ vā malinaṃ malināmbaram  || 211.14 ||
yogīndraṃ śvapacaṃ vāpi atithiṃ na vicārayet |
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ tasya yajamānapurogamāḥ  || 211.15 ||
brāhmaṇā dvijamanveṣṭuṃ dhāvitāḥ sarvatodiśam  |
tāvatkathaṃcitkenāpi gahanaṃ vanamāśritaḥ  || 211.16 ||
dṛṣṭo dṛṣṭa iti proktaṃ tena te sarva āgatāḥ  |
tataḥ paśyanti taṃ vipraṃ sthāṇuvanniścalaṃ sthitam  || 211.17 ||
krandate na calati spandate na ca paśyati  |
jalpanti karuṇaṃ kecitstuvanti ca tathāpare  || 211.18 ||
vāgbhiḥ satatamiṣṭābhiḥ stūyamānastrilocanaḥ  |
kṣudhārditānāṃ deveśa brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
vinaṣṭamannaṃ sarveṣāṃ punaḥ saṃkartumarhasi  || 211.19 ||
śrutvā tu vacanaṃ teṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
parayā kṛpayā devaḥ prasannastānuvāca ha  || 211.20 ||
mayā prasannena mahānubhāvāstadeva vo'nnaṃ vihitaṃ sudheva  |
bhuñjantu viprāḥ saha bandhubhṛtyairarcantu nityaṃ mama maṇḍalaṃ ca  || 211.21 ||
tataścāyatanaṃ pārtha devadevasya śūlinaḥ  |
muṇḍināmeti vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham  |
kārttikyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa gayātīrthena tatsamam  || 211.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe muṇḍitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 212

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
athānyatsampravakṣyāmi devasya caritaṃ mahat |
śrutamātreṇa yenāśu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 212.1 ||
bhikṣurūpaṃ paraṃ kṛtvā devadevo maheśvaraḥ  |
ekaśālāṃ gato grāmaṃ bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ  || 212.2 ||
akṣasūtrodyatakaro bhasmaguṇṭhitavigrahaḥ  |
sphurattriśūlo viśveśo jaṭākuṇḍalabhūṣitaḥ  || 212.3 ||
kṛttivāsā mahākāyo mahāhikṛtabhūṣaṇaḥ  |
vādayanvai ḍamarukaṃ ḍiṇḍimapratimaṃ śubham  || 212.4 ||
kapālapāṇirbhagavānbālakairbahubhirvṛtaḥ  |
kvacidgāyanhasaṃścaiva nṛtyanvadan kvacitkvacit || 212.5 ||
yatra yatra gṛhe devo līlayā ḍiṇḍamaṃ nyaset |
bhārākrāntaṃ gṛhaṃ pārtha tatratatra vinaśyati  || 212.6 ||
evaṃ sampracaran devo veṣṭito bahubhirjanaiḥ  |
dṛśyādṛśyena rūpeṇa nirjagāma bahiḥ prabhuḥ  || 212.7 ||
itaścetaśca dhāvantaṃ na paśyanti yadā janāḥ  |
vismitāste sthitāḥ śambhurbhaviṣyati tato'stuvan  || 212.8 ||
teṣāṃ tu stuvatāṃ bhaktyā śaṅkaraṃ jagatāṃ patim  |
ḍiṇḍirūpo hi bhagavāṃstadāsau pratyadṛśyata  || 212.9 ||
tadāprabhṛti deveśo ḍiṇḍimeśvara ucyate  |
darśanātsparśanādrājan sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 212.10 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe ekaśālaḍiṇḍimeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvādaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 213

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
punaranyatpravakṣyāmi devasya caritaṃ mahat |
śrutamātreṇa yenaiva sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 213.1 ||
abālo bālarūpeṇa grāmaṇyairbālakaiḥ saha  |
āmalaiḥ krīḍate śambhustatte vakṣyāmi bhārata  || 213.2 ||
sarvaistairāmalāḥ kṣiptā ye te devena pāṇḍava  |
ānītāstatkṣaṇādeva tataḥ paścātkṣipeddharaḥ  || 213.3 ||
yāvadgatvā diśo digbhya āgacchanti pṛthakpṛthak |
tāvattamāmalaṃ bhūtaṃ paśyanti parameśvaram  || 213.4 ||
tṛtīye caiva yatkarma devadevasya dhīmataḥ  |
sthānānāṃ paramaṃ sthānamāmaleśvaramuttamam  || 213.5 ||
tena pūjitamātreṇa prāpyate paramaṃ padam  || 213.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe āmaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayodaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 214

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
caturthaṃ sampravakṣyāmi devasya caritaṃ mahat |
śrutamātreṇa yenaiva sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 214.1 ||
kapālī kānthiko bhūtvā yathā sa vyacaranmahīm  |
piśācairrākṣasairbhūtairḍākinīyoginīvṛtaḥ  || 214.2 ||
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsthāya pretāsanaparigrahaḥ  |
trailokyasyābhayaṃ dattvā cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ  || 214.3 ||
āṣāḍhī tu kṛtā tatra hyāṣāḍhīnāma viśrutam  |
kanthā muktā tato'nyatra devena parameṣṭhinā  || 214.4 ||
tadāprabhṛti rājendra sa kantheśvara ucyate  |
tasya darśanamātreṇa hyaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 214.5 ||
devo mārge punastatra bhramate ca yadṛcchayā  |
vikrīṇāti balākāro dṛṣṭvā cokto hareṇa tu  || 214.6 ||
yadi bhadra na cetkopaṃ karoṣi mayi sāmpratam  |
balābhirbhara me liṅgaṃ dadāmi bahu te dhanam  || 214.7 ||
evamukto'tha devena sa vaṇiglobhamohitaḥ  |
yojayāmāsa balakā liṅge cottamamadhyamān  || 214.8 ||
tāvadyāvatkṣayaṃ sarve gatāḥ kāle susaṃcitāḥ  |
sthitaṃ samunnataṃ liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvā śokamupāgamat || 214.9 ||
kṛtvā tu khaṇḍakhaṇḍāni sa devaḥ parameśvaraḥ  |
uvāca prahasanvākyaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā gatasādhvasam  || 214.10 ||
na ca me pūritaṃ liṅgaṃ yāsyāmi yadi manyase  |
dadāmi tatra vittaṃ te yadi liṅgaṃ prapūritam  || 214.11 ||

vaṇiguvāca -
adhanyaḥ kṛtapuṇyo'haṃ nigrāhyaḥ parameśvara  |
tava priyamakurvāṇaḥ śociṣye śāśvatīḥ samāḥ  || 214.12 ||
etacchrutvā vacastasya vaṇikputrasya bhārata  |
asaṃkṣayaṃ dhanaṃ dattvā sthitastatra maheśvaraḥ  || 214.13 ||
tadā prabhṛti rājendra balākairiva bhūṣitam  |
pratyayārthaṃ sthitaṃ liṅgaṃ lokānugrahakāmyayā  || 214.14 ||
devena racitaṃ pārtha krīḍayā supratiṣṭhitam  |
devamārgamiti khyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam  |
paśyan prapūjayan vāpi sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 214.15 ||
devamārge tu yo gatvā pūjayedbalākeśvaram  |
pañcāyatanamāsādya rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 214.16 ||
devamārge mṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  |
na bhavetpunarāvṛttī rudralokātkadācana  || 214.17 ||
devamārgasya māhātmyaṃ bhaktyā śrutvā narottama  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 214.18 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śrīkapālatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 215

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṅgitīrthaṃ tato gacchenmokṣadaṃ sarvadehinām  |
mṛtānāṃ tatra rājendra mokṣaprāptirna saṃśayaḥ  || 215.1 ||
tatraiva piṇḍadānena pitḥṇāmanṛṇo bhavet |
tena puṇyena pūtātmā labhedgāṇeśvarīṃ gatim  || 215.2 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe śṛṅgitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcadaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 216

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
aṣāḍhītīrthamāgacchettato bhūpālanandana  |
kāmikaṃ rūpamāsthāya sthito yatra maheśvaraḥ  || 216.1 ||
cāturyugamidaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrtheṣvanuttamam  |
tatra snātvā naro rājan rudrasyānucaro bhavet || 216.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yaḥ kaścitkurute prāṇamokṣaṇam  |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 216.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe aṣāḍhītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 217

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
eraṇḍīsaṅgamaṃ gacchetsurāsuranamaskṛtam  |
tattu tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  || 217.1 ||
upavāsaparo bhūtvā niyatendriyamānasaḥ  |
tatra snātvā vidhānena mucyate brahmahatyayā  || 217.2 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yo bhaktyā prāṇatyāgaparo bhavet |
anivartikā gatistasya rudralokādasaṃśayam  || 217.3 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe eraṇḍītīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma saptadaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 218

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharādhīśa tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam  |
jamadagniriti khyātaṃ yatra siddho janārdanaḥ  || 218.1 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
kathaṃ siddho dvijaśreṣṭha vāsudevo jagadguruḥ  |
mānuṣaṃ rūpamāsthāya lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 218.2 ||
etatsarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ devadevasya cakriṇaḥ  |
caritaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathyamānaṃ tvayānagha  || 218.3 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
āsītpūrvaṃ mahārāja haihayādhipatirmahān  |
kārtavīrya iti khyāto rājā bāhusahasravān  || 218.4 ||
hastyaśvarathasampannaḥ sarvaśastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ  |
vedavidyāvratasnātaḥ sarvabhūtābhayapradaḥ  || 218.5 ||
māhiṣmatyāḥ patiḥ śrīmānrājā hyakṣauhiṇīpatiḥ  |
sa kadācinmṛgānhantuṃ nirjagāma mahābalaḥ  || 218.6 ||
bahubhirdivasaiḥ prāpto bhṛgukacchamanuttamam  |
jamadagnirmahātejā yatra tiṣṭhati tāpasaḥ  || 218.7 ||
reṇukāsahitaḥ śrīmānsarvabhūtābhayapradaḥ  |
tasya putro'bhavadrāmaḥ sākṣānnārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  || 218.8 ||
sarvakṣatraguṇairyukto brahmavidbrāhmaṇottamaḥ  |
toṣayanparayā bhaktyā pitarau paramārthavat || 218.9 ||
taṃ tadā cārjunaṃ dṛṣṭvā jamadagniḥ pratāpavān  |
carantaṃ mṛgayāṃ gatvā hyātithyena nyamantrayat || 218.10 ||
tatheti coktvā sa nṛpaḥ sabhṛtyabalavāhanaḥ  |
jagāma cāśramaṃ puṇyamṛṣestasya mahātmanaḥ  || 218.11 ||
tatkṣaṇādeva sampannaṃ śriyā paramayā vṛtam  |
vismayaṃ paramaṃ tatra dṛṣṭvā rājā jagāma ha  || 218.12 ||
gatamātrastu siddhena paramānnena bhojitaḥ  |
sabhṛtyabalavānrājā brāhmaṇena yadṛcchayā  |
kimetaditi papraccha kāraṇaṃ śaktimeva ca  || 218.13 ||
kāmadhenoḥ prabhāvaṃ taṃ jñātvā prāha tato dvijam  |
dakṣiṇāṃ dehi me vipra kalmaṣāṃ dhenumuttamām  || 218.14 ||
śataṃ śatasahasrāṇāmayutaṃ niyutaṃ param  |
bhūṣitānāṃ ca dhenūnāṃ dadāmi tava cārbudam  || 218.15 ||

jamadagniruvāca -
ayutaiḥ prayutairnāhaṃ śatakoṭibhiruttamām  |
kāmadhenumimāṃ tāta na dadmi pratigamyatām  || 218.16 ||
evamuktaḥ sa rājendrastena vipreṇa bhārata  |
krodhasaṃraktanayana idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 218.17 ||
yasyedṛśaḥ kāmacāro mayyapi dvijapāṃsana  |
ahaṃ te paśyatastasmānnayāmi surabhiṃ gṛhāt || 218.18 ||

dvija uvāca -
kaḥ krīḍati saroṣeṇa nirbhayo hi mahāhinā  |
mṛtyudṛṣṭotareṇāpi mama dhenuṃ nayeta yaḥ  || 218.19 ||
evamuktvā mahādaṇḍaṃ brahmadaṇḍamivāparam  |
gṛhītvā paramakruddho jamadagniruvāca ha  || 218.20 ||
yasyāsti śaktistejo vā kṣatriyasya kulādhamaḥ  |
dhenuṃ nayatu me sadyaḥ kṣīṇāyuḥ saparicchadaḥ  || 218.21 ||
etacchrutvā vacaḥ krūraṃ haihayaḥ śataśo vṛtaḥ  |
dhāvamānaḥ kṣititale brahmadaṇḍahato'patat || 218.22 ||
huṃkṛtena tato dhenvāḥ khaḍgapāśāsipāṇayaḥ  |
nirgacchantaḥ pradṛśyante kalmaṣāyāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 218.23 ||
nāsāpuṭāgrādromāgrātkirātā māgadhā gudāt |
randhrāntareṣu cotpannāḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 218.24 ||
evamanyo'nyamāhatya haihayaṣṭaṅkaṇāndahan  |
vināśaṃ saha vipreṇa gatā hyarjunatejasā  || 218.25 ||
kārtavīryo jayaṃ labdhvā saṃkhye hatvā dvijottamam  |
jagāma svāṃ purīṃ hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtāntavaśamohitaḥ  || 218.26 ||
tatastvarānvitaḥ prāptaḥ paścādrāmo gate ripau  |
ākrandamānāṃ jananīṃ dadarśa piturantike  || 218.27 ||

rāma uvāca -
kenedamātmanāśāya hyajñānātsāhasaṃ kṛtam  |
mama tātaṃ jighāṃsuryo draṣṭuṃ mṛtyumihecchati  || 218.28 ||
tataḥ sā rāmavākyena gatasattveva vihvalā  |
udaraṃ karayugmena tāḍayantī hyuvāca tam  || 218.29 ||
arjunena nṛśaṃsena kṣatriyairaparaiḥ saha  |
ihāgatya pitā tena nihato bāhuśālinā  || 218.30 ||
taṃ paśya nihataṃ tātaṃ gatāsuṃ gatacetasam  |
saṃskṛtya vidhivatputra tarpayasva yathātatham  || 218.31 ||
etacchrutvā sa vacanaṃ jananīmabhivādya tām  |
pratijñāmakarodyāṃ tāṃ śṛṇuṣva ca narādhipa  || 218.32 ||
triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ pṛthivīṃ niḥkṣatriyakulānvayām  |
snātvā ca teṣāmasṛjā tarpayiṣyāmi te patim  || 218.33 ||
tasyāpi paraśunā bāhūn kārtavīryasya durmateḥ  |
chittvā pāsyāmi rudhiramiti satyaṃ śṛṇuṣva me  || 218.34 ||
evaṃ pratijñāṃ kṛtvāsau jāmadagnyaḥ pratāpavān  |
krodhena mahatāviṣṭaḥ saṃskṛtya pitaraṃ tataḥ  || 218.35 ||
māhiṣmatīṃ purīṃ rāmo jagāma krodhamūrchitaḥ  |
chittvā bāhuvanaṃ tasya hatvā taṃ kṣatriyādhamam  || 218.36 ||
jagāma kṣatriyāntāya pṛthivīmavalokayan  |
saptadvīpārṇavayutāṃ saśailavanakānanām  || 218.37 ||
pūrvataḥ paścimāmāśāṃ dakṣiṇottarataḥ kurūn  |
samantapañcake pañca cakāra rudhirahradān  || 218.38 ||
sa teṣu rudhirāmbhastu hradeṣu krodhamūrchitaḥ  |
pitḥn saṃtarpayāmāsa rudhireṇeti naḥ śrutam  || 218.39 ||
atharcīkādaya upetya pitaro brāhmaṇarṣabham  |
taṃ kṣamasveti jagadustataḥ sa virarāma ha  || 218.40 ||
teṣāṃ samīpe yo deśo hradānāṃ rudhirāmbhasām  |
samaṃ tapaṃ cakramiti puṇyaṃ tatparikīrtitam  || 218.41 ||
nivartya karmaṇastasmātpitḥn provāca pāṇḍava  |
rāmaḥ paramadharmātmā yadidaṃ rudhiraṃ mayā  || 218.42 ||
kṣiptaṃ pañcasu tīrtheṣu tadbhūyāttīrthamuttamam  |
tathetyuktvā tu te sarve pitaro'dṛśyatāṃ gatāḥ  || 218.43 ||
evaṃ rāmasya saṃsargo devamārge yudhiṣṭhira  |
sarvapāpakṣayakaro darśanātsparśanānnṛṇām  || 218.44 ||
reṇukāpratyayārthāya adyāpi pitṛdevatāḥ  |
dṛśyante devamārgasthāḥ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkarāḥ  || 218.45 ||
tatra tīrthe tu rājendra narmadodadhisaṅgame  |
sthānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena mucyante pātakairnarāḥ  || 218.46 ||
kuśāgreṇāpi kaunteya na spṛṣṭavyo mahodadhiḥ  |
anena tatra mantreṇa snātavyaṃ nṛpasattama  || 218.47 ||
namaste viṣṇurūpāya namastubhyamapāṃ pate  |
sānnidhyaṃ kuru deveśa sāgare lavaṇāmbhasi  |
iti sparśanamantraḥ  || 218.48 ||
agniśca tejo mṛḍayā ca dehe reto'tha viṣṇuramṛtasya nābhiḥ  |
etadbruvan pāṇḍava satyavākyaṃ tato'vagāheta patiṃ nadīnām  || 218.49 ||
pañcaratnasamāyuktaṃ phalapuṣpākṣatairyutam  |
mantreṇānena rājendra dadyādarghaṃ mahodadheḥ  || 218.50 ||
sarvaratnanidhānastvaṃ sarvaratnākarākaraḥ  |
sarvāmarapradhāneśa gṛhāṇārghaṃ namo'stu te  |
ityarghamantraḥ  || 218.51 ||
ā janmajanitātpāpānmāmuddhara mahodadhe  |
yāhyarcito ratnanidhe parvatān pārvaṇottama  |
iti visarjanamantraḥ  || 218.52 ||
ko'paraḥ sāgarāddevātsvargadvāravipāṭana  |
tatra sāgaraparyantaṃ mahātīrthamanuttamam  || 218.53 ||
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa tatra devaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
yatra devāḥ sagandharvā munayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ  || 218.54 ||
upāsate virūpākṣaṃ jamadagnimanuttamam  |
reṇukāṃ caiva ye devīṃ paśyanti bhuvi mānavāḥ  || 218.55 ||
priyavāse śive loke vasanti kālamīpsitam  |
tatra snātvā naro rājaṃstarpayanpitṛdevatāḥ  || 218.56 ||
tārayennarakādghorātkulānāṃ śatamuttaram  |
snātvā dattvātra sahitāḥ śrutvā vai bhaktipūrvakam  || 218.57 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe jāmadagnyatīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 219

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ koṭīśvaraṃ param  |
yatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 219.1 ||
tatra devāḥ sagandharvā ṛṣayo ye tathāmalāḥ  |
koṭitīrthe parāṃ siddhiṃ samprāptā bhuvi durlabhām  || 219.2 ||
sthāpitaśca mahādevastatra koṭīśvaro nṛpa  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā devadeveśaṃ siddhiṃ prāpnotyanuttamām  || 219.3 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yatkiṃcicchubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  |
kriyate tannṛpaśreṣṭha sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 219.4 ||
tatra dakṣiṇamārgasthā ye kecinmunisattamāḥ  |
siddhā mṛtāḥ padaṃ yānti pitṛlokaṃ dhruvaṃ hi te  || 219.5 ||
uttaraṃ narmadākūlaṃ ye śreṣṭhā munipuṃgavāḥ  |
devalokaṃ gatāḥ pūrvamiti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  || 219.6 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe koṭitīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 220

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gaccheddharādhīśa loṭaṇeśvaramuttamam  |
uttare narmadākūle sarvapātakanāśanam  || 220.1 ||
tatkṣaṇādeva tatsarvaṃ saptajanmārjitaṃ tvagham  |
naśyate devadevasya darśanādeva tannṛpa  || 220.2 ||
bālyātprabhṛti yatpāpaṃ yauvane cāpi yatkṛtam  |
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti devadevasya darśanāt || 220.3 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
āścaryabhūtaṃ lokeṣu narmadācaritaṃ mahat |
tvayā vai kathitaṃ vipra sakalaṃ pāpanāśanam  || 220.4 ||
yadekaṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthaphalapradam  |
śrotumicchāmi tatsarvaṃ dayāṃ kṛtvā vadāśu me  || 220.5 ||
ye keciddurlabhāḥ praśnāstriṣu lokeṣu sattama  |
tvatprasādena te sarve śrutā me saha bāndhavaiḥ  || 220.6 ||
etamekaṃ paraṃ praśnaṃ sarvapraśnavidāṃ vara  |
śrutvāhaṃ tvatprasādena yatra yāmi sabāndhavaḥ  || 220.7 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhusādhu mahāprājña yasya te matirīdṛśī  |
durlabhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu tasya te nāsti kiṃcana  || 220.8 ||
dharmamarthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca bharatarṣabha  |
kāle kāle ca yo vetti kartavyastena dhīmatā  || 220.9 ||
tasmātte sampravakṣyāmi praśnasyāsyottaraṃ śubham  |
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyante bhuvi mānavāḥ  || 220.10 ||
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā sarvatīrthamayī śubhā  |
viśeṣaḥ kathitastasyā revāsāgarasaṅgame  || 220.11 ||
āgacchantīṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha dṛṣṭvā revāṃ mahodadhiḥ  |
praṇamya ca punardevīṃ saṅgame revayā saha  || 220.12 ||
saṃcintya manasā keyamiti māṃ vai saridvarā  |
jñātvā saṃcintya manasā revāṃ liṅgodbhavāṃ parām  || 220.13 ||
luṭhanvai sammukhastāta gato revāṃ mahodadhiḥ  |
samudre narmadā yatra praviṣṭāsti mahānadī  || 220.14 ||
tatra devādhidevasya samudre liṅgamutthitam  |
liṅgodbhūtā mahābhāgā narmadā saritāṃ varā  || 220.15 ||
layaṃ gatā tatra liṅge tena puṇyatamā hi sā  |
narmadāyāṃ vasannityaṃ narmadāmbu pibansadā  |
dīkṣitaḥ sarvayajñeṣu somapānaṃ dine dine  || 220.16 ||
saṅgame tatra yaḥ snātvā loṭaṇeśvaramarcayet |
so'śvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  || 220.17 ||
vācikaṃ mānasaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā yatkṛtaṃ nṛpa  |
loṭaṇeśvaramāsādya sarvaṃ vilayatāṃ vrajet || 220.18 ||
kārttikyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa kathitaṃ śaṅkareṇa tu  |
tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpaśreṣṭha sarvapāpāpanodanam  || 220.19 ||
samprāptāṃ kārttikīṃ dṛṣṭvā gatvā tatra nṛpottama  |
caturdaśyāmupoṣyaiva snātvā vai narmadājale  || 220.20 ||
saṃtarpya pitṛdevāṃśca śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryātsampūjya loṭaṇeśvaram  || 220.21 ||
saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ tasya saphalaṃ tasya ceṣṭitam  |
paṅgavaste na sandeho janma teṣāṃ nirarthakam  || 220.22 ||
ekāgramanasā yaistu na dṛṣṭo loṭaṇeśvaraḥ  |
piśācatvaṃ viyonitvaṃ na bhavettasya vai kule  || 220.23 ||
saṅgame tatra yo gatvā snānaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  |
puṇyaiścaiva tathā kuryādgītairnṛtyaiḥ prabodhanam  || 220.24 ||
tataḥ prabhātāṃ rajanīṃ dṛṣṭvā natvā mahodadhim  |
āmantrya snānavidhinā snānaṃ tatra tu kārayet || 220.25 ||
oṃ namo viṣṇurūpāya tīrthanāthāya te namaḥ  |
sānnidhyaṃ kuru me deva samudra lavaṇāmbhasi  |
ityāmantraṇamantraḥ  || 220.26 ||
agniśca tejo mṛḍayā ca deho reto'dhā viṣṇuramṛtasya nābhiḥ  |
evaṃ bruvan pāṇḍava satyavākyaṃ tato'vagāheta patiṃ nadīnām  |
iti snānamantraḥ  || 220.27 ||
ājanmaśatasāhasraṃ yatpāpaṃ kṛtavānnaraḥ  |
sakṛtsnānādvyapoheta pāpaughaṃ lavaṇāmbhasi  || 220.28 ||
anyathā hi kuruśreṣṭha devayonirasau vibhuḥ  |
kuśāgreṇāpi vibudhairna spraṣṭavyo mahārṇavaḥ  || 220.29 ||
sarvaratnapradhānastvaṃ sarvaratnākarākara  |
sarvāmarapradhāneśa gṛhāṇārghaṃ namo'stu te  |
iti arvamantra  || 220.30 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṃśca saṃtarpya tadanantaram  |
uttīrya tīre tasyaiva pañcabhirdvijapuṃgavaiḥ  || 220.31 ||
śrāddhaṃ samācaretpaścāllokapālānurūpibhiḥ  |
kṛtvāgre lokapālāṃstu pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi  || 220.32 ||
sampūjya ca yathānyāyaṃ tāneva brāhmaṇaiḥ saha  |
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ paścāttebhyaḥ sarvaṃ nivedayet || 220.33 ||
bālyātprabhṛti yatpāpaṃ kṛtaṃ vārdhakayauvane  |
prakhyāpayitvā tebhyo'gre lokapālānnimantrayet || 220.34 ||
bālyātprabhṛti yatkiṃcitkṛtamā janmato'śubham  |
viprebhyaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ tatsāṃnidhyaṃ sthiteṣu me  || 220.35 ||
ityuktvā sa luṭhetpaścāttebhyo'greṇa ca sammukham  |
anumānya ca tānpañca paścātsnānaṃ samācaret || 220.36 ||
śrāddhaṃ ca kāryaṃ vidhivatpitṛbhyo nṛpasattama  |
evaṃ kṛte nṛpaśreṣṭha sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 220.37 ||
jijñāsārthaṃ tu yaḥ kaścidātmānaṃ jñātumicchati  |
śubhāśubhaṃ ca yatkarma tasya niṣṭhāmimāṃ śṛṇu  || 220.38 ||
snātvā tatra mahātīrthe luṭhamāno vrajennaraḥ  |
pāpakarmānyato yāti dharmakarmā vrajennadīm  || 220.39 ||
pāpakarmā tato jñātvā pāpaṃ me pūrvasaṃcitam  |
snātvā tīrthavare tasmindānaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi  || 220.40 ||
loṭaṇeśvaramabhyarcya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
avakragamanaṃ gatvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 220.41 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñātvaivaṃ nṛpasattama  |
snātavyaṃ mānavaistatra yatra saṃnihito haraḥ  || 220.42 ||
evaṃ snātvā vidhānena brāhmaṇān vedapāragān  |
pūjayetpṛthivīpāla sarvapāpopaśāntaye  || 220.43 ||
evaṃ guṇaviśiṣṭaṃ hi tattīrthaṃ nṛpasattama  |
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa  || 220.44 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ  |
śrāddhaṃ yaḥ kurute tatra pitḥṇāṃ bhaktibhāvitaḥ  || 220.45 ||
dānaṃ dadāti viprebhyo gobhūtilahiraṇyakam  |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi koṭirvarṣaśatāni ca  || 220.46 ||
vimānavaramārūḍhaḥ svargaloke mahīyate  |
narmadāsarvatīrthebhyaḥ snāne dāne ca yatphalam  || 220.47 ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti revāsāgarasaṅgame  |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ maṇimauktikabhūṣaṇam  || 220.48 ||
govṛṣaṃ ca mahīṃ dhānyaṃ tatra dattvākṣayaṃ phalam  |
śubhasyāpyaśubhasyāpi tatra tīrthe na saṃśayaḥ  || 220.49 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ kaścitprāṇatyāgaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
karoti bhaktyā vidhivattasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 220.50 ||
koṭivarṣaṃ tu varṣāṇāṃ krīḍitvā śivamandire  |
vedavedāṅgavidvipro jāyate vimale kule  || 220.51 ||
putrapautrasamṛddho'sau dhanadhānyasamanvitaḥ  |
sarvavyādhivinirmukto jīvecca śaradāṃśatam  || 220.52 ||
api dvādaśayātrāsu somanāthe yadarcite  |
kārttikyāṃ kṛttikāyoge tatpuṇyaṃ loṭaṇeśvare  || 220.53 ||
gayā gaṅgā kurukṣetre naimiṣe puṣkare tathā  |
tatpuṇyaṃ labhate pārtha loṭaṇeśvaradarśanāt || 220.54 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā paṭhyamānamidaṃ śubham  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 220.55 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe loṭaṇeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma viṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 221

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tato gacchettu rājendra revāyā dakṣiṇe taṭe  |
krośadvayāntare tīrthaṃ matṛtīrthādanuttamam  || 221.1 ||
nāmnā haṃseśvaraṃ puṇyaṃ vaimanasyavināśanam  |
kaśyapasya kule jāto haṃso dākṣāyaṇīsutaḥ  || 221.2 ||
brahmaṇo vāhanaṃ jātaḥ purā taptvā tapo mahat |
saikadā vidhinirdeśaṃ vinā vaiyagryamāsthitaḥ  || 221.3 ||
abhibhūtaḥ śivagaṇaiḥ praṇanāśa yudhiṣṭhira  |
dakṣayajñapramathane kāṃdiśīko vidhiṃ vinā  || 221.4 ||
brahmaṇā saṃsṛto'pyāśu nāyāti sa yadā khagaḥ  |
tadā taṃ śaptavānbrahmā pātayāmāsa vai padāt || 221.5 ||
tataḥ sa śaptamātmānaṃ matvā haṃsastvarānvitaḥ  |
pitāmahamupāgamya praṇipatyedamabravīt || 221.6 ||

haṃsa uvāca -
tiryagyonisamutpannaṃ bhavāñchaptuṃ na cārhati  |
svabhāva eva tiryakṣu vivekavikalaṃ manaḥ  || 221.7 ||
tathāpi deva pāpo'smi yadahaṃ svāminaṃ tyaje  |
kiṃ tu dhāvadbhiratyugrairgaṇaiḥ śārvaiḥ pitāmaha  |
sahasāhaṃ bhayākrāntastrastastyaktvā palāyitaḥ  || 221.8 ||
adyāpi bhayamevāhaṃ paśyannasmi vibho puraḥ  |
tena smṛto'pi bhavatā nāvrajaṃ bhavadantike  || 221.9 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
iti bruvanneva hi dhāturagre haṃsaḥ śvasatyakṣipūjyaḥ sudīnaḥ  |
tiryañcaṃ māṃ pāpinaṃ mūḍhabuddhiṃ prabho puraḥ patitaṃ pāhi pāhi  || 221.10 ||
eko devastvaṃ hi sargasya kartā nānāvidhaṃ sṛṣṭametattvayaiva  |
ahaṃ sṛṣṭastvīdṛśo yattvayā vai so'yaṃ doṣo dhātaraddhā tavaiva  || 221.11 ||
śāpasya vānugrahasyāpi śaktastvatto nānyaḥ śaraṇaṃ kaṃ vrajāmi  |
sevādharmādvicyutaṃ dāsabhūtaṃ capeṭairhantavyaṃ vai tāta māṃ trāhi bhaktam  || 221.12 ||
vidyāvidye tvatta evāvirāstāṃ dharmādharmau sadasaddyurniśe ca  |
nānābhāvāñjagatastvaṃ vidhatsestaṃ tvāmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vai prapadye  || 221.13 ||
eko'si bahurūpo'si nānācitraikakarmataḥ  |
niṣkarmākhilakarmāsi tvāmataḥ śaraṇaṃ vraje  || 221.14 ||
namonamo vareṇyāya varadāya namonamaḥ  |
namo dhātre vidhātre ca śaraṇyāya namonamaḥ  || 221.15 ||
śikṣākṣaraviyukteyaṃ vāṇī me stauti kiṃ vibho  |
kā śaktiḥ kiṃ parijñānamidamuktaṃ kṣamasva me  || 221.16 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ vadati haṃse vai brahmā prāha prasannadhīḥ  |
śikṣā dattā tavaiveyaṃ mā viṣādaṃ kṛthāḥ khaga  || 221.17 ||
tapasā śodhayātmānaṃ yathā śāpāntamāpnuyāḥ  |
revāsevāṃ kuru snātvā sthāpayitvā maheśvaram  |
acireṇaiva kālena tataḥ saṃsthānamāpsyasi  || 221.18 ||
yacceṣṭvā bahubhiryajñaiḥ samāptavaradakṣiṇaiḥ  |
gosvarṇakoṭidānaiśca tatphalaṃ sthāpite śive  || 221.19 ||
brahmaghno vā surāpo vā svarṇahṛdgurutalpagaḥ  |
revātīre śivaṃ sthāpya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 221.20 ||
tasmādbhargasarittīre sthāpayitvā triyambakam  |
viyuktaḥ sarvadoṣaistvaṃ yāsyase padamuttamam  || 221.21 ||
evamuktaḥ sa vidhinā hṛṣṭatuṣṭaḥ khagottamaḥ  |
tathetyuktvā jagāmāśu narmadātīramuttamam  || 221.22 ||
tapastaptvā kiyatkālaṃ sthāpayāmāsa śaṅkaram  || 221.23 ||
svanāmnā bharataśreṣṭha haṃseśvaramanuttamam  |
pūjayitvā paraṃ sthānaṃ prāptavānkhagasattamaḥ  || 221.24 ||
tatra haṃseśvare tīrthe gatvā snātvā yudhiṣṭhira  |
pūjayetparameśānaṃ sa pāpaiḥ parimucyate  || 221.25 ||
stuvannekamanā devaṃ na dainyaṃ prāpnuyātkvacit |
śrāddhaṃ dīpapradānaṃ ca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam  |
dattvā śaktyā nṛpaśreṣṭha svargaloke mahīyate  || 221.26 ||
trikālamekakālaṃ vā yo bhaktyā pūjayecchivam  |
navaprasūtāṃ dhenuṃ ca dattvā pārtha dvijottame  |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  || 221.27 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe haṃseśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 222

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ krośāntare gacchettilādaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
tilaprāśanakṛdyatra jābāliḥ śuddhimāptavān  || 222.1 ||
pitṛmātṛparityāgī bhrāturbhāryābhilāṣakṛt |
putravikrayakṛtpāpaśchalakṛdguruṇā saha  || 222.2 ||
evaṃ doṣasamāviṣṭo yatra yatrāpi gacchati  |
tatra tatrāpi dhikkāraṃ labhate satsu bhārata  |
na ko'pi saṃgatiṃ dhatte tena sārddhaṃ sabhāsvapi  || 222.3 ||
iti lajjānvito vipraḥ kāle na mahatā nṛpa  |
cintāmavāpa mahatīmagatijño hi pāvane  || 222.4 ||
cakāra sarvatīrthāni revāṃ cāpyavagāhayat || 222.5 ||
aṇivāpāntamāsādya dakṣiṇe narmadātaṭe  |
tasthau yatra vratī pārtha jābāliḥ prāśayaṃstilān  || 222.6 ||
tilairekāśanaṃ kurvaṃstathaivaikāntarāśanam  |
tryahaṣaḍdvādaśāhāśī pakṣamāsāśanastathā  || 222.7 ||
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādīni vratāni ca tilairapi  |
tilādatvamanuprāpto hyabdadvāsaptatiṃ kramāt || 222.8 ||
kālena gacchatā tasya prasanno'bhavadīśvaraḥ  |
prādādihāmutrikīṃ tu śuddhiṃ sālokyamātmakam  || 222.9 ||
tena sa sthāpito devaḥ svanāmnā bharatarṣabha  |
tilādeśvarasaṃjñāṃ ca prāpa lokādapi prabhuḥ  || 222.10 ||
tadā prabhṛti vikhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam  |
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca  || 222.11 ||
upavāsaparaḥ pārtha tathaiva harivāsare  |
tilahomī tilodvartī tilasnāyī tilodakī  || 222.12 ||
tiladātā ca bhoktā ca nānāpāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
tilairāpūrayelliṅgaṃ tilatailena dīpadaḥ  |
rudralokamavāpnoti punātyā saptamaṃ kulam  || 222.13 ||
tilapiṇḍapradānena śrāddhe nṛpatisattama  |
vikarmasthāśca gacchanti gatimiṣṭāṃ hi pūrvajāḥ  || 222.14 ||
svargalokasthitāḥ śrāddhairbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ  |
akṣayāṃ tṛptimāsādya modante śāśvatīḥ samāḥ  || 222.15 ||
pituḥ kulaṃ mātṛkulaṃ tathā bhāryākulaṃ nṛpa  |
kulatrayaṃ samuddhṛtya svargaṃ nayati vai naraḥ  || 222.16 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tilādeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 223

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ krośāntare pārtha vāsavaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
vasubhiḥ sthāpitaṃ tatra sthitvā vai dvādaśābdakam  || 223.1 ||
dharo dhruvaśca somaśca āpaścaivānilo'nalaḥ  |
pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca vasavo'ṣṭāvime purā  || 223.2 ||
pitṛśāpaparikliṣṭā garbhavāsāya bhārata  |
nārmadaṃ tīrthamāsādya tapaścakruryatendriyāḥ  || 223.3 ||
ārādhayantaḥ paramaṃ bhavānīpatimavyam  |
dvādaśābdāni rājendra tatastuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ  || 223.4 ||
pratyakṣaḥ pradadau tebhyastvabhīṣṭaṃ varamuttamam  |
tataḥ svanāmnā saṃsthāpya vasavastaṃ maheśvaram  |
jagmurākāśamāviśya prasanne sati śaṅkare  || 223.5 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vikhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ tadvāsavāhvayam  |
tasmiṃstīrthe mahārāja yo bhaktyā pūjayecchivam  |
yathālabdhopahāraiśca dīpaṃ dadyātprayatnataḥ  || 223.6 ||
śuklapakṣe tadāṣṭamyāṃ pratyahaṃ vāpi śaktitaḥ  |
aṣṭau varṣasahasrāṇi sa vasecchivasaṃnidhau  || 223.7 ||
tataḥ śivālayaṃ yāti garbhavāsaṃ na paśyati  |
puṣpairvā pallavairvāpi phalairdhānyaistathāpi vā  || 223.8 ||
pūjayeddevamīśānaṃ sa dainyaṃ nāpnuyātkvacit |
sarvaśokavinirmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate  || 223.9 ||
ekāhamapi kaunteya yo vasedvāsaveśvare  |
pāparāśiṃ vinirdhūya bhānuvaddivi modate  || 223.10 ||
viprāṃśca bhojayedbhaktyā dadyādvāsāṃsi dakṣiṇām  || 223.11 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vāsaveśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 224

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ krośāntare pārtha tīrthaṃ koṭīśvaraṃ param  |
yatra snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca japahomārcanādikam  |
bhaktyā kṛtaṃ naraistatra sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 224.1 ||
tatra devāḥ sagandharvā ṛṣayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ  |
jaladhiṃ pratigacchanti narmadāṃ vīkṣituṃ kila  || 224.2 ||
militāḥ koṭiśo rājanrevāsāgarasaṅgame  |
vinodamatulaṃ dṛṣṭvā revārṇavasamāgame  || 224.3 ||
snātvā śivaṃ ca saṃsthāpya pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
koṭīśvarābhidhānaṃ tu svasvabhaktyā vidhānataḥ  || 224.4 ||
koṭītīrthe parāṃ siddhiṃ samprāptāḥ sarvatoṣaṇāt |
tena tatpuṇyamatulaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu cottamam  || 224.5 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yatkiṃcicchubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham  |
kriyate nṛpaśārdūla sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 224.6 ||
tatra tīrthe tu mārgasthā ye kecidṛṣisattamāḥ  |
siddhāmṛtapadaṃ yānti pitṛlokaṃ tathottamam  || 224.7 ||
uttare narmadātīre dakṣiṇe cāśritāśca ye  |
devalokaṃ gatāstatra iti me niścitā matiḥ  || 224.8 ||
bilvārkapuṣpairdhattūrakuśakāśaprasūnakaiḥ  |
ṛtūdbhavaistathānyaiśca pūjayitvā maheśvaram  || 224.9 ||
nānopacārairvidhivanmantrapūrvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
dhūpadīpārdhanaivedyaistoṣayitvā ca dhūrjaṭim  || 224.10 ||
śivalokamavāpnoti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  |
pauṣakṛṣṇāṣṭamīyoge viśeṣaḥ pūjane smṛtaḥ  || 224.11 ||
nityaṃ ca nṛpatiśreṣṭha caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca  |
śivamabhyarcya viprāṃśca bhojayedbhaktito varān  || 224.12 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe koṭīśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 225

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ krośāntare gacchedalikātīrthamuttamam  |
alikā nāma gāndharvī kuśīlā kuṭilāśayā  || 225.1 ||
citrasenasya dauhitrī vidyānandamṛṣiṃ gatā  |
vavre te svīkṛtā tena daśavarṣāṇi taṃ śritā  || 225.2 ||
patiṃ jaghāna taṃ suptaṃ kasmiṃścitkāraṇāntare  |
gatvā nivedayāmāsa pitaraṃ ratnavallabham  || 225.3 ||
pitrā mātrā ca saṃtyaktā bahubhirbhartsitā nṛpa  |
garbhaghnī tvaṃ patighnī tvamiti darśaya mā mukham  || 225.4 ||
brahmaghnī yāhi pāpiṣṭhe parityaktā gṛhādvraja  || 225.5 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
iti duḥkhānvitā mūḍhā tābhyāṃ nirbhartsitā satī  |
tanuṃ tyaktuṃ manaścakre prāpya tīrthāntaraṃ kvacit || 225.6 ||
saṃpṛcchyamānā tīrthāni brāhmaṇebhyo yudhiṣṭhira  |
śrutvā pāpaharaṃ tīrthaṃ revāsāgarasaṅgame  || 225.7 ||
tatra pārtha tapaścakre nirāhārā jitavratā  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrapārākamahāsāṃtapanādibhiḥ  || 225.8 ||
cāndrāyaṇairbrahmakūrcaiḥ karśayāmāsa vai tanum  |
evaṃ varṣaśataṃ sārddhaṃ vyatītaṃ tapasā nṛpa  || 225.9 ||
tasyā viśuddhimicchantyāḥ śivadhyānārcanādibhiḥ  |
tataḥ katipayāhobhistasyā jñātvā haṭhaṃ param  |
parituṣṭaḥ śivaḥ prāha pārvatyā paricoditaḥ  || 225.10 ||

īśvara uvāca -
putri mā sāhasaṃ kārṣīḥ śuddhadehāsi sāmpratam  |
tuṣṭo'haṃ tapasā te'dya varaṃ varaya vāñchitam  || 225.11 ||

alikovāca -
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa varārhā yadyahaṃ matā  |
nānāpāpāgnitaptāyā dehi śuddhiṃ parāṃ mama  || 225.12 ||
tvaṃ me nātho hyanāthāyāstvameva jagatāṃ guruḥ  |
dīnānāthasamuddhartā śaraṇyaḥ sarvadehinām  || 225.13 ||

īśvara uvāca -
tvaṃ bhadre śuddhadehāsi mā kiṃcidanuśocithāḥ  |
svanāmnā sthāpayitveha māṃ tataḥ svargameṣyasi  || 225.14 ||
ityuktvā devadeveśastatraivāntaradhīyata  |
alikāpi tato bhaktyā snātvā saṃsthāpya śaṅkaram  || 225.15 ||
dattvā dānaṃ ca viprebhyo lokamāpa mahotkaṭam  |
pitaraṃ ca samāsādya mātaraṃ ca yudhiṣṭhira  || 225.16 ||
taiśca saṃmānitā prītyā bandhubhiḥ sālikā tataḥ  |
vimānavaramārūḍhā divyamālānvitā nṛpa  || 225.17 ||
gaurīlokamanuprāptasakhitve'dyāpi modate  |
tataḥ prabhṛti tatpārtha vikhyātamalikeśvaram  || 225.18 ||
tatra tīrthe tu yā nārī puruṣo vā yudhiṣṭhira  |
snātvā sampūjayedbhaktyā mahādevamumāyutam  || 225.19 ||
sa pāpairvividhairmukto lokamāpnoti śāṃkaram  |
mānasaṃ vācikaṃ pāpaṃ kāyikaṃ yatpurā kṛtam  || 225.20 ||
sarvaṃ tadvilayaṃ yāti bhojayitvā dvijānsadā  |
dīpaṃ dattvā ca devāgre na rogaiḥ paribhūyate  || 225.21 ||
dhūpapātraṃ vimānaṃ ca ghaṇṭāṃ kalaśameva ca  |
dattvā devāya rājendra śākraṃ lokamavāpnuyāt || 225.22 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe revāsāgarasaṅgame'likeśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 226

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
tataḥ krośāntare puṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ tadvimaleśvaram  |
yatra snānena dānena japahomārcanādibhiḥ  || 226.1 ||
vimaleśvaramārādhya yo yadicchetsa tallabhet |
svargalābhādikaṃ vāpi pārthivaṃ vā yathepsitam  || 226.2 ||
purā triśirasaṃ hatvā tvaṣṭuḥ putraṃ śatakratuḥ  |
yasya tīrthasya māhātmyādvaimalyaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ  || 226.3 ||
yatra vedanidhirvipro mahattaptvā tapaḥ purā  |
nānākarmamalaiḥ kṣīṇairvimalo'bhavadarkavat || 226.4 ||
mahādevaprasādena somavatpriyadarśanaḥ  |
purā bhānumatīṃ bhānuḥ sutāṃ smaraśarārditaḥ  || 226.5 ||
cakame tena doṣeṇa kuṣṭharogārdito'bhavat |
sa cāpyatra tapastaptvā vimalatvamupāgataḥ  || 226.6 ||
mahādevena tuṣṭena svasthānaṃ mudito'bhajat |
tathaiva ca purā pārtha vibhāṇḍakasuto muniḥ  || 226.7 ||
yogisaṅgaṃ vane prāpya pure ca nṛpatestathā  |
rājasaṃsargadoṣādvai mālinyaṃ paramātmanaḥ  || 226.8 ||
vicārayannabhyupetya revāsāgarasaṅgamam  |
śāntayā bhāryayā sārddhaṃ taptvā dvādaśavatsarān  || 226.9 ||
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇairdevaṃ toṣayaṃstryambakaṃ muniḥ  |
mahādevena tuṣṭena so'pi vaimalyamāptavān  || 226.10 ||
śarvāṇyā preritaḥ śarvaḥ purā dāruvane nṛpa  |
mohanānmunipatnīnāṃ svaṃ dīkṣya vimalaṃ kila  || 226.11 ||
vicārya paramasthānaṃ narmadodadhisaṅgamam  |
tatra sthitvā mahārāja tapastaptvā sahomayā  || 226.12 ||
vimalo'sau yato jātastenāsau vimaleśvaraḥ  |
tena nāmnā svayaṃ tasthau lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 226.13 ||
tatastilottamāṃ sṛṣṭvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  |
prajānātho'pi tāṃ sṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvāgre sumanoharām  || 226.14 ||
bhāviyogabalākrāntaḥ sa tasyāmabhiko'bhavat |
tena vīkṣya sadoṣatvaṃ revātīradvayaṃ śritaḥ  || 226.15 ||
tīrthānyanusaranmaunī trisnāyī saṃsmarañchivam  |
revārṇavasamāyoge snātvā sampūjya śaṅkaram  |
kālenālpena rājarṣe brahmāpyamalatāṃ gataḥ  || 226.16 ||
evamanye'pi bahuśo devarṣinṛpasattamāḥ  |
tyaktvā doṣamalaṃ tatra vimalā bahavo'bhavan  || 226.17 ||
tathā tvamapi rājendra tatra snātvā śivārcanāt |
amalo'pi viśeṣeṇa vaimalyaṃ prāpsyase param  || 226.18 ||
tatra snātvā naro nārī pūjayitvā maheśvaram  |
pāpadoṣavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate  || 226.19 ||
tatropavāsaṃ yaḥ kṛtvā paśyeta vimaleśvaram  |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ sarvaparvasu pārthiva  || 226.20 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ hitvā yāti śivālayam  |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena pitḥṇāmanṛṇī bhavet |
brāhmaṇān bhojayecchaktyā tebhyo dadyācca dakṣiṇām  || 226.21 ||
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccaivātmahitaṃ gṛhe  |
tattadguṇavate deyaṃ tatraivākṣayamicchatā  |
svarṇadhānyāni vāsāṃsi chatropānatkamaṇḍalum  || 226.22 ||
gṛhaṃ devasya vai śaktyā kṛtvā syādbhuvi bhūpatiḥ  |
gītanṛtyakathābhiśca toṣayetparameśvaram  || 226.23 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe vimaleśvaratīrthamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 227

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
etāni tava saṃkṣepātprādhānyātkathitāni ca  |
na śakto vistarādvaktuṃ saṃkhyāṃ tīrtheṣu pāṇḍava  || 227.1 ||
eṣā pavitrā vimalā nadī trailokyaviśrutā  |
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā mahādevasya vallabhā  || 227.2 ||
manasā saṃsmaredyastu narmadāṃ satataṃ nṛpa  |
cāndrāyaṇaśatasyāśu labhate phalamuttamam  || 227.3 ||
aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣā nāstikāścātra ye sthitāḥ  |
patanti narake ghore prāhaivaṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 227.4 ||
narmadāṃ sevate nityaṃ svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ  |
tena puṇyā nadī jñeyā brahmahatyāpahāriṇī  || 227.5 ||
iyaṃ māheśvarī gaṅgā maheśvaratanūdbhavā  |
proktā dakṣiṇagaṅgeti bhāratasya yudhiṣṭhira  || 227.6 ||
jāhnavī vaiṣṇavī gaṅgā brāhmī gaṅgā sarasvatī  |
iyaṃ māheśvarī gaṅgā revā nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ  || 227.7 ||
yathā hi puruṣe devastraimūrtatvamupāśritaḥ  |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśākhyaṃ na bhedastatra vai yathā  |
tathā sarittraye pārtha bhedaṃ manasi mā kṛthāḥ  || 227.8 ||
koṭiśo hyatra tīrthāni lakṣaśaścāpi bhārata  |
tathā sahasraśo revātīradvayagatāni tu  || 227.9 ||
vṛkṣāntarikṣasaṃsthāni jalasthalagatāni ca  |
kaḥ śaktastāni nirṇetuṃ vāgīśo vā maheśvaraḥ  || 227.10 ||
smaraṇājjanmajanitaṃ darśanācca trijanmajam  |
saptajanmakṛtaṃ naśyetpāpaṃ revāvagāhanāt || 227.11 ||
devakāryaṃ kṛtaṃ tena agnayo vidhivaddhutāḥ  |
vedā adhītāścatvāro yena revāvagāhitā  || 227.12 ||
prādhānyāccāpi saṃkṣepāttīrthānyuktāni te mayā  |
na śakyo vistaraḥ pārtha śrotuṃ vaktuṃ ca vai mayā  || 227.13 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
vidhānaṃ ca yamāṃścaiva niyamāṃśca vadasva me  |
prāyaścittārthagamane ko vidhistaṃ vadasva me  || 227.14 ||

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahārāja yacchreyaḥ pāralaukikam  |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā yathājñānaṃ vadāmi te  || 227.15 ||
adhruveṇa śarīreṇa dhruvaṃ karma samācaret |
avaśyameva yāsyanti prāṇāḥ prāghūrṇikā iva  || 227.16 ||
dānaṃ vittādṛtaṃ vācaḥ kīrtidharmau tathā khyuṣaḥ  |
paropakaraṇaṃ kāyādasārātsāramuddharet || 227.17 ||
asminmahāmohamaye kaṭāhe sūryāgninā rātridivendhanena  |
māsartudarvīparighaṭṭanena bhūtāni kālaḥ pacatīti vārtā  || 227.18 ||
jñātvā śāstravidhānoktaṃ karma kartumihārhasi  |
nāyaṃ loko'sti na paro na sukhaṃ saṃśayātmanaḥ  || 227.19 ||
mantre tīrthe dvije deve daivajñe bheṣaje gurau  |
yādṛśī bhāvanā yasya siddhirbhavati tādṛśī  || 227.20 ||
aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattaṃ tapastaptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat |
asadityucyate pārtha na ca tatpretya no iha  || 227.21 ||
yaḥ śāstravidhimutsṛjya vartate kāmakārataḥ  |
na sa siddhimavāpnoti na sukhaṃ na parāṃ gatim  || 227.22 ||
santīha vividhopāyā nṛṇāṃ dehaviśodhanāḥ  |
tīrthasevāsamaṃ nāsti svaśarīrasya śodhanam  || 227.23 ||
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādyairvā dvitīyaṃ tīrthasevayā  |
yadā tīrthaṃ samuddiśya prayāti puruṣo nṛpa  |
tadā devāśca pitarastaṃ vrajantyanu khecarāḥ  || 227.24 ||
paramā modapūrṇāste prayāntyasyānuyāyinaḥ  |
kṛtvābhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ samāpṛcchaya tu devatām  || 227.25 ||
iṣṭabandhūṃśca viṣṇuṃ ca śaṅkaraṃ sagaṇeśvaram  |
vrajeddvijābhyanujñāto gṛhītvā niyamānapi  || 227.26 ||
ekāśanaṃ brahmacaryaṃ bhūśayyāṃ satyavāditām  |
varjanaṃ ca parānnasya pratigrahavivarjanam  || 227.27 ||
varjayitvā tathā drohavañcanādi nṛpottama  |
sādhuveṣaṃ samāsthāya vinayena vibhūṣitaḥ  || 227.28 ||
dambhāhaṅkāramukto yaḥ sa tīrthaphalamaśnute  |
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaścaiva susaṃyatam  || 227.29 ||
vidyā tapaśca kīrtiśca sa tīrthaphalamaśnute  |
akrodhanaśca rājendra satyaśīlo dṛḍhavrataḥ  || 227.30 ||
ātmopamaśca bhūteṣu sa tīrthaphalamaśnute  |
muṇḍanaṃ copavāsaśca sarvatīrtheṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ  || 227.31 ||
varjayitvā kurukṣetraṃ viśālāṃ virajāṃ gayām  |
snānaṃ surārcanaṃ caiva śrāddhe vai piṇḍapātanam  || 227.32 ||
viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ śaktyā sarvatīrtheṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ  |
prāyaścittanimittaṃ ca yo vrajedyatamānasaḥ  || 227.33 ||
tasyāpi ca vidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu pārtha samāhitaḥ  |
ekāśanaṃ brahmacaryamakṣāralavaṇāśanam  || 227.34 ||
snātvā tīrthābhigamanaṃ haviṣyaikānnabhojanam  |
varjayetpatitālāpaṃ bahubhāṣaṇameva ca  || 227.35 ||
parīvādaṃ parānnaṃ ca nīcasaṅgaṃ vivarjayet |
vrajecca nirupānatko vasāno vāsasī śuciḥ  || 227.36 ||
saṃkalpaṃ manasā kṛtvā brāhmaṇānujñayā vrajet |
tīrthe gatvā tathā snātvā kṛtvā caiva surārcanam  || 227.37 ||
duṣkarmato vimuktaḥ syādanutāpī bhavedyadi  |
vede tīrthe ca deve ca daivajñe cauṣadhe gurau  || 227.38 ||
yādṛśī bhāvanā yasya siddhirbhavati tādṛśī  |
uktatīrthaphalānāṃ ca purāṇeṣu smṛtiṣvapi  || 227.39 ||
arthavādabhavāṃ śaṅkāṃ vihāya bharatarṣabha  |
kṛtvā vicāraṃ śāstroktaṃ parikalpya yathocitam  || 227.40 ||
kāyena kṛcchracaraṇe hyaśaktānāṃ viśuddhaye  |
jñātvā tīrthāviśeṣaṃ hi prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 227.41 ||
tacchṛṇuṣva mahārāja narmadāyāṃ yathocitam  |
caturviṃśatisaṃkhyebhyo yojanebhyo vrajennaraḥ  || 227.42 ||
caturviṃśatikṛcchrāṇāṃ phalamāpnoti śobhanam  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ yojaneṣu pādakṛcchramudāhṛtaḥ  || 227.43 ||
tanmadhye ca mahārāja yo vrajecchuddhikāṅkṣayā  |
yojane yojane tasya prāyaścittaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 227.44 ||
praṇavākhye mahārāja tathā revorisaṃgame  |
bhṛgukṣetre tathā gatvā phalaṃ taddviguṇaṃ smṛtam  || 227.45 ||
saṅgame devanadyāśca śūlabhede nṛpottama  |
dviguṇaṃ pādahīnaṃ syātkarajāsaṃgame tathā  || 227.46 ||
eraṇḍīsaṅgame tadvatkapilāyāśca saṃgame  |
kecittriguṇitaṃ prāhuḥ kubjārevotthasaṅgame  || 227.47 ||
oṃkāre ca mahārāja tadapi syātsamañjasam  |
saṅgameṣu tathānyāsāṃ nadīnāṃ revayā saha  || 227.48 ||
prāhuste sārdhakṛcchraṃ vai phalaṃ pūrvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira  |
triguṇaṃ kṛcchramāpnoti revāsāgarasaṅgame  || 227.49 ||
kṛcchraṃ caturguṇaṃ proktaṃ śuklatīrthe yudhiṣṭhira  |
yojane yojane gatvā caturviṃśatiyojanam  |
tatra tatra vasedyastu suciraṃ nṛvarottama  || 227.50 ||
revāsevāsamācāraḥ saṃyuktaḥ śuddhabuddhimān  |
dambhāhaṅkārarahitaḥ śuddhyarthaṃ sa vimucyate  || 227.51 ||
iti te kathitaṃ pārtha prāyaścittārthalakṣaṇam  |
revāyātrāvidhānaṃ ca guhyametadyudhiṣṭhira  || 227.52 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
yojanasya pramāṇaṃ me vada tvaṃ munisattama  |
yajjñātvā niścitaṃ me syānmanaḥśuddhestu kāraṇam  || 227.53 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
śṛṇu pāṇḍava vakṣyāmi pramāṇaṃ yojanasya yat |
tathā yātrāviśeṣeṇa viśeṣaṃ kṛcchrasambhavam  || 227.54 ||
tiryagyavodarāṇyaṣṭāvūrdhvā vā vrīhayastrayaḥ  |
pramāṇamaṅgulasyāhurvitastirdvādaśāṃgulā  || 227.55 ||
vitastidvitayaṃ hastaścaturhastaṃ dhanuḥ smṛtam  |
sa eva daṇḍo gadito viśeṣajñairyudhiṣṭhira  || 227.56 ||
dhanuḥsahasre dve krośaścatuḥkrośaṃ ca yojanam  |
etadyojanamānaṃ te kathitaṃ bharatarṣabha  || 227.57 ||
yena yātrāṃ vrajan vetti phalamānaṃ nijārjitam  |
uktaṃ kṛcchraphalaṃ tīrthe jalarūpe nṛpottama  || 227.58 ||
yathāviśeṣaṃ te vacmi pūrvokte tatra tatra ca  |
tanme śṛṇu mahīpāla śraddadhānāya kathyate  || 227.59 ||
yasmiṃstīrthe hi yatproktaṃ phalaṃ kṛcchrādikaṃ nṛpa  |
tatrāpyupoṣaṇātkṛcchraphalaṃ prāpnotyathādhikam  || 227.60 ||
dinajāpyācca labhate phalaṃ kṛcchrasya śaktitaḥ  |
tatra vikhyātadeveśaṃ snātvā dṛṣṭvābhipūjya ca  || 227.61 ||
praṇamya labhate pārtha phalaṃ kṛcchrabhavaṃ sudhīḥ  |
tīrthe mukhyaphalaṃ snānāddvitīyaṃ cāpyupoṣaṇāt || 227.62 ||
tṛtīyaṃ khyātadevasya darśanābhyarcanādibhiḥ  |
caturthaṃ jāpyayogena dehaśaktyā tvaharniśam  || 227.63 ||
pañcamaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu kalpanīyaṃ hi dūrataḥ  |
tīrastho yojanādarvāgdaśāṃśaṃ labhate phalam  || 227.64 ||
uktatīrthaphalātpārtha nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 227.65 ||
upavāsena sahitaṃ mahānadyāṃ hi majjanam  |
apyarvāgyojanātpārtha dadyātkṛcchraphalaṃ nṛṇām  || 227.66 ||
ṣaḍyojanavahā kulya nadyo'lpā dvādaśaiva ca  |
caturviṃśatigā nadyo mahānadyastato'dhikāḥ  || 227.67 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthayātrādividhānaviśeṣakathanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 228

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca -
parārthaṃ tīrthayātrāyāṃ gacchataḥ kasya kiṃ phalam  |
kiyanmātraṃ muniśreṣṭha tanme brūhi kṛpānidhe  || 228.1 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
parārthaṃ gacchatastanme vadataḥ śṛṇu pārthiva  |
yathā yāvatphalaṃ tasya yātrādivihitaṃ bhavet || 228.2 ||
uttameneha varṇena dravyalobhādinā nṛpa  |
nādhamasya kvacitkāryaṃ tīrthayātrādisevanam  || 228.3 ||
dharmakarma mahārāja svayaṃ vidvānsamācaret |
śarīrasyāthavā śaktyā anyadvā kāryayogataḥ  || 228.4 ||
dharmakarma sadā prāyaḥ savarṇenaiva kārayet |
putrapautrādikairvāpi jñātibhirgotrasambhavaiḥ  || 228.5 ||
śreṣṭhaṃ hi vihitaṃ prāhurdharmakarma yudhiṣṭhira  |
taireva kārayettasmānnottamairnādhamairapi  || 228.6 ||
adhamena kṛtaṃ samyaṅna bhavediti me matiḥ  |
uttamaścādhamārthe vai kurvandurgatimāpnuyāt || 228.7 ||
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyānnocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam  |
na cāsyopadiśeddharmaṃ na cāsya vratamādiśet || 228.8 ||
japastapastīrthayātrā pravrajyā mantrasādhanam  |
devatārādhanaṃ dīkṣā strīśūdrapatanāni ṣaṭ || 228.9 ||
pativatnī patatyeva vidhavā sarvamācaret |
sabhartṛkāśake patyau sarvaṃ kuryādanujñayā  || 228.10 ||
gatvā parārthaṃ tīrthādau ṣoḍaśāṃśaphalaṃ labhet |
gacchataśca prasaṅgena tīrthamarddhaphalaṃ smṛtam  || 228.11 ||
anusaṅgena tīrthasya snāne snānaphalaṃ viduḥ  |
naiva yātrāphalaṃ tajjñāḥ śāstroktaṃ kalmaṣāpaham  || 228.12 ||
pitrarthaṃ ca pitṛvyasya māturmātāmahasya ca  |
mātulasya tathā bhrātuḥ śvaśurasya sutasya ca  || 228.13 ||
poṣakārthādayoścāpi mātāmahyā gurostathā  |
svasurmātṛṣvasuḥ paitryā ācāryādhyāpakasya ca  || 228.14 ||
ityādyarthe naraḥ snātvā svayamaṣṭāṃśamāpnuyāt |
sākṣātpitroḥ prakurvāṇaścaturthāṃśamavāpnuyāt || 228.15 ||
patipatnyormithaścārddhaṃ phalaṃ prāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  |
bhāgineyasya śiṣyasya bhrātṛvyasya sutasya ca  |
ṣaṭtripañcacaturbhāgānphalamāpnoti vai naraḥ  || 228.16 ||
iti te kathitaṃ pārtha pāramparyakramāgatam  |
kartavyaṃ jñātivargasya parārthe dharmasādhanam  || 228.17 ||
varṣāṛtusamāyoge sarvā nadyo rajasvalāḥ  |
muktvā sarasvatīṃ gaṅgāṃ narmadāṃ yamunānadīm  || 228.18 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe parārthatīrthayātrāphalakathanaṃ nāmāṣṭaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 229

śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca -
evaṃ te kathitaṃ rājanpurāṇaṃ dharmasaṃhitam  |
śivaprītyā yathā proktaṃ vāyunā devasaṃsadi  || 229.1 ||
ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭistathaiva ca  |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu narmadāyāṃ pade pade  || 229.2 ||
mayā dvādaśasāhasrī saṃhitā yā śrutā purā  |
devadevasya gadataḥ sāmprataṃ kathitā tava  || 229.3 ||
pṛṣṭastvayāhaṃ bhūpāla parvate'marakaṇṭake  |
sthitaḥ saṃkṣepataḥ sarvaṃ mayā tatkathitaṃ tava  || 229.4 ||
narmadācaritaṃ puṇyaṃ śṛṇu tasyāsti yatphalam  |
yatphalaṃ sarvavedaiḥ syātsaṣaḍaṅgapadakramaiḥ  || 229.5 ||
paṭhitaiśca śrutairvāpi tasmādbahutaraṃ bhavet |
satrayājī phalaṃ yatra labhate dvādaśābdikam  || 229.6 ||
carite tu śrute devyā labhate tādṛśaṃ phalam  |
sarvatīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ snātvā sāgaramāditaḥ  || 229.7 ||
sakṛtsnātvā tathā śrutvā narmadāyāṃ phalaṃ hi tat |
ādimadhyāvasānena narmadācaritaṃ śubham  || 229.8 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  |
sa prāpya śivasaṃsthānaṃ rudrakanyāsamāvṛtaḥ  || 229.9 ||
rudrasyānucaro bhūtvā tenaiva saha modate  |
etaddharmamupākhyānaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu sattamam  || 229.10 ||
deśe vā maṇḍale vāpi vā grāme nagare'pi vā  |
gṛhe vā tiṣṭhate yasya cāturvarṇyasya bhārata  || 229.11 ||
sa brahmā sa śivaḥ sākṣātsa ca devo janārdanaḥ  |
trividhaṃ kāraṇaṃ loke dharmapanthānamuttamam  || 229.12 ||
devatānāṃ guruṃ śāstraṃ paramaṃ siddhikāraṇam  |
śrutveśvaramukhātpārtha mayāpi tava kīrtitam  || 229.13 ||
dakṣiṇe cottare kūle yāni tīrthāni kānicit |
pradhānataḥ supuṇyāni kathitāni viśeṣataḥ  || 229.14 ||
sparśanāddarśanātteṣāṃ kīrtanācchravaṇāttathā  |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo rudralokaṃ sa gacchati  || 229.15 ||
idaṃ yaḥ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ purāṇaṃ śivabhāṣitam  |
brāhmaṇo vedavidyāvān kṣatriyo vijayī bhavet || 229.16 ||
dhanabhāgī bhavedvaiśyaḥ śūdro vai dharmabhāgbhavet |
saubhāgyaṃ santatiṃ svargaṃ nārī śrutvāpnuyāddhanam  || 229.17 ||
brahmaghnaśca surāpaśca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ  |
māhātmyaṃ narmadāyāstu śrutvā pāpabahiṣkṛtāḥ  || 229.18 ||
pāpabhedī kṛtaghnaśca svāmiviśvāsaghātakaḥ  |
goghnaśca garadaścaiva kanyāvikrayakārakaḥ  || 229.19 ||
ete śrutvaiva pāpebhyo mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
ye punarbhāvitātmānaḥ śṛṇvanti satataṃ nṛpa  || 229.20 ||
pūjayanta idaṃ devāḥ pūjitā guravaśca taiḥ  |
narmadā pūjitā tena bhagavāṃśca maheśvaraḥ  || 229.21 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena gandhapuṣpavibhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
pūjitaṃ parayā bhaktyā śāstrametatphalapradam  || 229.22 ||
lekhāpayitvā sakalaṃ narmadācaritaṃ śubham  |
uttamaṃ sarvaśāstrebhyo yo dadāti dvijanmane  || 229.23 ||
narmadāsarvatīrtheṣu snāne dāne ca yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti sa naro nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 229.24 ||
etatpurāṇaṃ rudroktaṃ mahāpuṇyaphalapradam  |
svargadaṃ putradaṃ dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ kīrtivardhanam  || 229.25 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ pārtha duḥkhaduḥsvapnanāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ rājan sarvakāmārthasiddhidam  || 229.26 ||
śāntirastu śivaṃ cāstu lokāḥ santu nirāmayāḥ  |
gobrāhmaṇebhyaḥ svastyastu dharmaṃ dharmātmajāśrayaḥ  || 229.27 ||
narakāntakarī revā satīrthā viśvapāvanī  |
narmadā dharmadā cāstu śarmadā pārtha te sadā  || 229.28 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyayudhiṣṭhirasaṃvāde revākhaṇḍapaṭhanaśravaṇadānādiphalaśrutivarṇanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 230

sūta uvāca -
ityuktvopararāmatha pāṇḍoḥ putrāya vai muniḥ  |
mṛkaṇḍatanayo dhīmānsaptakalpasmaraḥ puraḥ  || 230.1 ||
mārkaṇḍamuninā proktaṃ yathā pārthāya sattamāḥ  |
tathā vaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ revāmāhātmyamuttamam  || 230.2 ||
iyaṃ puṇyā saricchreṣṭhā revā viśvaikapāvanī  |
rudradehasamudbhūtā sarvabhūtābhayapradā  || 230.3 ||
oṅkārajaladhiṃ yāvaduvāca bhṛgunandanaḥ  |
tīrthasaṅgamabhedānvai dharmaputrāya pṛcchate  || 230.4 ||
samāsenaiva munayastathāhaṃ kathayāmi vaḥ  |
saptaṣaṣṭisahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyastathaiva ca  || 230.5 ||
kathaṃ kenātra śakyante vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi  |
tathāpyatra muniśreṣṭhāḥ proktaṃ pārthāya vai yathā  || 230.6 ||
tīrthamoṃkāramārabhya vakṣye tīrthāvaliṃ śubhām  |
procyamānāṃ samāsena tāṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ maharṣayaḥ  || 230.7 ||
natvā somaṃ maheśānaṃ natvā brahmācyutāvubhau  |
sarasvatīṃ gaṇeśānaṃ devyāsāṅghripañkajam  || 230.8 ||
pūrvācāryāṃstathā sarvāndṛṣṭvādṛṣṭārthavedinaḥ  |
praṇamya narmadāṃ devīṃ vakṣye tīrthāvaliṃ tvimām  || 230.9 ||
oṃ namo viśvarūpāya oṅkārāyākhilātmane  |
yamārabhye pravakṣyāmi revātīrthāvaliṃ dvijāḥ  || 230.10 ||
asminmārkaṇḍagadite revātīrthakrame śubhe  |
purāṇasaṃhitādhyāyā mārkaṇḍāśramavarṇanam  || 230.11 ||
tataḥ praśnādhikāraśca praśaṃsā narmadodbhavā  |
tathā pañcadaśānāṃ ca pravāhānāṃ prakīrtanam  || 230.12 ||
nāmanirvacanaṃ tadvattathā kalpasamudbhavāḥ  |
ekaviṃśatikalpānāṃ tadvannāmānukīrtanam  || 230.13 ||
mārkaṇḍeyānubhūtānāṃ saptānāṃ lakṣaṇāni ca  |
māhātmyaṃ caiva revāyāḥ śivaviṣṇvostathaiva ca  || 230.14 ||
saṃhāralakṣaṇaṃ tadvadoṅkārasya ca sambhavaḥ  |
tathaivauṃkāramāhātmyamamarakaṇṭakīrtanam  || 230.15 ||
amareśvaratīrthaṃ ca tathā dāruvanaṃ mahat |
dārukeśvaratīrthaṃ ca tīrthaṃ vai carukeśvaram  || 230.16 ||
carukāsaṅgamastadvyadvatīpāteśvaraṃ tathā  |
pātāleśvaratīrthaṃ ca koṭiyajñāhvayaṃ tathā  || 230.17 ||
varuṇeśvaratīrthaṃ ca liṅgānyaṣṭottaraṃ śatam  |
siddheśvaraṃ yameśaṃ ca brahmeśvaramataḥ param  || 230.18 ||
sārasvataṃ cāṣṭarudraṃ sāvitraṃ somasaṃjñitam  |
śivakhātaṃ mahātīrthaṃ rudrāvartaṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 230.19 ||
brahmāvartaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ sūryāvartamataḥ param  |
pippalāvartatīrthaṃ ca pippalyāścaiva saṅgamaḥ  || 230.20 ||
amarakaṇṭamāhātmyaṃ kapilāsaṅgamastathā  |
viśalyāsambhavaścāpi bhṛgutuṅgādrikīrtanam  || 230.21 ||
viśalyāsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ karamardāsamāgamaḥ  |
karamardeśvaraṃ tīrthaṃ cakratīrthamanuttamam  || 230.22 ||
saṅgamo nīlagaṅgāyāḥ vidhvaṃsastripurasya ca  |
kīrtanaṃ tīrthadānānāṃ madhukatṛtīyāvratam  || 230.23 ||
apsareśvaratīrthaṃ ca dehakṣepe vidhistataḥ  |
tīrthaṃ jvāleśvaraṃ nāma jvālāyāḥ saṅgamastathā  || 230.24 ||
śakratīrthaṃ kuśāvartaṃ haṃsatīrthaṃ tathaiva ca  |
ambarīṣasya tīrthaṃ ca mahākāleśvaraṃ tathā  || 230.25 ||
mātṛkeśvaratīrthaṃ ca bhṛgutuṅgānuvarṇanam  |
tatra bhairavamāhātmyaṃ capaleśvarakīrtanam  || 230.26 ||
caṇḍapāṇeśca māhātmyaṃ kāverīsaṅgamastathā  |
kubereśvaratīrthaṃ ca vārāhīsaṅgamastathā  || 230.27 ||
saṅgamaścaṇḍavegāyāstīrthaṃ caṇḍeśvaraṃ tathā  |
eraṇḍīsaṅgamaḥ puṇya eraṇḍeśvaramuttamam  || 230.28 ||
pitṛtīrthaṃ ca tatraiva oṅkārasya ca sambhavam  |
māhātmyaṃ pañcaliṅgānāmoṅkārasya munīśvarāḥ  || 230.29 ||
koṭitīrthasya māhātmyaṃ tīrthaṃ kākahradaṃ tathā  |
jambukeśvaratīrthaṃ ca sārasvatamataḥ param  || 230.30 ||
kapilāsaṅgamastadvattīrthaṃ ca kapileśvaram  |
daityasūdanatīrthaṃ ca cakratīrthaṃ ca vāmanam  || 230.31 ||
tīrthalakṣaṃ viduḥ pūrve kapilāyāstu saṅgame  |
svargasya narakasyāpi lakṣaṇaṃ munibhāṣitam  || 230.32 ||
vyavasthānaṃ śarīrasya gopradānānuvarṇanam  |
aśokavanikātīrthaṃ mataṅgāśramavarṇanam  || 230.33 ||
aśokeśvaratīrthaṃ ca mataṅgeśvaramuttamam  |
tathā mṛgavanaṃ puṇyaṃ tatra tīrthaṃ manoratham  || 230.34 ||
saṅgamo'ṅgāragartāyā aṅgāreśvaramuttamam  |
tathā meghavanaṃ tīrthaṃ devyā nāmānukīrtanam  || 230.35 ||
saṅgamaścāpi kubjāyāstīrthaṃ kubjeśvaraṃ tathā  |
bilvāmrakaṃ tathā tīrthaṃ pūrṇadvīpamataḥ param  || 230.36 ||
tathā hiraṇyagarbhāyāḥ saṅgamaḥ puṇyakīrtanaḥ  |
dvīpeśvaraṃ nāma tīrthaṃ puṇyaṃ yajñeśvaraṃ tathā  || 230.37 ||
māṇḍavyāśramatīrthaṃ ca viśokāsaṅgamastathā  |
vāgīśvaraṃ nāma tīrthaṃ puṇyo vai vāgusaṅgamaḥ  || 230.38 ||
sahasrāvartakaṃ tatra tīrthaṃ saugandhikaṃ tathā  |
saṅgamaśca sarasvatyā īśānaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 230.39 ||
devatātrayatīrthaṃ ca śūlakhātaṃ tataḥ param  |
brahmodaṃ śāṅkaraṃ saumyaṃ sārasvatamataḥ param  || 230.40 ||
sahasrayajñatīrthaṃ ca kapālamocanaṃ tathā  |
āgneyamaditīśaṃ ca vārāhaṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 230.41 ||
tathā devapathaṃ tīrthaṃ tīrthaṃ yajñasahasrakam  |
śuklatīrthaṃ dīptikeśaṃ viṣṇutīrthaṃ ca yodhanam  || 230.42 ||
narmadeśvaratīrthaṃ ca varuṇeśaṃ ca mārutam  |
yogeśaṃ rohiṇītīrthaṃ dārutīrthaṃ ca sattamāḥ  || 230.43 ||
brahmāvartaṃ ca pattreśaṃ vāhnaṃ sauraṃ ca kīrtyate  |
meghanādaṃ dārutīrthaṃ devatīrthaṃ guhāśrayam  || 230.44 ||
narmadeśvarasaṃjñaṃ tatkapilātīrthamuttamam  |
karañjeśaṃ kuṇḍaleśaṃ pippalādamataḥ param  || 230.45 ||
vimaleśvaratīrthaṃ ca puṣkariṇyāśca saṅgamaḥ  |
praśaṃsā śūlabhedasya tatraivāndhakavikramaḥ  || 230.46 ||
devāśvāsanadānaṃ ca tathaivāndhakanigrahaḥ  |
śūlabhedasya cotpattistathā pātraparīkṣaṇam  || 230.47 ||
praśaṃsā dānadharmasya ṛṣiśṛṅgānubhāvanam  |
svargatiṃ dīrghatapaso bhānumatyāstatheṅgitam  || 230.48 ||
śabarasvargagamanaṃ māhātmyaṃ śūlabhedajam  |
kapileśvaratīrthaṃ ca mokṣatīrthamataḥ param  || 230.49 ||
saṅgamo mokṣanadyāśca tīrthaṃ ca vimaleśvaram  |
tathaivolūkatīrthaṃ ca puṣkariṇyāśca saṅgamaḥ  || 230.50 ||
ādityeśvaratīrthaṃ ca tīrthaṃ vai saṅgameśvaram  |
saṅgamo bhīmakulyāyāstīrthaṃ bhīmeśvaraṃ śubham  || 230.51 ||
mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrthaṃ ca tathā vai pippaleśvaram  |
karoṭīśvaratīrthaṃ ca tīrthamindreśvaraṃ śubham  || 230.52 ||
agastyeśaṃ kumāreśaṃ vyāseśvaramanuttamam  |
vaidyanāthaṃ ca kedāramānandeśvarasaṃjñitam  || 230.53 ||
mātṛtīrthaṃ ca muṇḍeśaṃ cauraṃ kāmeśvaraṃ tathā  |
saṅgamaścānuduhyā vai tīrthe bhīmārjunāhvaye  |
tīrthaṃ dharmeśvaraṃ nāma luṅkeśvaramataḥ param  || 230.54 ||
tato dhanadatīrthaṃ ca jaṭeśaṃ maṅgaleśvaram  |
kapileśvaratīrthaṃ ca gopāreśvaramuttamam  || 230.55 ||
maṇināgeśvaraṃ nāma maṇinadyāśca saṅgamaḥ  |
tilakeśvaratīrthaṃ ca gautameśamataḥ param  || 230.56 ||
tatraiva mātṛtīrthaṃ ca muninoktaṃ munīśvarāḥ  |
śaṅkhacūḍaṃ ca kedāraṃ pārāśaramataḥ param  || 230.57 ||
bhīmeśvaraṃ ca candreśamaśvavatyāśca saṅgamaḥ  |
bahvīśvaraṃ nāradeśaṃ vaidyanāthaṃ kapīśvaram  || 230.58 ||
kumbheśvaraṃ ca mārkaṇḍaṃ rāmeśaṃ lakṣmaṇeśvaram  |
megheśvaraṃ matsyakeśamapsarāhradasaṃjñakam  || 230.59 ||
dadhiskandaṃ madhuskandaṃ nandikeśaṃ ca vāruṇam  |
pāvakeśvaratīrthaṃ ca tathaiva kapileśvaram  || 230.60 ||
nārāyaṇāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ cakratīrthamanuttamam  |
caṇḍādityaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ caṇḍikātīrthamuttamam  || 230.61 ||
yamahāsāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ tathā gaṅgeśvaraṃ śubham  |
nandikeśvarasaṃjñaṃ ca naranārāyaṇāhvayam  || 230.62 ||
naleśvaraṃ ca mārkaṇḍaṃ śuklatīrthamataḥ param  |
vyāseśvaraṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ tatra siddheśvaraṃ tathā  || 230.63 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ prabhātīrthaṃ vāsukīśvaramuttamam  |
saṅgamaśca karañjāyā mārkaṇḍeśvaramuttamam  || 230.64 ||
tīrthaṃ koṭīśvaraṃ nāma tathā saṃkarṣaṇāhvayam  |
kanakeśaṃ manmatheśaṃ tīrthaṃ caivānasūyakam  || 230.65 ||
eraṇḍīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyo mātṛtīrthaṃ ca śobhanam  |
tīrthaṃ svarṇaśalākākhyaṃ tathā caivāmbikeśvaram  || 230.66 ||
karañjeśaṃ bhārateśaṃ nāgeśaṃ mukuṭeśvaram  |
saubhāgyasundarī tīrthaṃ dhanadeśvaramuttamam  || 230.67 ||
rohiṇyaṃ cakratīrthaṃ ca uttareśvarasaṃjñitam  |
bhogeśvaraṃ ca kedāraṃ niṣkalaṅkamataḥ param  || 230.68 ||
mārkaṇḍaṃ dhautapāpaṃ ca tīrthamāṅgiraseśvaram  |
koṭavīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaṃ koṭitīrthaṃ ca tatra vai  || 230.69 ||
ayonijaṃ paraṃ tīrthamaṅgāreśvaramuttamam  |
skāndaṃ ca nārmadaṃ brāhmaṃ vālmīkeśvarasaṃjñitam  || 230.70 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ kapāleśaṃ pāṇḍutīrthaṃ trilocanam  |
kapileśaṃ kambukeśaṃ prabhāsaṃ kohaneśvaram  || 230.71 ||
indreśaṃ vālukeśaṃ ca deveśaṃ śakrameva ca  |
nāgeśvaraṃ gautameśamahalyātīrthamuttamam  || 230.72 ||
rāmeśvaraṃ mokṣatīrthaṃ tathā kuśalaveśvarau  |
narmadeśaṃ kapardīśaṃ sāgareśamataḥ param  || 230.73 ||
dhaurādityaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ tīrthaṃ cāparayonijam  |
piṅgaleśvaratīrtha ca bhṛgvīśvaramanuttamam  || 230.74 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ tīrthaṃ koṭitīrthaṃ ca sattamāḥ  |
mārkaṇḍaṃ brahmatīrthaṃ ca ādivārāhamuttamam  || 230.75 ||
āśāpūrābhidhaṃ tīrthaṃ kauberaṃ mārutaṃ tathā  |
varuṇeśaṃ yameśaṃ ca rāmeśaṃ karkaṭeśvaram  || 230.76 ||
śakreśaṃ somatīrthaṃ ca nandāhradamanuttamam  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ cakratīrthaṃ ca rāmakeśavasaṃjñitam  || 230.77 ||
tathaiva rukmiṇītīrthaṃ śivatīrthamanuttamam  |
jayavārāhartīrthaṃ ca tīrthamasmāhakāhvayam  || 230.78 ||
aṅgāreśaṃ ca siddheśaṃ tapeśvaramataḥ param  |
punaḥ siddheśvaraṃ nāmatīrthaṃ ca varuṇeśvaram  || 230.79 ||
parāśareśvaraṃ puṇyaṃ kusumeśamanuttamam  |
kuṇḍaleśvaratīrthaṃ ca tathā kalakaleśvaram  || 230.80 ||
nyaṅkuvārāhasaṃjñaṃ ca aṅkolaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
śvetavārāhatīrthaṃ ca bhārgalaṃ sauramuttamam  || 230.81 ||
huṅkārasvāmitīrthaṃ ca śuklatīrthaṃ ca śobhanam  |
saṅgamo madhumatyāśca tīrthaṃ vai saṅgameśvaram  || 230.82 ||
narmadeśvarasaṃjñaṃ ca nadītritayasaṅgamaḥ  |
anekeśvaratīrthaṃ ca śarbheśaṃ mokṣasaṃjñitam  || 230.83 ||
kāverīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyastīrthaṃ gopeśvarāhvayam  |
mārkaṇḍeśaṃ ca nāgeśamudambaryāśca saṅgamaḥ  || 230.84 ||
sāmbādityāhvayaṃ tīrthamudambaryāśca saṅgamaḥ  |
siddheśvaraṃ ca mārkaṇḍaṃ tathā siddheśvarīkṛtam  || 230.85 ||
gopeśaṃ kapileśaṃ ca vaidyanāthamanuttamam  |
piṅgaleśvaratīrthaṃ ca saindhavāyatanaṃ mahat || 230.86 ||
bhūtīśvarāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ gaṅgāvāhamataḥ param  |
gautameśvaratīrthaṃ ca daśāśvamedhikaṃ tathā  || 230.87 ||
bhṛgutīrthaṃ tathā puṇyaṃ khyātā saubhāgyasundarī  |
vṛṣakhātaṃ ca tatraiva kedāraṃ dhūtapātakam  || 230.88 ||
tīrthaṃ dhūteśvarīsaṅgameraṇḍīsaṃjñakaṃ tathā  |
tīrthaṃ ca kanakeśvaryā jvāleśvaraṃ tataḥ param  || 230.89 ||
śālagrāmāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ somanāthamanuttamam  |
tathaivodīrṇavārāhaṃ tīrthaṃ candraprabhāsakam  || 230.90 ||
dvādaśādityatīrthaṃ ca tathā siddheśvarābhidham  |
kapileśvaratīrthaṃ ca tathā traivikramaṃ śubham  || 230.91 ||
viśvarūpāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ nārāyaṇakṛtaṃ tathā  |
mūlaśrīpatitīrthaṃ ca caulaśrīpatisaṃjñakam  || 230.92 ||
devatīrthaṃ haṃsatīrtha prabhāsaṃ tīrthamuttamam  |
mūlasthānaṃ ca kaṇṭheśamaṭṭahāsamataḥ param  || 230.93 ||
bhūrbhuveśvaratīrthaṃ ca khyātā śūleśvarī tathā  |
sārasvataṃ dārukeśamaśvinostīrthamuttamam  || 230.94 ||
sāvitrītīrthamatulaṃ vālakhilyeśvaraṃ tathā  |
narmadeśaṃ mātṛtīrthaṃ devatīrthamanuttamam  || 230.95 ||
macchakeśvaratīrthaṃ ca śikhitīrthaṃ ca śobhanam  |
koṭitīrthaṃ muniśreṣṭhāstatra koṭīśvarī mṛḍā  || 230.96 ||
tīrthaṃ paitāmahaṃ nāma māṇḍavye śvarasaṃjñitam  |
tatra nārāyaṇeśaṃ ca akrūreśamataḥ param  || 230.97 ||
devakhātaṃ siddharudraṃ vaidyanāthamanuttamam  |
tathaiva mātṛtīrthaṃ ca uttareśamataḥ param  || 230.98 ||
tathaiva narmadeśāṃ ca mātṛtīrthaṃ tathā punaḥ  |
tathā ca kurarītīrthaṃ ḍhauṇḍheśaṃ daśakanyakam  || 230.99 ||
suvarṇabindutīrthaṃ ca ṛṇapāpapramocanam  |
bhārabhūteśvaraṃ tīrthaṃ tathā muṇḍīśvaraṃ viduḥ  || 230.100 ||
ekaśālaṃ ḍiṇḍipāṇiṃ tīrthaṃ cāpsarasaṃ param  |
munyālayaṃ ca mārkaṇḍaṃ gaṇitādevatāhvayam  || 230.101 ||
āmaleśvaratīrthaṃ ca tīrthaṃ kantheśvaraṃ tathā  |
āṣāḍhītīrthamityāhuḥ śṛṅgītīrthaṃ tathaiva ca  || 230.102 ||
bakeśvaratīrthaṃ ca kapāleśaṃ tathaiva ca  |
mārkaṇḍaṃ kapileśaṃ ca eraṇḍīsaṅgamastathā  || 230.103 ||
eraṇḍīdevatātīrthaṃ rāmatīrthamataḥparam  |
jamadagneḥ paraṃ tīrthaṃ revāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ  || 230.104 ||
loṭaneśvaratīrtha talluṅkeśanāmakaṃ tathā  |
vṛṣarakhātaṃ tatra kuṇḍaṃ tathaiva ṛṣisattamāḥ  || 230.105 ||
tathā haṃseśvaraṃnāma tilādaṃ vāsaveśvaram  |
tathā koṭīśvaraṃ tīrthamalikātīrthamuttamam  |
vimaleśvaratīrthaṃ ca revāsāgarasaṅgame  || 230.106 ||
evaṃ tīrthāvaliḥ puṇyā mayā proktā maharṣayaḥ  |
tīrthasuktāvaliḥ puṇyā grathitā taṭarajjunā  || 230.107 ||
narmadānīranirṇiktā mārkaṇḍeyavinirmitā  |
maṇḍanāyeha sādhūnāṃ sarvalokahitāya ca  || 230.108 ||
daritadhvāntaśamanīdhāryā dharmārthibhiḥ sadā  |
ahorātrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sakṛjjaptvāśu nāśayet || 230.109 ||
trikālaṃ japtvā māsotthaṃ śivāgre ca trimāsikam  |
māsaṃ japtvātha varṣotthaṃ varṣaṃ japtvā śatābdikam  || 230.110 ||
śrāddhakāle ca viprāṇāṃ bhuñjatāṃ purataḥ sthitaḥ  |
paṭhaṃstīrthāvaliṃ puṇyāṃ gayāśrāddhaprado bhavet || 230.111 ||
pūjākāle ca devānāṃ śraddhayā purataḥ paṭhan  |
prīṇayetsarvadevāṃśca punāti sakalaṃ kulam  || 230.112 ||
evaṃ tīrthāvaliḥ puṇyā revātīradvayāśritā  |
mayā proktā muniśreṣṭhāstathaivaśṛṇutānaghāḥ  || 230.113 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthāvalikathanaṃ triṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 231

śrīsūta uvāca -
tathaiva tīrthastabakān vakṣye'hamṛṣisattamāḥ  |
yaistu tīrthāvalīgumphaḥ pūrvoktairekataḥ kṛtaḥ  || 231.1 ||
vibhakto bhaktalokānāmānandaprathanaḥ śubhaḥ  |
mṛkaṇḍatanayaḥ pūrvaṃ prāha pārthāya pṛcchate  || 231.2 ||
yathā tathāhaṃ vakṣyāmi tīrthānāṃ stabakāniha  |
śivāmbupānajā puṇyā revā kalpalatā kila  || 231.3 ||
tīradvayodbhūtatīrthaprasūnaiḥ puṣpitā śubhā  |
yatpuṇyagandhalakṣmyā vai trailokyaṃ surabhīkṛtam  || 231.4 ||
tatpuṣpamakarandasya rasāsvādaviduttamaḥ  |
bhramaraḥ khalu mārkaṇḍo munirmatimatāṃ varaḥ  || 231.5 ||
tatpuṣpamālāṃ hṛdaye tīrthastabakacitritām  |
dadhāti satataṃ puṇyāṃ munirbhṛgukulodvahaḥ  |
tasyāḥ stabakasaṃsthānaṃ vakṣye'hamṛṣisattamāḥ  || 231.6 ||
oṅkāratīrthamārabhya yāvatpaścimasāgaram  |
saṃgamāḥ pañcatriṃśadvai nadīnāṃ pāpanāśanāḥ  || 231.7 ||
daśaikamuttare tīre satriviṃśati dakṣiṇe  |
pañcatriṃśattamaḥ śreṣṭho revāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ  || 231.8 ||
saṅgamaḥ sahitānyevaṃ revātīradvaye'pi ca  |
catuḥśatāni tīrthāni prasiddhāni dvijottamāḥ  || 231.9 ||
triśataṃ śivatīrthāni trayīstriṃśatsamanvitam  |
tatrāpi vyaktito vakṣye śṛṇudhvaṃ tāni sattamāḥ  || 231.10 ||
mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrthāni daśa teṣu munīśvarāḥ  |
daśādityabhavānyatra navaiva kapileśvarāḥ  || 231.11 ||
somasaṃsthāpitānyaṣṭau tāvanto narmadeśvarāḥ  |
koṭitīrthānyathāṣṭau ca sapta siddheśvarāstathā  || 231.12 ||
nāgeśvarāśca saptaiva revātīradvaye'pi tu  |
saptaiva vahnivihitānyathāpyāvartasaptakam  || 231.13 ||
kedāreśvaratīrthāni pañca pañcendrajāni ca  |
varuṇeśāśca pañcaiva pañcaiva dhanadeśvarāḥ  || 231.14 ||
devatīrthāni pañcaiva catvāro vai yameśvarāḥ  |
vaidyanāthāśca catvāraścatvāro vānareśvarāḥ  || 231.15 ||
aṅgāreśvaratīrthāni tāvantyeva munīśvarāḥ  |
sārasvatāni catvāri catvāro dārukeśvarāḥ  || 231.16 ||
gautameśvaratīrthāni trīṇi rāmeśvarāstrayaḥ  |
kapāleśvaratīrthāni trīṇi haṃsakṛtāni ca  || 231.17 ||
trīṇyeva mokṣatīrthāni trayo vai vimaleśvarāḥ  |
sahasrayajñatīrthāni trīṇyeva munirabravīt || 231.18 ||
bhīmeśvarāstrayaḥ khyātāḥ svarṇatīrthāni trīṇi ca  |
dhautapāpadvayaṃ proktaṃ karañjeśadvayaṃ tathā  || 231.19 ||
ṛṇamocanatīrthe dve tathā skandeśvaradvayam  |
daśāśvamedhatīrthe dve nandītīrthadvayaṃ dvijāḥ  || 231.20 ||
manmatheśadvayaṃ caiva bhṛgutīrthadvayaṃ tathā  |
parāśareśvarau dvau ca ayonīsaṃbhavadvayam  || 231.21 ||
vyāseśvaradvayaṃ proktaṃ pitṛtīrthadvayaṃ tathā  |
nandikeśvaratīrthe dve dvau ca gopeśvarau smṛtau  || 231.22 ||
māruteśadvayaṃ tadvaddvau ca jvāleśvarau smṛtau  |
śuklatīrthadvayaṃ puṇyamapsareśadvayaṃ tathā  || 231.23 ||
pippaleśvaratīrthe dve māṇḍavyeśvarasaṃjñite  |
dvīpeśvaradvayaṃ caiva prāha tadvadbhṛgūdvahaḥ  |
uttareśvaratīrthe dve aśokeśadvayī tathā  || 231.24 ||
dve yodhanapure caiva rohiṇītīrthakadvayam  |
luṅkeśvaradvayaṃ khyātamākhyānaṃ muninā tathā  || 231.25 ||
saikonaviṃśatiśataṃ tīrthānyekaikaśo dvijāḥ  |
stabakeṣu kṛtaṃ tīrthaṃ dviśataṃ sacaturdaśam  || 231.26 ||
śaivānyetāni tīrthāni vaiṣṇavāni ca sattamāḥ  |
śṛṇudhvaṃ procyamānāni brāhmaśāktāni ca kramāt || 231.27 ||
aṣṭaviṃśatitīrthāni vaiṣṇavānyabravīnmuniḥ  |
teṣu vārāhatīrthāni ṣaḍeva munisattamāḥ  || 231.28 ||
catvāri cakratīrthāni śeṣāṇyaṣṭādaśaiva hi  |
viṣṇunādhiṣṭhitānyeva prāha pūrvaṃ mṛkaṇḍajaḥ  || 231.29 ||
tathaiva brahmaṇā siddhyai saptatīrthānyavīvadat |
triṣu ca brahmaṇaḥ pūjā brahmeśāścaturo'pare  |
aṣṭāviṃśanmayā khyātā yathāsaṅkhyaṃ yathākramam  || 231.30 ||
etatpavitramatulaṃ hyetatpāpaharaṃ param  |
narmadācaritaṃ puṇyaṃ māhātmyaṃ munibhāṣitam  || 231.31 ||

sūta uvāca -
evamuddeśataḥ prokto revātīrthakramo mayā  |
yathā pārthāya saṃkṣepānmārkaṇḍo munirabravīt || 231.32 ||
avāntarāṇi tīrthāni teṣu guptānyanekaśaḥ  |
yatra yāvatpramāṇāni tānyākarṇayatānaghāḥ  || 231.33 ||
oṅkāratīrthaparitaḥ parvatādamarakaṇṭāt |
krośadvaye sarvadikṣu sārdhakoṭītrayī matā  || 231.34 ||
tīrthānāṃ saṃkhyayā guptaprakaṭānāṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
koṭirekā tu tīrthānāṃ kapilāsaṅgame pṛthak || 231.35 ||
aśokavanikāyāśca tīrthaṃ lakṣaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  |
śatamaṃ gāragartāyāḥ saṅgame munisattamāḥ  || 231.36 ||
tīrthānāmayutaṃ tadvatkubjāyāḥ saṅgame sthitam  |
śataṃ hiraṇyagarbhāyāḥ saṅgame samavasthitam  || 231.37 ||
tīrthānāmaṣṭaṣaṣṭiśca viśokāsaṅgame sthitā  |
tathā sahasraṃ tīrthānāṃ saṃsthitaṃ vāgusaṅgame  || 231.38 ||
śataṃ sarasvatīsaṅge śuklatīrthe śatadvayam  |
sahasraṃ viṣṇutīrtheṣu mahiṣmatyāmathāyutam  || 231.39 ||
śūlabhede ca tīrthānāṃ sāgraṃ lakṣaṃ sthitaṃ dvijāḥ  |
devagrāme sahasraṃ ca tīrthānāṃ munirabravīt || 231.40 ||
luṅkeśvare ca tīrthānāṃ sāgrā saptaśatī sthitā  |
tīrthānyaṣṭottaraśataṃ maṇinadyāśca saṅgame  |
vaidyanāthe ca tīrthānāṃ śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ viduḥ  || 231.41 ||
evaṃ tāvatpramāṇāni tīrthe kumbheśvare dvijāḥ  |
sāgraṃ lakṣaṃ ca tīrthānāṃ sthitaṃ revorasaṅgame  || 231.42 ||
tataścāpyadhikāni syuriti mārkaṇḍabhāṣitam  |
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi vyāsadvīpāśritāni ca  || 231.43 ||
saṅgame ca karañjāyāḥ sthitamaṣṭottarāyutam  |
eraṇḍīsaṅgame tadvattīrthānyaṣṭādhikaṃ śatam  || 231.44 ||
dhūtapāpe ca tīrthānāṃ ṣaṣṭiraṣṭādhikā sthitā  |
