skandatīrthe śataṃ puṇyaṃ tīrthānāṃ muniruktavān  || 231.45 ||
kohaneśa ca tīrthānāṃ ṣaṣṭiraṣṭādhikā sthitā  |
sārdhakoṭī ca tīrthānāṃ sthitā vai korilāpure  || 231.46 ||
rāmakeśavatīrthe ca sahasraṃ sāgramuktavān  |
asmāhake sahasraṃ ca tīrthāni nivasanti hi  || 231.47 ||
lakṣāṣṭakaṃ sahasre dve śuklatīrthe dvijottamāḥ  |
tīrthāni kathayāmāsa purā pārthāya bhārgavaḥ  || 231.48 ||
śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ prāha pratyekaṃ saṅgameṣu ca  |
nadīnāmavaśiṣṭānāṃ kāverīsaṅgamaṃ vinā  || 231.49 ||
kāveryāḥ saṅgame viprāḥ sthitā pañcaśatī tathā  |
tīrthānāṃ parvasu tathā viśeṣo muninoditaḥ  || 231.50 ||
mokṣatīrthaṃ hi satprāhuḥ purāṇapuruṣāśritam  |
bhṛgoḥ kṣetre ca tīrthānāṃ koṭirekā samāśritā  || 231.51 ||
sādhikānāmṛṣiśreṣṭhā vaktuṃ śakto hi ko bhavet |
sarvāmarāśrayaṃ proktaṃ sarvatīrthāśrayaṃ tathā  || 231.52 ||
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātaṃ pūjitaṃ siddhisādhanam  |
bhārabhūtyāṃ ca tīrthānāṃ sthitamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam  || 231.53 ||
akrūreśvaratīrthe ca sārdhaṃ tīrthaśataṃ sthitam  |
vimaleśvaratīrthe tu revāsāgarasaṅgame  |
daśāyutāni tīrthānāṃ sādhikānyabravīnmuniḥ  || 231.54 ||

|| iti  śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthasaṃkhyāparigaṇanavarṇanaṃ nāmaikatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||


ṛKV adhyāya 232

sūta uvāca -
iti vaḥ kathitaṃ viprā revāmāhātmyamuttamam  |
yathopadiṣṭaṃ pārthāya mārkaṇḍeyena vai purā  || 232.1 ||
tathā tīrthakadambāśca teṣu tīrthaviśeṣataḥ  |
prādhānyena mayā khyātā yathāsaṅkhyaṃ yathākramam  || 232.2 ||
etatpavitramatulaṃ hyetatpāpaharaṃ param  |
narmadācaritaṃ puṇyaṃ māhātmyaṃ munibhāṣitam  || 232.3 ||
saptakalpānugo vipro narmadāyāṃ munīśvarāḥ  |
mṛkaṇḍatanayo dhīmānparamārthaviduttamaḥ  || 232.4 ||
saṃsevya sarvatīrthāni nadīḥ sarvāśca vai purā  |
bahukalpasmarāṃ revāmālakṣya śivadehajām  || 232.5 ||
me kaleti ca śarvoktāṃ śaraṇaṃ śarvajāṃ yayau  |
ajarāmamarāṃ devīṃ daityadhvaṃsakarīṃ parām  || 232.6 ||
mahāvibhavasaṃyuktāṃ bhavaghnīṃ bhavajāhnavīm  |
tasyāmābadhya satprema jātaḥ so'pyajarāmaraḥ  || 232.7 ||
ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyaśca sattamāḥ  |
vyavasthitāni revāyāstīrayugme pade pade  || 232.8 ||
sāritaḥ paritaḥ santi satīrthāstu sahasraśaḥ  |
na tulāṃ yānti revāyāstāśca manye munīśvarāḥ  || 232.9 ||
etadvaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yatpṛṣṭamakhilaṃ dvijāḥ  |
yanmaheśamukhācchrutvā vāyurāha ṛṣīnprati  || 232.10 ||
tadvanmṛkaṇḍatanayo'pyanubhūyākhilāṃ nadīm  |
satīrthāṃ padaśaḥ prāha pāṇḍuputrāya pāvanīm  || 232.11 ||
etacca kathitaṃ sarvaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa dvijottamāḥ  |
narmadācaritaṃ puṇyaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham  || 232.12 ||
kimanyaiḥ saritāṃ toyaiḥ sevitaistu sahasraśaḥ  |
yadi saṃsevyate toyaṃ revāyāḥ pāpanāśanam  || 232.13 ||
mekalājalasaṃsevī muktimāpnoti śāśvatīm  || 232.14 ||
yathā yathā bhajenmartyo yadyadicchati tīrthagaḥ  |
tattadāpnoti niyataṃ śraddhayāśraddhayāpi ca  || 232.15 ||
idaṃ brahmā hariridamidaṃ sākṣātparo haraḥ  |
idaṃ brahma nirākāraṃ kaivalyaṃ narmadājalam  || 232.16 ||
tāvadgarjanti tīrthāni nadyo hṛdayaphalapradāḥ  |
yāvanna smaryate revā sevāhevā kalau naraiḥ  || 232.17 ||
dhruvaṃ loke hitārthāya śivena svaśarīrataḥ  |
śaktiḥ kāpi saridrūpā reveyamavatāritā  || 232.18 ||
tāvadgarjanti yajñāśca vanakṣetrādayo bhṛśam  |
yāvanna narmadānāmakīrtanaṃ kriyate kalau  || 232.19 ||
garimā gāṇyate tāvattapodānavratādiṣu  |
narairvā prāpyate yāvadbhuvi bhargabhavā dhunī  || 232.20 ||
ye vasantyuttare kūle rudrasyānucarā hi te  |
vasanti yāmyatīre ye lokaṃ te yānti vaiṣṇavam  || 232.21 ||
dhanyāste deśavaryāste yeṣu deśeṣu narmadā  |
narakāntakarī śaśvatsaṃśritā śarvanirmitā  || 232.22 ||
kṛtapuṇyāśca te lokāḥ śokāya na bhavanti te  |
ye pibanti jalaṃ puṇyaṃ pārvatīpatisindhujam  || 232.23 ||
idaṃ pavitramatulaṃ revāyāścaritaṃ dvijāḥ  |
śṛṇoti yaḥ kīrtayate mucyate sarvapātakaḥ  || 232.24 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvavedaiśca saṣaḍaṅgapadakramaiḥ  |
śrutaiśca paṭhitaistasmātphalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet || 232.25 ||
satrayājī phalaṃ yacca labhate dvādaśābdikam  |
śrutvā sakṛcca revāyāścaritaṃ tatphalaṃ labhet || 232.26 ||
sarvatīrthāvagāhācca yatphalaṃ sāgarādiṣu  |
sakṛcchrutvā ca māhātmyaṃ revāyāstatphalaṃ labhet || 232.27 ||
etaddharmyamupākhyānaṃ sarvaśāstreṣvanuttamam  |
deśe vā maṇḍale vāpi nagare grāmamadhyataḥ  || 232.28 ||
gṛhe vā tiṣṭhate yasya likhitaṃ sārvavārṇikam  |
sa brahmā sa śivaḥ sākṣātsa ca devo janārdanaḥ  || 232.29 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ mārge'yaṃ devasevitaḥ  |
gurūṇāṃ ca guruḥ śāstraṃ paramaṃ siddhikāraṇam  || 232.30 ||
yaścedaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ purāṇaṃ devabhāṣitam  |
brāhmaṇo vedavānbhūyātkṣatriyo vijayī bhavet || 232.31 ||
dhanāḍhyo jāyate vaiśyaḥ śūdro vai dharmabhāgbhavet || 232.32 ||
saubhāgyasantatiṃ nārī śrutvaitatsamavāpnuyāt |
śriyaṃ saukhyaṃ svargavāsaṃ janma caivottame kule  || 232.33 ||
rasabhedī kṛtaghnaśca svāmidhruṅmitravañcakaḥ  |
goghnaśca garadaścaiva kanyāvikrayakārakaḥ  || 232.34 ||
brahmaghnaśca surāpī ca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ  |
narmadācaritaṃ śṛṇvaṃstāmabdaṃ yo'bhiṣevate  || 232.35 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
pākabhedī vṛthāpākī devabrāhmaṇanindakaḥ  || 232.36 ||
parīvādī guroḥ pitroḥ sādhūnāṃ nṛpatestathā  |
te'pi śrutvā ca pāpebhyo mucyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 232.37 ||
ye punarbhāvitātmānaḥ śastraṃ śṛṇvanti nityaśaḥ  |
pūjayanti ca tacchāstraṃ nārmadaṃ vastrabhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 232.38 ||
puṣpaiḥ phalaiścandanādyairbhojanairvividhairapi  |
śāstre'sminpūjite devāḥ pūjitā guravastathā  || 232.39 ||
iha loke pare caiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena gandhavastrādibhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 232.40 ||
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā vācakaṃ śāstrameva ca  |
vedapāṭhaiśca yatpuṇyamagnihotraiśca pālitaiḥ  || 232.41 ||
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti narmadācarite śubhe  |
kurukṣetre ca yatpuṇyaṃ prabhāse puṣkare tathā  || 232.42 ||
rudrāvarte gayāyāṃ ca vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
gaṅgādvāre prayāge ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame  || 232.43 ||
evamādiṣu tīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ jāyate nṛṇām  |
narmadācaritaṃ śrutvā tatpuṇyaṃ sakalaṃ labhet || 232.44 ||
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu narmadācaritaṃ śubham  |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā śṛṇudhvaṃ tatphalaṃ mahat || 232.45 ||
samāpya śivasaṃsthānaṃ devakanyāsamāvṛtaḥ  |
rudrasyānucaro bhūtvā śivena saha modate  || 232.46 ||
dharmākhyānamidaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvākhyāneṣvanuttamam  |
gṛhe'pi paṭhyate yasya caturvarṇasya sattamāḥ  || 232.47 ||
dhanyaṃ tasya gṛhaṃ manye gṛhasthaṃ cāpi tatkulam  |
pustakaṃ pūjayedyastu narmadācaritasya tu  || 232.48 ||
narmadā pūjitā tena bhagavāṃśca maheśvaraḥ  |
vācake pūjite tadvaddevāśca ṛṣayo'rcitāḥ  || 232.49 ||
lekhayitvā ca sakalaṃ revācaritamuttamam  |
bhūṣaṇaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ yo dadāti dvijanmane  || 232.50 ||
narmadāsarvatīrtheṣu snānadānena yatphalam  |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti sa naro nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 232.51 ||
etatpurāṇaṃ rudroktaṃ mahāpuṇyaphalapradam  |
svargadaṃ putradaṃ dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ kīrttivardhanam  || 232.52 ||
dharmyamāyuṣyamatulaṃ duḥkhaduḥsvapnanāśanam  |
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ cāpi sarvakāmārthasiddhidam  || 232.53 ||
yatpradattamidaṃ puṇyaṃ purāṇaṃ vācyate dvijaiḥ  |
śivaloke sthitistasya purāṇākṣaravatsarī  || 232.54 ||
iti nigaditametannarmadāyāścaritraṃ pavanagaditamagryaṃ śarvavaktrādavāpya  |
tribhuvanajanavandyaṃ tvetadādau munīnāṃ kulapatipuratastatsūtamukhyena sādhu  || 232.55 ||

|| iti śrīskānde mahāpurāṇa ekāśītisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ pañcama āvantyakhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍasamāptirevākhaṇḍapustakadānādimāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
